Explanatory Remarks:

1. Editorial notes or additions are enclosed in ().

Text:

gopālabhaṭṭa haribhaktivilāsaḥ
gauravavilāsa
haribhaktivilāsaḥ
prathamo vilāsaḥ
atha maṅgalācaraṇam
caitanyadevaṃ bhagavantamāśraye
śrīvaiṣṇavānāṃ pramude'ñjasā likhan |
āvaśyakaṃ karma vicārya sādhubhiḥ
sārdhaṃ samāhṛtya samastaśāstrataḥ || 1.1 ||
bhaktervilāsāṃścinute prabodhā
nandasya śiṣyo bhagavatpriyasya |
gopālabhaṭṭo raghunāthadāsaṃ
santoṣayan rūpasanātanau ca || 1.2 ||
mathurānāthapādābjapremabhaktivilāsataḥ |
jātaṃ bhaktivilāsākhyaṃ tadbhaktāḥ śīlayantvimam || 1.3 ||
jīyāsurātyantikabhaktiniṣṭhāḥ śrīvaiṣṇavā māthuramaṇḍale'tra |
kāśīśvaraḥ kṛṣṇavane cakāstu śrīkṛṣṇadāsaśca salokanāthaḥ || 1.4 ||
tatra lekhyapratijñā
ādau sakāraṇaṃ lekhyaṃ śrīgurvāśrayaṇaṃ tataḥ |
guruḥ śiṣyaḥ parīkṣādirbhagavānmanavo'sya ca || 1.5 ||
mantrādhikārī siddhyādiśodhanaṃ mantrasaṃskriyāḥ |
dīkṣā nityaṃ brāhmakāle śubhotthānaṃ pavitratā |
prātaḥ smṛtyādi kṛṣṇasya vādyādaiśca prabodhanam || 1.6 ||
nirmālyottāraṇādyādau maṅgalārātrikaṃ tataḥ |
maitrādikṛtyaṃ śaucācamanaṃ dantasya dhāvanam || 1.7 ||
snānaṃ tāntrikasandhyādi devasadmādisaṃskriyā || 1.8 ||
tulasyādyāhṛtirgehasnānamuṣṇodakādikam |
vastraṃ pīṭhaṃ cordhvapuṇḍraṃ śrīgopīcandanādikam || 1.9 ||
cakrādimudrā mālā ca gṛhasandhyārcanaṃ guroḥ |
māhātmyaṃ cātha kṛṣṇasya dvāraveśmāntarārcanam || 1.10 ||
pūjārthāsanamarghyādisthāpanaṃ vighnavāraṇam |
śrīgurvādinatirbhūtaśuddhiḥ prāṇaviśodhanam || 1.11 ||
nyāsamudrāpañcakaṃ ca kṛṣṇadhyānāntarārcane |
pūjā padāni śrīmūrtiśālagrāmaśilāstathā || 1.12 ||
dvārakodbhavacakrāṇi śuddhayaḥ pīṭhapūjanam |
āvāhanādi tanmudrā āsanādisamarpaṇam || 1.13 ||
snapanaṃ śaṅkhaghaṇṭādivādyaṃ nāmasahasrakam |
purāṇapāṭho vasanamupavītaṃ vibhūṣaṇam || 1.14 ||
gandhaḥ śrītulasīkāṣṭhacandanaṃ kusumāni ca |
patrāṇi tulasī cāṅgopāṅgāvaraṇapūjanam || 1.15 ||
dhūpo dīpaśca naivedyaṃ pānaṃ homo balikriyā |
avagaṇḍūṣādyāsyavāso divyagandhādikaṃ punaḥ || 1.16 ||
rājopacārā gītādi mahānīrājanaṃ tathā |
śaṅkhādivādanaṃ sāmbuśaṅkhanīrājanaṃ stutiḥ || 1.17 ||
natiḥ pradakṣiṇā karmādyarpaṇaṃ japa yācane |
āgaḥkṣamāpaṇaṃ nānāgāṃsi nirmālyadhāraṇam || 1.18 ||
śaṅkhāmbutīrthaṃ tulasīpūjā tanmṛttikādi ca |
dhātrīsnānaniṣedhasya kālo vṛtterupārjanam || 1.19 ||
madhyāhne vaiśadevādiśrāddhaṃ cānarpyamacyute |
vinārcāmaśane doṣāstathānarpitabhojane || 1.20 ||
naivedyabhakṣaṇaṃ santaḥ satsaṅgo'sadasaṅgatiḥ |
asadgatirvaiṣṇavopahāsanindādiduṣphalam || 1.21 ||
satāṃ bhaktirviṣṇuśāstraṃ śrīmadbhāgavataṃ tathā |
līlākathā ca bhagavaddharmāḥ sāyaṃ nijakriyāḥ || 1.22 ||
karmapātaparīhārastrikālārcā viśeṣataḥ |
naktaṃ kṛtyānyatho pūjāphalasiddhyādidarśanam || 1.23 ||
viṣṇvarthadānaṃ vividhopacārā nyūnapūraṇam |
śayanaṃ mahimārcāyāḥ śrīmannāmnastathādbhutaḥ || 1.24 ||
nāmāparādhā bhaktiśca premāthāśrayaṇādayaḥ |
pakṣeṣvekādaśī sāṅgā śrīdvādaśyaṣṭakaṃ mahat || 1.25 ||
kṛtyāni mārgaśīrṣādimāseṣu dvādśeṣvapi |
puraścaraṇakṛtyāni mantraṃ siddhasya lakṣaṇam || 1.26 ||
mūrtyāvirbhāvanaṃ mūrtipratiṣṭhā kṛṣṇamandiram |
jīrṇoddhṛtiḥ śrītulasīvivāho'nanyakarma ca || 1.27 ||
tatra śrīgurūpasattikāraṇam
kṛpayā kṛṣṇadevasya tadbhaktajanasaṅgataḥ |
bhaktermāhātmyamākarṇya tāmicchan sadguruṃ bhajet || 1.28 ||
atrānubhūyate nityaṃ duḥkhaśreṇī paratra ca |
duḥsahā śrūyate śāstrāttitīrṣedapi tāṃ sudhīḥ || 1.29 ||
tathā coktamekādaśaskandhe
labdhvā sudurlabhamidaṃ bahusambhavānte
mānuṣyamarthadamanityamapīha dhīraḥ |
tūrṇaṃ yateta na patedanumṛtyu yāvan
niḥśreyasāya viṣayaḥ khalu sarvataḥ syāt || 1.30 ||
svayaṃ śrībhagavatā ca
nṛdehamādyaṃ sulabhaṃ sudurlabhaṃ
plavaṃ sukalpaṃ gurukarṇadhāram |
mayānukūlena nabhasvateritaṃ
pumān bhavābdhiṃ na taretsa ātmahā || 1.31 ||
atha śrīgurūpasattiḥ
tatraiva śrīprabuddhayogeśvaroktau
tasmādguruṃ prapadyeta jijñāsuḥ śreya uttamam |
śābde pare ca niṣṇātaṃ brahmaṇyupaśamāśrayam || 1.32 ||
svayaṃ śrībhagavaduktau
madabhijñaṃ guruṃ śāntamupāsīta madātmakam || 1.33 ||
kramadīpikāyāṃ ca
vipraṃ pradhvastakāmaprabhṛtiripughaṭaṃ nirmalāṅgaṃ gariṣṭhāṃ
bhaktiṃ kṛṣṇāṅghripaṅkeruhayugalarajorāgiṇīmudvahantam |
vettāraṃ vedaśāstāgamavimalapathāṃ sammataṃ satsu dāntaṃ
vidyāṃ yaḥ saṃvivitsuḥ pravaṇatanumanā deśikaṃ saṃśrayeta || 1.34 ||
śrutāvapi
tadvijñānārthaṃ sa gurumevābhigacchetsamitpāṇiḥ śrotriyaṃ brahmaniṣṭham |
ācāryavān puruṣo veda || 1.35 ||
atha gurūpasattinityatā
śrībhāgavate daśamaskandhe śrutistutau
vijitahṛṣīkavāyubhiradāntamanasturagaṃ
ya iha yatanti yantumatilolamupāyakhidaḥ |
vyasanaśatānvitāḥ samavahāya guroścaraṇaṃ
vaṇija ivāja santyakṛtakarṇadharā jaladhau || 1.36 ||
śrutau ca
naiṣā tarkeṇa matirāpaneyā
proktānyenaiva sujñānāya preṣṭha || 1.37 ||
atha viśeṣataḥ śrīgurorlakṣaṇāni
mantramuktāvalyām
avadātānvayaḥ śuddhaḥ svocitācāratatparaḥ |
āśramī krodharahito vedavitsarvaśāstravit || 1.38 ||
śraddhāvānanasūyaśca priyavākpriyadarśanaḥ |
śuciḥ suveśastaruṇaḥ sarvabhūtahite rataḥ || 1.39 ||
dhīmānanuddhatamatiḥ pūrṇo'hantā vimarśakaḥ |
saguṇo'rcāsu kṛtadhīḥ kṛtajñaḥ śiṣyavatsalaḥ || 1.40 ||
nigrahānugrahe śakto homamantraparāyaṇaḥ |
ūhāpohaprakārajñaḥ śuddhātmā yaḥ kṛpālayaḥ |
ityādilakṣaṇairyukto guruḥ syādgarimānidhiḥ || 1.41 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ ca
devatopāsakaḥ śānto viṣayeṣvapi niḥspṛhaḥ |
adhyātmavidbrahmavādī vedaśāstrārthakovidaḥ || 1.42 ||
uddhartuṃ caiva saṃhartuṃ samartho brāhmaṇottamaḥ |
tattvajño yantramantrāṇāṃ marmabhettā rahasyavit || 1.43 ||
puraścaraṇakṛddhomamantrasiddhaḥ prayogavit |
tapasvī satyavādī ca gṛhastho gururucyate || 1.44 ||
viṣṇusmṛtau
paricaryāyaśolābhalipsuḥ śiṣyādgururnahi |
kṛpāsindhuḥ susampūrṇaḥ sarvasattvopakārakaḥ || 1.45 ||
niḥspṛhaḥ sarvataḥ siddhaḥ sarvavidyāviśāradaḥ |
sarvasaṃśayasaṃchettā nālaso gururāhṛtaḥ || 1.46 ||
śrīnāradapañcarātre śrībhagavannāradasaṃvāde
brāhmaṇaḥ sarvakālajñaḥ kuryātsarveṣvanugraham |
tadabhāvāddvijaśreṣṭhaḥ śāntātmā bhagavanmayaḥ || 1.47 ||
bhāvitātmā ca sarvajñaḥ śāstrajñaḥ satkriyāparaḥ |
siddhitrayamāyukta ācāryatve'bhiṣecitaḥ || 1.48 ||
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ kṣatriyo'nugrahe kṣamaḥ |
kṣatriyasyāpi ca gurorbhāvādīdṛśo yadi || 1.49 ||
vaiśyaḥ syāttena kāryaśca dvaye nityamanugrahaḥ |
sajātīyena śūdreṇa tādṛśena mahāmate |
anugrahābhiṣekau ca kāryau śūdrasya sarvadā || 1.50 ||
kiṃ ca
varṇottam'tha ca gurau sati yā viśrute'pi ca |
svadeśato'thatvānyatra nedaṃ kāryaṃ śubhārthinā || 1.51 ||
vidyamāne tu yaḥ kuryātyatra tatra viparyayam |
tasyehāmutra nāśaḥ syāttasmācchāstroktamācaret ||
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīyaḥ prātilomyaṃ na dīkṣayet || 1.52 ||
pādme ca
mahābhāgavataḥ śreṣṭho brāhmaṇo vai gururnṛṇām |
sarveṣāmeva lokānāmasau pūjyo yathā hariḥ || 1.53 ||
mahākūlaprasūto'pi sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ |
sahasraśākhādhyāyī ca na guruḥ syādavaiṣṇavaḥ || 1.54 ||
iti
gṛhītaviṣṇudīkṣāko viṣṇupūjāparo naraḥ |
vaiṣṇavo'bhihito'bhijñairitaro'smādavaiṣṇavaḥ || 1.55 ||
tattvasāgare
bahvāśī dīrghasūtrī ca viṣayādiṣu lolupaḥ |
hetuvādarato duṣṭo'vāgvādī guṇanindakaḥ || 1.56 ||
aromā bahuromā ca ninditāśramasevakaḥ |
kāladanto'sitauṣṭhaśca durgandhiśvāsavāhakaḥ || 1.57 ||
duṣṭalakṣaṇasampanno yadyapi svayamīśvaraḥ |
bahupratigrhāsakta ācāryaḥ śrīkṣayāvahaḥ || 1.58 ||
atha śiṣyalakṣaṇāni
mantramuktāvalyām
śiṣyaḥ śuddhānvayaḥ śrīmān vinītaḥ priyadarśanaḥ |
satyavākpuṇyacarito'dabhradhīrdambhavarjitaḥ || 1.59 ||
kāmakrodhaparityāgī bhaktaśca gurupādayoḥ |
devatāpravaṇaḥ kāyamanovāgbhirdivāniśam || 1.60 ||
nīrujo nirjitāśeṣapātakaḥ śraddhyānvitaḥ |
dvijadevapitṝṇāṃ ca nityamarcāparāyaṇaḥ || 1.61 ||
yuvā viniyatāśeṣakaraṇaḥ karuṇālayaḥ |
ityādilakṣaṇairyuktaḥ śiṣyo dīkṣādhikāravān || 1.62 ||
ekādaśaskandhe ca
amānyamatsaro dakṣo nirmamo dṛṭhasauhṛdaḥ |
asatvaro'rthajijñāsuranasūyuramoghavāk || 1.63 ||
athāpekṣyāḥ
agastyasaṃhitāyām
alasā malināḥ kliṣṭā dāmbhikāḥ kṛpaṇāstathā |
daridrā rogiṇo ruṣṭā rāgiṇo bhogalālasāḥ || 1.64 ||
asūyāmatsaragrastāḥ śaṭhāḥ paruṣavādinaḥ |
anyāyopārjitadhanāḥ paradāraratāśca ye || 1.65 ||
viduṣāṃ vairiṇaścaiva ajñāḥ paṇḍitamāninaḥ |
bhraṣṭavratāśca ye kaṣṭavṛttayaḥ piśunāḥ khalāḥ || 1.66 ||
bahvāśinaḥ krūraceṣṭā durātmanaśca nindita |
ityevamādayo'pyanye pāpiṣṭhāḥ puruṣādhamāḥ || 1.67 ||
akṛtyebhyo'nivāryāśca guruśikṣāsahiṣṇavaḥ |
evambhūtāḥ parityājyāḥ śiṣyatve nopakalpitāḥ || 1.68 ||
yadyete hyupakalperan devatākrośabhājanāḥ |
bhavantīha dardrāste putradāravivarjitāḥ || 1.69 ||
nārakāścaiva dehānte tiryañcaḥ prabhavanti te || 1.70 ||
hayaśīrṣapañcarātre
jaiminiḥ sugataścaiva nāstiko nagna eva ca |
kapilaścākṣapādaśca ṣaḍete hetuvādinaḥ || 1.71 ||
etanmatānusāreṇa vartante ye narādhamāḥ |
te hetuvādinaḥ proktāstebhyastantraṃ na dāpayet || 1.72 ||
iti
tayoḥ parīkṣā cānyo'nyamekābdaṃ sahavāsataḥ |
vyavahārasvabhāvānubhavenaivābhijāyate || 1.73 ||
atha parīkṣaṇam
mantramuktāvalyāṃ
tayorvatsaravāsena jñātānyonyasvabhāvayoḥ |
gurutā śiṣyatā ceti nānyathaiveti niścayaḥ || 1.74 ||
śrutiśca
nāsaṃvatsaravāsine deyāt || 1.75 ||
sārasaṅgrahe'pi
sadguruḥ svāśritaṃ śiṣyaṃ varṣmekaṃ parīkṣayet || 1.76 ||
rājñi cāmātyajā doṣāḥ patnīpāpaṃ svabhartari |
tathā śiṣyārjitaṃ pāpaṃ guruḥ prāpnoti niścitam || 1.77 ||
kramadīpikāyāṃ tu
santoṣayedakuṭilādretarāntarātmā
taṃ svairdhanaiśca vapuṣāpyanukūlavāṇyā |
abdatrayaṅkamalanābhadhiyā'tidhīras
tuṣṭe vivakṣatu gurāvatha mantradīkṣām || 1.78 ||
atha viśeṣataḥ śrīgurusevāvidhiḥ
kaurme śrīvyāsagītāyām
udakumbhaṃ kuśān puṣpaṃ samidho'syāharetsadā |
mārjanaṃ lepanaṃ nityamaṅgānāṃ vāsasāṃ caret || 1.79 ||
nāsya nirmālyaśayanaṃ pādukopāsanahāvapi |
ākrāmedāsanaṃ chyāyāmāsandīṃ vā kadācana || 1.80 ||
sādhayeddantakāṣṭhādīn kṛtyaṃ cāsmai nivedayeet || 1.81 ||
anāpṛcchya na gantavyaṃ bhavetpriyahite rasaḥ |
na pādau sārayedasya sannidhāne kadācana || 1.82 ||
jṛmbhāhāsyādikaṃ caiva kaṇṭhaprāvaraṇaṃ tathā |
varjayetsannidhau nityamathāsphoṭanameva ca || 1.83 ||
kiṃ ca
śreyastu guruvadvṛttirnityameva samācaret |
guruputreṣu dāreṣu guroścaiva svabandhuṣu || 1.84 ||
utsādanaṃ vai gātrāṇāṃ snāpanocchiṣṭabhojane |
na kuryādguruputrasya pādayoḥ śaucameva ca || 1.85 ||
guruvatparipūjyāśca savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ |
asavarṇāstu sampūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ || 1.86 ||
abhyañjanaṃ snāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanameva ca |
gurupatnyā ca kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam || 1.87 ||
devyāgame śrīśivoktau
guruśayyāsanaṃ yānaṃ pāduke pādapīṭhakam |
snānodakaṃ tathā chāyāṃ laṅghayenna kadācana || 1.88 ||
guroragre pṛthakpūjāmadvaitaṃ ca parityajet |
dīkṣāṃ vyākhyāṃ prabhutvaṃ ca guroragre vivarjayet || 1.89 ||
śrīnāradoktau
yatra yatra guruṃ paśyettatra tatra kṛtāñjali |
praṇametdaṇḍavadbhūmau chinnamūla iva drumaḥ || 1.90 ||
gurorvākyāsanaṃ yānaṃ pādukopānahau tathā |
vastraṃ chāyāṃ tathā śiṣyo laṅghayenna kadācana || 1.91 ||
śrīmanusmṛtau
nodāharedgurornāma parokṣamapi kevalam |
na caivāsyānukurvīta gatibhāṣaṇaceṣṭitam || 1.92 ||
gurorgurau sannihite guruvaddhṛtimācaret |
na cāvisṛṣṭo guruṇā svān gurūnabhivādayet || 1.93 ||
śrīnāradapañcarātre
yathā tathā yatra tatra na gṛhṇīyācca kevalam |
abhaktyā na gurornāma gṛhṇīyācca yatātmavān || 1.94 ||
praṇavaḥ śrīstato nāma viṣṇuśabdādanantaram |
pādaśabdasametaṃ ca natamūrdhāñjalīyutaḥ || 1.95 ||
kiṃ ca
na tamājñāpayenmohāttasyājñāṃ na ca laṅghayet |
nānivedya guroḥ kiñcidbhoktavyaṃ vā gurostathā || 1.96 ||
anyatra ca
āyāntamagrato gacchedgacchantaṃ tamanuvrajet |
āsane śayane vāpi na tiṣṭhedagrato guroḥ || 1.97 ||
yatkiñcidannapānādi priyaṃ dravyaṃ manoramam |
samarpya gurave paścātsvayaṃ bhuñjīta pratyaham || 1.98 ||
śrīviṣṇusmṛtau
na gurorapriyaṃ kuryāttāḍitaḥ pīḍito'pi vā |
nāvamanyeta tadvākyaṃ nāpriyaṃ hi samācaret || 1.99 ||
ācāryasya priyaṃ kuryātprāṇairapi dhanairapi |
karmaṇā manasā vācā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 1.100 ||
śrīnāradapañcarātre
yo vakti nyāyarahitamanyāyena śṛṇoti yaḥ |
tāvubhau narakaṃ ghoraṃ vrajataḥ kālamakṣayam || 1.101 ||
vaiṣṇavatantre
trāyasva bho jagannātha guro saṃsāravahninā |
dagdhaṃ māṃ kāladaṣṭaṃ ca tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 1.102 ||
iti
tatra śrīvāsudevasya sarvadevaśiromaṇeḥ |
pādāmbujaikabhāgeva dīkṣā grāhyā manīṣibhiḥ || 1.103 ||
prathamaskandhe
sattvaṃ rajastama iti prakṛterguṇāstair
yuktaḥ paramapuruṣa eka ihāsya dhatte |
sthityādaye hariviriñcihareti saṃjñāḥ
śreyāṃsi tatra khalu sattvatanornṝṇāṃ syuḥ || 1.104 ||
kiṃ ca
athāpi yatpādanakhāvasṛṣṭaṃ
jagadviriñcopahṛtārhaṇāmbhaḥ |
seśaṃ punātyanyatamo mukundāt
ko nāma loke bhagavatpadārthaḥ || 1.105 ||
śrīdaśamaskandhe
tanniśamyātha munayo vismitā muktasaṃśayāḥ |
bhūyāṃsaṃ śraddadhurviṣṇuṃ yataḥ śāntiryato'bhayam || 1.106 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye yamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde
vyāmohāya carācarasya jagataste te purāṇāgamās
tāṃ tāmeva hi devatāṃ paramikāṃ jalpantu kalpāvadhi |
siddhānte punareka eva bhagavān viṣṇuḥ samastāgama
vyāpāreṣu vivecanavyatikaraṃ nīteṣu niścīyate || 1.107 ||
nārasiṃhe
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyamutkṣipya bhujamucyate |
vedācchāstraṃ paraṃ nāsti na devaḥ keśavātparaḥ || 1.108 ||
yataḥ pādme
arirmitraṃ viṣṃ pathyamadharmo dharmatāṃ vrajet |
suprasanne hṛṣīkeśe viparīte viparyayaḥ || 1.109 ||
tatraiva śrībhagavadvākyam
mannimittaṃ kṛtaṃ pāpamapi dharmāya kalpate |
māmanādṛtya dharmo'pi pāpaṃ syānmatprabhāvataḥ || 1.110 ||
ataevoktaṃ skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
vāsudevaṃ parityajya yo'nyaṃ devamupāsate |
svamātaraṃ parityajya śvapacīṃ vandate hi saḥ || 1.111 ||
tatraivānyatra
vāsudevaṃ parityajya yo'nyaṃ devamupāsate |
tyaktvāmṛtaṃ sa mūḍhātmā bhuṅkte halāhalaṃ viṣam || 1.112 ||
mahābhārate
yastu viṣṇuṃ parityajya mohādanyamupāsate |
sa hemarāśimutsṛjya pāṃśurāśiṃ jighṛkṣati || 1.113 ||
anādṛtya tu yo viṣṇumanyadevaṃ samāśrayet |
gaṅgāmbhasaḥ sa tṛṣṇārto mṛgatṛṣṇāṃ pradhāvati || 1.114 ||
pañcarātre
yo mohādviṣṇumanyena hīnadevena durmatiḥ |
sādhāraṇaṃ sakṛdbrūte so'ntyajo nāntyajo'ntyajaḥ || 1.115 ||
vaiṣṇavatantre
na labheyuḥ punarbhaktiṃ hareraikāntikīṃ jaṅāḥ |
ekāgramanasaścāpi viṣṇusāmānyadarśinaḥ || 1.116 ||
anyatra ca
yastu nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ brahmarudrādidaivataiḥ |
samatvenaiva vīkṣeta sa pāṣaṇḍī bhaveddhruvam || 1.117 ||
iti
sahasranāmastotrādau ślokaughāḥ santi cedṛśāḥ |
viśeṣataḥ sattvaniṣṭhaiḥ sevoyviṣṇurna cāparaḥ || 1.118 ||
tathā ca śrīharivaṃśe śivavākyam
harireva sadārādhyo bhavadbhiḥ sattvasaṃsthitaiḥ |
viṣṇumantraṃ sadā viprāḥ paṭhadhvaṃ dhyāta keśavam || 1.119 ||
iti
īdṛṅmāhātmyavākyeṣu saṅgṛhīteṣu sarvataḥ |
granthabāhulyadoṣaḥ syāllikhyante'pekṣitāni tat || 1.120 ||
atha śrīvaiṣṇavamantramāhātmyam
āgame
mantrān śrīmantrarājādīn vaiṣṇavān gurvanugrahāt |
sarvaiśvaryaṃ japan prāpya yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam || 1.121 ||
puṇyaṃ varṣasahasrairyaiḥ kṛtaṃ suvipulaṃ tapaḥ |
japanti vaiṣṇavānmantrānnarāste lokapāvanāḥ || 1.122 ||
vaiṣṇave ca
prajapan vaiṣṇavānmantrān yaṃ yaṃ paśyati cakṣuṣā |
padā vā saṃspṛśetsadyo mucyate'sau mahābhayāt || 1.123 ||
iti
likhyate viṣṇumantrāṇāṃ mahimātha viśeṣataḥ |
tātparyataḥ śrīgopālamantramāhātmyapuṣṭaye || 1.124 ||
tatra dvādaśākṣarāṣṭākṣarayormāhātmyam
padmapurāṇe devadūtavikuṇḍalasaṃvāde
sāṅgaṃ samudraṃ sanyāsaṃ sarṣidaivatam |
sadīkṣāvidhi sadhyānaṃ sayantraṃ dvādaśākṣaram || 1.125 ||
aṣṭākṣaraṃ ca mantreśaṃ ye japanti narottamāḥ |
tān dṛṣṭvā brahmahā śudhyete yato viṣṇavaḥ svayam || 1.126 ||
śaṅkhinaścakriṇo bhūtvā brahmāyurvanamālinaḥ |
vasanti vaiṣṇave loke viṣṇurūpeṇa te narāḥ || 1.127 ||
tatraiva dvādaśākṣarayascaturthaskandhe
japaśca paramo guhyaḥ śrūyatāṃ me nṛpātmaja |
yaṃ saptarātraṃ prapaṭhan pumān paśyati khecarān || 1.128 ||
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe
gatvā gatvā nivartante candrasūryādayo grahāḥ |
adyāpi na nivartante dvādaśākṣaracintakāḥ || 1.129 ||
aṣṭākṣarasya, yathā nāradapañcarātre
trayo vedāḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni chandāṃsi vividhāḥ surāḥ |
sarvamaṣṭākṣarāntaḥsthaṃ yaccānyadapi vāṅmayam || 1.130 ||
sarvavedāntasārārthaṃ saṃsārārṇavatāraṇaḥ |
gatiraṣṭākṣaro nṝṇāṃ na punarbhavakāṅkṣiṇām || 1.131 ||
yatrāṣṭākṣarasaṃsiddho mahābhāgo mahīyate |
na tatra sañcariṣyanti vyādhidurbhikṣataskarāḥ || 1.132 ||
devadānavagandharvāḥ siddhavidyādharādayaḥ |
praṇamanti mahātmānamaṣṭākṣaravidaṃ naram || 1.133 ||
vyaktaṃ hi bhagavāneva sākṣānnārāyaṇaḥ svayam |
aṣṭākṣarasvarūpeṇa mukheṣu parivartate || 1.134 ||
pādmottarakhaṇḍe
evamaṣṭākṣaro mantro jñeyaḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ |
sarvaduḥkhaharaḥ śrīmān sarvamantrātmakaḥ śubhaḥ || 1.135 ||
liṅgapurāṇe
kimanyairbahubhirmantraiḥ kimanyairbahubhirvrataiḥ |
namo nārāyaṇeti mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ || 1.136 ||
tasmātsarveṣu kāleṣu namo nārāyaṇeti yaḥ |
japetsa yāti viprendra viṣṇulokaṃ sabāndhavaḥ || 1.137 ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe
aṣṭākṣaro mahāmantraḥ sarvapāpaharaḥ paraḥ |
sarveṣāṃ viṣṇumantrāṇāṃ rājatve parikīrtitaḥ || 1.138 ||
śrīśukavyāsasaṃvāde ca
namo nārāyaṇāyeti mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ |
bhaktānāṃ japatāṃ tāta svargamokṣaphalapradaḥ || 1.139 ||
eṣa eva paro mokṣa eṣa svarga udāhṛtaḥ |
sarvavedarahasyebhyaḥ sāra eṣa samuddhṛtaḥ || 1.140 ||
viṣṇunā vaiṣṇavānāṃ tu hitāya manunā purā |
kīrtitaḥ sarvapāpaghnaḥ sarvakāmapradāyakaḥ || 1.141 ||
nārāyaṇāya nama ityayameva satyaṃ
saṃsāraghoraviṣasaṃharaṇāya mantraḥ |
śṛṇvantu satyamatayo muditāstarāgā
uccaistarāmupadiśāmyahamūrdhvabāhuḥ || 1.142 ||
bhūtvordhvabāhuradyāhaṃ satyapūrvaṃ bravīmi vaḥ |
he putraśiṣyāḥ śṛṇuta na mantro'ṣṭākṣarātparaḥ || 1.143 ||
ataevoktaṃ gāruḍe
āsīno vā śayāno vā tiṣṭhāno yatra tatra vā |
namo nārāyaṇeti mantraikaśaraṇo bhavet || 1.144 ||
tāpanīśrutiṣu
devā ha vai prajāpatimabruvannānuṣṭubhasya mantrarājasya nārasiṃhasya phalaṃ no brūhi bhagava iti sa hovāca prajāpatirya etaṃ mantrarājaṃ nārasiṃhamānuṣṭubhaṃ nityamadhīte so'gnipūto bhavati sa vāyupūto bhavati sa ādityapūto bhavati sa somapūto bhavati sa satyapūto bhavati sa brahmapūto bhavati sa viṣṇupūto bhavati sa rudrapūto bhavati sa sarvapūto bhavati sa sarvapūto bhavati || 1.145 ||
tatraivānte
anupanītaśatamekamekenopanītena tatsamam | upanītaśatamekamekena gṛhasthena tatsamam | gṛhasthaśatamekamekena vānaprasthena tatsamam | vānaprasthaśatamekamekena yatinā tatsamam | yatīnāṃ tu śataṃ pūrṇamekamekena rudrajāpakena tatsamam | rudrajāpakaśatamekamekena atharvaśiraḥśikhādhyāpakena tatsamam |
tadvā etatparamaṃ dhāma mantrarājādhyāpakasya yatra na sūryastapati yatra na vāyurvāti yatra na candramā bhāti yatra na nakṣatrāṇi bhānti yatra nāgnirdahati yatra na mṛtyuḥ praviśati yatra na duḥkhaṃ sadānandaṃ paramānandaṃ śāntaṃ śāśvataṃ sadāśivaṃ brahmādivanditaṃ yogidhyeyaṃ paramaṃ padaṃ yatra gatvā na nivartante yoginaḥ | tadetadṛcābhyuktam | tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ |
divīva cakṣurātatam | tadviprāso vipanyavo jāgṛvāṃsaḥ samindhate | viṣṇoryatparamaṃ padam || 1.146 ||
atha śrīrāmamantrāṇāṃ māhātmyam
agastyasaṃhitāyām
sarveṣu mantravargeṣu śreṣṭhaṃ vaiṣṇavamucyate |
gāṇapatyeṣu śaiveṣu śāktasaureṣvabhīṣṭadam || 1.147 ||
vaiṣṇaveṣvapi mantreṣu rāmamantrāḥ phalādhikāḥ |
gāṇapatyādimantrebhyaḥ koṭikoṭiguṇādhikāḥ || 1.148 ||
vinaiva dīkṣāṃ viprendra puraścaryāṃ vinaiva hi |
vinaiva nyāsavidhinā japamātreṇa siddhidāḥ || 1.149 ||
mantreṣvaṣṭasvanāyāsaphalado'yaṃ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ |
ṣaḍakṣaro'yaṃ mantrastu mahāghaughanivāraṇaḥ || 1.150 ||
mantrarāja iti proktaḥ sarveṣāmuttamottamaḥ |
dainandinaṃ tu duritaṃ pakṣamāsartuvarṣajam || 1.151 ||
sarvaṃ dahati niḥśeṣaṃ tūlācalamivānalaḥ |
brahmahatyāsahasrāṇi jñānājñānakṛtāni ca || 1.152 ||
svarṇasteyasurāpānagurutalpayutāni ca |
koṭikoṭisahasrāṇi hyupapāpāni yānyapi |
sarvāṇyapi praṇaśyanti rāmamantrānukīrtanāt || 1.153 ||
tāpanīśrutiṣu ca
ya etattārakaṃ brāhmaṇo nityamadhīte, sa pāpmānaṃ tarati, sa mṛtyuṃ tarati, sa bhrūhatyāṃ tarati, sa sarvahatyāṃ tarati, sa saṃsāraṃ tarati, sa sarvaṃ tarati, sa vimuktāśrito bhavati, so'mṛtatvaṃ ca gacchati || 1.154 ||
atha gopāladevamantramāhātmyam
mantrāstu kṛṣṇadevasya sākṣādbhagavato hareḥ |
sarvāvatārabījasya sarvato vīryavattamāḥ || 1.155 ||
tathā ca bṛhadgautamīye śrīgovindavṛndāvanākhye
sarveṣāṃ mantravaryāṇāṃ śreṣṭho vaiṣṇava ucyate |
viśeṣātkṛṣṇamanavo bhogamokṣaikasādhanam || 1.156 ||
yasya yasya ca mantrasya yo yo devastathā punaḥ |
abhedāttanmanūnāṃ ca devatā saiva bhāṣyate || 1.157 ||
kṛṣṇa eva paraṃ brahma saccidānandavigrahaḥ |
smṛtimātreṇa teṣāṃ vai bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ || 1.158 ||
iti
tatrāpi bhagavattāṃ svāṃ tanvato gopalīlayā |
tasya śreṣṭhatamā mantrāsteṣvapyaṣṭādaśākṣaraḥ || 1.159 ||
tāpanīśrutiṣu
oṃ munayo ha vai brahmāṇamūcuḥ | kaḥ paramo devaḥ | kuto mṛtyurbibheti | kasya jñānenākhilaṃ jñātaṃ bhavati | kenedaṃ viśvaṃ saṃsaratīti | tānu hovāca brāhmaṇaḥ kṛṣṇo vai paramaṃ daivatam | govindānmṛtyurbibheti | gopījanavallabhajñānenākhilaṃ jñātaṃ bhavati | svāhayedaṃ saṃsaratīti | tamu hocuḥ | kaḥ kṛṣṇo govindaḥ ko'sau gopījanavallabhaḥ kaḥ kā svāheti | tānuvāca brāhmaṇaḥ pāpakarṣaṇo gobhūmivedavidito veditā gopījanāvidyākalāprerakastanmāyā ceti | sakalaṃ paraṃ brahmaiva tat | yo dhyāyati rasati bhajati so'mṛto bhavati so'mṛto bhavatīti | te hocuḥ kiṃ tadrūpaṃ kiṃ rasanaṃ kathaṃ vāho tadbhajanam | tatsarvaṃ vividiṣatāmākhyāhīti | tadu hovāca hairaṇyaḥ gopaveśamabhrābhaṃ taruṇaṃ kalpadrumāśritam || 1.160 ||
kiṃ ca, tatraivāgre
bhaktirasya bhajanam | tadihāmutropādhinairāsyenaivāmuṣminmanaḥkalpanam | etadeva ca naiṣkarmyam |
kṛṣṇaṃ taṃ viprā bahudhā yajanti
govindaṃ santaṃ bahudhārādhayanti |
gopījanavallabho bhuvanāni dadhre
svāhāśrito jagadejayjatsvaretāḥ || 1.161 ||
vāyuryathaiko bhuvanaṃ praviṣṭo
janye janye pañcarūpo babhūva |
kṛṣṇastathaiko'pi jagaddhitārthaṃ
śabdenāsau pañcapado vibhāti || 1.162 ||
iti
kiṃ ca tatraivopāsanavidhikathanānantaram
eko vaśī sarvagaḥ kṛṣṇa īḍya
eko'pi san bahudhā yo vibhāti |
taṃ pīṭhasthaṃ ye'nubhajanti dhīrās
teṣāṃ sukhaṃ śāśvataṃ netareṣām || 1.163 ||
nityo nityānāṃ cetanaścetanānām
eko bahūnāṃ yo vidadhāti kāmān |
taṃ pīṭhagaṃ ye'nubhajanti dhīrās
teṣāṃ siddhiḥ śāśvatī netareṣām || 1.164 ||
etadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ ye
nityamuktāḥ saṃyajante na kāmān |
teṣāmasau goparūpaḥ prayatnāt
prakāśayedātmapadaṃ tadaiva || 1.165 ||
yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvaṃ
yo vidyāstasmai gopāyati sma kṛṣṇaḥ |
taṃ ha daivamātmabuddhiprakāśaṃ
mumukṣurvai śaraṇamanuvrajeta || 1.166 ||
oṃkāreṇāntaritaṃ ye japanti
govindasya pañcapadaṃ manum |
teṣāmasau darśayedātmarūpaṃ
tasmānmumukṣurabhyasennityaśāntyai || 1.167 ||
tasmādanye pañcapadādabhūvan
govindasya manavo mānavānām |
daśārṇādyāste'pi saṅkrandanādyair
abhyasyante bhūtikāmairyathāvat || 1.168 ||
kiṃ ca tatraiva
tadu hovāca brāhmaṇo'sāvanavarataṃ me dhyātaḥ stutaḥ parārdhānte so'budhyata | gopaveśo me puruṣaḥ purastādāvirbabhūva | tataḥ praṇatena mayā'nukūlena hṛdā mahyamaṣṭādaśārṇaṃ svarūpaṃ sṛṣṭaye dattvāntarhitaḥ | punaḥ sisṛkṣā me prādurabhūt | teṣvakṣareṣu bhaviṣyajjagadrūpaṃ prakāśayat | tadiha kādāpo | lātpṛthivī | īto'gniḥ | bindorinduḥ | tannādādarka iti klīṃkārādasṛjam | kṛṣṇādākāśaṃ yadvāyurityuttarātsurabhiṃ vidyāṃ prādurakārṣam | taduttarāttaduttarātstrīpumādi cedaṃ sakalamidamiti || 1.169 ||
tathā ca gautamīyatantre
klīṃkārādasṛjadviśvamiti prāha śruteḥ śiraḥ |
lakārātpṛthivī jātā kakārājjalasambhavaḥ || 1.170 ||
īkārādvahnirutpanno nādādāyurajāyata |
bindorākāśasambhūtiriti bhūtātmako manuḥ ||
svāśabdena ca kṣetrajño heti citprakṛtiḥ parā |
tayoraikyasamudbhūtirmukhaveṣṭanavarṇakaḥ ||
ataeva hi viśvasya layaḥ svāhārṇake bhavet || 1.171 ||
punaśca sā śrutiḥ
etasyaiva yajanena candradhvajo gatamohamātmānaṃ vedayitvā oṃkārāntarālakaṃ manumāvartayatsaṅgarahito'bhyānayat | tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ | divīva cakṣurātatam | tasmādenaṃ nityamabhyaset || 1.172 ||
ityādi
tatraivāgre
yasya pūrvapadādbhūmirdvitīyātsalilodbhavaḥ |
tṛtīyātteja udbhūtaṃ caturthādgandhavāhanaḥ || 1.173 ||
pañcamādambarotpattistamevaikaṃ samabhyaset |
candradhvajo'gamadviṣṇuḥ paramaṃ padamavyayam || 1.174 ||
tato viśuddhaṃ vimalaṃ
viśokamaśeṣalobhādinirastasaṅgam |
yattatpadaṃ pañcapadaṃ tadeva
sa vāsudevo na yato'nyadasti || 1.175 ||
tamekaṃ govindaṃ saccidānandavigrahaṃ pañcapadaṃ vṛndāvanasurabhūruhatalāsīnaṃ satataṃ samarudgaṇo'haṃ paramayā stutyā toṣayāmi || 1.176 ||
iti
kiṃ ca stutyanantaram
amuṃ pañcapadaṃ mantramāvartayedyaḥ sa yātyanāyāsataḥ kevalaṃ tatpadaṃ tat | anejadekaṃ manaso javīyo naitaddevā āpnuvan pūrvamarśāt || 1.177 ||
tasmātkṛṣṇa eva paro devastaṃ dhyāyettaṃ rasettaṃ yajediti oṃ tatsaditi || 1.178 ||
trailokyasaṃmohanatantre ca, devīṃ prati śrīmahādevoktāṣṭādaśākṣaraprasaṅga eva
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāmīśvaro jagadīśvaraḥ |
santi tasya mahābhāgā avatārāḥ sahasraśaḥ || 1.179 ||
teṣāṃ madhye'vatārāṇāṃ bālatvamatidurlabham |
amānuṣāṇi karmāṇi tāni tāni kṛtāni ca || 1.180 ||
śāpānugrahakartṛtve yena sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
tasya matnraṃ pravakṣyāmi sāṅgopāṅgamanuttamam || 1.181 ||
yasya vijñānamātreṇa naraḥ sarvajñatāmiyāt |
putrārthī putramāpnoti dharmārthī labhate dhanam || 1.182 ||
sarvaśāstrārthapārajño bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
trailokyaṃ ca vaśīkuryātvyākulīkurute jagat || 1.183 ||
mohayetsakalaṃ so'pi mārayetsakalān ripūn |
bahunā kimihoktena mumukṣurmokṣamāpnuyāt || 1.184 ||
yathā cintāmaṇiḥ śreṣṭho yathā gauśca yathā satī |
yathā dvijo yathā gaṅgā tathāsau mantra uttamaḥ || 1.185 ||
yathāvadakhilaśreṣṭhaṃ yathā śāstraṃ tu vaiṣṇavam |
yathā susaṃskṛtā vāṇī tathāsau mantra uttamaḥ || 1.186 ||
kiṃ ca
ato mayā sureśāni pratyahaṃ japyate manuḥ |
naitena sadṛśaḥ kaścidjagatyasmin caracare || 1.187 ||
sanatkumārakalpe'pi
gopālaviṣayā mantrāstrayastriṃśatprabhedataḥ |
teṣu sarveṣu mantreṣu mantrarājamimaṃ śṛṇu || 1.188 ||
suprasannamimaṃ mantraṃ tantre sammohanāhvaye |
gopanīyastvayā mantro yatnena munipuṅgava || 1.189 ||
anena mantrarājena mahendratvaṃ purandaraḥ |
jagāma devadeveśo viṣṇunā dattamañjasā || 1.190 ||
durvāsasaḥ purā śāpādasaubhāgyena pīḍitaḥ |
sa eva subhagavatvaṃ vai tenaiva punarāptavān || 1.191 ||
bahunā kimihoktena puraścaraṇasādhanaiḥ |
vināpi japamātreṇa labhate sarvamīpsitam || 1.192 ||
prabhuṃ śrīkṛṣṇacaitanyaṃ taṃ nato'smi gurūttamam |
kathañcidāśrayādyasya prākṛto'pyuttamo bhavet || 1.193 ||
athādhikāranirṇayaḥ
tāntrikeṣu ca mantreṣu dīkṣāyāṃ yoṣitāmapi |
sādhvīnāmadhikāro'sti śūdrādīnāṃ ca saddhiyām || 1.194 ||
tathā ca smṛtyarthasāre pādme ca vaiśākhamāhātmye śrīnāradāmbarīṣasaṃvāde
āgamoktena mārgeṇa strīśūdrairapi pūjanam |
kartavyaṃ śraddhayā viṣṇościntayitvā patiṃ hṛdi || 1.195 ||
śūdrāṇāṃ caiva bhavati nāmnā vai devatārcanam |
sarve'pyāgamamārgeṇa kuryurvedānukāriṇā || 1.196 ||
strīṇāmapyadhikāro'sti viṣṇorārādhanādiṣu |
patipriyaratānāṃ ca śrutireṣā sanātanī || 1.197 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ śrīrāmamantrarājamuddiśya
śucivratatamāḥ śūdrā dhārmikā dvijasevakāḥ |
striyaḥ pativratāścānye pratilomānulomajāḥ
lokāścāṇḍālaparyantāḥ sarve'pyatrādhikāriṇaḥ || 1.198 ||
iti
guruśca siddhasādhyādimantradāne vicārayet |
svakulānyakulatvaṃ ca bālaprauḍhatvameva ca || 1.199 ||
strīpuṃnapuṃsakatvaṃ ca rāśinakṣatramelanam |
suptaprabodhakālaṃ ca tathā ṛṇadhanādikam || 1.200 ||
atha siddhasādhyādiśodhanam
saradātilake
prākpratyagagrā rekhāḥ syuḥ pañca yāmyottarāgragāḥ |
tāvatyaśca catuṣkoṣṭhacatuṣkaṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhavet || 1.201 ||
indvagnirudranavanetrayugena dikṣu
ṛtvaṣṭaṣaḍaśacaturdaśabhautikeṣu |
pātālapañcadaśavahnihimāṃśukoṣṭhe
varṇāṃllikhellipibhavān kramaśastu dhīmān || 1.202 ||
janmarkṣākṣaratau yāvanmantrādimākṣaram |
caturbhiḥ koṣṭhakaistvekamiti koṣṭhacatuṣṭaye || 1.203 ||
punaḥ koṣṭhakakoṣṭheṣu savyato janmabhākṣarāt |
siddhasādhyasusiddhārikramājjñeyā vicakṣṇaiḥ || 1.204 ||
siddhaḥ sidhyati kālena sādhyastu japahomataḥ |
susiddho grahamātreṇa arirmūlanikṛntanaḥ || 1.205 ||
siddhasiddho yathoktena dviguṇātsiddhasādhakaḥ |
siddhasusiddho'rdhajapātsiddhārirhanti bāndhavān || 1.206 ||
sādhyasiddho dviguṇikaḥ sādhyasādhyo hyanarthakaḥ |
tatsusiddhastriguṇitātsādhyārirhanti gotrajān || 1.207 ||
susiddhasiddhordhajapāttatsādhyastu guṇādhikāt |
tatsusiddho grahādeva susiddhāriḥ svagotrahā || 1.208 ||
arisiddhaḥ sutān hanyādarisādhyastu kanyakāḥ |
tatsusiddhastu patnīghnastadarirhanti sādhakam || 1.209 ||
tathā ca tantre, asya ca mantraviśeṣe'pavādaḥ
nṛsiṃhārkavarāhāṇāṃ prāsādapraṇavasya ca |
vaidikasya ca mantrasya siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet || 1.210 ||
svapnalabdhe striyā datte mālāmantre ca tryakṣare |
ekākṣare tathā mantre siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet || 1.211 ||
svakulānyakulatvādi vijñeyaṃ cāgamāntarāt |
na vistarabhayādatra vyarthatvādapi likhyate || 1.212 ||
śrīmadgopāladevasya sarvaiśvaryapradarśinaḥ |
tādṛkśaktiṣu mantreṣu nahi kiñcidvicāryate || 1.213 ||
tathā ca kramadīpikāyām
sarveṣu varṇeṣu tathāśrameṣu
nārīṣu nānāhvayajanmabheṣu
dātā phalānāmabhivāñchitānāṃ
drāgeva gopālakamantra eṣaḥ || 1.214 ||
trailokyasaṃmohanatantre ca, aṣṭādaśākṣaramantramadhikṛtya śrīśivenoktam
na cātra śātravā doṣā narṇasvādivicāraṇā |
ṛkṣarāśivicāro vā na kartavyo manau priye || 1.215 ||
kecicchinnāśca ruddhāśca kecinmadasamuddhatāḥ |
malināḥ stambhitāḥ kecitkīlitā dūṣitā api |
etairdoṣairyuto nāyaṃ yatastribhunottamaḥ || 1.216 ||
iti
sāmānyataśca yathā bṛhadgautamīye
atha kṛṣṇamanūn vakṣye dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalapradān |
yān vai vijñāya munayo lebhire muktimañjasā || 1.217 ||
gṛhasthā vanagāścaiva yatayo brahmacāriṇaḥ |
striyaḥ śūdrādayaścaiva sarve yatrādhikāriṇaḥ || 1.218 ||
nātra cintyo'riśuddhyādirnārimitrādilakṣaṇam |
na vā prayāsabāhulyaṃ sādhane na pariśramaḥ || 1.219 ||
ajñānatūlarāśeśca analaḥ kṣaṇamātrataḥ |
siddhasādhyasusiddhārirūpā nātra vicāraṇā || 1.220 ||
sarveṣāṃ siddhamantrāṇāṃ yato brahmākṣaro manuḥ |
prajāpatiravāpāgryaṃ devarājyaṃ śacīpatiḥ |
avāpustridaśāḥ svargaṃ vāgīśatvaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ || 1.221 ||
ityādi
tatraivāntare
viṣṇubhaktyā viśeṣeṇa kiṃ na sidhyati bhūtale |
kīṭādibrahmaparyantaṃ govindānugrahānmune || 1.222 ||
sarvasampattinilayāḥ sarvatrāpyakutobhayāḥ |
ityādi kathitaṃ kiñcinmāhātmyaṃ vo munīśvarāḥ || 1.223 ||
ākāśe tārakā yadvatsindhoḥ saikatasṛṣṭivat |
etadvijñānamātreṇa labhenmuktiṃ caturvidhām || 1.224 ||
etadanyeṣu mantreṣu doṣāḥ santi pare ca ye |
tadarthaṃ mantrasaṃskārā lipyante tantrato daśa || 1.225 ||
saradātilake
jananaṃ jīvanaṃ ceti tāḍanaṃ rodhanaṃ tathā |
athābhiṣeko vimalīkaraṇāpyāyane punaḥ |
tarpaṇaṃ dīpanaṃ guptirdaśaitā mantrasaṃskriyāḥ || 1.226 ||
mantrāṇāṃ mātṛkāmadhyāduddhāro jananaṃ smṛtam |
praṇavāntaritān kṛtvā mantravarṇān japetsudhīḥ || 1.227 ||
etajjīvanamityāhurmantratantraviśāradāḥ |
manorvarṇān samālikhya tāḍayeccandanāmbhasā || 1.228 ||
pratyekaṃ vāyunā mantrī tāḍanaṃ tadudāhṛtam |
vilikhya mantraṃ taṃ mantrī prasūnaiḥ karavīrajaiḥ || 1.229 ||
tanmantrākṣarasaṅkhyātairhanyādyattena rodhanam |
svatantroktavidhānena mantrī mantrārṇasaṅkhyayā || 1.230 ||
aśvatthapallavairmantramabhiṣiñcedviśuddhaye |
saṃcintya manasā mantraṃ yotirmantreṇa nirdahet || 1.231 ||
mantre mūlatrayaṃ mantrī vimalīkaraṇaṃ tvidam |
tāravyomāgnimanuyugadaṇḍī jyotirmanurmataḥ |
kuśodakena japtena pratyarṇaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ manoḥ || 1.232 ||
tena mantreṇa vidhivadetadāpyāyanaṃ smṛtam |
mantreṇa vāriṇā yantre tarpaṇaṃ tarpaṇaṃ smṛtam || 1.233 ||
tāramāyāramāyogo manordīpanamucyate |
japyamānasya mantrasya gopanaṃ tvaprakāśanam || 1.234 ||
balitvātkṛṣṇamantrāṇāṃ saṃskārāpekṣaṇaṃ nahi |
sāmānyoddeśamātreṇa tathāpyetadudīritam || 1.235 ||
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse
gauravo nāma prathamo vilāsaḥ
dvitīyo vilāsaḥ daikṣikavilāsaḥ
daikṣikaḥ
taṃ śrīmatkṛṣṇacaitanyaṃ vande jagadgurum | yasyānukampayā śvāpi mahābdhiṃ santaretsukham || 2.1 || atha dīkṣāvidhiḥ
dīkṣāvidhirlikhyate'trānusṛtya kramadīpikām | vinā dīkṣāṃ hi pūjāyāṃ nādhikāro'sti karhicit || 2.2 ||
āgame dvijānāmanupetānāṃ svakarmādhyayanādiṣu | yathādhikāro nāstīha syāccopanayanādanu || 2.3 || tathātrādīkṣitānāṃ tu mantradevārcanādiṣu | nādhikāro'styataḥ kuryādātmānaṃ śivasaṃstutam || 2.4 ||
skānde kārttikaprasaṅge śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde te narāḥ paśavo loke kiṃ teṣāṃ jīvane phalam | yairna labdhā harerdīkṣā nārcito vā janārdanaḥ || 2.5 ||
tatraiva śrīrukmāṅgadamohinīsaṃvāde, viṣṇuyāmale ca adikṣitasya vāmoru kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ nirarthakam | paśuyonimavāpnoti dīkṣāvirahito janaḥ || 2.6 ||
viśeṣato viṣṇuyāmale snehādvā lobhato vāpi yo gṛhṇīyādadīkṣayā | tasmin gurau saśiṣye tu devatāśāpa āpatet || 2.7 ||
viṣṇurahasye ca avijñāya vidhānoktaṃ haripūjāvidhikriyām | kurvan bhaktyā samāpnoti śatabhāgaṃ vidhānataḥ || 2.8 || divyaṃ jñānaṃ yato dadyātkuryātpāpasya saṅkṣayam | tasmāddīkṣeti sā proktā deśikaistattvakovidaiḥ || 2.9 || ato guruṃ praṇamyaivaṃ sarvasvaṃ vinivedya ca | gṛhṇīyādvaiṣṇavaṃ mantraṃ dīkṣāpūrvaṃ vidhānataḥ || 2.10 ||
skānde tatraiva śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde tapasvinaḥ karmaniṣṭhāḥ śreṣṭhāste vai narā bhuvi | prāptā yaistu harerdīkṣā sarvaduḥkhavimocinī || 2.11 ||
tantrasāgare ca yathā kāñcanatāṃ yāti kāṃsyaṃ rasavidhānataḥ | tathā dīkṣāvidhānena dvijatvaṃ jāyate nṛṇām || 2.12 ||
atha dīkṣākālaḥ tatra māsaśuddhiḥ
āgame mantrasvīkaraṇaṃ caitre bahuduḥhaphalapradam | vaiśākhe ratnalābhaḥ syājjyaiṣṭhe tu maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 2.13 || āṣāḍhe bandhunāśāya śrāvaṇe tu bhayāvaham | prajāhānirbhādrapade sarvatra śubhamāśvine || 2.14 || kārttike dhanavṛddhiḥ syānmārgaśīrṣe śubhapradam | pauṣe tu jñānahāniḥ syānmāghe medhāvivardhanam | phālgune sarvavaśyatvamācāryaiḥ parikīrtitam || 2.15 ||
kvacicca samṛddhiḥ śrāvaṇe nūnaṃ jñānaṃ syātkārttike tathā | phālgune'pi samṛddhiḥ syānmalamāsaṃ parityajet || 2.16 ||
gautamīye mantrārambhastu caitre syātsamastapuruṣārthadaḥ | vaiśākhe ratnalābhaḥ syātjyaiṣṭhe tu maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 2.17 || āṣāḍhe bandhunāśaḥ syātpūrṇāyuḥ śrāvaṇe bhavet | prajānāśo bhavedbhādre āśvine ratnasañcayaḥ || 2.18 || kārttike mantrasiddhiḥ syātmārgaśīrṣe tathā bhavet | pauṣe tu śatrupīḍā syātmāghe medhāvivardhanam | phālgune sarvakāmāḥ syurmalamāsaṃ parityajet || 2.19 ||
skānde tatraiva śrīrukmāṅgadamohinīsaṃvāde kārttike tu kṛtā dīkṣā nṝṇāṃ janmanikṛntanī | tasmātsarvaprayatnena dīkṣāṃ kurvīta kārttike || 2.20 || śrīmadgopālamantrāṇāṃ dīkṣāyāṃ tu na duṣyati | caitramāse yaduktā taddīkṣā tatraiva deśikaiḥ || 2.21 ||
atha bāraśuddhiḥ
ravau gurau tathā some kartavyaṃ budhaśukrayoḥ || 2.22 ||
atha nakṣatraśuddhiḥ
nāradatantre rohiṇī śravaṇārdrā ca dhaniṣṭhā cottarātrayaḥ | puṣyaṃ śatabhiṣaścaiva dīkṣānakṣatramucyate || 2.23 ||
kvacicca aśvinīrohiṇīsvātiviśākhāhastabheṣu ca | jyeṣṭhottarātrayeṣveva kuryānmantrābhiṣecanam || 2.24 ||
atha tithiśuddhiḥ
sārasaṅgrahe dvitīyā pañcamī caiva ṣaṣṭhī caiva viśeṣataḥ | dvādaśyāmapi kartavyaṃ trayodaśyāmathāpi ca || 2.25 ||
kvacicca pūrṇimā pañcamī caiva dvitīyā saptamī tathā | trayodaśī ca daśamī praśastā sarvakāmadā || 2.26 || | iti |
evaṃ śuddhe dine śuklapakṣe śukragurūdaye | sallagne candratārānukūle dīkṣā praśasyate || 2.27 || athātrāpavādaḥ (viśeṣavidhiḥ)
rudrayāmale sattīrthe'rkavidhugrāse tantudāmanaparvaṇoḥ | mantradīkṣāṃ prakurvīta māsarkṣādi na śodhayet || 2.28 || sulagnacandratārādibalamatra sadaiva hi | labdho'tra mantordīrghāyuḥsampatsantativardhanaḥ || 2.29 || sūryagrahaṇakālena samāno nāsti kaścana | yatra yadyatkṛtaṃ sarvamanantaphaladaṃ bhavet | na māsatithivārādiśodhanaṃ sūryaparvaṇi || 2.30 ||
tattvasāgare ca durlabhe sadgurūṇāṃ ca sakṛtsaṅga upasthite | tadanujñā yadā labdhā sa dīkṣāvasaro mahān || 2.31 || grāme vā yadi vāraṇye kṣetre vā divase niśi | āgacchati gururdaivādyadā dīkṣā tadājñayā || 2.32 || yadaivecchā tadā dīkṣā gurorājñānurūpataḥ | na tīrthaṃ na vrataṃ homo na snānaṃ na japakriyā | dīkṣāyāḥ karaṇaṃ kintu svecchāprāpte tu sadgurau || 2.33 ||
atha maṇḍapanirmāṇavidhiḥ
kriyāvatyādibhedena bhaveddīkṣā caturvidhā | tatra kriyāvatī dīkṣā saṅkṣepeṇaiva likhyate || 2.34 || bhūmiṃ saṃskṛtya tasyāṅcārcayitvā vāstudevatāḥ | saptahastamitaṃ kuryānmaṇḍapaṃ ramyavedikam || 2.35 || aṣṭadhvajaṃ caturdhāraṃ kṣīrapādapatoraṇam | triguṇīkṛtasūtrāḍhyaṃ kuśamālābhiveṣṭitam || 2.36 ||
atha kuṇḍanirmāṇavidhiḥ
tasmiṃśca diśi kauveryāṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ trimekhalam | kuṇḍe kuryāccaturviṃśatyaṅgulipramitaṃ budhah || 2.37 || khātaṃ trimekhalocchrāyasahitaṃ tāvadācaret | tasmātkhātādbahiḥ kuryātkaṇṭhamekāṅgulaṃ dhruvam || 2.38 || tatrādymekhalocchrāyavistārau caturaṅgulau | tryaṅgulau tau dvitīyāyāstṛtīyāyā yugāṅgulau || 2.39 || yoniṃ ca paścime bhāge mekhalātritayopari | ṣaḍaṅgulāṃ ca vistāre dairghye ca dvādaśāṅgulām || 2.40 || ekāṅgulāṃ tathocchrāye madhye chidrasamanvitām | gadādharākṛtiṃ kuryādvidhivanmekhalānvitām || 2.41 || śatārdhahome kuṇḍaṃ syādūrdhvamuṣṭikaronmitam || 2.42 || śatahome'ratnimātraṃ sahasre pāṇinā mitam | lakṣe caturbhirhastaiśca koṭau tairaṣṭabhirmitam | caturasraṃ kuṇḍakhātaṃ kurvītādhaśca tādṛśam || 2.43 || homastvadhikasaṅkhākaḥ kuṇḍe vai nyūnasaṅkhyayā | kṛte kāryo na canyūnasaṅkhyākaḥ saṅkhayādhike || 2.44 || yathāvidhyeva kartavyaṃ kuṇḍaṃ yatnena dhīmatā | anyathā bahavo doṣā bhaveyurbahuduḥkhadāḥ || 2.45 ||
taduktaṃ tāntrikaiḥ evaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ kuṇḍamiṣṭaphalapradam | anekadoṣadaṃ kuṇḍaṃ yatra nyūnādhikāṃ bhavet || 2.46 || tasmātsamyakparīkṣyaiva kartavyaṃ śubhamicchatā | hastamātraṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ vā saṃkṣipte homakarmaṇi || 2.47 ||
hārītenāpi vistārādhikyahīnatve alpāyurjāyate dhruvam | khātādhikye bhavedyogī hīne tu dhanasaṃkṣayaḥ | kuṇḍe vakre ca santāpo maraṇaṃ chinnamekhale || 2.48 || śokastu mekhalonatve tadādhikye paśukṣayaḥ | bhāryānāśo yonihīne kaṇṭhahīne śubhakṣayaḥ || 2.49 ||
aṅguliparimāṇaṃ coktam tiryagyavodarāṇyaṣṭāvūrdhvā vā brīhayastrayaḥ | jñeyamaṅgulimānaṃ tu madhyamā madhyaparvaṇā || 2.50 || | iti |
viśeṣo'pekṣito'nyatra sraksruvaprakriyādikaḥ | jñeyo granthāntarātso'trādhikyabhītyā na likhyate || 2.51 ||
atha dīkṣāmaṇḍalavidhiḥ
athokṣite pañcagavyairgandhāmbhobhiśca maṇḍape | yathāvidhi likheddīkṣāmaṇḍalaṃ vedikopari || 2.52 || tanmadhye cāṣṭapatrābjaṃ bahirvṛttatrayaṃ tataḥ | tato rāśīṃstataḥ pīṭhaṃ catuṣpādasamanvitam || 2.53 || tasmādbahiścaturdikṣu likhedvīthīcatuṣṭayam | śobhāpaśobhākoṇāḍhyaṃ tato dvāracatuṣṭayam || 2.54 ||
atha dīkṣāṅgapūjā
prātaḥkṛtyaṃ guruḥ kṛtvā yathāsthānaṃ nyasettataḥ | śaṅkhaṃ pūjopacārāṃśca purolekhyaprakārataḥ || 2.55 ||
tatrādau kumbhasthāpanavidhiḥ
gurūn gaṇeśaṃ cābhyarcya pīṭhapūjāṃ vidhāya ca | padmamadhye nyasetśālīṃstaṇḍulāṃśca kuśāṃstathā || 2.56 || vahnerdaśakalā yādivarṇādyāśca kuśopari | nyasyābhyarcya japaṃstāraṃ nyasetkumbhaṃ yathoditam || 2.57 ||
tāścoktāḥ dhūmrārciruṣmā jvalanī jvālinī visphuliṅinī | suśrīḥ surūpā kapilā havyakavyavahe api || 2.58 || | iti |
kādyaṣṭhāntairyutā bhādyairḍāntaiścārṇairvilomagaiḥ | sūryasya ca kalāḥ kumbhe dvādaśa nyasya pūjayet || 2.59 ||
adhunā tasmin kuṇḍe sūryakalānāṃ nyāsādikaṃ likhatikādyairiti | kakārādyaiṣṭhakārāntairarṇairvarṇairyutā dvādaśāpi kalāḥ | cakāraḥ samuccaye | bhakārādyairḍakārāntairvarṇairapi yutāḥ | nanu, bhakārādīnāṃ dvādaśavarṇānāṃ ḍakārāntatā kathaṃ syāt? krameṇa kṣakārāntatāprāptestatrāhavilomagaiḥ vyutkramaprāptaiḥ | ayamarthaḥanulomapaṭhitakakārādyaikaikamakṣaraṃ pratilomapaṭhitabhakārādyekaikākṣareṇa sahitamādau sūryakalāsu saṃyojya nyāsādikaṃ kuryāditi | prayogaśca kaṃ bhaṃ tapanyai nama ityādi || 2.59 ||
tāścoktāḥ tapanī tāpanī dhūmrā bhrāmarī jvālinī ruciḥ | suṣumṇā bhogadā viśvā bodhinī dhāriṇī kṣamā || 2.60 || | iti |
kubmhāntarnikṣipenmūlamantreṇa kusumaṃ sitam | sākṣataṃ sasitaṃ svarṇaṃ saratnaṃ ca kuśāṃstathā || 2.61 ||
tataścoktaprakāreṇādhārarūpamagniṃ kumbharūpaṃ sūryaṃ ca vicintya kumbhasya tasya antarmadhye śuklakusumādikaṃ kṣipet | sasitaṃ saśarkaram | taduktam prottolayitvā tanmadhye śuklapuṣpaṃ sitāyutam | svarṇaṃ ratnaṃ ca kūrcaṃ ca mūlenaiva vinikṣipet || | iti | yacca mūlagranthārthādadhikaṃ kiṃcillikhate, tatpūrvagatasya yathoditamityasyānuvartanāditi jñeyam || 2.61 ||
kumbhaṃ ca vidhinā tīrthāmbunā śuddhena pūrayet | jale cendukulā nyasya sasvarāḥ ṣoḍaśārcayet || 2.62 ||
tāścoktāḥ amṛtā mānadā pūṣā tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭī ratirdhṛtiḥ | śaśinī candrikā kāntirjyotsnā śrīḥ prītiraṅgadā | pūrṇā pūrṇāmṛtā ca || 2.63 || | iti |
śuddhāmbupūrite śaṅkhe kṣiptvā gandhāṣṭakaṃ kalāḥ | āvāhya sarvāstāḥ prāṇapratiṣṭhāmācaretkramāt || 2.64 ||
gandhāṣṭakaṃ coktam uśīraṃ kuṅkumaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ bālakaṃ cāgururmurā | jaṭāmāṃsī candanaṃ cetīṣṭaṃ gandhāṣṭakaṃ hareḥ || 2.65 || | iti |
kaiściccandanakarpūrāgurukuṅkumarocanāḥ | kakkolakapimāṃsyaśca gandhāṣṭakamidaṃ matam || 2.66 || tathaivākārajā varṇaiḥ kādibhirdaśabhirdaśa | ukārajāṣṭakārādyaiḥ pakārādyairmakārajāḥ || 2.67 || catasro bindujāḥ ṣādyaiścaturbhirnādajāḥ kalāḥ | svaraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryuktā nyasecchaṅkhe ca ṣoḍaśa || 2.68 ||
tāścoktāḥ sṛṣṭirṛddhiḥ smṛtirmedhā kāntirlakṣmīrdhṛitḥ sthirā | sthitiḥ siddhirakārotthāḥ kalā daśa samīritaḥ || 2.69 || jarā ca pālinī śāntiraiśvarī ratikāmike | varadā hlādinī prītirdīrghā cokārajāḥ kalāḥ || 2.70 || tīkṣṇā raudrā bhayā nidrā tantīrkṣutkrodhanī kriyā | utkārī caiva mṛtyuśca makārākṣarajāḥ kalāḥ || 2.71 || nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathaiva ca | indhikā dīpikā caiva recikā mocikā parā || 2.72 || sūkṣmā sūkṣmāmṛtā jñānājñānā cāpyāyanī tathā | vyāpinī vyomarūpā ca anantā nādasambhavāḥ || 2.73 || | iti | nyāsaṃ kalānāṃ sarvāsāṃ kuryādekaikaśaḥ kramāt | nāmoccārya caturthāntaṃ tattadvarṇairnamo'ntakam || 2.74 || pūrvaṃ prāṇapratiṣṭhāyāstāsāmāvāhanātparam | ṛcaḥ pañca yathāsthānaṃ paṭhettāścārcayetkalāḥ || 2.75 || haṃśaḥ śuciṣadityādau pratadviṣṇustataḥ param | tryambakaṃ tatsaviturviṣṇuryonimiti kramāt || 2.76 || tacca śaṅkhodakaṃ kumbhe mūlamantreṇa nikṣipet | pidadhyāttanmukhaṃ śakravallīcūtādipallavaiḥ || 2.77 || śarāVenātha puṣpādiyuktenācchādya tatpunaḥ | saṃveṣṭya vastrayugmena tataḥ kumbhaṃ ca maṇḍayet || 2.78 ||
atha kumbhe śrībhagavatpūjāvidhiḥ
tasminnāvāhya kalase paraṃ tejo yathāvidhi | sakalīkṛtya cācāryaḥ pūjayedāsanādibhiḥ || 2.79 ||
sakalīkaraṇaṃ coktam devatāṅge ṣaḍaṅgānāṃ nyāsaḥ syātsakalīkṛtiḥ || 2.80 || keciccāhuḥ karanyāsau vinākhilaiḥ | nyāsaistattejasaḥ sāṅgīkaraṇaṃ sakalīkṛtaḥ || 2.81 || evaṃ ca kumbhe taṃ sāṅgopāṅgaṃ sāvaraṇaṃ prabhum | agrato lekhyavidhinārcayedbhojyārpaṇāvadhi || 2.82 || naivedyārpaṇataḥ paścānmaṇḍalasya ca sarvataḥ | sadīpān paiṣṭikānnyasyetsabījāṅkurabhājanāt || 2.83 ||
atha dīkṣāhomavidhiḥ
tato dīkṣāṅgahomārthaṃ kuṇḍalasya ca sarvataḥ | saṃmārjya darbhamārjanyā yathāvidhyupalepayet || 2.84 || vikīrya sarṣapāṃstatra gavyaiḥ samprokṣya pañcabhiḥ | madhye sampūjayedvāstupuruṣaṃ dikṣu tatpatīn || 2.85 || śoṣaṇādīni kuṇḍasya kṛtvā prokṣya kuśāmbubhiḥ | ullikhya cāsmin yonyādisahitaṃ maṇḍalaṃ likhet || 2.86 || śrībījaṃ madhyayonau ca vilikhyābhukṣya pūjayet | nidhāya tatra puṣpādiviṣṭaraṃ sādhu kalpayet || 2.87 || tatra lakṣmīmṛtyusnānāṃ viṣṇuṃ cāvāhya pūjayet | tāmrādipātreṇānīyāgrato'gniṃ sthāpayecchubhram || 2.88 || gandhādināgnimabhyarcya viṣṇoḥ sakrīḍataḥ śriyā | retorūpaṃ vicintyāmuṃ kuṇḍaṃ tāreṇa cārcayet || 2.89 || vaiśvānareti mantreṇācchācyāgniṃ taṃ sadindhanaiḥ | citpiṅgaleti prajvālyopatiṣṭhedagnimityamum || 2.90 || jihvā nyasyetsapta tsminnapyaṅgeṣvaṅgadevatāḥ | ṣaṭsu ṣaṭnyasya mūrtīśca nyasyāṣṭābhyarcayecca tāḥ || 2.91 ||
saptajihvāścoktāḥ hiraṇyā gaganā raktā tathā kṛṣṇā ca suprabhā | bahurūpātirūpā ca sapta jihvā vasorimāḥ || 2.92 ||
athāṅgadevatāḥ
sahasrārciḥ svastipūrṇa uttiṣṭhapuruṣastathā | dhūmavyāpī saptajihvo dhanurdhara iti smṛtaḥ || 2.93 ||
aṣṭamūrtayaśca
jātavedāḥ saptajihvo havyavāhana eva ca | aśvodarajasaṃjñaśca tathā vaiśvānaro'paraḥ | kaumāratejāśca tathā viśvadevamukhāhvayau || 2.94 || | iti |
tato vahniṃ paristīrya saṃskṛtājyaṃ yathāvidhi | hutvā ca vyāhṛtīḥ paścāttrīn vārān juhuyātpunaḥ || 2.95 || tato'sya garbhadhānādīn vivhāntān yathākramam | saṃskārānācareduktamantreṇāṣṭāhutaistathā || 2.96 || itthaṃ hi saṃskṛte vahnau pīṭhamabhyarcya tatra ca | devamāvāhya gandhādidīpāntavidhinārcayet || 2.97 || taṃ cāgniṃ devarasanāṃ saṃkalpyāṣṭottaraṃ budhaḥ | sahasraṃ juhuyātsarpiḥśarkarāpāyasairyutaiḥ || 2.98 || hutvājyenātha mahatīvyāhṛtīrvidhinā kṛtī | graharkṣakaraṇādibhyo baliṃ dadyādyathoditam || 2.99 ||
atha homadravyādiparimāṇam
karṣamātraṃ ghṛtaṃ home śuktimātraṃ payaḥ smṛtam | uktāni pañcagavyāni tatsamāni manīṣibhiḥ || 2.100 || tatsamaṃ madhudugdhānnamakṣamātramudāhṛtam | dadhi prasṛtimātraṃ syātlājāḥ syuḥ muṣṭisaṃmitāḥ || 2.101 || | ityādi |
atha natvāmbupānārthaṃ pradāyācamanāni ca | ātmārpaṇāntamabhyarcya lekhyena vidhinācaret || 2.102 ||
atha guruśiṣyaniyamādiḥ
vratasthaṃ vāgyataṃ śiṣyaṃ praveśyātha yathāvidhi | taddehe mātṛkāṃ sāṅgāṃ nyasyāthopadiśecca tām || 2.103 || devaṃ sāvaraṇaṃ kumbhagataṃ cānusmaran guruḥ | japtvāṣṭottarasāhasraṃ śayīta prāśya kiṃcana || 2.104 || darbhoparyajine tvaiṇe niviṣṭo mātṛkāṃ smaran | guruṃ ca śiṣyo nidrāṇaṃ tāṃ śayīta japan vratī || 2.105 ||
iti pūrvadinakṛtyam |
atha taddinakṛtyāni
prātaḥkṛtyaṃ guruḥ kṛtvā kumbhaṃ cābhyarcya pūrvavat | hutvā dattvā baliṃ karmānyatkuryātsvārpaṇāvadhi || 2.106 || saṃhāramudrayā kṛṣṇe saṃyojyāvṛttidevatāḥ | taṃ cāmṛtamayaṃ dhyātvā svasmiṃścāgniṃ vilāpayet || 2.107 || dhvajatoraṇadikkumbhamaṇḍapādyadhidevatāḥ | sarvā vibhāvya cidrūpāḥ kumbhe saṃyojya pūjayet || 2.108 || ato guruṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca viṣvaksenaṃ ca pūjayet | udvāsya kalasaṃ spṛṣṭvā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japet || 2.109 || kṛtopavāsaḥ śiṣyo'thatprātaḥkṛtyaṃ vidhāya saḥ | śuklavastraḥ suveśaḥ san viprān dravyeṇa toṣayet || 2.110 || guruṃ ca bhagavaddṛṣṭyā parikramya praṇamya ca | dattvoktāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ tasmai svaśarīraṃ samarpayet || 2.111 ||
atha dīkṣāṅgapūjā
tathā ca daśamaskandhe iyadeva hi sacchiṣyaiḥ kartavyaṃ guruniṣkṛtam | yadvai viśuddhabhāvena sarvārthātmārpaṇaṃ gurau || 2.112 ||
athābhiṣecanavidhiḥ
yāgālayāduttarasyāmāśāyāṃ snānamaṇḍape | pīṭhe niveśya taṃ śiṣyaṃ kārayecchoṣaṇādikam || 2.113 || pīṭhanyāsāntamakhilaṃ mātṛkānyāsapūrvakam | nyāsaṃ śiṣyatanau kṛtvā pīṭhamantreṇa pūjayet || 2.114 || sadūrvākṣatapuṣpāṃ ca mūrdhni śiṣyasya rocanām | nidhāya kalasaṃ tasyāntike vādyādinā nayet || 2.115 || śrīkṛṣṇamatha samprārthya guruḥ kumbhasya vāsasā | nīrājya śiṣyaṃ tanmūrdhni nyasettatpallavādikam || 2.116 ||
taduktam vidhivatkumbhamuddhṛtya tanmukhasthān suradrumān | śiśoḥ śirasi vinyasya mātṛkāṃ manasā japet || 2.117 || tataḥ kumbhāmbhasā śiṣyaṃ prokṣya trirmūlamantrataḥ | viprāśīrmaṅgalodghoṣairabhiṣiñcenmanūn paṭhan || 2.118 ||
athābhiṣekamantrāḥ
vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām surāstvāmabhiṣiñcyaṃ tu brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ | vāsudevo jagannāthastathā saṅkarṣaṇo vibhuḥ || 2.119 || pradyumnaścāniruddhaśca bhavantu vibhavāya te | ākhaṇḍalo'gnirbhagavān yamo vai nirṛtistathā || 2.120 || varuṇaḥ pavanaścaiva dhanādhyakṣastathā śivaḥ | brahmaṇā sahitā hyete dikpālāḥ pāntu vaḥ sadā || 2.121 || kīrtirlakṣmīrdhṛtirmedhā puṣṭiḥ śraddhā kriyā gatiḥ | buddhirlajjā vapuḥ śāntirmāyā nidrā ca bhāvanā || 2.122 || etāstvāmabhiṣiñcantu rāhuḥ ketuśca pūjitāḥ | devadānavagandharvā yakṣarākṣasapannagāḥ || 2.123 || ṛṣayo munayo gāvo devamātara eva ca | devapatnyo dhruvā nāgā daityā apsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ || 2.124 || astrāṇi sarvaśastrāṇi rājāno vāhanāni ca | auṣadhāni ca ratnāni kālasyāvayavāśca ye || 2.125 || saritaḥ sāgarāḥ śailāstīrthāni jaladā nadāḥ | ete tvāmabhiṣiñcantu dharmakāmārthasiddhaye || 2.126 ||
atha mantrakathanavidhiḥ
paridhāyāṃśuke śiṣya ācānto yāgamaṇḍape | gatvā bhaktyā guruṃ natvā gurorāsīta dakṣiṇe || 2.127 || guruḥ samarpya gandhādīn puruṣāhārasaṃmitam | nivedya pāyasaṃ kṛṣṇe kuryātpuṣpāñjaliṃ tataḥ || 2.128 || sāmpradāyikamudrādibhūṣitaṃ taṃ kṛtāñjalim | pañcāṅgapramukhairnyāsaiḥ kuryātśrīkṛṣṇasācchiśum || 2.129 || nyasya pāṇitalaṃ mūrdhni tasya karṇe ca dakṣiṇe | ṛṣyādiyuktaṃ vidhavanmantraṃ vāratrayaṃ vadet || 2.130 || dīrghamantraṃ ca śiṣyasya yāvadāgrahaṇaṃ paṭhet | gurudaivatamantraikyaṃ śiṣyastaṃ bhāvayan paṭhet || 2.131 || sākṣataṃ gururādāya vāri śiṣyasya dakṣiṇe | kare'rpayedvadanmantro'yaṃ samo'stvāvayoriti || 2.132 || svasmājjyotirmayīṃ vidyāṃ gacchantīṃ bhāvayedguruḥ | āgatāṃ bhāvayecchiṣyo dhanyo'smīti viśeṣataḥ || 2.133 || mahāprasādaṃ śiṣyāya dattvā tatpāyasaṃ guruḥ | nidadhyādakṣatānmūrdhni tasya yacchan śubhāśiṣam || 2.134 || guruṇā kṛpayā dattaṃ śiṣyaścāvāpya taṃ manum | aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā samayān śṛṇuyāttataḥ || 2.135 ||
atha samayāḥ
śrīnāradapañcarātre svamantro nopadeṣṭavyo vaktavyaśca na saṃsadi | gopanīyaṃ tathā śāstraṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ śarīravat || 2.136 || vaiṣṇavānāṃ parā bhaktirācāryāṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ | pūjanaṃ ca yathāśakti tānāpannāṃśca pālayet || 2.137 || prāptamāyatanādviṣṇoḥ śirasāṃ praṇato vahet | nikṣipedambhasi tato na patedavanau yathā || 2.138 || somasūryāntarasthaṃ ca gavāśvatthāgnimadhyagam | bhāvayeddaivataṃ viṣṇuṃ guruvipraśarīragam || 2.139 || yatra yatra parivādo mātsaryācchrūyate guroḥ | tatra tatra na vastavyaṃ niryāyātsaṃsmaran harim || 2.140 || yaiḥ kṛtā ca gurornindā vibhoḥ śāstrasya nārada | nāpi taiḥ saha vastavyaṃ vaktavyaṃ vā kathañcana || 2.141 || pradakṣiṇe prayāṇe ca pradāne ca viśeṣataḥ | prabhāte ca pravāse ca svamantraṃ bahuśaḥ smaret || 2.142 || svapne vākṣisamakṣaṃ vā āścaryamatiharṣadam | akasmādyadi jāyeta na khyātavyaṃ gurorvinā || 2.143 ||
pañcarātrāntare samayāṃśca pravakṣyāmi saṃkṣepātpañcarātrakāt | na bhakṣayenmatsyamāṃsaṃ kūrmaśūkarakāṃstathā || 2.144 || kāṃsyapātre na bhuñjīta na plakṣabaṭapatrayoḥ | devāgāre na niṣṭhīvetkṣutaṃ cātra vivarjayet | na sopānatkacaraṇaḥ praviśedantaraṃ kvacit || 2.145 || ekādaśyāṃ na cāśnīyātpakṣayorubhayorapi | jāgaraṃ niśi kurvīta viśeṣāccārcayedvibhum || 2.146 ||
sammohanatantre ca gopayeddevatāmiṣṭāṃ gopayedgurumātmanaḥ | gopayecca nijaṃ mantraṃ gopayennijamālikām || 2.147 || | iti |
caturyukśatasaṅkhyeṣu prāgguroḥ samayeṣu ca | śiṣyeṇāṅgīkṛteṣveva dīkṣā kaiścana manyate || 2.148 ||
tathā ca viṣṇuyāmale guruḥ parīkṣayecchiṣyaṃ saṃvatsaramatandritaḥ | niyamān vihitān varjyān śrāvayecca catuḥśatam || 2.149 || brāhme muhūrta utthānaṃ mahāviṣṇoḥ prabodhanam | nīrājanaṃ ca vādyena prātaḥsnānaṃ vidhānataḥ || 2.150 || viśuddhāhatayugvastradhāraṇaṃ devatārcanam | gopīcandanamṛtsnāyāḥ sarvadā corddhvapuṇḍrakam || 2.151 || pañcāyudhānāṃ vidhṛtiścaraṇāmṛtasevanam | tulasīmaṇimālādibhūṣādhāraṇamanvaham || 2.152 || śālagrāmaśilāpūjā pratimāsu ca bhaktitaḥ | nirmālyatulasībhakṣastulasyavacayo vidheḥ || 2.153 || vidhinā tāntrikī sandhyā śikhābandho hi karmaṇi | viṣṇupādodakenaiva pitṝṇāṃ tarpaṇakriyā | mahārājopacāraiśca śaktyāṃ sampūjanaṃ hareḥ || 2.154 || viṣṇubhaktyavirodhena nityanaimittikī kriyā | bhūtaśuddhyādikaraṇaṃ nyāsāḥ sarve yathāvidhi || 2.155 || navīnaphalapuṣpāderbhaktitaḥ saṃnivedanam | tulasīpūjanaṃ nityaṃ śrībhāgavatapūjanam || 2.156 || trikālaṃ viṣṇupūjā ca purāṇaśrutiranvaham | viṣṇorniveditānāṃ vai vastrādīnāṃ ca dhāraṇam || 2.157 || sarveṣāṃ puṇyakāryāṇāṃ svāmidṛṣṭyā pravartanam | gurvājñāgrahaṇaṃ tatra viśvāso guruṇodite || 2.158 || yathāsvamudrāracanaṃ gītanṛtyādi bhaktitaḥ | śaṅkhādidhvanimāṅgalyalīlādyabhinayo hareḥ | nityahomavidhānaṃ ca balidānaṃ yathāvidhi || 2.159 || sādhūnāṃ svāgataṃ pūjā śeṣanaivedyabhojanam | tāmbūlaśeṣagrahaṇaṃ vaiṣṇavaiḥ saha saṅgamaḥ || 2.160 || viśiṣṭadharmajijñāsā daśamyādidinatraye | vrate niyamataḥ svāsthyaṃ santoṣo yena kena vai || 2.161 || parvayātrādikaraṇaṃ vāsarāṣṭakasadvidhiḥ | viṣṇoḥ sarvartucaryā ca mahārājopacārataḥ || 2.162 || sarveṣāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca vratānāṃ paripālanam | gurāvīśvarabhāvaśca tulasīsaṅgrahaḥ sadā || 2.163 || śayanādyupacāraśca rāmādīnāṃ ca cintanam || 2.164 || sandhyayoḥ śayanaṃ naiva na śaucaṃ mṛttikāṃ vinā | tiṣṭhatācamanaṃ naiva tathā gurvāsanāsanam || 2.165 || gurvagre pādavistāracchāyāyā laṅghanaṃ guroḥ | śaktau snānakriyāhānirdevatārcanalopanam || 2.166 || devatānāṃ gurūṇāṃ ca pratyutthānādyabhāvanam | guroḥ purastātpāṇḍityaṃ prauḍhapādakriyā tathā || 2.167 || amantratilakācāmo nīlīvastravidhāraṇam | abhaktaiḥ saha maitryādi asacchāstraparigrahaḥ | tucchasaṅgasukhāsaktirmadyamāṃsaniṣevaṇam || 2.168 || mādakauṣadhasevā ca masurādyannabhojanam | śākaṃ tumbī kalañjādi tathābhaktānnasaṅgrahaḥ | avaiṣṇavavratārambhastathā japyamavaiṣṇavam || 2.169 || abhicārādikaraṇaṃ śaktyā gauṇopacārakam | śokādipāravaśyaṃ ca digviddhaikādaśīvratam || 2.170 || śuklākṛṣṇāvibhedaścāsadvyāpāro vrate tathā | śaktau phalādibhuktiśca śrāddhaṃ caikādaśīdine || 2.171 || dvādaśyāṃ ca divāsvāpastulasyāvacayastathā | tatra viṣṇordivāsnānaṃ śrāddhaṃ haryaniveditaiḥ || 2.172 || vṛddhāvatulasīśrāddhaṃ tathā śrāddhamavaiṣṇavam | caraṇāmṛtapāne'pi śuddhyarthācamanakriyā || 2.173 || kāṣṭhāsanopaviṣṭena vāsudevasya pūjanam | pūjākāle'sadālāpaḥ karavīrādipūjanam || 2.174 || āyasaṃ dhūpapātrādi tiryakpuṇḍraṃ pramādataḥ | pūjā cāsaṃskṛtairdravyaistathā cañcalacittataḥ || 2.175 || ekahastapraṇāmādi akāle svāmidarśanam | paryuṣitādiduṣṭānāmannādīnāṃ nivedanam || 2.176 || saṅkhyāṃ vinā mantrajapastathā mantraprakāśanam | sadā śaktyāṃ mukhyalopo gauṇakālaparigrahaḥ || 2.177 || prasādāgrahaṇaṃ viṣṇorvarjayedvaiṣṇavaḥ sadā | catuḥśataṃ vidhīnetānniṣedhān śrāvayedguruḥ || 2.178 || aṅgīkāre kṛte bāḍhaṃ tannīrājanapūrvakam | devapūjāṃ kārayitvā dakṣakarṇe mantraṃ japet || 2.179 || | iti |
tataścotthāya pūrṇātmā daṇḍavatpraṇamedgurum | tatpādapaṅkajaṃ śiṣyaḥ pratiṣṭhāpya svamūrdhani || 2.180 || atha nyāsān guruḥ svasmin kṛtvāntaryajanaṃ tathā | sāṣṭaṃ sahasraṃ tanmantraṃ svaśaktyakṣataye japet || 2.181 || śiṣyaḥ kumbhādi tatsarvaṃ dravamanyacca śaktitaḥ | dattvābhyarcya guruṃ natvā viprān saṃpūjya bhojayet || 2.182 || śrīgurorbrāhmaṇānāṃ ca śubhāśīrbhiḥ samedhitaḥ | tānanujñāpya gurvādīn bhuñjīta saha bandhubhiḥ || 2.183 || iti dīkṣāvidhānena yo mantraṃ labhate guroḥ | sa bhāgyavān cirañjīvī kṛtakṛtyaśca jāyate || 2.184 ||
tathā ca saṃmohanatantre śrīśivomāsaṃvāde evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ kare tasya vibhūtayaḥ | ataḥ paraṃ mahābhāge nānyatkarmāsti bhūtale | yasyācaraṇamātreṇa sākṣātkṛṣṇaḥ prasīdati || 2.185 || prāyaḥ prapañcasārādāvukto'yaṃ tāntriko vidhiḥ | dīkṣāyā likhyate divyo vidhiḥ paurāṇiko'dhunā || 2.186 ||
atha varāhapurāṇoktadīkṣāvidhiḥ
idānīṃ śṛṇu me devi pañcapātakanāśanam | yajanaṃ devadevasya viṣṇoḥ putravasupradam || 2.187 || iha janmani dāridryavyādhikuṣṭhādipīḍitaḥ | alakṣmīvānaputrastu yo bhavetpuruṣo bhuvi | tasya sadyo bhavellakṣmīrāyurvittaṃ sutāḥ sukham || 2.188 || dṛṣṭvā tu maṇḍale devi devaṃ devyā samanvitam | nārāyaṇaṃ paraṃ devaṃ yaḥ paśyati vidhānataḥ || 2.189 || pūjitaṃ navanābhe tu ṣoḍaśābjadale tathā | ācāryadarśitaṃ devaṃ mantramūrtimayonijam || 2.190 || kārttike māsi śuddhāyāṃ dvādaśyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ | sarvāsu ca yajeddevaṃ dvādaśīṣu vidhānataḥ || 2.191 || saṅkrāntau ca mahābhāge candrasūryagrahe'pi vā | yaḥ paśyati hariṃ devaṃ pūjitaṃ guruṇā śubhe | tasya sadyo bhavettuṣṭiḥ pāpadhvaṃso'pyaśeṣataḥ || 2.192 || sa sāmānyo hi devānāṃ bhavatīti na saṃśayaḥ || 2.193 || brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca parīkṣaṇam | saṃvatsaraṃ guruḥ kuryājjātiśaucakriyādibhiḥ || 2.194 || upasannāṃstato jñātvā hṛdayenāvadhārayet | te'pi bhaktimato jñātvā ātmanaḥ parameśvaram | saṃvatsaraṃ gurorbhaktiṃ kuryurviṣṇāvivācalām || 2.195 || saṃvatsaraṃ tataḥ pūrṇe guruṃ caiva prasādayet || 2.196 || bhagavaṃstvatprasādena saṃsārārṇavatāraṇam | icchāmastvaihikīṃ lakṣmīṃ viśeṣeṇa tapodhana || 2.197 || evamabhyarthya medhāvī guruṃ viṣṇumivāgrataḥ | abhyarcya tadanujñāto daśamyāṃ kārttikasya tu || 2.198 || kṣīravṛkṣasamudbhūtaṃ mantritaṃ parameṣṭhinā | bhakṣayitvā śayītorvyāṃ devadevasya sannidhau || 2.199 || svapnān dṛṣṭvā guroragre śrāvayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ | tataḥ śubhāśubhaṃ tadvadālapetparamo guruḥ | ekādaśyāmupoṣyātha snātvā devālayaṃ vrajet || 2.200 || guruśca maṇḍalaṃ bhūmau kalptāyāṃ tu vartayet | lakṣaṇairvividhairbhūmiṃ lakṣayitvā vidhānataḥ || 2.201 || ṣoḍaśāraṃ likheccakraṃ navanābhamathāpi vā | aṣṭapatramatho vāpi likhitvā darśayedbudhaḥ || 2.202 || netrabandhaṃ prakurvīta sitavastreṇa yatnataḥ | varṇānukramataḥ śiṣyān purṣpahastān praveśayet || 2.203 || navanābhaṃ yadā kuryānmaṇḍalaṃ varṇakairbudhaḥ | tadānīṃ pūrvato devamindramaindryāṃ tu pūjayet || 2.204 || lokapālamathāgneyyāmagniṃ sampūjayeddvijaḥ | yamaṃ tadanu yāmyāyāṃ nairṛtyāṃ nirṛtiṃ nyaset | vāruṇyāṃ varuṇaṃ caiva vāyavyāṃ pavanaṃ yajet || 2.205 || dhanadaṃ cottare nyasya rudramaiśānagocare | sampūjyaivaṃ vidhānena dikpatreṣu viśeṣataḥ | adhaḥpatre tathā viṣṇumarcayetparameśvaram || 2.206 || pūrvapatre balaṃ pūjya pradyumnaṃ dakṣiṇe tathā | aniruddhaṃ tathā pūjya paścime cottare tathā | pūjayedvāsudevaṃ tu sarvapātakaśāntidam || 2.207 || aiśānyāṃ vinyasecchaṅkhamāgneyyāṃ cakrameva ca | saumyāyāṃ tu gadā pūjyā vāyavyāṃ padmameva ca || 2.208 || nairṛtyāṃ muṣalaṃ pūjyaṃ dakṣiṇe garuḍaṃ tathā | vāmato vinyasellakṣmīṃ devadevasya buddhimān || 2.209 || dhanuścaiva ca khaḍgaṃ ca devasya purato nyaset | śrīvatsaṃ kaustubhaṃ caiva devasya purato'rcayet || 2.210 || evaṃ pūjya yathānyāyaṃ devadevaṃ janārdanam | diṅmaṇḍale ca vinyasya cāṣṭau kumbhān vidhānataḥ | vaiṣṇavaṃ kalasaṃ caiva navamaṃ tatra kalpayet || 2.211 || snāpayenmuktikāmāṃstu vaiṣṇavena ghaṭena tu | śrīkāmān snāpayettadvadaindreṇātha ghaṭena tu || 2.212 || jayapratāpakāmāṃstu āgneyenābhiṣecayet | mṛtyuñjayavidhānena yāmyena snāpanaṃ tathā || 2.213 || duṣṭapradhvaṃsnāyālaṃ nairṛtena vidhīyate | śāntaye vāruṇyenātha pāpanāśāya vāyavam || 2.214 || dravyasampattikāmasya kauvareṇa vidhīyate | raudreṇa jñānahetustu lokapālaghaṭāstvime || 2.215 || ekaikena naraḥ snātaḥ sarvapāpavarjitaḥ | bhavedavyāhatajñānaḥ śrīmāṃśca puruṣaḥ sadā || 2.216 || kiṃ punarnavabhiḥ snāto naraḥ pātakavarjitaḥ | jāyate viṣṇusadṛśaḥ sadyo rājāthavā punaḥ || 2.217 || athavā dikṣu sarvāsu yathāsaṅkhyena lokapān | pūjayetsvasvanāmnā tu ṣaḍbhinnena vidhānataḥ || 2.218 || evaṃ sampūjya devāṃstu lokapālān prasannadhīḥ | paścātparīkṣitān śiṣyān baddhanetrān praveśayet || 2.219 || āgneyadhāraṇādagdhān vāyunā vidhūtāṃstataḥ | somenāpyāyitān paścācchrāvayenniyamān budhaḥ || 2.220 || na nindedabrāhmaṇān devān viṣṇuṃ brāhmaṇameva ca | rudramādiyamagniṃ ca lokapālān grahāṃstathā | vandeta vaiṣṇavaṃ cāpi puruṣaṃ pūrvadīkṣitam || 2.221 || evaṃ tu samayān śrāvya paścāddhomaṃ tu kārayet | tattvāni śiṣyadeheṣu vinyasya ca viśodhayet || 2.222 || oṃ namo bhagavate viṣṇave sarvarūpiṇe huṃ svāhā || 2.223 || ṣoḍaśākṣaramantreṇa homayejjvalitānalaḥ | garbhādhānādikāścaiva kriyāḥ sarvāśca kārayet || 2.224 || tribhistribhirāhutibhirdevadevasya sannidhau | tato'panīya dṛgbandhaṃ puraḥ śiṣyaṃ niveśya ca | prāyaḥ pūrvoktavidhinā mantraṃ tasmai gururdiśet || 2.225 || homānte dīkṣitaḥ paścāddāpayedgurudakṣiṇām | hastyaśvaratnakaṭakaṃ hemagrāmādikaṃ nṛpaḥ || 2.226 || dāpayedgurave prājño madhyamo madhyamāṃ tathā | dāpayeditaro yugmaṃ sahiraṇyaṃ yathāvidhi || 2.227 || evaṃ kṛte tu yatpuṇyaṃ māhātmyaṃ jāyate dhare | tadaśakyaṃ tu gaditumapi varṣaśatairapi || 2.228 || dīkṣitātmā gurorbhūtvā vārāhaṃ śṛṇuyādyadi | tena vedāḥ purāṇāni sarve mantrāḥ susaṅgrahāḥ || 2.229 || japtāḥ syuḥ puṣkare tīrthe prayāge sindhusāgare | devahūte kurukṣetre vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 2.230 || graheṇa viṣuve caiva yatphalaṃ japatāṃ bhavet | tatphalaṃ dviguṇaṃ tasya dīkṣito yaḥ śṛṇoti ca || 2.231 || devā api tapaḥ kṛtvā dhyāyanti ca vadanti ca | kadā me bhārate varṣe janma syādbhūtadhāriṇi || 2.232 || dīkṣitāśca bhaviṣyāmo vārāhaṃ śṛṇumaḥ kadā | vārāhaṃ ṣoḍaśātmānaṃ yuktā dehe kadācana | paśyāmaḥ paramaṃ sthānaṃ yadgatvā na punarbhavet || 2.233 || evaṃ jalpanti vibudhā manasā cintayanti ca | vārāhayāgaṃ kārttikyāṃ kadā drakṣyāmahe dhare || 2.234 || eṣa te vidhiruddiṣṭo mayā te bhūtadhāriṇi | devagandharvayakṣāṇāṃ sarvathā durlabho hyasau || 2.235 || evaṃ yo vetti tattvena yaśca paśyati maṇḍalam | yaścemaṃ śṛṇuyāddevi sarve muktā iti śrutiḥ || 2.236 ||
atha saṅkṣiptadīkṣā
saṅkṣiptaścātha dīkṣāyā vidhireṣa vilikhyate | mukhyakalpe hyaśaktasya janasya syāddhitāya ca || 2.237 || sumuhūrte'tha samprāpte sarvatobhadramaṇḍale | nūtanaṃ gandhapuṣpādimaṇḍitaṃ kalasaṃ nyaset || 2.238 || vastrāvṛtaṃ payaḥ pūrṇaṃ pañcapallavasaṃyutam | sarvauṣadhipañcaratnamṛtsnāsaptakagarbhitam || 2.239 ||
mṛttikāśca saptoktāḥ aśvasthānādgajasthānādvalmīkācca catuṣpathāt | rājadvārācca goṣṭhācca nadyāḥ kūlānmṛdaḥ smṛtāḥ || 2.240 || | iti |
kṛṣṇamabhyarcya taṃ kumbhaṃ kuśakūrcena deśikaḥ | deyamantreṇa sāṣṭaṃ tu sahasramabhimantrayet || 2.241 || tadadbhiḥ pūrvavacchiṣyamabhiṣicya diśenmanum | śiṣyo'rcayedguruṃ bhaktyā yathāśakti dvijānapi || 2.242 ||
athopadeśastattvasāgare atrāpyaśaktaḥ kaściccedabjamabhyarcya sākṣatam | tadambhasābhiṣicyāṣṭa vārānmūlena ke karam || 2.243 || nidhāyāmuṃ japetkarṇe upadeśeṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ | candrasūryagrahe tīrthe siddhakṣetre śivālaye | mantramātraprakathanamupadeśaḥ sa ucyate || 2.244 ||
tatra tatraiva viśeṣaḥ śrīnāradapañcarātre | vittalobhādvimuktasya svalpavittasya dehinaḥ | saṃsārabhayabhītasya viṣṇubhaktasya tattvataḥ || 2.245 || agnāvājyānvite bījaiḥ salilaiḥ kevalaiśca vā | dravyahīnasya kurvīta vacasānugrahaṃ guruḥ || 2.246 || yaḥ samaḥ sarvabhūteṣu virāgo vītamatsaraḥ | jitendriyaḥ śucirdakṣaḥ sarvāṅgāvayavānvitaḥ || 2.247 || karmaṇā manasā vācā bhīte cābhayadaḥ sadā | samabuddhipadaṃ prāptastatrāpi bhagavanmayaḥ || 2.248 || pañcakālaparaścaiva pañcarātrārthavittathā | viṣṇutattvaṃ parijñāya ekaṃ cānekabhedagam | vīkṣayenmedinīṃ sarvāṃ kiṃ punaścopasannatān || 2.249 ||
atha mantradānamāhātmyam
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde iha kīrtiṃ vadānyatvaṃ prajāvṛddhiṃ dhanaṃ sukham | vidyādānena labhate sāttviko nātra saṃśayaḥ || 2.250 || yathā surāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ paramaḥ parameśvaraḥ | tathaiva sarvadānānāṃ vidyādānaṃ paraṃ smṛtam || 2.251 || yāvacca pātakaṃ tena kṛtaṃ janmaśatairapi | tatsarvaṃ nāśamāpnoti vidyādānena dehinām || 2.252 || vidyādānātparaṃ dānaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati | yena dattena cāpnoti śivaṃ paramakāraṇam || 2.253 ||
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse daikṣiko nāma dvitīyo vilāsaḥ |
tṛtīyavilāsaḥ śaucīyavilāsaḥ
vande'nantādbhutaiśvaryaṃ śrīcaitanyaṃ mahāprabhum | nīco'pi yatprasādātsyātsadācārapravartakaḥ || 3.1 || puṃso gṛhītadīkṣasya śrīkṛṣṇaṃ pūjayiṣyataḥ | ācāro likhyate kṛtyaṃ śrutismṛtyanusārataḥ || 3.2 ||
atha dīkṣitasya pūjāyā nityatā
labdhvā mantraṃ tu yo nityaṃ nārcayenmantradevatām | sarvakarmaphalaṃ tasyāniṣṭaṃ yacchati devatā || 3.3 ||
atha sadācāraḥ
na kiñcitkasyacitsidhyetsadācāraṃ vinā yataḥ | tasmādavaśyaṃ sarvatra sadācāro hyapekṣyate || 3.4 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe vaṛṇāśramācaravatā puruṣeṇa paraḥ pumān | viṣṇurārādhyate panthā nānyattattoṣakāraṇam || 3.5 ||
atha sadācārasya nityatā
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe śrīmadālasālarkasaṃvāde gṛhasthena sadā kāryamācāraparipālanam | na hyācāravihīnasya sukhamatra paratra ca || 3.6 || yajñadānatapāṃsīha puruṣasya na bhūtaye | bhavanti yaḥ sadācāraṃ samullaṅghya pravartate || 3.7 ||
bhaviṣyottare ca śrīkṛṣṇayudhiṣṭhirasaṃvāde ācārahīnaṃ na punanti vedāḥ yadyapyadhītāḥ saha ṣaḍbhiraṅgaiḥ | chandāṃsyenaṃ mṛtyukāle tyajanti nīḍaṃ śakuntā iva jātapakṣāḥ || 3.8 || kapālasthaṃ yathā toyaṃ śvadṛtau vā yathā payaḥ | duṣṭaṃ syātsthānadoṣeṇa vṛttahīne tathāśubham | ācārarahito rājanneha nāmutra nindati || 3.9 || | iti |
lekhyena smaraṇādīnāṃ nityatvenaiva setsyati | smaraṇādyātmakasyāpi sadācārasya nityatā || 3.10 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe tatraiva gṛhidharmaprasaṅge sadācāravatā puṃsā jitau lokāvubhāvapi || 3.11 || sādhavaḥ kṣīṇadoṣāstu sacchabdaḥ sādhuvācakaḥ | teṣāmācaraṇaṃ yattu sadācāraḥ sa ucyate || 3.12 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe skandāgastyasaṃvāde anadhyayanaśīlaṃ ca sadācāravilaṅghinam | sālasyaṃ ca durannādaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bādhate'ntakaḥ || 3.13 || tato'bhyasetprayatnena sadācāraṃ sadā dvijaḥ | tīrthānyapyabhilaṣyanti sadācārasamāgamam || 3.14 ||
bhaviṣyottare ca tatraiva ācāraprabhavo dharmaḥ santaścācāralakṣaṇāḥ | sādhūnāṃ ca yathā vṛttaṃ sa sadācāra iṣyate || 3.15 || tasmātkuryātsadācāraṃ ya icchedgatimātmanaḥ | sarvalakṣaṇahīno'pi samucācāravānnṛpa | śraddadhāno'nasūyaśca sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt || 3.16 ||
kiṃ ca ācāra eva dharmasya mūlaṃ rājan kulasya ca | ācārādvicyuto janturna kulīno na dhārmikaḥ || 3.17 ||
kiṃ ca ācāro bhūtijanana ācāraḥ kīrtivardhanaḥ | ācārādvardhate hyāyurācāro hantyalakṣaṇam || 3.18 ||
ācāra eva nṛpapuṅgava sevyamāno dharmārthakāmaphalado bhaviteha puṃsām | tasmātsadaiva viduṣāvahitena rājan śāstrodito hyanudinaṃ paripālanīyaḥ || 3.19 ||
atha tatra nityakṛtyāni
brāhme muhūrta utthāya kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇeti kīrtayan | prakṣālya pāṇipādau ca dantadhāvanamācaret || 3.20 || ācamya vasanaṃ rātrestyaktvānyatparidhāya ca | punarācamane kuryāllekhyena vidhināgrataḥ || 3.21 || athecchan paramāṃ śuddhiṃ mūrdhni dhyātvā guroḥ padau | stutvā ca kīrtayan kṛṣṇaṃ smaraṃścaitadudīrayet || 3.22 ||
atha prātaḥsmaraṇakīrtane
jayati jananivāso devakījanmavādo yaduvarapariṣatsvairdorbhirasyannadharmam | sthiracaravṛjinaghnaḥ susmita śrīmukhena vrajapuravanitānāṃ vardhayan kāmadevam || 3.23 ||
smṛte sakalakalyāṇabhājanaṃ yatra jāyate | puruṣastamajaṃ nityaṃ vrajāmi śaraṇaṃ harim || 3.24 ||
vidagdhagopālavilāsinīnāṃ sambhogacihnāṅkitasarvagātram | pavitramāmnāyagirāmagamyaṃ brahma prapadye navanītacauram || 3.25 ||
daśamaskandhe udgāyatīnāmaravindalocanaṃ vrajāṅganānāṃ divamaspṛśaddhvaniḥ | dadhnaśca nirmanthanaśabdamiśrito nirasyate yena diśāmamaṅgalam || 3.26 || | iti |
paṭhetpunaśca sādhūnāṃ sampradāyānusārataḥ | catuḥślokīmimāṃ sarvadoṣaśāntyai śubhāptaye || 3.27 ||
prātaḥ smarāmi bhavabhītimahārtiśāntyai nārāyaṇaṃ garuḍavāhanamabjanābham | grāhābhibhūtavaravārivāraṇamuktihetuṃ cakrāyudhaṃ taruṇavārijapatranetram || 3.28 ||
prātarnamāmi manasā vacasā ca mūrdhnā pādāravindayugalaṃ paramasya puṃsaḥ | nārāyaṇasya narakārṇavatāraṇasya pārāyaṇapravaṇavipraparāyaṇasya || 3.29 ||
prātarbhajāmi bhajatāmabhayaṅkaraṃ taṃ prāksarvajanmakṛtapāpabhayāvahatyai | yo grāhavaktrapatitāṅghrigajendraghora śokapraṇāśamakaroddhṛtaśaṅkhacakraḥ || 3.30 ||
ślokatrayamidaṃ puṇyaṃ prātaḥ prātaḥ paṭhettu yaḥ | lokatrayagurustasmai dadyādātmapadaṃ hariḥ || 3.31 || | iti |
tadetallikhitaṃ kutra kutracidvyavahārataḥ | kintu svābhīṣṭarūpādi śrīkṛṣṇasya vicintayet || 3.32 || itthaṃ vidadhyādbhagavatkīrtanasmaraṇādikam | sarvatīrthābhiṣekaṃ vai bahirantarviśodhanam || 3.33 ||
tathā ca skānde skandaṃ prati śrīśivoktau sakṛnnārāyaṇaetyuktvā pumān kalpaśatatrayam | gaṅgādisarvatīrtheṣu snāto bhavati putraka || 3.34 ||
anyatra ca śayanādutthito yastu kīrtayenmadhusūdanam | kīrtanāttasya pāpasya nāśamāyātyaśeṣataḥ || 3.35 || | iti |
māhātmyaṃ kīrtanasyāgre lekhyaṃ mukhyaprasaṅgataḥ | smaraṇasya tu māhātmyamadhunā likhyate kiyat || 3.36 ||
tatrādau tasya nityatā
pādme bṛhatsahasranāmni stotre smartavyaḥ satataṃ viṣṇurvismartavyo na jātucit | sarve vidhiniṣedhāḥ syuretayoreva kiṅkarāḥ || 3.37 ||
skānde kārttikaprasaṅge śrīmadagastyoktau sā hānistanmahacchidraṃ sa mohaḥ sa ca vibhramaḥ | yanmuhūrtaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vāpi vāsudevo na cintyate || 3.38 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe ca śrīdhruvacarite iyameva parā hānirupasargo'yameva ca | abhāgyaṃ paramaṃ caitadvāsudevaṃ na yatsmaret || 3.39 || ye muhūrtāḥ kṣaṇā ye ca yāḥ kāṣṭhā ye nimeṣakāḥ | ṛte viṣṇusmṛteryātāsteṣu muṣṭo yamena saḥ || 3.40 || | iti | nityatve'pyasya māhātmyaṃ vicitraphaladānataḥ | jñeyaṃ śāstroditaṃ darśapūrṇamāsādivadbudhaiḥ || 3.41 ||
atha smaraṇamāhātmyam tatra sarvatīrthasnānādhikatvam
uktaṃ ca smārtairapi māntraṃ pārthivamāgneyaṃ vāyavyaṃ divyameva ca | vāruṇaṃ mānasaṃ ceti snānaṃ saptavidhaṃ smṛtam || 3.42 || śaṃ na āpastu vai māntraṃ mṛdālambhaṃ tu pārthivam | bhasmanā snānamāgneyaṃ snānaṃ gorajasānilam || 3.43 || ātape sati yā vṛṣṭirdivyaṃ snānaṃ taducyate | bahirnadyādiṣu snānaṃ vāruṇaṃ procyate budhaiḥ | dhyānaṃ yanmanasā viṣṇormānasaṃ tatprakīrtanam || 3.44 ||
kiṃ ca asāmarthyena kāyasya kāladeśādyapekṣayā | tulyaphalāni sarvāṇi syurityāha parāśaraḥ || 3.45 || snānānāṃ mānasaṃ snānaṃ manvādyaiḥ paramaṃ smṛtam | kṛtena yena mucyante gṛhasthā api vai dvijāḥ || 3.46 ||
paramaśodhaktavam
gāruḍe śrīnāradoktau, viṣṇudharme ca pulasytyoktau apavitraḥ pavitro vā sarvāvasthāṃ gato'pi vā | yaḥ smaretpuṇḍarīkākṣaṃ sa bāhyābhyantaraḥ śuciḥ || 3.47 || yadyapyupahataḥ pāpairmanasāyantadustaraiḥ | tathāpi saṃsmaran viṣṇuṃ sa bāhyābhyantaraḥ śuciḥ || 3.48 ||
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe prāyaścittānyaśeṣāṇi tapaḥ karmātmakāni vai | yāni teṣāmaśeṣāṇāṃ kṛṣṇānusmaraṇaṃ param || 3.49 || kṛte pāpe'nutāpo vai yasya puṃsaḥ prajāyate | prāyaścittaṃ tu tasyaikaṃ harisaṃsmaraṇaṃ param || 3.50 ||
kiṃ ca kalikalmaṣamatyugraṃ narakārtipradaṃ nṝṇām | prayāti vilayaṃ sadyaḥ sakṛdyatrānusaṃsmṛte || 3.51 ||
bṛhannāradīye śukrabalisaṃvāde harirharati pāpāni duṣṭacittairapi smṛtaḥ | anicchayāpi saṃspṛṣṭo dahatyeva hi pāvakaḥ || 3.53 ||
tatraiva prāyaścittaprasaṅgānte mahāpātakayukto vā yukto vā sarvapātakaiḥ | sa vai vimucyate sadyo yasya viṣṇuparaṃ manaḥ || 3.54 ||
brahmavaivarte karmaṇā manasā vācā yaḥ kṛtaḥ pāpasañcayaḥ | so'pyaśeṣaḥ kṣayaṃ yāti smṛtvā kṛṣṇāṅghrapaṅkajam || 3.55 ||
ataevoktaṃ skānde kārttikaprasaṅge śrīparāśareṇa yamamārgaṃ mahāghoraṃ narakāṃśca yamaṃ tathā | svapne'pi na naraḥ paśyedyaḥ smaredgaruḍadhvajam || 3.56 ||
ṣaṣṭhaskandhe śrīśukena sakṛnmanaḥ kṛṣṇapadāravindayor niveśitaṃ tadguṇarāgi yairiha | na te yamaṃ pāśabhṛtaśca tadbhaṭān svapne'pi paśyanti hi cīrṇaniṣkṛtāḥ || 3.57 ||
sarvāpadvimocakatvam
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe śrīprahlādoktau dantā gajānāṃ kulśāgraniṣṭhurāḥ śīrṇā yadete na balaṃ mamaitat | mahāvipatpātavināśano'yaṃ janārdanānusmaraṇānubhāvaḥ || 3.58 ||
vāmanapurāṇe ca viṣṭayo vyatipātāśca ye'nye durnītisambhavāḥ | te sarve smaraṇādviṣṇornāśamāyāntyupadravāḥ || 3.59 ||
pādme māghamāhātmye devadyutistutau yasya smaraṇamātreṇa na moho na ca durgatiḥ | na rogo na ca duḥkhāni tamanantaṃ namāmyaham || 3.60 ||
durvāsanonmūlanatvam dvādaśaskandhe yathā hemni sthito vahnirdurvarṇaṃ hanti dhātujam | evamātmagato viṣṇuryogināmaśubhāśayam || 3.61 || sarvamaṅgalakāritvam
pāṇḍavagītāyām lābhasteṣāṃ jayasteṣāṃ kutasteṣāṃ parābhavaḥ | yeṣāmindīvaraśyāmo hṛdayasthajanārdanaḥ || 3.62 || sarvasatkarmaphaladatvam skānde kārttikaprasaṅge'gastyoktau deveṣu yajñeṣu tapaḥsu caiva dāneṣu tīrtheṣu vrateṣu caiva | iṣṭeṣu pūrteṣu ca yatpradiṣṭaṃ nṝṇāṃ smṛte tatphalamacyute ca || 3.63 || karmasādguṇyakāritvam
bṛhannāradīye nyūnātiriktatā siddhā kalau vedoktakarmaṇām | harisaṃsmaraṇamevātra sampūrṇaphaladāyakam || 3.64 ||
smṛtau ca pramādātkurvatāṃ karma pracyavetādhvareṣu yat | smaraṇādeva tadviṣṇoḥ sampūrṇaṃ syāditi śrutiḥ || 3.65 || sarvakarmādhikatvam
bṛhannāradīye kaliprasaṅge tulāpuruṣadānānāṃ rājasūyāśvamedhayoḥ | phalaṃ viṣṇoḥ smṛtisamaṃ na jātu dvijasattama || 3.66 ||
dvādaśaskandhe vidyātapaḥprāṇanirodhamaitrī tīrthābhiṣekavratadānajapyaiḥ | nātyantaśuddhiṃ labhate'ntarātmā yathā hṛdisthe bhagavatyanante || 3.67 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe hiraṇyakaśipuṃ prati śrīprahlādoktau bhayaṃ bhayānāmapahāriṇi sthite manasyanante mama kutra tiṣṭhati | yasmin smṛte janmajarodbhavāni bhayāni sarvāṇyapayānti tāta || 3.68 ||
tatraivānyatra viṣṇusaṃsmaraṇātkṣīṇasamastakleśasaṃcayaḥ | muktiṃ prayāti svargāptistasya vighno'numīyate || 3.69 ||
bṛhannāradīye varaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadaṃ purāṇaṃ nijaprabhābhāvitasarvalokam | saṅkalpitārthapradamādidevaṃ smṛtvā vrajenmuktipadaṃ manuṣyaḥ || 3.70 ||
skānde yasya smaraṇamātreṇa janmasaṃsārabandhanāt | vimucyate namastasmai viṣṇave prabhaviṣṇave || 3.71 ||
tatraiva kārttikaprasaṅge śrīparāśaroktau tadaiva puruṣo mukto janmaduḥkhajarādibhiḥ | bhaktyā tu parayā nūnaṃ yadaiva smarate harim || 3.72 || bhagavatprasādanam
bṛhannāradīye yena kenāpyupāyena smṛto nārāyaṇo'vyayaḥ | api pātakayuktasya prasannaḥ syānna saṃśayaḥ || 3.73 || śrīvaikuṇṭhalokaprāpakatvam vāmanapurāṇe anādyanantamajarāmaraṃ hariṃ ye saṃsmarantyaharaharniyataṃ narā bhuvi | tatsarvagaṃ brahma paraṃ purāṇaṃ te yānti vaiṣṇavapadaṃ dhurvamavyayaṃ ca || 3.74 ||
pādme devadūtavikuṇḍalasaṃvāde yamasya dūtānuśāsane smaranti ye sakṛdbhūtāḥ prasaṅgenāpi keśavam | te vidhvastākhilāghaughā yanti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam || 3.75 ||
brahmapurāṇe viṣṇurahasye śāṭhyenāpi narā nityaṃ ye smaranti janārdanam | te'pi yānti tanuṃ tyaktvā viṣṇulokamanāmayam || 3.76 ||
viṣṇudharmottare nirāśīrnirmamo yastu viṣṇordhyānaparo bhavet | tatpadaṃ samavāpnoti yatra gatvā na śocati || 3.77 ||
sārūpyaprāpaṇam
kāśīkhaṇḍe śrībindumādhavaprasaṅge agnibindustutau ye tvāṃ trivikrama sadā hṛdi śīlayanti kādambinīrucirarociṣamambujākṣa | saudāminīvilasitāṃśukavītamūrte te'pi spṛśanti tava kāntimacintyarūpām || 3.78 ||
śrībhagavadgītāsu antakāle ca māmeva smaranmuktvā kalevaram | yaḥ prayāti sa madbhāvaṃ yāti nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ || 3.79 ||
daśamaskandhe pṛthukopākhyāne smarataḥ pādakamalamātmānamapi yacchati | kiṃ nvarthakāmān bhajato nātyabhīṣṭān jagadguruḥ || 3.80 ||
vaiṣṇave vāsudeve mano yasya japahomārcanādiṣu | tasyāntarāyo maitreya devendratvādi satphalam || 3.81 ||
gāruḍe mahatastapaso mūlaṃ prasavaḥ puṇyasantateḥ | jīvitasya phalaṃ svādu niyataṃ smaraṇaṃ hareḥ || 3.82 ||
dvitīyaskandhe etāvān sāṅkhyayogābhyāṃ svadharmapariniṣṭhayā | janmalābhaḥ paraḥ puṃsāmante nārāyaṇasmṛtiḥ || 3.83 ||
ataeva jarāsandhaniruddhanṛpavargaiḥ prārthitaṃ daśamaskandhe taṃ naḥ samādiśopāyaṃ yena te caraṇābjayoḥ | smṛtiryathā na viramedapi saṃsaratāmiha || 3.84 ||
śrīnāradenāpi dṛṣṭaṃ tavāṅghriyugalaṃ janatāpavargaṃ brahmādibhirhṛdi vicintyamagādhabodhaiḥ | saṃsārakūpapatitottaraṇāvalambaṃ dhyāyaṃścarāmyanugṛhāṇa yathā smṛtiḥ syāt || 3.85 || | iti | kṛṣṇasmaraṇamāhātmyamahābdhirdustaro dhiyā | yo yiyāsati tatpāraṃ sa hi caitanyavañcitaḥ || 3.86 || tataḥ pādodakaṃ kiñcitprākpītvā tulasīdalaiḥ | gṛhītenācarettena svamūrdhanyabhiṣecanam || 3.87 || athādau śrīguruṃ natvā śrīkṛṣṇasya padābjayoḥ | kiñcidvijñāpayan sarvasvakṛtyānyarpayennamet || 3.88 ||
atha prātaḥ praṇāmaḥ
vāmanapurāṇe sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalyaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadaṃ śivam | nārāyaṇaṃ namaskṛtya sarvakarmāṇi kārayet || 3.89 ||
atha vijñāpanam
viṣṇudharmottare yadutsavādikaṃ karma tattvayā prerito hare | kariṣyāmi tvayā jñeyamiti vijñāpanaṃ mama || 3.90 || prātaḥ prabodhito viṣṇo hṛṣīkeśena yattvayā | yadyatkārayasīśāna tatkaromi tavājñayā || 3.91 ||
trailokyacaitanyamayādideva śrīnātha viṣṇo bhavadājñayaiva | prātaḥ samutthāya tava priyārthaṃ saṃsārayātrāmanuvartayiṣye || 3.92 ||
saṃsārayātrāmanuvartamānaṃ tvadājñayā śrīnṛhare'ntarātman | spardhātiraskārakalipramāda bhayāni mā mābhibhavantu bhūman || 3.93 ||
jānāmi dharmaṃ na ca me pravṛttir jānāmyadharmaṃ na ca me nivṛttiḥ | tvayā hṛṣīkeśa hṛdi sthitena yathā niyukto'smi tathā karomi || 3.94 ||
atha praṇāmavākyāni
mahābhārate namo brahmaṇyadevāya gobrāhmaṇahitāya ca | jagaddhitāya kṛṣṇāya govindāya namo namaḥ || 3.95 ||
garuḍapurāṇe asuravibudhasiddhairjñāyate yasya nāntaḥ sakalamunibhirantaścintyate yo viśuddhaḥ | nikhilahṛdi niviṣṭo vetti yaḥ sarvasākṣī tamajamamṛtamīśaṃ vāsudevaṃ nato'smi || 3.96 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe yajñibhiryajñapuruṣo vāsudevaśca sātvataḥ | vedāntavedibhirviṣṇuḥ procyate yo nato'smi tam || 3.97 || evaṃ vijñāpayan dhyāyan kīrtayaṃśca yathāvidhi | praṇāmānācarecchaktyā catuḥsaṅkhyāvarān budhaḥ || 3.98 || śrīgopīcandanenordhvapuṇḍraṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi | āsīta prāṅmukho bhūtvā śuddhasthāne śubhāsane || 3.99 ||
tathā ca nāradīyapañcarātre nirgatyācamya vidhivatpraviśya ca punaḥ sudhīḥ | āsane prāṅmukho bhūtvā vihite copaviśya vai || 3.100 || sampradāyānusāreṇa bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāya ca | prāṇāyāmāṃśca vidhivatkṛṣṇaṃ dhyāyetyathoditam || 3.101 ||
tathā coktam upapātakeṣu sarveṣu pātakeṣu mahatsu ca | praviśya rajanīpādaṃ viṣṇudhyānaṃ samācaret || 3.102 ||
vaihāyasapañcarātre ca tathaiva rātriśeṣaṃ tu kālaṃ sūryodayāvadhi | kartavyaṃ sajapaṃ dhyānaṃ nityamārādhakena vai || 3.103 || vibhajya pañcadhā rātriṃ śeṣe devārcanādikam | japaṃ homaṃ tathā dhyānaṃ nityaṃ kurvīta sādhakaḥ || 3.104 ||
ataeva viṣṇusmṛtau rātrestu paścime yāme muhūrtau brāhmya ucyate || 3.105 || | iti | pādodapānādīnāṃ ca sa vidhirmahimāgrataḥ | lekhyo'dhunā tu dhyānasya sa saṅkṣepeṇa likhyate || 3.106 ||
tāpanīyaśrutiṣu satpuṇḍarīkanayanaṃ meghābhaṃ vaidyutāmbaram | dvibhujaṃ jñānamudrāḍhyaṃ vanamālinamīśvaram || 3.107 || gopagopīgavāvītaṃ suradrumatalāśritam | dviyālaṅkaraṇopetaṃ ratnapaṅkajamadhyagam || 3.108 || kālindījalakallolasaṅgimārutasevitam | cintayaṃścetasā kṛṣṇaṃ mukto bhavati saṃsṛteḥ || 3.109 ||
mṛtyuñjayasaṃhitānusāroditasāradātilake ca smaredvṛndāvane ramye mohayantamanāratam | govindaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ gopakanyāḥ sahasraśaḥ || 3.110 || ātmano vadanāmbhojapreritākṣimadhuvratāḥ | kāmabāṇena vivaśāściramāśleṣeṇotsukāḥ || 3.111 || muktāhāralasatpīnottuṅgastanabharānatāḥ | srastadhammillavasanā madaskhalitabhāṣaṇāḥ || 3.112 || dantapaṅktiprabhodbhāsispandamānādharāñcitāḥ | vilobhayantīrvividhairvibhramairbhāvagarbhitaiḥ || 3.113 ||
phullendīvarakāntiminduvadanaṃ barhāvataṃsapriyaṃ śrīvatsāṅkamudārakaustubhadharaṃ pītāmbaraṃ sundaram | gopīnāṃ nayanotpalārcitatanuṃ gogopasaṅghāvṛtaṃ govindaṃ kalaveṇuvādanaparaṃ divyāṅgabhūṣaṃ bhaje || 3.114 || | iti | śrīgautamīyatantrādau taddhyānaṃ prathitaṃ param | agrato'trāpi saṃlekhyaṃ yadiṣṭaṃ tatra tadbhajet || 3.115 ||
bṛhatśātātāpasmṛtau pakṣopavāsādyatpāpaṃ puruṣasya praṇaśyati | prāṇāyāmaśatenaiva yatpāpaṃ naśyate nṝṇām || 3.116 || prāṇāyāmasahasreṇa yatpāpaṃ naśyate nṝṇām | kṣaṇamātreṇa tatpāpaṃ harerdhyānātpraṇaśyati || 3.117 ||
viṣṇudharme sarvapāpaprasakto'pi dhyāyannimiṣamacyutam | bhūtastapasvī bhavati paṅktipāvanapāvanaḥ || 3.118 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe ca dhyāyennārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ snānādiṣu ca karmasu | prāyaścittaṃ hi sarvasya duṣkṛtasyeti niścitam || 3.119 || kalidoṣaharatvam
bṛhannāradīye kaliprasaṅge samastajagadādhāraṃ paramārthasvarūpiṇam | ghore kaliyuge prāpte viṣṇuṃ dhyāyanna sīdati || 3.120 || sarvadharmādhikāritvam
skānde kārttikamāhātmye agastyoktau kintvasya bahubhistīrthaiḥ kiṃ tasya bahubhirvrataiḥ | yo nityaṃ dhyāyate devaṃ nārāyaṇamananyadhīḥ || 3.121 || mokṣapradatvam
bṛhannāradīye kaliprasaṅge ye mānavā vigatarāgaparāvarajñā nārāyaṇaṃ suraguruṃ satataṃ smaranti | dhyānena tena hata kilbiṣacetanāste mātuḥ payodhararasaṃ na punaḥ pibanti || 3.122 || śrīvaikuṇṭhaprāpakatvam skānde śrībrahmoktau muhūrtamapi yo dhyāyennārāyaṇamatandritaḥ | so'pi sadgatimāpnoti kiṃ punastatparāyaṇaḥ || 3.123 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye yamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde dhyāyanti puruṣaṃ divyamacyutaṃ ye smaranti ca | labhante te'cyutasthānaṃ śrutireṣā purātanī || 3.124 ||
sārūpyaprāpaṇam
ekādaśaskandhe vaireṇa yaṃ nṛpatayaḥ śiśupālapauṇḍra śālvādayo gativilāsavilokanādyaiḥ | dhyāyanta ākṛtadhiyaḥ śayanāsanādau tatsāmyamāpuranuraktadhiyāṃ punaḥ kim || 3.125 ||
svataḥ paramaphalatvam
caturthaskandhe śrīpṛthūktau bhajantyatha tvāmata eva sādhavo vyudastamāyāguṇavibhramodayam | bhavatpadānusmaraṇādṛte satāṃ nimittamanyadbhagavanna vidmahe || 3.126 ||
skandapurāṇe brahmoktau ca āloḍya sarvaśāstrāṇi vicārya ca punaḥ punaḥ | idame eva suniṣpannaṃ dhyeyo nārāyaṇaḥ sadā || 3.127 ||
ataevoktaṃ hāyaśīrṣapañcarātre nārāyaṇavyūhastave ye tyaktalokadharmārthā viṣṇubhaktivaśaṃ gatāḥ | dhyāyanti paramātmānaṃ tebhyo'pīha namo namaḥ || 3.128 || | iti |
smaraṇe yattanmāhātmyaṃ taddhyāne'pyakhilaṃ viduḥ | bhedaḥ kalpyeta sāmānyaviśeṣābhyāṃ tayoḥ kiyān || 3.129 ||
atha śrībhagavatprabodhanam
tato devālaye gatvā ghaṇṭādyudghoṣapūrvakam | prabodhya stutibhiḥ kṛṣṇaṃ nīrājyaṃ prārthayedidam || 3.130 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe so'sāvadabhrakaruṇo bhagavān vivṛddha premasmitena nayanāmburuhaṃ vijṛmbhan | utthāya viśvavijayāya ca no viṣādaṃ mādhvyā girāpanayatātpuruṣaḥ purāṇaḥ || 3.131 ||
devaprapannārtihara prasādaṃ kuru keśava | avalokanadānena bhūyo māṃ pārayācyuta || 3.132 || | iti | devālayaṃ praviśyātha stotrāṇīṣṭāni kīrtayan | kṛṣṇasya tulasīvarjaṃ nirmālyamapasārayet || 3.133 || atha nirmālyottāraṇam
atrismṛtau prātaḥkāle sadā kuryānnirmālyottāraṇaṃ budhaḥ | tṛṣitāḥ paśavo baddhāḥ kanyakā ca rajasvalā | devatā ca sanirmālyā hanti puṇyaṃ purākṛtam || 3.134 ||
nārasiṃhe śrīyamoktau devamālyāpanayanaṃ devāgāre samūhanam | snāpanaṃ sarvadevānāṃ gopradānasamaṃ smṛtam || 3.135 ||
nāradapañcarātre yaḥ prātarutthāya vidhāya nityaṃ nirmālyamīśasya nirākaroti | na tasya duḥkhaṃ na daridratā ca nākālamṛtyurna ca rogamātram || 3.136 ||
aruṇodayavelāyāṃ nirmālyaṃ śalyatāṃ vrajet | prātastu syānmahāśalyaṃ ghaṭikāmātrayogataḥ || 3.137 || atiśalyaṃ vijānīyāttato vajraprahāravat | aruṇodayavelāyāṃ śalyaṃ tatkṣamate hariḥ || 3.138 || ghaṭikāyāmatikrāntau kṣudraṃ pātakamāvahet | muhūrte samatikrānte pūrṇaṃ pātakamucyate || 3.139 || atipātakamevsyātghaṭikānāṃ catuṣṭaye | muhūrtatritaye pūrṇe mahāpātakamucyate || 3.140 || tataḥ paraṃ brahmavadho mahāpātakapañcakam | prahare pūrṇatāṃ yāte prāyaścittaṃ tato na hi || 3.141 || nirmālyasya vilambe tu prāyaścittamathocyate | atikrānte muhūrtārdhe sahasraṃ japamācaret || 3.142 || pūrṇe muhūrte sañjāte sahasraṃ sārdhamucyate | sahasradvitīyaṃ kuryātghaṭikānāṃ catuṣṭaye || 3.143 || muhūrtatritaye'tīte ayutaṃ japamācaret | prahare pūrṇatāṃ yāte puraścaraṇamucyate | prahare samatikrānte prāyaścittaṃ na vidyate || 3.144 ||
atha śrīmukhaprakṣālanam
śrīhastāṅghrimukhāmbhojakṣālanāya ca tadgṛhe | gaṇḍūṣāṇi jalairdattvā dantakāṣṭhaṃ samarpayet || 3.145 || jihvollekhanikāṃ dattvā pāduke śuddhamṛttikām | salilaṃ ca punardadyādvāso'pi mukhamārjanam || 3.146 || tataḥ śrītulasīṃ puṇyāmarpayetbhagavatpriyām | tanmāhātmyaṃ ca tanmukhyaprasaṅge lekhyamagrataḥ || 3.147 ||
atha dantakāṣṭhārpaṇamāhātmyam
viṣṇudharmottare dantakāṣṭhapradānena dantasaubhāgyamṛcchati | jihvollekhanikāṃ dattvā virogastvabhijāyate || 3.148 || pādukāyāḥ pradānena gatimiṣṭāmavāpnuyāt | mṛdbhāgadānāddevasya bhūmimāpnotyanuttamām || 3.149 ||
atha maṅgalanīrājanam
