paṭhitvātha priyān ślokānmahāvāditranisvanaiḥ | prabhornīrājanaṃ kuryānmaṅgalākhyaṃ jagaddhitam || 3.150 || nīrājanaṃ tvidaṃ sarvaiḥ kartavyaṃ śucivigrahaiḥ | paramaśraddhayotthāya draṣṭavyaṃ ca sadā naraiḥ || 3.151 || strīṇāṃ puṃsāṃ ca sarveṣāmetatsarveṣṭapūrakam | samastadainyadāridryaduritādyupaśāntikṛt || 3.152 ||
atha prātaḥsnānārthodyamaḥ
tato'ruṇodayasyānte snānārthaṃ niḥsaredbahiḥ | kīrtayan kṛṣṇanāmāni tīrthaṃ gacchedanantaram || 3.153 ||
tathā ca śukrasmṛtau brāhme muhūrte cotthāya śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ | svastikādyāsanaṃ baddhvā dhyātvā kṛṣṇapādāmbujam || 3.154 || tato nirgatya nilayānnāmānīmāni kīrtayet | śrīvāsudevāniruddhapradyumnādhokṣajācyuta | śrīkṛṣṇānanta govinda saṅkarṣaṇa namo'stu te || 3.155 || gatvā tīrthādikaṃ tatra nikṣipya snānasādhanam | vidhinācarya maitryādikṛtyaṃ śaucaṃ vidhāya ca | ācamya khāni saṃmārjya snānaṃ kuryātyathocitam || 3.156 ||
atha maitryādikṛtyavidhiḥ
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe aurvasagarasaṃvāde gṛhidharmakathane tataḥ kalye samutthāya kuryānmūtraṃ nareśvara | nairṛtyāmiṣuvikṣepamatītyādhikaṃ gṛhāt || 3.157 || dūrādāvasathānmūtraṃ purīṣaṃ ca samutsṛjet | pādāvasecanocchiṣṭe prakṣipenna gṛhāṅgaṇe || 3.158 || ātmacchāyāṃ tarośchāyāṃ gosūryāgnyanilāṃstathā | guruṃ dvijādīṃśca budho na meheta kadācana || 3.159 || na kṛṣṭe śasyamadhye vā govraje janasaṃsadi | na vartmani na nadyāditīrtheṣu puruṣarṣabha || 3.160 || nāpsu naivāmbhasastīre na śmaśāne samācaret | utsargaṃ vai purīṣasya mūtrasya ca visarjanam || 3.161 || udaṅmukho divotsargaṃ viparītamukho niśi | kurvītānāpadi prājño mūtrotsargaṃ ca pārthiva || 3.162 || tṛṇairācchādya vasudhāṃ vastraprāvṛtamastakaḥ | tiṣṭhennāticiraṃ tatra naiva kiñcidudīrayet || 3.163 ||
tathā kaurme vyāsagītāyām nidhāya dakṣiṇe karṇe brahmasūtramudaṅmukhaḥ | antardhāpya mahīṃ kāṣṭhaiḥ patrairloṣṭraistṛṇena vā || 3.164 || prāvṛtya tu śiraḥ kuryādvinmūtrasya visarjanam | na caivābhimukhaḥ strīṇāṃ gurubrāhmaṇayorgavām | na devadevālayornāpāmapi kadācana || 3.165 || nadīṃ jyotīṃṣi vīkṣitvā na vāyvagnimukho'pi vā | prayādityaṃ pratyanalaṃ pratisomaṃ tathaiva ca || 3.166 ||
kāśīkāṇḍe śrīskandāgastyasaṃvāde tataścāvaśyakaṃ kartuṃ nairṛtīṃ diśamāśrayet | grāmāddhanuḥśataṃ gacchennagarācca caturguṇam || 3.167 || karṇopavītyudagvaktro divase sandhyayorapi | vinmūtre visṛjenmaunī niśāyāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ || 3.168 || nālokayeddiśo bhāgān jyotiścakraṃ nabho'malam | vāmena pāṇinā śiśnaṃ dhṛtvottiṣṭhetprayatnavān || 3.169 ||
tatraivāgre na mūtraṃ govraje kuryānna valmīke na bhasmani | na garteṣu sasattveṣu na tiṣṭhanna vrajannapi || 3.170 || yathāsukhamukho rātrau divā chāyāndhakārayoḥ | bhītiṣu prāṇabādhāyāṃ kuryānmalavisarjanam || 3.171 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe tatraiva valmīkamūṣikotkhātāṃ mṛdaṃ nāntarjalāttathā | śaucāvaśiṣṭāṃ gehācca na dadyāllepasambhavām || 3.172 || antaḥprāṇyavapannāṃ ca halotkhātāṃ ca pārthiva | parityajenmṛdaścaitāḥ sakalāḥ śaucasādhane || 3.173 || ekā liṅge gude tisrasdaśa vāmakare nṛpa | hastadvaye saptānyā mṛdaḥ śaucopapādikāḥ || 3.174 ||
yamasmṛtau tisrastu pādayordeyāḥ śuddhikāmena nityaśaḥ || 3.175 ||
kiṃ ca tisrastu mṛttikā deyāḥ kṛtvā tu nakhaśodhanam || 3.176 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe ca tatraiva guhye dadyānmṛdaṃ caikāṃ pāyau pañcāmbusāntarāḥ | daśa vāmakare cāpi sapta pāṇidvaye mṛdaḥ || 3.177 || ekaikāṃ pādayordadyāttisraḥ pāṇyormṛdaḥ smṛtāḥ | itthaṃ śaucaṃ gṛhī kuryādgandhalepakṣayāvadhi || 3.178 || kramāddviguṇametattu brahmacaryādiṣu triṣu | divā vihitaśaucācca rātrāvardhaṃ samācaret || 3.179 || rujārdhaṃ ca tadardhaṃ ca pathi caurādipīḍite | tadardhayoṣitāṃ cāpi svāsthye nyūnaṃ na kārayet | ārdradhātrīphalonmānā mṛdaḥ śauce prakīrtitāḥ || 3.180 ||
śaṅkhasmṛtau mṛttikā tu samuddiṣṭā triparvī pūryate yayā || 3.181 ||
dakṣasmṛtau ardhaprasṛtimātrā tu prathamā mṛttikā smṛtā | dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā ca tadardhaṃ parikīrtitā || 3.182 || atha kevalamūtrotsarge
dakṣaḥ ekā liṅge tu savye trirubhayormṛddvayaṃ smṛtam || 3.183 ||
brāhme pādayordve gṛhītvā ca suprakṣālitapāṇinā | ācamya tu tataḥ śuddhaḥ smṛtvā viṣṇuṃ sanātanam || 3.184 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe tatraiva acchenāgandhaphenena jalenābudbudena ca | ācāmeta mṛdaṃ bhūyastathā dadyātsamāhitaḥ || 3.185 || niṣpāditāṅghriśaucastu pāpāvabhyukṣya vai punaḥ | triḥ pibetsalilaṃ tena tathā dviḥ parimārjayet || 3.186 || śīrṣaṇyāni tataḥ khāni mūrdhānaṃ ca mṛdā labhet | bāhū nābhiṃ ca toyena hṛdayaṃ cāpi saṃspṛśet || 3.187 ||
atra ca viśeṣo dakṣeṇoktaḥ prakṣālya hastau pādau ca triḥ pibedambu vīkṣitam | saṃvṛttāṅguṣṭhamūlena dviḥ pramṛjyāttato mukham || 3.188 || saṃhatya tisṛbhiḥ pūrvamāsyamevamupaspṛśet | aṅguṣṭhena pradeśinyā ghrāṇaṃ paścādanantaram || 3.189 || aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu cakṣuḥśrotre punaḥ punaḥ | kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayornābhiṃ hṛdayaṃ tu talena vai | sarvābhistu śiraḥ paścādbāhū cāgreṇa saṃspṛśet || 3.190 ||
tathā kāśīkhaṇḍe tatraiva prāgāsya udagāsyo vā sūpaviṣṭaḥ śucau bhuvi | upaspṛśedvihīnāyāṃ tuṣāṅgārāsthibhasmabhiḥ || 3.191 || anuṣṇābhiraphenābhiradbhirhṛdgābhiratvaraḥ | brāhmaṇo brahmatīrthena dṛṣṭipūtanābhirācamet || 3.192 || kaṇṭhagābhirnṛpaḥ śudhyettālugābhistathorujaḥ | strīśūdrāvāsyasaṃsparśamātreṇāpi visudhyataḥ || 3.193 ||
yājñavalkyasmṛtau pādakṣālanaśeṣeṇa nācāmetvāriṇā dvijaḥ | yadyācametsrāvayitvā bhūmau baudhāyano'bravīt || 3.194 ||
bharadvājasmṛtau pāṇinā dakṣiṇenaiva saṃhatāṅgulinācamet | muktāṅguṣṭhakaniṣṭhena nakaspṛṣṭā apastyajet || 3.195 ||
kaurme ca vyāsagītāyām bhuktvā pītvā ca suptvā ca snātvā rathyopasarpaṇe | auṣṭhau vilomakau spṛṣṭvā vāso viparidhāya ca || 3.196 || retomūtrapurīṣāṇāmutsarge'nṛtabhāṣaṇe | ṣthīvitvādhyayanārambhe kāśaśvāsāgame tathā || 3.197 || catvaraṃ vā śmaśānaṃ vā samabhyasya dvijottamaḥ | sandhyorubhayostadvadācānto'pyācametpunaḥ || 3.198 ||
kiṃ ca śiraḥ prāvṛtya kaṇṭhaṃ vā muktakacchaśikho'pi vā | akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucamācānto'pyaśucirbhavet || 3.199 || sopānatkau jalastho vā noṣṇīṣī cācamedbudhaḥ | na caiva varṣadhārābhirhastocchiṣṭe tathā budhaḥ || 3.200 || naikahastārpiuttajalairvinā sūtreṇa vā punaḥ | na pādukāsanastho vā bahirjānurathāpi vā || 3.201 || atha vaiṣṇavācamanam
triḥpāne keśavaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ mādhavamapyatha | prakṣālane dvayoḥ pāṇyorgovindaṃ viṣṇumapyubhau || 3.202 || madhusūdanamekaṃ ca mārjane'nyaṃ trivikramam || 3.203 || unmārjane'pyadharayorvāmanaśrīdharāvubhau || 3.204 || prakṣālane punaḥ pāṇyorhṛṣīkeśaṃ ca pādayoḥ | padmanābhaṃ prokṣaṇe tu mūrdhno dāmodaraṃ tataḥ || 3.205 || vāsudevaṃ mukhe saṅkarṣaṇaṃ pradyumnamityubhau | nāsayornetrayugale'niruddhaṃ puruṣottamam | adhokṣajaṃ nṛsiṃhaṃ ca karṇayornābhito'cyutam || 3.206 || janārdanaṃ ca hṛdaye upendraṃ mastake tataḥ | dakṣiṇe tu hariṃ bāhau vāme kṛṣṇaṃ yathāvidhi | namo'nantaṃ ca caturthyantamācāmetkramato japan || 3.207 || aśaktaḥ kevalaṃ dakṣaṃ spṛśetkarṇaṃ tathā ca vāk | kurvītālabhanaṃ vāpi dakṣiṇaśravaṇasya vai || 3.208 ||
atha dantadhāvanavidhiḥ
tatra kātyāyanaḥ utthāya netraṃ prakṣālya śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ | parijapya ca mantreṇa bhakṣayeddantadhāvanam || 3.209 ||
mantraścāyam āyurbalaṃ yaśo varcaḥ prajā paśuvasūni ca | brahma prajñāṃ ca medhāṃ ca tvaṃ no dhehi vanaspate || 3.210 ||
tasya nityatā
kāśīkhaṇḍe atho mukhaviśuddhyarthaṃ gṛhṇīyāddantadhāvanam | ācānto'pyaśuciryasmādakṛtvā dantadhāvanam || 3.211 ||
vārāhe ca dantakāṣṭhamakhāditvā yastu māmupasarpati | sarvakālakṛtaṃ karma tena caikena naśyati || 3.212 ||
atha dantakāṣṭhaniṣiddhadināni
manuḥ caturdaśyaṣṭamīdarśapaurṇamāsyarkasaṅkramaḥ | eṣu strītailamāṃsāni dantakāṣṭhāni varjayet || 3.213 ||
saṃvartakaḥ ādye tithau navamyāṃ ca kṣaye candramasastathā | ādityvāre śaure ca varjayeddantadhāvanam || 3.214 ||
kātyāyanaḥ pratipaddarśaṣaṣṭhīṣu navamyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ | dantānāṃ kāṣṭhasaṃyogo dahatyāsaptamaṃ kulam || 3.215 ||
vṛddhavaśiṣṭhaḥ upavāse tathā śrāddhena khādeddantadhāvanam | dantānāṃ kāṣṭhasaṃyogo hanti saptakulāni vai || 3.216 ||
anyatra ca pratipaddaraṣaṣṭhīṣu navamyekādaśīravau | dantānāṃ kāṣṭhasaṃyogo hanti puṇyaṃ purākṛtam || 3.217 ||
atha tatra pratinidhiḥ dinaeṣveteṣu kāṣṭhairhi dantānāṃ dhāvanasya tu | niṣiddhatvāttṛṇaiḥ kuryāttathā kāṣṭhetaraiśca tat || 3.218 ||
tathā ca vyāsaḥ pratipaddarśaṣaṣṭhīṣu navamyāṃ dantadhāvanam | parṇairanyatra kāṣṭhaiśca jīvollekhhaḥ sadaiva hi || 3.219 ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ alābhe ca niṣedhe vā kāṣṭhānāṃ dantadhāvanam | parṇādinā viśuddhena jihvollekhaḥ sadaiva hi || 3.220 ||
atha tatraivāpavādaḥ
kāṣṭhaiḥ pratipadādau yanniṣiddhaṃ dantadhāvanam | tṛṇaparṇaistu tatkuryādamāmekādaśīṃ vinā || 3.221 ||
ata eva vyāsasya vacanāntaram alābhe dantakāṣṭhānāṃ niṣiddhāyāṃ tathā tithau | apāṃ dvādaśagaṇḍūṣairvidadhyāddantadhāvanam || 3.222 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe tatraiva mukhe paryuṣite yasmādbhavedaśucibhāgnaraḥ | tataḥ kuryātprayatnena śuddhyarthaṃ dantadhāvanam || 3.225 || upavāse'pi no duṣyeddantadhāvanamañjanam | gandhālaṅkārasadvastrapuṣpamālānulepanam || 3.226 ||
atha dantakāṣṭhāni
smṛtau sarva kaṇṭakinaḥ puṇyāḥ āyurdāḥ kṣīriṇaḥ smṛtāḥ | kaṭutiktakaṣāyāśca balārogyasukhapradāḥ || 3.227 ||
kiṃ ca palāśānāṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ pāduke caiva varjayet | varjayecca prayatnena baṭaṃ vāśvatthameva ca || 3.228 ||
kaurme śrīvyāsagītāyām madhyāṅgulisamasthaulyaṃ dvādaśāṅgulasammitam | satvacaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ yattadagre na tu dhārayet || 3.229 || kṣīrivṛkṣasamudbhūtaṃ mālatīsambhavaṃ śubham | apāmārgaṃ ca bilvaṃ vā karavīraṃ viśeṣatah || 3.230 || varjayitvā ninitāni gṛhītvaikaṃ yathoditam | parihṛtya dinaṃ pāpaṃ bhakṣayedvai vidhānavit || 3.231 || na pāṭayetdantakāṣṭhaṃ nāṅgulyagreṇa dhārayet | prakṣālya bhuktvā tajjahyātśucau deśe samāhitaḥ || 3.232 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe ca tatraiva kaniṣṭhāgraparīṇāhaṃ satvacaṃ nirvraṇamṛjum | dvādaśāṅgulamānaṃ ca sārdraṃ syāddantadhāvanam | jihvollekhanikāṃ vāpi kuryāccāpākṛtiṃ śubhām || 3.233 ||
rāmārcanacandrikāyāṃ ca dantollekho vitastyā bhavati parimitādannamityādimantrāt prātaḥ kṣīryādikāṣṭhādvaṭakhadirapalāśairvinārkāmrabilvaiḥ | bhuktvā gaṇḍūṣaṣaṭkaṃ dvirapi kuśamṛte deśinīmaṅgulībhir nandābhūtāṣṭaparvaṇyapi na khalu navamyarkasaṅkrāntipāte || 3.234 ||
atha keśaprasādhanādiḥ
tataścācamya vidhivatkṛtvā keśaprasādhanam | smṛtvā praṇavagāyatryau nibadhnīyācchikhāṃ dvijaḥ || 3.235 ||
tathā coktaṃ na dakṣiṇāmukho nordhvaṃ kuryātkeśaprasādhanam | smṛtvoṅkāraṃ ca gāyatrīṃ nibadhnīyāccikhāntataḥ || 3.236 ||
atha snānam
viṣṇupurāṇe tatraiva nadīnadataḍāgeṣu devakhātajaleṣu ca | nityakriyārthaṃ snāyīta giriprasravaṇeṣu ca || 3.237 || kūpeṣūddhṛtatoyena snānaṃ kurvīta vā bhuvi | snāyītoddhṛtatoyena athavā bhuvyasambhave || 3.238 ||
atha snānanityatā
tatra kātyāyanaḥ yathāhani tathā prātarnityaṃ snāyādanāturaḥ | atyantamalinaḥ kāyo navacchidrasamanvitaḥ | sravateva divārātrau prātaḥsnānaṃ viśodhanam || 3.239 ||
dakṣaḥ prātarmadhyāhnayoḥ snānaṃ vānaprasthagṛhasthayoḥ | yatestrisavanaṃ snānaṃ sakṛttu brahmacāriṇaḥ || 3.240 || sarve cāpi sakṛtkuryuraśaktau codakaṃ vinā || 3.241 ||
kiṃ ca aśiraskaṃ bhavetsnānamaśaktau karmiṇāṃ sadā | ārdreṇa vāsasā vāpi pāṇinā vāpi mārjanam || 3.242 ||
śaṅkhaśca asnātastu pumānnārho japādihavanādiṣu || 3.243 ||
kaurme vyāsagītāyām prātaḥsnānaṃ vinā puṃsāṃ pāpitvaṃ karmasu smṛtam | home jape viśeṣeṇa tasmātsnānaṃ samācaret || 3.244 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe prasvedalālasādyāklinno nidrādhīno yato naraḥ | prātaḥsnānāttato'rhaḥ syānmantrastotrajapādiṣu || 3.245 ||
pādme ca devahūtivikuṇḍalasaṃvāde snānaṃ vinā tu yo bhuṅkte malāśī sa sadā naraḥ | asnāyino'śucestasya vimukhāḥ pitṛdevatāḥ || 3.246 || snānahīno naraḥ pāpaḥ snānahīno naro'śuciḥ | asnāyī narakaṃ bhuṅkte puṃskīṭādiṣu jāyate || 3.247 ||
atha snānamāhātmyam
mahābhārate udyogaparvaṇi śrīviduroktau guṇā daśa snānaśīlaṃ bhajante balaṃ rūpaṃ svaravarṇapraśuddhiḥ | sparśaśca gandhaśca viśuddhatā ca śrīḥ saukumāryaṃ pravarāśca nāryaḥ || 3.248 ||
pādme ca tatraiva yāmyaṃ hi yātanāduḥkhaṃ nityasnāyī na paśyati | nityasnānena pūyante api pāpakṛto narāḥ || 3.249 || prātaḥsnānaṃ haredvaiśya bāhyābhyantarajaṃ malam | prātaḥsnānena niṣpāpo naro na nirayaṃ vrajet || 3.250 || ye punaḥ srotasi snānamācarantīha parvaṇi | tenaiva narakaṃ yānti na jāyante kuyoniṣu || 3.251 || duḥsvapnā duṣṭacintāśca bandhyā bhavanti sarvadā | prātaḥsnānena śuddhānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ viśāṃ vara || 3.252 ||
atrismṛtau snāne manaḥprasādaḥ syāddevā abhimukhāḥ sadā | saubhāgyaṃ śrīḥ sukhaṃ puṣṭiḥ puṇyaṃ vidyā yaśo dhṛtiḥ || 3.253 || mahāpāpānyalakṣmīṃ ca duritaṃ durvicintitam | śokaduḥkhādi harate prātaḥsnānaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 3.254 ||
kaurme tatraiva prātaḥsnānaṃ praśaṃsanti dṛṣṭādṛṣṭakaraṃ hi tat | prātaḥsnānena pāpāni pūyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 3.255 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe ca prātaḥsnānātyataḥ śudhyetkāyo'yaṃ malinaḥ sadā | chidrito navabhiśchidraiḥ sravatyeva divāniśam || 3.256 || utsāhamedhāsaubhāgyarūpasampatpravartakam | manaḥprasannatāhetuḥ prātaḥsnānaṃ praśasyate || 3.257 || prātaḥ prātastu yatsnānaṃ saṃjāte cāruṇodvaye | prājāpatyasamaṃ prāhustanmahāghavighātakṛt || 3.258 || prātaḥsnānaṃ haretpāpamalakṣmīṃ glānimeva ca | aśucitvaṃ ca duḥsvapnaṃ tuṣṭiṃ puṣṭiṃ prayacchati || 3.259 || nopasarpanti vai duṣṭāḥ prātaḥsnāyijanaṃ kvacit | dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalaṃ tasmātprātaḥsnānaṃ samācaret || 3.260 || snānamātraṃ tathā prātaḥsnānaṃ cātra niyojitam | yadyapyanyo'nyamilite pṛthagjñeye tathāpyamū || 3.261 ||
atha snānavidhiḥ
atha tīrthagatastatra dhautavastraṃ kuśāṃstathā | mṛttikāṃ ca taṭe nyasya snāyātsvasvavidhānataḥ || 3.262 || adhautena tu vastreṇa nityanaimittikīṃ kriyām | kurvanna phalamāpnoti kṛtā cenniṣphalā bhavet || 3.263 || dhautāṅghripāṇirācāntaḥ kṛtvā saṅkalpamādarāt | gaṅgādismaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tīrthāyārghyaṃ samarpayet || 3.264 || sāgarasvananirghoṣadaṇḍahastāsurāntaka | jagatsraṣṭarjaganmardinnamāmi tvāṃ sureśvara || 3.265 || imaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya tīrthasnānaṃ samācaret | anyathā tatphalasyārdhaṃ tīrtheśo harati svayam || 3.266 || natvātha tīrthaṃ snānārthamanujñāṃ prārthayedimām | devadeva jagannātha śaṅkhacakragadādhara | dehi viṣṇo mamānujñāṃ tava tīrthaniṣevaṇe || 3.267 || | iti | vidhivanmṛdamādāya tīrthatoye praviśya ca | pravāhābhimukho nadyāṃ syādanyatrārkasammukhaḥ || 3.268 || digbandhaṃ vidhinācarya tīrthāni parikalpya ca | āvāhayedbhagavatīṃ gaṅgāmādityamaṇḍalāt || 3.269 || darbhapāṇiḥ kṛtaprāṇāyāmaḥ kṛṣṇapadāmbujam | dhyātvā tannāma saṅkīrtya nimajjetpuṇyavāriṇi || 3.270 || ācamya mūlamantraṃ ca saprāṇāyāmakaṃ japan | kṛṣṇaṃ dhyāyan jale bhūyo nimajjya snānamācaret || 3.271 || kṛtvāghamarṣaṇāntaṃ ca nāmabhiḥ keśavādibhiḥ | tatra dvādaśadhā toye nimajjya snānamācaret || 3.272 || tatra viśeṣaḥ
śrīnāradapañcarātre prasiddheṣu ca tīrtheṣu yadyanyasyābhidhāṃ smaret | snātakaṃ taṃ tu tattīrthamabhiśapya kṣaṇādvrajet || 3.273 || | iti | iti vaidikatāntrikamiśrito vidhiḥ |
pādme vaiśākhyamāhātmye śrīnāradāmbarīṣasaṃvāde evamuccārya tattīrthe pādau prakṣālya vāgyataḥ | smarannārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ snānaṃ kuryādvidhānataḥ || 3.274 || tīrthaṃ prakalpayeddhīmānmūlamantramimaṃ paṭhan | auṃ namo nārāyaṇāya mūlamantra udāhṛtaḥ || 3.275 || darbhapāṇistu vidhivadācāntaḥ praṇato bhuvi | caturhastasamāyuktaṃ caturasraṃ samantataḥ || 3.276 || prakalpyāvāhayedgaṅgāṃ mantreṇānena mānavaḥ | viṣṇupādaprasūtāsi vaiṣṇavī viṣṇudevatā | trāhi nastvenasastasmādājanmamaraṇāntikāt || 3.277 || | ityādi |
saptavārābhijaptena karasampuṭayojite | mūrdhni kṛtvā jalaṃ bhūpaścaturvā pañca sapta vā | snānaṃ kṛtvā mṛdā tadvadāmantrya tu vidhānataḥ || 3.278 || aśvakrānte rathakrānte viṣṇukrānte vasundhare | mṛttike hara me pāpaṃ yanmayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam || 3.279 || uddhṛtāsi varāheṇa viṣṇunā śatabāhunā | namaste sarvalokānāṃ prabhavāraṇi suvrate || 3.280 || | iti | guroḥ sannihitasyātha pitrośca caraṇodakaiḥ | viprāṇāṃ ca padāmbhodhiḥ kuryānmūrdhanyabhiṣecanam || 3.281 ||
tathā ca pādme guroḥ pādodakaṃ putra tīrthakoṭiphalapradam || 3.282 || kiṃ ca viprapādodakaklinnaṃ yasya tiṣṭhati vai śiraḥ | tasya bhāgīrathīsnānamahanyahani jāyate || 3.283 ||
tathā gautamīyatantre pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni tāni tīrthāni sāgare | sasāgarāṇi tīrthāni pāde viprasya dakṣiṇe || 3.284 || | iti | śaṅkhe vasanti sarvāṇi tīrthāni ca viśeṣataḥ | śaṅkhena mūlamantreṇābhiṣekaṃ punarācaret || 3.285 || tathaiva tulasīmiśraśālagrāmaśilāmbhasā | abhiṣekaṃ vidadhyācca pītvā tatkiṃcidagrataḥ || 3.286 ||
taduktaṃ gautamīyatantre śālagrāmaśilātoyaṃ tulasīgandhamiśritam | kṛtvā śaṅkhe bhrāmayaṃstriḥ prakṣipennijamūrdhani || 3.287 || śālagrāmaśilātoyamapītvā yastu mastake | prekṣepaṇaṃ prakurvīta brahmahā sa nigadyate || 3.288 || viṣṇupādodakān pūrvaṃ viprapādodakaṃ pibet | viruddhamācaranmohādbrahmahā sa nigadyate || 3.289 ||
śrīcaraṇāmṛtadhāraṇamantraḥ
akālamṛtyuharaṇaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam | viṣṇoḥ pādodakaṃ pītvā śirasā dhārayāmyaham || 3.290 || | iti | lekhyo'grye kṛṣṇapādābjatīrthadhāraṇapānayoḥ | mahimātra tu tattīrthenābhiṣekasya likhyate || 3.291 ||
atha śrīcaraṇodakābhiṣekamāhātmyam
padmapurāṇe sa snātaḥ sarvatīrtheṣu sarvayajñeṣu dīkṣitaḥ | śālagrāmaśilātoyairyo'bhiṣekaṃ samācaret || 3.292 || gaṅgā godāvarī revā nadyo muktipradāstu yāḥ | nivasanti satīrthāstāḥ śālagrāmaśilājale || 3.293 || koṭitīrthasahasraistu sevitaiḥ kiṃ prayojanam | tīrthaṃ yadi bhavetpuṇyaṃ śālagrāmaśilodbhavam || 3.294 ||
tatraiva śrīgautamāmbarīṣasaṃvāde yeṣāṃ dhautāni gātrāṇi hareḥ pādodakena vai | ambarīṣa kule teṣāṃ dāso'smi vaśagaḥ sadā || 3.295 || rājante tāni tāvacca tīrthāni bhuvanatraye | yāvanna prāpyate toyaṃ śālagrāmābhiṣekajam || 3.296 ||
skānde kārttikamāhātmye gṛhe'pi vasatastasya gaṅgāsnānaṃ dine dine | śālagrāmaśilātoyairyo'biṣiñcati mānavaḥ || 3.297 ||
tatraivānyatra ca yāni kāni ca tīrthāni brahmādyā devatāstathā | viṣṇupādodakasyaite kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 3.298 || śālagrāmodbhvao devo devo dvāravatībhavaḥ | ubhayoḥ snānatoyena brahmahatyā nivartate || 3.299 ||
kiṃ ca sa vai cāvabhṛtasnātaḥ sa ca gaṅgājalāplutaḥ | viṣṇupādodakaṃ kṛtvā śaṅkheyaḥ snāti mānavaḥ || 3.300 ||
śrīnṛsiṃhapurāṇe gaṅgāprayāgagayanaimiṣapuṣkarāṇi puṇyāni yāni kurujāṅgalayāmunāni | kālena tīrthasalilāni punanti pāpaṃ pādodakaṃ bhagavataḥ prapunāti sadyaḥ || 3.301 ||
smṛtau ca trirātriphaladā nadyo yāḥ kāścidasamudragāḥ | samudragāśca pakṣasya māsasya saritāṃ patiḥ || 3.302 || ṣaṇmāsaphaladā godā vatsarasya tu jāhnavī | pādodakaṃ bhagavato dvādaśābdaphalapradam || 3.303 ||
tannityatā
garuḍapurāṇe jalaṃ ca yeṣāṃ tulasīvimiśritaṃ pādodakaṃ cakraśilāsamudbhavam | nityaṃ trisandhyaṃ plavate na gātraṃ khagendra te dharmabahiṣkṛtā narāḥ || 3.304 || | iti | tato jalāñjalīn kṣiptvā mūrdhni trīn kumbhamudrayā | mūlenāthāviśeṣeṇa kuryāddevāditarpaṇam || 3.305 ||
atha sāmānyato devāditarpaṇam
tacca vaidikeṣu prasiddhameva brahmādayo ye devāstān devān tarpayāmi | bhūrdevāṃstarpayāmi | bhuvardevāṃstarpayāmi | svardevāṃstarpayāmi | bhūrbhuvaḥsvardevāṃstarpayāmi || 3.306 || | ityādi |
ācāmyāṅgāni saṃmārjya snānavastrāṇyavāsasā | paridhāyāṃśuke śukle niviśyācamanaṃ caret || 3.307 || vidhivattilakaṃ kṛtvā punaścācamya vaiṣṇavaḥ | vidhāya vaidikīṃ sandhyāmathopāsīta tāntrikīm || 3.308 ||
atha vaidikī sandhyā
kaurme tatraiva prākkuleṣu tataḥ sthitvā darbheṣu susamāhitaḥ | prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyetsandhyāmiti śrutiḥ || 3.309 ||
manusmṛtau brāhmaṇāḥ śāktikāḥ sarve na śaivā na ca vaiṣṇavāḥ | yata upāsate devīṃ gāyatrīṃ vedamātaram || 3.310 || yā ca sandhyā jagatsūtirmāyātītā hi niṣkalā | aiśvarī kevalā śaktistattvatrayasamudbhavā || 3.311 || dhyātvārkamaṇḍalagatāṃ sāvitrīṃ tāṃ japedbudhaḥ | prāṅmukhaḥ satataṃ vipraḥ sandhyopāsanamācaret || 3.312 ||
kiṃ ca sahasraparamāṃ nityaṃ śatamadhyāṃ daśāvarām | sāvitrīṃ vai japedvidvān prāṅmukhaḥ prayataḥ sthitaḥ || 3.313 ||
kiṃ ca sandhyāhīno'śucirnityamanarhaḥ sarvakarmasu | yadanyatkurute kiñcinna tasya phalamapnuyāt || 3.314 || yo'nyatra kurute yatnaṃ dharmakārye dvijottamaḥ | vihāya sandhyāpraṇatiṃ sa yāti narakāyutam || 3.315 || ananyacetasaḥ śāntā brāhmaṇā vedapāragāḥ | upāsya vidhivatsandhyāṃ prāptāḥ pūrve parāṃ gatim || 3.316 ||
atha tāntrikī sandhyā
tataḥ sampūjya salile nijāṃ śrīmantradevatām | tarpayedvidhinā tasya tathivāvaraṇāni ca || 3.317 ||
tathā ca bodhāyanasmṛtau haviṣāgnau jale puṣpairdhyānena hṛdaye harim | arcanti sūrayo nityaṃ japena ravimaṇḍale || 3.318 ||
pādme ca tatraiva sūrye cābhyarhaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ salile salilādibhiḥ || 3.319 ||
atha tadvidhiḥ
mūlamantramathoccārya dhyāyan kṛṣṇāṅghripaṅkaje | śrīkṛṣṇaṃ tarpayāmīti triḥ samyaktarpayetkṛtī || 3.320 || dhyānoddiṣṭasvarūpāya sūryamaṇḍalavartine | kṛṣṇāya kāmagāyatryā dadyādarghyamanantaram || 3.321 || atha kāmagāyatrī
śrīsanatkumārakalpe ādau manmathamuddhṛtya kāmadevapadaṃ vadet | āyānte vidmahe puṣpabāṇāyeti padaṃ vadet | dhīmahīti tathoktvātha tanno'naṅgaḥ pracodayāt || 3.322 || | iti | athārkamaṇḍale kṛṣṇaṃ dhyātvaitāṃ daśadhā japet | kṣamasveti tamudvāsya dadyādarghyaṃ vivasvate || 3.323 || vidhistāntirkasandhyāyā jale'rcāyāśca kaścana | yo'nyo manyeta so'pyatra tadviśeṣāya likhyate || 3.324 || atha matāntaratāntrikasandhyāvidhiḥ
ādau dakṣiṇahastena gṛhṇīyādvāri vaiṣṇavaḥ | tato hṛdayamantreṇa vāmapāṇitale'rpayet || 3.325 || tadaṅgulīviniryātāmbhaḥkaṇairdakṣapāṇinā | mastake netramantreṇa kuryātsamprokṣaṇaṃ tataḥ || 3.326 || śiṣṭaṃ taccāstramantreṇādāyāmbho dakṣapāṇinā | adhaḥ kṣipetpunaścaivamiti vāracatuṣṭayam || 3.327 || punarhṛdayamantreṇādāyāmbho dakṣapāṇinā | nāsāpuṭena vāmenāghrāyānyena visarjayet || 3.328 || athāmbho'ñjalimādāya sūryamaṇḍalavartine | arghyaṃ gopālagāyatryā kṛṣṇāya trirnivedayet || 3.329 ||
sā coktā
brūyādgopījanaṃ ṅe'ntaṃ vidmahe ityataḥ param | punargopījanaṃ tadvaddhīmahīti tataḥ param | tannaḥ kṛṣṇa iti prānte prapūrvaṃ codayāditi || 3.330 || mūrdhni nyasettadaṅgāni lalāṭe netrayordvayoḥ | bhujayoḥ pādayoścaiva sarvāṅgeṣu tathā kramāt || 3.331 ||
tāni coktāni pañcabhiśca tirbhiścaiva pañcabhiśca tribhiḥ punaḥ | caturbhiśca caturbhiśca kuryādaṅgāni varṇakiḥ || 3.332 || | iti | rāsakrīḍārataṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dhyātvā cādityamaṇḍale | tatsammukhotkṣiptabhujo gāyatrīṃ tāṃ japetkṣaṇam || 3.333 ||
atha tatra jale śrībhagavatpūjāvidhiḥ
aṅganyāsaṃ svamantreṇa kṛtvāthābjaṃ jalāntare | sañcintya pīṭhamantreṇa tarpayecca sakṛtsakṛt || 3.334 || tasmiṃśca kṛṣṇamāvāhya sakalīkṛtya mānasān | pañcopacārātdattvāpsu dhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet || 3.335 || tajjalaṃ cāmṛtaṃ dhyātvā svamantreṇābhimantrya ca | aṣṭottaraśataṃ kṛṣṇottamāṅge tarpayetkṛtī || 3.336 || tataśca mūlamantreṇa vārān vai pañcaviṃśatim | abhijaptenodakenācamanaṃ vidhinā caret || 3.337 ||
atha viśeṣato devāditarpaṇam
pādme tatraiva brahmāṇaṃ tarpayetpūrvaṃ viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ prajāpatīn | devā yakṣāstathā nāgā gandharvāpsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ || 3.338 || krūrāḥ sarpāḥ suparṇāśca taravo jambhakādayaḥ | vidyādharā jaladharāstathaivākāśagāminaḥ || 3.339 || nirādhārāśca ye jīvā pāpadharmaratāśca ye | teṣāmāpyāyanāyaitaddīyate salilaṃ mayā || 3.340 || kṛtopavīto devebhyo nivītī ca bhavettataḥ | manuṣyāṃstarpayedbhaktyā ṛṣiputrānṛṣīṃstathā || 3.341 || sanakaśca sanandaśca tṛtīyaśca sanātanaḥ | kapilaścāsuriścaiva voḍhuḥ pañcaśikhastathā | sarve te tṛptimāyāntu maddattenāmbunā sadā || 3.342 || marīcimatryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum | pracetasaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca bhṛguṃ nāradameva ca | devabrahmarṣīn sarvāṃstarpayetsākṣatodakaiḥ || 3.343 || apasavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvāsavyaṃ jānu ca bhūtale | agniṣvāttāstathā saumyā bahiṣmantastathoṣmapāḥ || 3.344 ||
kavyānalau barhiṣadastathā caivājyapāḥ punaḥ | tarpayetpitṛbhaktyā ca satilokdakacandanaiḥ || 3.345 || yamāya dharmarājāya mṛtyave cāntakāya ca | vaivasvatāya kālāya sarvabhūtākṣayāya ca || 3.346 || auḍumbarāya dadhnāya nīlāya parameṣṭhine | vṛkodarāya citrāya citraguptāya vai namaḥ || 3.347 || darbhapāṇiḥ suprayataḥ pitṝn svān tarpayettataḥ || 3.348 || pitrādīnnāmagotreṇa tathā mātāmahānapi | santarpya vidhinā sarvānimaṃ mantramudīrayet || 3.349 || ye'bāndhavā bāndhavā vā ye'nyajanmani bāndhavāḥ | te tṛptimakhilāṃ yāntu ye cāsmattoyakāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 3.350 || | iti | sandhyopāsanataḥ pūrvaṃ keciddevāditarpaṇam | manyante sakṛdevedaṃ purāṇoktānusārataḥ || 3.351 ||
tathā ca pādme, snāne mṛdgrahaṇānantaram evaṃ snātvā tataḥ paścādācamya suvidhānataḥ | utthāya vāsasī śukle śuddhe tu paridhāya vai | tatastu tarpaṇaṃ kuryāttrailokyāpyāyanāya vai || 3.352 ||
ata eva śrīrāmārcanacandrikāyām niṣpīḍayitvā vastraṃ tu paścātsandhyāṃ samācaret | anyathā kurute yastu snānaṃ tasyāphalaṃ bhavet || 3.353 ||
kiṃ ca vastraṃ triguṇitaṃ yastu niṣpīḍayati mūḍhadhīḥ | vṛthā snānaṃ bhavettasya niṣpīḍayati cāmbuni || 3.354 ||
kāśīkāṇḍe api sarvanadītoyairmṛtkūṭaiścātha gorasaiḥ | āpātamācarecchaucaṃ bhāvaduṣṭo na śuddhibhāk || 3.355 || naktaṃ dinaṃ nimajjyāpsu kaivartāḥ kimu pāvanāḥ | śataśo'pi tathā snātā na śuddhā bhāvadūṣitāḥ || 3.356 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye śrīnāradāmbarīṣasaṃvāde puṇyena gāṅgena jalena kāle deśe'pi yaḥ snānaparo'pi bhūpa | ājanmato bhāvahato'pi dātā na śuddhyatītyeva mataṃ mamaitat || 3.357 ||
prajvālya vahniṃ ghṛtatailasiktaṃ pradakṣiṇāvartaśikhaṃ svakāle | praviśya dagdhaḥ kila bhāvaduṣṭo na svargamāpnoti phalaṃ na cānyat || 3.358 ||
ataeva bhaviṣyottare yasya hastau ca pādau ca vāṅmanaśca susaṃyatam | vidyātapaśca kīrtiśca sa tīrthaphalamāpnuyāt || 3.359 || aśraddadhānaḥ pāpātmā nāstiko'cchinnasaṃśayaḥ | hetuniṣṭhaśca pañcaite na tīrthaphalabhāginaḥ || 3.360 ||
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse śaucīyo nāma tṛtīyo vilāsaḥ |
caturtho vilāsaḥ śrīvaiṣṇavālaṅkāraḥ
snātvā śrīkṛṣṇacaitanyanāmatīrthottame sakṛt |
nityāśuciḥ śucīndraḥ san svadharmaṃ vaktumarhati || 4.1 ||
atha svagṛhamāgacchedādau natveṣṭadevatām |
gurūn jyeṣṭhāṃśca puṣpaidhaḥkuśāmbhodhāraketarān || 4.2 ||
tathā ca śrīnṛsiṃhapurāṇe
jale devaṃ namaskṛtya tato gacchedgṛhaṃ pumān |
pauruṣeṇa tu sūktena tato viṣṇuṃ samarcayet || 4.3 ||
atha śrībhagavanmandirasaṃskāraḥ
mandiraṃ mārjayedviṣṇorvidhāyācamanādikam |
kṛṣṇaṃ paśyan kīrtayaṃśca dāsyenātmānamarpayet || 4.4 ||
śuddhaṃ gomayamādāya tato mṛtsnāṃ jalaṃ tathā |
bhaktyā tatparito limpedabhukṣecca tadaṅganam || 4.5 ||
tathā ca navamaskandhe śrīmadambarīṣopākhyāne
sa vai manaḥ kṛṣṇapadāravindayor
vacāṃsi vaikuṇṭhaguṇānuvarṇane
karau harermandiramārjanādiṣu
śrutiṃ cakārācyutasatkathodaye || 4.6 ||
ekādaśaskandhe śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde bhagavaddharmakathane
sammārjanopalepābhyāṃ sekamaṇḍalavartanaiḥ |
gṛhaśuśrūṣaṇaṃ mahyaṃ dāsavadyadamāyayā || 4.7 ||
atha tatra saṃmārjanamāhātmyam
śrīnṛsiṃhapurāṇe
narasiṃhagṛhe nityaṃ yaṃ sammārjanamācaret |
samastapāpanirmukto viṣṇuloke sa modate || 4.8 ||
śrīviṣṇudharmottare
saṃmārjanaṃ tu yaḥ kuryātpuruṣaḥ keśavālaye |
rajastamobhyāṃ nirmuktaḥ sa bhavennātra saṃśayaḥ || 4.9 ||
pāṃśūnāṃ yāvatāṃ rājan kuryātsaṃmārjanaṃ naraḥ
tāvantyabdāni sa sukhī nākamāsādya modate || 4.10 ||
śrīvārāhe
yāvatkāni prahārāṇi bhūmisaṃmārjane daduḥ |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi śākadvīpe mahīyate || 4.11 ||
jāyate mama bhaktaśca sarvadharmasamanvitaḥ |
śucirbhāgavataḥ śuddho hyaparādhavivarjitaḥ || 4.12 ||
tato bhuktvā sarvabhogān tīrtvā saṃsārasāgaram |
śākadvīpātparibhraṣṭaḥ svargalokaṃ sa gacchati || 4.13 ||
nandanaṃ vanamāśritya modate cāpsaraiḥ saha |
nandanācca paribhraṣṭo mama karmavyavasthitaḥ |
sarvasaṅgātparityajya mama lokaṃ tu gacchati || 4.14 ||
athopalepanamāhātmyam
tatraiva
gomayaṃ gṛhya vai bhūmiṃ mama veśmopalepayet |
yāvatastu padāṃstatra samantādupalepayet |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi madbhakto jāyate tathā || 4.15 ||
samīpe yadi vā dūre yaścālayati gomayam |
yāvattasya padāgrāṇi tāvatsvarge mahīyate || 4.16 ||
śālmalau tatparibhraṣṭo rājā bhavati dhārmikaḥ |
madbhaktaścaiva jāyate sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ || 4.17 ||
yaścālepayate bhūmiṃ gomayena dṛḍhavrataḥ |
tasya dṛṣṭvānulepaṃ tu mama tuṣṭiḥ prajāyate || 4.18 ||
gośca yasyāḥ purīṣeṇa kriyate bhūmilepanam |
ekenaiva tu lepena goyonyā vipramucyate || 4.19 ||
sthānopalepane bhūme salilaṃ yo dadāti me |
tasya puṇyaṃ mahābhāge śṛṇu tattvena niṣkalam || 4.20 ||
yāvanti jalabindūni lipyamānasya sundari |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate || 4.21 ||
yāvanto bindavaḥ kecitpānīyasya vasundhare |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi krauñcadvīpe mahīyate || 4.22 ||
krauñcadvīpātparibhraṣṭaḥ sarvadharmaparāyaṇaḥ |
sarvasaṅgān parityajya mama lokaṃ ca gacchati || 4.23 ||
śrīviṣṇudharmottare
kṛtvopalepanaṃ viṣṇornarastvāyatane sadā |
gomayena śubhān lokānayatnādeva gacchati || 4.24 ||
hastapramāṇaṃ bhūbhāgamupalipya narādhipa |
devarāmāśataṃ nāke labhate satataṃ naraḥ || 4.25 ||
nārasiṃhe
gomayena mṛdā toyairyaḥ kuryādupalepanam |
cāndrāyaṇaphalaṃ prāpya viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 4.26 ||
tatraiva śrīdharmarājasya dūtānuśāsane
saṃmārjanaṃ yaḥ kurute gomayenopalepanam |
karoti bhavane viṣṇostyājyaṃ teṣāṃ kulatrayam || 4.27 ||
athābhukṣaṇamāhātmyam
śrīviṣṇudharmottare
abhyukṣaṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryātpānīyena surālaye |
sa śāntatāpo bhavati nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 4.28 ||
abhyukṣaṇaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāddevadevājire naraḥ |
sarvapāpavinirmukto vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute || 4.29 ||
sarvatobhadrapadmādīnabhijñaḥ svastikāni ca |
viracayya vicitrāṇi maṇḍayeddharimandiram || 4.30 ||
tathā ca nārasiṃhe
saṃmārjanopalepābhyāṃ raṅgapadmādiśobhanam |
kuryātsthānaṃ mahāviṣṇoḥ sojjvalāṅgaṃ mudānvitaḥ || 4.31 ||
atha maṇḍalamāhātmyam
skandapurāṇe kārttikaprasaṅge
agamyagamane pāpamabhakṣyasya ca bhakṣaṇe |
sarvaṃ tannāśamāpnoti maṇḍayitvā harergṛham || 4.32 ||
aṇumātraṃ tu yaḥ kuryānmaṇḍalaṃ keśavāgrataḥ |
mṛdā dhātuvikāraiśca divi kalpaśataṃ vaset || 4.33 ||
śālagrāmaśilāgre tu yaḥ kuryātsvastikaṃ śubham |
kārttike tu viśeṣeṇa punātyāsaptamaṃ kulam || 4.34 ||
maṇḍalaṃ kurute nityaṃ yā nārī keśavāgrataḥ |
saptajanmāni vaidhavyaṃ na prāpnoti kadācana || 4.35 ||
gṛhītvā gomayaṃ yā tu maṇḍalaṃ keśavāgrataḥ |
bharturviyogaṃ nāpnoti santateśca dhanasya ca || 4.36 ||
prāṅgaṇaṃ varṇakopetaṃ svastikaiśca samanvitam |
devasya kurute yastu krīḍate bhuvanatraye || 4.37 ||
nāradīye
mṛdā dhātuvikārairvā varṇakairgomayena vā |
viṣṇuloke'tha tatra rathaiḥ saspṛhaṃ vīkṣyate sukhī || 4.38 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
upalipyālayaṃ viṣṇościtrayitvātha varṇakaiḥ |
viṣṇuloke'tha tatrasthaiḥ saspṛhaṃ vīkṣyate sukhī || 4.39 ||
atha svastikalakṣaṇam
āgame
vidiggatacatuṣkāṇi bhittvā ṣoḍaśadhā sudhīḥ |
mārjayetsvastikākāraṃ śvetapītāruṇāsitaiḥ || 4.40 ||
tatra ca pañcarātravacanam
rajāṃsi pañcavarṇāni maṇḍalārthaṃ hi kārayet |
śālitaṇḍulacūrṇena śuklaṃ vā yavasambhavam || 4.41 ||
raktakuṅkumasindūragairikādisamudbhavam |
haritālodbhavaṃ pītaṃ rajanīsambhavaṃ kvacit |
kṛṣṇaṃ dagdhairharidyavairharitpītairvimiśritam || 4.42 ||
atha tatra dhvajapatākādyāropaṇam
tato dhvajapatākādi vinyasya harimandire |
vicitraṃ bhūṣayettacca bhagavadbhaktimānnaraḥ || 4.43 ||
atha dhvarāropaṇamāhātmyam
dhvajamāropayedyastu prāsādopari bhaktitaḥ |
tasya brahmapade vāsaḥ krīḍate brahmaṇā saha || 4.44 ||
bṛhannāradīye
yaḥ kuryādviṣṇubhavane dhvajāropaṇamuttamam |
saṃpūjyate viriñcyādyaiḥ kimanyairbahubhāṣitaiḥ || 4.45 ||
tatraivāgre ca
paṭo dhvajasya viprendra yāvaccalati vāyunā |
tāvanti pāpajālāni naśyantyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 4.46 ||
mahāpātakayukto vā yukto vā sarvapātakaiḥ |
dhvajaṃ viṣṇugṛhe kṛtvā mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 4.47 ||
āropitaṃ dhvajaṃ dṛṣṭvā ye'bhinandanti dhārmikāḥ |
te'pi sarve pramucyante mahāpātakakoṭibhiḥ || 4.48 ||
|iti|
evaṃ bṛhannāradīye khyātaṃ yaccānyadadbhutam |
dhvajāropaṇamāhātmyaṃ taddraṣṭavyamihākhilam || 4.49 ||
atha patādāropaṇamāhātmyam
dvārakāmāhātmye
kṛṣṇālayaṃ yaḥ kurute patākābhiśca śobhitam |
sadaiva tasya loke tu vāsastasya na cānyataḥ || 4.50 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
patākāṃ ca śubhāṃ dattvā tathā keśavaveśmani |
vāyulokam avāpnoti bahūnabdagaṇān dvijaḥ || 4.51 ||
dodhūyate yathā sā tu vāyunā keśavālaye |
tathā tasyāpi sakalaṃ dehātpāpaṃ vidhūyate || 4.52 ||
atha vandanamālākadalīstambhāropaṇamāhātmyam
dvārakāmāhātmye tatraiva
bhūpa vandanamālāṃ tu kurute kṛṣṇaveśmani |
devakanyāvṛtairlakṣaiḥ sevyate suranāyakaiḥ || 4.53 ||
yaḥ kuryātkṛṣṇabhavanaṃ kadalīstambhaśobhitam |
nandate cāpsaroyuktaḥ svāgataṃ tasya devarāṭ || 4.54 ||
atha pīṭhapātravastrādisaṃskāraḥ
tatra tāmrādipātraṃ yatprabhorvastrādikaṃ ca yat |
pīṭhādikaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ yathoktaṃ ca viśodhayet || 4.55 ||
tatra pīṭhasya saṃskāraḥ
nārasiṃhe
pādapīṭhaṃ ca kṛṣṇasya bilvapatreṇa dharṣayet |
uṣṇāmbunā ca prakṣālya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 4.56 ||
atha taijasādipātrāṇāṃ saṃskāraḥ
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe
uḍumbarāṇāmamlena kṣāreṇa trapusīsayoḥ |
bhasmāmbubhiśca kāmsyānāṃ śuddhiḥ plāvo dravasya ca || 4.57 ||
vāyupurāṇe ca
maṇivajrapravālānāṃ muktāśaṅkhopalasya ca |
siddhārthakānāṃ kalkena tilakalkena vā punaḥ || 4.58 ||
brāhme
suvarṇarūpyaśaṅkhāśmaśuktiratnamayāni ca |
kāṃsyāyastāmraraityāni trapusīsamayāni ca || 4.59 ||
nirlepāni tu śudhyanti kevalenodakena tu |
śūdrocchiṣṭhāni śodhyāni tridhā kṣārāmlavāribhiḥ || 4.60 ||
|iti|
atiduṣṭaṃ tu pātrādi viśodhyātithyakarmaṇe |
yuñjyāttatparivartāya prabhukarmāntarāya vā || 4.61 ||
etasya parivartena prabhave'nyatsamarpayet |
ityayaṃ sarvato loke sadācāro virājate || 4.62 ||
manuḥ
tāmrāyaḥkāṃsyaraityānāṃ trapuṇaḥ sīsakasya ca |
śaucaṃ yathārhaṃ kartavyaṃ kṣārāmlodakavāribhiḥ || 4.63 ||
śaṅkhaḥ
amlodakena tāmrasya sīsasya trapuṇastathā |
kṣāreṇa śuddhiṃ kāṃsyasya lauhasya ca vinirdiśet || 4.64 ||
kiṃ ca
sūtikocchiṣṭhabhāṇḍasya surādyupahatasya ca |
triḥsaptamārjanācchuddhirna tu kāṃsyasya tāpanam || 4.65 ||
anyatra ca
tāmramamlena śudhyena na cedāmiṣalepanam |
āmiṣeṇa tu yalliptaṃ punardāhena śudhyanti || 4.66 ||
brāhme
sūtikāśavaviṇmūtrarajaḥsvalahatāni ca |
prakṣeptavyāni tānyagnau yacca yāvatsahedapi || 4.67 ||
ata eva devalaḥ
lohānāṃ dahanācchuddhirbhasmanā gomayena vā |
dahanātkhananādvāpi śailānāmambhasāpi vā || 4.68 ||
kāṣṭhānāṃ takṣaṇācchuddhirmṛdgomayajalairapi |
mṛṇmayānāṃ tu pātrāṇāṃ dahanācchuddhiriṣyate || 4.69 ||
manuḥ
madyairmūtraiḥ purīṣairvā ṣṭhīvanaihpūyaśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śuddhyeta punaḥpākena mṛnmayam || 4.70 ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ
saṃhatānāṃ tu pātrāṇāṃ yadekamupahanyate |
tasyaivaṃ śodhanaṃ proktaṃ sāmānyadravyaśuddhikṛt || 4.71 ||
atha vastrādīnāṃ saṃskāraḥ
tatra śaṅkhaḥ
tāntavaṃ malinaṃ pūrvamadbhiḥ kṣāraiśca śodhayet |
aṃśubhiḥ śoṣayitvā vā vāyunā vā samāharet || 4.72 ||
ūrṇāpaṭṭāṃśukakṣaumadukūlāvikacarmaṇām |
alpāśauce bhavecchuddhiḥ śoṣaṇaprokṣaṇādibhiḥ || 4.73 ||
tānyevāmedhyaliptāni nenijyādgaurasarṣapaiḥ |
dhānyakalkaiḥ parṇakalkaiḥ rasaiśca phalabalkalaiḥ || 4.74 ||
tulikādyupadhānāni puṣparatnāmbarāṇi ca |
śodhayitvātape kiṃcitkarairunmārjayenmuhuḥ || 4.75 ||
paścācca vāriṇā prokṣya śucītyevamudāharet |
tānyapyatimalāktāni yathāvatpariśodhayet || 4.76 ||
śātātapaḥ
kusumbhakuṅkumāraktāstathā lākṣārasena ca |
prakṣālanena śudhyanti caṇḍālasparśane tathā || 4.77 ||
yamaḥ
kṛṣṇājinānāṃ vātaiśca bālānāṃ mṛdbhirambhasā |
gomūtreṇāsthidantānāṃ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ || 4.78 ||
śaṅkhaḥ
siddhārthakānāṃ kalkena dantaśṛṅgamayasya ca |
gobālaiḥ phalapātrāṇāmasthnāṃ syācchṛṅgavattathā || 4.79 ||
kiṃ ca
niryāsānāṃ guḍānāṃ ca lavaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca |
kusumbhakusumānāṃ ca ūrṇākārpāsayostathā |
prokṣaṇātkathitā śuddhirityāha bhagavān yamaḥ || 4.80 ||
manuḥ
adbhistu prokṣaṇaṃ śaucaṃ bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām |
prakṣālanena tvalpānāmadbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate || 4.81 ||
cailavatcarmaṇāṃ śuddhirvaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca |
śākamūlaphalānāṃ ca dhānyavatśuddhiriṣyate || 4.82 ||
prokṣaṇāttṛṇakāṣṭhaṃ ca palālaṃ caiva śudhyati |
mārjanopāñjanairveśma punaḥpākena mṛtmayam || 4.83 ||
kiṃ ca
yāvannāpaityamedhyāktādgandho lepaśca tatkṛtaḥ |
tāvanmṛdvāri cādeyaṃ sarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu || 4.84 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ
vastravaidalacarmādeḥ śuddhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ smṛtam |
atiduṣṭasya tanmātraṃ tyajecchittvā tu śuddhaye || 4.85 ||
viṣṇuḥ
mṛtparṇatṛṇakāṣṭhānāṃ śvāsthicāṇḍālavāyasaiḥ |
sparśane vihitaṃ śaucaṃ somasūryāṃśumārutaiḥ || 4.86 ||
baudhāyanaḥ
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ nāvaḥ panthāstṛṇāni ca |
mārutārkeṇa śudhyanti pakveṣṭaracitāni ca || 4.87 ||
atha dhānyādīnāṃ saṃskāraḥ
tatra baudhāyanaḥ
vrīhayaḥ prokṣaṇādadbhiḥ śākamūlaphalāni ca |
tanmātrasyāpahārādvā nistuṣīkaraṇena ca || 4.88 ||
śaṅkhaḥ
śrapaṇaṃ ghṛtatailānāṃ plāvanaṃ gorasasya ca |
bhāṇḍāni plāvayedadbhiḥ śākamūlaphalāni ca || 4.89 ||
brāhme
dravadravyāṇi bhūrīṇi pariplāvyāni cāmbhasā || 4.90 ||
śasyāni vrīhayaścaiva śākamūlaphalāni ca |
tyaktvā tu dūṣitaṃ bhāgaṃ plāvyānyatha jalena tu || 4.91 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ
tāpanaṃ ghṛtatailānāṃ plāvanaṃ gorasasya ca |
tanmātramuddhṛtaṃ śudhyetkaṭhinaṃ tu payodadhi || 4.92 ||
avilīnaṃ tathā sarpirvilīnaṃ śrapaṇena tu |
ādhāradoṣe tu nayetpātrātpātrāntaraṃ dravam || 4.93 ||
ghṛtaṃ ca pāyasaṃ kṣīraṃ tathaikṣavaraso guḍaḥ |
śūdrabhāṇḍasthitaṃ takraṃ tathā madhu na duṣyati || 4.94 ||
kiṃ ca manuḥ
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃ cana |
anidhāyaiva taddravyamācāntaḥ śucitāmiyāt || 4.95 ||
|iti|
anye'pi śuddhividhayo dravyāṇāṃ smṛtiśāstrataḥ |
apekṣyā vaiṣṇavairjñeyāstattadvistāraṇairalam || 4.96 ||
atha pūjārthatulasīpuṣpādyāharaṇam
praṇamyātha mahāviṣṇuṃ prārthyānujñāṃ tu vaiṣṇavaḥ |
samāharetśrītulasīṃ puṣpādi ca yathoditam || 4.97 ||
yacca hārītavacanam
snānaṃ kṛtvā tu ye kecitpuṣpaṃ gṛhṇanti vai dvijāḥ |
devatāstanna gṛhṇanti bhasmībhavati kāṣṭhavat || 4.98 ||
|iti|
tacca madhyāhnasnānaviṣayam, yata uktaṃ pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye
asnātvā tulasīṃ chittvā devārthaṃ pitṛkarmaṇi |
tatsarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ yāti pañcagavyena śudhyati || 4.99 ||
atha gṛhasnānavidhiḥ
svagṛhe vācaran snānaṃ prakṣālyāṅghrī karau tathā |
ācamyāyamya ca prāṇān kṛtanyāso hariṃ smaret || 4.100 ||
tato gaṅādikaṃ smṛtvā tulasīmiśritairjalaiḥ |
pūrṇe pātre samastāni tīrthānyāvāhayetkṛtī || 4.101 ||
āvāhanamantraścāyam
gaṅge ca yamune caiva godāvari sarasvati |
narmade sindhu kāveri jale'smin sannidhiṃ kuru || 4.102 ||
|iti ||
athavā jāhnavīmeva sarvatīrthamayīṃ budhaḥ |
āvāhayeddvādaśabhirnāmabhirjalabhājane || 4.103 ||
dvādaśanāmāni
nalinī nandinī sītā mālinī ca mahāpagā |
viṣṇupādārghyasambhūtā gaṅgā tripathagāminī |
bhāgīrathī bhogavatī jāhnavī tridaśeśvarī || 4.104 ||
padmapurāṇe ca vaiśākhamāhātmye
nandinītyeva te nāma vedeṣu nalinīti ca |
dakṣā pṛthvī ca vihagā viśvagāthā śivapriyā || 4.105 ||
vidyādharī mahādevī tathā lokaprasādinī |
kṣemaṃkarī jāhnavī ca śāntā śāntipradāyinī || 4.106 ||
athācamya guruṃ smṛtānujñāṃ prārthya ca pūrvavat |
kṛṣṇapādābjato gaṅgāṃ patantīṃ mūrdhni cintayet || 4.107 ||
tathā coktaṃ śrīnāradapañcarātre
svasthitaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ mantramūrtiṃ prabhuṃ smaret |
anantādityasaṅkāśaṃ vāsudevaṃ caturbhujam || 4.108 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadharaṃ pītāmbarāvṛtam |
śyāmalaṃ śāntavadanaṃ prasannaṃ varadekṣaṇam || 4.109 ||
divyacandanaliptāṅgaṃ cārahāsamukhāmbujam |
anekaratnasaṃchannajvalanmakarakuṇḍalam || 4.110 ||
vanamālāparivṛtaṃ nāradādibhirarcitam |
keyūravalayopetaṃ suvarṇamukuṭojjvalam |
sarvāṅgasundaraṃ devaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 4.111 ||
tatpādapaṅkajāddhārāṃ nipatantīṃ svamūrdhani |
cintayedbrahmarandhreṇa praviśantīṃ svakāṃ tanum |
tayā saṃkṣālayetsarvamantardehagataṃ malam || 4.112 ||
tatkṣaṇādvirajā mantrī jāyate sphaṭikopamaḥ |
idaṃ snānāntaraṃ māntrātsahasramadhikaṃ smṛtam || 4.113 ||
|iti|
sakṛnnārāyaṇetyādi vacanaṃ tatra kīrtayet |
snānakāle tu tannāma saṃsmarecca mahāprabhum || 4.114 ||
tathā ca kūrmapurāṇe
āpo nārāyaṇodbhūtāstā evāsyāyanaṃ yathaḥ |
tasmānnārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ snānakāle smaredbudhaḥ || 4.115 ||
|iti|
snāyāduṣṇodakenāpi śakto'pyāmalakaistathā |
tilaistailaiśca saṃvarjya pratiṣiddhadināni tu || 4.116 ||
athoṣṇodakasnānam
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate
āpaḥ svabhāvato medhyā viśeṣādagniyogataḥ |
tena santaḥ praśaṃsanti snānamuṣṇena vāriṇā || 4.117 ||
yamaśca
āpaḥ svayaṃ sadā pūtā vahnitaptā viśeṣataḥ |
tasmātsarveṣu kāleṣu uṣṇāmbhaḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam || 4.118 ||
yaccoktaṃ śaṅkhena
snātasya vahnitaptena tathaivātapavāriṇā |
śarīraśuddhirvijñeyā na tu snānaphalaṃ bhavet || 4.119 ||
|iti|
tattu kāmyanaimittikaviṣayam | ata evoktaṃ gargeṇa
kuryānnaimittikaṃ snānaṃ śītādbhiḥ kāmyameva ca |
nityaṃ yādṛcchikaṃ caiva yathāruci samācaret || 4.120 ||
atha tatra niṣiddhadināni
tatra yamaḥ
putrajanmani saṅkrāntau grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
aspṛśyasparśane caiva na snāyāduṣṇavāriṇā || 4.121 ||
vṛddhamanuḥ
paurṇamāsyāṃ tathā darśe yaḥ snāyāduṣṇavāriṇā |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ prāpnotīha na saṃśayaḥ || 4.122 ||
athāmalakasnānam
tatra mārkaṇḍeyaḥ
tuṣyatyāmalakairviṣṇurekādaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
śrīkāmaḥ sarvadā snānaṃ kurvītāmalakairnaraḥ || 4.123 ||
saptamyāṃ na spṛśettailaṃ nīlīvastraṃ na dhārayet |
na cāpyāmalakaṃ snāyānna kuryātkalahaṃ naraḥ || 4.124 ||
bhṛguḥ
amāṃ ṣaṣṭhīṃ saptamīṃ ca navamīṃ ca trayodaśīm |
saṅkrāntau ravivāre ca snānamālakaistyajet || 4.125 ||
yājñavalkyaḥ
dhātrīphalairamāvasyāsaptamīnavamīṣu ca |
yaḥ snāyāttasya hīyante tejaścāyurdhanaṃ sutāḥ || 4.126 ||
atha tilasnānam
tatra bṛhaspatiḥ
sarvakālaṃ tilaiḥ snānaṃ punarvyāso'bravīnmuniḥ || 4.127 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate
tathā saptamyamāvasyāsaṅkrāntigrahaṇeṣu ca |
dhanaputrakalatrārthī tilaspṛṣṭaṃ na saṃspṛśet || 4.128 ||
atha tailasnānam
tatraiva
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tailamanāyuṣyaṃ caturthīṣvapi ca parvasu || 4.129 ||
yogīyājñavalkyaḥ
daśamyāṃ tailamapṛṣṭvā yaḥ snāyādavicakṣaṇaḥ |
catvāri tasya naśyanti āyuḥ prajñā yaśodhanam || 4.130 ||
mohātpratipadaṃ ṣaṣṭhīṃ kuhūṃ riktātithiṃ tathā |
tailenābhyañjayedyastu caturbhiḥ parihīyate || 4.131 ||
pañcadaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ saptamyāṃ ravisaṅkrame |
dvādaśyāṃ saptamīṃ ṣaṣṭhīṃ tailasparśaṃ vivarjayet || 4.132 ||
anyacca
saptamyāṃ na spṛśettailaṃ navamyāṃ pratipadyapi |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃmamāvasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 4.133 ||
kiṃ ca
snāne vā yadi vāsnāne pakkatailaṃ na duṣyati || 4.134 ||
kiṃ ca atrismṛtau
tailābhyukto ghṛtābhyakto viṇmūtre kurute dvijaḥ |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || 4.135 ||
athāṅgamalamuttārya snātvā vidhivadācaret |
nāsālagnena culukodakenaivāghamarṣaṇam || 4.136 ||
tato gurvādipādodaiḥ prāgvatkṛtvābhiṣecanam |
kāryo'bhiṣekaḥ śaṅkhena tulasīmiśritairjalaiḥ || 4.137 ||
atha tulasījalābhiṣekamāhatmyam
gāruḍe
mārjayatyabhiṣeke tu tulasyā vaiṣṇavo naraḥ |
sarvatīrthamayaṃ dehaṃ tatkṣaṇātdvija jāyate || 4.138 ||
tulasīdalajasnāne ekādaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
mucyate sarvapāpebhyo yadyapi brahmahā bhavet || 4.139 ||
tanmūlamṛttikābhyaṅgaṃ kṛtvā snāti dine dine |
daśāśvamedhāvabhṛtaṃ labhate snānajaṃ phalam || 4.140 ||
tulasīdalasaṃmiśraṃ toyaṃ gaṅgāsamaṃ viduḥ |
yo vahecchirasā nityaṃ dhṛtā bhavati jāhnavī || 4.141 ||
pādodakaṃ tāmrapātre kṛtvā satulasīdalam |
śaṅkhaṃ kṛtvābhiṣiñceta mūlenaiva svamūrdhani || 4.142 ||
tanmāhātmye coktaṃ pādme kārttikamāhātmye
dvārakācakrasaṃyuktaśālagrāmaśilājalam |
śaṅkhe kṛtvā tu nikṣiptaṃ snānārthaṃ tāmrabhājane |
tulasīdalasaṃyuktaṃ brahmahatyāvināśanam || 4.143 ||
|iti|
snānaśāṭītareṇaiva vāsasāmbhāṃsi gātrataḥ |
saṃmārjya vāsasī dadhyātparidhānottarīyake || 4.144 ||
atha vastradhāraṇavidhiḥ
tatrātriḥ
adhautaṃ kārudhautaṃ vā paredyudhautameva vā |
kāṣāyaṃ malinaṃ vastraṃ kaupīnaṃ ca parityajet || 4.145 ||
na cārdrameva vasanaṃ paridadhyātkadācana || 4.146 ||
nagno malinavastraḥ syātnagnaścārdhapaṭastathā |
nagno dviguṇavastraḥ syānnagno raktapaṭastathā || 4.147 ||
nagnaśca syūtavastraḥ syānnagnaḥ snigdhapaṭastathā |
dvikaccho'nuttarīyaśca nagnaścāvastra eva ca || 4.148 ||
śrautaṃ smārtaṃ tathā karma na nagnaścintayedapi |
mohātkurvannadho gacchettadbhavedāsuraṃ smṛtam || 4.149 ||
japahomopavāseṣu dhautavastradharo bhavet |
alaṅkṛtaḥ śucirmaunī śrāddhādau ca jitendriyaḥ || 4.150 ||
gobhilaḥ
ekavastro na bhuñjīta na kuryāddevanārcanam || 4.151 ||
trailokyasammohanapañcarātre
śuklavāso bhavennityaṃ raktaṃ caiva vivarjayet || 4.152 ||
aṅgirāḥ
śaucaṃ sahasraromāṇāṃ vāyvagnyarkenduraśmibhiḥ |
retaḥspṛṣṭaṃ śavaspṛṣṭamāvikaṃ naiva duṣyati || 4.153 ||
anyatra ca
chinnaṃ vā sandhitaṃ dagdhmāvikaṃ na praduṣyati |
āvikena tu vastreṇa mānavaḥ śrāddhamācaret |
gayāśrāddhasamaṃ proktaṃ pitṛbhyo dattamakṣayam || 4.154 ||
na kuryātsandhitaṃ vastraṃ devakarmaṇi bhūmipa |
na dagdhaṃ na ca vai chinnaṃ pārakyaṃ na tu dhārayet || 4.155 ||
kākaviṣṭhāsamaṃ hyuktamavidhautaṃ ca yadbhavet |
rajakādāhṛtaṃ yacca na tadvastraṃ bhavecchuci || 4.156 ||
kīṭaspṛṣṭaṃ tu yadvastraṃ purīṣaṃ yena kāritam |
mūtraṃ vā maithunaṃ vāpi tadvastraṃ parivarjayet || 4.157 ||
āvikaṃ tu sadā vastraṃ pavitraṃ rājasattama |
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇāṃ kriyāyāṃ ca praśasyate || 4.158 ||
dhautādhautaṃ tathā dagdhaṃ sandhitaṃ rajakāhṛtam |
śukramūtraraktaliptaṃ tathāpi paramaṃ śuci || 4.159 ||
agnirāvikavastraṃ ca brahmaṇāśca tathā kuśāḥ |
caturṇāṃ na kṛto doṣo brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā || 4.160 ||
kiṃ cānyatra
dhārayedvāsasī śuddhe paridhānottarīyake |
acchinnasudaśe śukle ācāmetpīṭhasaṃsthitaḥ || 4.161 ||
atha pīṭham
bahvṛcapariśiṣṭe
yatīnāmāsanaṃ śuklaṃ kūrmākāraṃ tu kārayet |
anyeṣāṃ tu catuṣpādaṃ caturasraṃ tu kārayet || 4.162 ||
gośakṛnmayaṃ bhinnaṃ tathā palāśapaippalam |
lohabaddhaṃ sadaivārkaṃ varjayedāsanaṃ budhaḥ || 4.163 ||
athāsanavidhiḥ
tatraiva
dānamācamanaṃ homaṃ bhojanaṃ devatārcanam |
prauḍhapādo na kurvīta svādhyāyaṃ caiva tarpaṇam || 4.164 ||
āsanārūḍhapādastu jānunorvātha jaṅghayoḥ |
kṛtāvaskthiko yastu prauḍhapādaḥ sa ucyate || 4.165 ||
|iti|
tato bhūmigatāṅghriḥ sanniviśyācamya darbhabhṛt |
ūrdhvapuṇḍrādikaṃ kuryātśrīgopīcandanādinā || 4.166 ||
tatrādāvanulepena bhagavaccaraṇābjayoḥ |
nirmālyena prasādena sarvāṇyaṅgāni mārjayet || 4.167 ||
taduktaṃ brāhme śrībhagavatā
śālagrāmaśilālagnaṃ candanaṃ dhārayetsadā |
sarvāṅgeṣu mahāśuddhisiddhaye kamalāsana || 4.168 ||
|iti|
tato dvādaśabhiḥ kuryānnāmabhiḥ keśavādibhiḥ |
dvādaśāṅgeṣu vidhivadūrdhvapuṇḍrāṇi vaiṣṇavaḥ || 4.169 ||
atha dvādaśatilakavidhiḥ
padmapurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe
lalāṭe keśavaṃ dhyāyennārāyaṇamathodare |
vakṣaḥsthale mādhavaṃ tu govindaṃ kaṇṭhakūpake || 4.170 ||
viṣṇuṃ ca dakṣiṇe kukṣau bāhau ca madhusūdanam |
trivikramaṃ kandhare tu vāmanaṃ vāmapārśvake || 4.171 ||
śrīdharaṃ vāmabāhau tu hṛṣīkeśaṃ tu kandhare |
pṛṣṭhe tu padmanābhaṃ ca kaṭyāṃ dāmodaraṃ nyaset || 4.172 ||
tatprakṣālanatoyaṃ tu vāsudeveti mūrdhani || 4.173 ||
kiṃ ca
ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ lalāṭe tu sarveṣāṃ prathamaṃ smṛtam |
lalāṭādikrameṇaiva dhāraṇaṃ tu vidhīyate || 4.174 ||
|iti|
evaṃ nyāsaṃ samācarya sampradāyānusārataḥ |
nyasetkirīṭamantraṃ ca mūrdhni sarvārthasiddhaye || 4.175 ||
atha kirīṭamantraḥ
oṃ śrīkirīṭakeyūrahāramakarakuṇḍalacakraśaṅkhagadāpadmahastapītāmbaradhara śrīvatsāṅkitavakṣaḥsthala śrībhūmisahitasvātmajyotirdīptikarāya sahasrādityatejase namo namaḥ || 4.176 ||
|iti|
athordhvapuṇḍranityatā
pādme śrībhagavaduktau
matpriyārthaṃ śubhārthaṃ vā rakṣārthe caturānana |
matpūjāhomakāle ca sāyaṃ prātaḥ samāhitaḥ |
madbhakto dhārayennityamūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ bhayāpaham || 4.177 ||
tatraiva śrīnāradoktau
yajño dānaṃ tapo homaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛtarpaṇam |
vyarthaṃ bhavati tatsarvamūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ vinā kṛtam || 4.178 ||
tatraivottarakhaṇḍe
ūrdhvapuṇḍrairvihīnastu kiṃcitkarma karoti yaḥ |
iṣṭāpūrtādikaṃ sarvaṃ niṣphalaṃ syānna saṃśayaḥ || 4.179 ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍrairvihīnastu sandhyākarmādikaṃ caret |
tatsarvaṃ rākṣasaṃ nityaṃ narakaṃ cādhigacchati || 4.180 ||
anyacca
ūrdhvapuṇḍre tripuṇḍraṃ yaḥ kurute narādhamaḥ |
bhaṅktvā viṣṇugṛhaṃ puṇḍraṃ sa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam || 4.181 ||
ata eva pādme śrīnāradoktau
yaccharīraṃ manuṣyāṇāmūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ vinā kṛtam |
draṣṭavyaṃ naiva tattāvatśmaśānasadṛśaṃ bhavet || 4.182 ||
padmapurāṇe
ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ mṛdā saumyaṃ lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate |
sa cāṇḍālo'pi śuddhātmā pūjya eva na saṃśayaḥ || 4.183 ||
skānde
tiryakpuṇḍraṃ na kurvīta samprāpte maraṇe'pi ca |
naivānyannāma ca brūyātpumānnārārāyaṇādṛte || 4.184 ||
dhārayedviṣṇunirmālyaṃ dhūpaśeṣaṃ vilepanam |
vaiṣṇavaṃ kārayetpuṇḍraṃ gopīcandanasambhavam || 4.185 ||
tatraiva kārttikaprasaṅge
yasyordhvapuṇḍraṃ dṛśyeta lalāṭe no narasya hi |
taddarśanaṃ na kartavyaṃ dṛṣṭvā sūryaṃ nirīkṣayet || 4.186 ||
anyatrāpi
vaiṣṇavānāṃ brāhmaṇānāmūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ vidhīyate |
anyeṣāṃ tu tripuṇḍraṃ syāditi brahmavido viduḥ || 4.187 ||
tripuṇḍraṃ yasya viprasya ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ na dṛśyate |
taṃ spṛṣṭvāpyathavā dṛṣṭvā sacelaṃ snānamācaret || 4.188 ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍre na kurvīta vaiṣṇavānāṃ tripuṇḍrakam |
kṛtatripuṇḍramartyasya kriyā na prītaye hareḥ || 4.189 ||
ataevottarakhaṇḍe
aśvatthapatrasaṅkāśo veṇupatrākṛtistathā |
padmakuṭmalasaṅkāśo mohanaṃ tritayaṃ smṛtam || 4.190 ||
athordhvapuṇḍramāhātmyam
skānde kārttikaprasaṅge
ūrdhvapuṇḍro mṛdā śubhro lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate |
caṇḍālo'pi viśuddhātmā yāti brahaṃ sanātanam || 4.191 ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍre sthitā lakṣmīrūrdhvapuṇḍre sthitaṃ yaśaḥ |
ūrdhvapuṇḍre sthitā muktirūrdhvapuṇḍre sthito hariḥ || 4.192 ||
padmapurāṇe
ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ mudā saumyaṃ lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate |
sa cāṇḍālo'pi śuddhātmā pūjya eva na saṃśayaḥ || 4.193 ||
tatraivottarakhaṇḍe śivomāsaṃvāde
ūrdhvapuṇḍrasya madhye tu viśāle sumanohare |
lakṣmyā sārdhaṃ samāsīno devadevo janārdanaḥ || 4.194 ||
tasmādyasya śarīre tu ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ dhṛto bhavet |
tasya dehaṃ bhagavato vimalaṃ mandiraṃ śubham || 4.195 ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍradharo vipraḥ sarvalokeṣu pūjitaḥ |
vimānavaramāruhya yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam || 4.196 ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍradharaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
namaskṛtvāthavā bhaktyā sarvadānaphalaṃ labhet || 4.197 ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍradharaṃ vipraṃ yaḥ śrāddhe bhojayiṣyati |
ākalpakoṭipitarastasya tṛptā na saṃśayaḥ || 4.198 ||
ūrdhvapuṇḍradharo yastu kuryācchrāddhaṃ śubhānane |
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi gayāśrāddhaphalaṃ labhet || 4.199 ||
yajñadānatapaścaryājapahomādikaṃ ca yat |
ūrdhvapuṇḍradharaḥ kuryāttasya puṇyamanantakam || 4.200 ||
śrībrahmāṇḍapurāṇe
aśucirvāpya nācāro manasā pāpamācaran |
śucireva bhavennityamūrdhvapuṇḍrāṅkito naraḥ || 4.201 ||
tatraiva śrībhagavadvacanam
ūrdhvapūṇḍradharo martyo mriyate yatra kutracit |
śvapāko'pi vimānastho mama loke mahīyate || 4.202 ||
ūrdhvapūṇḍradharo martyo gṛhe yasyānnamaśnute |
tadā viṃśatkulaṃ tasya narakāduddharāmyahaṃ || 4.203 ||
athohvapūṇḍrermāṇavidhiḥ
śrībrahmāṇḍapurāṇe
vīkṣyādarśe jale vāpi yo vidadhyātprayatnataḥ |
ūrdhvapūṇḍraṃ mahābhāga sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 4.204 ||
daśāṅgulapramāṇaṃ tu uttamottamamucyate |
navāṅgulaṃ madhyamaṃ syādaṣṭāṅgulamataḥ param || 4.205 ||
etairaṅgulibhedaistu kārayenna nakhaiḥ spṛśet || 4.206 ||
padmapurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe tatraiva
ekāntino mahābhāgāḥ sarvabhūtahite ratāḥ |
sāntarālaṃ prakurvīran puṇḍraṃ haripadākṛtim || 4.207 ||
śyāmaṃ śāntikaraṃ proktaṃ raktaṃ vaśyakaraṃ tathā |
śrīkaraṃ pītamityāhuḥ śvetaṃ mokṣakaraṃ śubham || 4.208 ||
vartulaṃ tiryagachidraṃ hrasvaṃ dīrghaṃ tataṃ tanum |
vakraṃ virūpaṃ baddhāgraṃ chinnamūlaṃ padacyutam || 4.209 ||
aśubhaṃ rūkṣamāsaktaṃ tathā nāṅgulikalpitam |
vigandhamavasahyaṃ ca puṇḍramāhuranarthakam || 4.210 ||
ārabhya nāsikāmūlaṃ lalāṭāntaṃ likhenmṛdā |
nāsikāyāstrayo bhāgā nāsāmūlaṃ pracakṣyate || 4.211 ||
samārabhya bhruvormadhyamantarālaṃ prakalpayet || 4.212 ||
tatraiva
nirantarālaṃ yaḥ kuryādūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ dvijādhamaḥ |
sa hi tatra sthitaṃ viṣṇuṃ śriyaṃ caiva vyapohati || 4.213 ||
acchidramūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ tu ye kurvanti dvijādhamāḥ |
teṣāṃ lalāṭe satataṃ śunaḥ pādo na saṃśayaḥ || 4.214 ||
tasmācchidrānvitaṃ puṇḍraṃ daṇḍākāraṃ suśobhanam |
viprāṇāṃ satataṃ kāryaṃ strīṇāṃ ca śubhadarśane || 4.215 ||
harimandiralakṣaṇam
nāsādikeśaprayantamūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ suśobhanam |
madhye chidrasamāyuktaṃ tadvidyādharimandiram || 4.216 ||
vāmapārśve sthito brahmā dakṣiṇe ca sadāśivaḥ |
madhye viṣṇuṃ vijānīyāttasmānmadhyaṃ na lepayet || 4.217 ||
vāyupurāṇe sevāparādhe
adhṛtvā cordhvapuṇḍraṃ ca hareḥ pūjāṃ karoti yaḥ |
tiryakpuṇḍradharo yastu yajeddevaṃ janārdanam || 4.218 ||
accidreṇordhvapuṇḍreṇa bhasmanā tiryagaṅginā |
adhṛtvā śaṅkhacakre ca cetyādinā doṣa uktaḥ || 4.219 ||
śrutiśca, yajurvedasya hiraṇyakeśauryaśakhāyām
hareḥ padākrāntimātmani dhārayati yaḥ |
sa parasya priyo bhavati sa puṇyavān |
madhye chidramūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ
yo dhārayati sa muktibhāgbhavati || 4.220 ||
|iti|
tilakaracanāṅguliniyamaḥ
smṛtiḥ
anāmikā kāmadoktā madhyamāyuskarī bhavet |
aṅguṣṭhaḥ puṣṭidaḥ proktastarjanī mokṣadāyinī || 4.221 ||
athordhvapuṇḍramṛttikāḥ
padmapurāṇe tatraiva
parvatāgre nadītīre bilvamūle jalāśaye |
sindhutīre ca valmīke harikṣetre viśeṣataḥ || 4.222 ||
viṣṇoḥ snānodakaṃ yatra pravāhayati nityaśaḥ |
puṇḍrāṇāṃ dhāraṇārthāya gṛhṇīyāttatra mṛttikām || 4.223 ||
śrīraṅge veṅkaṭādrau ca śrīkūrme dvārake śubhe |
prayāge nārasiṃhādrau vārāhe tulasīvane || 4.224 ||
gṛhītvā mṛttikāṃ bhaktyā viṣṇupādajalaiḥ saha |
dhṛtvā puṇḍrāṇi cāṅgeṣu viṣṇusāyujyamāpnuyāt || 4.225 ||
tatraiva
yattu divyaṃ harikṣetre tasyaiva mṛdamāharet || 4.226 ||
uktaṃ ca pādme śrīnāradena
brahmaghno vātha goghno vā haitukaḥ sarvapāpakṛt |
gopīcandanasamparkātpūto bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 4.227 ||
gopīcandanakhaṇḍaṃ tu yo dadātii hi vaiṣṇave |
kulamekottaraṃ tena sambhavettāritaṃ śatam || 4.228 ||
skandapurāṇe
śaṅkhacakrāṅkitatanuḥ śirasā mañjarīdharaḥ |
gopīcandanaliptāṅgo dṛṣṭaścettadaghaṃ kṛtaḥ || 4.229 ||
gopīmṛttulasī śaṅkhaḥ śālagrāmaḥ sacakrakaḥ |
gṛhe'pi yasya pañcaite tasya pāpabhayaṃ kutaḥ || 4.230 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe ca śrīyamena
śrīkhaṇḍe kva sa āmodaḥ svarovarṇaḥ kva tādṛśaḥ |
tatpāvitryaṃ kva vai tīrthe śrīgopīcandane yathā || 4.231 ||
atha gopīcandanordhvapuṇḍramāhātmyam
uktaṃ ca garuḍapurāṇe nāradena
yo mṛttikāṃ dvāravatīsamudbhavāṃ
kare samādāya lalāṭapaṭṭake |
karoti nityaṃ tvatha cordhvapuṇḍraṃ
kriyāphalaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ sadā bhavet || 4.232 ||
kriyāvihīnaṃ yadi mantrahīnaṃ
śraddhāvihīnaṃ yadi kālavarjitam |
kṛtvā lalāṭe yadi gopīcandanaṃ
prāpnoti tatkarmaphalaṃ sadā kṣayam || 4.233 ||
gopīcandanasambhavaṃ suruciraṃ puṇḍraṃ lalāṭe dvijo
nityaṃ dhārayate yadi dvijapate rātrau divā sarvadā |
yatpuṇyaṃ kurujāṅgale ravigrahe mādhyāṃ prayāge tathā
tatprāpnoti khagendra viṣṇusadane santiṣṭhate devavat || 4.234 ||
yasmin gṛhe tiṣṭhati gopīcandanaṃ
bhaktyā lalāṭe manujo bibharti |
tasmin gṛhe tiṣṭhati sarvadā hariḥ
śraddhānvitaḥ kaṃsahā vihaṅgama || 4.235 ||
yo dhārayetkṛṣṇapurīsamudbhavāṃ
sadā pavitrāṃ kalikilbiṣāpahām |
nityaṃ lalāṭe harimantrasaṃyutāṃ
yamaṃ na paśyedyadi pāpasaṃvṛtaḥ || 4.236 ||
yasyāntakāle khaga gopīcandanaṃ
bāhvorlalāṭE hṛdi mastake ca |
prayāti lokaṃ kamalālayaṃ prabhor
gobālaghātī yadi brahmahā bhavet || 4.237 ||
grahā na pīḍanti na rakṣasāṃ gaṇāḥ
yakṣāḥ piśācoragabhūtadānavāḥ |
lalāṭapaṭṭe khaga gopīcandanaṃ
santiṣṭhate yasya hareḥ prasādataḥ || 4.238 ||
padmapurāṇe śrīgotamena
ambarīṣa mahāghasya kṣayārthe kuru vīkṣaṇam |
lalāṭe yaiḥ kṛtaṃ nityaṃ gopīcandanapuṇḍrakam || 4.239 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe ca śrīyamena
dūtāḥ śṛṇuta yadbhālaṃ gopīcandanalāñchitam |
jvaladindhanavatso'pi tyājyo dūre prayatnataḥ || 4.240 ||
|iti|
atha tasyopari śrīmattulasīmūlamṛtsnayā |
tatraiva vaiṣṇavaiḥ kāryamūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ manoharam || 4.241 ||
atha śrītulasīmūlamṛttikāpuṇḍramāhātmyam
tanmṛdaṃ gṛhya yaiḥ puṇḍraṃ lalāṭe dhāritaṃ naraiḥ |
pramāṇakaṃ kṛtaṃ taistu mokṣāya gamanaṃ prati || 4.242 ||
tatraiva ca kārttikamāhātmye brahmanāradasaṃvāde
tulasīmṛttikāpuṇḍraṃ lalāṭe yasya dṛśyate |
dehaṃ na spṛśati pāpaṃ kriyamāṇastu nārada || 4.243 ||
garuḍapurāṇe
tulasīmṛttikāpuṇḍraṃ yaḥ karoti dine dine |
tasyāvalokanātpāpaṃ yāti varṣakṛtaṃ nṛṇām || 4.244 ||
|iti|
tasyopariṣṭhādbhagavannirmālyamanulepanam |
tathaiva dhāryamevaṃ hi trividhaṃ tilakaṃ smṛtam || 4.245 ||
tato nārāyaṇīṃ mudrāṃ dhārayetprītaye hareḥ |
matsyakūrmādicihnāni cakrādīnyāyudhāni ca || 4.246 ||
atha mudrādhāraṇanityatā
smṛtau
aṅkitaḥ śaṅkhacakrābhyāmubhayorbāhumūlayoḥ |
samarcayeddhariṃ nityaṃ nānyathā pūjanaṃ bhavet || 4.247 ||
ādityapurāṇe
śaṅkhacakrordhvapuṇḍrādirahitaṃ brāhmaṇādhamam |
gardabhaṃ tu samāropya rājā rāṣṭrātpravāsayet || 4.248 ||
gāruḍe śrībhagavaduktau
sarvakarmādhikāraśca śucīnāmeva coditaḥ |
śucitvaṃ ca vijānīyānmadīyāyudhadhāraṇāt || 4.249 ||
pādme cottarakhaṇḍe
śaṅkhacakrādibhiścihnairvipraḥ priyatamairhareḥ |
rahitaḥ sarvadharmebhyaḥ pracyuto narakaṃ vrajet || 4.250 ||
śrutau ca yajuḥkaṭhaśākhāyām
dhṛtordhvapuṇḍraḥ kutacakradhārī
viṣṇuṃ paraṃ dhyāyati yo mahātmā |
svareṇa mantreṇa sadā hṛdi sthitaṃ
parātparaṃ yanmahato mahāntam || 4.251 ||
atharvaṇi ca
ebhirvayamurukramasya cihnair
aṅkitā loke subhagā bhavema |
tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ
ye gacchanti lāṅchitāḥ || 4.252 ||
| ityādi |
ataeva brahmapurāṇe
kṛṣṇāyudhāṅkitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sammānaṃ na karoti yaḥ |
dvādaśābdārjitaṃ puṇyaṃ cāphalayopagacchati || 4.253 ||
atha mudrādhāraṇamāhātmyam
skānde śrīsanatkumāramārkaṇḍeyasaṃvāde
yo viṣṇubhakto viprendra śaṅkhacakrādicihnitaḥ |
sa yāti viṣṇulokaṃ vai dāhapralayavarjitam || 4.254 ||
tatra vāntyatra ca
nārāyaṇāyudhairnityaṃ cihnitaṃ yasya vigraham |
pāpakoṭiyuktasya tasya kiṃ kurute yamaḥ || 4.255 ||
śaṅkhoddhāre tu yatproktaṃ vasatāṃ varṣakoṭibhiḥ |
tatphalaṃ likhite śaṅkhe pratyahaṃ dakṣiṇe bhuje || 4.256 ||
yatphalaṃ puṣkare nityaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣadarśane |
śaṅkhopari kṛte padme tatphalaṃ samavāpnuyāt || 4.257 ||
vāme bhuje gadā yasya likhitā dṛśyate kalau |
gadādharo gayāpuṇyaṃ pratyahaṃ tasya yacchati || 4.258 ||
yaccānandapure proktaṃ cakrasvāmīsamīpataḥ |
gadādhollikhite cakre tatphalaṃ kṛṣṇadarśane || 4.259 ||
śrībhagavaduktau
yaḥ punaḥ kalikāle tu matpurīsambhavāṃ mṛdam |
matsyakūrmādikāṃ cihnaṃ gṛhītvā kurute naraḥ || 4.260 ||
dehe tasya praviṣṭo'haṃ jānantu tridaśottamāḥ |
tasya me nāntaraṃ kiṃcitkartavyaṃ śreya icchatā || 4.261 ||
mamāvatāracihnāni dṛśyante yasya vigrahe |
martyairmartyo na vijñeyaḥ sa nūnaṃ māmakī tanūḥ || 4.262 ||
pāpaṃ sukṛtarūpaṃ tu jāyate tasya dehinaḥ |
mamāyudhāni tasyāṅge likhitāni kalau yuge || 4.263 ||
ubhābhyāmapi cihnābhyāṃ yo'ṅkito matsyamudrayā |
kūrmayāpi svakaṃ tejo nikṣiptaṃ tasya vigrahe || 4.264 ||
śaṅkhaṃ ca padmaṃ ca gadāṃ rathāṅgaṃ
matsyaṃ ca kūrmaṃ racitaṃ svadehe |
karoti nityaṃ sukṛtasya vṛddhiṃ
pāpakṣayaṃ janmaśatārjitasya || 4.265 ||
tatraiva śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
kṛṣṇaśastrāṅkakavacaṃ durbhedyaṃ devadānavaiḥ |
adṛśyaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ śatrūṇāṃ rakṣasāmapi || 4.266 ||
lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī durgā sāvitrī harivallabhā |
nityaṃ tasya vaseddehe yasya śaṅkhāṅkitā tanuḥ || 4.267 ||
gaṅgā gayā kurukṣetraṃ prayāgaṃ puṣkarādi ca |
nityaṃ tasya sadā tiṣṭhedyasya padāṅkitaṃ vapuḥ || 4.268 ||
yasya kaumodakīcihnaṃ bhuje vāme kalipriya |
pratyahaṃ tatra draṣṭavyo gaṅgāsāgarasaṅgamaḥ || 4.269 ||
savye kare gadādhastadrathāṅgaṃ tiṣṭhate yadi |
kṛṣṇena sahitaṃ tatra trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 4.270 ||
trayo'gnayastrayo devā viṣṇostrīṇi padāni ca |
nivasanti sadā yasya yasya dehe sudarśanam || 4.271 ||
kiṃ ca
kṛṣṇāyudhāṅkitā mudrā yasya nārāyaṇī kare |
ūrdhvalokādhikārī ca sa jñeyastridaśāṃ patiḥ || 4.272 ||
kṛṣṇamudrāprayuktastu daivaṃ pitryaṃ karoti yaḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ pratyahaṃ cākṣayaṃ bhavet || 4.273 ||
pīḍayanti na tatraiva grahā ṛkṣāṇi rāśayaḥ |
aṣṭākṣarāṅkitā mudrā yasya dhātumayī kare || 4.274 ||
vārāhe śrīsanatkumāroktau
kṛṣṇāyudhāṅkitaṃ dehaṃ gopīcandanamṛtsnayā |
prayāgādiṣu tīrtheṣu sa gatvā kiṃ kariṣyati || 4.275 ||
yadā yasya prapaśyeta dehaṃ śaṅkhādicihnitam |
tadā tasya jagatsvāmī tuṣṭo harati pātakam || 4.276 ||
bhavate yasya dehe tu ahorātraṃ dine dine |
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmaṃ likhitaṃ so'cyutaḥ svayam || 4.277 ||
nārāyaṇāyudhairyuktaṃ kṛtvātmānaṃ kalau yuge |
kurute puṇyakarmāṇi merutulyāni tāni vai || 4.278 ||
śaṅkhādināṅkito bhaktyā śrāddhaṃ yaḥ kurute dvija |
vidhihīnaṃ tu sampūrṇaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tu gayāsamam || 4.279 ||
yathāgnirdahate kāṣṭhaṃ vāyunā prerito bhṛśam |
tathā dahyanti pāpāni dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇāyudhāni vai || 4.280 ||
brāhmye śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
viṣṇunāmāṅkitāṃ mudrāmaṣṭākṣarasamanvitām |
śaṅkhādikāyudhādikairyuktāṃ svarṇarūpyamayīmapi || 4.281 ||
dhatte bhāgavato yastu kalikāle viśeṣataḥ |
prahlādasya samo kṣeyo nānyathā kalavallabha || 4.282 ||
kiṃ ca
śaṅkhāṅkitatanurvipro bhuṅkte yasya ca veśmani |
tadannaṃ svayamaśnāti pitṛbhiḥ saha keśavaḥ || 4.283 ||
kṛṣṇāyudhāṅkito yastu śmaśāne mriyate yadi |
prayāge yā gatiḥ proktā sā gatistasya nārada || 4.284 ||
kṛṣṇāyudhaiḥ kalau nityaṃ maṇḍitaṃ yasya vigraham |
tatrāśrayaṃ prakurvanti vivadhā vāsavādayaḥ || 4.285 ||
yaḥ karoti hareḥ pūjāṃ kṛṣṇaśastrāṅkito naraḥ |
aparādhasahasrāṇi nityaṃ harati keśavaḥ || 4.286 ||
kṛtvā kāṣṭhamayaṃ bimbaṃ kṛṣṇaśastraistu cihnitam |
yo hyaṅkayati cātmānaṃ tatsamo nāsti vaiṣṇavaḥ || 4.287 ||
pāṣaṇḍapatitavrātyairnāstikālāpapātakaiḥ |
na lipyate kalikṛtaiḥ kṛṣṇaśastrāṅkito naraḥ || 4.288 ||
kiṃ ca
aṣṭākṣarāṅkitā mudrā yasya dhātumayī bhavet |
śaṅkhapadmādibhiryuktā pūjyate'sau surāsuraiḥ || 4.289 ||
dhṛtā nārāyaṇī mudrā prahlādena purā kṛte |
vibhīṣaṇena balinā dhruveṇa ca śukena ca || 4.290 ||
māndhātṛṇāmbarīṣeṇa mārkaṇḍapramukhairdvijaiḥ |
śaṅkhādicihnitaiḥ śastrairdehe kṛtvā kalipriya |
ārādhya keśavātprāptaṃ samīhitaphalaṃ mahat || 4.291 ||
kiṃ ca
gopīcandanamṛtsnāyā likhitaṃ yasya vigrahe |
śaṅkhapadmādicakraṃ vā tasya dehe vaseddhariḥ || 4.292 ||
tatraiva śrīsanatkumāroktau
yasya nārāyaṇī mudrā dehaṃ śaṅkhādicihnitam |
dhātrīphalakṛtā mālā tulasīkāṣṭhasambhavā || 4.293 ||
dvādaśākṣaramantraistu niyuktāni kalevare |
āyudhāni ca viprasya matsamaḥ sa ca vaiṣṇavaḥ || 4.294 ||
kiṃ ca
yasya nārāyaṇī mudrā dehe śaṅkhādi cihnitā |
sarvāṅgaṃ cihnitaṃ yasya sastrairnārāyaṇodbhavaiḥ |
praveśo nāsti pāpasya kavacaṃ tasya vaiṣṇavam || 4.295 ||
anyatra ca
ebhirbhāgavataiścihnaiḥ kalikāle dvijātayaḥ |
bhavanti martyaloke te śāpānugrahakārakāḥ || 4.296 ||
atha mudrādhāraṇavidhiḥ
gautamīye
cakraṃ ca dakṣiṇe bāhau śaṅkhaṃ vāme'pi dakṣiṇe |
gadāṃ vāme gadādhastātpunaścakraṃ ca dhārayet || 4.297 ||
śaṅkhopari tathā padmaṃ punaḥ padmaṃ ca dakṣiṇe |
khaḍgaṃ vakṣasi cāpaṃ ca saśaraṃ śīrṣṇi dhārayet || 4.298 ||
iti pañcāyudhānyādau dhārayedvaiṣṇavo janaḥ |
matsyaṃ ca dakṣiṇe haste kūrmaṃ vāmakare tathā || 4.299 ||
tathā coktaṃ
dakṣiṇe tu bhuje vipro vibhṛṣādvai sudarśanam |
matsyaṃ padmaṃ cāpare'tha śaṅkhaṃ padmaṃ gadāstathā || 4.300 ||
|iti|
sāmpradāyikaśiṣṭānāmācārācca yathāruci |
śaṅkhacakrādicihnāni sarveṣvaṅgeṣu dhārayet || 4.301 ||
bhaktyā nijeṣṭadevasya dhārayellakṣaṇānyapi || 4.302 ||
cakraśaṅkhau ca dhāryate saṃmiśrāveva kaiścana || 4.303 ||
śrīgopīcandanenaivaṃ cakrādīni budho'nvaham |
dhārayecchayanādau tu taptāni kila tāni hi || 4.304 ||
atha cakrādīnāṃ lakṣaṇāni
dvādaśāraṃ tu ṣaṭkoṇaṃ valayatrayasaṃyutam |
cakraṃ syāddakṣiṇāvartaṃ śaṅkhaṃ ca śrīhareḥ smṛtaḥ || 4.305 ||
gadāpadmādikaṃ lokasiddhameva mataṃ budhaiḥ |
mudrā vā bhagavannāmāṅkitā vāṣṭākṣarādibhiḥ || 4.306 ||
atha mālādidhāraṇam
tataḥ kṛṣṇārpitā mālā dhārayettulasīdalaiḥ |
padmākṣaistulasīkāṣṭhaiḥ phalairdhātryāśca nirmitāḥ || 4.307 ||
dhārayettulasīkāṣṭhabhūṣaṇāni ca vaiṣṇavaḥ |
mastake karṇayorbāhvoḥ karayośca yathāruci || 4.308 ||
atha mālādhāraṇavidhiḥ
skānde
sannivedyaiva haraye tulasīkāṣṭhasambhavām |
mālāṃ paścātsvayaṃ dhatte sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ || 4.309 ||
haraye nārpayedyastu tulasīkāṣṭhasambhavām |
mālāṃ dhatte svayaṃ mūḍhaḥ sa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam || 4.310 ||
kṣālitāṃ pañcagavyena mūlamantreṇa mantritām |
gāyatryā cāṣta kṛtvā vai mantritāṃ dhūpayecca tām |
vidhivatparayā bhaktyā sadyojātena pūjayet || 4.311 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhasambhūte māle kṛṣṇajanapriye |
bibharmi tvāmahaṃ kaṇṭhe kuru māṃ kṛṣṇavallabham || 4.312 ||
yathā tvaṃ vallabhā viṣṇornityaṃ viṣṇujanapriyā |
tathā māṃ kuru deveśi nityaṃ viṣṇujanapriyam || 4.313 ||
dāne lādhāturuddiṣṭo lāsi māṃ harivallabhe |
bhaktebhyaśca samastebhyastena mālā nigadyase || 4.314 ||
evaṃ samprārthya vidhivanmālāṃ kṛṣṇagale'rpitām |
dhārayedvaiṣṇavo yo vai sa gacchedvaiṣṇavaṃ padam || 4.315 ||
tatraiva kārttikaprasaṅge
dhātrīphalakṛtāṃ mālāṃ kaṇṭhasthāṃ yo vahenna hi |
vaiṣṇavo na sa vijñeyo viṣṇupūjārato yadi || 4.316 ||
gāruḍe
dhārayanti na ye mālāṃ haitukāḥ pāpabuddhayaḥ |
narakānna nivartante dagdhāḥ kopāgninā hareḥ || 4.317 ||
ata eva skānde
na jahyāttulasīmālāṃ dhātrīmālāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
mahāpātakasaṃhantrīṃ dharmakāmārthadāyinīm || 4.318 ||
atha mālādhāraṇamāhātmyam
agastyasaṃhitāyām
nirmālyatulasīmālāyukto yaścārcayeddharim |
yadyatkaroti tatsarvamanantaphaladaṃ bhavet || 4.319 ||
nāradīye
ye kaṇṭhalagnatulasīnalinākṣamālā
ye vā lalāṭaphalke lasadūrdhvapuṇḍrāḥ |
ye bāhumūlaparicihnitaśaṅkhacakrās
te vaiṣṇavā bhuvanamāśu pavitrayanti || 4.320 ||
kiṃ ca
bhujayugamapi cihnairaṅkitaṃ yasya viṣṇoḥ
paramapuruṣanāmnāṃ kīrtanaṃ yasya vāci |
ṛjutaramapi puṇḍraṃ mastake yasya kaṇṭhe
sarasijamaṇimālā yasya tasyāsmi dāsaḥ || 4.321 ||
viṣṇudharmottare śrībhagavaduktau
tulasīkāṣṭhamālāṃ ca kaṇṭhasthāṃ vahate tu yaḥ |
apyaśauco'pyanācāro māmevaiti na saṃśayaḥ || 4.322 ||
skānde
dhātrīphalakṛtā mālā tulasīkāṣṭhasambhavā |
dṛśyate yasya dehe tu sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ || 4.323 ||
tulasīdalajāṃ mālāṃ kaṇṭhasthāṃ vahate tu yaḥ |
viṣṇūttīrṇā viśeṣeṇa sa namasyo divaukasām || 4.324 ||
tulasīdalajā mālā dhātrīphalakṛtāpi ca |
dadāti pāpināṃ muktiṃ kiṃ punarviṣṇusevinām || 4.325 ||
tatraiva kārttikaprasaṅge
yaḥ punastulasīmālāṃ kṛtvā kaṇṭhe janārdanam |
pūjayetpuṇyamāpnoti pratipuṣpaṃ gavāyutam || 4.326 ||
yāvalluṭhati kaṇṭhasthā dhātrīmālā narasya hi |
tāvattasya śarīre tu prītyā luṭhati keśavaḥ || 4.327 ||
spṛśecca yāni lomāni dhātrīmālā kalau nṛṇām |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi vasate keśavālaye || 4.328 ||
yāvaddināni vahate dhātrīmālāṃ kalau naraḥ |
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi vaikuṇṭhe vasatirbhavet || 4.329 ||
mālāyugmaṃ vahedyastu dhātrītulasisaṃbhavam |
vahate kaṇṭhadeśe tu kalpakoṭiṃ divaṃ vaset || 4.330 ||
gāruḍe ca mārkaṇḍeyoktau
tulasīdalajāṃ mālāṃ kṛṣṇottīrṇā vahettu yaḥ |
patre patre'śvamedhānāṃ daśānāṃ labhate phalam || 4.331 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhasambhūtāṃ yo mālāṃ vahate naraḥ |
phalaṃ yacchati daitāriḥ pratyahaṃ dvārakodbhavam || 4.332 ||
nivedya viṣṇave mālāṃ tulasīkāṣṭhasambhavam |
vahate yo naro bhaktyā tasya vai nāsti pātakam || 4.333 ||
sadā prītamanāstasya kṛṣṇa devakīnandanaḥ |
tulasīkāṣṭhasambhūtāṃ yo mālāṃ vahate naraḥ |
prāyaścittaṃ na tasyāsti nāśaucaṃ tasya vigrahe || 4.334 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhasambhūtāṃ śiraso yasya bhūṣaṇam |
bāhvoḥ kare ca martyasya dehe tasya sadā hariḥ || 4.335 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhamālābhirbhūṣitaḥ puṇyamācaret |
pitṝṇāṃ devatānāṃ ca kṛtaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ kalau || 4.336 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhamālāṃ tu pretarājasya dūtakāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā naśyanti dūreṇa vātoddhūtaṃ yathā dalam || 4.337 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhamālābhirbhūṣito bhramate yadi |
duḥsvapnaṃ durnimittaṃ ca na bhayaṃ śastrajaṃ kvacit || 4.338 ||
atha gṛhe sandhyopāsanavidhiḥ
sandhyopāstyādikaṃ karma tataḥ kuryātyathāvidhi |
kṛṣṇapādodakenaiva tatra devāditarpaṇam || 4.339 ||
śirasā viṣṇunirmālyaṃ pādodenāpi tarpaṇam |
pitṝṇāṃ devatānāṃ ca vaiṣṇavaistu samaṃ matam || 4.340 ||
sandhyopāstau ca vaśiṣṭhavacanam
gṛhe tvekaguṇā sandhyā goṣṭhe daśaguṇā smṛtā |
śatasāhasrikā nadyāmanantā viṣṇusannidhau || 4.341 ||
atha śrīgurupūjā
pūjayiṣyaṃstataḥ kṛṣṇamādau sannihitaṃ gurum |
praṇamya pūjayedbhaktyā dattvā kiṃcidupāayanam || 4.342 ||
smṛtimahārṇave
riktapāṇirna paśyeta rājānaṃ bhiṣajaṃ gurum |
nopāyanakaraḥ putraṃ śiṣyaṃ bhṛtyaṃ nirīkṣayet || 4.343 ||
kiṃ ca, śrībhagavaduktau
prathamaṃ tu guruṃ pūjya tataścaiva mamārcanam |
kurvan siddhimavāpnoti hyanyathā niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 4.344 ||
śrīnāradena ca
gurau sannihite yastu pūjayedanyamagrataḥ |
sa durgatimavāpnoti pūjanaṃ tasya niṣphalam || 4.345 ||
śrutiṣu
yasya deve parā bhaktiḥ yathā deve tathā gurau |
tasyaite kathitā hyarthāḥ prakāśante mahātmanaḥ || 4.346 ||
ekādaśaskandhe śrībhagavaduktau
ācāryaṃ māṃ vijānīyānnāvamanyeta karhicit |
na martyabuddhyāsūyeta sarvadevamayo guruḥ || 4.347 ||
daśamaskandhe ca
nāhamijyāprajātibhyāṃ tapasopaśamena ca |
tuṣyeyaṃ sarvabhūtātmā guruśuśrūṣayā yathā || 4.348 ||
saptamaskandhe
yasya sākṣādbhagavati jñānadīpaprade gurau |
martyāsaddhīḥ śrutaṃ tasya sarvaṃ kuñjaraśaucavat || 4.349 ||
anyatrāpi
sādhakasya gurau bhaktiṃ mandīkurvanti devatāḥ |
yanno'tītya vrajedviṣṇuṃ śiṣyo bhaktyā gurau dhruvan || 4.350 ||
manusmṛtau
ajño bhavati vai bālaḥ pitā bhavati mantradaḥ |
ajñaṃ hi bālamityāhuḥ pitetyeva tu mantradam || 4.351 ||
kiṃ ca
gururbrahmā gururviṣṇurgururdevo maheśvaraḥ |
gurureva paraṃ brahma tasmātsampūjayetsadā || 4.352 ||
vāmanakalpe
yo mantraḥ sa guruḥ sākṣātyo guruḥ sa hariḥ smṛtaḥ |
gururyasya bhavettuṣṭastasya tuṣṭo hariḥ svayam |
guroḥ samāsane naiva na caivoccāsane vaset || 4.353 ||
viṣṇurahasye
tasmātsarvaprayatnena yathāvidhi tathā gurum |
abhedenārcayetyastu sa muktiphalamāpnuyāt || 4.354 ||
viṣṇudharme śrībhāgavate ca hariścandrasya
gurusuśrūṣaṇaṃ nāma sarvadharmottamottamam |
tasmāddharmātparo dharmaḥ pavitraṃ naiva vidyate || 4.355 ||
kāmakrodhādikaṃ yadyadātmano'niṣṭakāraṇam |
etatsarvaṃ gurau bhaktyā puruṣo hyañjasā jayet || 4.356 ||
pādme
piturādhikyabhāvena ye'rcayanti guruṃ sadā |
bhavantyatithayo loke brahmaṇaste viśāṃ vara || 4.357 ||
tatraiva devahūtistutau
bhaktiryathā harau me'sti tadvanniṣṭhā gurau yadi |
mamāsti tena satyena svaṃ darśayatu me hariḥ || 4.358 ||
adityapurāṇe
avidyo vā savidyo vā gurureva janārdanaḥ |
mārgastho vāpyamārgastho gurureva sadā gatiḥ || 4.359 ||
anyatra ca
harau ruṣṭe gurustrātā gurau ruṣṭe na kaścana |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena gurumeva prasādayet || 4.360 ||
brahmavaivarte
api ghnantaḥ śapanto vā viruddhā api ye krudhāḥ |
guravaḥ pūjanīyāste gṛhaṃ natvā nayeta tān || 4.361 ||
tatślāghyaṃ janma dhanyaṃ tatdinaṃ puṇyātha naḍikā |
yasyāṃ guruṃ praṇamate samupāsya tu bhaktitaḥ || 4.362 ||
upadeṣṭāramāmnāyagataṃ pariharanti ye |
tānmṛtānapi kravyādāḥ kṛtaghnānnopabhuñjate || 4.363 ||
bodhaḥ kaluṣitastena daurātmyaṃ prakaṭīkṛtam |
gururyena parityaktastena tyaktaḥ purā hariḥ || 4.364 ||
anyatra ca
pratipadya guruṃ yastu mohādvipratipadyate |
sa kalpakoṭiṃ narake pacyate puruṣādhamaḥ || 4.365 ||
pañcarātre
avaiṣṇavopadiṣṭena mantreṇa nirayaṃ vrajet |
punaśca vidhinā samyaggrāhayedvaiṣṇavādguroḥ || 4.366 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ
ye gurvājñāṃ na kurvanti pāpiṣṭhāḥ puruṣādhamāḥ |
na teṣāṃ narakakleśanistāro munisattama || 4.367 ||
yaiḥ śiṣyaiḥ śaśvadārādhyā guravo hyavamānitāḥ |
putramitrakalatrādisampadbhyaḥ pracyutā hi te || 4.368 ||
adhikṣipya guruṃ mohātparuṣaṃ pravadanti ye |
śūkaratvaṃ bhavatyeva teṣāṃ janmaśateṣvapi || 4.369 ||
ye gurudrohiṇo mūḍhāḥ satataṃ pāpakāriṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ ca yāvatsukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ syānna saṃśayaḥ || 4.370 ||
ataḥ prāggurumabhyarcya kṛṣṇabhāvena buddhimān |
tryavarānasamān kuryātpraṇāmān daṇḍapātavat || 4.371 ||
ata eva kaurme śrīvyāsagītāyām
vyatyasta pāṇinā kāryamupasaṅgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena tu dakṣiṇaḥ || 4.372 ||
|iti|
atha śrīgurupādānāṃ prāpyānujñāṃ ca sādhakaḥ |
prāksaṃskṛtaṃ harergehaṃ pravekṣyan pāduke tyajet || 4.373 ||
tathā cāpastamba
agnyāgāre gavāṃ goṣṭhe devabrāhmaṇasannidhau |
jape bhojanakāle ca pāduke parivarjayet || 4.374 ||
|iti|
tataḥ śrībhagavatpūjāmandirasyāṅganaṃ gataḥ |
prakṣālya hastau pādau ca dvirācamanamācaret || 4.375 ||
tathā ca mārkaṇḍeye
devārcanādikāryāṇi tathā gurvabhivādanam |
kurvīta samyagācamya tadvadeva bhujikriyām || 4.376 ||
|iti|
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse
śrīvaiṣṇavālaṅkāro nāma
caturtho vilāsaḥ
pañcamavilāsaḥ ādhiṣṭhānikaḥ
śrīcaitanyaprabhuṃ vande bālo'pi yadanugrahāt |
tarennānāmatagrāhavyāptaṃ pūjākramārṇavam || 5.1 ||
sanātanaḥ - śrīcaitanyāya namaḥ | bālo'jñaḥ | pakṣe śiśuḥ | nānāvidhamatānyeva grāhastairvyāptam | pūjāyāḥ kramo vidhiḥ | vidhyanukramo vā sa evārṇavastam
śrīmadgopāladevasyāṣṭādaśākṣarayantrataḥ |
likhyate'rcāvidhirgūḍhaḥ kramadīpikayekṣitaḥ || 5.2 ||
sanātanaḥ - aṣṭādaśākṣaramantreṇa yo'rcāvidhiḥ pūjāprakāraḥ sa likhyate | yadyapi daśākṣarādināpi pūjāvidhau bhedo nāsti, tathāpi nyāsādibhedāpekṣayā, tathā likhitam | gūḍho'pi kramadīpikayā śrīkeśavācāryaviracitayā īkṣitaḥ darśitaḥ san | ataḥ kramadīpikoktānusāreṇa lekhya iti bhāvaḥ
āgamoktena mārgeṇa bhagavān brāhmaṇairapi |
sadaiva pūjyo'to lekhyaḥ prāya āgamiko vidhiḥ || 5.3 ||
tathā ca viṣṇuyāmale
kṛte śrutyuktamārgaḥ syāttretāyāṃ smṛtibhāvitaḥ |
dvāpare tu purāṇoktaḥ kalāvāgamasambhavaḥ || 5.4 ||
aśuddhāḥ śūdrākalpā hi brāhmaṇāḥ kalisambhavāḥ |
teṣāmāgamamārgeṇa śuddhirna śrautavartmanā || 5.5 ||
sanātanaḥ - teṣāmāgamamārgeṇa śrautavartmanetyanena tairapi āgamikavidhinaiva pūjā kāryeti bhāvaḥ | tathā caikādaśaskandhe nānātantravidhānena kalāvapi tathā śṛṇu iti | tatra śrīdharasvāmipādāḥnānātantravidhāneneti kalau tantramārgasya prādhānyaṃ darśayati iti
atha dvārapūjā
śrīkṛṣṇadvāradevebhyo dattvā pādyādikaṃ tataḥ |
gandhapuṣpairarcayettān yathāsthānaṃ yathākramam || 5.6 ||
sanātanaḥ - tān śrīkṛṣṇadvāradevān, praṇavādicaturthyantaṃ devanāma namo'ntakamityagre lekhyatvādatraivaṃ prayogaḥśrīkṛṣṇadvāradevatābhyo namaḥ | anena mantreṇa pādyārdhyādikaṃ dattvā gandhādibhiḥ punarviśeṣeṇa pūjayedityarthaḥ | evamagre'pi saparivārebhyaḥ śrīkṛṣṇapārṣadebhyo namaḥ ityādi prayogo draṣṭavyaḥ
dvārāgre saparīvārān bhūpīṭhe kṛṣṇapārṣadān |
tadagre garuḍaṃ dvārasyordhve dvāraśriyaṃ yajet || 5.7 ||
prāgdvārobhayapārśve tu yajeccaṇḍapracaṇḍakau |
dvāre ca dakṣiṇe dhātṛvidhātārau ca paścime || 5.8 ||
jayaṃ ca vijayaṃ caiva balaṃ prabalamuttare |
dvandvaśastvevamabhyarcya dehalyāṃ vāstupuruṣam || 5.9 ||
sanātanaḥ - evaṃ sāmānyena sarveṣāmeva pūjāvidhirlikhitaḥ | idānīṃ yathāsthānaṃ yathākramamiti yallikhitaṃ, tadeva vivicya likhatidvārāgra iti dvārābhyām | tatrāpyādau dvārasyāgre yatbhūrūpaṃ pīṭhaṃ, tatra samastaparivārānvitān śrīkṛṣṇapārṣadān yajetpūjayet | anantaraṃ tasya dvārasyāgre garuḍam | yadyapi dvāraśriyo'rcanaṃ prabalārcanānantarameva kramadīpikāyāmuktam, tathāpi iṣṭveti kṭvāpratyayena caṇḍādipūjātaḥ pūrvakāla eveti bodhitam | tathaivaa sadācārāt | kiṃ ca, dvandva ityagre likhanāt, caṇḍapracaṇḍābhyāṃ namaḥ ityevaṃ yugmatvena prayogo jñeyaḥ
dvārāntaḥpārśvayorgaṅgāṃ yamunāṃ ca tato'rcayet |
tatpārśvayoḥ śaṅkhanidhiṃ tathā padmanidhiṃ yajet || 5.10 ||
sanātanaḥ - dvārasyāntaḥ abhyantare tatpārśvadvaye tayorgaṅgāyamunayoḥ pārśvadvaye
gaṇeśaṃ mandirasyāgnikoṇe durgāṃ ca nairṛte |
vāṇīṃ vāyavya aiśāne kṣetrapālaṃ tathārcayet || 5.11 ||
sanātanaḥ - āgneye koṇe gaṇeśamarcayet | tathā coktaṃ kramadīpikāyām
parivārārāḥ kṛtāḥ sarve punaḥ śrīviṣṇupārṣadāḥ |
dvārāgrābalipīṭhe'rcyāḥ pakṣīndraśca tadagrataḥ ||
caṇḍapracaṇḍau prāgdhātṛvidhātārau ca dakṣiṇe |
jayaḥ savijayaḥ paścādbalaḥ prabala uttare ||
ūrdhve dvāraśriyaṃ ceṣṭvā dvāryetān yugmaśo'rcayet |
pūjyo vāstupumāṃstatra tatra dvāḥpīthamadhyataḥ ||
dvārāntaḥpārśvayorarcyā gaṅgā ca yamunā nadī |
koṇeṣu vighnaṃ durgāṃ ca vāṇīṃ kṣetre samarcayet ||
| iti |
dvāḥśākhāmāśrayan vāmāṃ saṅkocyāṅgāni dehalīm |
aspṛṣṭvā praviśedveśma nyasyan prāgdakṣiṇaṃ padam || 5.12 ||
sanātanaḥ - vāmāṃ svavāmabhāgavartinīṃ dvāraśākhāmāśrayanīṣatspṛṣadnijāṅgāni saṃkocya dehalīmaspṛṣṭvā na laṅghayitvetyarthaḥ | dakṣiṇaṃ padaṃ prākādau nyasyan | dakṣiṇapādanyāsakrameṇetyarthaḥ | veśma śrībhagavanmandiraṃ harergehaṃ pravekṣyanniti pūrvalikhanāt | praviśettanmadhyaṃ śanaiḥ pūjako gacchet
tathā ca sāradātilake
kiṃcit spṛśan vāmaśākhāṃ dehalīṃ laṅghayan guruḥ |
aṅgaṃ saṅkocayannantaḥ praviśeddakṣiṇāṅghriṇā || 5.13 ||
sanātanaḥ - gururiti dīkṣāvidhānoktaḥ
atha gṛhapraveśamāhātmyam
tanmāhātmyaṃ ca haribhaktisudhodaye
praviśannālayaṃ viṣṇorarcanārthaṃ subhaktimān |
na bhūyaḥ praviśanmātuḥ kukṣikārāgṛhaṃ sudhīḥ || 5.14 ||
atha gṛhāntaḥpūjā
nairṛte vāstupuruṣaṃ brahmāṇamapi pūjayet |
āsanastho yajettāṃstānanyatra bhagavadgṛhāt || 5.15 ||
tatpūjāmantraścoktaḥ
praṇavādicaturthyantaṃ devanāma namo'ntakam |
pūjāmantramidaṃ proktaṃ sarvatrārcanakarmaṇi || 5.16 ||
| iti |
atha kṛṣṇāgratastiṣṭhan kṛtvā digbandhanaṃ kṣipet |
puṣpākṣatān samastāsu dikṣu tatroktamantrataḥ || 5.17 ||
atha pūjārthāsanam
tataścāsanamantreṇābhimantryābhyarcya cāsanam |
tasminnupaviśetpadmāsanena svastikena vā || 5.18 ||
tatra kṛṣṇārcakaḥ prāyo divase prāṅmukho bhavet |
udaṅmukho rajanyāṃ tu sthiramūrtiśca sammukhaḥ || 5.19 ||
tatra ca ekādaśaskandhe
āsīnaḥ prāgudagvārcetsthirāyāntvatha sammukhaḥ || 5.20 ||
athāsanamantraḥ
āsanamantrasya merupṛṣṭha ṛṣiḥ sutalaṃ chandaḥ |
kūrmo devatā āsanābhimantreṇa viniyogaḥ || 5.21 ||
pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā lokā devi tvaṃ viṣṇunā dhṛtā |
tvaṃ ca dhāraya māṃ nityaṃ pavitraṃ kuru cāsanam || 5.22 ||
athāsanavidhiḥ
nāradapañcarātre
vaṃśāśmadārudharaṇītṛṇapallavanirmitam | varjayedāsanaṃ vidvān dāridryavyādhiduḥkhadam |
kṛṣṇājinaṃ kambalaṃ vā nānyadāsanamiṣyate || 5.23 ||
anyatra ca
kṛṣṇājinaṃ vyāghracarma kauṣeyaṃ vetranirmitam |
vastrājinaṃ kambalaṃ vā kalpayedāsanaṃ mṛdu || 5.24 ||
atha viśeṣata āsanadoṣavidhiḥ
nāradapañcarātre
vaṃśādāhurdaridratvaṃ pāṣāṇe vyādhisambhavam |
dharaṇyāṃ duḥkhasambhūtiṃ daurbhāgyaṃ dāravāsane || 5.25 ||
tṛṇāsane yaśohāniṃ pallave cittavibhramam |
darbhāsane vyādhināśaṃ kambalaṃ duḥkhamocanam || 5.26 ||
kiṃ ca, śrībhagavadgītāsu
śucau deśe pratiṣṭhāpya sthiramāsanamātmanaḥ |
nātyucchritaṃ nātinīcaṃ cailājinakuśottaram || 5.27 ||
| iti |
yathokatamupaviśyātha sampradāyānusārataḥ |
śaṅkhādipūjāsambhārānnyasettattatpadeṣu tān || 5.28 ||
atha pātrāsādanam
svasya vāmāgrataḥ śaṅkhaṃ sādhāraṃ sthāpayedbudhaḥ |
tatraivārdhyādipātrāṇi nyasyecca dvāri bhāgaśaḥ || 5.29 ||
tulasīgandhapuṣpādibhājanāni ca dakṣiṇe |
vāme ca sthāpayetpārśve kalasaṃ pūrṇamambhasā || 5.30 ||
dakṣiṇe ghṛtadīpaṃ ca tailadīpaṃ ca vāmataḥ |
sambhārānaparānnyasetsvadṛṣṭiviṣaye pade |
karaprakṣālanārthaṃ ca pātramekaṃ svapṛṣṭhataḥ || 5.31 ||
atha pātrāṇi tanmāhātmyaṃ ca
devīpurāṇe
nānāvicitrarūpāṇi puṇḍarīkākṛtīni ca |
śaṅkhanīlotpalābhāni pātrāṇi parikalpayet || 5.32 ||
ratnādiracitānyeva kāñcīmūlayutāni ca |
yathāśobhaṃ yathālābhaṃ tathā pātrāṇi kārayet || 5.33 ||
kiṃ ca
haṃsapātreṇa sarvāṇi cepsitāni labhenmune |
arghyaṃ dattvā tathā raupyeṇāyurājyaṃ śubhaṃ bhavet |
tāmrapātreṇa saubhāgyaṃ dharmaṃ mṛṇmayasambhavam || 5.34 ||
vārāhe
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ kāṃsyaṃ yena dīyate bhājanam |
tān sarvān samparityajya tāmraṃ tu mama rocate || 5.35 ||
pavitrāṇāṃ pavitraṃ yo maṅgalānāṃ ca maṅgalam |
viśuddhānāṃ śuciṃ caiva tāmraṃ saṃsāramokṣaṇam || 5.36 ||
dīkṣitānāṃ viśuddhānāṃ mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ |
sadā tāmreṇa kartavyamevaṃ bhūmi mama priyam || 5.37 ||
| iti |
kecicca tāmrapātreṣu gavyāderyogadoṣataḥ |
tāmrātiriktamicchanti madhuparkasya bhājanam || 5.38 ||
tathaiva śaṅkhamevārdhyapātramicchanti kecana |
śaṅkhe kṛtvā tu pānīyaṃ sapuṣpaṃ salilākṣatam |
arghyaṃ dadāti devasyetyevaṃ skānde'bhidhānataḥ || 5.39 ||
atha maṅgalaghaṭasthāpanam
maṅgalārthaṃ ca kalasaṃ sajalaṃ karakānvitam |
phalādisahitaṃ divyaṃ nyasedbhagavato'grataḥ || 5.40 ||
tathā ca skānde
kumbhaṃ sakarakaṃ divyaṃ phalakarpūrasaṃyutam |
nyasedarcanakāle tu kṛṣṇasyātīva vallabham || 5.41 ||
| iti |
kiṃ ca
sanīraṃ ca sakarpūraṃ kumbhaṃ kṛṣṇāya yo nyaset |
kalpaṃ tasya na pāpekṣāṃ kurvanti prapitāmahāḥ || 5.42 ||
athārghyādipātrāṇi
prakṣipedarghyapātre tu gandhapuṣpākṣatān yavān |
kuśāgratiladūrvāśca siddhārthānapi sādhakaḥ |
keciccātra jalādīni dravyāṇyaṣṭau vadanti hi || 5.43 ||
yata uktaṃ bhaviṣye
āpaḥ kṣīraṃ kuśāgrāṇi dadhyakṣatatilastathā |
yavāḥ siddhārthakāścaivamarghyo'ṣṭāṅgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 5.44 ||
pādyapātre ca kamalaṃ dūrvā śyāmākameva ca |
nikṣipedviṣṇupatrīṃ cetyevaṃ dravyacatuṣṭayam || 5.45 ||
tathaivācamanīyārthaṃ pātre dravyatrayaṃ budhaḥ |
jātīphalaṃ lavaṅgaṃ ca kakkolamapi nikṣipet || 5.46 ||
madhuparkīyapātre ca gavyaṃ dadhi payo ghṛtam |
madhukhaṇḍamapītyevaṃ nikṣipeddravyapañcakam || 5.47 ||
kecittrīṇyeva pātre'smin dravyāṇīcchanti sādhavaḥ || 5.48 ||
yata uktaṃ śrīviṣṇudharme
ghṛtaṃ dadhi tathā kṣaudraṃ madhuparko vidhīyate || 5.49 ||
ādivārāhe ca
dadhisarpirmadhusamaṃ pātre auḍumbare mama |
madhunastu alābhe tu guḍena saha miśrayet || 5.50 ||
ghṛtasyālābhe suśroṇi lājaiśca saha miśrayet |
tathā dadhno'pyalābhe tu kṣīreṇa saha miśrayet || 5.51 ||
teṣāmabhāve puṣpādi tattadbhāvanayā kṣipet |
nāradastvāha vimalenodakenaiva pūryate || 5.52 ||
mūlena pātreṇaikena maṣṭakṛtvo'bhimantrayet |
kuryācca teṣāṃ pātrāṇāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ cakramudrayā || 5.53 ||
pūjāmārabhamāṇo hi yathoktāsanamāsthitaḥ |
paṭhenmaṅgalaśāntiṃ tāṃ yārcane sammatā satām || 5.54 ||
atha maṅgalaśāntiḥ
oṃ bhadraṃ karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devā
bhadraṃ paśyemākṣabhiryajatrā |
sthirairaṅgaistuṣṭuvāṃsastanubhir
vyaśema devahitaṃ yadāyuḥ || 5.55 ||
svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ | svasti naḥ pūṣā viśvadevāḥ |
svasti nastārkṣo'riṣṭanemiḥ | svasti no bṛhaspatirdadhātu || 5.56 ||
| iti |
paṭhan, oṃ śāntiḥ śrīkṛṣṇapādapadmārādhaneṣu śāntirbhavatu ||
| iti |
atha vighnanivāraṇam
apasarpantu te bhūtā ye bhūtā bhuvi saṃsthitāḥ |
ye bhūtā vighnakartāraste naśyantu śivājñayā || 5.57 ||
ityudīryāstramantreṇa vāmapādasya pārṣṇinā |
ghātaistribhirbudho vighnān bhaumān sarvānnivārayet || 5.58 ||
āntarīkṣāṃśca tenaivordhvordhvatālatrayeṇa hi |
nirasyotsārayeddivyānmāntriko divyadṛṣṭitaḥ || 5.59 ||
śrīgurvādinatiḥ
tataḥ kṛtāñjalirvāme śrīguruṃ paramaṃ gurum |
parameṣṭhiguruṃ ceti namedguruparamparām || 5.60 ||
gaṇeśaṃ dakṣiṇe bhāge durgāmagre'tha pṛṣṭhataḥ |
kṣetrapālaṃ namedbhaktyā madhye cātmeṣṭadaivatam || 5.61 ||
tataścāstreṇa saṃśodhya karau kurvīta tena hi |
tālatrayaṃ diśāṃ bandhamagniprākārameva ca || 5.62 ||
atha bhūtaśuddhiḥ
