śarīrākārabhūtānāṃ bhūtānāṃ yadviśodhanam |
avyayabrahmasamparkādbhūtaśuddhiriyaṃ matā || 5.63 ||
bhūtaśuddhiṃ vinā karturjapahomādikāḥ kriyāḥ |
bhavanti niṣphalāḥ sarvā yathāvidhyapyaniṣṭhitāḥ || 5.64 ||
tatprakāraśca
karakacchapikāṃ kṛtvātmānaṃ buddhyā hṛdabjataḥ |
śiraḥsahasrapatrābje paramātmani yojayet |
pṛthivyādīni tattvāni tasmin līnāni bhāvayet || 5.65 ||
vāmahastaṃ tathottānamadho dakṣiṇabandhitam |
karakacchapikā mudrā bhūtaśuddhau prakīrtitā || 5.66 ||
dehaṃ saṃśoṣya dagdhvedamāplāvyāmṛtavarṣataḥ |
utpādya draḍhayitvāsupratiṣṭhāṃ vidhinācaret || 5.67 ||
ātmānamevaṃ saṃśodhya nītvā kṛṣṇārcanārhatām |
vātsalyāddhṛdgataṃ kṛṣṇaṃ yaṣṭuṃ hṛtpunarānayet || 5.68 ||
tathā ca trailokyasaṃmohanatantre
nābhisthavāyunā dehaṃ sapāpaṃ śodhayedbudhaḥ |
vahninā hṛdayasthena dahettacca kalevaram || 5.69 ||
sahasrāre mahāpadme lalāṭasthe sthitaṃ vidhum |
sampūrṇamaṇḍalaṃ śuddhaṃ cintayedamṛtātmakam || 5.70 ||
tasmādgalitadhārābhiḥ plāvayedbhasmasādbudhaḥ |
ābhirvarṇamayībhiśca pañcabhūtātmakaṃ vapuḥ |
pūrvavadbhāvayeddevīm || 5.71 ||
| ityādi |
kiṃ cāgre
tatastasmātsamākṛṣya praṇavena tu mantravit |
tattejo hṛdaye nyasya cintayedviṣṇumavyayam || 5.72 ||
kiṃ vā cintanamātreṇa bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāya tām |
prāṇāyāmāṃśtataḥ kuryātsampradāyānusarataḥ || 5.73 ||
atha prāṇāyāmaḥ
recaḥ ṣoḍaśamātrābhiḥ pūro dvātriṃśatā bhavet |
catuḥṣaṣṭhyā bhavetkumbha evaṃ syātprāṇasaṃyamaḥ || 5.74 ||
virecya pavanaṃ pūrvaṃ saṃkocya gudamaṇḍalam |
pūrayitvā vidhānena svaśaktyā kumbhake sthitaḥ || 5.75 ||
tatra praṇavamabhyasyan bījaṃ vā mantramūrdhvagam |
ṛṣyādismaraṇaṃ kṛtvā kuryāddhyānamatandritaḥ || 5.76 ||
taddhyānaṃ coktam
viṣṇuṃ bhāsvatkirīṭāṅgadavalayakalākalpahārodarāṅghri
śroṇībhūṣaṃ savakṣomaṇimakaramahākuṇḍalāmṛṣṭagaṇḍam |
hastodyacchaṅkhacakrāmbujagadamamalaṃ pītakauśeyavāsaṃ
vidyotadbhāsamudyaddinakarasadṛśaṃ padmasaṃsthaṃ namāmi || 5.77 ||
kvacicca
rudrastu recake brahmā pūrake dhyeyadevatā |
śrīviṣṇuḥ kumbhake jñeyo dhyānasthānaṃ gurormukhāt || 5.78 ||
tathā hi
nābhisthāne pūrakeṇa cintayetkamalāsanam |
brahmāṇaṃ raktagaurāṅgaṃ caturvaktraṃ pitāmaham || 5.79 ||
nīlotpaladalaśyāmaṃ hṛdi madhye pratiṣṭhitam |
caturbhujaṃ mahātmānaṃ kumbhakena tu cintayet || 5.80 ||
recekanaiśvaraṃ dhyānaṃ lalāṭe sarvapāpaham |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ kuryādvai nirmalaṃ budhaḥ || 5.81 ||
| iti |
ekāntibhiśca bhagavān sarvadevamayaḥ prabhuḥ |
kṛṣṇaḥ priyajanopetaścintanīyo hi sarvataḥ || 5.82 ||
atha prāṇāyāmamāhātmyam
pādme devahūtivikuṇḍalasaṃvāde
yamalokaṃ na paśyanti prāṇāyāmaparāyaṇāḥ |
api duṣkṛtakarmāṇastaireva hatakilbiṣāḥ || 5.83 ||
divase divase vaiśya prāṇāyāmāstu ṣoḍaśa |
api brahmahaṇaṃ sākṣātpunantyaharahaḥ kṛtāḥ || 5.84 ||
tapāṃsi yāni tapyante vratāni niyamāśca ye |
gosahasrapradānaṃ ca prāṇāyāmastu tatsamaḥ || 5.85 ||
ambubinduṃ yaḥ kuśāgreṇa māse māse naraḥ pibet |
saṃvatsaraśataṃ sāgraṃ prāṇāyāmastu tatsamaḥ || 5.86 ||
pātakaṃ tu mahadyacca tathā kṣudropapātakam |
prāṇāyāmaiḥ kṣaṇātsarvaṃ bhasmasātkurute naraḥ || 5.87 ||
| iti |
nyāsān vinā japaṃ prāhurāsuraṃ viphalaṃ budhāḥ |
ato yathāsampradāyaṃ nyāsān kuryādyathāvidhi || 5.88 ||
tatrādau mātṛkānyāsaḥ
ṛṣicchandodevatādi smṛtvādau mātṛkāmanoḥ |
śirovaktrahṛdādau ca nyasya taddhyānamācaret || 5.89 ||
taccoktam
pañcāśallipibhirvibhaktamukhadoḥpanmadhyavakṣaḥsthalīṃ
bhāvanmaulinibaddhacandraśakalāmāpīnatuṅgastanīm |
mudrāmakṣaguṇaṃ sudhāḍhyakalasaṃ vidyāṃ ca hastāmbujair
bibhrāṇāṃ viśadaprabhāṃ trinayanāṃ vāgdevatāmāśraye || 5.90 ||
akārādīn kṣakārāntān varṇānādau tu kevalān |
lalāṭādiṣu cāṅgeṣu nyasedvidvān yathākramam || 5.91 ||
tacca vivicyoktam
lalāṭamukhabimbākṣiśrutighrāṇeṣu gaṇḍayoḥ |
oṣṭhadantottamāṅgāsye doḥpatsandhyagrakeṣu ca || 5.92 ||
pārśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhato nābhau jaṭhare hṛdaye'ṃsake |
kakudyase ca hṛtpūrvaṃ pāṇipādayuge tataḥ |
jaṭharānanayornyasenmātṛkārṇān yathākramam || 5.93 ||
| iti |
sānusvārān visargāḍhyān sānusvāravisargakān |
nyasedbhūyo'pi tān vidvānevaṃ vāracatuṣṭayam || 5.94 ||
atha mātṛkānyāsaḥ
kaṇṭhahṛnnābhiguhyeṣu pāyubhrūmahdyayostathā |
sthite ṣoḍaśapatrābje krameṇa dvādaśacchade || 5.95 ||
daśapatre ca ṣaṭpatre catuṣpatre dvipatrake |
nyasedekaikapatrānte sabindvekaikamakṣaram || 5.96 ||
atha keśavādinyāsaḥ
smṛtvā ṛṣyādikāṃ varṇānmūrtibhiḥ keśavādibhiḥ |
kīrtyādibhiḥ śaktibhiśca nyasettān pūrvavatkramāt || 5.97 ||
nyaseccaturthīnatyantā mūrtīḥ śaktīśca yādibhiḥ |
saptadhātūn prāṇajīvau krodhamapyātmane'ntakān || 5.98 ||
tatra dhyānam
udyatpradyotanaśataruciṃ taptahemāvadānaṃ
pārśvadvandve jaladhisutayā viśvadhātryā ca juṣṭam |
nānāratnollasitavividhākalpamāpītavastraṃ
viṣṇuṃ vande darakamalakaumodakīcakrapāṇim || 5.99 ||
atha śrīmūrtayaḥ
prathamaṃ keśavo nārāyaṇaḥ paścācca mādhavaḥ |
govindaśca tathā viṣṇurmadhusūdana eva ca || 5.100 ||
trivikramo vāmano'tha śrīdharaśca tataḥ param |
ṛṣīkeśaḥ padmanābhastato dāmodarastathā || 5.101 ||
vāsudevaḥ saṅkarṣaṇaḥ pradyumno'thāniruddhakaḥ |
cakrī gadī tathā śārṅgī khaḍgī śaṅkhī halī tathā || 5.102 ||
muṣalī ca tathā śūlī pāśī caivāṅkuśī tathā |
mukundo nandajaścaiva tathā nandī narastathā || 5.103 ||
narakajiddhariḥ kṛṣṇaḥ satyaḥ sātvata eva ca |
tataḥ śauristathā śūrastataḥ paścājjanārdanaḥ || 5.104 ||
bhūdharo viśvamūrtiśca vaikuṇṭhaḥ puruṣottamaḥ |
balo balānujo bālo vṛṣaghno vṛṣa eva ca || 5.105 ||
haṃso varāho vimalo nṛsiṃhaśceti mūrtayaḥ || 5.106 ||
atha śaktayaḥ
kīrtiḥ kāntistuṣṭipuṣṭī dhṛtiḥ śāntiḥ kriyā dayā |
medhā harṣā tathā śraddhā lajjā lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī || 5.107 ||
prītī ratirjayā durgā prabhā satyā ca caṇḍikā |
vāṇī vilāsinī caiva vijayā virajā tathā || 5.108 ||
viśvā ca vinadā caiva sunandā ca smṛtistathā |
ṛddhiḥ samṛddhiḥ śuddhiśca buddhirmūrtirnatiḥ kṣamā || 5.109 ||
ramomā kledinī klinnā vasudā vasudhā parā |
parāyaṇā ca sūkṣmā ca sandhyā prajñā prabhā niśā || 5.110 ||
amoghā vidyutetyekapañcāśatśaktayo matāḥ |
dadātyayaṃ keśavādinyāso'trākhilasampadam || 5.111 ||
amutrācyutasārūpyaṃ nayati nyāsamātrataḥ || 5.112 ||
taduktaṃ
dhyātvaivaṃ paramapumāṃsamakṣarairyo
vinyaseddinamanu keśavādiyuktaiḥ |
medhāyuḥsmṛtidhṛtikīrtikāntilakṣmī
saubhāgyaiściramupabṛṃhito bhavetsaḥ || 5.113 ||
anyatra ca
keśavādirayaṃ nyāso nyāsamātreṇa dehinaḥ |
acyutatvaṃ dadātyeva satyaṃ satyaṃna saṃśayaḥ || 5.114 ||
| iti |
yaśca kuryādimaṃ nyāsaṃ lakṣmībījapuraḥsaram |
bhaktiṃ muktiṃ ca bhuktiṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ ca labhate'cirāt || 5.115 ||
tathā coktam
amumeva ramāpuraḥsaraṃ
prabhajedyo manujo vidhiṃ budhaḥ |
samupetya ramāṃ prathīyasīṃ
punarante haritāṃ vrajatyasau || 5.116 ||
atha tattvanyāsaḥ
makārādikakārāntavarṇairyuktaṃ sabindukaiḥ |
namaḥ parāyeti pūrvamātmane nama ityanu || 5.117 ||
nāma jīvāditattvānāṃ nyasettattatpade kramāt |
nyāsenānena loko hi bhavetpūjādhikāravān || 5.118 ||
tatrādau sakale nyasejjīvaprāṇau kalevare |
hṛdaye matyahaṅkāramanāṃsīti trayaṃ tataḥ || 5.119 ||
śabdaṃ sparśaṃ tato rūpaṃ rasaṃ gandhaṃ ca mastake |
mukhe hṛdi ca guhye ca pādayośca yathākramam || 5.120 ||
śrotraṃ tvacaṃ dṛśaṃ jihvāṃ ghrāṇaṃ svasvapade tataḥ |
vākpāṇipāyūpasthāni svasvapade tathā || 5.121 ||
ākāśavāyutejāṃsi jalaṃ pṛthvīṃ ca mūrdhani |
vadane hṛdaye liṅge pādayośca yathākramam || 5.122 ||
hṛdi hṛtpuṇḍarīkaṃ ca dviṣaṭdvyaṣṭadaśādikam |
kalāvyāpteti pūrvaṃ ca sūryacandrāgnimaṇḍalam |
varṇaiḥ saha sarephaiśca kramānnyasetsabindukaiḥ || 5.123 ||
vāsudevaṃ ṣakāreṇa parameṣṭhiyutaṃ ca ke |
yakāreṇa mukhe saṅkarṣaṇaṃ nyasetpumanvitam || 5.124 ||
hṛdi nyasellakāreṇa pradyumnaṃ viśvasaṃyutam |
aniruddhaṃ nivṛttyāḍhyaṃ vakāreṇa ca guhyake |
nārāyaṇaṃ ca sarvāḍhyaṃ lakāreṇaiva pādayoḥ || 5.125 ||
nṛsiṃhaṃ kopasaṃyuktaṃ tadbījenākhilātmani |
tattvanyāso'yamacirātkṛṣṇasānnidhyakārakaḥ || 5.126 ||
tathā coktam
atattvavyāptyarūpasya tatprāpterhetunā punaḥ |
tattvanyāsamiti prāhurnyāsatattvavido budhāḥ || 5.127 ||
yaḥ kuryāttattvavinyāsaṃ sa pūto bhavati dhruvam |
tadātmanānupraviśya bhagavāniha tiṣṭhati |
yataḥ sa eva tattvāni sarvaṃ tasmin pratiṣṭhitam || 5.128 ||
atha punaḥ prāṇāyāṃviśeṣaḥ
prāṇāyāmāṃstataḥ kuryānmūlamantraṃ japan kramāt |
vārau dvau caturaḥ ṣaṭca recapūrakakumbhakaḥ || 5.129 ||
athavā recakādīṃstān kuryādvārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa |
dvātriṃśacca catuḥṣaṣṭhiṃ kāmabījaṃ japan kramāt || 5.130 ||
tathā ca kramadīpikāyām
recayenmārutaṃ dakṣayā dakṣiṇaḥ
pūrayedvāmayā madhyanāḍyā punaḥ |
dhārayedīritaṃ recakāditrayaṃ syāt
kalādantavidyākhyamātrācyukam || 5.131 ||
sanātanaḥ - tadeva kramadīpikoktyā saṃvādayan tatraiva kiṃcidviśeṣaṃ ca darśayatirecayediti | dakṣayā dakṣiṇanāḍyā, dakṣiṇaḥ vidvān janaḥ | madhyanāḍyā suṣumṇayā dhārayet | evaṃ recakapūrakakumbhakākhyaṃ trayaṃ syāt | recakādiṣu triṣu krameṇāvadhikālamāhakalāḥ ṣoḍaśa | dantā dvātriṃśat | vidyāścatuḥṣaṣṭhistattatsaṅkhyakamātrātmakamityarthaḥ | mātrā cavāmāṅguṣṭhena vāmakaniṣṭhādyaṅgulīnāṃ pratyekaṃ parvatrayasamparkakālaḥ | vāmahastena vāmajānumaṇḍalasya prādakṣiṇyena sparśakālo vā | tatrāpyaṅguliniyamo'pyuktaḥ
kaniṣṭhānāmikāṅguṣṭhairyannāsāpuṭadhāraṇam |
prāṇāyāmaḥ sa vijñeyastarjanīmadhyame vinā ||
| iti |
tatra teṣu prāṇāyāmeṣu purvaṃ recakādiṣu saṅkhyoktā | atra ca prāṇāyāmeṣviti bhedaḥ 
tatra kālaḥ saṅkhyādikaṃ ca
tatraiva
purato japasya parato'pi
vihitamatha tattrayaṃ budhaiḥ |
ṣoḍaśa ya iha samācareddineśaḥ
paripūyate sa khalu māsato'ṃ haṃsaḥ || 5.132 ||
sanātanaḥ - japasya purata ādau parataḥ ante ca iti prāṇāyāmeṣu kālaḥ | tattrayaṃ prāṇāyāmatrayamiti saṅkhyā | yo jano dinaśaḥ pratyahaṃ ṣoḍaśaprāṇāyāmānācaret, sa māsataḥ māsenaikena aṃhasaḥ pāpātparipūyate śuddho bhavatīti sāmānyataḥ phalam | paraṃ ca sarvaṃ purvaṃ likhitameva
atha pīṭhanyāsaḥ
tato nijatanūmeva pūjāpīṭhaṃ prakalpayet |
pīṭhasyādhāraśaktyādīnnyasetsvāṅgeṣu tāravat || 5.133 ||
ādhāraśaktiṃ prakṛtiṃ kūrmānantau ca tatra tu |
pṛthivīṃ kṣīrasindhuṃ ca śvetadvīpaṃ ca bhāsvaram || 5.134 ||
śrīratnamaṇḍapaṃ caiva kalpavṛkṣaṃ tathā hṛdi |
nyasetpradakṣiṇatvena dharmajñāne tato'ṃsayoḥ || 5.135 ||
ūrvorvairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ tathaivādharmamānane |
trike'jñānamavairāgyamanaiśvaryaṃ ca pārśvayoḥ || 5.136 ||
hṛdabje'nantapadmaṃ ca sūryenduśikhinān tathā |
maṇḍalāni kramādvarṇaiḥ praṇavāṃśaiḥ sabindukaiḥ || 5.137 ||
sattva rajastamaścātmāntarātmānau ca tatra hi |
paramātmānamapyātmādyādyavarṇaiḥ sabindukaiḥ || 5.138 ||
jñānātmānaṃ ca bhuvaneśvarībījena saṃyutam |
tasyāṣṭadikṣu madhye'pi navaśaktīśca dikkramāt || 5.139 ||
tāścoktāḥ
vimalotkarṣiṇī jñānā kriyā yogeti śaktayaḥ |
prahvī satyā tatheśānānugrahā navaṃ smṛtā || 5.140 ||
| iti |
nyasettadupariṣṭhācca pīṭhamantraṃ yathoditam |
ṛṣyādikaṃ smaredasyāṣṭādaśārṇamanostataḥ || 5.141 ||
jñeyāścaikāntibhiḥ kṣīrasamudrādicatuṣṭayam |
kramācchrīmathurāvṛndāvanaṃ tatkuñjanīpakāḥ || 5.142 ||
tato pīṭhanyāsaḥ
tathā ca brahmasaṃhitāyāmādipuruṣarahasyastotre
sa yatra kṣīrābdhiḥ sravati surabhībhyaśca sumahān
nimeṣārdhākhyo vā vrajati na hi yatrāpi samayaḥ |
bhaje śvetadvīpaṃ tamahamiha golokamiti yaṃ
vidantaste santaḥ kṣitiviralacārāḥ katipaye || 5.143 ||
kramadīpikāyāṃ
evaṃ hṛdayaṃ bhagavān viṣṇuḥ sarvānvitaśca bhūtātmā |
ṅe'ntāḥ savāsudevāḥ sarvātmayutaṃ ca saṃyogaṃ || 5.144 ||
yogāvadhaśca padmaṃ pīṭhātṅeyuto natiścānte |
pīṭhamahāmanurvyaktaḥ paryāpto'yaṃ saparyāsu || 5.145 ||
sanātanaḥ - tāraḥ praṇavaḥ | tato hṛdayaṃ nama iti padam | tataśca bhagavāniti viṣṇuriti ca | sarvānvitaḥ sarvaśabdayukto bhūtātmā sarvabhūtātmeti | ete trayaḥ savāsudevā vāsudevasahitāḥ pratyekaṃ ṅe'ntāścaturthyantāḥ | tataśca sarvātmanā yutaṃ saṃyogaṃ sarvātmasaṃyogamiti napuṃsakatvamārṣam | tataśca yogasyāvadhau ante padmaṃ yogapadmamiti | tadante ṅeyuktaścaturthyantaḥ pīṭhātmā | tadante ca natiḥ namaḥśabdaḥ | evamoṃ namo bhagavate viṣṇave sarvabhūtātmane vāsudevāya sarvātmasaṃyogayogapadmapīṭhātmane nama iti siddham | tathā ca śāradātilake
namo bhagavate brūyādviṣṇave ca padaṃ vadet |
sarvabhūtātmane vāsudevāyeti vadettataḥ ||
sarvātmasaṃyogapadādyogapadmapadaṃ punaḥ |
pīṭhātmane hṛdanto'yaṃ mantrastārādirīritaḥ ||
| iti |
sanatkumārakalpe ca
oṃ namaḥ padamābhāṣya tathā bhagavatepadam |
vāsudevāya ityuktvā sarvātmeti padaṃ tathā ||
saṃyogayogetyuktvā ca tathā pīṭhātmane padam |
vahnipatnīsamāyuktaḥ pīṭhamantra itīritaḥ ||
| iti |
|1445||
atha ṛṣyādismaraṇam
omaṣṭādaśākṣaramantrasya śrīnārada ṛṣirgāyatrīcchandaḥ, sakalalokamaṅgalo nandatanayo devatā, hrīṃ bījaṃ, svāhā śaktiḥ, kṛṣṇaḥ prakṛtir, durgādhiṣṭhātrī devatā, abhimatārthe viniyogaḥ || 5.146 ||
tathā ca saṃmohanatantre śivomāsaṃvāde
ṛṣirnārada ityukto gāyatrīcchanda ucyate |
gopaveśadharaḥ kṛṣṇo devatā parikīrtitaḥ || 5.147 ||
bījaṃ manmathasaṃjñaṃ tu priyā śaktirhavirbhujaḥ |
tvameva parameśāni asyādhiṣṭhātṛdevatā |
caturvargaphalāvāptyai viniyogaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 5.148 ||
athāṅganyāsaḥ
catuścaturbhirvarṇaiśca catvāryaṅgāni kalpayet |
dvābhyāmastrākhyamaṅgaṃ ca tasyetyaṅgāni pañca vai || 5.149 ||
nyasyecca vyāpakatvena tānyaṅgāni karadvaye |
tānyaṅgulīṣu pañcātha kecidvarṇān svarānapi || 5.150 ||
te coktāḥ
drāvaṇakṣobhaṇākarṣavaśīkṛtsrāvaṇāstathā |
śoṣaṇo mohanaḥ sandīpanastāpanamādanau || 5.151 ||
| iti |
kiṃ ca
namo'ntaṃ hṛdayaṃ cāṅgaiḥ śiraḥ svāhānvitaṃ śikhām |
vaṣaḍyutaṃ ca kavacaṃ huṃyugastraṃ ca phaḍyutam || 5.152 ||
nyasyanti punaraṅguṣṭhau tarjanyau madhyame tathā |
anāmike kaniṣṭhe ca kramādaṅgaiśca pañcabhiḥ || 5.153 ||
punaśca hṛdayādīni tathāṅguṣṭhādikāni ca |
nyasyanti yugapatsarvāṇyaṅgaistaiḥ pañcabhiḥ kramāt || 5.154 ||
nyasyanti ca ṣaḍaṅgāni hṛdayādīni tanmanoḥ |
hṛdayādiṣu caiteṣāṃ pañcaikaṃ dikṣu ca kramāt || 5.155 ||
ṣaḍaṅgāni coktāni sammohanatantre sanatkumārakalpe
varrṇenaikena hṛdayaṃ tirbhireva śiro matam |
caturbhiśca śikhā proktā tathaiva kavacaṃ matam |
netraṃ tathā caturvarṇairastraṃ dvābhyāṃ tathā matam || 5.156 ||
| iti |
tataścāpādamākeśānnyaseddorbhyāmimaṃ manum |
vārāṃstrīn vyāpakatvena nyasecca praṇavaṃ sakṛt || 5.157 ||
athākṣaranyāsaḥ
tato'ṣṭādaśavarṇāṃśca mantrasyāsya yathākramam |
dante lalāṭe bhrūmadhye karṇayornetrayordvayoḥ || 5.158 ||
nāsayorvadane kaṇṭhe hṛdi nābhau kaṭidvaye |
guhye jānudvaye caikaṃ nyasedekaṃ ca pādayoḥ || 5.159 ||
santo nyasyanti tārādinamo'nantāṃstān sabindukān |
śrīśaktikāmabījaiśca sṛṣṭyādikramato'pare || 5.160 ||
atha padanyāsaḥ
tāraṃ śirasi vinyasya pañca mantrapadāni ca |
nyasennetradvaye vaktre hṛdguhyāṅghriṣu ca kramāt || 5.161 ||
dehe ca vyāpakatvena nyasettānyakhile punaḥ |
kecittāni namo'ntāni nyasyantyādyākṣaraiḥ saha || 5.162 ||
svāhāntāni tathā trīṇi saṃmiśrāṇyuttarottaraiḥ |
guhyādgalānmastakācca vyāpayya caraṇāvadhi || 5.163 ||
nyāso'tra jñānaniṣṭhānāṃ guhyādiviṣayastu yaḥ |
svasvavarṇatanoḥ kāryastattadvarṇeṣu vaiṣṇavaiḥ || 5.164 ||
atha ṛṣyādinyāsaḥ
ṛṣyādīn saptabhāgāṃśca nyasedasya manoḥ kramāt |
mūrdhāsyahṛtsu kucayoḥ punarhṛdi punarhṛdi || 5.165 ||
atha mudrāpañcakam
veṇvākhyāṃ vanamālākhyāṃ mudrāṃ sandarśayettataḥ |
śrīvatsākhyāṃ kaustubhākhyāṃ blivākhyāṃ ca manoramām || 5.166 ||
itthaṃ naystaśarīraḥ san kṛtvā digbandhanaṃ punaḥ |
karakacchapikāṃ kṛtvā dhāyecchrīnandanandanam || 5.167 ||
atha śrīnandandanabhagavaddhyānavidhiḥ
atha prakaṭasaurabhodgalitamādhvikotphullasat
prasūnanavapallavaprakaranamraśākhairdrumaiḥ |
praphullanavamañjarīlalitavallarīveṣṭitaiḥ
smarecchiśiritaṃ śivaṃ sitamatistu vṛndāvanam || 5.168 ||
athānantaraṃ sitamatiḥ śuddhamanāḥ san vṛndāvanaṃ cintayet | kīdṛśaṃ ? drumaiḥ śiśiritaṃ śītalīkṛtam | kīdṛśaiḥ ? prakaṭamudbhaṭaṃ saurabhaṃ yasya tacca | tadudgalitamādhvīkaṃ ca pracyutamadhu | utphullaṃ ca vikasitam | sacca uttamaṃ yatprasūnaṃ puṣpaṃ navapallavaṃ ca | tayoḥ prakaraḥ samūhaḥ | tena namrāḥ śākhā yeṣāṃ taiḥ | mādhviketihrasvatvaṃ mahākavinibaddhatvātsoḍhavyam | prakaṭasaurabhākulitamattabhṛṅgollasaditi pāṭhastu sugama eva | punaḥ kīdṛśaiḥ ? praphullābhirnavamañjarībhirlalitā manoharā yā vallaryaḥ agraśākhā latā vā, tabhirveṣṭitaiḥ | śivaṃ maṅgalarūpaṃ, nirbādhatvātparamakalyāṇakaratvācca
vikāsisumanorasāsvādanamañjulaiḥ sañcarac
chilīmukhamukhodgatairmukharitāntaraṃ jhaṅkṛtaiḥ |
kapotaśukaśārikāparabhṛtādibhiḥ patribhir
virāṇitamitastato bhujagaśatrunṛtyākulam || 5.169 ||
kalindaduhituścalallaharivipruṣāṃ vāhibhir
vinidrasarasīruhodararajaścayoddhūsaraiḥ |
pradīpitamanobhavavrajavilāsinīvāsasāṃ
vilolanavihāribhiḥ satatasevitaṃ mārutaiḥ || 5.170 ||
pravālanavapallavaṃ marakatacchadaṃ vajramauktika
prakarakorakaṃ kamalarāganānāphalam | sthaviṣṭhamakhilartubhiḥ satatasevitaṃ kāmadaṃ
tadantarapi kalpakāṅghripamudañcitaṃ cintayet || 5.171 ||
suhemaśikharāvaleruditabhānuvadbhāsvaram
adho'sya kanakasthalīmamṛtaśīkarāsāriṇaḥ |
pradīptamaṇikuṭṭimāṃ kusumareṇupuñjojjvalāṃ
smaretpunaratandrito vigataṣaṭtaraṅgāṃ budhaḥ || 5.172 ||
tadratnakuṭṭimaniviṣṭamahiṣṭhayoga
pīṭhe'ṣṭapatramaraṇaṃ kamalaṃ vicintya |
udyadvirocanasarociramuṣya madhye
sañcintayetssukhaniviṣṭamatho mukundam || 5.173 ||
sūtrāmaratnadalitāñjanameghapuñja
pratyagranīlajalajanmasamānabhāsam |
susnigdhanīlaghanakuñcitakeśajālaṃ
rājanmanojñaśitikaṇṭhaśikhaṇḍacūḍam || 5.174 ||
rolambalālitasuradrumasūnakalpi
tottaṃsamutkacanavotpalakarṇapūram |
lolālakasphuritabhālatalapradīpta
gorocanātilakamuccalacillimālam || 5.175 ||
āpūrṇaśāradagatāṅkaśaśāṅkabimba
kāntānanaṃ kamalapatraviśālanetram |
ratnasphuranmakarakuṇḍalaraśmidīpta
gaṇḍasthalīmukuramunnatacārunāsam || 5.176 ||
sindūrasundaratarādharamindukunda
mandāramandahasitadyutidīpitāṅgam |
vanyapravālakusumapracayāvakpta
graiveyakojjvalamanoharakambukaṣṭham || 5.177 ||
mattabhramadbhramarajuṣṭavilambamāna
santānakaprasavadāmapariṣkṛtāṃsam |
hārāvalībhagaṇarājitapīvaroro
vyomasthalīlalitakaustubhabhānumantam || 5.178 ||
śrīvatsalakṣaṇasulakṣitamunnatāṃsa
ājānupīnaparivṛttasujātabāhum |
ābandhurodaramudāragabhīranābhiṃ
bhṛṅgāṅganānikaravañjularomarājim || 5.179 ||
nānāmaṇipraghaṭitāṅgadakaṅkaṇormi
graiveyasārasananūpuratundabandham |
divyāṅgarāgaparipiñjaritāṅgayaṣṭi
māpītavastraparivītanitambabimbam || 5.180 ||
cārūrujānumanuvṛttamanojñajaṅghaṃ
kāntonnataprapadaninditakūrmakāntim |
māṇikyadarāṇalasannakharājirāja
dratnāṅgulicchadanasundarapādapadmam || 5.181 ||
matsyāṅkuśāradaraketuyavābjavajra
saṃlakṣitāruṇakarāṅghritalābhirāmam |
lāvaṇyasārasamudāyavinirmitāṅga
saundaryanirjitamanobhavadehakāntim || 5.182 ||
āsyāravindaparipūritaveṇurandhra
lolatkarāṅgulisamīritadivyarāgaiḥ |
śaśvadddravīkṛtavikṛṣṭasamastajantu
santānasantatimanantasukhāmburāśim || 5.183 ||
gobhirmukhāmbujavilīnavilocanābhi
rūdhobharaskhalitamantharamandagābhiḥ |
dantāgradaṣṭapariśiṣṭatṛṇāṅkurābhi
rālambvāladhilatābhivītam || 5.184 ||
saprasravastanavicūṣaṇapūrṇaniśca
lāsyāvaṭakṣaritapheniladugdhamugdhaiḥ |
veṇupravartitamanoharamandragīta
dattoccakarṇayugalairapi tarṇakaiśca || 5.185 ||
pratyagraśṛṅgamṛdumastakasamprahāra
saṃrambhavalganavilolakhurāgrapātaiḥ |
āmedurairbahulasāsnagalairudagra
pucchaiśca vatsataravatsatarīnikāyaiḥ || 5.186 ||
hambāravakṣubhitadigvalayairmahadbhi
rapyukṣabhiḥ pṛthukakudbharabhārakhinnaiḥ |
uttambhitaśrutipuṭīparivītavaṃśa
dhvānāmṛtoddhatavikāśiviśālaghoṇaiḥ || 5.187 ||
gopaiḥ samānaguṇaśīlavayovilāsa
veśaiśca mūrcchitakalasvanaveṇuvīṇaiḥ |
mandroccatārapaṭagānaparairvilola
dorvallarīlalitalāsyavidhānadakṣaiḥ || 5.188 ||
jaṅghāntapīvarakaṭīrataṭīnibaddha
vyālolakiṅkiṇighaṭāraṭitairaṭadbhiḥ |
mugdhaistarakṣunakhakalpitakaṇṭhabhūṣai
ravyaktamañjuvacanaiḥ pṛthukaiḥ parītam || 5.189 ||
atha sulalitagopasundarīṇāṃ
pṛthunivivīṣanitambamantharāṇām |
gurukucabharabhaṅgurāvalagna
trivalivijṛmbhitaromarājibhājām || 5.190 ||
tadatimadhuracāruveṇuvādyā
mṛtarasapallavitāṅgajāṅghripāṇām |
mukulavisararamyarūḍharomo
dgamasamalaṅkṛtagānavallarīṇām || 5.191 ||
tadatiruciramandahāsacandrā
tapaparijṛmbhitarāgavārirāśeḥ |
taralatarataraṅgabhaṅgavipruṭ
prakarasamaśramabindusantatānām || 5.192 ||
tadatilalitamandacillicāpa
cyutaniśitekṣaṇamārabāṇavṛṣṭyā |
dalitasakalamarmavihvalāṅga
pravisṛtaduḥsahavepathuvyathānām || 5.193 ||
tadatisubhagakamrarūpaśobhā
mṛtarasapānavidhānalālasābhyām |
praṇayasalilapūravāhinīnā
malasavilolavilocanāmbujābhyām || 5.194 ||
visraṃsatkavarīkalāpavigalatphullaprasūnasrava
nmādhvīlampaṭacañcarīkaghaṭayā saṃsevitānāṃ muhuḥ |
māronmādamadaskhalanmṛdugirāmālolakāñcyucchvasa
nnīvīviślathamānacīnahicayāntāvirnitambatviṣām || 5.195 ||
skhalitalalitapādāmbhojamandābhidhāna
kvaṇitamaṇitulākoṭyākulāśāmukhānām |
caladadharadalānāṃ kuṭnalapakṣmalākṣi
dvayasarasiruhāṇāmullasatkuṇḍalānām || 5.196 ||
skhalitasya skhalanayuktasya lalitasya ca pādāmbhojasya mandābhighātena īṣadbhūbhāgaaprahāreṇa kvaṇitaḥ kṛtaśabdo maṇimayo yastulākoṭirnūpuraṃ, tenākulaṃ śabdavyāptamāśānāṃ diśāṃ mukhaṃ yābhyastāsām | kuḍmalatmukulāyamānaṃ pakṣmalaṃ ca utkṛṣṭapakṣmayuktamakṣidvayasarasiruhaṃ yāsām
drāghiṣṭhaśvasanasamīraṇābhitāpa
pramlānībhavadaruṇoṣṭhapallavānām |
nānopāyanavilasatkarāmbujānā
mālībhiḥ satataniṣevitaṃ samantāt || 5.197 ||
drāghiṣṭho'tidīrghaḥ śvāsanasamīraṇaḥ śvāsavāyustena abhitāpaḥ santāpastena pramlānībhavanaruṇoṣṭhapallavo yāsām
tāsāmāyatalolanīlanayanavyākoṣanīlāmbuja
sragbhiḥ samparipūjitākhilatanuṃ nānāvinodāspadam |
tanmugdhānanapaṅkajapravigalanmādhvīrasāsvādinīṃ
vibhrāṇaṃ praṇayonmadākṣimadhukṛnmālāṃ manohāriṇīm || 5.198 ||
vyākośaṃ vikasitaṃ, praṇayādunmade udgatamade akṣiṇī eva madhukṛnmālā bhramarapaṅktiḥ | tāṃ bibhrāṇaṃ prakaṭayantam | śrīlocanayoritastato bahudhā nipatanena sarvato darśanānmāletyuktam | kīdṛśīm ? tāsāṃ yanmugdhaṃ manoharamānanapaṅkajam | tasmātpravigalito mādhvīrasasya makarandasya āsvādana śīlām | ata eva manohāriṇīṃ
gopagopīpaśūnāṃ bahiḥ smared
agrato'sya gīrvāṇaghaṭām |
vittārthinīṃ viriñcitrinayana
śatamanyupūrvikāṃ stotraparām || 5.199 ||
idānīṃ krameṇa vittadharmamokṣakāmākhyapuruṣārthacatuṣṭayasya tathā sarvataḥ śreṣṭhasya pañcamapuruṣārtharūpāyā bhakteśca vāñchāyāḥ pradānāṃ devādīnāṃ dhyānamāhagopeti pañcabhiḥ | asya kṛṣṇasya agrataḥ sammukhe
taddakṣiṇato muninikaraṃ
dṛḍhadharmavāñchamānāyaparam |
yogīndrānatha pṛṣṭhe mumukṣa
māṇān samādhinā sanakādyān || 5.200 ||
dakṣiṇe cāsya muninikaraṃ smaret | dṛḍhā dharme vāñchā yasya tam
savye sakāntānatha yakṣasiddha
gandharvavidyādharacāraṇāṃśca |
sakinnarānapsarasaśca mukhyāḥ
kāmārthino nartanagītavādyaiḥ || 5.201 ||
sakāntān patnīsahitān yakṣādīṃśca smaret | kathambhūtān ? nartanādyaiḥ kāmārthino nijanijābhīṣṭaprārthakān | mukhyāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ urvaśyādyā apsarasaśca smaret
śaṅkhendukundadhavalaṃ sakalāgamajñaṃ
saudāmanītatipiṅgajaṭākalāpam |
tatpādapaṅkajagatāmacalāṃ ca bhaktiṃ
vāñchantamujjhitatarānyasamastasaṅgam || 5.202 ||
tasya śrīkṛṣṇasya pādapaṅkajagatāṃ tadviṣayiṇīmityarthaḥ | ujjhitataro nitarāṃ parityakto'nyasmin bhaktivyatirikte samaste saṅga āsaktiryena tam 
nānāvidhaśrutigaṇānvitasaptarāga
grāmatrayīgatamanoharamūrchanābhiḥ |
samprīṇayantamuditābhiramuṃ mahatyā
sañcintayennabhasi dhātṛsutaṃ munīndram || 5.203 ||
ata eva amuṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ mahatyākhyayā kacchapikayā svakīyavīṇayā prīṇayantam | kābhiḥ ? nānāvidhaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadbhedātmako yaḥ śrutigaṇo nādasamūhastenānvitā ye sapta rāgāḥ niṣādādisvarā meghanādavasantādirāgā vā, teṣu vā grāmatrayī tatra grāmāṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ samāhārastasyāṃ gatāḥ prāptā yā manoharā mūrchanāstābhiḥ | kimbhūtābhiḥ ? uditābhiḥ svayameva prākaṭyaṃ prāptābhiḥ | mahatyoditābhiriti vā sambandhaḥ | ata eva munīndraṃ munigaṇaśreṣṭhaṃ dhātṛsutaṃ śrīnāradaṃ nabhasi samyakcintayet
śrīgautamīyatantre
atha dhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
pītāmbaradharaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇam || 5.204 ||
raktanetrādharaṃ raktapāṇapādanakhaṃ śubham |
kaustubhodbhāsitoraskaṃ nānāratnavibhūṣitam || 5.205 ||
taddhāmavilasanmuktābaddhahāropaśobhitam |
nānāratnaprabhodbhāsimukuṭaṃ divyatejasam || 5.206 ||
harakeyūrakaṭakakuṇḍalaiḥ parimaṇḍitam |
śrīvatsavakṣasaṃ cārunūpurādyupaśobhitam || 5.207 ||
nānāratnavicitraiśca kaṭisūtrāṅgulīyakaiḥ |
barhipatrakṛtāpīḍaṃ vanyapuṣpairalaṅkṛtam || 5.208 ||
kadambakusumodbaddhavanamālāvibhūṣitam |
sacandratārakānandivimalāmbarasannibham || 5.209 ||
veṇuṃ gṛhītvā hastābhyāṃ mukhe saṃyojya saṃsthitam |
gāyantaṃ divyagānaiśca goṣṭhamadhyagataṃ harim || 5.210 ||
svargādiva paribhraṣṭakanyakāśataveṣṭitam |
sarvalakṣaṇasampannaṃ saundaryeṇābhiśobhitam || 5.211 ||
śubhaṃ jaganmaṅgalarūpaṃ, tasya kaustubhasya dhāmnā tejasā vilasantībhirmuktābhirācchannena saṃveṣṭitena hāreṇa upaśobhitam | muktābaddheti vā pāṭhaḥ | kaṭisūtreṇāṅgulīyakaiścālaṅkṛtam | sacandrābhistārābhirānandaṃ sukhakaraṃ yadvimalamambaraṃ vyoma tatsadṛśam | atra candrasthāne kaustubhaḥ | tārāsthāne kadambamālā | ambarasthāne śrīmadvakṣaḥsthalamūhyam | svargādiva paribhraṣṭānāṃ paramasundarīṇāmityarthaḥ | tadṛśīnāṃ kanyānāṃ śrīgopakumārīṇāṃ śatena veṣṭitam | śataśabdo'trāsaṅkhyatve
mohanaṃ sarvagopīnāṃ sarvāsāṃ ca gavāmapi |
lelihyamānaṃ vatsaiśca dhenubhiśca samantataḥ || 5.212 ||
siddhagandharvayakṣaiśca apsarobhirvihaṅgamaiḥ |
surāsuramanuṣyaiśca sthāvaraiḥ pannagairapi || 5.213 ||
mṛgairvidyādharaiścaiva vīkṣyamāṇaṃ suvismitaiḥ |
nāradena vaśiṣṭhena viśvāmitreṇa dhīmatā || 5.214 ||
parāśareṇa vyāsena bhṛguṇāṅgirasā tathā |
dakṣeṇa śaunakātribhyāṃ siddhena kapilena ca || 5.215 ||
sanakādyirmunīndraiśca brahmalokagatairapi |
anyairapi ca saṃyuktaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dhyāyedaharniśam || 5.216 ||
saṅkṣepeṇa śrīsanatkumārakalpe'pi
avyānmīlatkalāyadyutirahiripupicchollasatkeśajālo
gopīnetrotsavārādhitalalitavapurgopagovṛndavītaḥ |
śrīmadvaktrāravindapratisahitśaśāṅkākṛtiḥ pītavāsā
devo'sau veṇunādakṣapitajanadhṛtirdevakīnandano naḥ || 5.217 ||
| iti |
asau anirvacanīyamāhātmyaḥ śrīdevakīnandano devo naḥ asmānavyātrakṣatu | kalāyasya tatpuṣpasyeva dyutiḥ śyāmā kāntiryasya saḥ 
athāntaryāgaḥ
dhyātvaivaṃ bhagavantaṃ taṃ samprārthya ca yathāsukham |
ādau sampūjayetsarvairupacāraiśca mānasaiḥ || 5.218 ||
lekhyā ye bahirarcayāmupacārā vibhāgaśaḥ |
te sarve'pyantararcāyāṃ kalpanīyā yathāruci || 5.219 ||
atha prārthanāvidhiḥ
śrīnāradapañcarātre
svāgataṃ devadeveśa sannidhau bhava keśava |
gṛhāṇa mānasīṃ pūjāṃ yathārthaparibhāvinām || 5.220 ||
| iti |
athopacārairbāhyaiśca svātmanyeva sthitaṃ prabhum |
pūjayan sthāpayedādau śaṅkhaṃ satsampradāyataḥ || 5.221 ||
atha śaṅkhapratiṣṭhā
svasya vāmāgrato bhūmāvullikhya tryasramaṇḍalam |
tatāstrakṣālitaṃ śaṅkhaṃ sādhāraṃ sthāpayedbudhaḥ || 5.222 ||
śaṅkhe hṛdayamantreṇa gandhapuṣpākṣatān kṣipet |
vyutkrāntairmātṛkārṇaistaṃ śiro'ntaiḥ kena pūrayet || 5.223 ||
sabindunā makāreṇa tadādhāre'gnimaṇḍalam |
sampūjayedakāreṇa śaṅkhe cādityamaṇḍalam || 5.224 ||
ukāreṇa jale somamaṇḍalaṃ ca tathārcayet |
tīrthamantreṇa tīrthānyāvāhayeccārkamaṇḍalam || 5.225 ||
kṛṣṇaṃ cāvāhya hṛtpadmādgālinīṃ śikhayekṣayet |
netramantreṇa vīkṣyāntaḥ kavacenāvaguṇṭhaet || 5.226 ||
kuryānnyāsaṃ jale mūlamantrāṅgānāṃ tato diśaḥ |
baddhvāstreṇāmṛtīkuṛyādatha taddhenumudrayā || 5.227 ||
taccakramudrayā rakṣya salilaṃ matsyamudrayā |
ācchādya saṃspṛśan śaṅkhaṃ japenmūlaṃ tato'ṣṭaśaḥ || 5.228 ||
tajjalaṃ prokṣaṇīpātre kiṃcitkṣiptvā trirukṣayet |
taccheṣeṇārcanadravyajātāni svatanūmapi || 5.229 ||
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakau saktau karayoritaretaram |
tarjanīmadhyamānāmāḥ saṃhatā bhugnasajjitāḥ |
mudraiṣā gālinī proktā śaṅkhasyopari cālitā || 5.230 ||
tato'pāsyāvaśiṣṭāntaḥ śaṅkhaṃ vardhanikāmbunā |
punarāpūrya kṛṣṇāgre nyasedācārataḥ satām || 5.231 ||
atha svadehe pīṭhapūjā
gurūnmūrdhni gaṇeśaṃ ca mūlādhāre'bhipūjya tam |
pīṭhanyāsānusāreṇa pīṭhaṃ cātmani pūjayet || 5.232 ||
atha devāṅgeṣu mantrāṅgādinyāsaḥ
tato japan kāmabījaṃ tristhānasthaṃ paraṃ mahaḥ |
mūlamantrātmakaṃ bījenaikībhūtaṃ vicintayet || 5.233 ||
tacca pañcāṅganyāsena sākāraṃ sveṣṭadaivatam |
vicintya pañcāṅgādīni nyasyettasmin yathātmani || 5.234 ||
kuryurbhagavati prādurbhūte kṛṣṇe ca vaiṣṇavāḥ |
tattannyāsānabhedāya manorbhagavatā saha || 5.235 ||
kecinnyasyanti tattvādīnnavyaktāni yathoditam |
mantrārṇaiḥ svarahaṃsādyairbhūṣaṇeṣu prabhoḥ kramāt || 5.236 ||
atha bāhyopacārairantaḥpūjā
tasmin pīṭhe tamāsīnaṃ bhagavantaṃ vibhāvayan |
āsanādyaistu puṣpāntairyathāvidhyarcayedbudhaḥ || 5.237 ||
tato mukhe'rcayedveṇuṃ vanamālāṃ ca vakṣasi |
dakṣastanordhve śrīvatsaṃ savye tatraiva kaustubham || 5.238 ||
vaiṣṇavaścandanenāmumālipyopakaniṣṭhayā |
prāgvaddīpaśikhākāratilakāni dviṣaḍlikhet || 5.239 ||
yathoktaṃ pañcabhiḥ puṣpāñjalibhiścābhipūjya tam |
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ mukhavāsādi cārpayet || 5.240 ||
gītādibhiśca santoṣya kṛṣṇamasmai tato'khilam |
aśakto bahirarcāyāmarpayejjapamācaret || 5.241 ||
athāntaryāgamāhātmyam
vaiṣṇavatantre
aśvamedhasahasrāṇi vājapeyaśatāni ca |
ekasya dhyānayogasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 5.242 ||
bṛhannāradīye śrīvāmanaprādurbhāve
yannāmoccāraṇādeva sarve naśyantyupadravāḥ |
stotrairvā arhaṇābhirvā kimu dhyānena kathyate || 5.243 ||
nāradapañcarātre śrībhagavannāradasaṃvāde
ayaṃ yo mānaso yāgo jarāvyādhibhayāpahaḥ |
sarvapāpaughaśamano bhāvābhāvakaro dvija |
satatābhyāsayogena dehabandhādvimocayet || 5.244 ||
yaścaivaṃ parayā bhaktyā sakṛtkuryānmahāmate |
kramoditena vidhinā tasya tuṣyāmyahaṃ mune || 5.245 ||
smaraṇadhyānayoḥ pūrvaṃ māhātmyaṃ likhitaṃ ca yat |
jñeyaṃ tadadhikaṃ cātrāntaryāgāṅgatayā tayoḥ || 5.246 ||
evaṃ yathāsampradāyaṃ śaktyā yāvanmanaḥsukham |
antaḥpūjāṃ vidhāyādāvārabheta bahistataḥ || 5.247 ||
tathā coktaṃ nāradena
dhyātvā ṣoḍaśasaṅkhyātairupacāraiśca mānasaiḥ |
samyagārādhanaṃ kṛtvā bāhyapūjāṃ samācaret || 5.248 ||
atha bahiḥpūjā
anujñāṃ dehi bhagavan bahiryoge mama prabho |
śrīkṛṣṇamityanujñāpya bahiḥ pūjāṃ samācaret || 5.249 ||
tatra tvanekaśaḥ santi pūjāsthānāni tatra ca |
śrīmūrtayo bahuvidhāḥ śālagrāmaśilāstathā || 5.250 ||
atha pūjāsthānāni
saṃmohanatantre
śālagrāme manau yantre sthaṇḍile pratimādiṣu |
hareḥ pūjā tu kartavyā kevale bhūtale na tu || 5.251 ||
ekādaśaskandhe śrīmaduddhavasaṃvāde
sūryo'gnirbrāhmaṇā gāvo vaiṣṇavaḥ khaṃ marujjalam |
bhūrātmā sarvabhūtāni bhadra pūjāpadāni me || 5.252 ||
sūrye tu vidyayā trayyā haviṣāgnau yajeta mām |
ātithyena tu viprāgrye goṣvaṅga yavasādinā || 5.253 ||
vaiṣṇave bandhusatkṛtyā hṛdi khe dhyānaniṣṭhayā |
vāyau mukhyadhiyā toye dravyaistoyapuraḥsaraiḥ || 5.254 ||
sthaṇḍile mantrahṛdayairbhogairātmānamātmani |
kṣetrajñaṃ sarvabhūteṣu samatvena yajeta mām || 5.255 ||
dhiṣṇyeṣvityeṣu madrūpaṃ śaṅkhacakragadāmbujaiḥ |
yuktaṃ caturbhujaṃ śāntaṃ dhyāyannarcetsamāhitaḥ || 5.256 ||
atha śrīmūrtayaḥ
tatraiva
śailī dārumayī lauhī lepyā lekhyā ca saikatī |
manomayī maṇimayī pratimāṣṭavidhā matā || 5.257 ||
calācaleti dvividhā pratiṣṭhā jīvamandiram |
udvāsāvāhane na staḥ sthirāyāmuddhavārcane || 5.258 ||
asthirāyāṃ vikalpaḥ syātsthaṇḍile tu bhaveddvayam |
snapanaṃ tvavilepyāyāmanyatra parimārjanam || 5.259 ||
gopālamantroddiṣṭatvāttacchrīmūrtirapekṣitā |
tathāpi vaiṣṇavaprītyai lekhyāḥ śrīmūrtayo'khilāḥ || 5.260 ||
atha śrīmūrtilakṣaṇāni
śrīhayaśīrṣapañcarātre śrībhagavatśrīhayaśīrṣabrahmasaṃvāde
ādimūrtirvāsudevaḥ saṅkarṣaṇamathāsṛjat |
caturmūrtiḥ paraṃ proktamekaiko bhidyate tridhā |
keśavādiprabhedena mūrtidvādaśakaṃ smṛtam || 5.261 ||
paṅkajaṃ dakṣiṇe dadyātpāñcajanyaṃ tathopari |
vāmopari gadā yasya cakraṃ cādho vyavasthitam |
ādimūrtestu bhedo'yaṃ keśaveti parkīrtyate || 5.262 ||
adharottarabhāvena kṛtametattu yatra vai |
nārāyaṇākhyā sā mūrtiḥ sthāpitā bhuktimuktidā || 5.263 ||
savyādhaḥ paṅkajaṃ yasya pāñcajanyaṃ tathopari |
dakṣiṇordhve gadā yasya cakraṃ cādho vyavasthitam |
ādimūrtestu bhedo'yaṃ mādhaveti prakīrtyate || 5.264 ||
dakṣiṇādhaḥsthitaṃ cakraṃ gadā yasyopari sthitā |
vāmordhvasaṃsthitaṃ padmaṃ śaṅkhaṃ cādho vyavasthitam |
saṅkarṣaṇasya bhedo'yaṃ govindeti prakīrtyate || 5.265 ||
dakṣiṇopari padmaṃ tu gadā cādho vyavasthitā |
saṅkarṣaṇasya bhedo'yaṃ viṣṇurityabhiśabdyate || 5.266 ||
dakṣiṇopari śaṅkhaṃ ca cakraṃ cādhaḥ pradarśyate |
vāmopari tathā padmaṃ gadā cādhaḥ pradarśyate |
madhusūdananāmāyaṃ bhedaḥ saṅkarṣaṇasya ca || 5.267 ||
vāmordhvasaṃsthitaṃ cakramadhaḥ śaṅkhaṃ pradarśyate |
brahmāṇḍagaṃ vāmapādaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ śeṣapṛṣṭhagam || 5.269 ||
dakṣiṇordhvaṃ sahasrāraṃ pāñcajanyamadhaḥsthitam |
saptatālapramāṇena vāmanaṃ kārayetsadā || 5.270 ||
ūrdhvaṃ dakṣiṇataścakramadhaḥ padmaṃ vyavasthitam |
padmā padmakarā vāme pārśve yasya vyavasthitā || 5.271 ||
sthito vāpyupaviṣṭo vā sānurāgo vilāsavān |
pradyumnasya hi bhedo'yaṃ śrīdhareti prakīrtyate || 5.272 ||
dakṣiṇordhvaṃ mahācakraṃ kaumudī tadadhaḥsthitā |
vāmordhve nalinaṃ yasya adhaḥ śaṅkhaṃ virājate |
hṛṣīkeśeti vijñeyaḥ sthāpitaḥ sarvakāmadaḥ || 5.273 ||
dakṣiṇordhve puṇḍarīkaṃ pāñcajanyamadhastathā |
vāmordhve saṃsthitaṃ cakraṃ kaumudī tadadhaḥsthitā |
padmanābheti sā mūrtiḥ sthāpitā mokṣadāyinī || 5.274 ||
dakṣiṇordhve pāñcajanyamadhastāttu kuśeśayam |
savordhve kaumudī caiva hetirājamadhaḥsthitam |
aniruddhasya bhedo'yaṃ dāmodara iti smṛtaḥ || 5.275 ||
eteṣāṃ tu striyau kārye padmavīṇādhare śubhe || 5.276 ||
iti krameṇa mārgādhimāsādhipāḥ keśavādayo dvādaśa |
atha caturviṃśatimūrtayaḥ
siddhārthasaṃhitāyām
vāsudevo gadāśaṅkhacakrapadmadharo mataḥ |
padmaṃ śaṅkhaṃ tathā cakraṃ gadāṃ vahati keśavaḥ || 5.277 ||
śaṅkhaṃ padmaṃ gadāṃ cakraṃ dhatte nārāyaṇaḥ sadā |
gadāṃ cakraṃ tathā śaṅkhaṃ padmaṃ vahati mādhavaḥ || 5.278 ||
cakraṃ padmaṃ tathā śaṅkhaṃ gadāṃ ca puruṣottamaḥ |
padmaṃ kaumodakīṃ śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ dhatte'pyadhokṣajaḥ || 5.279 ||
saṅkarṣaṇo gadāśaṅkhapadmacakradharaḥ smṛtaḥ |
cakraṃ gadāṃ padmaśaṅkhau govindo dharate bhujaiḥ || 5.280 ||
gadāṃ padmaṃ tathā śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ viṣṇurbibharti yaḥ |
cakraṃ śaṅkhaṃ tathā padmaṃ gadāṃ ca madhusūdanaḥ || 5.281 ||
gadāṃ sarojaṃ cakraṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ dhatte'cyutaḥ sadā |
śaṅkhaṃ kaumodakīṃ cakramupendraḥ padmamudvahet || 5.282 ||
cakraśaṅkhagadāpadmadharaḥ pradyumna ucyate |
padmaṃ kaumodakīṃ cakraṃ śaṅkhaṃ dhatte trivikramaḥ || 5.283 ||
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ padmaṃ vāmano vahate sadā |
padmaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ śaṅkhaṃ śrīdharo vahate bhujaiḥ || 5.284 ||
cakraṃ padmaṃ gadāṃ śaṅkhaṃ narasiṃho bibharti yaḥ |
padmaṃ sudarśanaṃ śaṅkhaṃ gadāṃ dhatte janārdanaḥ || 5.285 ||
aniruddhaścakragadāśaṅkhapadmalasadbhujaḥ |
hṛṣīkeśo gadāṃ cakraṃ padmaṃ śaṅkhaṃ ca dhārayet || 5.286 ||
padmanābho vahetśaṅkhaṃ padmaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ tathā |
padmaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ śaṅkhaṃ dhatte dāmodaraḥ sadā || 5.287 ||
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ sarojaṃ ca gadāṃ vahati yo hariḥ |
śaṅkhaṃ kaumodakīṃ padmaṃ cakraṃ viṣṇurbibharti yaḥ || 5.288 ||
etāśca mūrtayo jñeyā dakṣiṇādhaḥkarakramāt || 5.289 ||
matsyapurāṇe ca
etaduddeśataḥ proktaṃ pratimālakṣaṇaṃ tathā |
vistareṇa na śaknoti bṛhaspatirapi dvijāḥ || 5.290 ||
| iti |
sevāniṣṭhā hareḥ śrīmadvaiṣṇavāḥ pāñcarātrikāḥ |
prākaṭyādakhilāṅgānāṃ śrīmūrtiṃ bahu manyate || 5.291 ||
sevyā nijanijaireva mantraiḥ svasveṣṭamūrtayaḥ |
śālagrāmātmake rūpe niyamo naiva vidyate || 5.292 ||
dvibhujā jaladaśyāmā tribhaṅgī madhurākṛtiḥ |
sevyā dhyānānurūpaiśca mūrtiḥ kṛṣṇasya daivataiḥ || 5.293 ||
anyāśca vividhā śrīmadavatārādimūrtayaḥ |
prādurbhāvavidhāvagre lekhyāstattadviśeṣataḥ || 5.294 ||
nityakarmaprasaṅge'tra mūrtijanmapratiṣṭhayoḥ |
vidhirna likhituṃ yogyaḥ sa tu lekhiṣyate'grataḥ || 5.295 ||
atha śālagrāmaśilāḥ
gautamīyatantre
gaṇḍakyāścaiva deśe ca śālagrāmasthalaṃ mahat |
pāṣāṇaṃ tadbhavaṃ yattatśālagrāmamiti smṛtam || 5.296 ||
skandapurāṇe
snigdhā kṛṣṇā pāṇḍarā vā pītā nīlā tathaiva ca |
vakrā rukṣā ca raktā ca mahāsthūlā tvalāñchitā || 5.297 ||
kapilā dardurā bhagnā bahucakraikacakrikā |
bṛhanmukhī bṛhaccakrā lagnacakrāthavā punaḥ |
baddhacakrāthavā kācidbhagnacakrā tvadhomukhī || 5.298 ||
atha tāsāṃ varṇādibhedena guṇadoṣau
tatraiva
snigdhā siddhikarī mantre kṛṣṇā kīrtiṃ dadāti ca |
pāṇḍarā pāpadahanī pītā putraphalapradā || 5.299 ||
nīlā sandiśate lakṣmīṃ raktā rogapradāyikā |
rakṣā codvegadā nityaṃ vakrā dāridryadāyikā || 5.300 ||
sthūlā nihati caivāyurniṣphalā tu alāñchitā |
kapilā karburā bhagnā bahucakraikacakrikā || 5.301 ||
bṛhanmukhī bṛhaccakrā lagnacakrāthavā punaḥ || 5.302 ||
baddhacakrāthavā yā syādbhagnacakrā tvadhomukhī |
pūjayedyaḥ pramādena duḥkhameva labheta saḥ || 5.303 ||
agnipurāṇe ca
tathā vyālamukhī bhagnā viṣayā baddhacakrikā |
vikārāvartanābhiśca nārasiṃhī tathaiva ca || 5.304 ||
kapilā vibhramāvartā rekhāvartā ca yā śilā |
duḥkhadā sā tu vijñeyā sukhadā na kadācana || 5.305 ||
snigdhā śyāmā tathā muktāmāyā vā samacakrikā |
ghoṇimūrtiranantākhyā gambhīrā sampuṭā tathā || 5.306 ||
sūkṣmamūrtiramūrtiśca sammukhā siddhidāyikā |
dhātrīphalapramāṇā yā kareṇobhayasampuṭā |
pūjanīyā prayatnena śilā caitādṛśī śubhā || 5.307 ||
iṣṭā tu yasya yā mūrtiḥ sa tāṃ yatnena pūjayet |
pūjite phalamāpnoti ihaloke paratra ca || 5.308 ||
doṣāścaite sakāmārcanaviṣayāḥ
yata uktaṃ śrībhagavatā brāhme
khaṇḍitaṃ sphuṭitaṃ bhagnaṃ pārśvabhinnaṃ vibheditam |
śālagrāmasamudbhūtaṃ śailaṃ doṣāvahaṃ na hi || 5.309 ||
śrīrudreṇa ca skānde
khaṇḍitaṃ truṭitaṃ bhagnaṃ śālagrāme na doṣabhāk |
iṣṭā tu yasya yā mūrtiḥ sa tāṃ yatnena pūjayet || 5.310 ||
tathā
cakraṃ vā kevalaṃ tatra padmena saha saṃyutam |
kevalā vanamālā vā harirlakṣmyā saha sthitaḥ || 5.311 ||
mukhyāḥ snighdādayastatrāmukhyā raktādayo matāḥ |
mukhyābhāve tvamukhyā hi pūjyā ityucyate paraiḥ || 5.312 ||
atha tāsāmeva lakṣaṇaviśeṣeṇa saṃjñaviśeṣaḥ
brāhme śrībhagavadbrahmasaṃvāde
nivasāmi sadā brahman śālāgrāmākhyaveśmani |
tatraiva rathacakrāṅkabhedanāmāni me śṛṇu || 5.313 ||
dvāradeśe same cakre dṛśyate nāntarīyake |
vāsudevaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ śuklābhaścātiśobhanaḥ || 5.314 ||
dve cakre ekalagne tu pūrvabhāgastu puṣkalaḥ |
saṅkarṣaṇākhyo vijñeyo raktābhiścātiśobhanaḥ || 5.315 ||
pradyumnaḥ sūkṣmacakrastu pītadīptistathaiva ca |
śuṣiraṃ chidrabahulaṃ dīrghākāraṃ tu tadbhavet || 5.316 ||
aniruddhastu nīlābho vartulaścātiśobhanaḥ |
rekhātrayaṃ tu taddvāri pṛṣṭhaṃ padmena lāñchitam || 5.317 ||
saubhāgyaṃ keśavo dadyātcatuṣkoṇo bhavettu yaḥ |
śyāmaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ vidyānnābhicakraṃ tathonnatam || 5.318 ||
dīrgharekhāsamopetaṃ dakṣiṇe śuṣiraṃ pṛthu |
ūrdhvaṃ mukhaṃ vijānīyātdvāre ca harirūpiṇam || 5.319 ||
kāmadaṃ mokṣadaṃ caiva arthadaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
parameṣṭhī lohitabhaḥ padmacakrasamanvitaḥ || 5.320 ||
bilvākṛtistathā pṛṣṭhe śuṣiraṃ cātipuṣkalam |
kṛṣṇavarṇastathā viṣṇuḥ sthūle cakre suśobhanaḥ |
bramacaryeṇa pūjyo'sāvanyathā vighnado bhavet || 5.321 ||
kvacicca
kapilo narasiṃho'tha pṛthucakre ca śobhane |
brahmacaryādhikārī syānnānyathā pūjanaṃ bhavet || 5.322 ||
narasiṃhastribinduḥ syātkapilaḥ pañcabindukaḥ |
brahmacaryeṇa pūjyaḥ syādanyathā sarvavighnadaḥ || 5.323 ||
sthūlaṃ cakradvayaṃ madhye guḍalākṣāsavarṇakam |
dvāropari tathā rekhā padmākārā suśobhanā || 5.324 ||
sphuṭitaṃ viṣamaṃ cakraṃ nārasiṃhaṃ tu kāpilam |
sampūjya muktimāpnoti saṃgrāme vijayī bhavet || 5.325 ||
pādme kārttikamāhātmye ca
yasya dīrghaṃ mukhaṃ pūrvakathitairlakṣaṇairyutam |
rekhāśca keśarākārā nārasiṃho mato hi saḥ || 5.326 ||
brāhme
vārāhaṃ śaktiliṅge ca cakre ca viṣame smṛte |
indranīlanibhaṃ sthūlaṃ trirekhālāñchitaṃ śubham || 5.327 ||
pādme ca tatraiva
varāhākṛtirābhugnaścakrarekhāsvalaṅkṛtaḥ |
vārāha iti sa prokto bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ || 5.328 ||
brāhma eva
dīrghā kāñcanavarṇā yā bindutrayavibhūṣitā |
matsyākhyā sā śilā jñeyā bhuktimuktiphalapradā || 5.329 ||
kvacicca
matsyarūpaṃ tu deveśaṃ dīrghākāraṃ tu yadbhavet |
bindutrayamāyuktaṃ kāsyavarṇaṃ viśobhanam || 5.330 ||
brāhma eva
kūrmastathonnataḥ pṛṣṭhe vartulāvartapūritaḥ |
haritaṃ varṇamādhatte kaustubhena ca cihnitaḥ || 5.331 ||
pādme ca tatraiva
kūrmākārā ca cakrāṅkā śilā kūrmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 5.332 ||
brāhma eva
hayagrīvo'ṅkuśākāro rekhā cakraśamīpagāḥ |
bahucakrasamāyuktaṃ pṛṣṭhe nīradanīlakam || 5.333 ||
kvacicca
hayagrīvāṅkuśākāre rekhāḥ pañca bhavanti hi |
bahubindusamākīrṇe dṛśyante nīlarūpakāḥ || 5.334 ||
pādme ca tatraiva
hāyagrīvā yathā lambā rekhāṅkā yā śilā bhavet |
tathāsau syāddhayagrīvaḥ pūjito jñānado bhavet || 5.335 ||
kiṃ ca
aśvākṛti mukhaṃ yasya sākṣamālaṃ śirastathā |
padmākṛtirbhavedvāpi hayaśīrṣastvasau mataḥ || 5.336 ||
brāhme eva
vaikuṇṭhaṃ maṇivarṇābhaṃ cakramekaṃ tathā dhvajam |
dvāropari tathā rekhā padmākārā suśobhanā || 5.337 ||
śrīdharastu tathā devaścihnito vanamālayā |
kadambakusumākāro rekhāpañcakabhūṣitaḥ || 5.338 ||
vartulaścātihrasvaśca vāmanaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
atasīkusumaprakhyo bindunā pariśobhitaḥ || 5.339 ||
anyatra ca
vāmanākhyo bhaveddevo hrasvo yaḥ syānmahādyutiḥ |
ūrdhvacakrastvadhaścakraḥ so'bhīṣṭārthaprado'rcitaḥ || 5.340 ||
brāhme eva
sudarśanastathā devaḥ śyāmavarṇo mahādyutiḥ |
vāmapārśve gadācakre rekhe caiva tu dakṣiṇe || 5.341 ||
pādme kārttikamāhātmye
cakrākāreṇa paṅktiḥ sā yatra rekhāmayī bhavet |
sa sudarśana ityevaṃ khyātaḥ pūjāphalapradaḥ || 5.342 ||
brāhme
dāmodarastathā sthūlo madhye cakraṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
dūrvābhaṃ dvārasaṅkīrṇaṃ pītā rekhā tathaiva ca || 5.343 ||
pādme ca tatraiva
uparyadhaśca cakre dve nātidīrghaṃ mukhe bilam |
cakre ca rekhā lambaikā sa ca dāmodaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 5.344 ||
anyatra ca
sthūlo dāmodaro jñeyaḥ sūkṣmarandhro bhavettu yaḥ |
cakre ca madhyadeśasthe pūjitaḥ sukhadaḥ sadā || 5.345 ||
nānāvarṇo hyanantākhyo nāgabhogena cihnitaḥ |
anantaḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ sarvapūjāphalapradaḥ || 5.346 ||
pādme ca tatraiva
anantacakro bahubhiścihnairapyupalakṣitaḥ |
anantaḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ sarvapūjāphalapradaḥ || 5.347 ||
dṛśyate śikhare liṅgaṃ śālagrāmasamudbhavam |
yasya yogeśvaro nāma brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || 5.348 ||
āraktaḥ padmanābhākhyaṃ paṅkajacchatrasaṃyutam |
tulasyā pūjayennityaṃ daridrastvīśvaro bhavet || 5.349 ||
candrākṛtiṃ hiraṇyākhyaṃ raśmijālaṃ vinirdiśet |
suvarṇarekhābahulaṃ sphaṭikadyutiśobhitam || 5.350 ||
kiṃ ca
ardhacandrākṛtirdevo hṛṣīkeśa udāhṛtaḥ |
tamarcya labhate svargaṃ viṣayāṃśca samīhitām || 5.351 ||
vāmapārśve same cakre kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ sa bindukaḥ |
lakṣmīnṛsiṃho vikhyāto bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ || 5.352 ||
trivikramastathā devaḥ śyāmavarṇo mahādyutiḥ |
vāmapārśve tathā cakre rekhā caiva tu dakṣiṇe || 5.353 ||
pradakṣiṇāvartakṛtavanamālāvibhūṣitā |
yā śilā kṛṣṇasaṃjñā sā dhanadhānyasukhapradā || 5.354 ||
gautamīye
bahubhirjanmabhiḥ puṇyairyadi kṛṣṇaśilāṃ labhet |
goṣpadena tu cihnena janustena samāpyate || 5.355 ||
catasro yatra dṛśyante rekhāḥ pārśvasamīpagāḥ |
dve cakre madhyadeśe tu sā śilā tu caturmukhā || 5.356 ||
kiṃ ca, pādme tatraiva
vajrakīṭodbhavā rekhāḥ paṅktībhūtāśca yatra vai |
śālagrāmaśilā yā sā viṣṇupañjarasaṃjñitā || 5.357 ||
nāgavatkuṇḍalībhūtarekhāpaṅktiḥ sa śeṣakaḥ |
padmākāre ca paṅktī dve madhye lambā ca rekhikā |
garuḍaḥ sa tu vijñeyaścatuścakro janārdanaḥ || 5.358 ||
catuścakraḥ sūkṣmadvāro vanamālāṅkitodaraḥ |
lakṣmīnārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ || 5.359 ||
etallakṣaṇayuktāstu śālagrāmaśilāḥ śubhāḥ |
yāśca tāsvapi sūkṣmāḥ syustāḥ praśastakarāḥ smṛtāḥ || 5.360 ||
tathā ca śrībhagavadbrahmasaṃvāde tatraiva
yathā yathā śilā sūkṣmā mahatpuṇyaṃ tathā tathā |
tasmāttāṃ pūjayennityaṃ dharmakāmārthasiddhaye || 5.361 ||
tatrāpyāmalakītulyā sūkṣmā cātīva yā bhavet |
tasyāmeva sadā brahman śriyā saha vasāmyaham || 5.362 ||
atha śrīśālagrāmaśilāmāhātmyam
śālagrāmaśilāsparśātkoṭijanmāghanāśanam |
kiṃ punaryajanaṃ tatra harisānnidhyakārakam || 5.363 ||
pādme māghamāhātmye tatraiva
yaḥ pūjayeddhariṃ cakre śālagrāmaśilodbhave |
rājasūyasahasreṇa teneṣṭaṃ prativāsaram || 5.364 ||
yadāmananti vedāntā brahma nirguṇamacyutam |
tatprasādo bhavennṛṇāṃ śālagrāmaśilārcanāt || 5.365 ||
mahākāṣṭhasthito vahnirmathyamānaḥ prakāśate |
yathā tathā harirvyāpī śālagrāme prakāśate || 5.366 ||
api pāpasamācārāḥ karmaṇyanadhikāriṇaḥ |
śālagrāmārcakā vaiśya naiva yānti yamālayam || 5.367 ||
na tathā ramate lakṣmyāṃ na tathā nijamandire |
śālagrāmśilācakre yathā sa ramate sadā || 5.368 ||
agnihotraṃ hutaṃ tena dattā pṛthvī sasāgarā |
yenārcito hariścakre śālagrāmaśilodbhave || 5.369 ||
kāmaiḥ krodhaiḥ pralobhaiśca vyāpto yo'tra narādhamaḥ |
so'pi yāti harerlokaṃ śālagrāmaśilārcanāt || 5.370 ||
yaḥ pūjayati govindaṃ śālagrāme sadā naraḥ |
āhūtasamplavaṃ yāvatna sa pracyavate divaḥ || 5.371 ||
vinā tīrthairvinā dānairvinā yajñairvinā matim |
muktiṃ yāti naro vaiśya śālagrāmaśilārcanāt || 5.372 ||
narakaṃ garbhavāsaṃ ca tiryaktvaṃ kṛmiyonitām |
na yāti vaiśya pāpo'pi śālagrāme'cyutārcakaḥ || 5.373 ||
dīkṣāvidhānamantrajñaścakre yo balimāharet |
sa yāti vaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma satyaṃ satyaṃ mayoditam || 5.374 ||
naivedyairvividhaiḥ puṣpairdhūpairdīpairvilepanaiḥ |
gītavāditrastotrādyaiḥ śālagrāmaśilārcanam || 5.375 ||
kurute mānavo yastu kalau bhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi ramate sannidhau hareḥ || 5.376 ||
liṅgaistu koṭibhirdṛṣṭairyatphalaṃ pūjitaistu taiḥ |
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu ekenāpīha tatphalam || 5.377 ||
śālagrāmaśilārūpī yatra tiṣṭhati keśavaḥ |
tatra devāsurā yakṣā bhuvanāni caturdaśa || 5.378 ||
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute naraḥ |
pitarastasya tiṣṭhanti tṛptāḥ kalpaśataṃ divi || 5.379 ||
śālagrāmaśilā yatra tattīrthaṃ yojanatrayam |
yatra dānaṃ japo homaḥ sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 5.380 ||
śālagrāmasamīpe tu krośamātraṃ samantataḥ |
kīkaṭo'pi mṛto yāti vaikuṇṭhabhavanaṃ nara || 5.381 ||
śālagrāmaśilācakraṃ yo dadyāddānamuttamam |
bhūcakraṃ tena dattaṃ syātsaśailavanakānanam || 5.382 ||
skānde kārttikamāhātmye śrīśivaskandasaṃvāde
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu trailokyaṃ sacarācaram |
mayā saha mahāsena līnaṃ tiṣṭhati sarvadā || 5.383 ||
dṛṣā praṇamitā yena snāpitā pūjitā tathā |
yajñakoṭisamaṃ puṇyaṃ gavāṃ koṭiphalaṃ labhet || 5.384 ||
kāmāsakto'pi yo nityaṃ bhaktibhāvavivarjitaḥ |
śālagrāmaśilāṃ vipra sampūjyaivācyuto bhavet || 5.385 ||
śālagrāmaśilābimbaṃ hatyākoṭivināśanam |
smṛtaṃ saṅkīrtitaṃ dhyātaṃ pūjitaṃ ca namaskṛtam || 5.386 ||
śālagrāmaśilāṃ dṛṣṭvā yānti pāpānyanekaśaḥ |
siṃhaṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā yānti vane mṛgagaṇā bhayāt || 5.387 ||
namaskaroti manujaḥ śālagrāmaśilārcane |
pāpāni vilayaṃ yānti tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā || 5.388 ||
kāmāsakto'thavā kruddhaḥ śālagrāmaśilārcanam |
bhaktyā vā yadi vā'bhaktyā kṛtvā muktimavāpnuyāt || 5.389 ||
vaivasvataṃ bhayaṃ nāsti tathā maraṇajanmanoḥ |
yaḥ kathāṃ kurute viṣṇoḥ śālagrāmaśilāgrataḥ || 5.390 ||
gītairvādyaistathā stotraiḥ śālagrāmaśilārcanam |
kurute mānavo yastu kalau bhaktiparāyaṇaḥ |
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi ramate viṣṇusadmani || 5.391 ||
śālagrāmanamaskāre'bhāvenāpi naraiḥ kṛte |
bhayaṃ naiva kariṣyanti madbhaktā ye narā bhuvi || 5.392 ||
madbhaktibaladarpiṣṭhā matprabhuṃ na namanti ye |
vāsudevaṃ na te jñeyā madbhaktāḥ pāpino hi te || 5.393 ||
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu sadā putra vasāmyaham |
dattaṃ devena tuṣṭena svasthānaṃ mama bhaktitaḥ || 5.394 ||
padmakoṭisahasraistu pūjite mayi yatphalam |
tatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ śālagrāmaśilārcane || 5.395 ||
pūjito haṃ na tairmartyairnamito'haṃ na tairnaraiḥ |
na kṛtaṃ martyaloke yaiḥ śālagrāmaśilārcanam || 5.396 ||
śālagrāmaśilāgre tu yaḥ karoti mamārcanam |
tenārcito'haṃ satataṃ yugānāmekaviṃśatim || 5.397 ||
kimarcitairliṅgaśatairviṣṇubhaktivivarjitaiḥ |
śālagrāmaśilābimbaṃ nārcitaṃ yadi putraka || 5.398 ||
anarhaṃ mama naivedyaṃ patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam |
śālagrāmaśilālagnaṃ sarvaṃ yāti pavitratām || 5.399 ||
yo hi māheśvaro bhūtvā vaiṣṇavaliṅgamuttamam |
dveṣṭi vai yāti narakaṃ yāvadindrāścaturdaśa || 5.400 ||
sakṛdapyarcite bimbe śālagrāmaśilodbhave |
muktiṃ prayānti manujā nūnaṃ sāṅkhyena varjitāḥ || 5.401 ||
malliṅgaiḥ koṭibhirdṛṣṭairyatphalaṃ pūjitaistu taiḥ |
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu ekenāpi hi tadbhavet || 5.402 ||
tasmādbhaktyā ca madbhaktaiḥ prītyarthe mama putraka |
kartavyaṃ satataṃ bhaktyā śālagrāmaśilārcanam || 5.403 ||
śālagrāmaśilārūpī yatra tiṣṭhati keśavaḥ |
tatra devāsurā yakṣā bhuvanāni caturdaśa || 5.404 ||
śālagrāmaśilāgre tu sakṛtpiṇḍena tarpitāḥ |
vasanti pitarastasya na saṅkhyā tatra vidyate || 5.405 ||
pramāṇamasti sarvasya sukṛtasya hi putraka |
phalaṃ pramāṇahīnaṃ tu śālagrāmaśilārcane || 5.406 ||
yo dadāti śilāṃ viṣṇoḥ śālagrāmasamudbhavām |
viprāya viṣṇubhaktāya teneṣṭaṃ bahubhiḥ makhaiḥ || 5.407 ||
(atraprabhṛti ślokā ākare na dṛśyante)
mānuṣye durlabhā loke śālagrāmodbhavā śilā |
prāpyate na vinā puṇyaiḥ kalikāle viśeṣataḥ || 5.408 ||
sa dhanyaḥ puruṣo loke saphalaṃ tasya jīvitam |
śālagrāmaśilā śuddhā gṛhe yasya ca pūjitā || 5.409 ||
saṃniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ śālagrāmaśilārcanam |
yaḥ kuryānmānavo bhaktyā puṣpe puṣpe'śvamedhabhāk || 5.410 ||
kāle vā yadi vākāle śālagrāmaśilārcanam |
bhaktyā vā yadi vābhaktyā yaḥ karoti sa puṇyabhāk || 5.411 ||
dveṣeṇāpi ca lobhena dambhena kapaṭena vā |
śālagrāmodbhavaṃ devaṃ dṛṣṭvā pāpātpramucyate || 5.412 ||
aśucirvā durācāraḥ satyaśaucavivarjitaḥ |
śālagrāmaśilāṃ spṛṣṭvā sadya eva śucirbhavet || 5.413 ||
tilaprasthaśataṃ bhaktyā yo dadāti dine dine |
tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti śālagrāmaśilārcane || 5.414 ||
patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ mūlaṃ toyaṃ dūrvākṣataṃ suta |
jāyate meruṇā tulyaṃ śālagrāmaśilārpitam || 5.415 ||
vidhihīno'pi yaḥ kuryātkriyāmantravivarjitaḥ |
cakrapūjāmavāpnoti samyakśāstroditaṃ phalam || 5.416 ||
tatraiva cānyatra
skandhe kṛtvā tu yo'dhvānaṃ vahate śailanāyakam |
tenoḍhaṃ tu bhavetsarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram || 5.417 ||
brahmahatyādikaṃ pāpaṃ yatkiñcitkurute naraḥ |
tatsarvaṃ nirdahatyāśu śālagrāmaśilārcanam || 5.418 ||
na pūjanaṃ na mantrāśca na japo na ca bhāvanā |
na stutirnopacāraśca śālagrāmaśilārcane || 5.419 ||
śālagrāmaśilā yatra tattīrthaṃ yojanatrayam |
tatra dānaṃ ca homaśca sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 5.420 ||
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute naraḥ |
pitarastasya tiṣṭhanti tṛptāḥ kalpaśataṃ divi || 5.421 ||
śālagrāmasamīpe tu krośamātraṃ samantataḥ |
kīkaṭo'pi mṛto yāti vaikuṇṭhabhuvanaṃ naraḥ || 5.422 ||
pādme ca
śālagrāmaśilācakraṃ yo dadyāddānamuttamam |
bhūcakraṃ tena dattaṃ syātsaśailavanakānanam || 5.423 ||
garuḍapurāṇe
tiṣṭhanti nityaṃ pitaro manuṣyās
tīrthāni gaṅgādikapuṣkarāṇi |
yajñāśca medhā hyapi puṇyaśailāś
cakrāṅkitā yasya vasanti gehe || 5.424 ||
pādme kārttikamāhātmye śrīyamadhūmrakeśasaṃvāde
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu yairnaraiḥ pūjito hariḥ |
saṃśodhya teṣāṃ pāpāni muktaye buddhito bhavet || 5.425 ||
kārttike mathurāyāṃ tu sārūpyaṃ diśate hariḥ |
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ vai pitṝṇuddiśya pūjitaḥ |
kṛṣṇaḥ samuddharettasya pitṝnetān svalokatām || 5.426 ||
bṛhannāradīye ca yajñadhvajopākhyānānte
śālagrāmaśilārūpī yatra tiṣṭhati keśavaḥ |
na bādhante'surāstatra bhūtavetālakādayaḥ || 5.427 ||
śālagrāmaśilā yatra tattīrthaṃ tattapovanam |
yataḥ sannihitastatra bhagavānmadhusūdanaḥ || 5.428 ||
śālagrāmaśilāstāśca yadi dvādaśa pūjitāḥ |
śataṃ vā pūjitaṃ bhaktyā tadā syādadhikaṃ phalam || 5.429 ||
atha bāhulye tāsāṃ phalaviśeṣaḥ
pādme māghamāhātmye devadūtavikuṇḍalasaṃvāde
śilā dvādaśa bho vaiśya śālagrāmaśilodbhavāḥ |
vidhivatpūjitā yena tasya puṇyaṃ vadāmi te || 5.430 ||
koṭidvādaśaliṅgaistu pūjitaiḥ svarṇapaṅkajaiḥ |
yatsyāddvādaśakāleṣu dinenaikena tadbhavet || 5.431 ||
yaḥ punaḥ pūjayedbhaktyā śālagrāmaśilāśatam |
uṣitvā sa harerloke cakravartīha jāyate || 5.432 ||
skānde kārttikamāhātmye śrīśivaskandasaṃvāde
dvādaśaiva śilā yo vai śālagrāmasamudbhavāḥ |
arcayedvaiṣṇavo nityaṃ tasya puṇyaṃ nibodha me || 5.433 ||
koṭiliṅgasahasraistu pūjitairjāhnavītaṭe |
kāśīvāse yugānyaṣṭau dinenaikena tadbhavet || 5.434 ||
kiṃ punarbahunā yastu pujayedvaiṣṇavo naraḥ |
na hi brahmādayo devāḥ saṃkhyāṃ kurvanti puṇyataḥ || 5.435 ||
atha tatkrayavikrayaniṣedhaḥ
tatraiva
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ yo mūlyamudghātayennaraḥ |
vikretā cānumantā ca yaḥ parīkṣāmudīrayet || 5.436 ||
sarve te narakaṃ yānti yāvadāhūtasamplavam |
ataḥ saṃvarjayedvipra cakrasya krayavikrayam || 5.437 ||
atha pratiṣṭhā niṣedhaḥ
tatraiva
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu pratiṣṭhā naiva vidyate |
mahāpūjāṃ tu kṛtvādau pūjayettāṃ tato budhaḥ || 5.438 ||
| iti |
ato'dhiṣṭhānavargeṣu sūryādiṣviva mūrtiṣu |
śālagrāmaśilaiva syādadhiṣṭhānottamaṃ hareḥ || 5.439 ||
atha sarvādhiṣṭhānaśraiṣṭhyam
pādme tatraiva
hṛdi sūrye jale vātha pratimāsthaṇḍileṣu ca |
samabhyarcya hariṃ yānti narāste vaiṣṇavaṃ padam || 5.440 ||
athavā sarvadā pūjyo vāsudevo mumukṣubhiḥ |
śālagrāmeśilācakre vajrakīṭavinirmite || 5.441 ||
adhiṣṭhānaṃ hi tadviṣṇoḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam |
sarvapuṇyapradaṃ vaiśya sarveṣāmapi muktidam || 5.442 ||
tatraiva kārttikamāhātmye yamadhūmrakeśasaṃvāde
pūjā ca vihitā tasya pratimāyāṃ nṛpātmaja |
śailī dārumayī lauhī lepyā lekhyā ca saikatā |
manomayī maṇimayī śrīmūrtiraṣṭadhā smṛtā || 5.443 ||
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu sākṣātśrīkṛṣṇasevanam |
nityaṃ saṃnihitastatra vāsudevo jagadguruḥ || 5.444 ||
skānde kārttikamāhātmye śrīśivaskandasaṃvāde
suvarṇārcā na ratnārcā na śilārcā surottama |
śālagrāmaśilāyāṃ tu sarvadā vasate hariḥ || 5.445 ||
ata evoktam
hatyāṃ hanti yadaṅghrisaṅgatulasī steyaṃ ca toyaṃ pade
naivedyaṃ bahumadyapānaduritaṃ gurvaṅganāsaṅgajam |
śrīśādhīnamatiḥ sthitirharijanaistatsaṅgajaṃ kilbiṣaṃ
śālagrāmaśilānṛsiṃhamahimā ko'pyeṣa lokottaraḥ || 5.446 ||
śālagrāmaśilārūpabhagavanmahimāmbudheḥ |
ūrmīn gaṇayituṃ śakyaḥ śrīcaitanyāśrito'pi kaḥ || 5.447 ||
atha śālagrāmaśilāpūjānityatā
pādme
śālagrāmaśilāpūjā vinā yo'śnāti kiñcana |
sa caṇḍālādiviṣṭhāyāmākalpaṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ || 5.448 ||
skānde ca
gauravācalaśṛṅgāgrairbhidyate yasya vai tanuḥ |
na matirjāyate yasya śālagrāmaśilārcane || 5.449 ||
| iti |
evaṃ śrībhagavān sarvaiḥ śālagrāmaśilātmakaḥ |
dvijaiḥ strībhiśca śūdraiśca pūjyo bhagavataḥ paraiḥ || 5.450 ||
tathā skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde cāturmāsyavrate śālagrāmaśilārcāprasaṅge
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ sacchūdrāṇāmathāpi vā |
śālagrāme'dhikāro'sti na cānyeṣāṃ kadācana || 5.451 ||
tatraivānyatra
striyo vā yadi vā śūdrā brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyādayaḥ |
pūjayitvā śilācakraṃ labhante śāśvataṃ padam || 5.452 ||
| iti |
ato niṣedhakaṃ yadyadvacanaṃ śrūyate sphuṭam |
avaiṣṇavaparaṃ tattadvijñeyaṃ tattvadarśibhiḥ || 5.453 ||
yathā
brāhmaṇasyaiva pūjyo'haṃ śucerapyaśucerapi |
strīśūdrakarasaṃsparśo vajrādapi suduḥsahaḥ || 5.454 ||
praṇavoccāraṇārcaiva śālagrāmaśilārcanāt |
brāhmaṇīgamanāccaiva śūdraścaṇḍālatāmiyāt || 5.455 ||
sanātanaḥ - tadeva śrīnāradoktyā pramāṇayatibrāhmaṇeti | satāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ, śālagrāme śrīśālagrāmaśilārcane, anyeṣāmasatāṃ śūdrāṇām | ataeva śūdramadhikṛtyoktaṃ vāyupurāṇe
ayācakaḥ pradātā syātkṛṣiṃ vṛttyarthamācaret |
purāṇaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ śālagrāmaṃ ca pūjayet ||
| iti |
evaṃ mahāpurāṇānāṃ vacanaiḥ sahabrāhmaṇasyaiva pūjyo'hamiti vacanasya virodhānmātsaryaparaiḥ smārtaiḥ kaiścitkalpitamiti mantavyam | yadi ca yuktyā siddhaṃ samūlaṃ syāttarhi cāvaiṣṇaviḥ śūdraistādṛśībhiśca strībhistatpūjā na kartavyā, yathāvidhi gṛhītaviṣṇudīkṣākaiśca taiḥ kartvyeti vyavasthāpanīyam | yataḥ śūdreṣvantyajeṣvapi madhye ye vaiṣṇavāste śūdrādayo na kilocyante | tathā ca nāradīye
śvapaco'pi mahīpāla viṣṇorbhakto dvijodhikaḥ iti |
itihāsasamuccaye
na śūdrā bhagavadbhaktaṃ niṣādaṃ śvapacaṃ tathā |
vīkṣate jātisāmānyātsa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam ||
| iti |
pādme ca
na śūdrā bhagavadbhaktāste tu bhāgavatā narāḥ |
sarvavarṇeṣu te śūdrā ye na bhaktā janārdane ||
| iti |
etadādikaṃ cāgre vaiṣṇavamāhātmye vistareṇa vyaktaṃ bhāvi | kiṃ ca, bhagavaddīkṣāprabhāvena śūdrādīnāmapi viprasāmyaṃ siddhameva | tathā ca tatrayathā kāñcanatāṃ yāti ityādi | etacca prāgdīkṣāmāhātmye likhitameva | ata eva tṛtīyaskandhe devahūtivākyam
yannāmadheyaśravaṇānukīrtanād
yatprahvaṇādyatsmaraṇādapi kvacit |
śvādo'pi sadyaḥ savanāya kalpate
kutaḥ punaste bhagavannu darśanāt ||
| iti |
savanāya yajanāya kalpate yogyo bhavatītyarthaḥ | ata eva vipraiḥ saha vaiṣṇavānāmekatraiva gaṇanā | tathā ca haribhaktisudhodaye śrībhagavadbrahmasaṃvāde
tīrthānyaśvatthataravo gāvo viprāstathā svayam |
madbhaktāśceti vijñeyāḥ pañca te tanavo mama ||
| iti |
caturthaskandhe śrīpṛthumahārājavarṇane
sarvatrāskhalitādeśaḥ saptadvīpaikadaṇḍadhṛk |
anyatra brāhmaṇakulādanyatrācyutagotrataḥ ||
| iti |
īmahārājasyoktau
mā jātu tejaḥ prabhavenmaharddhibhis
titikṣayā tapasā vidyayā ca |
dedīpyamāne ñjitadevatānāṃ
kule svayaṃ rājakulāddvijānām ||
| iti |
atra śrīsvāmipādānāṃ ṭīkāmahatyaśca tā ṛddhayaśca tābhiryadrājakulasya tejastattasmātsakāśāddvijānāṃ viprāṇāṃ kule ajito devatāpūjyo yeṣāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ, teṣāṃ kule mā jātu prabhavet | kadācidapi prabhavaṃ na karotu | kathambhūte ? samṛddhibhirvināpi svayameva titikṣādibhirdedīpyamāna iti |
purañjanoktau ca
tasmin dadhe damamahaṃ tava vīrapatni
yo'nyatra bhūsurakulātkṛtakilbiṣastam |
paśye na vītabhayamunmuditaṃ trilokyām
anyatra vai murariporitaratra dāsāt ||
| iti |
tatrāpi saiva ṭīkāhe vīrapatni ! yaste kṛtāparādhaḥ | tasminnahaṃ brāhmaṇakulādanyatra anyasminmuraripudāsāditaratra ca damaṃ dadhe, daṇḍaṃ karomītyadi | īdṛśāni ca vacanāni śrībhāgavatādau bahūnyeva santi | itthaṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha sāmyameva sidhyati | kiṃ caviprāddviṣaḍguṇayutātityādivacanairvaiṣṇavabrāhmaṇebhyo nīcajātijātānāmapi vaiṣṇavānāṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ nirdiśyatetarām | ata evoktaṃ śrībhagavatā śrīhayagrīveṇa śrīhayaśīrṣapañcarātre śrīpuruṣottamapratiṣṭhānte
mūrtipānāṃ tu dātavyā deśikārdhena dakṣiṇā |
tadardhaṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ tu tadardhaṃ taddvijanmanām ||
| iti |
ato yuktameva likhita sarvairbhagavataḥ paraiḥ pūjya iti | tathā ca brahmavaivarte priyavratopākhyāne dharmavyādhasyāpi śrīśālagrāmaśilāpūjanamuktam
tataḥ sa vismitaḥ śrutvā dharmavyādhasya tadvacaḥ |
tasthau sa ca samānīya darśayāmāsa tavubhau ||
niniktavasanau vṛddhāvāsanasthau nijau gurū |
śālagrāmaśilāṃ caiva tatsamīpe supūjitam ||
| iti |
atrācāraścasatāṃ madhyadeśe'smin viśeṣato dakṣiṇadeśe ca mahattamānāṃ śrīvaiṣṇavānāṃ pramāṇamiti dik | evaṃ śrībhāgavatapāṭhaādāvapyadhikāro vaiṣṇavānāṃ draṣṭavyaḥ | yato vidhiniṣedhā bhagavadbhaktānāṃ na bhavantīti devarṣibhūtāptanṝṇāṃ pitṝṇām ityādivacanaiḥ | tathā karmaparityāgādināpi na kaściddoṣo ghaṭata iti tāvatkarmāṇi kurvīta iti, yadā yasyānugṛhṇāti bhagavān ityādi vacanaiśca vyaktaṃ bodhitamevāsti | etatsarvamagre śrīvaiṣṇavamāhātmye vistareṇa vyaktaṃ bhāvi 
śvādatvamatra śvabhakṣakajātiviśeṣatvameva śvānamattīti niruktervartamānaprayogātkravyādavattacchīlatvaprāpteḥ | kādācitkabhakṣaṇaprāyaścittavivakṣāyāṃ tvatītaḥ prayogaḥ kriyeta | rūḍhiryogamapaharatīti nyāyena ca tadvirudhyate | ataeva śvapaca iti tairvyākhyātam | savanaṃ cātra somayāga ucyate | tataścāsya bhagavannāmaśravaṇādyekatarātsadya eva savanayogyatāpratikūladurjātitvaprārambhakaprārabdhapāpanāśaḥ pratipadyate | uddhavaṃ prati bhagavatā ca tasmātbhaktiḥ punāti manniṣṭhā śvapākānapi sambhavātiti kaimutyārthameva proktamityāyāti | kintu yogyatvamatra śvapacatvaprāpakaprārabdhapāpavicchinnatvamātramucyate | savanārthaṃ tu guṇāntarādhānamapekṣata eva | brāhmaṇakumārāṇāṃ śaukre janmani yogyatve satyapi sāvitradaiksyajanmāpekṣāvat | sāvitrādijanmani tu sadācāraprāpteriti savane pravṛttirna yujyate | tasmātpūjyatvamātre tātparyamityabhipretya ṭīkākṛdbhirapyuktamanena pūjyatvaṃ lakṣyata iti | tathāpi jātidoṣaharatvena prārabdhahāritvaṃ tu vyaktamevāyātam |
sandhāryaā vaiṣṇavairyatnācchālagrāmaśilā'suvat |
sā cārcyā dvārakācakrāṅkitopetaiva sarvadā || 5.456 ||
brāhme tatraiva
śālagrāmodbhavo devo devo dvāravatībhavaḥ |
ubhayoḥ saṅgamo yatra muktistatra na saṃśayaḥ || 5.457 ||
skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
cakrāṅkitā śilā yatra śālagrāmaśilāgrataḥ |
tiṣṭhate muniśārdūla vardhante tatra sampadaḥ || 5.458 ||
tatraivānyatra
pratyahaṃ dvādaśa śilāḥ śālagrāmasya yo'rcayet |
dvāravatyāḥ śilāyuktāḥ sa vaikuṇṭhe mahīyate || 5.459 ||
atha śrīdvārakācakrāṅkalakṣaṇāni
śrīprahlādasaṃhitāyām
ekaḥ sudarśano dvābhyāṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
tribhistrivikramo nāma caturbhiśca janārdanaḥ || 5.460 ||
pañcabhirvāsudevastu ṣaḍbhiḥ pradyumna ucyate |
saptabhirbaladevastu aṣṭabhiḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 5.461 ||
navabhiśca navavyūho daśabhirdaśamūrtikaḥ |
ekādaśaiścāniruddho dvādaśairdvādaśātmakaḥ |
anyeṣū bahucakreṣu anantaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 5.462 ||
atha dvādaśacakrāṅkamāhātmyam
vārāhe
ye keciccaiva pāṣāṇā viṣṇucakreṇa mudritāḥ |
teṣāṃ sparśanamātreṇa mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 5.463 ||
gāruḍe
sudarśanādyāstu śilāḥ pūjitāḥ sarvakāmadāḥ || 5.464 ||
skānde ca
bhaktyā vā yadi vābhaktyā cakrāṅkaṃ pūjayennaraḥ |
api cetsudurācāro mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 5.465 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye ca dvārakāgatānāṃ śrībrahmādīnāmuktau
etadvai cakratīrthaṃ tu yacchilā cakracihnitā |
muktidā pāpināṃ loke mlecchadeśe'pi pūjitā || 5.466 ||
atha teṣveva cakrabhedena phalabhedaḥ
kapilapañcarātre
ekacakrastu pāṣāṇo dvāravatyāḥ suśobhanaḥ |
sudarśanābhidho yo'sau mokṣaikaphaladāyakaḥ || 5.467 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇo dvābhyāṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ |
ebhiścācyutarūpo'sau phalamaindraṃ prayacchati || 5.468 ||
caturbhujaścatuścakraścaturvargaphalapradaḥ |
pañcabhirvāsudevaśca janmamṛtyubhayāpahaḥ || 5.469 ||
ṣaḍbhiḥ pradyumna evāsau lakṣmīṃ kāntiṃ dadāti saḥ |
saptabhirbalabhadro'sau gotrakīrtivivardhanaḥ || 5.470 ||
dadāti vāñchitaṃ sarvamaṣṭabhiḥ puruṣottamaḥ |
navacakro nṛsiṃhastu phalaṃ yacchatyanuttamam || 5.471 ||
rājyaprado daśabhistu daśāvatārakaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ekādaśabhiraiśvaryamaniruddhaḥ prayacchati || 5.472 ||
nirvāṇaṃ dvādaśātmāsau saukhyadaśca supūjitaḥ || 5.473 ||
atha varṇādibhedena doṣaguṇāḥ pūjyatvāpūjyatve ca
tatraiva
kṛṣṇa mṛtyuprado nityaṃ dhūmraścaiva bhayāvahaḥ |
asvāsthyaṃ karburo dadyānnīlastu dhanahānidaḥ || 5.474 ||
chidro dāridryaduḥkhāni dadyātsampūjito dhruvam |
pāṇḍarastu mahadduḥkhaṃ bhagno bhāryāviyogadaḥ || 5.475 ||
putrapautradhanaiśvaryasukhamatyantamuttamam |
dadāti śuklavarṇaśca tasmādenaṃ samarcayet || 5.476 ||
śrīprahlādasaṃhitāyām
kṛṣṇā mṛtyupradā nityaṃ kapilā ca bhayāvahā |
rogārtiṃ karburā dadyātpītā vittavināśinī || 5.477 ||
dhūmrābhā vittanāśāya bhagnā bhāryāvināśikā |
sacchidrā ca trikoṇā ca tathā viṣamacakrikā |
ardhacandrākṛtiryā ca pūjyāstā na bhavanti hi || 5.478 ||
gārgyagālavayoḥ
sukhadā samacakrā tu dvādaśī cottamā śubhā |
vartulā caturasrā ca narāṇāṃ ca sukhapradā || 5.479 ||
trikoṇā viṣamā caiva chidrā bhagnā tathaiva ca |
ardhacandrākṛtiryā tu pūjārhā na bhavettu sā |
phalaṃ notpadyate tatra pūjitāyāṃ kadācana || 5.480 ||
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse
ādhiṣṭhāniko nāma
pañcamo vilāsaḥ
ṣaṣṭhavilāsaḥ snāpanikaḥ
śrīcaitanyaprasādena tadrūpaṃ gokulotsavam |
manojñaṃ yaṣṭukāmasya mūrtyarcāvidhirucyate || 6.1 ||
svayaṃ vyaktāḥ sthāpanāśca mūrtayo dvividhā matāḥ |
svayaṃ vyaktāḥ svayaṃ kṛṣṇaḥ sthāpanāstu pratiṣṭhayā || 6.2 ||
yathā ca pādmottarakhaṇḍe
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi tadarcāvasathaṃ hareḥ |
sthāpanaṃ ca svayaṃ vyaktaṃ dvividhaṃ tatprakīrtitam || 6.3 ||
śilāmṛddārulohādyaiḥ kṛtvā pratikṛtiṃ hareḥ |
śrautasmārtāgamaproktavidhinā sthāpanaṃ hi yat || 6.4 ||
tatsthāpanamiti proktaṃ svayaṃ vyaktaṃ hi me śṛṇu |
yasmin sannihito viṣṇuḥ svameva nṝṇāṃ bhuvi || 6.5 ||
pāṣāṇadārvorātmeśaḥ svayaṃ vyaktaṃ hi tatsmṛtam |
durlabhatvātsvayaṃ vyaktamūrteḥ śrīvaiṣṇavottamaḥ |
yathāvidhi pratiṣṭhāpya sthāpitāṃ mūrtimarcayet || 6.6 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
naikaṃ svavaṃśaṃ tu narastārayatyakhilaṃ jagat |
arcāyāmīpsitaṃ nṝṇāṃ phalaṃ yāgādidurlabham |
pratimāmāśrito'bhīṣṭapradāṃ kalpalatāṃ yathā || 6.7 ||
athaśrīmūrteḥ prasādanamātmādiśuddhayaśca
śrīmūrtiṃ kṣālanārhāṃ tu śastagandhajalādinā |
praksālayettadanyāṃ tu mūlamantreṇa mārjayet || 6.8 ||
śrīmūrtihṛdayaṃ spṛṣṭvā svamantraṃ cāṣṭadhā japet |
evaṃ prasādanaṃ mūrterātmanastatprasādanāt |
śuddhirekā dvitīyā tu syādavyagratayāpi ca || 6.9 ||
sthānaśuddhistathā dravyaśuddhiśca likhitā purā |
iti prakārabhedena bhavecchuddhicatuṣṭayam || 6.10 ||
uktaṃ ca śrīnāradena
puṣpeṇāmbu gṛhītvā tu prokṣayete sarvasādhanam |
malasnānaṃ tataḥ kuryātpātre devaṃ nidhāya ca || 6.11 ||
anyenāpi
puṣpākṣatādidravyānāṃ kuryānmantrādiśodhanam |
kṣālanenāmbulepādermūrtiśuddiṃ samācaret |
avyagratvenātmaśuddhiṃ ksitiśuddhiṃ tataścaret || 6.12 ||
| iti ||
mantraśuddhiṃ parāṃ cittaśuddhiṃ cecchanti kecana |
evaṃ ṣaṭśuddhayaḥ puṇyāḥ sampradāyānusārataḥ || 6.13 ||
atha pīṭhapūjā
tāmrādipīṭhe śrīkhaṇḍādyālipte'ṣṭadalaṃ likhet |
sakarṇikaṃ trivṛttāḍhyaṃ padmaṃ ṣoḍaśakeśaram || 6.14 ||
sadalāgraṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ caturdvāravibhūṣitam |
pūjāyantraṃ samuddhṛtya pīṭhārcāṃ tatra sādhayet || 6.15 ||
pīṭhe bhagavato vāme śrīgurūn gurupādukām |
nāradādīn pūrvasiddhān yajedanyāṃśca vaiṣṇavān || 6.16 ||
dakṣiṇe cārcayeddurgāṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca sarasvatīm |
tatra prāglikhitanyāsasyānusāreṇa pūjayet || 6.17 ||
madhye ādhāraśaktyādīn dharmādīṃśca vidikṣvatha |
adharmādīṃścaturdikṣvanantādīnmadhyataḥ punaḥ || 6.18 ||
śaktīrnavāṣṭapatreṣu karṇikāyāṃ ca pūjayet |
tathā tadupariṣṭhācca pīṭhamantraṃ yathoditam || 6.19 ||
tatpīṭhe mūlamantreṇa śrīmūrtiṃ sthāpayedatha |
puṣpāñjaliṃ gṛhitveṣṭadevarūpaṃ vicintayet || 6.20 ||
tataśca mūlamantreṇa kṣiptvā puṣpāñjalitrayam |
nijeṣṭadevamūrteśca paramaikyaṃ vibhāvayet || 6.21 ||
athāvāhanādīni
tato devārcane prauḍhapādatāyā niṣedhanāt |
bhūmai nihitapādaḥ san kuryādāvāhanādikam || 6.22 ||
yaccāvāhyamadhiṣṭhānaṃ tatrāvāhanamācaret |
śālagrāmasthāpane ca nāvāhanavisarjane || 6.23 ||
tathā coktam
udvāsāvāhane na staḥ sthāvare vai yathā tathā |
śālagrāmārcane naiva hyāvāhanavisarjane || 6.24 ||
śālagrāme tu bhagavānāvirbhūto yathā hariḥ |
na tathānyatra sūryādau vaikuṇṭhe'pi ca sarvagaḥ || 6.25 ||
athāvāhanādividhiḥ
āvāhanādimudrāśca saṃdarśyāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ |
tathā saṃsthāpanaṃ sannidhāpanaṃ sannirodhanam || 6.26 ||
sakalīkaraṇaṃ cāvaguṇṭhanaṃ ca yathāvidhi |
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryātparamīkaraṇaṃ tathā || 6.27 ||
tathāvāhanādyarthaḥ
āgame
āvāhanaṃ cādareṇa sammukhīkaraṇaṃ prabhoḥ |
bhaktyā niveśanaṃ tasya saṃsthāpanamudrāhṛtam || 6.28 ||
tavāsmīti tvadīyatvadarśanaṃ sannidhāpanam |
kriyāsamāptiparyantaṃ sthāpanaṃ sannirodhanam || 6.29 ||
saklaīkaraṇaṃ coktaṃ tatsarvāṅgaprakāśanam |
ānandaghanatātyantaprakāśo hyavaguṭhanam || 6.30 ||
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ sarvairevāṅgairavaruddhatā |
paramīkaraṇaṃ nāmābhīṣṭasampādanaṃ param || 6.31 ||
athāvāhanamāhātmyam
nārasiṃhe
āgaccha narasiṃheti āvāhyākṣatapuṣpakaiḥ |
etāvatāpi rājendra sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 6.32 ||
| iti|
nyasedyathāsampradāyaṃ deve'ṅgādīni pūrvavat |
śaṅkhacakrādikāścātha mudrā vidvān pradarśayet || 6.33 ||
tathā ca tattvasāgare
āvāhanādimudrāśca darśayitvā tataḥ punaḥ |
aṅganyāsaṃ ca devasya kṛtvā mudrāḥ pradarśayet || 6.34 ||
atha mudrāḥ
āgame
āvāhanīṃ sthāpanīṃ ca tathānyāṃ sannidhāpanīm |
saṃnirodhakarīṃ cānyāṃ sakalīkaraṇīṃ parām || 6.35 ||
tathāvaguṇṭhanīṃ paścādamṛtīkaraṇīṃ tathā |
paramīkaraṇaṃ cānyā prāgaṣṭau darśayedimāḥ || 6.36 ||
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ padmaṃ muṣalaṃ śārṅgameva ca |
khaḍgaṃ pāśāṅkuśī tadvadvainateyaṃ tathaiva ca || 6.37 ||
śrīvatsakaustubhau veṇumabhītivaradau tathā |
vanamālāṃ tathā mantrī darśayetkṛṣṇapūjane || 6.38 ||
mudrā cāpi prayoktavyā nitya'bilvaphalākṛtiḥ |
ityetāśca punaḥ saptadaśa mudrāḥ pradarśayet || 6.39 ||
gandadigdhau karau kṛtvā mudrāḥ sarvatra yojayet |
yo'nyathā kurute mūḍho na siddhaḥ phalabhāgbhavet || 6.40 ||
atha mudrāmāhātmyam
agastyasaṃhitāyām
etābhiḥ saptadaśabhirmudrābhistu vicaksaṇaḥ |
yo vai māmarcayennityaṃ mohayetsa sureśvaram |
drāpayedapi viprendra tataḥ prārthitamāpnuyat || 6.41 ||
kramadīpikāyāṃ bilvamudrāmadhikṛtya
manovāṇīdehairyadiha ca purā vāpi vihitaṃ
tvamatyā matyā vā tadakhilamasau duṣkṛticayam |
imāṃ mudrāṃ jānan kṣapayati narastaṃ suragaṇā
namantyasyādhīnā bhavati satataṃ sarvajanatā || 6.42 ||
athāsanādyarpaṇam
tato nikṣipya devasyopari puṣpāñjalitrayam |
dattvāsanārthaṃ puspaṃ ca svāgataṃ vidhinācaret || 6.43 ||
āsanādyupacāreṣu mudrāḥ ṣoḍaśa darśayet |
prasiddhāḥ padmasvastyādyā vidvān ṣoḍaśasu kramāt || 6.44 ||
śrīkṛṣṇāyārpayedarghyaṃ pādyamācamanīyakam |
madhuparkaṃ punaścācamanīyaṃ ca vidhiryathā || 6.45 ||
tathā ca smṛtyarthasāre
āvāhanāsanaṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam |
snānamācamanaṃ vastrācamanaṃ copavītakam || 6.46 ||
ācamanaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ dhūpadīpaṃ prakalpayet |
naivedyaṃ punarācāmaṃ natvā stutvā visarjayet || 6.47 ||
anyatra ca
ādau puṣpāñjaliṃ dattvā pādārcanamataḥ param |
pādyamarghaṃ tvācamanaṃ madhuparkaṃ yathoditam || 6.48 ||
abhyaṅgodvartane kṛtvā mahāsnānaṃ samācaret |
abhiṣekāṅgavastraṃ ca dattvā nīrājayeddharim || 6.49 ||
| iti|
śrīmūrtau tu śirasyarghyaṃ dadyātpādyaṃ ca pādayoḥ |
mukhe cācamanīyaṃ trirmadhuparkaṃ ca tatra hi || 6.50 ||
sarveṣvapyupacāreṣu pādyādiṣu pṛthakpṛthak |
ādau puṣpāñjaliṃ kecidicchanti bhagavatparāḥ || 6.51 ||
athāsanādyarpaṇamāhātmyam
narasiṃhapurāṇe
dattvāsanamathārghyaṃ ca pādyamācamanīyakam |
devadevasya vidhinā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 6.52 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
āsanānāṃ pradānena sthānaṃ sarvatra vindati |
godānaphalamāpnoti tathā pādyaprado naraḥ || 6.53 ||
tatastvarhaṇadānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
tathaivācamaīyasya dātā brāhamaṇasattamāḥ || 6.54 ||
tīrthatoyaṃ tathā dattvā devasyācamanaṃ punaḥ |
svargalokamavāpnoti sarvapāpavivarjitaḥ |
narastvācamanīyasya dātā bhavati nirmalaḥ || 6.55 ||
madhuparkasya dānena paraṃ padamihāśnute || 6.56 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe ca
madhuparkavidhiṃ kṛtvā madhuparkaṃ prayacchati |
brahman sa yāti paramaṃ sthānametanna saṃśayaḥ || 6.57 ||
atha snānam
vijñāpya devaṃ snānārthaṃ pāduke purato'rpayet |
mahāvidyādinā taṃ ca snānasthānaṃ tato nayet || 6.58 ||
prāgvattatrāsanaṃ pādyaṃ tatraivācamanīyakam |
nivedya darśayenmudrāmamṛtīkaraṇīṃ budhaḥ || 6.59 ||
śālagrāmaśilārūpaṃ tato devaṃ niveśayet |
snānapātre nijābhīṣṭāṃ calāṃ śrīmūrtimeva vā || 6.60 ||
atha snānapātram
skandapurāṇe
kṛtvā tāmramaye pātre yo'rcayenmadhusūdanam |
phalamāpnoti pūjāyāḥ pratyahaṃ śatvārṣikam || 6.61 ||
yo'rcayenmādhavaṃ bhaktyā aśvatthadalasaṃsthitam |
pratyahaṃ labhate puṇyaṃ padmayutasamudbhavam || 6.62 ||
rambhādalopari hariṃ kṛtvā yo'bhyarcayennaraḥ |
varṣāyutaṃ bhavetpītaḥ keśavaḥ priyayā saha || 6.63 ||
ye paśyanti sakṛdbhaktyā padmapatropari sthitam |
bhaktyā padmālayākāntaṃ tairāptaṃ durlabhaphalam || 6.64 ||
| iti|
tataḥ śaṅkhenābhiṣekaṃ kuryādghaṇṭādiniḥsvanaiḥ |
mūlenāṣṭākṣareṇāpi dhūpayannantarāntarā || 6.65 ||
tatra tu prathamaṃ bhaktyā vidadhīta sugandhibhiḥ |
divyaistailādibhirdravyairabhyaṅgaṃ śrīhareḥ śanaiḥ || 6.66 ||
athābhyaṅgadravyāṇi tanmāhātmyaṃ ca
skande
mālatījātimādāya sugandhānāṃ tu vā punaḥ || 6.67 ||
tathānyapuṣpajātīnāṃ gṛhītvā bhaktito narāḥ |
ye snāpayanti deveśamutsave vai harerdine || 6.68 ||
medinīdānatulyaṃ hi phalamuktaṃ svayambhuvā |
yaḥ punaḥ puṣpatailena divyauṣadhiyutena hi || 6.69 ||
abhyaṅgaṃ kurute viṣṇormadhye kṣiptvā tu kuṅkumam |
romāñcitatanurbhūtvā priyayā saha mādhavaḥ |
prītyā bibharti svotsaṅge manvantaraśataṃ hariḥ || 6.70 ||
viṣṇudharmottare ca
gandhatailāni divyāni sugandhīni śucīni ca |
keśavāya naro dattvā gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 6.71 ||
atha pañcāmṛtasnapanam
tataḥ śaṅkhabhṛtenaiva kṣīreṇa snāpayetkramāt |
dadhnā ghṛtena madhunā khaṇḍena ca pṛthakpṛthak || 6.72 ||
pañcāmṛtādyaiḥ snapanaṃ sadā necchanti tatpriyāḥ |
kintu taiḥ kāladeśādiviśeṣe kārayanti tat || 6.73 ||
atha tatparimāṇam
brahmapurāṇe
devānāṃ pratimā yatra ghṛtābhyaṅgastato bhavet |
palāni tasya deyāni śraddhayā pañcaviṃśatiḥ || 6.74 ||
aṣṭottarapalaśataṃ deyaṃ ca sarvadā |
dve sahasre palānāṃ tu mahāsnāne ca saṅkhyayā || 6.75 ||
dātavye yena sarvāsu dikṣu niryāti tadghṛtam || 6.76 ||
| iti|
dugdhādāvapi saṅkhyeyamevaṃ jñeyā manīṣibhiḥ |
palasaṅkhyā ca vijñeyā yājñavalkyādivākyataḥ || 6.77 ||
tathā hi
pañcakṛṣṇalako māṣaste suvarṇastu ṣoḍaśa |
suvarṇānāṃ ca catvāraḥ palamityabhidhīyate || 6.78 ||
| iti|
kiṃ ca
snānārthaṃ surabhīkṣīraṃ mahiṣyādyāstu kutsitāḥ || 6.79 ||
viṣṇudharmottare ca
śarīraduḥkhaśamanaṃ manoduḥkhavināśanam |
kṣīreṇa snapanaṃ viṣṇoḥ kṣīrābhodhipradaṃ tathā || 6.80 ||
agnipurāṇe
gavāṃ śatasya viprebhyaḥ samyagdattasya yatphalam |
ghṛtaprasthena tadviṣṇorlabhetsnānānna saṃśayaḥ || 6.81 ||
indradyumnena samprāptā saptadvīpā vasundharā |
ghṛtodakena saṃyuktā pratimā snāpitā kila || 6.82 ||
pratimāsaṃ sitāṣṭamyāṃ ghṛtena jagatāṃ patim |
snāpayitvā samabhyarcya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 6.83 ||
jñānato'jñānato vāpi yatpāpaṃ kurute naraḥ |
tatkṣālayati sandhyāyāṃ ghṛtasnapanatoṣitaḥ || 6.84 ||
yeṣu kṣīravahā nadyo pāyasakardamāḥ |
tān lokān puruṣā yānti kṣīrasnapanakā hareḥ || 6.85 ||
viṣṇudharme śrīpulastyaprahlādasaṃvāde ca
dvādaśyāṃ pañcadaśyāṃ ca gavyena havisā hareḥ |
snāpanaṃ daityaśārdūla mahāpātakanāśanam || 6.86 ||
dadhyādīnāṃ vikārāṇāṃ kṣīrataḥ sambhavo yathā |
tathaivāśeṣakāmānāṃ kṣīrasnānaṃ tato hareḥ || 6.87 ||
nārasiṃhe
payasā yastu deveśaṃ snāpayedgaruḍadhvajam |
sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 6.88 ||
snāpya dadhnā sakṛdviṣṇuṃ nirmalaṃ priyadarśanam |
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti sevyamānaḥ surottamaḥ || 6.89 ||
duḥsvapnaśamanaṃ jñeyamamaṅgalyavināśanam |
māṅgalyavṛddhidaṃ dadhnā snapanaṃ narapuṅgava || 6.90 ||
yaḥ karoti harerarcāṃ madhunā snāpitāṃ naraḥ |
agniloke sa moditvā punarviṣṇupure vaset || 6.91 ||
madhunā snapanaṃ kṛtvā saubhāgyamadhigacchati |
lokamitrāṇyavāpnoti tathaivekṣurasena ca || 6.92 ||
śrīdvārakāmāhātmye ca śrīmārkaṇḍeyendradyumnasaṃvāde
kṣīrasnānaṃ prakurvanti ye narā viṣṇumūrdhani |
tenāśvamedhajaṃ puṇyaṃ bindunā bindunā smṛtam || 6.93 ||
kṣīrāddaśaguṇaṃ dadhnā ghṛtaṃ tasmāddaśottaram |
ghṛtāddaśaguṇaṃ kṣaudraṃ khaṇḍaṃ tasmāddaśottaram || 6.94 ||
puṣpodakaṃ ca gandhodaṃ vardhate ca daśottaram |
mantrodakaṃ ca darbhodaṃ tathaiva nṛpasattama || 6.95 ||
drākṣārasaṃ cūtarasaṃ śatavājimakhaiḥ samam |
tathaiva tīrthanīraṃ ca phalaṃ yacchati bhūmipa || 6.96 ||
snāpanaṃ kṛṣṇadevasya yaḥ karoti svaśaktitaḥ |
phalamāpnoti tatproktaṃ niṣkāmo muktimāpnuyāt || 6.97 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
tīrthodakāni puṇyāni svayamānīya mānavaḥ |
tailasya snapanaṃ dattvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 6.98 ||
atha snapane dhūpane dhūpanamāhātmyam
skānde
snānakāle tu kṛṣṇasya aguruṃ dahate tu yaḥ |
praviṣṭo nāsikārandhraṃ pāpaṃ janmāyutaṃ dahet || 6.99 ||
udvartanaṃ ca tailāderapasāraṇakāraṇam |
devasya kārayeddravyairupayuktyairanantaram || 6.100 ||
athodvartanaṃ tanmāhātmyaṃ ca
nārasiṃhe
yavagodhūmaiścūrṇairudvartyoṣṇena vāriṇā |
prakṣālya devadeveśaṃ vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 6.101 ||
viṣṇudharmottare ca
godhūmayavacūrṇaistu tamutsādya janārdanam |
lodhracūrṇakasaṅkīrṇairbalarūpaṃ tathāpnuyāt || 6.102 ||
masūramāsacūrṇaṃ ca kuṅkumakṣodasaṃyutam |
nivedya devadevāya gandharvaiḥ saha modate || 6.103 ||
vārāhe
kalāyakasya cūrṇena piṣṭacūrṇena vā punaḥ |
tenaivodvartanaṃ kuryādgandhapuṣpaiśca saṃyutam |
yadīcchetparamāṃ siddhiṃ mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ || 6.104 ||
| iti|
tataḥ samarpayetkūrcamuṣīrādivinirmitam |
malāpakarṣaṇādyarthaṃ śrīmanmūrtyaṅgasandhitaḥ || 6.105 ||
atha kūrcaṃ tanmāhātmyam
viṣṇudharmottare
uṣīrakūrcakaṃ dattvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
dattvā gobālajaṃ kūrcaṃ sarvāṃstāpān vyapohati |
dattvā cāmarakaṃ kūrcaṃ śriyamāpnotyanuttamam || 6.106 ||
atha śuddhajalasnapanam
tataḥ koṣṇena saṃsnāpya saṃskṛtena sugandhinā |
śītalenāmbunā śaṅkhabhṛtena snāpayetpunaḥ || 6.107 ||
taduktamekādaśaskandhe
candanośīrakarpūra kuṅkumāguruvāsitaiḥ |
salilaiḥ snāpayenmantrairnityadā vibhave sati || 6.108 ||
atha jalaparimāṇam
bhaviṣye
snāne palaśataṃ deyamabhyaṅge pañcaviṃśatiḥ |
palānāṃ dve sahasre tu mahāsnānaṃ prakīrtitam || 6.109 ||
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ
na naktodakapuṣpādyairarcanaṃ snānamarhati || 6.110 ||
viṣṇuḥ
na naktaṃ gṛhītodakena daivakarma kuryāt || 6.111 ||
hārītaḥ
rātrāvetā āpo varuṇaṃ prāviśanta
tasmānna rātrau gṛhṇīyāt || 6.112 ||
atha snapanamāhātmyam
nārasiṃhe
nirmālaymapanīyātha toyena snāpya keśavam |
narasiṃhākṛtiṃ rājan sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 6.113 ||
godānajaṃ phalaṃ prāpya yānenāmbaraśobhinā |
narasiṃhapuraṃ prāpya modate kālamakṣayam || 6.114 ||
kiṃ ca
snāpya toyena bhaktyā tu narasiṃhaṃ narādhipa |
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokaṃ mahīyate || 6.115 ||
narasiṃhaṃ tu saṃsnāpya karpūrāguruvāriṇā |
canddraloke sa moditvā paścādviṣṇupuraṃ vaset || 6.116 ||
kiṃ ca
kuśapuṣpodakenāpi viṣṇulokamavāpnuyāt |
ratnodakena sāvitraṃ kauveraṃ hemavāriṇā || 6.117 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
ratnodakapradānena śriyamāpnotyanuttamām |
bījodakapradānena kriyāsāphalyamāpnuyāt || 6.118 ||
puṣpatoyapradānena śrīmān bhavati mānavaḥ |
phalatoyapradānena saphalāṃ vindate kriyām || 6.119 ||
hayaśīrṣapañcarātre
sugandhinā yastoyena snāpayejjalaśāyinam |
brahmalokamavāpnoti yāvadindrāścaturdaśa || 6.120 ||
gāruḍe
tulasīmiśratoyena snāpayanti janārdanam |
pūjayanti ca bhāvena dhanyāste bhuvi mānavāḥ || 6.121|
agnipurāṇe
mahāsnānena govindaṃ samyaksaṃsnāpya mānavaḥ |
yaṃ yaṃ prārthayate kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayaḥ || 6.122 ||
pādme śrīpulastyabhagīrathasaṃvāde
snānamabhyarcanaṃ yastu kurute keśave sadā |
tasya puṇyasya yā saṅkhyā nāsti sā jñāne gocarā || 6.123 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
snānārthaṃ devadevasya yastu gandhaṃ prayacchati |
bhavanti vaśagāstasya nāryaḥ sarvatra sarvadā || 6.124 ||
puṣpadānāttathā loke bhavatīha phalānvitaḥ |
dattvā mṛgamadasnānaṃ sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt || 6.125 ||
sarvauṣadhipradānena vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet |
dattvā jātīphalaṃ mukhyaṃ saphalāṃ vindati kriyām || 6.126 ||
atha sarvauṣadhiḥ
murā māsī vacā kuṣṭhaṃ śaileyaṃ rajanīdvayam |
śaṭī campakamustaṃ ca sarvauṣadhigaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 6.127 ||
gandhaścāgame
gandhaścandanakarpūrakālāgurubhirīritaḥ || 6.128 ||
atha śaṅkhamāhātmyam
skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
śaṅkhasthitena toyena yaḥ snāpayati keśavam |
kapilāśatadānasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 6.129 ||
śaṅke tīrthodakaṃ kṛtvā yaḥ snāpayati mādhavam |
dvādaśyāṃ bindumātreṇa kulānāṃ tārayecchatam || 6.130 ||
kapilākṣīramādāya śaṅkhe kṛtvā janārdanam |
yaḥ snāpayati dharmātmā yajñāyutaphalaṃ labhet || 6.131 ||
anyagosambhavaṃ kṣīraṃ śaṅkhe kṛtvā tu nārada |
yaḥ snāpayati deveśaṃ rājasūyaphalaṃ labheet || 6.132 ||
śaṅkhe kṛtvā ca pānīyaṃ sākṣataṃ kusumānvitam |
snāpayeddevadeveśaṃ hanyātpāpaṃ cirārjitam || 6.133 ||
sākṣataṃ kusumopetaṃ śaṅkhe toyaṃ sacandanam |
yaḥ kṛtvā snāpayeddevaṃ mama loke vasecciram || 6.134 ||
kṣiptvā gandhodakaṃ śaṅkhe yaḥ snāpayati keśavam |
namo nārāyaṇāyeti mucyate yonisaṅkaṭān || 6.135 ||
nādyaṃ taḍāgajaṃ vāri vāpīkūpahradādijam |
gāṅgeyaṃ ca bhavetsarvaṃ kṛtaṃ śaṅkhe kalipriya || 6.136 ||
trailokye yāni tīrthāni vāsudevasya cājñayā |
śaṅkhe tiṣṭhanti viprendra tasmātśaṅkhaṃ sadārcayet || 6.137 ||
śaṅkhe kṛtvā tu pānīyaṃ sapuṣpaṃ satilākṣatam |
arghyaṃ dadāti devasya sasāgaradharāphalam || 6.138 ||
arghyaṃ dattvā tu śaṅkhena yaḥ karoti pradakṣiṇam |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtā tena saptadvīpā vasundharā || 6.139 ||
darśanenāpi śaṅkhasya kiṃ punaḥ sparśane kṛte |
vilayaṃ yānti pāpāni himaṃ sūryodaye yathā || 6.140 ||
nitye naimittike kāmye snānārcanavilepane |
śaṅkhamudvahate yastu śvetadvīpe vasecciram || 6.141 ||
natvā śaṅkhaṃ kare dhṛtvā mantreṇānena vaiṣṇavaḥ |
yaḥ snāpayati govindaṃ tasya puṇyamanantakam || 6.142 ||
mantraḥ
tvaṃ purā sāgarotpanno viṣṇunā vidhṛtaḥ kare |
mānitaḥ sarvadevaiśca pāñcajanya namo'stu te || 6.143 ||
tava nādena jīmūtā vitrasyanti surāsurāḥ |
śaśāṅkāyutadīptābha pāñcajanya namo'stu te || 6.144 ||
garbhā devārinārīṇāṃ vilīyante sahasradhā |
tava nādena pātāle pāñcajanya namo'stu te || 6.145 ||
vārāhe ca
dakṣiṇāvartaśaṅkhena tilamiśrodakena ca |
udake nābhimātre tu yaḥ kuryādabhiṣecanam || 6.146 ||
prāksrotasi ca nadyāṃ vai narastvekāgramānasaḥ |
yāvajjīvakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati || 6.147 ||
dakṣiṇāvartaśaṅkhena pātre auḍumbare sthitam |
udakaṃ yaḥ pratīccheta śirasā kṛṣṇamānasaḥ |
tasya janmakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva naśyati || 6.148 ||
āgame
bṛhattvaṃ snigdhatācchatvaṃ śaṅkhasyeti guṇatrayam || 6.149 ||
āvartabhaṅgadoṣastu hemayogānna jāyate |
nālikāyāṃ svabhāvena yadi chidraṃ bhavennahi || 6.150 ||
| iti|
ghaṇṭāvādyaṃ ca nitarāṃ snānakāle praśasyate |
yato bhagavato viṣṇostatsadā paramaṃ priyam || 6.151 ||
āvāhanārghye dhūpe ca puṣpanaivedyayojane |
nityametāṃ prayuñjīta tanmantreṇābhimantritām || 6.152 ||
tanmantraḥ
jayadhvaniṃ tato mantramātaḥ svāhetyudīrya ca |
abhyarcya vādayan ghaṇṭāṃ dhūpaṃ nīcaiḥ pradāpayet || 6.153 ||
pūjākālaṃ vinānyatra hitaṃ nāsyāḥ pracālanam |
na tayā ca vinā kuryātpūjanaṃ siddhilālasaḥ || 6.154 ||
atha ghaṇṭāmāhātmyam
uktaṃ ca skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
snānārcanakriyākāle ghaṇṭānādaṃ karoti yaḥ |
purato vāsudevasya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 6.155 ||
varṣakoṭisahasrāṇi varṣakoṭiśatāni ca |
vasate devaloke tu apsarogaṇasevitaḥ || 6.156 ||
sarvavādyamayī ghaṇṭā keśavasya sadā priyā |
vādanāllabhate puṇyaṃ yajñakoṭisamudbhavam || 6.157 ||
vāditraninadaistūryagītamaṅgalanisvanaiḥ |
yaḥ snāpayati govindaṃ jīvanmukto bhaveddhi saḥ || 6.158 ||
vāditrāṇāmabhāve tu pūjākāle hi sarvadā |
ghaṇṭāśabdo naraiḥ kāryaḥ sarvavādyamayī yataḥ || 6.159 ||
sarvavādyamayī ghaṇṭā devadevasya vallabhā |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena ghaṇṭānādaṃ tu kārayet || 6.160 ||
manvantarasahasrāṇi manvantaraśatāni ca |
ghaṇṭānādena deveśaḥ prīto bhavati keśavaḥ || 6.161 ||
viṣṇudharmottare śrībhagavatprahlādasaṃvāde
śṛṇu daityendra vakṣyāmi ghaṇṭāmāhātmyamuttamam |
prahlāda tvatsamo nāsti madbhakto bhuvanatraye || 6.162 ||
