mama nāmāṅkitā ghaṇṭā purato mama tiṣṭhati |
arcitā vaiṣṇavagṛhe tatra māṃ viddhi daityaja || 6.163 ||
vainateyāṅkitāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ sudarśanayutāṃ yadi |
mamāgre sthāpayedyastu dehe tasya vasāmyaham || 6.164 ||
yastu vādayate ghaṇṭā vainateyena cihnitām |
dhūpe nīrājanae snāne pūjākāle vilepane || 6.165 ||
mamāgre pratyahaṃ vatsa pratyekaṃ labhate phalam |
mamāyutaṃ goyutaṃ ca cāndrāyaṇaśatodbhavam || 6.166 ||
vidhibāhyakṛtā pūjā saphalā jāyate nṝṇām |
ghaṇṭānādena tusṭo'haṃ prayacchāmi svakaṃ padam || 6.167 ||
nāgāricihnitā ghaṇṭā rathāṅgena samanvitā |
vādanātkurute nāśaṃ janmamṛtyubhayasya ca || 6.168 ||
garuḍenāṅktāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ dṛṣṭvāhaṃ pratyahaṃ sadā |
prītiṃ karoti daityendra lakṣmīṃ prāpya yathādhanaḥ || 6.169 ||
dṛṣṭvāmṛtaṃ yathā devāḥ prītiṃ kurvantyaharniśam |
suparṇe ca tathā prītiṃ ghaṇṭāśikharasaṃsthite || 6.170 ||
svakareṇa prakurvanti ghaṇṭānādaṃ svabhaktitaḥ |
madīyārcanakāle tu phalaṃ koṭyaindavaṃ kalau || 6.171 ||
anyatra ca
ghaṇṭādaṇḍasya śikhare sacakraṃ sthāpayettu yaḥ |
garuḍaṃ vai priyaṃ viṣṇoḥ sthāpitaṃ bhuvanatrayam || 6.172 ||
sacakra ghaṇṭānādaṃ tu mṛtyukāle śṛṇoti yaḥ |
pāpakoṭiyutasyāpi naśyanti yamakiṅkarāḥ || 6.173 ||
sarve doṣāḥ pralīyante ghaṇṭānāde kṛte harau |
devatānāṃ munīndrāṇāṃ pitṝṇāmutsavo bhavet || 6.174 ||
abhāve vainateyasya cakrasyāpi na saṃśayaḥ |
ghaṇṭānādena bhaktānāṃ prasādaṃ kurute hariḥ || 6.175 ||
gṛhe yasmin bhavennityaṃ ghaṇṭā nāgārisaṃyutā |
na sarpāṇāṃ bhayaṃ tatra nāgnividyutsamudbhavam || 6.176 ||
yasya ghaṇṭā gṛhe nāsti śaṅkhaśca purato hareḥ |
kathaṃ bhāgavataṃ nāma gīyate tasya dehinaḥ || 6.177 ||
| iti|
ato bhagavataḥ prītyai ghaṇṭā śrīgaruḍānvitā |
saṅgṛhyā vaiṣṇavairyatnāccakreṇopari maṇḍitā || 6.178 ||
snāne śaṅkhādivādyāṃ tu nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ hareḥ |
gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ purāṇādipaṭhanaṃ ca praśasyate || 6.179 ||
atha snāne vādyādimāhātmyam
skandapurāṇe
snānakāle tu kṛṣṇasya śaṅkhādīnāṃ tu vādanam |
kurute brahmaloke tu vasate brahmavāsaram || 6.180 ||
snānakāle tu samprāpte kṛṣṇasyāgre tu nartanam |
gītaṃ caiva punātyatra ṛcoktaṃ vadanena hi || 6.181 ||
tatraiva śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
mṛdaṅgavādyena yutaṃ paṇavena samanvitam |
arcanaṃ vāsudevasya sanṛtyaṃ mokṣadaṃ nṝṇām || 6.182 ||
gītaṃ vādyaṃ ca nṛtyaṃ ca tathā pustakavācanam |
pūjākāle tu kṛṣṇasya sarvadā keśavapriyam || 6.183 ||
nṛtyavādyādyabhāve tu kuryātpustakavācanam |
pūjākāle tvidaṃ putra sarvadā prītidāyakam || 6.184|
pustakasyāpyabhāve tu viṣṇunāmasahasrakam |
stavarājaṃ miniśreṣṭha gajendrasya ca mokṣaṇam || 6.185 ||
pūjākāle tu devasya gītāstotramanusmṛtiḥ |
pañcastaavā mahābhāga mahāprītikarā hareḥ || 6.186 ||
vihāya gītavādyāni pūjākāle sadā hareḥ |
paṭhanīyaṃ mahābhaktyā viṣṇornāmasahasrakam || 6.187 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye
snānakāle tu kṛṣṇasya jayaśabdaṃ karoti yaḥ |
karatāḍanasaṃyuktaṃ gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ prakurvate || 6.188 ||
unmattaceṣṭāṃ kurvāṇo hasan jalpan yathecchayā |
nottānaśāyī bhavati māturaṅke nareśvara || 6.189 ||
atha sahasranāmamāhātmyam
tatraiva
snānakāle tu devasya paṭhennāmasahasrakam |
pratyaksaraṃ labhetpuṇyaṃ kapilāgośatodbhavam || 6.190 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
kṛtvā nāmasahasreṇa stutiṃ tasya mahātmanaḥ |
viyogamāpnoti naraḥ sarvānarthairna saṃśayaḥ || 6.191 ||
skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
viṣṇornāmasahasraṃ tu pūjākāle paṭhanti ye |
vedānāṃ caiva puṇyānāṃ phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ || 6.192 ||
ślokenaikena devarṣe sahasranāmakasya yat |
paṭhitena phalaṃ proktaṃ na tatkratuśatairapi || 6.193 ||
mantrahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ yatkṛtaṃ pūjanaṃ hareḥ |
paripūrṇaṃ bhavetsarvaṃ sahasranāmakīrtanāt || 6.194 ||
kiṃ ca
jñānājñānakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ paṭhitvā viṣṇusannidhau |
nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ viṣṇostu prajahāti mahārujam || 6.195 ||
brahmahatyādipāpāni kāmacārakṛtānyapi |
vilayaṃ yānti vai nūnamanyapāpe tu kā kathā || 6.196 ||
siddhyanti sarvakāryāṇi manasā cintitāni ca |
yaḥ paṭhetprātarutthāya viṣṇornāmasahasrakam || 6.197 ||
tatraiva śrīkṛṣṇārjunasaṃvāde
adhītāstena vai vedāḥ surāḥ sarvasamarcitāḥ |
nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ yo'dhīte muktistasya kare sthitā || 6.198 ||
kurvatpāpasahasrāṇi bhuñjāno'pi yatastataḥ |
paṭhennāmasahasrastu durgandhaṃ na sa paśyati || 6.199 ||
muktvā nāmasahasrastu nānyo dharmo'sti kaścana |
kalau prāpte guḍākeśa satyametanmayeritam || 6.200 ||
yajñairdānaistapabhiśca stavaiḥ prītirna me'rjuna |
santuṣṭistu na cānyena vinā nāmasahasrakam || 6.201 ||
stavaṃ nāmasahasrākhyaṃ ye na jānanti vai kalau |
bhramanti te nar¸a loke sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ || 6.202 ||
stavaṃ nāmasahasrākhyaṃ likhitaṃ yasya veśmani |
pūjyate mama sānnidhye pūjāṃ gṛhṇāmi tasya vai || 6.203 ||
yasminnāmasahasraṃ me gṛhe tiṣṭhati sarvadā |
likhitaṃ pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha tatra no viśate kaliḥ || 6.204 ||
tasmāttvamapi kaunteya madbhakto manmanā bhava |
paṭhannāmasahasraṃ me sarvān kāmānavāpsyasi || 6.205 ||
ahamārādhitaḥ pūrvaṃ brahmaṇā lokakartṛṇā |
tato nāmasahasraṃ me prāptaṃ lokahitaṃ param || 6.206 ||
nāradena tataḥ pūrvaṃ prāptaṃ ca parameṣṭhinaḥ |
nāradena tataḥ proktavṛṣīṇāmūrdhvaretasām || 6.207 ||
ṛṣibhistu mahābāho devaloke prakāśitam |
martyaloke manuṣyāṇāṃ vyāsena paribhāṣitam || 6.208 ||
tapasogreṇa mahatā śaṅkareṇa mahātmanā |
matprasādādanuprāptaṃ guhyānāmuttamottamam || 6.209 ||
dattaṃ bhavānyai rudreṇa nāmnāṃ me hi sahasrakam |
viśrutaṃ triṣu lokeṣu mayā te parikīrtitam || 6.210 ||
aśeṣārtiharaṃ pārtha mama nāmasahasrakam |
sadyaḥ prītikaraṃ puṇyaṃ surāṇāmamṛtaṃ yathā || 6.211 ||
aṣṭādaśapurāṇānāṃ sāremetaddhanañjaya |
mayoddhṛtya samākhyātaṃ tava nāmasahasrakam || 6.212 ||
sahasranāmamāhātmyaṃ devo jānāti śaṅkaraḥ |
sahasranāmamāhātmyaṃ yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi |
aparādhasahasreṇa na sa lipyetkadācana || 6.213 ||
atha śrībhagavadgītāmāhātmyam
skānde avantīkhaṇḍe śrīvyāsoktau
gītā sugītā kartavyā kimanyaiḥ śāstravistaraiḥ |
yā svayaṃ padmanābhasya mukhapadmādviniḥsṛtā || 6.214 ||
sarvaśāstramayī gītā sarvadevamayī yataḥ |
sarvadharmamayī yasmāttasmādetāṃ samabhyaset || 6.215 ||
śālagrāmaśilāgre tu gītādhyāyaṃ paṭhettu yaḥ |
manvantarasahasrāṇi vasate brahmaṇaḥ pure || 6.216 ||
hatvā hatvā jagatsarvaṃ muṣitvā sacarācaram |
pāpairna lipyate caiva gītādhyāyī kathañcana || 6.217 ||
teneṣṭaṃ kratubhiḥ sarvairdattaṃ tena gavāyutam |
gītāmabhyasyatā nityaṃ tenāptaṃ padamavyayam || 6.218 ||
gītādhyāyaṃ paṭhedyastu ślokaṃ ślokārdhameva vā |
bhavapāpavinirmukto yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam || 6.219 ||
yo nityaṃ viśvarūpākhyamadhyāyaṃ paṭhati dvijaḥ |
vibhūtiṃ devadevasya tasya puṇyaṃ vadāmyaham || 6.220 ||
vedairadhītairyatpuṇyaṃ setihāsaiḥ purātanaiḥ |
ślokenaikena tatpuṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 6.221 ||
ābrahmastambaparyantaṃ jagattṛptiṃ karoti saḥ |
viśvarūpaṃ sadādhyāyaṃ vibhūtiṃ ca paṭhettu yaḥ || 6.222 ||
ahanyahani yo martyo gītādhyāyaṃ paṭhettu vai |
dvātriṃśadaparādhāṃstu kṣamate tasya keśavaḥ || 6.223 ||
likhitvā vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca gītāśāstraṃ prayacchati |
dine dine ca yajate hariṃ cātra na saṃśayaḥ || 6.224 ||
caturṇāmeva vedānāṃ sāramuddhṛtya viṣṇunā |
trailokyasyopakārāya gītāśāstraṃ prakāśitam || 6.225 ||
bhāratāmṛtasarvasvaṃ viṣṇorvaktrādviniḥsṛtam |
gītāgaṅgodakaṃ pītvā punarjanma na vidyate || 6.226 ||
dharmaṃ cārthaṃ ca kāmaṃ ca mokṣaṃ cāpīcchatā sadā |
śrotavyā paṭhanīyā ca gītā kṛṣṇamukhodgatā || 6.227 ||
yo naraḥ paṭhate nityaṃ gītāśāstraṃ dine dine |
vimuktaḥ sarvapāpebhyo yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam || 6.228 ||
atha purāṇapāṭhādimāhātmyam
pādme devadūtavikuṇḍalasaṃvāde
vicārayanti ye śāstraṃ vedābhyāsaratāśca ye |
purāṇaṃ saṃhitāṃ ye ca śrāvayanti paṭhanti ca || 6.229 ||
vyākurvanti smṛtiṃ ye ca ye dharmapratibodhakāḥ |
vedānteṣu niṣaṇṇā ye tairiyaṃ jagatī dhṛtā || 6.230 ||
tattadabhyāsamāhātmyaiḥ sarve te hatakilbiṣāḥ |
gacchanti brahmaṇo lokaṃ yatra moho na vidyate || 6.231 ||
tatraiva śrīśivomāsaṃvāde
antaṃ gato'pi vedānāṃ sarvaśāstrārthavedyapi |
puṃso'śrutapurāṇasya na samyaggatidarśanam || 6.232 ||
vedārthādadhikaṃ manye purāṇārthaṃ ca bhāmini |
purāṇamanyathā kṛtvā tiryagyonimavāpnuyāt || 6.233 ||
bṛhannāradīye ca
purāṇeṣvarthavādatvaṃ ye vadanti narādhamāḥ |
tairarjitāni puṇyāni kṣayaṃ yānti dvijottamāḥ || 6.234 ||
purāṇeṣu dvijaśreṣṭhāḥ sarvadharmapravaktṛṣu |
pravadantyarthavādatvaṃ ye te narakabhājanāḥ || 6.235 ||
anāyāsena yaḥ puṇyānīcchatīha dvijottamāḥ |
śrotavyāni bhaktyā tenaiva purāṇāni na saṃśayaḥ || 6.236 ||
purārjitāni pāpāni naśamāyānti tasya vai |
purāṇaśravaṇe buddhistasyaiva bhavati dhruvam || 6.237 ||
kiṃ ca
purāṇe vartamāne'pi pāpapāśena yantritaḥ |
anādṛtyānyagāthāsu saktabuddhiḥ pravartate || 6.238 ||
atha vastrārpaṇam
snānamudrāṃ pradarśyātha śuddhasūkṣmāṅgavāsasā |
śanaiḥ saṃmārjya gātrāṇi divye vastre samarpayet || 6.239 ||
madhyadeśīyanepathyādyanusāreṇa bhaktitaḥ |
ke'pyatra kañcukosṇīṣādyambarāṇyarpayanti ca || 6.240 ||
tathā ca matsye
tattaddeśīyabhūṣāḍhyāṃ tattanmūrtiṃ ca kārayet || 6.241 ||
ekādaśaskandhe
alaṅkurvīta saprema madbhakto māṃ yathocitam || 6.242 ||
bhaviṣye ca
vāsobhiḥ pūjayedviṣṇuṃ ynyevātmapriyāṇi tu |
tathānyaiśca śubhiardivyairarcayecca dukūlaiḥ || 6.243 ||
vāsāṃsi ca vicitrāṇi sāravanti śucīni ca |
dhūpitāni harerdadyātvikeśāni navāni ca || 6.244| ||
bhūṣayedbahubhirvastrairvicitraiḥ kañcukādibhiḥ |
bhogānantarameveti bahūnāṃ sammataṃ satām || 6.245 ||
atha śrīmadaṅgamārjanamāhātmyam
dvārakāmāhātmye
kṛṣṇaṃ snānārdragātraṃ tu vastreṇa parimārjati |
tasya lakṣārjitasyāpi bhavetpāpasya mārjanam || 6.246 ||
atha vastrārpaṇamāhātmyam
nārasiṃhe
vastrābhyāmacyutaṃ bhaktyā paridhāpya vicitritam |
somaloke vasitvā tu viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 6.247 ||
skānde śrīśivomāsaṃvāde
vastrāṇi supavitrāṇi sāravanti mṛdūni ca |
rūpavanti harerdattvā sadaśāni navāni ca || 6.248 ||
yāvadvastrasya tantunā parimāṇaṃ bhavatyatha |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 6.249 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
rāṅkavasya pradānena sarvān kānāmavāpnuyāt |
kārpāsikaṃ vastrayugaṃ yaḥ pradadyājjanārdane || 6.250 ||
yāvanti tasya tantūni hastamātramitāni tu |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 6.251 ||
mahārghatā yathā tasya sādhudeśodbhavo yathā |
sūkṣmatā ca yathā viprāstathā proktaṃ phalaṃ mahat || 6.252 ||
kiṃ ca tatraivānyatra
śuklavastrapradānena śriyamāpnotyanuttamām |
mahārajanaraktena saubhāgyaṃ mahadaśnute || 6.253 ||
tathā kuṅkumaraktena strīṇāṃ vallabhatāṃ vrajet |
nīlīraktaṃ vinā raktaṃ śeṣaraṅgairdvijottamāḥ |
dattvā bhavati dharmātmā sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ || 6.254 ||
kauśeyāni ca vastrāṇi sumṛdūni laghūni ca |
yaḥ prayacchati devāya so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 6.255 ||
rāṅkavā mṛgalomyāśca kadalyāśca tathā śubhā |
yo dadyāddevadevāya so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 6.256 ||
nānābhaktivicitrāṇi cīrajāni navāni ca |
dattvā vāsāṃsi śubhrāṇi rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet || 6.257 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye ca
nānādeśasamudbhūtaiḥ suvastraiśca sukomalaiḥ |
dhūpayitvā subhaktyā ca pradhāpayati mādhavam |
manvantarāṇi vasate tantusaṃkhyaṃ harergṛhe || 6.258 ||
atha vastrārpaṇe niṣiddham
viṣṇudharmottare
nīlīraktaṃ tathā jīrṇaṃ vastramanyadhṛtaṃ tathā |
devadevāya yo dadyātsa tu pāpairhi yujyate || 6.259 ||
atrāpavādaḥ
tatraiva
āvike paṭṭavastre ca nīlīrāgo na duṣyati || 6.260 ||
atha yajñopavītam
vastrasyārpaṇamudrāṃ ca pradarśya paridhāpya tat |
upavītaṃ samārpyātha tanmudrāṃ ca pradarśayet || 6.261 ||
athopavītārpaṇamāhātmyam
trivṛtśuklaṃ ca pītaṃ ca paṭṭasūtrādinirmitam |
yajñopavītaṃ govinde dattvā vedāntago bhavet || 6.262 ||
nandipurāṇe
yajñopavītadānena surebhyo brāhmaṇāya vā |
bhavedvidvāṃścaturvedī śuddhadhīrnātra saṃśayaḥ || 6.263 ||
atha pādyatilakācamanāni
atha pādyaṃ nivedyādāvūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ manoharam |
nirmāya bhāle kṛṣṇasya dadyādācamanaṃ tataḥ || 6.264 ||
atha bhūṣaṇam
tato devāya divyāni bhūṣaṇāni nivedya ca |
paridhāpya yathāyuktaṃ tanmudrāṃ ca pradarśayet || 6.265 ||
atha bhūṣaṇārpaṇamāhātmyam
skānde śivomāsaṃvāde
maṇimauktikasaṃyuktaṃ dattvābharaṇamuttamam |
svaśaktyā bhūṣaṇaṃ dattvā agniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet || 6.266 ||
kiṃ ca
guñjāmātraṃ suvarṇasya yo dadyādviṣṇumūrdhani |
indrasya bhavane tiṣṭhedyāvadāhūtasamplavam || 6.267 ||
tasmādābharaṇaṃ devi dātavyaṃ viṣṇave sadā |
nārāyaṇo bhavetprīto bhaktyā paramayā mubhe || 6.268 ||
nandipurāṇe
alaṅkāraṃ tu yo dadyādviprāyātha surāya vā |
sa gacchedvāruṇaṃ lokaṃ nānābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ |
jātaḥ pṛthivyāṃ kāena bhaveddvīpapatirnṛpaḥ || 6.269 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
karṇābharaṇadānena bhavecchrutidharo naraḥ |
aśvamedhamavāpnoti saubhāgyaṃ cāpi vindati || 6.270 ||
karṇapūrapradānena śrutiṃ sarvatra vindati || 6.271 ||
mūrdhābharaṇadānena bhūrdhanyo bhūtale bhavet |
catuḥsamudravalayāṃ praśāsti ca vasundharām || 6.272 ||
tatraiva tṛtīyakhaṇḍe
vibhūṣaṇapradānena mūrdhanyo bhūtale bhavet |
ramyāṇi ratnacitrāṇi saurvarṇāni dvijottamāḥ || 6.273 ||
dattvābharaṇajātāni rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet |
pādāṅgulīyadānena guhyakādhipatirbhavet || 6.274 ||
pādābharaṇadānena sthānaṃ sarvatra vindati || 6.275 ||
śroṇīsūtrapradānena mahīṃ sāgaramekhalām |
praśāsti nihatāmitro nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 6.276 ||
saubhāgyaṃ mahadāpnoti kiṅkiṇīṃ pradadaddhareḥ |
hastāṅgulīyadānena paraṃ saubhāgyamāpnuyāt || 6.277 ||
tathaivāṅgadadānena rājā bhavati bhūtale |
keyūradānādbhavati śaturpakṣakṣayaṅkaraḥ || 6.278 ||
graiveyakāṇi dattvā ca sarvaśāstrārthavidbhavet |
nāryaśca vaśagāstasya bhavati dvijapuṅgavāḥ || 6.279 ||
dattvā pratisarānmukhyānna bhūtairabhibhūyate || 6.280 ||
kiṃ ca tatraiva
kṛtrimaṃ ca pradātavyaṃ tathaivābharaṇaṃ divjāḥ |
pratirūpakṛtaṃ dattvā kṣipraṃ puṣṭyā prayujyate || 6.281 ||
pādme
śaṅkhacakragadādīni pādādyavayaveṣu ca |
sauvarṇābharaṇaṃ kṛtvā viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 6.282 ||
nārasiṃhe
suvarṇābharaṇairdivyairhārakeyūrakuṇḍalaiḥ |
mukuṭaiḥ kaṭakādyaiśca yo viṣṇuṃ pūjayennaraḥ || 6.283 ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitaḥ |
indraloke vaseddhīmān yāvadindraścaturdaśa || 6.284 ||
garuḍapurāṇe
yasyārcā tiṣṭhate viṣṇorhemabhūṣaṇabhūṣitā |
ratnairmuktāviśeṣeṇa ahanyahani vāsava || 6.285 ||
kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi tasya vai bhuvane hareḥ |
vāso bhavati devendra kathitaṃ brahmaṇā mama || 6.286 ||
yaḥ paśyati naraḥ kṛṣṇaṃ hemabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam |
sakṛdbhaktyā kalau śakra punātyāsaptamaṃ kulam || 6.287 ||
| iti|
bahulaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ bhogātpaścādevānulepanam |
puṣpaṃ cecchanti santo'nulepanārcānubhūṣaṇam || 6.288 ||
samprārthyātha prabhuṃ prāgvatnivedya śucipāduke |
vādyagītātapatrānyaiḥ pūjāsthānaṃ punarnayet || 6.289 ||
prāgvaddattvāsanādīni gandhaṃ tanmudrayārpayet |
śaṅkhe nidhāya tulasīdalenaivātha candanam || 6.290 ||
atha gandhaḥ
āgame
candanāgurukarpūrapaṅkaṃ gandhamihocyate || 6.291 ||
gāruḍe
kastūrikāyā dvau bhāgau catvāraścandanasya tu |
kuṅkumasya trayaścaikaḥ śaśinaḥ syāccatuḥsamam || 6.292 ||
karpūraṃ candanaṃ darpaḥ kuṅkumaṃ ca catuḥsama |
sarvaṃ gandhamiti proktaṃ samastasuravallabham || 6.293 ||
vārāhe
karpūraṃ kuṅkumaścaiva varaṃ tagarameva ca |
rasyaṃ ca candanaṃ caiva aguruṃ guggulaṃ tathā |
etairvilepanaṃ dadyātśubhaṃ cāru vicakṣaṇaḥ || 6.294 ||
viṣṇudharmottarāgnipurāṇayoḥ
sugandhaiśca murāmāṃsīkarpūrāgurucandanaiḥ |
tathānyaiśca śubhairdravyairarcayejjagatīpatim || 6.295 ||
vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām
karpūrāgurumiśreṇa candanenānulepayet |
mṛgadarpaṃ viśeṣeṇa abhīṣṭaṃ cakrapāṇinaḥ || 6.296 ||
skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
gandhebhyaścandanaṃ puṇyaṃ candanādagururvaraḥ |
kṛṣṇāgurustataḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kuṅkumaṃ tu tato'dhikam || 6.297 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
na dātavyaṃ dvijaśreṣṭha ato'nyadanulepanam |
anulepanamukhyaṃ tu candanaṃ parikīrtitam || 6.298 ||
nāradīye
yathā viṣṇoḥ sadābhīṣṭaṃ naivedyaṃ śālisambhavam |
śukenoktaṃ purāṇe ca tathā tulasicandanam || 6.299 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ ca
saṃghṛṣya tulasīkāṣṭhaṃ yo dadyādrāmamūrdhani |
karpūrāgurukastūrīkuṅkumaṃ na ca tatsamam || 6.300 ||
athānulepanamāhātmyam
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde śaṅkhamāhātmye
vilepayanti deveśaṃ śaṅkhe kṛtvā tu candanam |
paramātmā parāṃ prītiṃ karoti śatvārṣikīm || 6.301 ||
gāruḍe
tulasīdalalagnena candanena janārdanam |
vilepayanti yo nityaṃ labhate vāñchitaṃ phalam || 6.302 ||
nārasiṃhe
kuṅkumāguruśrīkhaṇḍakardamairacyutākṛtim |
vilipya bhaktyā rājendra kalpakoṭiṃ vaseddivi || 6.303 ||
viṣṇudharmottarāgnipurāṇayoḥ
candanāgurukarpūrakuṅkumośīrapadmakaiḥ |
anulipto harirbhaktyā varān bhogān prayacchati || 6.304 ||
kāleyakaṃ turuṣkaṃ ca raktacandanamuttamam || 6.305 ||
nṛṇāṃ bhavanti dattāni puṇyāni puruṣottama || 6.306 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
candanenānulepyainaṃ candraloka avāpnuyāt |
śārīrairmānasairduḥkhaistathaiva ca vimucyate || 6.307 ||
kuṅkumenānulepyainaṃ sūryaloke mahīyate |
saubhāgyamuttamaṃ loke tathā prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 6.308 ||
karpūreṇānulpiyainaṃ vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnuyāt |
śārīrairmānasairduḥkhaistathaiva ca vimucyate || 6.309 ||
dattvā mṛgamadaṃ mukhyaṃ yaśasā ca virājate |
dattvā jātaphalakṣodaṃ kriyāsāphalyamaśnute || 6.310 ||
ramyeṇāgurusāreṇa anulipya janārdanam |
saubhāgyamatulaṃ loke balaṃ prāpnoti cottamam || 6.311 ||
tathā bakulaniryāsairagniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet |
bakulāgurumiśreṇa candanena sugandhinā |
samālipya jagannāthaṃ puṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet || 6.312 ||
ekīkṛtya tu sarvāṇi samālipya janārdanam |
aśvamedhasya mukhyasya phalaṃ prāpnotyasaṃśayam || 6.313 ||
yo'nulimpeta deveśaṃ kīrtitairanulepanaiḥ |
pārthivādyāni yāvanti paramāṇūni tatra vai || 6.314 ||
tāvadabdāni lokeṣu kāmacārī bhavatyasau |
keśasaugandhyajananaṃ kṛtvā mṛgamadaṃ naraḥ || 6.315 ||
sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute || 6.316 ||
yaḥ prayacchati gandhāni gandhayuktīkṛtāni ca |
gandharvatvaṃ dhruvaṃ tasya saubhāgyaṃ tathottamam || 6.317 ||
atha śrītulasīkāṣṭhacandanamāhātmyam
gāruḍe śrīnāradadhundhumāranṛpasaṃvāde
yo dadāti harernityaṃ tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam |
yugāni vasate svarge hyanantāni narottama || 6.318 ||
mahāviṣṇau kalau bhaktyā dattvā tulasīcandanam |
yo'rcayenmālatīpuṣpairna bhūyastanapo bhavet || 6.319 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhasambhūtaṃ candanaṃ yacchato hareḥ |
nirdahetpātakaṃ sarvaṃ pūrvajanmaśataiḥ kṛtam || 6.320 ||
sarveṣāmapi devānāṃ tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam |
pitṝṇāṃ ca viśeṣeṇa sadābhīṣṭaṃ hareryathā || 6.321 ||
mṛtyukāle tu samprāpte tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam |
bhavate yasya dehe tu harirbhūtvā hariṃ vrajet || 6.322 ||
tāvanmalayajaṃ viṣṇorbhāti kṛṣṇāgururnṛpa |
yāvanna dṛśyate puṇyaṃ tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam || 6.323 ||
tāvatkastūrikāmodaḥ karpūrasya sugandhinā |
yāvanna dīyate viṣṇostulasīkāṣṭhacandanam || 6.324 ||
kalau yacchanti ye viṣṇau tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam |
dhundhumāra na vai martyāḥ punarāyānti te bhuvi || 6.325 ||
yo hi bhāgavato bhūtvā kalau tulasīcandanam |
nārpayati sadā viṣṇorna sa bhāgavato naraḥ || 6.326 ||
prahlādasaṃhitāyāṃ
na tena sadṛśo loke vaiṣṇavo vidyate bhuvi |
yaḥ prayacchati kṛṣṇāya tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam || 6.327 ||
tulasīdārujātena candanena kalau naraḥ |
vilipya bhaktito viṣṇuṃ ramate sannidhau hareḥ || 6.328| ||
tulasīkāṣṭhajātena candanena vilepanam |
yaḥ kuryādviṣṇutoṣāya kapilāgophalaṃ labhet || 6.329 ||
tulasīkāṣṭhasambhūtaṃ candanaṃ yastu sevate |
mṛtyukāle viśeṣeṇa kṛtapāpo'pi mucyate || 6.330 ||
yo dadāti pitṝṇāṃ tu tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam |
teṣāṃ sa kurute tṛptiṃ śrāddhe vai śatavārṣikīm || 6.331 ||
viṣṇudharmottare ca
tulasīcandanāktāṅgaḥ kurute kṛṣṇapūjanam |
pūjanena dinaikena labhate śatavārṣikīm || 6.332 ||
vilepanārthaṃ kṛṣṇasya tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam |
mandire vasate yasya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 6.333 ||
tilaprasthāṣṭakaṃ dattvā yatpuṇyaṃ cottarāyaṇe |
tattulyaṃ jāyate puṇyaṃ prasādāccakrapāṇinaḥ || 6.334 ||
| iti|
deyaṃ malayajābhāve śītalatvātkadambajam |
yathā kiñcitsugandhitvāccandanaṃ devadārujam || 6.335 ||
gāruḍe
harermalayajaṃ śreṣṭhamabhāve devadārujam || 6.336 ||
athānulepe niṣiddhāni
viṣṇudharmottare
dāridryaṃ padmakaṃ kuryādasvāsthyaṃ raktacandanam |
uśīraṃ cittavibhraṃśamanye kuryurupadravam || 6.337 ||
| iti|
padmakādi na dātavyamaihikaṃ hīcchatā sukham |
mukhyālābhe tu tatsarvaṃ dātavyaṃ bhagavatparaiḥ || 6.338 ||
tato bhagavataḥ kuryādanulepādanantaram |
vidvān vicitrairvyajanaiścāmarairapi vījanam || 6.339 ||
vījanamāhātmyaṃ ca
viṣṇudharmottare
anulipya jagannāthaṃ tālavṛntena vījayet |
vāyulokamavāpnoti puruṣastena karmaṇā || 6.340 ||
cāmarairvījayedyastu devadevaṃ janārdanam |
tilaprasthapradānasya phalamāpnotyasaṃśayam || 6.341 ||
vyajanenātha vastreṇa subhaktyā mātariśvana |
devadevasya rājendra kurute tāpavāraṇam || 6.342 ||
tatkule yamaloke tu śamate nārako daraḥ |
vāyulokānmahīpāla na cyutirvidyate punaḥ || 6.343 ||
calaccāmaravātena kṛṣṇaṃ santoṣayennaraḥ || 6.344 ||
tasyottamāṅgaṃ deveśa stuvate svamukhena vai |
uṣṇakāle tvidaṃ jñeyaṃ yatsantaḥ pauṣamāghayoḥ |
śītalatvānmalayajamapi naivārpayanti hi || 6.345 ||
tathā coktam
na śīte śītalaṃ deyam || 6.346 ||
| iti|
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse
snāpaniko nāma ṣaṣṭho vilāsaḥ |
aṣṭamavilāsaḥ prātararcasamāpanaḥ
śrīcaitanyaprabhuṃ vande yatpādāśrayavīryataḥ |
saṃgṛhṇātyākaravrātādraṅko ratnāvalīmayam || 8.1 ||
atha dhūpanaṃ
tataśca dhūpamutsṛjya nīcaistanmudrayārpayet |
kṛṣṇaṃ saṅkīrtayan ghaṇṭaṃ vāmahastena vādayan || 8.2 ||
tathā ca bahvṛcapariśiṣṭe
dhūpasya vijane caiva dhūpenāṅgavidhūpane |
nīrājaneṣu sarveṣu viṣṇornāmāni kīrtayet || 8.3 ||
jayaghoṣaṃ prakurvīta kāruṇyaṃ cābhikīrtayet |
tathā maṅgalaghoṣaṃ ca jagadbījasya ca stutim || 8.4 ||
anyatra ca
tataḥ samarpayeddhūpaṃ ghaṇṭavādyajayasvanaiḥ |
dhūpasthānaṃ samabhyarcya tarjanyā vāmayā hareḥ || 8.5 ||
tatra mantraḥ
vanaspatirasotpanno gandhāḍhyo gandha uttamaḥ |
aghreyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ dhūpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatam || 8.6 ||
atha dhūpaḥ
vāmanapurāṇe
ruhikākhyaṃ kaṇo dārusihlakaṃ cāguruḥ sitā |
śaṅkho jātiphalaṃ śrīśe dhūpāni syuḥ priyāṇi vai || 8.7 ||
mūlāgame
saguggulvaguruśīrasitājyamadhucandanaiḥ |
sārāṅgāravinikṣiptaiḥ kalpayeddhūpamuttamam || 8.8 ||
viṣṇudharmottare ca
tathaiva śubhagandhā ye dhūpaste jagataḥ pateḥ |
vāsudevasya dharmajñairnivedya dānaveśvara || 8.9 ||
atha dhūpeṣu niṣiddhaṃ
na dhūparthe jīvajatam || 8.10 ||
tatraivāpavādaḥ
vinā mṛgamadaṃ dhūpe jīvajātaṃ vivarjayet || 8.11 ||
kālikāpurāṇe
na yakṣadhūpaṃ vitarenmadhavaya kadacana || 8.12 ||
agnipurāṇe
na sallakijaṃ na tṛṇaṃ na śalkarasasambhrtaṃ dhūpam |
pratyaṅganirmuktaṃ dadyātkṛṣṇāya buddhimān || 8.13 ||
atha dhūpanamāhātmyam
nārasiṃhe śrīmarkandeyasatanikasamvade
mahiṣākhyaṃ gugguluṃ ca ājyayuktaṃ saśarkaram |
dhūpaṃ dadāti rajendra narasiṃhasya bhaktimān || 8.14 ||
sa dhūpitaḥ sarvadikṣu sarvapāpavivarjitaḥ |
apsarogaṇayuktena vimānena virājatā |
vāyulokaṃ samāsādya viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 8.15 ||
skānde
ye kṛṣṇaguruṇā kṛṣṇaṃ dhūpayanti kalau narāḥ |
sakarpūreṇa rājendra kṛṣṇatulyā bhavanti te || 8.16 ||
sājyena vai guggulunā sudhūpena janārdanam |
dhūpayitvā naro yāti padaṃ tasya sadāśivaṃ || 8.17 ||
aguruṃ tu sakarpūradivyacandanasaurabham |
dattvā nityaṃ harerbhaktyā kulānāṃ tārayecchatam || 8.18 ||
viṣṇudharmottaratrtiyakhaṇḍe
dhūpanamuttamaṃ tadvatsarvakamaphalapradam |
dhūpaṃ turuṣkakaṃ dattvā vahnistomaphalaṃ labhet || 8.19 ||
dattvā tu kṛtrimaṃ mukhyaṃ sarvakāmānavāpnuyāt |
gandhayuktakṛtaṃ dattvā yajñagosavamāpnuyāt || 8.20 ||
dattvā karpūraniryāsaṃ vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet |
vasante guggulaṃ dattvā vahnistomamavāpnuyāt || 8.21 ||
grīṣme candanasāreṇa rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet |
turuṣkasya pradānena pravṛṣyuttamatāṃ labhet || 8.22 ||
karpūradānāccharadi rājasūyamavāpnuyāt || 8.23 ||
hemante mrgadarpeṇa vājimedhaphalaṃ labhet |
śiśire'gurusarena sarvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 8.24 ||
padamuttamamāpnoti dhūpadaḥ puṣṭimaśnute |
dhūpalekha yathāivordhvaṃ nityameva prasarpati |
tathāivordhvagato nityaṃ dhūpadānādbhavennaraḥ || 8.25 ||
prahlādasaṃhitāyāṃ ca
yo dadāti harerdhūpaṃ tulasīkāṣṭhavahninā |
śatakratusamaṃ puṇyaṃ go'yutaṃ labhate phalaṃ || 8.26 ||
iti |
dhūpayecca tathā samyakśrīmadbhagavadālayam |
dhūpaśeṣaṃ tato bhaktyā svayaṃ seveta vaiṣṇavaḥ || 8.27 ||
tathā ca brāhme ambarisaṃ prati gautamapraśne
dhūpaśeṣaṃ tu kṛṣṇasya bhaktyā bhajasi bhūpate |
kṛtvā cārātrikaṃ viṣṇoḥ svamūrdhnā vandase nṛpa || 8.28 ||
atha śrībhagavadalayadhūpanamāhātmyaṃ
kṛṣṇāgurusamutthena dhūpena śrīdharālayam |
dhūpayedvaiṣṇavo yastu sa mukto narakārṇavat || 8.29 ||
dhūpaśeṣasevanamāhātmyam
pādme śrīgautamāmbarīṣasaṃvāde
tīrthakoṭiśatairdhauto yathā bhavati nirmalaḥ |
karoti nirmalaṃ dehaṃ dhūpaśeṣastathā hareḥ || 8.30 ||
na bhayaṃ vidyate tasya bhaumaṃ divyaṃ rāsātalam |
kṛṣṇadhūpāvaśeṣena yasyāṅgaṃ parivāsitam || 8.31 ||
nāpado vipadastasya bhavanti khalu dehinaḥ |
harerdattāvaśeṣena dhūpayedyastanuṃ sadā || 8.32 ||
nāsaukhyaṃ na bhayaṃ duḥkhaṃ nādhijaṃ naiva rogajam |
yaḥ sevayeddhūpaśeṣaṃ viṣṇoradbhutakarmaṇaḥ || 8.33 ||
krūrasattvabhayaṃ naiva na ca caurabhayaṃ kvacit |
sevayitvā harerdhūpaṃ nirmālyaṃ padayorjalam || 8.34 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye ca
āghrāṇaṃ yaddharerdattaṃ dhūpocchiṣṭasya sarvataḥ |
tadbhavavyāladaṣṭānāṃ bhavetkarmaviṣāpahaṃ || 8.35 ||
iti |
darśanādapi dhūpasya dhūpadānādijaṃ phalam |
sarvamanye'pi vindanti taccāgre vyatimeṣyati || 8.36 ||
atha dīpanaṃ
tathaiṭa dīpamutsṛjya prāgvadghaṇṭaṃ ca vādayan |
padābjādadṛgabjāntaṃ mudrayoccaiḥ pradīpayet || 8.37 ||
tatra mantraḥ
gautamīye
suprakāśo mahātejahsarvatastimirāpahaḥ |
sabāhyābhyantarajyotirdīpo'yaṃ pratigṛhyatām || 8.38 ||
atha dīpaḥ
dīpaṃ prajvalayetśaktau karpūrena ghṛtena vā |
gavyena tatrāsāmārthye tailenāpi sugandhinā || 8.39 ||
tathā ca nāradīyakalpe
saghṛtaṃ guggulaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ goghṛtadīpitam |
samastaparivārāya haraye śraddhayārpayet || 8.40 ||
bhaviṣyottare
ghṛtena dīpo dātavyo rājan tailena vā punaḥ || 8.41 ||
mahābhārate ca
haviṣā prathamaḥ kalpo dvitīyaścauṣadhorasaiḥ || 8.42 ||
atha dīpe niṣiddhaṃ
bhaviṣyottare
vasāmajjādibhirdīpo na tu deyaḥ kadācana || 8.43 ||
mahābhārate
vasāmajjāsthiniryāsairna kāryaḥ puṣṭimicchata || 8.44 ||
viṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe
nīlaraktadaśaṃ dīpaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || 8.45 ||
kālikāpurāṇe
dīpavṛkṣaśca kartavyastaijāsādyaiśca bhairava |
vṛkṣeṣu dīpo dātavyo na tu bhūmau kadācana || 8.46 ||
atha dīpamāhātmyaṃ
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde
prajvālya devadevasya karpūrena ca dīpakam |
aśvamedhamavāpnoti kulaṃ caiva samuddharet || 8.47 ||
atraivānyatra ca
yo dadāti mahīpāla kṛṣṇasyāgre tu dīpakam |
pātakaṃ tu samutsṛjya jyotīrūpaṃ labhetphalam || 8.48 ||
vārāhe
dīpaṃ dadāti yo devi madbhaktyā tu vyavasthitaḥ |
nātrāndhatvaṃ bhavettasya saptajanmani sundari || 8.49 ||
yastu dadyātpradīpaṃ me sarvataḥ sraddhayānvitaḥ |
svayamprabheṣu deśeṣu tasyotpattirvidhīyate || 8.50 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
dattaṃ svajyotiṣe jyotiryadvistārayati prabhām |
tadvaddharyati sajjyotirdātuḥ pāpatamo'pahaṃ || 8.51 ||
nārasiṃhe
ghṛtena vātha tailena dīpaṃ prajvalayennaraḥ |
viṣṇave vidhivadbhaktyā tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 8.52 ||
vihāya pāpaṃ sakalaṃ sahasrādityasaprabhaḥ |
jyotiṣmatā vimānena viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 8.53 ||
prahlādasaṃhitāyāṃ
ca tulasīpavakenaiva dīpaṃ yaḥ kurute hareḥ |
dīpalakṣasahasrāṇāṃ puṇyaṃ bhavati daityaja || 8.54 ||
iti |
paścāddīpaṃ ca taṃ bhaktyā mūrdhnā vandeta vaiṣṇavaḥ |
dhūpasyevekṣaṇāttasya labhante'nye'pi tatphalam || 8.55 ||
keciccānena dīpena śrīmūrtermūrdhni vaiṣṇavaḥ |
nīrājanamihecchanti mahānīrājane yathā || 8.56 ||
tathā ca rāmārcanacandrikāyāṃ dhūpānantaraṃ dīpaprasaṅge
ārātrikaṃ tu viṣamabahuvartisamanvitam |
abhyarcya rāmacandrāya vāmamadhyamathārpayet || 8.57 ||
namo dīpeśvarāyeti dadyātpuṣpāñjaliṃ tataḥ |
avadhūpyābhyarcya vādyairmūrdhni nīrājayetprabhum || 8.58 ||
iti |
ata eveṣyate tasya karābhyāṃ vandanaṃ ca taiḥ |
nāma cārātriketyādi vartyo'pi bahulāḥ samāḥ || 8.59 ||
prasaṅgāllikhyate'traiva śrīmadbhagavadālaye |
dīpadānasya māhātmyaṃ kārttikīyaṃ ca tadvinā || 8.60 ||
atha śrībhagavadālaye dīpapradānamāhātmyam
viṣṇudharmottare prathamakāṇḍe
dīpadānātparaṃ dānaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati |
keśavāyatane kṛtvā dīpavṛkṣāmanoharam |
atīva bhrājate lakṣmyā divamāsādya sarvataḥ || 8.61 ||
dīpamālāṃ prayacchanti ye narāḥ śārṅgiṇo gṛhe |
bhavanti te candrasamāḥ svargamāsādya mānavāḥ || 8.62 ||
dīpāgāraṃ naraḥ kṛtvā kūtāgāranibhaṃ śubham |
keśavālayamāsādya loke bhāti sa śakravat || 8.63 ||
yathojjvalo bhaveddīpaḥ sampradātāpi yādava |
tathā nityojjvalo loke nākapṛṣṭhe virājate || 8.64 ||
sadīpe ca yathā dese cakṣūṃsi phalavanti ca |
tathā dīpasya dataro bhavanti saphalekṣanaḥ || 8.65 ||
ekādaśyāṃ ca dvādaśyāṃ pratipakṣaṃ tu yo naraḥ |
dīpaṃ dadāti kṛṣṇāya tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 8.66 ||
suvarṇamaṇimuktāḍhyaṃ manojñamatisundaram |
dīpamālākulaṃ divyaṃ vimānamadhirohati || 8.67 ||
padmasutrodbhavaṃ vartti gandhatailena dīpakān |
virogaḥ subhagaścaiva dattvā bhavati mānavaḥ || 8.68 ||
dīpadanaṃ mahāpuṇyamanyadeveṣvapi dhruvam |
kiṃ punarvāsudevasyānantasya tu mahātmanaḥ || 8.69 ||
tatraiva tṛtīyakhaṇḍe
dīpaṃ cakṣuḥpradaṃ dadyāttathaivordhvagatipradam |
ūrdhvaṃ yathā dīpaśikhā dātā cordhvagatistathā || 8.70 ||
yāvadakṣinimeṣāṇi dīpo devālaye jvalet |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate || 8.71 ||
brhannāradīye vitihotraṃ prati yajñadhvajasya pūrvajanmavṛttakathane
pradīpaḥ sthāpitastatra suratārthaṃ dvijottama |
tenāpi mama duṣkarma nihśeṣaṃ kṣayamagatam || 8.72 ||
viṣṇudharme ca
vilīyate svahaste tu svatantre sati dīpakaḥ |
mahāphalo viṣṇugṛhe na datto narakāya saḥ || 8.73 ||
nāradīye mohinīṃ prati śrīrukmāṅgadoktau
tiṣṭhantu bahuvittāni dānārthaṃ varavarṇini |
hṛdayāyāsakartṛṇi dīpadānāddivaṃ vrajet || 8.74 ||
tasyāpyabhāve subhage paradīpaprabodhanam |
kartavyaṃ bhaktibhāvena sarvadānādhikaśca yat || 8.75 ||
iti |
sada kalaviśeṣe'pi bhaktyā bhagavadālaye |
mahādīpapradānasya mahimāpyatra likhyāte || 8.76 ||
atha mahādīpamāhātmyam
viṣṇudharmottare prathamakhaṇḍe
mahāvarttiḥ sadā deyā bhūmipāla mahāphalā |
kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣeṇa tatrāpi sa viśeṣataḥ || 8.77 ||
amāvasyā ca nirdiṣṭā dvādaśī ca mahāphalā |
aśvayujyāmatītāyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣaśca yo bhavet || 8.78 ||
amāvasyā tadā puṇyā dvādaśī ca viśeṣataḥ |
devasya dakṣiṇe pārśve deyā tailatulā nṛpa || 8.79 ||
palāṣṭakayutāṃ rājan vartti tatra ca dāpayet |
vāsasā tu samagreṇa sopavāso jitendriyaḥ || 8.80 ||
mahāvarttidvayamidaṃ sakṛddattvā mahāmate |
svarlokaṃ suciraṃ bhuktvā jāyate bhūtale yadā || 8.81 ||
tadā bhavati lakṣmīvān jayadraviṇasamyutaḥ |
rāṣṭre ca jāyate svasmin deśe ca nagare tathā || 8.82 ||
kule ca rājaśārdūla tatra syāddīpavatprabhaḥ |
pratyujjvalaśca bhavati yuddheṣu kalaheṣu ca || 8.83 ||
khyātiṃ yāti tathā loke sadguṇānāṃ ca sadguṇaiḥ |
ekamapyatha yo dadyādabhīṣṭatamayordvayoḥ || 8.84 ||
manuṣye sarvamāpnoti yaduktaṃ te mahānagha |
svarge tathātvamāpnoti bhogakāle tu yādava || 8.85 ||
samānyasya tu dīpasya rājan dānaṃ mahāphalam |
kiṃ punarmahato dīpasyātreyatta na vidyate || 8.86 ||
atha sonamalinadivastravarttya dīpadananisedhaḥ
śoṇaṃ vādarakaṃ vastraṃ jīrṇaṃ malinameva ca |
upabhuktaṃ na vā dadyātvarttikārthaṃ kadācana || 8.87 ||
iti |
svayamanyena vā dattaṃ dīpānna śrīharerharet |
nirvāpayenna hiṃsācca śubhamicchan kadācana || 8.88 ||
atha dīpanirvāpanādidoṣaḥ
viṣṇudharmottare prathamakhaṇḍe
dattvā dīpo na hartavyastena karma vijānatā |
nirvāpanaṃ ca dīpasya hiṃsanaṃ ca vigarhitam || 8.89 ||
yaḥ kuryāddhiṃsanaṃ tena karmaṇā puṣpitekṣaṇaḥ |
dīpahartā bhavedandhaḥ kaṇo nirvāṇakṛdbhavet || 8.90 ||
viṣṇudharme ca nārakān prati śrīdharmarājoktau
yuṣmābhiryauvanonmādamuditairavivekibhiḥ |
dyutodyotaya govindagehāddīpaḥ purā hṛtaḥ || 8.91 ||
tenādya narake ghore kṣuttṛṣṇāparipīḍitāḥ |
bhavanti patitāstīvre śītavātavidāritāḥ || 8.92 ||
tatraiva śrīpulastyoktau ca
tasmādāyātane viṣṇordadyāddīpān dvijottama |
tāmśca dattvā na hiṃseta na ca tailaviyojitān || 8.93 ||
kurvīta dīpahantā ca mūko'ndho jāyate mṛtaḥ |
andhe tamasi duṣpare narake pacyate kila || 8.94 ||
bhūmau dīpadananisedhaḥ
kālikāpurāṇe
dīpavṛkṣaśca kartavyastaijāsādyaiśca bhairava |
vṛkṣeṣu dīpo dātavyo na tu bhūmau kadācana || 8.95 ||
atha naivedyaṃ
dattvā puṣpāñjaliṃ pīṭhaṃ padyamācamanaṃ tathā |
kṛtvā pātreṣu kṛṣṇāyārpayedbhojyaṃ yathāvidhi || 8.96 ||
atha naivedyarpanavidhiḥ
astraṃ japtvāmbunā prokṣya naivedyaṃ cakramudrayā |
saṃrakṣya prokṣayedvāyubījajaptajalena ca || 8.97 ||
tena saṃśoṣya taddoṣamagnibījaṃ ca dakṣiṇe |
dhyātvā karatale'nyattatpṛṣṭhe saṃyojya darśyate || 8.98 ||
tadutthavahninā tasya śuṣkadoṣaṃ hṛdā dahet |
tataḥ karatale savye'mṛtabījaṃ vicintayet || 8.99 ||
tatpṛṣṭhe dakṣiṇaṃ panitalaṃ saṃyojya darśayet |
tadutthayā nivedyaṃ tatsicedamṛtadhārayā || 8.100 ||
jalena mūlajaptena prokṣya taccāmṛtātmakam |
sarvaṃ vicintya saṃspṛśya mūlaṃ vārāṣṭakaṃ japet || 8.101 ||
amṛtīkṛtya taddhenumudrayā salilādibhiḥ |
tacca kṛṣṇaṃ ca sampūjya gṛhitvā kusumāñjalim || 8.102 ||
śrīkṛṣṇaṃ prārthya tadvaktrāttejo dhyātvā vinirgatam |
saṃyojya ca nivedyaitatpatraṃ vāmena saṃspṛśan || 8.103 ||
dakṣeṇa pāṇinādāya gandhapuṣpānvitaṃ jalam |
svāhāntaṃ mūlamuccārya tajjalaṃ visṛjedbhuvi || 8.104 ||
tatpāṇibhyāṃ samutthāya nivedyaṃ tulasīyutam |
patrāḍhyaṃ tasya mantreṇa bhaktyā bhāgavate'rpayet || 8.105 ||
nivedanamantraścayaṃ
nivedayami bhavate jusanedaṃ havirhare || 8.106 ||
iti |
amṛtopastaranamasi svāhetyuccārayan hareḥ |
dattvātha vidhivadvārigaṇḍūṣaṃ vāmapāṇinā |
darśayedgrāsamudrāṃ tu praphullotpalasannibham || 8.107 ||
prāṇādimudrāhastena dakṣiṇena tu darśayet |
mantraiścaturthīsvāhāntaistārādyaistattadāhvayaiḥ || 8.108 ||
tataḥ spṛśamśca karayoraṅguṣṭhābhyāmanāmike |
pradarśayennivedyasya mudrāṃ tasya manuṃ japan || 8.109 ||
mantraścāyaṃ kramadīpikāyām
nandajo'mbumanubinduyuṅnatiḥ
pārśvarāmarudavātmane ni ca |
ruddhaṅeyutanivedyamātmabhūr
māsapārśvamanilastathā'miyuk || 8.110 ||
nivedyasya manutvena svābhīṣṭaṃ manumeva te |
ekāntino japantastu grāsamudrāṃ vitanvate || 8.111 ||
na ca dhyāyante te kṛṣṇavaktrāttejovinirgamam |
mañjulavyavahāreṇa bhojayanti hariṃ mudā || 8.112 ||
anyatra ca
śālībhaktaṃ subhaktaṃ śiśirakarasitaṃ pāyasaṃ pūpasūpam
lehyaṃ peyaṃ sucūṣyaṃ sitamamṛtaphalaṃ ghārikādyaṃ sukhādyam |
ājyaṃ prājyaṃ samijyaṃ nayanarucikaraṃ vājikailamarīca
svādiyaḥ śākarājiparikaramamṛtāhārajoṣaṃ juṣasva || 8.113 ||
kiṃ cagaruḍapurāṇe
naivedyaṃ parayā bhaktyā ghaṇṭādyairjayanisvanaiḥ |
nīrājanaiśca haraye dadyāddīpāsanaṃ budhaḥ || 8.114 ||
atha naivedyapatrāṇi
skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
naivedyapātraṃ vakṣyāmi keśavasya mahātmanaḥ |
hairaṇyaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ kaṃsyaṃ mṛnmayameva ca |
palāśaṃ padmapatraṃ ca pātraṃ viṣṇoratipriyam || 8.115 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
patrāṇāṃ tu pradānena narakaṃ ca na gacchati || 8.116 ||
patraparimanaṃ
devīpurāṇe
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaṃ pātramuttamaṃ parikīrtitam |
madhyamaṃ ca tribhāgonaṃ kanyasaṃ dvādaśāṅgulam |
vasvaṅgulavihīnaṃ tu na pātraṃ kārayetkvacit || 8.117 ||
atha bhojyani
ekādaśaskande
guḍapāyasasarpīṃṣi saṣkulyāpūpamodakān |
saṃyāvadadhisūpāmśca naivedyaṃ sati kalpayet || 8.118 ||
kiṃ ca
yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ loke yaccātipriyamātmanaḥ |
tattannivedayenmahyaṃ tadānantyāya kalpate || 8.119 ||
aṣṭamaskānde
naivedyaṃ cādhiguṇavaddadyātpuruṣatuṣṭidam || 8.120 ||
baudhāyanasmṛtau ca
nānāvidhānnapānaiśca bhakṣanādyairmanoharaiḥ |
naivedyaṃ kalpayedviṣṇostadabhāve ca pāyasaṃ
kevalaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ || 8.121 ||
vāmanapurāṇe
haviṣā saṃskṛtā ye ca yavagodhūmaśalayaḥ |
tilamudgādayo māṣā vrīhayaśca priyā hareḥ || 8.122 ||
gāruḍe
annaṃ caturvidhaṃ puṇyaṃ guṇāḍhyaṃ cāmṛtopamam |
niṣpaṇṇaṃ svagṛhe yadvā śraddhayā kalpayeddhareḥ || 8.123 ||
bhaviṣye
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ tathā dīpaṃ naivedyaṃ sumanoharam |
khaṇḍalaḍḍukaśrīvestakāsārāśokavartikāḥ || 8.124 ||
svastikollasikadugdhatilaveṣṭakilāṭikāḥ |
phalāni caiva pakvāni nāgaraṅgādikāni ca || 8.125 ||
anyāni vidhinā dattvā bhakṣyāṇi vividhāni ca |
evamādini dāpayedbhaktito nrpa || 8.126 ||
vārāhe
yastu bhāgavato devi annādyena tu prīṇayet |
prīṇitastiṣṭhate'sau vā bahujanmāni mādhavi || 8.127 ||
sarvavrīhimayaṃ gṛhyaṃ śubhaṃ sarvarasānvitam |
mantreṇa me pradīyeta na kiṃcidapi saṃspṛśet || 8.128 ||
iṅgudiphalabilvāni badarāmalakāni ca |
kharjurāmścāsanāmścaiva mānavāmśca parūṣakān || 8.129 ||
śāloḍḍambarikāmścaiva tathā plakṣaphalāni ca |
paippalaṃ kaṇṭakīyaṃ ca tumburuṃ ca priyaṅgukam || 8.130 ||
marīcaṃ śiṃśapākaṃ ca bhallātakaramardakam |
drākṣāṃ ca dāḍimaṃ caiva piṇḍakharjūrameva ca || 8.131 ||
sauvīraṃ kelikaṃ caiva tathā śubhaphalāni ca |
piṇḍārakaphalaṃ caiva punnāgaphalameva ca || 8.132 ||
śamīṃ caiva kavīraṃ ca kharjūrakamahāphalam |
kumudasya phalaṃ caiva vaheḍakaphalaṃ tathā || 8.133 ||
ajaṃ karkoṭakaṃ caiva tathā talaphalāni ca |
kadambaḥ kaumudaṃ caiva dvividhaṃ sthalakañjayoḥ || 8.134 ||
piṇḍikaṇḍeti vikhyātaṃ vaṃśanīpaṃ tataḥ param |
madhukaṇḍeti vikhyātaṃ māhiṣaṃ kaṇḍameva ca || 8.135 ||
karamardakakandaṃ ca tathā nilotpalasya ca |
mṛṇālaṃ pauṣkaraṃ caiva śālūkasya phalaṃ tathā || 8.136 ||
ete cānye ca bahavaḥ kāṇḍamūlaphalāni ca |
etāni copayojyāni ye mayā parikalpitāḥ || 8.137 ||
mūlakasya tataḥ śākaṃ ciñcaśākaṃ tathaiva ca |
śākaṃ caiva kalāyasya sarṣapasya tathaiva ca || 8.138 ||
vaṃsakasya tu śākaṃ ca śākameva kalambikam |
ārdrakasya ca śākaṃ vai pālaṅkaṃ śākameva ca || 8.139 ||
ambiloḍakaśākaṃ ca kāśaṃ kaumārakaṃ tathā |
śukamaṇḍalapatraṃ ca dvāveva taruvānakau || 8.140 ||
carasya caiva śākaṃ ca madhukoḍḍumbaraṃ tathā |
ete cānye ca bahavahśataśo'tha sahasraśaḥ |
karmaṇyāścaiva sarve vai ye mayā parikīrtitāḥ || 8.141 ||
vrīhīṇāṃ ca pravakṣyāmi upayogāmśca mādhavi |
ekacittaṃ samādhāya tatsarvaṃ śṛṇu sundari || 8.142 ||
dharmādhārmikaraktaṃ ca sugandhaṃ raktaśālikam |
dīrghaśūkaṃ mahāśāliṃ varakuṅkumapatrakam || 8.143 ||
grāmaśāliṃ samadrāśāṃ saśrīśāṃ kuśaśālikām |
yavāśca dvividhā jñeyāḥ karmaṇyā mama sundari || 8.144 ||
karmaṇyāścaiva mudgāśca tilāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ kulatthakāḥ |
godhūmakaṃ mahāmudgamudgāṣṭakamavāṭajit || 8.145 ||
karmaṇyetāni coktāni vyajanāni priyānvitān |
pratigṛhṇāmyahaṃ hyetān sarvān bhāgavatān priyān || 8.146 ||
kiṃ ca
ye mayaivopayojyāni gavyaṃ dadhi payo ghṛtam || 8.147 ||
skānde ca brahmanāradasaṃvāde
haviḥ śalyodanaṃ divyamājyayuktaṃ saśarkaram |
naivedyaṃ devadevāya yāvakaṃ pāyasaṃ tathā || 8.148 ||
naivedyānāmabhāve tu phalāni vinivedayet |
phalānāmapyabhāve tu tṛṇagulmauṣadhīrapi || 8.149 ||
auṣadhīnāmalābhe tu toyaṃ ca vinivedayet |
tadalābhe tu sarvatra mānasaṃ pravaraṃ smṛtam || 8.150 ||
skānde mahendraṃ prati śrīnāradavacanaṃ
yacchanti tulasīśākaṃ śrutaṃ ye mādhavāgrataḥ |
kalpāntaṃ viṣṇuloke tu vasanti pitṛbhiḥ saha || 8.151 ||
atha naivedyaniṣiddhāni
hāritasmṛtau
nābhakṣyaṃ naivedyārthe bhakṣyesvapyajāmahiṣīkṣīraṃ pañcanakhā matsyāśca || 8.152 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye
nīlīkṣetraṃ vāpayanti mūlakaṃ bhakṣayanti ye |
naivāsti narakottārahkalpakoṭiśatairapi || 8.153 ||
vārāhe
māhiṣaṃ cāvikaṃ cājamayajñīyamudāhṛtam || 8.154 ||
kiṃ ca
māhiṣaṃ varjayenmahyaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ yadi || 8.155 ||
viṣṇudharmottare tṛtīyakhaṇḍe
abhakṣyaṃ capyahṛdyaṃ ca naivedyaṃ na nivedayet |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca tathā cāvihitaṃ ca yat || 8.156 ||
mūṣikālāṅgulopetamavadhūtamavakṣutam |
uḍḍumbaraṃ kapitthaṃ ca tathā dantaśaṭhaṃ ca yat |
evamādīni devāya na deyāni kadācana || 8.157 ||
athābhakṣyāṇi
kaurme
vṛntākaṃ jālikāśākaṃ kusumbhāśmantakaṃ tathā |
palāṇḍuṃ laśunaṃ śuklaṃ niryāsaṃ caiva varjayet || 8.158 ||
gṛñjanaṃ kiṃśukaṃ caiva kukuṇḍaṃ ca tathaiva ca |
uḍumbaramalābuṃ ca jagdhvā patati vai dvijaḥ || 8.159 ||
vaiṣṇave
bhuñjītoddhṛtasārāṇi na kadācinnareśvara || 8.160 ||
skānde
na bhakṣayati vṛntākaṃ tasya dūrataro hariḥ || 8.161 ||
kiṃ cānyatra
dorbhyāṃ pādbhyāṃ ca jānubhyāmurasā śirasā dṛśā |
manasā vacasā ceti praṇāmo'ṣṭāṅga īritaḥ || 8. 162 ||
jānubhyāṃ caiva bāhubhyāṃ śirasā vacasā dhiyā |
pañcāṅgakaḥ praṇāmaḥ syātpūjāsu pravarāvimau || 8. 163 ||
ata evoktaṃ yāmale
yatra madyaṃ tathā māṃsaṃ tathā vṛntākamūlake |
nivedayennaiva tatra hareraikāntikī ratiḥ || 8.164 ||
atha naivedyarpanamāhātmyaṃ
skānde
naivedyāni manojñāni kṛṣṇasyāgre nivedayet |
kalpāntaṃ tatpitṝṇaṃ tu tṛptirbhavati śāśvatī || 8.165 ||
phalāni yacchate yo vai suhṛdyāni nareśvara |
kalpāntaṃ jāyate tasya saphalaṃ ca manorathaḥ || 8.166 ||
nārasiṃhe
haviḥ śālyodanaṃ diyyamājyayuktaṃ saśarkaram |
nivedya narasiṃhāya yāvakaṃ pāyasaṃ tathā || 8.167 ||
samāstaṇḍulaśaṅkhyāyā yāvatyastāvatīrnṛpa |
viṣṇuloke mahābhogan bhuñjānaste savaiṣṇavāḥ || 8.168 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
annadastṛptimāpnoti svargalokaṃ ca gacchati |
dattvā ca saṃvibhāgāya tathaivānnamatandritaḥ |
trailokyatarpite puṇyaṃ tatkṣanātsamavāpnuyāt || 8.169 ||
akṣayyamannapānaṃ ca pitṛbhyaścopatiṣṭhate |
odanaṃ vyajanopetaṃ dattvā svargamavāpnuyāt || 8.170 ||
paramānnaṃ tathā dattvā tṛptimāpnoti śaśvatim |
viṣṇulokamavāpnoti kulamuddharate tathā || 8.171 ||
ghṛtaudanapradānena dīrghamāyuravāpnuyāt |
dadhyodanapradānena śriyamāpnotyanuttamām || 8.172 ||
kṣīrodanapradānena dīrghajīvitamāpnuyāt |
ikṣūṇāṃ ca pradānena paraṃ saubhāgyamaśnute || 8.173 ||
ratnānāṃ caiva bhāgī syātsvargalokaṃ ca gacchati |
phāṇitasya pradānena agnyādhānaphalaṃ labhet || 8.174 ||
tathā guḍapradānena kāmitābhīṣṭamāpnuyāt || 8.175 ||
nivedyekṣurasaṃ bhaktyā paraṃ saubhāgyamāpnuyāt |
sarvān kāmānavāpnoti kṣaudraṃ yaśca prayacchati || 8.176 ||
tadeva tuhitopetaṃ rājasūyamavāpnuyāt |
vahniṣṭomamavāpnoti yavākasya nivedakaḥ |
atirātramavāpnoti tathā pūpanivedakaḥ || 8.177 ||
vaidalānāṃ ca bhakṣyāṇāṃ dānātkāmānavāpnuyāt |
dīrghajīvitamāpnoti ghṛtapūranivedakaḥ || 8.178 ||
modakānāṃ pradānena kāmānāpnotyabhīpsitān || 8.179 ||
nānāvidhānāṃ bhakṣyāṇāṃ dānātsvargamavāpnuyāt |
bhojanīyapradānena tṛptimāpnotyanuttamām || 8.180 ||
tathā lehyapradānena saubhāgyamadhigacchati |
balavarṇamavāpnoti cūṣyāṇāṃ ca nivedane || 8.181 ||
kulmāṣollāsikadātā vahnyādheyaṃ phalaṃ labhet |
tathā kṛṣāradānena vahniṣṭomamavāpnuyāt || 8.182 ||
dhanānāṃ kṣaudrayuktānāṃ lājānāṃ ca nivedakaḥ |
mukhyaṃ caiva śaktūnāṃ vahniṣṭomamavāpnuyāt || 8.183 ||
vānaprasthāśritaṃ puṇyaṃ labhecchākanivedakaḥ |
dattvā haritakaṃ caiva tadeva phalamāpnuyāt || 8.184 ||
dattvā śākāni ramyāṇi viśokastvabhijāyate |
dattvā ca vyajanārthāya tathopakaraṇāni ca || 8.185 ||
sukule labhate janma kandamūlanivedakaḥ |
nīlotpalavidarīṇāṃ taruṭasya tathā dvijaḥ || 8.186 ||
kandadānādavāpnoti vānaprasthaphalaṃ śubham |
trapuṣervārukaṃ dattvā puṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet || 8.187 ||
karkandhuvadare dattvā tathā pāraivataṃ kalam |
parūṣakaṃ tathābhraṃ ca panasaṃ nārikelakam || 8.188 ||
bhavyaṃ mocaṃ tathā cocaṃ kharjūramatha dāḍimam |
āmrātakasruvāmloṭaphalamānapriyālakam || 8.189 ||
jambūbilvāmalaṃ caiva jātyaṃ vīṇātakaṃ tathā |
nāraṅgabījapūre ca bījaphalguphalanyapi || 8.190 ||
evamādīni divyani yaḥ phalāni prayacchati |
tathā kandāni mukhyāni devadevāya bhaktitaḥ || 8.191 ||
kriyāsāphalyamāpnoti svargalokaṃ tathaiva ca |
prāpnoti phalamārogyaṃ mṛdvīkānāṃ nivedakaḥ || 8.192 ||
rasānmukhyānavāpnoti saubhāgyamapi cottamam |
āmrairabhyarcya deveśamaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 8.193 ||
kiṃ ca
mocakaṃ panasaṃ jambū tathānyatkumbhāliphalam |
prācīnāmalakaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ madhukoḍḍumbarasya ca |
yatnapakvamapi grāhyaṃ kadalīphalamuttamam || 8.194 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye ca
yatkiñcidalpaṃ naivedyaṃ bhaktabhaktirasaplutam |
pratibhojayati śrīśastaddātṝn svasukhaṃ drutamiti || 8.195 ||
tataḥ prāgvadvicitrāṇi panakānyuttamāni ca |
sugandhi śītalaṃ svacchaṃ jalamapyarpayettataḥ || 8.196 ||
atha panakāni, tanmāhātmyaṃ ca
viṣṇudharmottare
pānakāni sugandhīni śītalāni viśeṣataḥ |
nivedya devadevāya vājimedhamavāpnuyāt || 8.197 ||
tvagelānāgakusumakarpūrasitasaṃyutaiḥ |
sitākṣaudraguḍopetairgandhavarṇaguṇānvitaiḥ || 8.198 ||
bījapūrakanāraṅgasahakārasamanvitaiḥ |
rājasūyamavāpnoti panakairviniveditaiḥ || 8.199 ||
nivedya nārikelāmbuvahniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet |
sarvakāmavahā nadyo nityaṃ yatra manoramāḥ |
tatra pānapradā yānti yatra rāmā guṇānvitāḥ || 8.200 ||
iti |
itthaṃ samarpya naivedyaṃ dattvā javanikaṃ tataḥ |
bahirbhūya yathāsakti japaṃ sandhyānamācaret || 8.201 ||
atha dhyānam
brahmeśādyaiḥ parita ṛṣibhiḥ sūpaviṣṭaiḥ sameto
lakṣmyā śiñjadvalayakarayā sādaraṃ vījyamana |
marmakrīḍaprahasitamukho hāsayan paṅktibhoktṝn
bhuṅkte pātre kanakaghaṭite ṣaḍrasaṃ śrīrameśaḥ || 8.202 ||
iti |
ekantibhiścātmakṛtaṃ savayasyasya gokule |
yaśodālālyamānasya dhyeyaṃ kṛṣṇasya bhojanam || 8.203 ||
atha homaḥ
nityaṃ cāvaśyakaṃ homaṃ kuryātśaktyanusārataḥ |
homāśaktau tu kurvīta japaṃ tasya caturguṇam || 8.204 ||
ke'pyevaṃ manvate'vaśyaṃ nityahomaṃ sadācaret |
puraścaranahomasyāśaktau hi sa vidhirmataḥ || 8.205 ||
pūrvaṃ dīkṣāvidhau homavidhiśca likhitaḥ kiyān |
tadvistāraśca vijñeyastattacchāstrāttadicchubhiḥ || 8.206 ||
samāptiṃ bhojane dhyātvā dattvā gaṇḍūṣikaṃ jalam |
amṛtāpidhānamasi svāhetyuccārayetsudhīḥ || 8.207 ||
visṛjeddevavaktre tattejaḥ saṃhāramudrayā |
naikantī tejasaḥ kuryānniṣkrāntimiva saṅkramam || 8.208 ||
atha balidānam
tato javanikā vidvānapasārya yathāvidhi |
viṣvaksenāya bhagavannaivedyāṃśaṃ nivedayet || 8.209 ||
tathā ca pañcarātre śrīnāradavacanam
viśvaksenāya dātavyaṃ naivedyaṃ tacchatāṃśakam |
pādodakaṃ prasādaṃ ca liṅge caṇḍeśvarāya ca || 8.210 ||
tadvidhiḥ
mukhyādīsānataḥ pātrānnaivedyāṃśaṃ samuddharet |
sarvadevasvarupāya parāya parameṣṭhine || 8.211 ||
śrīkṛṣṇasevayuktāya viṣvaksenāya te namaḥ |
ityuktvā śrīharervāme tīrthaklinnaṃ samarpayet || 8.212 ||
sataṃsaṃ vā sahasrāṃśamanyathā niṣphalaṃ bhavet || 8.213 ||
paścācca balirityādi ślokāvuccārya vaiṣṇavaḥ |
sarvebhyo vaiṣṇavebhyastacchatāṃsaṃ vinivedayet || 8.214 ||
tau ca ślokau
balirvibhīṣaṇo bhīṣmaḥ kapilo nārado'rjunaḥ |
prahlādaścāmbarīṣaśca vasurvāyusutaḥ sivaḥ || 8.215 ||
viṣvaksenoddhavākrūraḥ sanakādyāḥ śukādayaḥ |
śrīkṛṣṇasya prasādo'yaṃ sarve gṛhṇantu vaiṣṇavāḥ || 8.216 ||
idaṃ yadyapi yujyeta darpaṇārpaṇataḥ param |
tathāpi bhaktavātsalyātkṛṣṇasyātrāpi sambhavet || 8.217 ||
atha balidanamāhātmyaṃ
nārasiṃhe
tatastadannaśeṣeṇa pārṣadebhyaḥ samantataḥ |
puṣpākṣatairvimiśreṇa baliṃ yastu prayacchati || 8.218 ||
balinā vaiṣṇavenātha tṛptaḥ santo divaukasaḥ |
śāntiṃ tasya prayacchanti śriyamārogyameva ca || 8.219 ||
atha jalagandusadyarpanaṃ
upalipya tato bhūmiṃ punargāṇḍūṣikaṃ jalam |
dadyāttriragre kṛṣṇasya tato'smai dantasodhanam || 8.220 ||
punarācamanaṃ dattvā śrīpāṇyoḥ śrīmukhasya ca |
mārjanāyāṃśukaṃ dattvā sarvāṇyaṅgāni mārjayet || 8.221 ||
paridhāpyapare vastre punardattvāsanāntaram |
padyamācamanīyaṃ ca pūrvavatpunararpayet || 8.222 ||
candanāgurucūrṇādi pradadyātkaramārjanam |
karpūradyāsyavāsaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ tulasīmapi || 8.223 ||
atha mukhavasadimāhātmyaṃ
viṣṇudharmottare trtīyakhaṇḍe
pūgajātiphalaṃ dattvā jātipatraṃ tathaiva ca |
lavaṅgaphalakakkolamelakataphalaṃ tathā || 8.224 ||
tāmbūlīnāṃ kiśalayaṃ svargalokamavāpnuyāt |
saubhāgyamatulaṃ loke tathā rūpamanuttamam || 8.225 ||
skānde
tāmbūlaṃ ca sakarpūraṃ sapūgaṃ naranāyaka |
kṛṣṇāya yacchati prītyā tasya tuṣṭo hariḥ sadā || 8.226 ||
atha punargandharpanaṃ
divyaṃ gandhaṃ punardattvā yatheṣṭamanulepanaiḥ |
divyairvicitraiḥ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ bhakticchedena lepayet || 8.227 ||
ramyāṇi cordhvapuṇḍrāṇi sadvarṇena yathāspadam |
sugandhinānulepena kṛṣṇasya racayettarām || 8.228 ||
tathā cāgame dhyānaprasaṅge
lalāṭe hṛdaye kukṣau kaṇṭhe bahvośca pārśvayoḥ |
virājatordhvapuṇḍreṇa sauvarṇena vibhūṣitam || 8.229 ||
iti |
divyāni kañcukoṣṇīṣakāñcyādīni parāṇyapi |
vastrāṇi suvicitrāṇi śrīkṛṣṇaṃ paridhāpayet || 8.230 ||
tato divyakirīṭādibhūṣaṇani yathāruci |
vicitradivyamālyāni paridhāpya vibhūṣayet || 8.231 ||
atha mahārajopacararpanaṃ
tataśca cāmaracchatrapādukādīn parānapi |
mahārājopacārāmśca dattvādarśaṃ pradarśayet || 8.232 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
yathādeśaṃ yathākalaṃ rājaliṅgaṃ surālaye |
dattvā bhavati rājaiva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 8.233 ||
tatra cāmaramāhātmyaṃ
tathā cāmaradānena śrīmān bhavati bhūtale |
mucyate ca tathā pāpaiḥ svargalokaṃ ca gacchati || 8.234 ||
chatrasya māhātmyaṃ
tatraiva
chatraṃ bahuśalākaṃ ca jhallarīvastrasaṃyutam |
divyavastraiśca saṃyuktaṃ hemadaṇḍasamanvitam || 8.235 ||
yaḥ prayacchati kṛṣṇasya chatralakṣayutairvṛtaḥ |
prārthyate so'maraiḥ sarvaiḥ krīḍate pitṛbhiḥ saha || 8.236 ||
tatraiva vānyatra
rājā bhavati loke'smin chatraṃ dattvā dvijottamaḥ |
nāpnoti ripujaṃ duḥkhaṃ saṅgrāme ripujidbhayet || 8.237 ||
upānatsampradānena vimānamadhirohati |
yatheṣṭaṃ tena lokeṣu vicaratyamaraprabhaḥ || 8.238 ||
dhvajasya māhātmyaṃ
tatraiva
lokeṣu dhvajabhūtaḥ syāddattvā viṣṇorvaraṃ dhvajam |
śakralokamavāpnoti bahūnabdagaṇānnaraḥ || 8.239 ||
kiṃ ca
yuktaṃ pītapatākābhirnivedya garuḍadhvajam |
keśavāya dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ sarvaloke mahīyate iti || 8.240 ||
yatprasāde dhvajāropamāhātmyaṃ likhitaṃ purā |
tadatrāpyakhilaṃ jñeyaṃ tatrātratyamidaṃ tathā || 8.241 ||
kiṃ ca bhaviṣye
viṣṇordhvaje tu sauvarṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ |
patākā cāpi pītā syādgaruḍasya samīpagā || 8.242 ||
vyajanasya māhātmyaṃ
viṣṇudharmottare
talavrṇtapradānena nirvṛtiṃ prāpnuyātparam || 8.243 ||
vitanasya māhātmyaṃ
tatraiva
vitānakapradānena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
paraṃ nirvṛtimāpnoti yatra tatrābhijāyate || 8.244 ||
khadgadīnāṃ māhātmyaṃ
dattvā nistriṃśakānmukhyān śatrubhirnābhibhūyate |
dattvā tadbandhanaṃ mukhyamagnyādheyaphalaṃ labhet || 8.245 ||
kiṃ ca
patadgrahaṃ tathā dattvā śubhadastvabhijāyate |
pādapīṭhapradānena sthānaṃ sarvatra vindati || 8.246 ||
darpaṇasya pradānena rūpavān darpavān bhavet |
mārjayitvā tathā taṃ ca śubhagastvabhijāyate || 8.247 ||
yatkiñciddevadevāya dadyādbhaktisamanvitaḥ |
tadevākṣayamāpnoti svargalokaṃ sa gacchati || 8.248 ||
kiṃ ca, vāmanapurāṇe śrībaliṃ prati śrīprahlādoktau
śraddadhānairbhaktiparairyānyuddiśya janārdanam |
balidānāni dīyante akṣayāni vidurbudhaḥ || 8.249 ||
atrāpi kecidicchanti dattvā puṣpāñjalitrayam |
pūrvokta daśa śaṅkhādyā mudrāḥ sandarśayet || 8.250 ||
iti |
atha gītavadyanṛtyani
tato vicitrairlalitaiḥ kāritairvā svayaṃ kṛtaiḥ |
gītairvādyaiśca nṛtyaiśca śrīkṛṣṇaṃ paritoṣayet || 8.251 ||
atha tatra niṣiddhaṃ
nṛtyādi kurvato bhaktānnopaviṣṭo'valokayet |
na ca tiryagvrajettatra taiḥ sahāntarayan prabhum || 8.252 ||
tathā coktaṃ
nṛtyantaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ harsādāsīno yastu paśyati |
khañjo bhavati rājendra so'yaṃ janmani janmani || 8.253 ||
kiṃ ca
nṛtyatāṃ gāyatāṃ madhye bhaktānāṃ keśavasya ca |
tānṛte yastiro yāti tiryagyoniṃ sa gacchati || 8.254 ||
atha gītadimāhātmyam
adau samanyataḥ
nārasiṃhe
gītavādyādikaṃ nāṭyaṃ śaṅkhaturyādinisvanam |
yaḥ kārayati viṣṇostu sandhyāyāṃ mandire naraḥ |
sarvakāle viśeṣeṇa kāmagaḥ kāmarūpavān || 8.255 ||
susaṅgītavidagdhaiśca sevyamāno'psarogaṇaiḥ |
mahārheṇa vimānena vicitreṇa virājatā |
svargātsvargamanuprāpya viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 8.256 ||
skānde viṣṇunāradasaṃvāde
gītaṃ vādyaṃ ca nṛtyaṃ ca nāṭyaṃ viṣṇukathāṃ mune |
yaḥ karoti sa puṇyātmā trailokyopari saṃsthitaḥ || 8.257 ||
bṛhannāradīye śrīyamabhagīrathasaṃvāde
devatāyatane yastu bhaktiyuktaḥ pranṛtyati |
gītāni gāyatyathavā tatphalaṃ śṛṇu bhūpate || 8.258 ||
gandharvarājatāṃ gaṇairnṛtyādrudragaṇeśatām |
prāpnotyaṣṭakulairyuktastataḥ syānmokṣabhāṅnaraḥ || 8.259 ||
laiṅge śrīmārkaṇḍeyāmbarīṣasaṃvāde
viṣṇukṣetre tu yo vidvān kārayedbhaktisaṃyutaḥ |
gananṛtyādikaṃ caiva viṣṇvākhyaṃ ca kathāṃ tathā || 8.260 ||
jātiṃ smṛtiṃ ca medhāṃ ca tathaiva paramāṃ sthitim |
prāpnoti viṣṇusālokyaṃ satyametannārādhipa || 8.261 ||
anyatra ca śrībhagavaduktau
visṛjya lajjāṃ yo'dhīte gāyate nṛtyate'pi ca |
kulakoṭisamāyukto labhate māmakaṃ padam || 8.262 ||
ata evoktaṃ
bhārate nṛtyagīte tu kuryātsvābhāvike'pi vā |
svābhāvikena bhagavān prīṇātītyāha śaunakaḥ || 8.263 ||
ata eva nāradīye
viṣṇorgītaṃ ca nṛtyaṃ ca naṭānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
brahman brāhmaṇajātīnāṃ kartavyaṃ nityakarmavat || 8.264 ||
kintu smṛtau
gītanṛtyāni kurvīta devadvijādituṣṭaye |
na jīvanāya yuñjīta vipro pāpabhiyā kvacit || 8.265 ||
evaṃ kṛṣṇaprīṇanatvādgītādernityatā parā |
saṃsiddhairaviśeṣena jñeyā sā harivasare || 8.266 ||
tathā coktaṃ
keśavāgre nṛtyagītaṃ na karoti harerdine |
vahninā kiṃ na dagdho'sau gataḥ kiṃ na rasātalaṃ || 8.267 ||
atha viśeṣato gītasya māhātmyaṃ
dvārakāmāhātmye śrīmarkandeyendradyumnasaṃvāde
kṛṣṇaṃ santoṣayedyastu sugītairmadhurasvanaiḥ |
sarvavedaphalaṃ tasya jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 8.268 ||
skānde śrīmahādevoktau
śrutikoṭisamaṃ japyaṃ japakoṭisamaṃ haviḥ |
havihkoṭisamaṃ geyaṃ geyaṃ geyasamaṃ viduḥ || 8.269 ||
kasikhaṇḍe viṣṇudūtaśivaśarmasaṃvāde
yadi gītaṃ kvacidgītaṃ śrīmaddhariharāṅkitam |
mokṣaṃ tu tatphalaṃ prāhuhsānnidhyamathavā tayoḥ || 8.270 ||
viṣṇuśarme śrībhagavaduktau
rāgeṇākṛṣyate ceto gāndharvābhimukhaṃ yadi |
mayi buddhiṃ samāsthāya gāyetha mama satkathāḥ || 8.271 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
yo gāyati samaniśaṃ bhuvi bhaktā uccaiḥ
sa drāṇsamastajanapāpabhide'lamekaḥ |
dīpesvasatsvapi nanu pratigehamantar
dhvāntaṃ kimatra vilasatyamale dyunāthe || 8.272 ||
yadānandakalaṃ gāyan bhaktaḥ puṇyāśru varṣati |
tatsarvatīrthasalilasnānaṃ svamalaśodhanaṃ || 8.273 ||
vārāhe
brāhmaṇo vāsudevārthaṃ gāyamano'niśaṃ param |
samyaktālaprayogeṇa sannipātena vā punaḥ || 8.274 ||
nava varṣasahasrāṇi nava varṣaśatāni ca |
kuverabhavanaṃ gatvā modate vai yadṛcchayā || 8.275 ||
kuverabhavanādbhraṣṭaḥ svacchandagamanālayaḥ |
phalamāpnoti suśroṇi mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ || 8.276 ||
nārāyaṇānāṃ vidhinā gānaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ smṛtam |
gānenārādhito viṣṇuhsvakīrtijanavarcasā dadāti |
tuṣṭaḥ sthānaṃ svaṃ yathāsmai kauśikayā vai || 8.277 ||
kiṃ ca
eṣa vo muniśārdūlaḥ prokto gītakramo muneḥ |
brāhmaṇo vāsudevākhyaṃ gāyamāno'niśaṃ param || 8.278 ||
hareḥ sālokyamāpnoti rudragānādhiko bhavet |
karmaṇā manasā vācā vāsudevaparāyaṇaḥ |
gāyannṛtyaṃstamāpnoti tasmādgeyaṃ paraṃ viduḥ || 8.279 ||
prathamaskandhe śrīnāradoktau
pragāyataḥ svavīryāṇi tīrthapadaḥ priyaśravaḥ |
ahuta iva me sighraṃ darśanaṃ yāti cetasi || 8.280 ||
dvādaśaskandhe śrīsūtoktau
mṛṣā girastā hyasatīrasatkathā
na kathyate yadbhagavānadhokṣajaḥ |
tadeva satyaṃ taduhaiva maṅgalaṃ
tadeva puṇyaṃ bhagavadgunodayam || 8.281 ||
tadeva ramyaṃ ruciraṃ navaṃ navaṃ
tadeva śaśvanmanaso mahotsavam |
tadeva śokārṇavaśoṣaṇaṃ nṝṇāṃ
yaduttamaślokayaśo'nugīyate || 8.282 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
dattvā ca gītaṃ dharmajñā gandharvaiḥ saha modate |
svayaṃ gītena sampūjya tasyaivānucaro bhavet || 8.283 ||
pādme śrīkṛṣṇasatyabhāmāsaṃvādīyakārttikamāhātmye śrīpṛthunāradasaṃvāde śrībhagavaduktau
nāhaṃ vasāmi vaikuṇṭhe na yogihṛdayeṣu vā |
madbhaktā yatra gāyanti tatra tiṣṭhāmi nārada || 8.284 ||
teṣāṃ pūjādikaṃ gandhapadyādyaiḥ kriyate naraiḥ |
tena prītiṃ parāṃ yāmi na tathā matpūjanāt || 8.285 ||
ata evoktaṃ
karmāṇyaupāyikatvena brāhmaṇo'nya iti smṛtaḥ |
kārikāyāmataḥ proktaṃ vipro gītai ramediti || 8.286 ||
atha nṛtyasya māhātmyaṃ
dvārakāmāhātmye tatraiva
yo nṛtyati prahṛṣṭātmā bhāvairbahusubhaktitaḥ |
sa nirdahati pāpāni janmāntaraśateṣvapi || 8.287 ||
haribhaktisudhodaya
bahudhotsāryate harṣādviṣṇubhaktasya nṛtyataḥ |
padbhyāṃ bhūmerdiśo'kṣibhyāṃ dorbhyāṃ vāmaṅgalaṃ divaḥ || 8.288 ||
vārāhe
yaśca nṛtyati suśroṇi purāṇoktaṃ samāsataḥ |
triṃśadvarṣasahasrāṇi triṃśadvarṣaśatāni ca |
puṣkaradvīpamāsādya modate vai yadṛcchayā || 8.289 ||
puṣkarācca paribhraṣṭaḥ svacchandagamanālayaḥ |
phalamāpnoti suśroṇi mama karmaparāyaṇaḥ || 8.290 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
nṛtyaṃ dattvā tathāpnoti rudralokamasaṃśayam |
svayaṃ nṛtyena sampūjya tasyaivānucaro bhavet || 8.291 ||
anyatra śrīnāradoktau
nṛtyatāṃ śrīpateragre tālikāvādanairbhṛśam |
uḍḍīyante śarīrasthāḥ sarve pātakapakṣiṇaḥ || 8.292 ||
saṅgītaśāstre
vīṇāvādanatattvajñahśrutijātiviśāradaḥ |
tālajñaścāprayāsena mokṣamārgaṃ niyacchati || 8.293 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
vādyaṃ dattvā tathā viprahśakralokamavāpnuyāt |
svayaṃ vadyena sampūjya tasyaivanucaro bhavet || 8.294 ||
vādyānāmapi devasya tantrīvādyaṃ sadā priyam |
tena sampūjya varadaṃ gāṇapatyamavāpnuyāt || 8.295 ||
ataḥ saktau punaḥ pūja
śaktaścetsaparivāraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ gandhādibhiḥ punaḥ |
pañcopacārairmūlena sampūjyārghyaṃ samarpayet || 8.296 ||
atha nīrājanaṃ
tataśca mūlamantrena dattvā puṣpāñjalitrayam |
mahānīrājanaṃ kuryānmahāvādyajayasvanaiḥ || 8.297 ||
prajvalayettadarthaṃ ca karpūreṇa ghṛtena vā |
ārātrikaṃ śubhe pātre viṣamānekavartikaṃ || 8.298 ||
atha nīrājanamāhātmyam
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde
bahuvarttisamāyuktaṃ jvalantaṃ keśavopari |
kuryādārātrikaṃ yastu kalpakoṭiṃ vaseddivi || 8.299 ||
karpūrena tu yaḥ kuryādbhaktyā keśavamurdhani |
ārātrikaṃ muniśreṣṭha praviśedviṣṇumavyayaṃ || 8.300 ||
tatraivanyatra
dīptimantaṃ sakarpūraṃ karotyārātrikaṃ nṛpa |
kṛṣṇasya vasate loke sapta kalpāni mānavāḥ || 8.301 ||
tatraiva śrīsivomasaṃvāde
mantrahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ yatkṛtaṃ pūjanaṃ hareḥ |
sarvaṃ sampūrṇatāmeti kṛte nīrājane śive || 8.302 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
kṛtvā nīrājanaṃ viṣṇordīpāvalyā sudṛśyayā |
tamovikāraṃ jayati jite tasmimśca ko bhavaḥ || 8.303 ||
anyatra ca
koṭayo brahmahatyānāmagamyāgamakotayaḥ |
dahatyālokamātreṇa viṣṇoḥ sārātrikaṃ mukhaṃ || 8.304 ||
iti |
yacca dīpasya māhātmyaṃ pūrvaṃ likhitamasti tat |
drastavyaṃ sarvatrāpi prāyenābhedato'nayoḥ || 8.305 ||
ataḥ sādaramutthāya mahānīrājanaṃ tvidam |
draṣṭavyaṃ dīpavatsarvairvandyamārātrikaṃ ca yat || 8.306 ||
taduktaṃ śrīpulastyena viṣṇudharme
dhūpaṃ cārātrikaṃ paśyetkarābhyāṃ ca pravandate |
kulakoṭiṃ samuddhṛtya yāti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padaṃ || 8.307 ||
mūlāgame ca
nīrājanaṃ ca yaḥ paśyeddevadevasya cakriṇaḥ |
sapta janmāni vipraḥ syādante ca paramaṃ padam || 8.308 ||
atha śaṅkhādivādanamāhātmyam
bṛhannāradīye śrīyamabhagīrathasaṃvāde
keśavāyatane rājan kurvan śaṅkharavaṃ naraḥ |
sarvapāpavinirmukto brahmaṇā saha modate || 8.309 ||
karaśabdaṃ prakurvanti keśavāyataneṣu ye |
te sarve pāpanirmuktā vimāneśā yugadvayam || 8.310 ||
tālādikāṃsyaninadaṃ kurvan viṣṇugṛhe naraḥ |
yatphalaṃ labhate rājan śṛṇuṣva gadato mama || 8.311 ||
sarvapāpavinirmukto vimānaśatasaṅkulaḥ |
gīyamānaśca gandharvairviṣṇunā saha modate || 8.312 ||
bherīmṛdaṅgapaṭahamurajaiśca saḍiṇḍimaiḥ |
saṃprīṇayanti deveśaṃ teṣāṃ puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 8.313 ||
devastrīgaṇasaṃyuktāḥ sarvakāmaiḥ samarcitāḥ |
svargalokamanuprāpya modante kalpapañcakam || 8.314 ||
iti |
atha sajalaśaṅkhanīrājanaṃ
tataśca sajalaṃ śaṅkhaṃ bhagavanmastakopari |
tri bhrāmayitvā kurvīta punarnīrājanaṃ prabhoḥ || 8.315 ||
tanmāhātmyaṃ ca
dvārakāmāhātmye tatraiva
śaṅkhe kṛtvā tu pānīyaṃ bhrāmitaṃ keśavopari |
sannidhau vasate viṣṇoḥ kalpāntaṃ kṣīrasāgare || 8.316 ||
iti |
nīrājanadvayaṃ caitattāmbūlasyārpaṇaṃ param |
kecidicchanti kecicca darpaṇārpaṇataḥ param || 8.317 ||
tathā ca pañcarātre
punarācamanaṃ dadyātkarodvartanameva ca |
sakarpūraṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ kuryānnīrājanaṃ tathā || 8.318 ||
samarpya mukuṭādīni bhūṣaṇāni vicakṣaṇaḥ |
ādarśayettathādarśaṃ prakalpya chatracāmare || 8.319 ||
gāruḍe ca
atha bhuktavate dattvā jalaiḥ karpūravāsitaiḥ |
ācamanaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ candanaiḥ karamārjanam || 8.320 ||
puṣpāñjaliṃ tataḥ kṛtvā bhaktyādarśaṃ pradarśayet |
nīrājanaṃ punaḥ kāryaṃ karpūraṃ vibhave sati || 8.321 ||
ata eva vāyupurāṇe
ārātrikaṃ tu niḥsnehaṃ niḥsnehayati devatām |
ataḥ saṃśamayitvaiva punaḥ pūjanamācaret || 8.322 ||
ata eva dvārakāmāhātmye
tatraiva kṛtvā pūjādikaṃ sarvaṃ jvalantaṃ kṛṣṇamūrdhani |
ārātrikaṃ prakurvāṇo modate kṛṣṇasannidhau || 8.323 ||
iti |
kecinnīrājanatpaścādicchanti praṇatiṃ tataḥ |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tataḥ stotraṃ gītanṛtyādikaṃ tataḥ || 8.324 ||
evaṃ bhagavataḥ svasvasampradāyānusārataḥ |
pravartante prabhorbhaktau bhaktyā sarvaṃ hi śobhanam || 8.325 ||
tato nikṣipya devasyopari puspāñjalitrayam |
vicitrairmadhuraiḥ stotraihstutiṃ kurvīta bhaktimān || 8.326 ||
atha stutividhiḥ
mahābhārate
ārirādhayiṣuḥ kṛṣṇaṃ vācaṃ jigadiṣāmi yam |
tayā vyāsasamāsinyā prīyatāṃ madhusūdanaḥ || 8.327 ||
iti |
ārambhe ca stuteretaṃ ślokaṃ stutiparaḥ paṭhet |
satyāṃ tasyāṃ samāptau ca ślokaṃ saṅkīrtayedimam || 8.328 ||
iti vidyātapoyonirayonirviṣṇurīritaḥ |
vagyajñenārcito devaḥ prīyatāṃ me janārdanaḥ || 8.329 ||
pūrvatāpanīśrutiṣu
oṃ namo viśvarūpāya viśvasthityantahetave |
viśveśvarāya viśvāya govindāya namo namaḥ || 8.330 ||
namo vijñānarūpāya paramānandarūpiṇe |
kṛṣṇāya gopīnāthāya govindāya namo namaṃ || 8.331 ||
namaḥ kamalanetrāya namaḥ kamalamāline |
namaḥ kamalanābhāya kamalāpataye namaḥ || 8.332 ||
barhāpīḍābhirāmāya rāmayākuṇṭhamedhase |
ramāmānasahaṃsāya govindāya namo namaṃ || 8.333 ||
kaṃsavaṃśavināśāya keśicāṇūraghātine |
vṛṣabhadhvajavandyāya pārthasārathaye namaḥ || 8.334 ||
veṇuvādanaśīlāya gopālāyāhimardine |
kālindīkūlalolāya lolakuṇḍaladhāriṇe || 8.335 ||
vallavīvadanāmbhojamāline nṛtyaśāline |
namaḥ praṇatapālāya śrīkṛṣṇāya namo namaḥ || 8.336 ||
namaḥ pāpapraṇāśāya govardhanadharāya ca |
pūtanājīvitāntāya tṛṇāvārtāsuhāriṇe || 8.337 ||
niṣkalāya vimohāya śuddhāyāśuddhavairiṇe |
advitīyāya mahate śrīkṛṣṇāya namo namaḥ || 8.338 ||
prasīda paramānanda prasīda parameśvara |
ādhivyādhibhujaṅgena daṣṭaṃ māmuddhara prabho || 8.339 ||
śrīkṛṣṇa rukmiṇīkānta gopījanamanohara |
saṃsārasāgare magnaṃ māmuddhara jagadguro || 8.340 ||
keśava kleśaharaṇa nārāyaṇa janārdana |
govinda paramānanda māṃ samuddhara mādhava || 8.341 ||
ekādaśaskandhe
dhyeyaṃ sadā paribhavaghnamabhīṣṭadohaṃ
tīrthāspadaṃ śivaviriñcinutaṃ śaraṇyaṃ
bhṛtyārtihaṃ praṇatapāla bhavābdhipotaṃ
vande mahāpuruṣa te caraṇāravindam || 8.342 ||
tyaktvā sudustyajasurepsitarājyalakṣmīṃ
dharmiṣṭha āryavacasā yadagādaraṇyam |
māyāmṛgaṃ dayitayepsitamanvadhāvad
vande mahāpuruṣa te caraṇāravindam || 8.343 ||
atha vadyasya māhātmyaṃ
vaidikānīdṛśānyeva kṛṣṇe paurāṇikānyapi |
tāntrikāṇi ca śāstrāṇi stotrāṇyabhinavānyapi || 8.344 ||
viṣṇudharmottare haṃsagītayaṃ
abhraṣṭalakṣaṇaiḥ kṛtvā svayaṃ viracitākṣaraiḥ |
stavaṃ brāhmaṇaśārdūlastasmātkāmānavāpnuyāt || 8.345 ||
stutimāhātmyaṃ
viṣṇudharme
sarvadeveṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvadeveṣu yatphalam |
narastatphalamāpnoti stutvā devaṃ janārdanam || 8.346 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
na vittadānanicayairbahubhirmadhusūudanaḥ |
tathā toṣamavāpnoti yathā stotrairdvijottamaḥ || 8.347 ||
nārasiṃhe
stotrairjapaiśca devāgre yaḥ stauti madhusūdanam |
sarvapāpavinirmukto viṣṇulokamavāpnuyāt || 8.348 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
stuvannameyamāhātmyaṃ bhaktigrathitaramyavāk |
bhavedbrahmādidurlabhyaprabhukāruṇyabhājanam || 8.349 ||
yathā narasya stuvato bālakasyeva tuṣyati |
mugdhavākyairna hi tathā vibudhānāṃ jagatpitā || 8.350 ||
abalaṃ prabhurīpsitonnatiṃ
kṛtayatnaṃ svayaśahstave ghrṇī |
svayamuddharati stanārthinaṃ
padalagnaṃ jananīva bālakam || 8.351 ||
skānde amṛtasaroddhare
śrīkṛṣṇastavaratnaughairyeṣāṃ jihvā tvalaṅkṛtā |
namasyā munisiddhānāṃ vandanīyā divaukasām || 8.352 ||
tatraiva karttikamāhātmye śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
stotrāṇāṃ paramaṃ stotraṃ viṣṇornāmasahasrakam |
hitvā stotrasahasrāṇi paṭhanīyaṃ mahāmune || 8.353 ||
tenaikena muniśreṣṭha pathitena sadā hariḥ |
prītimāyāti deveśo yugakoṭiśatāni ca || 8.354 ||
iti |
snāne yatstotramāhātmyaṃ likhitaṃ lekhyamagrataḥ |
yacca kīrtanamāhātmyaṃ sarvaṃ jñeyamihāpi tat || 8.355 ||
tannityata
viṣṇudharme
nūnaṃ tatkaṇṭhaśālūkamathavā pratijihvikā |
rogo vānyo na sā jihvā yā na stauti harerguṇān || 8.356 ||
atha vandanaṃ
praṇamedatha sāṣṭāṅgaṃ tanmudrāṃ ca pradarśayet |
paṭhetpratipraṇāmaṃ ca prasīda bhagavanniti || 8.357 ||
taduktamekādaśe śrībhagavatā
stavairuccāvacaiḥ stotraiḥ paurāṇaiḥ prākṛtairapi |
stutvā prasīda bhagavanniti vandeta daṇḍavat || 8.358 ||
atha praṇāmavidhiḥ
tatraiva
śiro matpādayoḥ kṛtvā bāhubhyāṃ ca parasparam |
prapannaṃ pāhi māmīśa bhītaṃ mṛtyugrahārṇavāt || 8.359 ||
kiṃ cāgame
dorbhyāṃ padbhyāṃ ca jānubhyāmurasā śirasā dṛśā |
manasā vacasā ceti praṇāmo'ṣṭāṅga īritaḥ || 8.360 ||
jānubhyāṃ caiva bāhubhyāṃ śirasā vacasā dhiyā |
pañcāṅgakaḥ praṇāmaḥ syātpūjāsu pravarāvimau || 8.361 ||
iti |
garudaṃ dakṣine kṛtvā kuryāttatpṛṣṭhato budhaḥ |
avaśyaṃ ca praṇāmāṃstrīn śaktaścedadhikādhikān || 8.362 ||
tathā ca nāradapañcarātre
sandhiṃ vīkṣya hariṃ cādyaṃ gurūn svagurumeva ca |
dvicaturviṃśadathavā caturviṃśattadardhakam |
namettadardhamathavā tadardhaṃ sarvathā namet || 8.363 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
devārcādarśanādeva praṇamenmadhusūdanam |
snānāpekṣā na kartavyā dṛṣṭvārcāṃ dvijasattamaḥ |
devārcādṛṣṭapūtaṃ hi śuci sarvaṃ prakīrtitam || 8.364 ||
atha namaskaramāhātmyaṃ
nārasiṃhe
namaskāraḥ smṛto yajñaḥ sarvayajñeṣu cottamaḥ |
namaskāreṇa cakena sāṣṭāṅgena hariṃ vrajet || 8.365 ||
skānde
daṇḍapraṇāmaṃ kurute viṣṇave bhaktibhāvitaḥ |
reṇusaṅkhyaṃ vasetsvarge manvantaraśataṃ naraḥ || 8.366 ||
tatraiva śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
praṇamya daṇḍavadbhūmau namaskāreṇa yo'rcayet |
sa yāṃ gatimavāpnoti na tāṃ kratuśatairapi |
namaskāreṇa caikena naraḥ puto hariṃ vrajet || 8.367 ||
tatraiva śrīsivomasaṃvāde
bhūmimāpīḍya jānubhyāṃ śira āropya vai bhuvi |
praṇamedyo hi deveśaṃ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 8.368 ||
tatraivanyatra
tīrthakoṭisahasrāṇi tīrthakoṭiśatāni ca |
nārāyaṇapraṇāmasya kālaṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 8.369 ||
śāṭhyenāpi namaskāraṃ kurvataḥ śārṅgadhanvane |
śataśanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣanādeva naśyati || 8.370 ||
reṇumaṇḍitagātrasya kaṇā dehe bhavanti yat |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṇuloke mahīyate || 8.371 ||
viṣṇudharmottare
abhivādyaṃ jagannāthaṃ kṛtārthaśca tathā bhavet |
namaskārakriyā tasya sarvapāpapraṇāśinī || 8.372 ||
jānubhyāṃ caiva pāṇibhyāṃ śirasā ca vicakṣaṇaḥ |
kṛtvā praṇāmaṃ devasya sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt || 8.373 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe
anādinidhanaṃ devaṃ daityadānavadāraṇam |
ye namanti narā nityaṃ na hi paśyanti te yamam || 8.374 ||
ye janā jagatāṃ nāthaṃ nityaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ dvijāḥ |
namanti na hi te viṣṇoḥ snānādanyatra gāminaḥ || 8.375 ||
nāradīye
eko'pi kṛṣṇāya kṛtaḥ praṇāmo
daśāśvamedhāvabhṛthairna tulyaḥ |
daśāśvamedhī punareti janma
kṛṣṇapraṇāmī na punarbhavāya || 8.376 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
viṣṇordaṇḍapraṇāmārthaṃ bhaktena patito bhuvi |
patitaṃ pātakaṃ kṛtsnaṃ nottiṣṭhati punaḥ saha || 8.377 ||
pādme devadūtavikuṇḍalasaṃvāde
tapastaptvā naro ghoramaraṇye niyatendriyaḥ |
yatphalaṃ samavāpnoti tannatvā garuḍadhvajam || 8.378 ||
kṛtvāpi bahuśaḥ pāpaṃ naro mohasamanvitaḥ |
na yāti narakaṃ natvā sarvapāpaharaṃ harim || 8.379 ||
tatraiva vedanidhistutau
api pāpaṃ durācāraṃ naraṃ tatpraṇato hareḥ |
nekṣante kiṅkarā yāmyā ulūkāstapanaṃ yathā || 8.380 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe śrīyamasya nijabhaṭānuśāsane
harimamaragaṇārcitāṅghripadmaṃ
praṇamati yaḥ paramārthato hi martyaḥ |
tamapagatasamastapāpabandhaṃ
vraja parihṛtya yathāgnimājyasiktam || 8.381 ||
brahmavaivarte
śaraṇāgatarakṣaṇodyataṃ
harimīśaṃ praṇamanti ye narāḥ |
na patanti bhavāmbudhau sphuṭaṃ
patitānuddharati sma tānasau || 8.382 ||
aṣṭamaskandhe ca balivākye
aho praṇāmāya kṛtaḥ samudyamaḥ
prapannabhaktārthavidhau samāhitaḥ |
yallokapālaistvadanugraho'marair
alabdhapūrvo'pasade'sure'rpitaḥ || 8.383 ||
ataiva nārāyaṇavyūhastave
aho bhāgyamaho bhāgyamaho bhāgyaṃ nṝṇāmidam |
yeṣāṃ haripadābjāgre śiro nyastaṃ yathā tathā || 8.384 ||
kiṃ ca, nārasiṃhe śrīyamoktau
tasya vai narasiṃhasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ |
praṇāmaṃ ye prakurvanti teṣāmapi namo namaḥ || 8.385 ||
bhaviṣyottare ca
viṣṇordevajagaddhāturjanārdanajagatpateḥ |
praṇāmaṃ ye prakurvanti teṣāmapi namo namaḥ || 8.386 ||
iti |
atha praṇāmanityata
bṛhannāradīye lubdhakopākhyānārambhe
sakṛdvā na namedyastu viṣṇave sarmakāriṇe |
śavoparaṃ vijānīyātkadācidapi nālapet || 8.387 ||
kiṃ ca, pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye yamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde
paśyanto bhagavaddvāraṃ nāma śāstraparicchadam |
akṛtvā tatpraṇāmādi yānti te narakaukasaḥ || 8.388 ||
atha namaskaraniṣiddhani
viṣṇusmṛtau
janmaprabhṛti yatkiñcitpumān vai dharmamācaret |
sarvaṃ tanniṣphalaṃ yātyekahastābhivādanāt || 8.389 ||
vārāhe
vastraprāvṛtadehastu yo naraḥ praṇameta mām |
śvitrī sa jāyate mūrkhahsapta janmāni bhāminī || 8.390 ||
kiṃ cānyatra
agre pṛṣṭhe vāmabhāge samīpe garbhamandire |
japahomanamaskārānna kuryātkeśavālaye || 8.391 ||
api ca
sakṛdbhūmau nipatito na śaktaḥ praṇamenmuhuḥ |
utthāyotthāya kartavyaṃ daṇḍavatpraṇipātanam || 8.392 ||
iti |
atha pradakṣiṇā
tataḥ pradakṣināṃ kuryādbhaktyā bhagavato hareḥ |
nāmāni kīrtayan śaktau tāṃ ca sāṣṭāṅgavandanām || 8.393 ||
pradakṣiṇāśaṅkhyā
nārasiṃhe
ekāṃ cāṇḍyāṃ ravau sapta tisro dadyādvināyake |
catasraḥ keśave dadyātśive tvardhapradakṣinām || 8.394 ||
atha pradakṣiṇāmāhātmyaṃ
vārāhe
pradakṣiṇāṃ ye kurvanti bhaktiyuktena cetasā |
na te yamapuraṃ yānti yānti puṇyakṛtāṃ gatim || 8.395 ||
yastriḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kuryātsāṣṭāṅgakapraṇāmakam |
daśāśvamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnuyānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 8.396 ||
skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde
viṣṇorvimānaṃ yaḥ kuryātsakṛdbhaktyā pradakṣiṇām |
aśvamedhasahasrasya phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ || 8.397 ||
tatra caturmasyamāhātmye
caturvāraṃ bhramībhistu jagatsarvaṃ carācaram |
krāntaṃ bhavati viprāgrya tattīrthagamanādhikam || 8.398 ||
tatraivānyatra
pradakṣiṇāṃ tu yaḥ kuryāthariṃ bhaktyā samanvitaḥ |
haṃsayuktavimānena viṣṇulokaṃ sa gacchati || 8.399 ||
nārasiṃhe
pradakṣiṇena caikena devadevasya mandire |
kṛtena yatphalaṃ nṛṇāṃ tacchṛṇuṣva nṛpātmaja |
pṛthvīpradakṣiṇaphalaṃ yattatprāpya hariṃ vrajet || 8.400 |||
anyatra ca
evaṃ kṛtvā tu kṛṣṇasya yaḥ kuryāddviḥ pradakṣiṇām |
saptadvīpavatīpuṇyaṃ labhate tu pade pade |
paṭhannāmasahasraṃ tu nāmānyevātha kevalam || 8.401 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye
viṣṇuṃ pradakṣiṇīkurvāṇyastatrāvartate punaḥ |
tadevāvartanaṃ tasya punarnāvartate bhave || 8.402 ||
bṛhannāradīye yamabhagīrathasaṃvāde
pradakṣiṇātrayaṃ kuryādyo viṣṇormanujeśvara |
sarvapāpavinirmukto devendratvaṃ samaśnute || 8.403 ||
tatraiva pradakṣiṇāmāhātmye sudharmopākhyānārambhe
bhaktyā kurvanti ye viṣṇohpradakṣiṇācatuṣṭayam |
te'pi yanti paraṃ sthānaṃ sarvalokottamottamam || 8.404 ||
iti |
tatkhyātaṃ yatsudharmasya pūrvasmin gṛdhrajanmani |
kṛṣṇapradakṣiṇābhyāsānmahāsiddhirabhūditi || 8.405 ||
atha pradakṣiṇāyaṃ niṣiddhaṃ
viṣṇusmṛtau
ekahastapraṇāmaśca ekā caiva pradakṣiṇā |
akāle darśanaṃ viṣṇorhanti puṇyaṃ purākṛtam || 8.406 ||
kiṃ ca
kṛṣṇasya purato naiva sūryasyaiva pradakṣiṇām |
kuryādbhramarikārūpāṃ vaimukhyapadanīṃ prabhoḥ || 8.407 ||
tathā coktaṃ
pradakṣiṇāṃ na kartavyaṃ vimukhatvācca kāraṇāt || 8.408 ||
atha karmādyarpaṇam
tataḥ śrīkṛṣṇapādābje dāsyenaiva samarpayet |
tribhirmantraiḥ svakarmāṇi sarvāṇyātmānamapyatha || 8.409 ||
mantraśca
itaḥ pūrvaṃ prāṇabuddhidharmādhikārato jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyavasthāsu manasā vācā karmaṇā hastābhyāṃ padbhyāmudareṇa śiśnā yatsmṛtaṃ yaduktaṃ yatkṛtaṃ tatsarvaṃ śrīkṛṣṇārpaṇaṃ bhavatu svāhā māṃ madīyaṃ ca sakalaṃ haraye samarpayāmīti | oṃ tatsat || 8.410 ||
iti |
atha tatra karmarpanaṃ
bṛhannāradīye
virāgī cetkarmaphale na kiñcidapi kārayet |
arpayetsvakṛtaṃ karma prīyatāmiti me hariḥ || 8.411 ||
ata eva kūrmapurāṇe
prīṇātu bhagavānīśaḥ karmaṇānena śāśvataḥ |
karoti satataṃ buddhyā brahmārpaṇamidaṃ param || 8.412 ||
yadvā phalānāṃ sannyāsaṃ prakuryātparameśvare |
karmaṇāmetadapyāhurbrahmārpaṇamanuttamam || 8.413 ||
atha karmārpaṇavidhiḥ
dakṣeṇa pāṇinārghyasthaṃ gṛhītvā culukodakam |
nidhāya kṛṣṇapādābjasamīpe prārthayedidam || 8.414 ||
pādatrayakramākrānta trailokeśvara keśava |
tvatprasādādidaṃ toyaṃ pādyaṃ te'stu janārdana || 8.415 ||
atha karmarpanamāhātmyaṃ
bṛhannāradīye
paralokaphalaprepsuḥ kuryātkarmāṇyatandritaḥ |
harernivedayettāni tatsarvaṃ tvakṣayaṃ bhavet || 8.416 ||
ata eva nārāyaṇavyūhastave
kṛṣṇārpitaphalāḥ kṛṣṇaṃ svadharmeṇa yajanti ye |
viṣṇubhaktyarthino dhanyāstebhyo'pīha namo namaḥ || 8.417 ||
atha svārpaṇavidhiḥ
ahaṃ bhagavato'ṃśo'smi sadā dāso'smi sarvathā |
tatkṛpāpekṣako nityami tyātmānaṃ samarpayet || 8.418 ||
tathā coktaṃ śrīśaṅkarācāryapādaiḥ
satyapi bhedāpagame nātha tavāhaṃ na māmakīnastvam |
sāmudro hi taraṅgaḥ kvacana samudro na tāraṅgaḥ || 8.419 ||
athātmārpaṇamāhātmyam
saptamaskandhe śrīprahlādoktau
dharmārthakāma iti yo'bhihitastrivarga
īkṣā trayī nayadamau vividhā ca vārtā |
manye tadetadakhilaṃ nigamasya satyaṃ
svātmārpaṇaṃ svasuhṛdaḥ paramasya puṃsaḥ || 8.420 ||
ekādaśe śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde
martyo yadā tyaktasamastakarmā
niveditātmā vicikīrṣito me |
tadāmṛtatvaṃ pratipadyamāno
mayātmabhūyāya ca kalpate vai || 8.421 ||
atha japaḥ
japasya purataḥ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ budhaḥ |
mantrārthasmṛtipūrvaṃ ca japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
mūlaṃ lekhyena vidhinā sadaiva japamālayā || 8.422 ||
śaktau'ṣṭādhikasāhasraṃ japettaṃ cārpayan japam |
prāṇāyāmāṃśca kṛtvā trīn dadyātkṛṣṇakare jalam || 8.423 ||
tatra cāyaṃ mantraḥ
guhyātiguhyagoptā tvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmatkṛtaṃ japam |
siddhirbhavatu me deva tvatprasādāttvayi sthite || 8.424 ||
iti |
japaprakāro yo'pekṣyo mālādiniyamātmakaḥ |
puraścaryāprasaṅge tu sa vilikhyate'grataḥ || 8.425 ||
arpitaṃ taṃ ca sañcintya svīkṛtaṃ prabhuṇākhilam |
punaḥ stutvā yathāśakti praṇamya prārthayedidam || 8. 426 ||
āgame
mantrahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ bhaktihīnaṃ janārdana |
yatpūjitaṃ mayā deva paripūrṇaṃ tadastu me || 8.427 ||
kiṃ ca
yaddattaṃ bhaktimātreṇa patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ jalam |
āveditaṃ nivedyaṃ tu tadgṛhāṇānukampayā || 8.428 ||
vidhihīnaṃ mantrahīnaṃ yatkiñcidupapāditam |
kriyāmantravihīnaṃ vā tatsarvaṃ kṣantumarhasi || 8.429 ||
kiṃ ca
ajñānādathavā jñānādaśubhaṃ yanmayā kṛtam |
kṣantumarhasi tatsarvaṃ dāsyenaiva gṛhāṇa mām || 8.430 ||
sthitiḥ sevā gatiryātrā smṛtiścintā stutirvacaḥ |
bhūyātsarvātmanā viṣṇo madīyaṃ tvayi ceṣṭitam || 8.431 ||
api ca
kṛṣṇa rāma mukunda vāmana vāsudeva jagadguro |
matsya kacchapa nārasiṃha varāha rāghava pāhi mām || 8.432 ||
devadānavanāradādivandya dayānidhe |
devakīsuta dehi me tava pādabhaktimacalām || 8.433 ||
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe
nātha yonisahasreṣu yeṣu yeṣu vrajāmyaham |
teṣu teṣvacyutā bhaktiracyute'stu sadā tvayi || 8.434 ||
yā prītiravivekānāṃ viṣayeṣvanapāyinī |
tvāmanusmarataḥ sā me hṛdayānnāpasarpatu || 8.435 ||
pāṇḍavagītāyāṃ
kīṭeṣu pakṣiṣu mṛgeṣu sarīsṛpeṣu
rakṣaḥpiśācamanujeṣvapi yatra tatra |
jātasya me bhavatu keśava te prasādāt
tvayyeva bhaktiratulāvyabhicāriṇī ca || 8.436 ||
pādme
yuvatīnāṃ yathā yūni yūnāṃ ca yuvatau yathā |
mano'bhiramate tadvanmano'bhiramatāṃ tvayi || 8.437 ||
athāparādhakṣamāpaṇam
tato'parādhān śrīkṛṣṇaṃ kṣamāśīlaṃ kṣamāpayet |
sakāku kīrtayan ślokānuttamān sāmpradāyikān || 8.438 ||
tathā hi
aparādhasahasrāṇi kriyante'harniśaṃ mayā |
dāso'hamiti māṃ matvā kṣamasva madhusūdana || 8.439 ||
kiṃ ca
pratijñā tava govinda na me bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati |
iti saṃsmṛtya saṃsmṛtya prāṇān saṃdhārayāmyaham || 8.440 ||
athāparādhāḥ
āgame
yānairvā pādukairvāpi gamanaṃ bhagavadgṛhe |
devotsavādyasevā ca apraṇāmastadagrataḥ || 8.441 ||
ucchiṣṭe vāpyaśauce vā bhagavadvandanādikam |
ekahastapraṇāmaśca tatpurastātpradakṣiṇam || 8.442 ||
pādaprasāraṇaṃ cāgre tathā paryaṅkabandhanam |
śayanaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ cāpi mithyābhāṣaṇameva ca || 8.443 ||
uccairbhāṣā mitho jalpo rodanāni ca vigrahaḥ |
nigrahānugrahau caiva nṛṣu ca krūrabhāṣaṇam || 8.444 ||
kambalāvaraṇaṃ caiva paranindā parastutiḥ |
aślīlabhāṣaṇaṃ caiva adhovāyuvimokṣaṇam || 8.445 ||
śaktau gauṇopacāraśca aniveditabhakṣaṇam |
tattatkālodbhavānāṃ ca phalādīnāmanarpaṇam || 8.446 ||
viniyuktāvaiśiṣṭhasya pradānaṃ vyañjanādike |
pṛṣṭhīkṛtyāsanaṃ caiva pareṣāmabhivādanam || 8.447 ||
gurau maunaṃ nijastotraṃ devatānindanaṃ tathā |
aparādhāstathā viṣṇordvātriṃśatparikīrtitāḥ || 8.448 ||
vārāhe
dvātriṃśadaparādhā ye kīrtyante vasudhe mayā |
vaiṣṇavena sadā te tu varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 8.449 ||
ye vai na varjayantyetānaparādhānmayoditān |
sarvadharmaparibhraṣṭāḥ pacyante narake ciram || 8.450 ||
rājānnabhakṣaṇaṃ caivamāpadyapi bhayāvaham |
dhvāntāgāre hareḥ sparśaḥ paraṃ sukṛtanāśanaḥ || 8.451 ||
tathaiva vidhimullaṅghya sahasā sparśanaṃ hareḥ |
dvārodghāṭo vinā vādyaṃ kroḍamāṃsanivedanam || 8.452 ||
pādukābhyāṃ tathā viṣṇormandirāyopasarpaṇam |
kukkurocchiṣṭakalanaṃ maunabhaṅgo'cyutārcane || 8.453 ||
tathā pūjanakāle viḍutsargāya sarpaṇam |
śrāddhādikamakṛtvā ca navānnasya ca bhakṣaṇam || 8.454 ||
adattvā gandhamālyādi dhūpanaṃ madhughātinaḥ |
akarmaṇyaprasūnena pūjanaṃ ca harestathā || 8. 455 ||
akṛtvā dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca kṛtvā nidhūvanaṃ tathā |
spṛṣṭvā rajasvalāṃ dīpaṃ tathā mṛtakameva ca || 8.456 ||
raktaṃ nīlamadhautaṃ ca pārakyaṃ malinaṃ paṭam |
paridhāya mṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā vimucyāpānamārutam || 8.457 ||
krodhaṃ kṛtvā śmaśānaṃ ca gatvā bhuktāpyajīrṇayuk |
bhakṣayitvā kroḍamāṃsaṃ pinyākaṃ jālapādakam || 8.458 ||
tathā kusumbhaśākaṃ ca tailābhyaṅgaṃ vidhāya ca |
hareḥ sparśo hareḥ karmakaraṇaṃ pātakāvaham || 8.459 ||
kiṃ tatraiva
mama śāstraṃ bahiṣkṛtya asmākaṃ yaḥ prapadyate |
muktvā ca mama śāstrāṇi śāstramanyatprabhāṣase || 8.460 ||
madyapastu samāsādya praviśedbhavanaṃ mama || 8.461 ||
yo me kusumbhaśākena prāpaṇaṃ kurute naraḥ || 8.462 ||
api ca
mama dṛṣṭerabhimukhaṃ tāmbūlaṃ carvayettu yaḥ |
kurūvakaḥ palāśasthaiḥ puṣpaiḥ kuryānmamārcanam || 8.463 ||
mamārcāmāsure kāle yaḥ karoti vimūḍhadhīḥ |
pīṭhāsanopaviṣṭo yaḥ pūjayedvā nirāsanaḥ || 8.464 ||
vāmahastena māṃ dhṛtvā snāpayedvā vimūḍhadhīḥ |
pūjā paryuṣitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ ṣṭhīvanaṃ garvakalpanam || 8.465 ||
tiryakpuṇḍradharo bhūtvā yaḥ karoti mamārcanam |
yācitaiḥ patrapuṣpādyairyaḥ karoti mamārcanam || 8.466 ||
aprakṣālitapādo yaḥ praviśenmama mandiram |
avaiṣṇavasya pakvānnaṃ yo mahyaṃ vinivedayet || 8.467 ||
avaiṣṇaveṣu paśyatsu mama pūjāṃ karoti yaḥ |
apūjayitvā vighneśaṃ sambhāṣya ca kapālinam || 8.468 ||
naraḥ pūjāṃ tu yaḥ kuryātsnapanaṃ ca nakhāmbhasā |
amaunī dharmaliptāṅgo mama pūjāṃ karoti yaḥ || 8.469 ||
jñeyāḥ pare'pi bahavo'parādhāḥ sadasammataiḥ |
ācāraiḥ śāstravihitaniṣiddhātikramādibhiḥ |
tatrāpi sarvathā kṛṣṇanirmālyaṃ tu na laṅghayet || 8.470 ||
tathā ca nārasiṃhe śantanuṃ prati nāradavākyam
ataḥ paraṃ tu nirmālyaṃ na laṅghaya mahīpate |
narasiṃhasya devasya tathānyeṣāṃ divaukasām || 8.471 ||
kṛṣṇasya paritoṣepsurna tacchapathamācaret |
nānādevasya nirmālyamupayuñjīta na kvacit || 8.472 ||
tathā viṣṇudharmottare
āpādyapi ca kaṣṭāyāṃ deveśaśapathaṃ naraḥ |
na karoti hi yo brahmaṃstasya tuṣyati keśavaḥ || 8.473 ||
na dhārayati nirmālyamanyadevadhṛtaṃ tu yaḥ |
bhuṅkte na cānyanaivedyaṃ tasya tuṣyati keśavaḥ || 8.474 ||
iti |
athāparādhaśamanam
saṃvatsarasya madhye tu tīrthe śaukarake mama |
kṛtopavāsaḥ snānena gaṅgāyāṃ śuddhimāpnuyāt || 8. 475 ||
mathurāyāṃ tathāpyevaṃ sāparādhaḥ śuci bhavet || 8. 476 ||
anayostīrthayorekaṃ yaḥ seveta sukṛtī naraḥ |
sahasrajanmajanitānaparādhān jahāti saḥ || 8. 477 ||
skānde
ahanyahani yo martyo gītādhyāyaṃ paṭhettu vai |
dvātriṃśadaparādhāṃstu kṣamate tasya keśavaḥ || 8. 478 ||
tatra kārttikamāhātmye
tulasyā ropaṇaṃ kāryaṃ śrāvaṇeṣu viśeṣataḥ |
aparādhasahasrāṇi kṣamate puruṣottamaḥ || 8. 479 ||
tatraivānyatra
dvādaśyāṃ jāgare viṣṇoryaḥ paṭhettulasīstavam |
dvātriṃśadaparādhāni kṣamate tasya keśavaḥ || 8. 480 ||
yaḥ karoti hareḥ pūjāṃ kṛṣṇaśatrāṅkito naraḥ |
aparādhasahasrāṇi nityaṃ harati keśavaḥ || 8.481 ||
atha śeṣagrahaṇam
tato bhagavatā dattaṃ manyamāno dayālunā |
mahāprasāda ityuktvā śeṣaṃ śirasi dhārayet || 8.482 ||
atha nirmālyadhāraṇanityatā
pādme śrīgautamāmbarīṣasaṃvāde
ambarīṣa harerlagnaṃ nīraṃ puṣpaṃ vilepanam |
bhaktyā na dhatte śirasā śvapacādadhiko hi saḥ || 8.483 ||
atha śrībhagavannirmālyamāhātmyaṃ
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde
kṛṣṇottīrṇaṃ tu nirmālyaṃ yasyāṅgaṃ spṛśate mune |
sarvarogairtathā pāpairmukto bhavati nārada || 8.484 ||
viṣṇornirmālyaśeṣeṇa yo gātraṃ parimārjayet |
duritāni vinaśyanti vyādhayo yānti khaṇḍaśaḥ || 8.485 ||
mukhe śirasi dehe tu viṣṇūttirnaṃ tu yo vahet |
tulasīṃ muniśārdūla na tasya spṛśate kaliḥ || 8.486 ||
kiṃ ca
viṣṇumūrtisthitaṃ puṣpaṃ śirasā yo vahennaraḥ |
aparyūṣitapāpastu yāvadyugacatuṣṭayam || 8.487 ||
kiṃ kariṣyati susnāto gaṅgāyāṃ bhūsurottama |
yo vahetśirasā nityaṃ tulasīṃ viṣṇusevitāṃ || 8.488 ||
viṣṇupādābjasaṃlagnāmahorātroṣitāṃ śubhām |
tulasīṃ dhārayedyo vai tasya puṇyamanantakam || 8.489 ||
ahoratraṃ śire yasya tulasī viṣṇusevitā |
na sa lipyati pāpena padmapatramivāmbhasā || 8.490 ||
kiṃ ca
viṣṇoḥ śiraḥparibhraṣṭaṃ bhaktyā yastulasīṃ vahet |
sidhyānti sarvakāryāṇi manasā cintitāni ca || 8.491 ||
api ca
pramārjayati yo dehaṃ tulasyā vaiṣṇavo naraḥ |
sarvatīrthamayaṃ dehaṃ tatkṣanātdvija jāyate || 8.492 ||
gāruḍe
harermūrtyavaśeṣaṃ tu tulasīkāṣṭhacandanam |
nirmālyaṃ tu vahedyastu koṭitīrthaphalaṃ labhet || 8.493 ||
nāradapañcarātre
bhojananāntaraṃ viṣṇorarpitaṃ tulasīdalam |
tatkṣanātpāpanirmoktascāndrāyaṇaśatādhikaḥ || 8.494 ||
kiṃ cānyatra
kautukaṃ śṛṇu me devi viṣṇornirmālyavahninā |
tāpitaṃ nāśamāyāti brahmahatyādipātakam || 8.495 ||
ekādaśaskandhe śrībhagavantaṃ pratyuddhavoktau
tvayopayuktasraggandhavāso'laṅkāracarcitaḥ |
ucchistabhojino dāsāstava māyāṃ jayema hi || 8.496 ||
ata eva skānde śrīyamasya dūtānuśāsane
pādodakaratā ye ca harernirmālyadhārakāḥ |
viṣṇubhaktiratā ye vai te tu tyājyaḥ sudurataḥ || 8.497 ||
iti |
visarjanaṃ tu cetkāryaṃ visṛjyavaraṇāni tat |
deve tanmudrayā prārthya devaṃ hṛdi visarjayet || 8.498 ||
tathā coktaṃ
