pūjito'si mayā bhaktyā bhagavan kamalapate |
sa lakṣmīko mama svāntaṃ viśa viśrāntihetave || 8.499 ||
prārthyaivaṃ pāduke dattvā saṅgamudvāsayeddharim |
prāṇāyāmaṃ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ ca kṛtvā mudrāṃ visarjanīm || 8.500 ||
atha pūjavidhivivekaḥ
ayaṃ pūjavidhirmantrasiddhyarthasya japasya hi |
aṅgaṃ bhaktestu tanniṣṭhairnyāsādīnantareṣyate || 8.501 ||
tatra devalaye pūja nityatvena mahāprabhoḥ |
kāmyatvenāpi gehe tu prāyo nityatayā matā || 8.502 ||
sevādiniyamo devālaye devasya ceṣyate |
prāyaḥ svagehe svacchandasevā svavratarakṣayā || 8.503 ||
kiṃ ca viṣṇudharmottare
ghṛtena snapitaṃ devaṃ candanenānulepayet |
sitajātyāśca kusumaihpūjayettadanantaram || 8.504 ||
śvetena vastrayugmena tathā muktāphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
mukhyakarpūradhūpena payasā pāyasena ca || 8.505 ||
padmasūtrasya varttyā ca ghṛtadhūpena cāpyatha |
pūjayetsarvathā yatnātsarvakāmapradārcanam || 8.506 ||
kṛtvemaṃ mucyate rogī rogātśīghramasaṃśayam |
duḥkhārto mucyate duḥkhādbaddho mucyeta bandhanāt || 8.507 ||
rājagrastaśca mucyeta tathā rājabhayānnaraḥ |
kṣemeṇa gacchedadhvānaṃ sarvānarthavivarjitaḥ || 8.508 ||
iti |
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse
prātararcāsamāpano nāmāṣṭamo vilāsaḥ |
navamavilāsaḥ mahāprasādaḥ 
kevala mātra ādiparāśakti kā mahānaivedya hī mahāprasāda hai| sa prasṛdatu caitanyadevo yasya prasādataḥ | mahāprasādajātārhaḥ sadyaḥ syādadhamo'pyaham || 9.1 || atha śaśkhodakaṃ tacca kṛṣñadṛṣṭisudhokṣitam | vaiṣñavebhyaḥ pradāyābhivandya mūrdhani dhārayet || 9.2 ||
śaśkhodakamāhātmyaṃ
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde śaśkhodakaṃ harerbhaktirnirmālyaṃ pādayorjalam | candanaṃ dhūpaśeṣaṃ tu brahmahatyāpahārakam || 9.3 ||
tatraiva śaśkhamāhātmye
śaśkhasthitaṃ tu yattoyaṃ bhrāmitaṃ keśavopari | vandate śirasā nityaṃ gaśgāsnānena tasya kim || 9.4 || na dāho na klamo nārtirnarakāgnibhayaṃ na hi | yasya śaśkhodakaṃ mūrdhni kṛṣñadṛṣṭyavalokitam || 9.5 || na grahā na ca kuṣmāñḍāḥ piśācoragarakṣasāḥ | dṛṣṭvā śaśkhodakaṃ mūrdhni vidravanti diśo daśa || 9.6 || kṛṣñamūrdhni bhrāmitaṃ tu jalaṃ tacchaśkhasaṃsthitam | kṛtvā mūrdhanyavāpnoti muktiṃ viṣñoḥ prasādataḥ || 9.7 || bhrāmayitvā harermūrdhni mandiraṃ śaśkhavāriñā | prokṣayedvaiṣñavo yastu nāśubhaṃ tadgṛhe bhavet || 9.8 || nṛrājanajalaṃ yatra yatra pādodakaṃ hareḥ | tiṣṭhate muniśārdūla vardhante tatra sampadaḥ || 9.9 ||
tatraivāgre nṛrājanajalaṃ viṣñoryasya gātrāñi saṃspṛśet | yajñāvabhṛtalakṣañaṃ snānajaṃ labhate phalam || 9.10 ||
tatraiva śrṛśivoktau pādodakena devasya hatyāyutasamanvitaḥ | śudhyate nātra sandehastathā śaśkhodakena hi || 9.11 ||
bṛhadviṣñupurāñe ca tṛrthādhikaṃ yajñaśatācca pāvanaṃ jalaṃ sadā keśavadṛṣṭisaṃsthitam | chinatti pāpaṃ tulasṛvimiśritaṃ viśeṣataścakraśilāvinirmitam || 9.12 ||
atha tṛrthadhārānaṃ
kṛṣñapadabjatṛrthaṃ ca vaiṣñavebhyaḥ pradāya hi | svayaṃ bhaktyabhivandyādau pṛtvā śirasi dhārayet || 9.13 || tasya mantravidhiśca prākprātaḥsnānaprasaśgataḥ | likhito hyadhunā pāne viśeṣo likhyate kiyān || 9.14 ||
sa coktaḥ oṃ carañaṃ pavitraṃ vitataṃ purāñaṃ yena pūtastarati duṣkṛtāni | tena pavitreña śuddhena pūtā api pāpmānamarātiṃ tarema || 9.15 ||
lokasya dvāramārcayatpavitraṃ jyotiṣmatvibhrājamānaṃ mahastadamṛtasya dhārā bahudhā dohamānaṃ carañaṃ loke sudhitaṃ dadhatu || 9.16 || iti |
imaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya sarvaduṣṭagrahāpaham | prāśnṛyātprokṣayeddehaṃ putramitraparigraham || 9.17 ||
kiṃ ca viṣñoḥ pādodakaṃ pṛtaṃ koṭihatyāghanāśanam | tadevaṣṭagunaṃ pāpaṃ bhumau bindunipatanat || 9.18 ||
atha śrṛcaranodakapanamāhātmyaṃ
pādme gautamāmbarṛṣasaṃvāde hareḥ snānāvaśeṣastu jalaṃ yasyodare sthitam | ambarṛṣa prañamyoccaiḥ pādapāṃśuḥ pragṛhyatām || 9.19 ||
tatraiva devadūtavikuñḍalasaṃvāde ye pibanti narā nityaṃ śālagrāmaśilājalam | pañcagavyasahasraistu sevitaiḥ kiṃ prayojanam || 9.20 || koṭitṛrthasahasraistu sevitaiḥ kiṃ prayojanam | nityaṃ yadi pibetpuñyaṃ śālagrāmaśilājalam || 9.21 || śālagrāmaśilātoyaṃ yaḥ pibedbindunā samam | mātuḥ stanyaṃ punarnaiva na pibedbhaktibhāśnaraḥ || 9.22 ||
kiṃ ca dahanti narakān sarvān garbhavāsaṃ ca dāruñam | pṛtaṃ yaistu sadā nityaṃ śālagrāmaśilājalam || 9.23 ||
tatraiva śrṛyamadhumraketusaṃvāde śālagrāmaśilātoyaṃ bindumātraṃ tu yaḥ pibet | sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyeta bhaktimārge kṛtodyamaḥ || 9.24 ||
tatraiva pulastyabhagṛrathasaṃvāde pādodakasya māhātmyaṃ bhagṛratha vadāmi te | pāvanaṃ sarvatṛrthebhyo hatyākoṭivināśakam || 9.25 || dhṛte śirasi pṛte ca sarvāstusyanti devatāḥ | prāyaścittaṃ tu pāpānāṃ kalau pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.26 ||
kiṃ ca tribhiḥ sārasvataṃ toyaṃ saptāhena tu nārmadam | sadyaḥ punāti gāśgeyaṃ darśanādeva yāmunam || 9.27 || punantyetāni toyāni snānadarśanakṛrtanaiḥ | punāti smarañādeva kalau pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.28 ||
kiṃ ca arcitaiḥ koṭibhirliśgairnityaṃ yatkriyate phalam | tatphalaṃ śatasahasraṃ pite pādodake hareḥ || 9.29 || aśucirvā durācāro mahāpātakasaṃyutaḥ | spṛṣṭvā pādodakaṃ viṣñoḥ sadā śudhyati mānavaḥ || 9.30 || pāpakoṭiyuto yastu mṛtyukāle śiromukhe | dehe pādodakaṃ tasya na prayāti yamālayam || 9.31 || na dānaṃ na haviryeṣāṃ svādhyāyo na surārcanam | te'pi pādodakaṃ pṛtvā prayanti paramāṃ gatim || 9.32 || kārttike kārttikṛyoge kiṃ kariṣyati puṣkare | nityaṃ ca puṣkaraṃ tasya yasya pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.33 || viśākharkṣasaṃyukta vaiśākhṛ hi kariṣyati | piñḍārake mahātṛrthe ujjāyinyāṃ bhagṛratha || 9.34 || māghamāse prayāge tu snānaṃ kiṃ kariṣyati | prayāgaṃ satataṃ tasya yasya pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.35 || prabodhavāsare prāpte mathurāyāṃ ca tasya kim | nityaṃ ca yāmunaṃ snānaṃ yasya pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.36 || kasyāmuttaravāhinyāṃ gaśgāyāṃ tu mṛtasya kim | yasya pādodakaṃ viṣñormukhe caivāvatiṣṭhate || 9.37 ||
kiṃ ca hitvā pādodakaṃ viṣñoryo'nyatṛrthāni gacchati | anarghaṃ ratnamutsṛjya loṣṭraṃ vāñchati durmatiḥ || 9.38 || kurukṣetrasamo deśo binduḥ pādodakaṃ mataḥ || 9.39 || patedyatrākṣayaṃ puñyaṃ nityaṃ bhavati tadgṛhe | gayāpiñḍasamaṃ puñyaṃ putrāñāmapi jāyate || 9.40 || pādodakena devasya ye kuryuḥ pitṛtarpañam | nāsurāñāṃ bhayaṃ tasya pretajanyaṃ ca rākṣasam || 9.41 || na rogasya bhayaṃ caiva nāsti vighnakṛtaṃ bhayam | na duṣṭā naiva ghorākṣāḥ svāpadotthabhayaṃ na hi || 9.42 || grahāḥ pṛdāṃ na kurvanti caurā naśyanti dāruñāḥ | kiṃ tasya tṛrthagamane devarṣṛñāṃ ca darśane || 9.43 || yasya pādodakaṃ mūrdhni śālagrāmaśilodbhavam | prṛto bhavati mārtañḍaḥ prṛto bhavati keśavaḥ | brahmā bhavati suprṛtaḥ prṛto bhavati saśkaraḥ || 9.44 || pādodakasya māhātmyaṃ yaḥ paṭhetkeśavagrataḥ | sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatra devo janardanaḥ || 9.45 ||
brahmāñḍapurāñe śrṛbrahmanāradasaṃvāde prāyaścittaṃ yadi prāptaṃ kṛcchraṃ vā tvaghamarṣañam | so'pi pādodakaṃ pṛtvā śuddhiṃ prāpnoti tatkṣañāt || 9.46 || aśaucaṃ naiva vidyeta sūtake mṛtake'pi ca | yeṣāṃ pādodakaṃ mūrdhni prāśanaṃ ye ca kurvate || 9.47 || antakāle'pi yasyeha dṛyate pādayorjalam | so'pi sadgatimāpnoti sadācārairbahiṣkṛtaḥ || 9.48 || apeyaṃ pibate yastu bhuśkte yaścāpyabhojanam | agamyāgamanā ye vai pāpācārāśca ye narāḥ | te'pi pūjyā bhavantyāśu sadyaḥ pādāmbusevanāt || 9.49 ||
kiṃ ca apavitraṃ yadannaṃ syātpānṛyaṃ cāpi pāpinām | bhuktvā pṛtvā viśuddhaḥ syātpṛtvā pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.50 || taptakṛcchrātpañcagavyānmahākṛcchrādviśiṣyate | cāndrāyañātpārakṛcchrātparākādapi suvrata | kāyaśuddhirbhavatyāśu pṛtvā pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.51 || aguruṃ kuśkumaṃ cāpi karpūraṃ cānulepanam | viṣñupādāmbusaṃlagnaṃ tadvai pāvanapāvanam || 9.52 || dṛṣṭipūtaṃ tu yattoyaṃ viṣñunā prabhaviṣñunā | tadvai pāpaharaṃ putra kiṃ punaḥ pādayorjalam || 9.53 || etadarthamahaṃ putra śirasā viṣñutatparaḥ | dhārāyāmi pibāmyadya māhātmyaṃ viditaṃ mama || 9.54 || priyastvamagrajaḥ putrastadarthaṃ gaditaṃ mayā | rahasyaṃ me tvanarhasya na vaktavyaṃ kadācana || 9.55 || dhārayasva sadā mūrdhni prāśanaṃ kuru nityaśaḥ | janmamṛtyujarāduḥkhairmokṣaṃ yāsyasi putraka || 9.56 ||
viṣñudharmottare sadyaḥ phalapradaṃ puñyaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam | sarvamaśgalamaśgalyaṃ sarvaduḥkhavināśanam || 9.57 || duḥsvapnanāśanaṃ puñyaṃ viṣñupādodakaṃ śubham | sarvopadravahantāraṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam || 9.58 || sarvotpātapraśamanaṃ sarvapāpanivārañam | sarvakalyāñasukhadaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam || 9.59 || sarvasiddhipradaṃ dhanyaṃ sarvadharmavivardhanam | sarvaśatrupraśamanaṃ sarvabhogapradāyakam || 9.60 ||
sarvatṛrthasya phaladaṃ mūrdhni pādāmbudhārañam | prayāgasya prabhāsasya puṣkarasya ca sevane | pṛthūdakasya tṛrthasya ācānto labhate phalam || 9.61 || cakratṛrthaṃ phalaṃ yādṛktādṛñpādāmbudhārañāt | sarasvatyāṃ gayāyāṃ ca gatvā yatprāpnuyātphalam | tatphalaṃ labhate śreṣṭhaṃ mūrdhni pādāmbudhārañāt || 9.62 ||
skānde pādodakasya māhātmyaṃ devo jānāti śaśkaraḥ | viṣñupādacyutā gaśgā śirasā yena dhāritā | sthānaṃ naivāsti pāpasya dehināṃ dehamadhyataḥ || 9.63 || sabāhyābhyantaraṃ yasya vyāptaṃ pādodakena vai | pādodaṃ viṣñunaivedyamudare yasya tiṣṭhati || 9.64 || nāśrayaṃ labhate pāpaṃ svayameva vinaśyati | mahāpāpagrahagrasto vyāpto rogaśatairyadi || 9.65 || hareḥ pādodakaṃ pṛtvā mucyate nātra saṃsayaḥ | śirasā tiṣṭhate yeṣāṃ nityaṃ pādodakaṃ hareḥ || 9.66 || kiṃ kariṣyati te loke tṛrthakoṭimanorathaiḥ | ayameva paro dharma idameva paraṃ tapaḥ | idameva paraṃ tṛrthaṃ viṣñupādāmbu yatpibet || 9.67 ||
tatraiva śrṛśivomāsaṃvāde vilayaṃ yānti pāpāni pṛte pādodake hareḥ | kiṃ punarviṣñupādodaṃ śālagrāmaśilācyutam || 9.68 || viśeṣena haretpāpaṃ brahmahatyādikaṃ priye | pṛte pādodake viṣñoryadi prāñairvimucyate | hatvā yamabhaṭān sarvān vaiṣñavaṃ lokamāpnuyāt || 9.69 ||
tatraiva śrṛśivakārttikeyasaṃvāde śrṛśālagrāmaśilāmāhātmye chinnastena mahāsena garbhāvāsaḥ sudāruñaḥ | pṛtaṃ yena sadā viṣñoḥ śālagrāmaśilājalam || 9.70 || ye pibanti narā nityaṃ śālagrāmaśilājalam | pañcagavyasahasraistu prāśitaiḥ kiṃ prayojanam || 9.71 || prāyaścitte samutpanne kiṃ dānaiḥ kimupoṣañaiḥ | cāndrāyañaiśca tṛrthaiśca pṛtvā pādodakaṃ śuci || 9.72 ||
bṛhannāradiye lubdhakopākhyānārambhe haripādodakaṃ yastu kṣañamātraṃ ca dhārayet | sa snātaḥ sarvatṛrthesu viṣñoḥ priyatarastathā || 9.73 || akālamṛtyuśamanaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam | sarvaduḥkhopaśamanaṃ haripādodakaṃ śubham || 9.74 ||
tatraiva tadupākhyānānte haripādodakasparśāllubdhako vṛtakalmaṣaḥ | divyaṃ vimānamāruhya munimenamathābravṛt || 9.75 || haripādodakaṃ yasmānmayi tvaṃ kṣiptavānmune | prāpito'smi tvayā tasmāttadviṣñoḥ paramaṃ padam || 9.76 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye pādaṃ pūrvaṃ kila spṛṣṭvā gaśgābhūtsmartṛmokṣadā | viṣñoḥ sadyastu satsaśgi pādāmbu katham ṛḍyate || 9.77 || tāpatrayānalo yo'sau na śāmyetsakalābdhibhiḥ | drutaṃ śāmyati so'lpena śrṛmadviṣñupadāmbunā || 9.78 || yuddhāstrābhedyakavacaṃ bhavāgnistambhanauṣadham | sarvāśgaiḥ sarvathā dhāryaṃ pādyaṃ śucipadaḥ sadā || 9.79 || amṛtatvāvahaṃ nityaṃ viṣñupādāmbu yaḥ pibet | sa pibatyamṛtaṃ nityaṃ māse māse tu devatāḥ || 9.80 || māhātmyamiyadityasya vaktā yo'pi sa nirbhayaḥ | nanvanarghamañermūlyaṃ kalpayannaghamaśnute || 9.81 ||
anyatrapi sa brahmacārṛ sa vratṛ āśramṛ ca sadāśuciḥ | viṣñupādodakaṃ yasya mukhe śirasi vigrahe || 9.82 || janmaprabhṛtipāpānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ yadṛcchati | śālagrāmaśilāvari pāpahāri niṣevyatām || 9.83 ||
ata eva tejodravinapañcaratre śrṛbrahmañoktam pṛṭhaprañālādudakaṃ pṛthagādāya putraka | siñcayenmūrdhni bhaktānāṃ sarvatṛrthamayaṃ hi tat || 9.84 || iti |
pādodakasya māhātmyaṃ vikhyātaṃ sarvaśāstrataḥ | likhituṃ śaknuyatko hi sindhūrmṛn gañayannapi || 9.85 || viśeṣataśca pādodaṃ tulasṛdalasaṃyutam | śaśkhe kṛtvā vaiṣñavebhyo dattvā prāgvatpibetsvayam || 9.86 ||
atha śaśkhakṛtapādodakamāhātmyaṃ
skānde śrṛbrahmanāradasaṃvāde kṛtvā pādodakaṃ śaśkhe vaiṣñavānāṃ mahātmanām | yo dadyāttulasṛmiśraṃ cāndrāyañaśataṃ labhet || 9.87 || gṛhṛtvā kṛṣñapādāmbu śaśkhe kṛtvā tu vaiṣñavaḥ | yo vahetśirasā nityaṃ sa munistapasottamaḥ || 9.88 ||
pādme devadūtavikuñḍalasaṃvāde śālagrāmaśilātoyaṃ yadi śaśkhabhṛtaṃ pibet | hatyākoṭivināśaṃ ca kurute nātra saṃsayaḥ || 9.89 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyām śālagrāmaśilātoyaṃ tulasṛdalavāsitam | ye pibanti punasteṣāṃ stanyapānaṃ na vidyate || 9.90 || iti | śrṛviṣñorvaiṣñavānāṃ ca pāvanaṃ carañodakam | sarvatṛrthamayaṃ pṛtvā kuryādācamanaṃ na hi || 9.91 ||
taduktaṃ skānde śivena viṣñoḥ pādodakaṃ pṛtvā paścādaśuciśaśkayā | ācamati ca yo mohādbrahmahā sa nigadyate || 9.92 ||
srutiśca bhagavān pavitraṃ bhagavatpādau pavitraṃ, bhagavatpādodakaṃ pavitraṃ, na tatpāna ācamanṛyam | yathā hi soma iti || 9.93 ||
sauparñe ca viṣñupādodakaṃ pṛtvā bhaktapādodakaṃ tathā | ya ācamati saṃmohādbrahmahā sa nigadyate || 9.94 || iti |
tataḥ śuddhaṃ payaḥpūrñaṃ gandhapuṣpākṣatānvitam | adharopari sannyasecchaśkhaṃ bhagavadagrataḥ || 9.95 ||
atha śrṛbhagavadagrataḥ śaśkhasthapanamahatamyaṃ
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde śaśkhamāhātmye purato vāsudevasya sapuṣpaṃ sajalākṣatam | śaśkhamabhyarcitaṃ paśyettasya lakṣmṛrna durlabhā || 9.96 || sapuṣpaṃ vārijaṃ yasya durvākṣatasamanvitam | purato vāsudevasya tasya śrṛḥ sarvatomukhṛ || 9.97 || iti |
bhūtvātha bhaktimān śrṛmattulasyā kānane prabhum | sampūjyābhyarcayettaṃ ca śrṛkṛṣñacarañapriyam || 9.98 ||
atha śrṛtulasṛvanapūjā
prāgdattvārghyaṃ tato'vyarcya gandhapuṣpākṣatādinā | stutvā bhagavatṛṃ tāṃ ca prañametprārhtya dañḍavat || 9.99 ||
athārghyamantraḥ
śriyaḥ śriye śriyāvāse nityaṃ śrṛdhārāsatkṛte | bhaktyā dattaṃ mayā devi arghyaṃ gṛhña namo'stu te || 9.100 || pūjā mantraḥ
nirmitā tvaṃ purā devairarcitā tvaṃ surāsuraiḥ | tulasṛ hara me pāpaṃ pūjāṃ gṛhña namo'stu te || 9.101 ||
stutiśca
mahāprasādajananṛ sarvasaubhāgyavardhinṛ | ādhivyādhiharo nityaṃ tulasṛ tvaṃ namo'stu te || 9.102 ||
prārthanā
śrṛyaṃ dehi yaśo dehi kṛrtimāyustathā sukham | balaṃ puṣṭiṃ tathā dharmaṃ tulasṛ tvaṃ prasṛda me || 9.103 ||
prañāmavākyam
avantṛkhañḍe yā dṛṣṭā nikhilāghasaśghaśamanṛ spṛṣṭā vapuḥpāvanṛ rogāñāmabhivanditā nirasinṛ siktāntakatrāsinṛ | pratyāsattividhāyinṛ bhagavataḥ kṛṣñasya saṃropitā nyastā taccarañe vimuktiphaladā tasyai tulasyai namaḥ || 9.104 ||
bhagavatyāstulasyāstu māhātmyāmṛtasāgare | lobhātkūrditumicchāmi kṣudrastatkṣamyatāṃ tvayā || 9.105 ||
atha tulasivanapujamahatmyam skānde śrāvañadvādaśṛyoge śālagrāmaśilārcane | yatphalaṃ saśgame proktaṃ tulasṛpūjanena tat || 9.107 ||
gāruḍe dhātrṛphalena yatpuñyaṃ jayantyāṃ samupoṣañe | khagendra bhavate nṛñāṃ tulasṛpūjanena tat || 9.107 || prayāgasnānaniratau kasyāṃ prāñavimokṣañe | yatphalaṃ vihitaṃ devaistulasṛpūjanena tat || 9.108 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyām caturñāmapi varñānāmāśramāñāṃ viśeṣataḥ | strṛñāṃ ca puruṣāñāṃ ca pūjiteṣṭaṃ dadāti hi || 9.109 || tulasṛ ropitā siktā dṛṣṭā spṛṣṭā ca pāvayet | ārādhitā prayatnena sarvakāmaphalapradā || 9.110 ||
atha tulasṛvanapūjamāhātmyaṃ
pradakṣiñaṃ bhramitvā ye namaskurvanti nityaśaḥ | na teṣāṃ duritaṃ kiñcidakṣṛñamavaśiṣyate || 9.111 ||
bṛhannāradiye yajñadhvajopākhyānānte pūjyamānā ca tulasṛ yasya veśmani tiṣṭhati | tasya sarvāñi śreyāṃsi vardhante'harahardvijāḥ || 9.112 ||
ata eva pādme devadūtavikuñḍalasaṃvāde pakṣe pakṣe tu samprāpte dvādaśyāṃ vaiśyasattama | brahmādayo'pi kurvanti tulasṛvanapūjanam || 9.113 ||
ata eva śrṛtulasṛstutimahimā
ananyamanasā nityaṃ tulasṛṃ stauti yo naraḥ | pitṛdevamanuṣyāñāṃ priyo bhavati sarvadā || 9.114 ||
atha tulasṛvanamāhātmyaṃ
skānde ratiṃ badhnāti nānyatra tulasṛkānanaṃ vinā | devadevo jagatsvāmṛ kalikāle viśeṣataḥ || 9.115 || hitvā tṛrthasahasrāñi sarvānapi śiloccayan | tulasṛkānane nityaṃ kalau tiṣṭhati keśavaḥ || 9.116 || nirṛkṣitā narairyaistu tulasṛvanavāṭikā | ropitā yaiśca vidhinā samprāptaṃ paramaṃ padam || 9.117 || na dhātrṛ saphalā yatra na viṣñustulasṛvanam | tatśmaśānasamaṃ sthānaṃ santi yatra na vaiṣñavāḥ || 9.118 || keśavārthe kalau ye tu ropayantṛha bhūtale | kiṃ kariṣyatyasantuṣṭo yamo'pi saha kiśkaraiḥ || 9.119 || tulasyā ropañaṃ kāryaṃ śravañena viśeṣataḥ | aparādhasahasrāñi kṣamate puruṣottamaḥ || 9.120 || devālayeṣu sarveṣu puñyakṣetreṣu yo naraḥ | vāpayettulasṛṃ puñyāṃ tattṛrthaṃ cakrapāñinaḥ || 9.121 || ghaṭairyantraghaṭṛbhiśca siñcitaṃ tulasṛvanam | jaladhārābhirviprendra prṛñitaṃ bhuvanatrayam || 9.122 ||
tatraiva śrṛbrahmanāradasaṃvāde tulasṛgandhamādāya yatra gacchati mārutaḥ | diśo daśa ca pūtāḥ syurbhūtagrāmāścaturvidhāḥ || 9.123 || tulasṛkānanodbhūtā chāyā yatra bhaveddvija | tatra śrāddhaṃ pradātavyaṃ pitṅñāṃ tṛptihetave || 9.124 || tulasṛbṛjanikarāḥ patate yatra nārada | piñḍadānaṃ kṛtaṃ tatra pitṅñāṃ dattamakṣayam || 9.125 ||
tatraivāgre dṛṣṭā spṛṣṭā tathā dhyātā kṛrtitā namitā śrutā | ropitā sevitā nityaṃ pūjitā tulasṛ śubhā || 9.126 || navadhā tulasṛṃ nityaṃ ye bhajanti dine dine | yugakoṭisahasrāñi te vasanti harergṛhe || 9.127 || ropitā tulasṛ yāvatkurute mūlavistaram | tāvatkoṭisahasraṃ tu tanoti sukṛtaṃ kalau || 9.128 || yāvacchākhāpraśākhābhirbṛjapuṣpaiḥ phalairmune | ropitā tulasṛ pumbhirvardhate vasudhātale || 9.129 || kule teṣāṃ tu ye jātā ye bhaviṣyanti ye mṛtaḥ | ākalpaṃ yugasāhasraṃ teṣāṃ vāso harergṛhe || 9.130 ||
tatraiva cāvantṛkhañḍe tulasṛṃ ye vicinvanti dhanyāstatkarapallavāḥ | keśavārthe kalau ye ca ropayantṛha bhūtale || 9.131 || snāne dāne tathā dhyāne prāśane keśavārcane | tulasṛ dahate pāpaṃ ropañe kṛrtane kalau || 9.132 ||
kāśṛkhañḍe svadūtān prati śrṛyamānuśāsane tulasyalaśkṛtā ye ye tulasṛnāmajāpakāḥ | tulasṛvanapālā ye te tyājyā dūrato bhaṭāḥ || 9.133 ||
tatraiva dhruvacarite tulasṛ yasya bhavane pratyahaṃ paripūjyate | tadgṛhe nopasarpanti kadācidyamakiśkarāḥ || 9.134 ||
pādme devadūtavikuñḍalasaṃvāde na paśyanti yamaṃ vaiśya tulasṛvanaropañāt | sarvapāpaharaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ tulasṛvanam || 9.135 || tulasṛkānanaṃ vaiśya gṛhe yasmiṃstu tiṣṭhate | tadgṛhaṃ tṛrthṛbhūtaṃ hi no yānti yamakiśkarāḥ || 9.136 || tāvadvarṣasahasrāñi yāvadbṛjadalāni ca | vasanti devaloke tu tulasṛṃ ropayanti ye || 9.137 || tulasṛgandhamāghrāya pitarastuṣṭamānasāḥ | prayānti garuḍārūḍhāstatpadaṃ cakrapāñinaḥ || 9.138 || darśanaṃ narmadāyāstu gaśgāsnānaṃ viśāṃ vara | tulasṛdalasaṃsparsaḥ samametattrayaṃ smṛtam || 9.139 || ropañātpālanātsekāddarśanātsparśanānnṛñām | tulasṛ dahate pāpaṃ vāśmanaḥkāyasañcitam || 9.140 || āmravṛkṣasahasreña pippalānāṃ śatena ca | yatphalaṃ hi tadekena tulasṛvitapena tu || 9.141 || viṣñupūjanasaṃyuktastulasṛṃ yastu ropayet | yugāyutadaśaikaṃ sa ropako ramate divi || 9.142 ||
tatraiva vaiśākhamāhātmye puṣkarādṛni tṛrthāni gaśgādyāḥ saritastathā | vāsudevādayo devā vasanti tulasṛdale || 9.143 || dāridryaduḥkharogārtipāpāni subahūnyapi | tulasṛ harati kṣipraṃ rogāniva harṛtakṛ || 9.144 ||
tatraiva kārttikamāhātmye yadgṛhe tulasṛ bhāti rakṣābhirjalasecanaiḥ | tadgṛhe yamadūtāśca dūrato varjayanti hi || 9.145 || tulasyāstarpañaṃ ye ca pitṅnuddiśya mānavāḥ | kurvanti teṣāṃ pitarastṛptā varṣayutaṃ jalaiḥ || 9.146 || paricaryāṃ ca ye tasya rakṣayābālabandhanaiḥ | śuśrūṣito haristaistu nātra kāryā vicārañā || 9.147 || nāvajñā jātu kāryāsyā vṛkṣabhāvānmanṛṣibhiḥ | yathā hi vāsudevasya vaikuñṭhabhogavigrahaḥ || 9.148 || śālagrāmaśilārūpaṃ sthāvaraṃ bhuvi dṛśyate | tathā lakṣmyaikyamāpannā tulasṛ bhogavigrahā || 9.149 || aparaṃ sthāvaraṃ rūpaṃ bhuvi lokahitāya vai | spṛṣṭā dṛṣṭā rakṣitā ca mahāpātakanāśinṛ || 9.150 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyām viṣñostrailokyanāthasya rāmasya janakātmajā | priyā tathaiva tulasṛ sarvalokaikapāvanṛ || 9.151 || tulasṛvāṭikā yatra puṣpāntaraśatāvṛtā | śobhate rāghavastatra sṛtayā sahitaṃ svayam || 9.152 || tulasṛvipinasyāpi samantātpāvanaṃ sthalam | krośamātraṃ bhavatyeva gāśgeyasyaiva pāthasaḥ || 9.153 || tulasṛsannidhau prāñān ye tyajanti munṛśvara | na teṣāṃ narakakleśahprayānti paramaṃ padam || 9.154 ||
kiṃ ca ananyadarśanāḥ prātarye paśyanti tapodhana | ahorātrakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatkṣañātpraharanti te || 9.155 ||
gāruḍe kṛtaṃ yena mahābhāga tulasṛvanaropañam | muktistena bhaveddattā prāñināṃ vinatāsuta || 9.156 || tulasṛ vāpitā yena puñyārāme vane gṛhe | pakṣṛndra tena satyoktaṃ lokāḥ sapta pratiṣṭhitāḥ || 9.157 || tulasṛkānane yastu muhūrtamapi viśramet | janmakoṭikṛtātpāpānmucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 9.158 || pradakṣiñāṃ yaḥ kurute paṭhannāmasahasrakam | tulasṛkānane nityaṃ yajñāyutaphalaṃ labhet || 9.159 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye nityaṃ sannihito viṣñuḥ saspṛhastulasṛvane | api me'kṣatapatraikaṃ kaściddhanyo'rpayediti || 9.160 ||
bṛhannāradṛye gaśgāprasaśge saṃsārapāpavicchedi gaśgānāma prakṛrtitam | tathā tulasyā bhaktiśca harikṛrtipravaktari || 9.161 || tulasṛkānanaṃ yatra yatra padmavanāni ca | purāñapaṭhanaṃ yatra tatra sannihito hariḥ || 9.162 ||
tatraiva śrṛyamabhagṛrathasaṃvāde tulasṛropañaṃ ye tu kurvate manujeśvara | teṣāṃ puñyaphalaṃ vakṣye vadatastvaṃ niśāmaya || 9.163 || saptakoṭikulairyukto mātṛtaḥ pitṛtastathā | vasetkalpaśataṃ sāgraṃ nārāyañasamṛpagaḥ || 9.164 || tṛñāni tulasṛmūlātyāvantyapahinoti vai | tāvatṛrbrahmahatyā hi chinnattyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 9.165 || tulasyā siñcayedyastu culukodakamātrakam | kṣṛrodaśāyinā sārdhaṃ vasedācandratārakam || 9.166 || kañṭakāvarañaṃ vāpi vṛtiṃ kāṣṭhaiḥ karoti yaḥ | tulasyāḥ śṛñu rājendra tasya puñyaphalaṃ mahat || 9.167 || yāvaddināni santiṣṭhetkañṭakāvarañaṃ prabho | kulatrayayutastāvattiṣṭhedbrahmapade yugam || 9.168 || prākārakalpako yastu tulasyā manujeśvara | kulatrayeña sahito viṣñoḥ sārūpyatāṃ vrajet || 9.169 ||
ataiva tatraiva yajñadhvajopākhyānānte durlabhā tulasṛsevā durlabhā saśgatiḥ satām | durlabhā haribhaktiśca saṃsārārñavapātinām || 9.170 ||
purāñāntareṣu yatphalaṃ kratubhiḥ sviṣṭaiḥ samāptavaradakṣiñaiḥ | tatphalaṃ koṭiguñitaṃ ropayitvā hareḥ priyām || 9.171 || tulasṛṃ ye prayacchanti surāñāmarcanāya vai | ropayanti śucau deśe teṣāṃ loko'kṣayaḥ smṛtaḥ || 9.172 || ropitāṃ tulasṛṃ dṛṣṭvā nareña bhuvi bhūmipa | vivarñavadano bhūtvā tallipiṃ mārjayedyamaḥ || 9.173 || tulasṛti ca yo brūyāttrikālaṃ vadane yadi | nityaṃ sa gosahasrasya phalamāpnoti bhūsura || 9.174 || tena dattaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ kṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ gayāśire | tapastaptaṃ khagaśreṣṭha tulasṛ yena ropitā || 9.175 || śrutābhilaṣitā dṛṣṭā ropitā siñcitā natā | tulasṛ dahate pāpaṃ yugāntāgnirivākhilam || 9.176 || keśavāyatane yastu kārayettulasṛvanam | labhate cākṣayaṃ sthānaṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha vaiṣñavaḥ || 9.177 ||
anyatrāpi tulasṛkānane śrāddhaṃ pitṅñāṃ kurute tu yaḥ | gayāśrāddhaṃ kṛtaṃ tena bhāṣitaṃ viṣñunā purā || 9.178 || tulasṛgahanaṃ dṛṣṭvā vimukto yāti pātakāt | sarvathā muniśārdūla brahmahā puñyabhāgbhavet || 9.179 ||
kiṃ ca, skānde vaśiṣṭhamāndhātṛsaṃvāde śuklapakṣe yadā rājan tṛtṛyā budhasaṃyutā | śravañena mahābhāga tulasṛ cātipuñyadā || 9.180 || iti |
prasaśgātśrṛtulasyā hi mṛdaḥ kāṣṭhasya cādhunā | māhātmyaṃ likhyate kṛṣñe arpitasya dalasya ca || 9.181 || atha śrṛtulasṛmṛttikākāṣṭhādimāhātmyam
skānde śrṛbrahmanāradasaṃvāde bhūgataistulasṛmūlairmṛttikā sparśitā tu yā | tṛrthakoṭisamā jñeyā dhāryā yatnena sā gṛhe || 9.182 || yasmin gṛhe dvijaśreṣṭha tulasṛmūlamṛttikā | sarvadā tiṣṭhate dehe devatā na sa mānuṣaḥ || 9.183 || tulasṛmṛttikālipto yadi prāñān parityajet | yamena nekṣituṃ śakto yuktaḥ pāpaśatairapi || 9.184 || śirasi kriyate yaistu tulasṛmūlamṛttikā | vighnāni tasya naśyanti sānukūlā grahāstathā || 9.185 || tulasṛmṛttikā yatra kāṣṭhaṃ patraṃ ca veśmani | tiṣṭhate muniśārdūla niścalaṃ vaiṣñavaṃ padam || 9.186 || atha vṛttisampadānaṃ
tatraivānyatra maśgalārthaṃ ca doṣaghnaṃ pavitrārthaṃ dvijottama | tulasṛmūlasaṃlagnāṃ mṛttikāmāvahedbudhaḥ || 9.187 || tanmūlamṛttikāṃ yo vai dhārayiṣyati mastake | tasya tuṣṭo varān kāmān pradadāti janārdanaḥ || 9.188 ||
bṛhannāradiye gaśgāprasaśge tulasṛmūlasambhūtā haribhaktapadodbhavā | gaśgodbhavā ca mṛllekhā nayatyacyutarūpatām || 9.189 ||
gāruḍe yadgṛhe tulasṛkāṣṭhaṃ patraṃ śuṣkamathārdrakam | bhavate naiva pāpaṃ tadgṛhe saśkramate kalau || 9.190 ||
śrṛprahlādasaṃhitāyāṃ tathā viṣñudharmottare'pi patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ tvakśākhāpallavāśkuram | tulasṛsambhavaṃ mūlaṃ pāvanaṃ mṛttikādyapi || 9.191 || homaṃ kurvanti ye viprāstulasṛkāṣṭhavahninā | lave lave bhavetpuñyamagniṣṭomaśatodbhavam || 9.192 || naivedyaṃ pacate yastu tulasṛkāṣṭhavahninā | merutulyaṃ bhavedannaṃ taddattaṃ keśavāya hi || 9.193 || śarṛraṃ dahyate yeṣāṃ tulasṛkāṣṭhavahninā | na teṣāṃ punarāvṛttirviṣñulokātkathañcana || 9.194 || grasto yadi mahāpāpairagamyāgamanādikaiḥ | mṛtaḥ śudhyati dāhena tulasṛkāṣṭhavahninā || 9.195 || tṛrthaṃ yadi na samprāptaṃ smṛtirvā kṛrtanaṃ hareḥ | tulasṛkāṣṭhadagdhasya mṛtasya na punarbhavaḥ || 9.196 || yadyekaṃ tulasṛkāṣṭhaṃ madhye kāṣṭhacayasya hi | dahakāle bhavenmuktihpāpakoṭiyutasya ca || 9.197 || janmakoṭisahasraistu toṣito yairjanārdanaḥ | dahyante te janā loke tulasṛkāṣṭhavahninā || 9.198 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyām yaḥ kuryāttulasṛkāṣṭhairakṣamālāṃ surūpiñṛm | kañṭhamālāṃ ca yatnena kṛtaṃ tasyākṣayaṃ bhavet || 9.199 ||
atha tulasṛpatradhārañamāhātmyam skānde śrṛbrahmanāradasaṃvāde yasya nābhisthitaṃ patraṃ mukhe śirasi karñayoḥ | tulasṛsambhavaṃ nityaṃ tṛrthaistasya makhaiśca kim || 9.200 ||
tatraivānyatra śatrughnaṃ ca supuñyaṃ ca śrṛkaraṃ roganāśanam | kṛtvā dharmamavāpnoti śirasā tulasṛdalam || 9.201 || yaḥ kaścidvaiṣñavo loke mithyācāro'pyanāśramṛ | punāti sakalān lokān śirasā tulasṛṃ vahan || 9.202 ||
bṛhannāradiye śrṛyamabhagṛrathasaṃvāde karñena dhārayedyastu tulasṛṃ satataṃ naraḥ | tatkāṣṭhaṃ vāpi rājendra tasya nāstyupapātakam || 9.203 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye vaiṣñavavipraṃ prati yamadūtanamuktau kasmāditi na jānṛmastulasyā hi priyo hariḥ | gacchantaṃ tulasṛhastaṃ rakṣañnevanugacchati || 9.204 ||
purāñāntare ca yaḥ kṛtvā tulasṛpatraṃ śirasā viṣñutatparaḥ | karoti dharmakāryāñi phalamāpnoti cākṣayam || 9.205 ||
garuḍapurāñe mukhe tu tulasṛpatraṃ dṛṣṭvā śirasi karñayoḥ | kurute bhaskaristasya duṣkṛtasya tu mārjanam || 9.206 || trikālaṃ vinatāputra prāśayettulasṛṃ yadi viśiṣyate | kāyaśuddhiścāndrāyañaśataṃ vinā || 9.207 ||
skānde śrṛvaśiṣthamandhatṛsaṃvāde cāndrāyañattaptakṛcchrātbrahmakūrcātkuśodakāt | viśiṣyate kāyaśuddhistulasṛpatrabhakṣañāt || 9.208 || tathā ca tulasṛpatrabhakṣañādbhāvavarjitaḥ | pāpo'pi sadgatiṃ prāpta ityetadapi viśrutam || 9.209 ||
tathā ca skānde śrṛbrahma nāradaṃ prati kathite amṛtasaroddhare lubdhakopākhyānānte yamadūtan prati śrṛviṣñudūtanaṃ vacanaṃ kṣṛrābdhau mathyamāne hi tulasṛ kāmarūpiñṛ | utpāditā mahābhāga lokoddhārañahetave || 9.210 || yasyāḥ smarañamātreña darśanātkṛrtanādapi | vilayaṃ yānti pāpāni kiṃ punarviṣñupūjanāt || 9.211 || jātarūpamayaṃ puṣpaṃ padmarāgamayaṃ śubham | hitvā tu ratnajātāni gṛhñāti tulasṛdalam || 9.212 || bhakṣitaṃ lubdhakenāpi patraṃ tulasṛsambhavam | paścāddiṣṭāntamāpanno bhasmṛbhūtaṃ kalevaram || 9.213 || sitāsitaṃ yathā nṛraṃ sarvapāpakṣayāvaham | tathā ca tulasṛpatraṃ prāśitaṃ sarvakāmadam || 9.214 || yathā jātabalo vahnirdahate kānanādikam | prāśitaṃ tulasṛpatraṃ yathā dahati pātakam || 9.215 || yathā bhaktirato nityaṃ naro dahati pātakam | tulasṛbhakṣañāttattaddahate pāpasañcayam || 9.216 || cāndrāyañasahasrasya parakāñāṃ śatasya ca | na tulyaṃ jāyate puñyaṃ tulasṛpatrabhakṣañāt || 9.217 || kṛtvā pāpasahasrāñi pūrve vayasi mānavaḥ | tulasṛbhakṣañānmucyetśrutametatpurā hareḥ || 9.218 || tāvattiṣṭhanti pāpāni dehināṃ yamakiśkarāḥ | yāvanna tulasṛpatraṃ mukhe śirasi tiṣṭhati || 9.219 || amṛtādutthitā dhātrṛ tulasṛ viṣñuvallabhā | smṛtā saśkṛrtitā dhyātā prāśitā sarvakāmadā || 9.220 ||
tatraiva śrṛyamaṃ prati śrṛbhagavadvakyaṃ dhātrṛphalaṃ ca tulasṛ mṛtyukāle bhavedyadi | mukhe yasya śire dehe durgatirnāsti tasya vai || 9.221 || yukto yadi mahāpāpaiḥ sukṛtaṃ nārjitaṃ kvacit | tathāpi dṛyate mokṣastulasṛ bhakṣitā yadi || 9.222 || lubdhakenātmadehena bhakṣitaṃ tulasṛdalam | samprāpto matpadaṃ nūnaṃ kṛtvā prāñasya saṃkṣayam || 9.223 ||
purāñāntare ca upoṣya dvādaśṛṃ śuddhāṃ parāñe tulasṛdalam | prāśayedyadi viprendra aśvamedhāṣṭakaṃ labhet || 9.224 || iti |
tathaiva tulasṛsparsatkṛṣñacakreña rakṣitaḥ | brahmabandhuriti khyāto haribhaktisudhodaye || 9.225 ||
ata evoktam kiṃ citramasyāḥ patitaṃ tulasyā dalaṃ jalaṃ vā patitaṃ punṛte | lagnādhibhālasthalamālavāla mṛtsnāpi kṛtsnāghavināśanāya || 9.226 || iti |
śrṛmattulasyāḥ patrasya māhātmyaṃ yadyapṛdṛśam | tathāpi vaiṣñavaistanna grāhyaṃ kṛṣñārpañaṃ vinā || 9.227 || kṛṣñapriyatvātsarvatra śrṛtulasyāḥ prasaśgataḥ | saśkṛrtyamānaṃ dhātryāśca māhātmyaṃ likhyate'dhunā || 9.228 ||
atha dhātrṛmāhātmyaṃ skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde dhātrṛcchāyāṃ samāśritya yo'rcayeccakrapāñinām | puṣpe puṣpe'śvamedhasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 9.229 ||
tatraivāgre dhātrṛcchāyāṃ tu saṃspṛśya kuryātpiñḍaṃ tu yo mune | muktiṃ prayānti pitaraḥ prasādānmādhavasya ca || 9.230 || mūrdhni ghrāñe mukhe caiva dehe ca munisattama | dhatte dhātrṛphalaṃ yastu sa mahātmā sudurlabhaḥ || 9.231 || dhātrṛphalaviliptāśgo dhātrṛphalavibhūṣitaḥ | dhātrṛphalakṛtāhāro naro nārāyaño bhavet || 9.232 || yaḥ kaścidvaiṣñavo loke dhatte dhātrṛphalaṃ mune | priyo bhavati devānāṃ manuṣyāñāṃ tu kā kathā || 9.233 || yaḥ kaścidvaiṣñavo loke mithyācāro'pi duṣṭadhṛḥ | punāti sakalān lokān dhātrṛphaladalānvitaḥ || 9.234 || dhātrṛphalāni yo nityaṃ vahate karasampuṭe | tasya nārāyaño devo varamekaṃ prayacchati || 9.235 || dhatrṛphalaṃ ca bhoktavyaṃ kadācitkarasampuṭāt | yaśaḥ śriyamavāpnoti prasādāccakrapāñinaḥ || 9.236 || dhātrṛphalaṃ ca tulasṛ mṛttikā dvārakodbhavā | saphalaṃ jṛvitaṃ tasya tritayaṃ yasya veśmani || 9.237 || dhātrṛphalaistu saṃmiśraṃ tulasṛdalavāsitam | pibate vahate yastu tṛrthakoṭiphalaṃ labhet || 9.238 || yasmin gṛhe bhavettoyaṃ tulasṛdalavāsitam | dhātrṛphalaiśca viprendra gāśgeyaiḥ kiṃ prayojanam || 9.239 || tulasṛdalanaivedyaṃ dhātryā yasya phalaṃ gṛhe | kavacaṃ vaiṣñavaṃ tasya sarvapāpavināśanam || 9.240 ||
brahmapurāñe ca dhātrṛphalāni tulasṛ hyantakāle bhavedyadi | mukhe caiva śirasyaśge pātakaṃ nāsti tasya vai || 9.241 || kṛtvā tu bhagavatpūjāṃ na tṛrthaṃ snānamācaret | na ca devālayopetāspṛśyasaṃsparśanādinā || 9.242 ||
atha snānaviśeṣakalaḥ smṛtyarthasāre na snāyādutsave tṛrthe māśgalyaṃ vinivartya ca | anuvrajya suhṛdbandhūnarcayitveṣṭadevatām || 9.243 ||
viṣñusmṛtau ca viṣñvālayasamṛpasthān viṣñusevārthamāgatān | cāñḍālān patitān vāpi spṛṣṭvā na snānamācaret || 9.244 || devayatravivāheṣu yajñopakarañeṣu ca | utsaveṣu ca sarveṣu spṛṣṭāspṛṣṭirna vidyate || 9.245 || evaṃ prātaḥ samabhyarcya śrṛkṛṣñaṃ tadanantaram | śāstrābhyāsaṃ dvijaḥ śaktyā kuryādvipro viśeṣataḥ || 9.246 ||
yaduktam śrutismṛtṛ ubhe netre viprāñaṃ parikṛrtite | ekena vikalaḥ kāño dvābhyāmandhaḥ prakṛrtitaḥ || 9.247 ||
kiṃ ca kaurme vyasagṛtāyām yo'nyatra kurute yatnamanādhṛtya śrutiṃ dvijaḥ | sa samūḍho na sambhāṣyo vedabāhyo dvijātibhiḥ || 9.248 || na vedapathamātreña santusyedesa vai dvijaḥ | yathoktācārahinastu paśke gauriva sidati || 9.249 || yo'dhṛtya vidhivadvedaṃ vedārthaṃ na vicārayet | sa cāndhaḥ śūdrakalpastu padārthaṃ na prapadyate || 9.250 || iti |
ato'dhṛtyānvahaṃ vidvānathādhyāpya ca vaiṣñavaḥ | samarpya tacca kṛṣñāya yateta nijavṛttaye || 9.251 || vṛttau satyāṃ ca śṛñuyātsādhūn saśgatya satkathām || 9.252 ||
saptamaskandhe ṛtāmṛtābhyāṃ jṛveta mṛtena pramṛtena vā | satyānrtābhyāmapi vā na svavṛttyā kadācana || 9.253 || ṛtamuñchaśilaṃ proktamamṛtaṃ syādayācitam | mṛtaṃ tu nityaṃ yācñā syātpramṛtaṃ karṣañaṃ smṛtam || 9.254 || satyānṛtaṃ tu vāñijyaṃ śvavṛttirnṛcasevanam || 9.255 || ātmano nṛcalokānāṃ sevanaṃ vṛttisiddhaye | nitarāṃ nindyate sadbhirvaiṣñavasya viśeṣataḥ || 9.256 ||
taduktaṃ sevā śvavṛttiryairuktā na samyaktairudāhṛtam | svacchandacaritaḥ kva śvā vikrṛtāsuḥ kva sevakaḥ || 9.257 || pañṛkṛtyātmanaḥ prāñān ye vartante dvijādhamāḥ | teṣāṃ durātmanāmannaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyañaṃ caret || 9.258 || iti |
śuklavṛtterasiddhau ca bhojyānnān śūdravargataḥ | tathaiva grāhyāgrāhyāñi jānṛyācchāstrato budhaḥ || 9.259 ||
śuklavṛttiśca
śrṛviṣñudharmottare trtiyakhañḍe pratigraheña yallabdhaṃ yajyataḥ śiṣyatastathā | gunānvitebhyo viprasya śuklaṃ tattrividhaṃ smṛtam || 9.260 || yuddhopakārāllabdhaṃ ca dañḍācca vyavahārataḥ | kṣatriyasya dhanaṃ śuklaṃ trividhaṃ parikṛrtitam || 9.261 || kṛṣivāñijyagorakṣaḥ kṛtvā śuklaṃ tathā viśaḥ | dvijaśuśrūṣayā labdhaṃ śuklaṃ śūdrasya kṛrtitam || 9.262 || kramāgataṃ prṛtidānaṃ prāptaṃ ca saha bhāryayā | aviśeṣena sarveṣāṃ dhanaṃ śuklaṃ prakṛrtitam || 9.263 || atha grāhyāgrāhyāñi
kaurme tatraiva nādyācchūdrasya vipro'nnaṃ mohādvā yadi kāmataḥ | na śūdrayoniṃ vrajati yastu bhuśkte hyanāpadi || 9.264 || duṣkṛtaṃ hi manuṣyasya sarvamanne pratiṣṭhitam | yo yasyānnaṃ samaśnāti sa tasyāśnāti kilbiṣam || 9.265 || ārdhikaḥ kulamitraśca svagopālaśca nāpitaḥ | ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā dattvā svalpapañaṃ budhaiḥ || 9.266 || pāyasaṃ snehapakvaṃ yadgorasaṃ caiva śaktavaḥ | piñyākaṃ caiva tailaṃ ca śūdrādgrāhyaṃ tathaiva ca || 9.267 ||
aśgirāḥ gorasaṃ caiva śaktūṃśca tailapiñyākameva ca | apūpān bhakṣayecchūdrātyatkiñcitpayasā kṛtam || 9.268 ||
atrismṛtau svasutāyāśca yo bhuśkte sa bhuśkte pṛthivṛmalam | narendrabhavane bhuktvā viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ || 9.269 || dāsanāpitagopāla kulamitrārdhasṛriñaḥ | bhojyānnāḥ śūdravarge'mṛ tathātmavinivedakaḥ || 9.270 || madhūdakaṃ phalaṃ mūlamedhāṃsyabhayadakṣiñā | abhyudyatāni tvetāni grāhyāñyapi nikṛṣṭataḥ || 9.271 || khalakṣetragataṃ dhanyaṃ kūpavāpisu yajjalam | agrāhyādapi tadgrāhyaṃ yacca goṣṭhagataṃ payaḥ || 9.272 || pānṛyaṃ pāyasaṃ bhakṣyaṃ ghṛtaṃ lavañameva ca | hastadattaṃ na gṛhñṛyāttulyaṃ gomāṃsabhakṣañaiḥ || 9.273 ||
manusmṛtau samudraṃ saindhavaṃ caiva lavañe paramādbhute | pratyakṣe api tu grāhye niṣedhastvanyagocaraḥ || 9.274 || āyasenaiva pātreña yadannamupanṛyate | bhoktā tadviṭsamaṃ bhuśkte dātā ca narakaṃ vrajet || 9.275 || gorakṣakān vāñijakān tathā kārukaśṛlinaḥ | proṣyān vārdhūṣikāṃścaiva viprān śūdravadācaret || 9.276 ||
kaurme ca tatraiva tṛñaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ prakāśaṃ vai harerbudhaḥ | dharmārthaṃ kevalaṃ vipra hyanyathā patito bhavet || 9.277 || tilamudgayavādṛnāṃ muṣṭirgrāhyā pathi sthitaiḥ | kṣudhārthairnānyathā vipra dharmavidbhiriti sthitiḥ || 9.278 || vaiṣñavānāṃ hi bhoktavyaṃ prārthyānnaṃ vaiṣñavaiḥ sadā | avaiṣñavānāmannaṃ tu parivarjyamamedhyavat || 9.279 ||
tathā ca pādme devadūtavikuñḍalasaṃvāde prārthayedvaiṣñavādannaṃ prayatnena vicakṣañaḥ | sarvapāpaviśudhyarthaṃ tadabhāve jalaṃ pibet || 9.280 ||
nāradiye mahāpātakasaṃyukto vrajedvaiṣñavamandiram | yācayedannamamṛtaṃ tadabhāve jalaṃ pibet || 9.281 ||
viṣñusmṛtau śrotriyānnaṃ vaiṣñavānnaṃ hutaśeṣaṃ ca yaddhaviḥ | ānakhātśodhayetpāpaṃ tuṣāgniḥ kanakaṃ yathā || 9.282 ||
skānde mārkañḍeyabhagṛrathasaṃvāde śuddhaṃ bhāgavatasyānnaṃ śuddhaṃ bhagṛrathijalam | śuddhaṃ viṣñuparaṃ cittaṃ śuddhamekādaśṛvratam || 9.283 || avaiṣñavagṛhe bhuktvā pṛtvā vā jānato'pi vā | śuddhiścāndrāyañe prokta iṣṭāpūrtaṃ vṛthā sadā || 9.284 ||
śrṛprahlādavākye ca keśavārcā gṛhe yasya na tiṣṭhati mahṛpate | tasyānnaṃ naiva bhoktavyamabhakṣyeña samaṃ smṛtam || 9.285 || kecidvṛttyanapekṣasya japaśraddhāvataḥ prabho | viśvastasyādiśantyasmin kāle'pi kṛtino japam || 9.286 ||
atha madhyāhnikakṛtyāni
madhyāhne snānataḥ pūrvaṃ puṣpādyāhṛtya vā svayam | bhṛtyādinā vā sampādya kuryānmadhyāhnikṛḥ kriyāḥ || 9.287 || snānāśaktau ca madhyāhne snānamācārya māntrikam | yathoktaṃ bhagavatpūjāṃ śaktaścetprāgvadācaret || 9.288 ||
atha vaiṣñavavaisvadevadividhiḥ
tataḥ kṛṣñārpitenaiva śuddhenānena vaiṣñavaḥ | vaiśvadevādikaṃ daivaṃ karma paitraṃ ca sādhayet || 9.289 || taduktaṃ ṣaṣṭhe dinavibhāge tu kuryātpañca mahāmakhān | daivo homena yajñaḥ syātbhautastu balidānatah || 9.290 || paitro viprānnadānena paitreña balināthavā | kiñcidannapradānādvā tarpañādvā caturvidhaḥ || 9.291 || nṛyajño'tithisatkārāthantakāreña cāmbunā | brahmayajño vedajapātpurāñapaṭhanena vā || 9.292 || tannityatā
kaurme akṛtvā tu dvijaḥ pañca mahāyajñān dvijottamaḥ | bhuñjṛta cetsumūḍhātmā tiryagyoniṃ sa gacchati || 9.293 || atha vaiṣñavaśrāddhavidhiḥ
prāpte śrāddhadine'pi prāgannaṃ bhagavate'rpayet | taccheṣeñaiva kurvṛta śrāddhaṃ bhāgavato narah || 9.294 ||
yacca smṛtau gṛhāgniśiṣudevānāṃ yatṛnāṃ brahmācāriñām | pitṛpāko na dātavyo yāvatpiñḍānna nirvapet || 9.295 || iti |
ṛdṛñsāmānyavacanaṃ viśeṣavacanavrajaih | śrutismṛtipurāñādi varttibhirbādhyate dhruvam || 9.296 ||
tathā ca pādme viṣñorniveditānnena yaṣṭavyaṃ devatāntaram | pitṛbhyaścāpi taddeyaṃ tadānantyāya kalpate || 9.297 ||
mokṣadharme nāradoktau sātvataṃ vidhimāsthāya prāñsūryamukhanisṛtam | pūjāyāmāsa deveśaṃ taccheṣeña pitāmahān || 9.298 ||
brahmāñḍapurāñe yaḥ śrāddhakāle haribhuktaśeṣam dadāti bhaktyā pitṛdevatānām | tenaiva piñḍāṃstulasṛvimiśrān ākalpakoṭiṃ pitaraḥ sutṛptāḥ || 9.299 ||
skānde devān pitṅn samuddiśya yadviṣñorviniveditam | tānuddiśya tathā kuryātpradānaṃ tasya caiva hi || 9.300 || prayānti tṛptimatulāṃ sodakena tu tena vai | mukundagātralagnena brāhmañānāṃ vilepanam || 9.301 || candanena tu piñḍanam kartavyaṃ pitṛtṛptaye | devānāṃ ca pitṅñāṃ ca jāyate tṛptirakṣayā || 9.302 || evaṃ kṛte mahṛpala mā bhavetsaṃśayaḥ kvacit || 9.303 ||
tatraiva śrṛpurusottamakhañḍe annādyaṃ śrāddhakāle tu patitādyairnirṛkṣitam | tulasṛdalamiśreña salilenābhiṣiñcayet || 9.304 || tadannaṃ śuddhatāmeti viṣñornaivedyamiśritam | viṣñornaivedyaśeṣaṃ tu tasmāddeyaṃ dvijātmanām | piñḍe caiva viśeṣena pitṅñāṃ tṛptimicchatā || 9.305 ||
tatraiva śrṛbrahmanāradasaṃvāde pitṅnuddiśya yaiḥ pūjā keśavasya kṛtā naraih | tyaktvā te nārakṛṃ pṛḍāṃ muktiṃ yānti mahāmune || 9.306 || dhanyāste mānavā loke kalikāle viśeṣatah | ye kurvanti harernityaṃ pitrarthaṃ pūjanaṃ mune || 9.307 || kiṃ dattairbahubhirpiñḍairgayāśrāddhādibhirmune | yairarcito harirbhaktyā pitrarthaṃ ca dine dine || 9.308 || yamuddiśya hareḥ pūjā kriyate munipuśgava | uddhṛtya narakāvāsāttaṃ nayetparamaṃ padam || 9.309 || yo dadāti hareḥ sthānaṃ pitṅnuddiśya nārada | kartavyaṃ hi pitṅñāṃ yattatkṛtaṃ tena bho dvija || 9.310 ||
śrutau ca eka eva nārāyaña āsṛt, na brahmā, neme dyāvāpṛthivyau, sarve devāḥ, sarve pitaraḥ, sarve manusyāḥ | viṣñunā aśitamaśnanti viṣñunāghrātaṃ jighranti, viṣñunā pṛtaṃ pibanti, tasmādvidhvaṃso viṣñūpāhṛtaṃ bhakṣayeyuḥ || 9.311 || iti |
ata evoktaṃ śrṛbhagavatā viṣñudharme prāñebhyo juhuyādannaṃ manniveditamuttamam | tṛpyanti sarvadā prāñā manniveditabhakṣañāt || 9.312 || tasmātsarvaprayatnena pradeyaṃ manniveditam | mamāpi hṛdayasthasya pitṅñāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 9.313 ||
kiṃ ca tatraivānyatra bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca yatkiñcidanivedyāgrabhoktari | na deyaṃ pitṛdevebhyaḥ prāyaścittṛ yato bhavet || 9.314 || svargādau kathito devairagrabhugbhagavān hariḥ | yajñabhāgabhujo devāstatastena prakalpitāḥ || 9.315 ||
skānde śrṛmārkañḍeyabhagṛrathasaṃvāde yastu vidyāvinirmuktaṃ mūrkhaṃ matvā tu vaiṣñavam | vedavidbhyo'vadādvipraḥ śrāddhaṃ tadrākṣasaṃ bhavet || 9.316 || sikthamātraṃ tu yadbhuśkte jalaṃ gañḍūṣamātrakam | tadannaṃ meruñā tulyaṃ tajjalaṃ sāgaropamam || 9.317 ||
brahmapurāñe śrṛbrahmavacanam śaśkhāśkitatanurvipro bhuśkte yasya ca veśmani | tadannaṃ svayamaśnāti pitṛbhiḥ saha keśavaḥ || 9.318 ||
smṛtiśca surābhāñḍasthapṛyūṣaṃ yathā naśyati tatkṣañāt | cakrāśkarahitaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathā śātātapo'bravṛt || 9.319 ||
kiṃ ca, śrṛviṣñurahasye niveśayennaro mohādanyapaśktau hareḥ priyam | sa patenniraye ghore paśktibhedṛ narādhamaḥ || 9.320 || atha śrṛbhagavadarpañe niṣiddham
niveditaṃ yadanyasmai taducchiṣṭaṃ hi kathyate | ataḥ kathañcidapi tanna śrṛbhagavate'rpayet || 9.321 ||
tathā caikādaśaskandhe śrṛbhagavaduktau api dṛpāvalokaṃ me nopayuñjyānniveditam || 9.322 ||
nāradṛye pitṛśeṣaṃ tu you dadyāddharaye paramātmane | retodāḥ pitarastasya bhavanti kleśabhāginaḥ || 9.323 ||
śrṛ viṣñudharme hariśeṣaṃ havirdadyātpitṅñāmakṣayaṃ bhavet | na punaḥ pitṛśeṣaṃ tu harerbrahmādisadguroḥ || 9.324 ||
anyatra ca dakṣādayaśca pitaro bhṛtyā indrādayaḥ surāḥ | atastadbhaktaśeṣaṃ tu viṣñornaiva nivedayet || 9.325 || iti |
evamāvaśyakaṃ kṛtvā vaiṣñavebhyo vibhajya ca | śrṛmanmahāprasādānnaṃ bhuñjṛta saha bandhubhiḥ || 9.326 ||
tathā ca prahlādapañcarātre svabhāvasthaiḥ karmajaḍān vañcayan draviñādibhiḥ | harernaivedyasambhārān vaiṣñavebhyaḥ samarpayet || 9.327 ||
ataiva vaiṣñavatantre harerniveditaṃ kiñccinna dadyātkarhicidbudhaḥ | abhaktebhyaḥ saśalyebhyo yaddavanniraye vrajet || 9.328 ||
viṣñudharmottare avaiṣñave devadhṛtaṃ nirmālyaṃ na prayacchati | naivedyaṃ vā mahābhāga tasya tuṣyati keśavaḥ || 9.329 || iti |
kathañcidapi nāśnṛyādakṛtvā kṛṣñapūjanam | na cāsamarpya govinde kiñcidbhuñjṛta vaiṣñavaḥ || 9.330 || atha pūjāvyatiriktabhojanadoṣāḥ
śrṛkūrmapurāñe anarcayitvā govindaṃ yairbhuktaṃ dharmavarjitaiḥ | śvānaviṣṭhāsamaṃ cānnaṃ nṛraṃ ca surayā samam || 9.331 ||
kiṃ ca yo mohādathavālasyādakṛtvā devatārcanam | bhuśkte sa yāti narakaṃ śūkareṣviha jāyate || 9.332 ||
viṣñudharmottare ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ pūjayeddharim | apūjya bhojanaṃ kurvannarakāñi vrajennaraḥ || 9.333 ||
nāradṛye ca prātarmadhyandinaṃ sāyaṃ viṣñupūjā smṛtā budhaiḥ | aśakto vistareñaiva prātaḥ sampūjya keśavam || 9.334 || madhyāhne caiva sāyaṃ ca puṣpāñjalimapi kṣipet | madhyāhne vā vistareña saṃkṣepeñāthavā harim || 9.335 || sambhojya bhojanaṃ kuryādanyathā narakaṃ vrajet || 9.336 || athānarpitabhoganiṣedhaḥ
hāyaśṛrṣapañcarātre na tvevāpūjya bhuñjṛta bhagavantaṃ janārdanam | na tatsvayaṃ samaśnṛyātyadviṣñau na nivedayet || 9.337 || brahmāñḍapurāñe patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyamannapānādyamauṣadham | anivedya ca bhuñjṛta yadāhārāya kalpitam || 9.338 || anivedyaṃ tu bhuñjānaḥ prāyaścittṛ bhavennaraḥ | tasmātsarvaṃ nivedyaiva viṣñorbhuñjṛta sarvadā || 9.339 ||
pādme gautamāmbarṛṣasaṃvāde ambarṛṣa gṛhe pakvaṃ yadabhṛṣṭaṃ sadātmanaḥ | anivedya harerbhuñjan saptakalpāni nārakṛ || 9.340 ||
tatraivottarakāñḍe śivomāsaṃvāde avaiṣñavānāṃ yaccānnaṃ patitānāṃ tathaiva ca | anarpitaṃ tathā viṣñau śvamāṃsasadṛśaṃ bhavet || 9.341 ||
viṣñusmṛtau anivedya tu yo bhuśkte haraye paramātmane | majjanti pitarastasya narake śāśvatṛḥ samāḥ || 9.342 ||
ataiva gautmāmbarṛṣasaṃvāde ambarṛṣa navaṃ vastraṃ phalamannaṃ rasādikam | kṛtvā viṣñūpabhuktaṃ tu sadā sevyaṃ hi vaiṣñavaiḥ || 9.343 ||
viṣñudharmāgnipurāñayoḥ gandhānnavarabhakṣyāṃśca srajo vāsāṃsi bhūṣañam | dattvā tu devadevāya taccheṣāñyupabhuñjate || 9.344 ||
gāruḍe pādodakaṃ pibennityaṃ naivedyaṃ bhakṣayeddhareḥ | śeṣāśca mastake dhāryā iti vedānuśāsanam || 9.345 ||
ṣaṣṭhaskandhe puṃsavanavrataprasaśge udvāsya devaṃ sve dhāmni tanniveditamagrataḥ | adyādātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ sarvakāmārthasiddhaye || 9.346 ||
aṣṭamaskandhe ca payovrataprasaśge niveditaṃ tadbhaktāya dadyādbhuñjṛta vā svayam || 9.347 ||
gautamṛyatantre śuklopacārasambhārairnityaśo harimarcayet | nivedya kṛṣñāya vidhivadannaṃ bhuñjṛta tatsvayam | athavā sātvate dadyādyadi labhyate bhaktitaḥ || 9.348 ||
śaratpradṛpe ca bhaktakṣañakṣaño devaḥ smṛtiḥ sevā svaveśmani | svabhojyasyārpañaṃ dānaṃ phalamindrādidurlabham || 9.349 ||
atha naivedyabhakṣañavidhiḥ
dṛṣṭvā mahāprasādānnaṃ tatprāśnatvābhimantrayet | sveṣṭanāmnā tato mūlamanunā vārasaptakam || 9.350 || dharmarājādibhāgaṃ cāpāsya śrṛcarañāmṛtam | tulasṛṃ cātra nikṣipya ślokān saśkṛrtayedimān || 9.351 || yasyocchiṣṭaṃ hi vāñchanti brahmādyā ṛṣayo'malāḥ | siddhādyāśca harestasya vayamucchiṣṭabhojinaḥ || 9.352 ||
kiṃ ca yasya nāmnā vinaśyanti mahāpātakarāśayaḥ | tasya śrṛkṛṣñadevasya vayamucchiṣṭabhojinaḥ || 9.353 || ucchiṣṭabhojinastasya vayamadbhutakarmañaḥ | yo bālyalṛlayā tāṃstān pūtanādṛnapātayat || 9.354 ||
ekādaśaskandhe tvayopabhuktasraggandhavāso'laśkāracarcitāḥ | ucchiṣṭabhojino dāsāstava māyāṃ jayemahi || 9.355 ||
yato'mṛtopastarañamasṛtyuktvā yathāvidhi | pañca prāñāhutṛḥ kṛtvā bhuñjṛta purataḥ prabhoḥ || 9.356 ||
śrṛviṣñupurāñe aurvasagarasaṃvāde praśastaratnapāñistu bhuñjṛta prayato gṛhṛ || 9.357 || puñyagandhau śastamālyadhārṛ caiva nareśvara | naikavastradharo'thārdrapāñipādo mahṛpate || 9.358 || viśuddhavadanaḥ prṛto bhuñjṛta na vidiśmukhaḥ | prāśmukhodaśmukho vāpi na caivānyamanā naraḥ || 9.359 || dattvā tu bhaktaṃ śiṣyebhyaḥ kṣudhitebhyastathā gṛhṛ | praśastaśuddhapātre tu bhuñjṛtākupito dvijaḥ || 9.360 || nāsandisaṃsthite pātre nādeśe ca nareśvara | nākāle nātisaśkṛrñe dattvāgraṃ ca naro'gnaye | nāśeṣaṃ puruṣo'śnṛyādanyatra jagatṛpate || 9.361 || madhvambhudadhisarpibhyaḥ saktubhyaśca vivekavān | aśnṛyāttanmayo bhūtvā pūrvaṃ tu madhuraṃ rasam || 9.362 || lavañāmle tathā madhye kaṭutiktādikāṃstataḥ | prāgdravaṃ puruṣo'śnṛyānmadhye kaṭhināśanam || 9.363 || ante punardravāśṛ tu balārogye na muñcati | pañcagrāsaṃ mahāmaunaṃ prāñādyāpyāyanāya tat || 9.364 || bhuktvā samyagathācamya prāśmukhodaśmukho'pi vā | yathāvatpunarācāmetpāñṛ prakṣālya mūlataḥ || 9.365 || svasthaḥ praśāntacittastu kṛtāsanaparigrahaḥ | abhṛṣṭadevatānāṃ tu kurvṛta smarañaṃ naraḥ || 9.366 ||
agastiragnirbaḍavānalaśca bhuktaṃ mayānnaṃ jarayatvaśeṣam | sukhaṃ ca me tatpariñāmasambhavaṃ yacchantvarogo mama cāstu dehe || 9.367 ||
viṣñuḥ samastendriyadehadehṛ pradhānabhūto bhagavān yathaikaḥ | satyena tenāttamaśeṣamannam ārogyadaṃ me pariñāmametu || 9.368 ||
ityuccārya svahastena parimṛjya tathodaram | anāyāsapradāyṛni kuryātkarmāñyatandritaḥ || 9.369 ||
kaurme vyāsagṛtāyām prāśmukho'nnāni bhuñjṛta sūryābhimukhameva vā | āsṛnaḥ svāsane siddhe bhūmyāṃ pādau nidhāya ca || 9.370 || āyuṣyaṃ prāśmukho bhuśkte yaśasyaṃ dakṣiñāmukhaḥ | śriyaṃ pratyaśmukho bhuśkte ṛtaṃ bhuśkte udaśmukhaḥ || 9.371 || pañcārdro bhojanaṃ kuryādbhūmau pātraṃ nidhāya ca | upavāsena tattulyaṃ manurāha prajāpatiḥ || 9.372 || upalipte śucau deśe pādau prakṣālya vai karau | ācamyārdrānano'krodhaḥ pañcārdro bhojanaṃ caret || 9.373 || mahāvyāhṛtibhistvannaṃ parivāryodakena tu | amṛtopastarañamasṛtyapośānakriyāṃ caret || 9.374 || svāhāprañavasaṃyuktāṃ prāñāyetyāhutiṃ tataḥ | apānāya tato hutvā vyānāya tadanantaram || 9.375 || udānāya tataḥ kuryātsamānāyeti pañcamṛm | vijñāya tattvameteṣāṃ juhuyādātmani dvijāḥ || 9.376 || śeṣamannaṃ yathākāmaṃ bhuñjṛta vyañjanairyutam | dhyātvā tanmanasā devamātmānaṃ vai prajāpatim || 9.377 || amṛtāpidhānamasṛtyupariṣṭādapaḥ pibet || 9.378 ||
kiṃ ca, tatraiva yadbhuśkte veṣṭitaśirā yacca bhuśkte vidiśmukhaḥ | sopānatkaśca yadbhuśkte sarvaṃ vidyāttadāsuram || 9.379 || nārdharātre na madhyāhne nājṛrñaṃ nārdravastradhṛk | na ca bhinnāsanagato na yāne saṃsthito'pi vā || 9.380 || na bhinnabhājane caiva na bhūmyāṃ na ca pāñiṣu | anārogyamanāyuṣyamasvargyaṃ cātibhojanam | apuñyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ tasmāttatparivarjayet || 9.381 ||
kiṃ ca na vāmahastenoddhṛtya pibedvaktreña vā jalam || 9.382 ||
viṣñusmṛtau pibataḥ patate toyaṃ bhājane mukhanirgayam | abhojyaṃ tadbhavedannaṃ bhuktvā cāndrāyañaṃ caret || 9.383 ||
mārkañḍeye bhuñjṛtānnaṃ ca taccitto hyantarjāsu sadā naraḥ | upaghātādṛte doṣānnānnasyodṛrayedbudhaḥ || 9.384 ||
anyatra ca hastādṛte'mbunānyenānaśnan pātrādṛte pibet | dakṣiñaṃ tu parityajya vāme nṛraṃ nighāpayet | abhojyaṃ tadbhavedannaṃ pānṛyaṃ ca surāsamam || 9.385 || tṛpto dadyāddhi tadannaṃ śeṣaṃ durgatatṛptaye || 9.386 || samyagācamya dakṣāśghreraśguṣṭhe vāri nikṣipet || 9.387 || tataḥ saṃsmṛtya santuṣṭaḥ puṣṭidāmiṣṭadevatām | sannikṛṣṭairvṛtaḥ śiṣṭairjapedannapatermanūn | annapate'nnasya no dehi || 9.388 || ityādi |
bhakṣayedatha tāmbūlaṃ prasādaṃ vallavṛprabhoḥ | śiṣṭairiṣṭairjapeddivyaṃ bhagavannāmamaśgalam || 9.389 ||
atha naivedyamāhātmyam vārāhe yo mamaivārcanaṃ kṛtvā tatra prāpañamuttamam | śeṣamannaṃ śamaśnāti tataḥ saukhyataraṃ nu kim || 9.390 ||
skānde tavopahāraṃ bhuktvā yaḥ sevate yajñapūruṣam | sevitaṃ tena niyataṃ puroḍāśo mahādhiyā || 9.391 ||
kiṃ ca tatraiva śaśkhodakaṃ tṛrthavarādvariṣṭhaṃ pādodakaṃ tṛrthagañādgariṣṭham | naivedyaśeṣaṃ kratukoṭipuñyaṃ nirmālyaśeṣaṃ vratadānatulyam || 9.392 ||
naivedyaśeṣaṃ tulasṛvimiśraṃ viśeṣataḥ pādajalena siktam | yo'śnāti nityaṃ purato murāreḥ prāpnoti yajñāyutakoṭipuñyam || 9.393 ||
ṣaḍbhirmāsopavāsaistu yatphalaṃ parikṛrtitam | viṣñornaivedyaśeṣe yatphalaṃ tadbhuñjatāṃ kalau || 9.394 ||
kiṃ ca, tatra śrṛśālagrāmaśilāmāhātmye bhaktyā bhunakti naivedyaṃ śālagrāmaśilārpitam | koṭiṃ makhasya labhate phalaṃ śatasahasraśaḥ || 9.395 || brahmavārigṛhasthaiśca vānaprasthaiśca bhikṣubhiḥ | bhoktavyaṃ viṣñunaivedyaṃ nātra kāryā vicārañā || 9.396 || bhuktvānyadevanaivedyaṃ dvijaścāndrāyañaṃ caret | bhuktvā keśavanaivedyaṃ yajñakoṭiphalaṃ labhet || 9.397 ||
tatraiva śrṛbrahmanāradasaṃvāde agniṣṭomasahasraistu vājapeyaśatairapi | tatphalaṃ prāpyate nūnaṃ viṣñornaivedyabhakṣañāt || 9.398 || hṛdi rūpaṃ mukhe nāma naivedyamudare hareḥ | pādodakaṃ ca nirmālyaṃ mastake yasya so'cyutaḥ || 9.399 ||
kiṃ ca pāvanaṃ viṣñunaivedyaṃ surasiddharṣibhiḥ smṛtam | anyadevasya naivedyaṃ bhuvattvācāndrāyañaṃ caret || 9.400 || koṭiyajñaistu yatpuñyaṃ māsopoṣañakoṭibhiḥ | tatphalaṃ prāpyate pumbhirviṣñornaivedyabhakṣañāt || 9.401 || tulasyāśca rajojuṣṭaṃ naivedyasya ca bhakṣañam | nirmālyaṃ ca dhṛtaṃ yena mahāpātakanāśanam || 9.402 ||
bṛhadviṣñupurāñe naivedyaṃ jagadṛśasya annapānādikaṃ ca yat | brahmavannirvikāraṃ hi yathā viṣñustathaiva tat || 9.403 || vikāraṃ ye prakurvanti bhakṣañe taddvijātayaḥ || 9.404 || kuṣṭhavyādhisamāyuktāḥ putradāravivarjitāḥ | nirayaṃ yānti te viprā yasmānnāvartate punaḥ || 9.405 ||
viṣñudharmottare navamannaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ nivedya madhusūdane | paścādbhuśkte svayaṃ yaśca tasya tuṣyati keśavaḥ || 9.406 ||
brahmāñḍapurāñe mukundāśanaśeṣaṃ tu yo hi bhuśkte dine dine | sikthe sikthe bhavetpuñyaṃ cāndrāyañaśatādhikam || 9.407 ||
anyatrāpi ekādaśṛsahasraistu māsopoṣañakoṭibhiḥ | tatphalaṃ prāpyate pumbhirviṣñornaivedyabhakṣañāt || 9.408 || iti |
tato yathoktamācamya tāmbūlādi vibhajya ca | mahāprasādaṃ dāsyena gṛhñṛyātprayataḥ svayam || 9.409 ||
tathā ca navamaskandhe śrṛmadambarṛṣacarite kāmaṃ ca dāsye na tu kāmakāmyayā yathottamaślokajanāśrayā ratiḥ || 9.410 ||
naivedyabhakṣañe yacca nirmālyagrahañe ca yat | māhātmyamādau likhitaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvamihāpi tat || 9.411 ||
iti śrṛgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrṛbhagavadbhaktivilāse mahāprasādo nāma navamo vilāsaḥ |
10. ṣatsamgamaVilasa
daśamavilāsaḥ
śrīkṛṣṇacaraṇāmbhojamadhupebhyo namo namaḥ | kathañcidāśrayādyeṣāṃ śvāpi tadgandhabhāgbhavet || 10.1 || atha śrīkṛṣṇabhaktānāṃ sabhāṃ savinayaṃ śubhām | gacchedvaiṣṇavacihnāḍhyaḥ pātuṃ kṛṣṇakathāsudhām || 10.2 ||
tathā ca smṛtiḥ itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ ṣaṣṭhasaptamakau nayet || 10.3 ||
atha śrībhagavadbhaktānāṃ lakṣaṇāni sāmānyataḥ laiṅge viṣṇureva hi yasyaiṣa devatā vaiṣṇavaḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.4 ||
atra viśeṣaḥ vratakarmaguṇajñānabhogajanmādimatsvapi | śaiveṣvapi ca kṛṣṇasya bhaktāḥ santi tathā tathā || 10.5 ||
atra vratiṣu madhye bhagavadbhaktihetuvrataparatā bhagavadbhaktalakṣaṇam
tathā skānde śrīmārkaṇḍeyabhagīrathasaṃvāde daśamīśeṣasaṃyuktaṃ dinaṃ vaiṣṇavavallabham | nopāsate mahīpāla te vai bhāgavatā narāḥ || 10.6 || prāṇātyaye na cāśnanti dinaṃ prāpya harernarāḥ | kurvanti jāgaraṃ rātrau sadā bhāgavatā hi te || 10.7 || upoṣya dvādaśīṃ śuddhāṃ rātrau jāgaraṇānvitām | alpāṃ tu sādhayedyastu sa vai bhāgavato naraḥ || 10.8 || bhaktirna vicyutā yeṣāṃ na cyutāni vratāni ca | supriyaḥ śrīpatiryeṣāṃ te syurbhāgavatā narāḥ || 10.9 ||
karmiṣu bhagavadarpaṇādinā tadājñābuddhyā vā bhaktihetuḥ sadācāraparatā | dharmārthaṃ jīvitaṃ yeṣāṃ santānārthaṃ ca maithunam | pacanaṃ vipramukhyārthaṃ jñeyāste vaiṣṇavā narāḥ || 10.10 || adhvagaṃ tu pathi śrāntaṃ kāle'tra gṛhamāgatam | yo'tithiṃ pūjayedbhaktyā vaiṣṇavaḥ sa na saṃśayaḥ || 10.11 || sadācāraratāḥ śiṣṭāḥ sarvabhūtānukampakāḥ | śucayastyaktarāgā ye sadā bhāgavatā hi te || 10.12 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye śrīnāradāmbarīṣasaṃvāde jīvitaṃ yasya dharmārthaṃ dharmo haryarthameva ca | ahorātrāṇi puṇyārthaṃ taṃ manye vaiṣṇavaṃ bhuvi || 10.13 ||
laiṅge ca viṣṇubhaktisamāyuktān śrautasmārtapravartakān | prīto bhavati yo dṛṣṭvā vaiṣṇavo'sau prakīrtitaḥ || 10.14 ||
guṇavatsu bhaktihetuḥ kṛpālutvādisadguṇaśīlatā |
skānde tatraiva paraduḥkhenātmaduḥkhaṃ manyante ye nṛpottama | bhagavaddharmaniratāste narā vaiṣṇavā nṛpa || 10.15 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīkapiladevahūtisaṃvāde titikṣavaḥ kāruṇikāḥ suhṛdaḥ sarvadehinām | ajātaśatravaḥ śāntāḥ sādhavaḥ sādhubhūṣaṇāḥ || 10.16 ||
pañcamaskandhe ṛṣabhadevasya putrānuśāsane mahatsevāṃ dvāramāhurvimuktes tamodvāraṃ yoṣitāṃ saṅgisaṅgam | mahāntaste samacittāḥ praśāntā vimanyavaḥ suhṛdaḥ sādhavo ye || 10.17 ||
ekādaśaskandhe bhagavatpradattoddhavapraśnottare kṛpālurakṛtadrohastitikṣuḥ sarvadehinām | satyasāro'navadyātmā samaḥ sarvopakārakaḥ || 10.18 || kāmairahatadhīrdānto mṛduḥ śucirakiñcanaḥ | anīho mitabhukśāntaḥ sthiro maccharaṇo muniḥ || 10.19 || apramatto gabhīrātmā dhṛtimāñjitaṣaḍguṇaḥ | amānī mānadaḥ kalyo maitraḥ kāruṇikaḥ kaviḥ || 10.20 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe yamatadbhaṭasaṃvāde na calati nijavarṇadharmato yaḥ samamatirātmasuhṛdvipakṣapakṣe | na harati na hanti kiñciduccaiḥ sthitamanasaṃ tamavehi viṣṇubhaktam || 10.21 ||
jñāniṣu bhaktiheturjñānavattā | ekādaśe haviyogeśvarottare sarvabhūteṣu yaḥ paśyedbhagavadbhāvamātmanaḥ | bhūtāni bhagavatyātmanyeṣa bhāgavatottamaḥ || 10.22 || na yasya svaḥ para iti vitteṣvātmani vā bhidā | sarvabhūtasamaḥ śāntaḥ sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ || 10.23 ||
ekādaśe śrībhagavaduktau jñātvājñātvātha ye vai māṃ yāvān yaścāsmi yādṛśaḥ | bhajantyananyabhāvena te me bhaktatamā matāḥ || 10.24 ||
tatraiva haviyogeśvarottare īśvare tadadhīneṣu bāliśeṣu dviṣatsu ca | premamaitrīkṛpopekṣā yaḥ karoti sa madhyamaḥ || 10.25 || arcāyāmeva haraye pūjāṃ yaḥ śraddhayehate | na tadbhakteṣu cānyeṣu sa bhaktaḥ prākṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.26 ||
bhogavatsu bhaktiheturbhogānāsaktatā | haviyogeśvarottare gṛhītvāpīndriyairarthān yo na dveṣṭi na hṛṣyati | viṣṇormāyāmidaṃ paśyan sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ || 10.27 ||
sajjanmavidyādimatsu bhaktiheturnirabhimānitā | tatraiva na yasya janmakarmabhyāṃ na varṇāśramajātibhiḥ | sajjate'sminnahambhāvo dehe vai sa hareḥ priyaḥ || 10.28 ||
bhāvāḥ kathañcidbhaktaiva jñānānāsaktyamānitā | bhaktiniṣṭhāpakā jātāstato hyuttamatoditā || 10.29 ||
śaiveṣu śrīśivakṛṣṇabhedakāḥ | bṛhannāradīye śive ca parameśāne viṣṇau ca paramātmani | samabuddhyā pravarttante te vai bhāgavatottamāḥ || 10.30 ||
anyacca teṣāṃ bhagavacchāstrārthaparatādikam | sākṣādbhaktyātmakaṃ mukhyaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ likhyate'dhunā || 10.31 ||
skānde yeṣāṃ bhāgavataṃ śāstraṃ sadā tiṣṭhati sannidhau | pūjayanti ca ye nityaṃ te syurbhāgavatā narāḥ || 10.32 || yeṣāṃ bhāgavataṃ śāstraṃ jīvitādadhikaṃ bhavet | mahābhāgavatāḥ śreṣṭhā viṣṇunā kathitā narāḥ || 10.33 ||
vaiṣṇavasammānaniṣṭhā | laiṅge viṣṇubhaktamathāyātaṃ yo dṛṣṭvā sumukhaḥ priyaḥ | praṇāmādi karotyeva vāsudeve yathā tathā | sa vai bhakta iti jñeyaḥ sa punāti jagattrayam || 10.34 || rukṣākṣarā giraḥ śṛṇvan tathā bhāgavateritāḥ | praṇāmapūrvakaṃ kṣāntvā yo vadedvaiṣṇavo hi saḥ || 10.35 || bhojanācchādanaṃ sarvaṃ yathāśaktyā karoti yaḥ | viṣṇubhaktasya satataṃ sa vai bhāgavataḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.36 ||
gāruḍe yena sarvātmanā viṣṇubhaktyā bhāvo niveśitaḥ | vaiṣṇaveṣu kṛtātmatvānmahābhāgavato hi saḥ || 10.37 ||
śrītulasīsevāniṣṭhā tulasīkānanaṃ dṛṣṭvā ye namaskurvate narāḥ | tatkāṣṭhāṅkitakarṇā ye te vai bhāgavatottamāḥ || 10.38 || tulasīgandhamāghrāya santoṣaṃ kurvate tu ye | tanmūlamṛddhūtā yaiśca te vai bhāgavatottamāḥ || 10.39 ||
śrībhagavataḥ kathāparatā
bṛhannāradīye śrībhagavanmārkaṇḍeyasaṃvāde matkathāśravaṇe yeṣāṃ vartate sāttvikī matiḥ | tadbhaktaviṣṇubhaktāśca te vai bhāgavatottamāḥ || 10.40 ||
skānde śrībhagavadarjunasaṃvāde matkathāṃ kurute yastu matkathāṃ ca śṛṇoti yaḥ | hṛṣyate matkathāyāṃ ca sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ || 10.41 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe tatraiva madāśrayāḥ kathā mṛṣṭāḥ śṛṇvanti kathayanti ca | tapanti vividhāstāpā naitānmadgatacetasaḥ || 10.42 ||
bṛhannāradīye tatraiva manmānasāśca madbhaktā madbhaktajanalolupāḥ | mannāmaśravaṇāsaktāste vai bhāgavatottamāḥ || 10.43 || ye'bhinandanti nāmāni hareḥ śrutvā'tiharṣitāḥ | romāñcitaśarīrāśca te vai bhāgavatottamāḥ || 10.44 ||
tatraivānyatra anyeṣāmudayaṃ dṛṣṭvā ye'bhinandanti mānavāḥ | harināmaparā ye ca te vai bhāgavatottamāḥ || 10.45 ||
smaraṇaparatā tatra svadharmaniṣṭhayā rāgadveṣanivṛttyā smaraṇam
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe yamatadbhaṭasaṃvāde na calati ya uccaiḥ śrībhagavatpadāravinde | sitamanāstamavehi viṣṇubhaktam || 10.46 || kalikaluṣamalena yasya nātmā vimalamatermalinīkṛtastamenam | manasi kṛtajanārdanaṃ manuṣyaṃ satatamavehi hareratīvabhaktam || 10.47 || kanakamapi rahasyavekṣya buddhyā tṛṇamiva yaḥ samavaiti parasvam | bhavati ca bhagavatyananyacetāḥ puruṣavaraṃ tamavehi viṣṇubhaktam || 10.48 || sphaṭikagiriśilāmalaḥ kva viṣṇur manasi nṝṇāṃ kva ca matsarādidoṣaḥ | na hi tuhinamayūkharaśmipuñje bhavati hutāśanadīptijaḥ pratāpaḥ || 10.49 || vimalamatiramatsaraḥ praśāntaḥ śucicarito'khilasattvamitrabhūtaḥ | priyahitavacano'stumānamāyo vasati sadā hṛdi tasya vāsudevaḥ || 10.50 || vasati hṛdi sanātane ca tasmin bhavati pumān jagato'sya saukhyarūpaḥ | kṣitirasamatiramyamātmano'ntaḥ kathayati cārutayaiva śālapotaḥ || 10.51 ||
anyavijaye vairāgyādinā ca smaraṇam
ekādaśaskandhe haviyogeśvarottare dehendriyaprāṇamanodhiyāṃ yo janmāpyayakṣudbhayatarṣakṛcchraiḥ | saṃsāradharmairavimuhyamānaḥ smṛtyā harerbhāgavatapradhānaḥ || 10.52 || tribhuvanavibhavahetave'pyakuṇṭha smṛtirajitātmasurādibhirvimṛgyāt | na calati bhagavatpadāravindāl lavanimiṣārdhamapi yaḥ sa vaiṣṇavāgryaḥ || 10.53 || bhagavata uruvikramāṅghriśākhā nakhamaṇicandrikayā nirastatāpe | hṛdi kathamupasīdatāṃ punaḥ sa prabhavati candra ivodite'rkatāpaḥ || 10.54 ||
skānde tatraiva ye'rcayanti sadā viṣṇuṃ yajñeśaṃ varadaṃ harim | dehinaḥ puṇyakarmāṇaḥ sadā bhāgavatā hi te || 10.55 ||
laiṅge viṣṇukṣetre śubhānyeva karoti snehasaṃyutaḥ | pratimāṃ ca harernityaṃ pūjayetpratayāmavān || 10.56 || viṣṇubhaktaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ karmaṇā manasā girā | nārāyaṇaparo nityaṃ bhūpa bhāgavato hi saḥ || 10.57 ||
atha vaiṣṇavadharmaniṣṭhādi
pādmottarakhaṇḍe tāpādipañcasaṃskārī navejyākarmakārakaḥ | arthapañcakavidvipro mahābhāgavataḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.58 ||
ekāntikatā gāruḍe ekāntena sadā viṣṇau yasmāddeve parāyaṇāḥ | tasmādekāntinaḥ proktāstadbhāgavatacetasaḥ || 10.59 ||
tadvijñānenānanyaparatā
ekādaśe uddhavapraśnottare jñātvājñātvātha ye vai māṃ yāvān yaścāsmi yādṛśaḥ | bhajantyananyabhāvena te vai bhāgavatā matāḥ || 10.60 ||
ekādaśaskandhe na kāmakarmabījānāṃ yasya cetasi sambhavaḥ | vāsudevaikanilayaḥ sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ || 10.61 ||
sā ca ekāntitā caturdhā
tatra dharmānādareṇa śrīmaduddhavapraśnottara eva ājñāyaivaṃ guṇān doṣānmayādiṣṭānapi svakān | dharmān santyajya yaḥ sarvānmāṃ bhajeta sa tu sattamaḥ || 10.62 ||
śrībhagavadgītāyām sarvadharmān parityajya māmekaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja | ahaṃ tvā sarvapāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ || 10.63 ||
caturthaskandhe yadā yasyānugṛhṇāti bhagavānatmabhāvitaḥ | na jahāti matiṃ loke vede ca pariniṣṭhitām || 10.64 ||
anyasarvanirapekṣatā
śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde ailopākhyāne santo'napekṣā maccittāḥ praśāntāḥ samadarśinaḥ | nirmamā nirahaṅkārā nirdvandvā niṣparigrahāḥ || 10.65 ||
ataeva śrīkapiladevahūtisaṃvāde ta ete sādhavaḥ sādhvi sarvasaṅgavivarjitāḥ | saṅgasteṣvatha te prārthyaḥ saṅgadoṣaharā hi te || 10.66 ||
vighnākulatve'pi manoratiparatā |
skānde tatraiva yasya kṛcchragatasyāpi keśave ramate manaḥ | na vicyutā ca bhaktirvai sa vai bhāgavato naraḥ || 10.67 || āpadgatasya yasyeha bhakiravyabhicāriṇī | nānyatra ramate cittaṃ sa vai bhāgavato naraḥ || 10.68 ||
premaikarasatā |
śryṛṣabhadevasya putrānuśāsane ye vā mayīśe kṛtasauhṛdārthā; janeṣu dehambharavārtikeṣu | gṛheṣu jāyātmajarātimatsu; na prītiyuktā yāvadarthāśca loke || 10.69 ||
tridhā premaikaparatā premṇaḥ syāttāratamyataḥ | uttamā madhyamā cāsau kaniṣṭhā ceti bhedataḥ || 10.70 ||
tatrottamā |
yathā ekādaśe haviyogeśvarottare sarvabhūteṣu yaḥ paśyedbhagavadbhāvamātmanaḥ | bhūtāni bhagavatyātmanyeṣa bhāgavatottamaḥ || 10.71 ||
sveṣṭadevasya bhāvaṃ yaḥ sarvabhūteṣu paśyati | bhāvayanti ca tānyasminnityarthaḥ sammataḥ satām || 10.72 ||
śrīkapiladevahūtisaṃvāde mayyananyena bhāvena bhaktiṃ kurvanti ye dṛḍhām | matkṛte tyaktakarmāṇastyaktasvajanabāndhavāḥ || 10.73 ||
śrīhaviyogeśvarottare visṛjati hṛdayaṃ na yasya sākṣād dhariravaśābhihito'pyaghaughanāśaḥ | praṇayarasanayā dhṛtāṅghripadmaḥ sa bhavati bhāgavatapradhāna uktaḥ || 10.74 ||
tatra madhyamā |
śrīhaviyogeśvarottare īśvare tadadhīneṣu bāliśeṣu dviṣatsu ca | premamaitrīkṛpopekṣā yaḥ karoti sa madhyamaḥ || 10.75 ||
tatra kaniṣṭhā |
tatraiva arcāyāmeva haraye pūjāṃ yaḥ śraddhayehate | na tadbhakteṣu cānyeṣu sa bhaktaḥ prākṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.76 ||
śraddhayā pūjanaṃ premabodhakaṃ bhakta ityapi | lakṣaṇāni ca yānyagre bhakterlekhyāni tānyapi || 10.77 || vandanādīni vidyante yeṣu bhāgavatā hi te | etāni lakṣaṇānītthaṃ gauṇamukhyādibhedataḥ || 10.78 || ūhyāni lakṣaṇānyevaṃ vivecyāni parāṇyapi || 10.79 || īdṛglakṣaṇavantaḥ syurdurlabhā bahavo janāḥ | divyā hi maṇayo vyaktaṃ na varterannitastataḥ || 10.80 ||
ataevoktaṃ mokṣadharme nārāyaṇīye jāyamānaṃ hi puruṣaṃ yaṃ paśyenmadhusūdanaḥ | sāttvikastu sa vijñeyo bhavenmokṣe ca niścitaḥ || 10.81 ||
evaṃ saṅkṣipya likhitādvaiṣṇavānāṃ tu lakṣaṇāt | māhātmyamapi vijñeyaṃ likhyate'nyacca tatkiyat || 10.82 ||
atha bhagavadbhaktānāṃ māhātmyam |
sauparṇe śrīśakroktau kalau bhāgavataṃ nāma yasya puṃsaḥ prajāyate | jananī putriṇī tena pitṝṇāṃ tu dhurandharaḥ || 10.83 || kalau bhāgavataṃ nāma durlabhaṃ naiva labhyate | brahmarudrapadotkṛṣṭaṃ guruṇā kathitaṃ mama || 10.84 || yasya bhāgavataṃ cihnaṃ dṛśyate tu harirmune | gīyate ca kalau devā jñeyāste nāsti saṃśayaḥ || 10.85 ||
śrīmārkaṇḍeyoktau samīpe tiṣṭhate yasya ha antakāle'pi vaiṣṇavaḥ | gacchate paramaṃ sthānaṃ yadyapi brahmahā bhavet || 10.86 ||
nāradīye śrīvāmadevarukmāṅgadasaṃvāde śvapaco'pi mahīpāla viṣṇubhakto dvijādhikaḥ | viṣṇubhaktivihīnastu dvijo'pi śvapacādhikaḥ || 10.87 ||
skānde revākhaṇḍe śrībrahmoktau indro maheśvaro brahmā paraṃ brahma tadaiva hi | śvapaco'pi bhavatyeva yadā tuṣṭā'si keśava || 10.88 || śvapacādapi kaṣṭatvaṃ brahmeśānādayaḥ surāḥ | tadaivācyuta yāntyete yadaiva tvaṃ parāṅmukhaḥ || 10.89 || sa kartā sarvadharmāṇāṃ bhakto yastava keśava | sa kartā sarvapāpānāṃ yo na bhaktastavācyuta || 10.90 || dharmo bhavatyadharmo'pi kṛto bhaktaistavācyuta | pāpaṃ bhavati dharmo'pi tavābhaktaiḥ kṛto hareḥ || 10.91 || niḥśeṣadharmakartā vāpyabhakto narake hare | sadā tiṣṭhati bhaktaste brahmahāpi viśudhyati || 10.92 || niścalā tvayi bhaktiryā saiva muktirjanārdana | muktā eva hi bhaktāste tava viṣṇo yato hare || 10.93 ||
tatraiva durvāsonāradasaṃvāde nūnaṃ bhāgavatā loke lokarakṣāviśāradāḥ | vrajanti viṣṇunādiṣṭā hṛdisthena mahāmune || 10.94 || bhagavāneva sarvatra bhūtānāṃ kṛpayā hariḥ | rakṣaṇāya caran lokān bhaktarūpeṇa nārada || 10.95 ||
tatraiva śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde yastu viṣṇuparo nityaṃ dṛḍhabhaktirjitendriyaḥ | svagṛhe'pi vasan yāti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam || 10.96 || aśvamedhasahasrāṇāṃ sahasraṃ yaḥ karoti vai | nāsau tatphalamāpnoti tadbhaktairyadavāpyate || 10.97 ||
tatraivāmṛtasāroddhāre śrīyamatadbhaṭasaṃvāde sarvatra vaiṣṇavāḥ pūjyāḥ svarge martye rasātale | devatānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ tathaivoragarakṣasām || 10.98 || yeṣāṃ smaraṇamātreṇa pāpalakṣaśatāni ca | dahyante nātra sandeho vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām || 10.99 || yeṣāṃ pādarajenaiva prāpyate jāhnavījalam | nārmadaṃ yāmunaṃ caiva kiṃ punaḥ pādayorjalam || 10.100 || yeṣāṃ vākyajalaughena vinā gaṅgājalairapi | vinā tīrthasahasreṇa snāto bhavati mānavaḥ || 10.101 ||
tatraiva cāturmāsyamāhātmye tāvadbhramanti saṃsāre pitaraḥ piṇḍatatparaḥ | yāvatkule bhaktiyuktaḥ suto naiva prajāyate || 10.102 || sa eva jñānavān loke yogināṃ prathamo hi saḥ | mahākratūnāmāhartā haribhaktiyuto hi yaḥ || 10.103 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe dhruvacarite na cyavante hi yadbhaktyā mahatyāṃ pralayāpadi | ato'cyuto'khile loke sa ekaḥ sarvago'vyayaḥ || 10.104 || na tasmādbhagavadbhaktādbhetavyaṃ kenacitkvacit | niyataṃ viṣṇubhaktā yena te syuḥ paratāpinaḥ || 10.105 ||
tatraivāgre brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyaḥ vaiśyaḥ śūdro vā yadi vetaraḥ | viṣṇubhaktisamāyukto jñeyaḥ sarvottamottamaḥ || 10.106 || śaṅkhacakrāṅkitatanuḥ śirasā mañjarīdharaḥ | gopīcandanaliptāṅgo dṛṣṭaścedtadaghaṃ kutaḥ || 10.107 ||
mahābhārate rājadharme īśvaraṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ jagataḥ prabhavāpyayam | bhaktā nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ durgāṇyatitaranti te || 10.108 ||
viṣṇudharmottare śayanādutthito yastu kīrtayenmadhusūdanam | kīrtanāttasya pāpāni nāśamāyāntyaśeṣataḥ || 10.109 ||
tatraiva yasyāpyanante jagatāmadhīśe bhaktiḥ parā yādavadevadeve | tasmātparaṃ nāparamasti kiñcit pātraṃ triloke puruṣapravīra || 10.110 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye śrīprahlādabalisaṃvāde nityaṃ ye prātarutthāya vaiṣṇavānāṃ tu kīrtanam | kurvanti te bhāgavatāḥ kṛṣṇatulyāḥ kalau bale || 10.111 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye svadarśanasparśanapūjanaiḥ kṛtī tamāṃsi viṣṇupratimeva vaiṣṇavaḥ | dhunvan vasatyatra janasya yanna tat svārthaṃ paraṃ lokahitāya dīpavat || 10.112 ||
itihāsasamuccaye śrīlomaśavākye ye bhajanti jagadyoniṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam | na tebhyo vidyate tīrthamadhikaṃ rājasattama || 10.113 || yatra bhāgavatāḥ snānaṃ kurvanti vimalāśrayāḥ | tattīrthamadhikaṃ viddhi sarvapāpaviśodhanam || 10.114 || yatra rāgādirahitā vāsudevaparāyaṇāḥ | tatra sannihito viṣṇurnṛpate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10.115 || na gandhairna tathā toyairna puṣpaiśca manoharaiḥ | sānnidhyaṃ kurute devo yatra santi na vaiṣṇavāḥ || 10.116 || balibhiścopavāsaiśca nṛtyagītādibhistathā | nityamārādhyamāno'pi tatra viṣṇurna tṛpyati || 10.117 || tasmādete mahābhāgā vaiṣṇavā vītakalmaṣāḥ | punanti sakalān lokāṃstattīrthamadhikaṃ tataḥ || 10.118 || śūdraṃ vā bhagavadbhaktaṃ niṣādaṃ śvapacaṃ tathā | vīkṣataṃ jātisāmānyātsa yāti narakaṃ dhruvam || 10.119 || tasmādviṣṇuprasādāya vaiṣṇavān paritoṣayet | prasādasumukho viṣṇustenaiva syānna saṃśayaḥ || 10.120 ||
tatraiva śrīnāradapuṇḍarīkasaṃvāde ye nṛśaṃsā durātmānaḥ pāpācāraratāḥ sadā | te'pi yānti paraṃ dhāma nārāyaṇaparāśrayāḥ || 10.121 || lipyante na ca pāpena vaiṣṇavā viṣṇutatparāḥ | punanti sakalān lokān sahasrāṃśurivoditaḥ || 10.122 || janmāntarasahasreṣu yasya syādbuddhirīdṛśī | dāso'haṃ vāsudevasya sarvān lokān samuddharet || 10.123 || sa yāti viṣṇusālokyaṃ puruṣo nātra saṃśayaḥ | kiṃ punastadgataprāṇāḥ puruṣāḥ saṃyatendriyāḥ || 10.124 || smṛtaḥ sambhāṣito vāpi pūjito vā dvijottamāḥ | punāti bhagavadbhaktaścāṇḍālo'pi yadṛcchayā || 10.125 ||
śrīvyāsavākye janmāntarasahasreṣu viṣṇubhakto na lipyate | yasya sandarśanādeva bhasmībhavati pātakam || 10.126 ||
itihāsasamuccaye śrībhagavadvākye na me priyaścaturvedī madbhaktaḥ śvapacaḥ priyaḥ | tasmai deyaṃ tato grāhyaṃ sa ca pūjyo yathā hyaham || 10.127 ||
tatraiva brahmavākye sabhartṛkā vā vidhavā viṣṇubhaktiṃ karoti yā | samuddharati cātmānaṃ kulamekottaraṃ śatam || 10.128 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye prahlādabalisaṃvāde saṅkīrṇayonayaḥ pūtā ye bhaktā madhusūdane | mlecchatulyāḥ kulīnāste ye na bhaktā janārdane || 10.129 ||
ādipurāṇe śrīkṛṣṇārjunasaṃvāde vaiṣṇavān bhaja kaunteya mā bhajasvānyadevatāḥ | punanti vaiṣṇavāḥ sarve sarvadevamidaṃ jagat | madbhakto durlabho yasya sa eva mama durlabhaḥ || 10.130 || tatparo durlabho nāsti satyaṃ satyaṃ dhanañjaya | jagatāṃ guravo bhaktā bhaktānāṃ guravo vayam | sarvatra guravo bhaktā vayaṃ ca guravo yathā | asmākaṃ bāndhavā bhaktā bhaktānāṃ bāndhavā vayam || 10.131 || asmākaṃ guravo bhaktā bhaktānāṃ guravo vayam | madbhaktā yatra gacchanti tatra gacchāmi pārthiva | bhaktānāmanugacchanti muktayaḥ śrutibhiḥ saha || 10.132 || ye me bhaktajanāḥ pārtha na me bhaktāśca te janāḥ | madbhaktānāṃ ca ye bhaktāste me bhaktatamā matāḥ || 10.133 || ye kecitprāṇino bhaktā madarthe tyaktabāndhavāḥ | teṣāmahaṃ parikrīto nānyakrīto dhanañjaya || 10.134 || eṣāṃ bhakṣyaṃ sunirṇītaṃ śrūyatāṃ niścitaṃ mama | ucchiṣṭamavaśiṣṭaṃ ca bhaktānāṃ bhojanadvayam || 10.135 || nāmayuktajanāḥ kecijjātyantarasamanvitāḥ | kurvanti me yathā prītiṃ na tathā vedapāragāḥ || 10.136 ||
bṛhannāradīye mārkaṇḍeyaṃ prati śrībhagavaduktau viṣṇurbhaktakuṭumbīti vadanti vibudhāḥ sadā | tadeva pālayiṣyāmi majjano nānṛtaṃ vadet || 10.137 || mama janma kule yasya tatkulaṃ mokṣagāmi vai | mayi tuṣṭe muniśreṣṭha kimasādhyaṃ jagattraye || 10.138 ||
mayi bhaktiparo yastu madyājī matkathāparaḥ | maddhyānī svakulaṃ sarvaṃ nayatyacyutarūpatām || 10.139 || madarthaṃ karma kurvāṇo matpraṇāmaparo naraḥ | manmanāḥ svakulaṃ sarvaṃ nayatyacyutarūpatām || 10.140 || ahameva dvijaśreṣṭha nityaṃ pracchannavigrahaḥ | bhagavadbhaktarūpeṇa lokān rakṣāmi sarvadā || 10.141 ||
tatraivāditimāhātmye śrīsūtoktau viprāḥ śṛṇudhvaṃ māhātmyaṃ haribhaktiratātmanām | haridhyānaparāṇāṃ tu kaḥ samarthaḥ prabādhitum || 10.142 || haribhaktiparo yatra tatra brahmā hariḥ śivaḥ | tatra devāśca siddhādyā nityaṃ tiṣṭhanti sattamāḥ || 10.143 || nimiṣaṃ nimṣārdhaṃ vā yatra tiṣṭhanti sattamāḥ | tatraiva sarvaśreyāṃsi tattīrthaṃ tattapovanam || 10.144 ||
tatraivāditiṃ prati śrībhagavaduttare rāgadveṣavihīnā ye madbhaktā matparāyaṇāḥ | vadanti satataṃ te māṃ gatāsūyā adāmbhikāḥ || 10.145 || paropatāpavimukhāḥ śivabhaktiparāyaṇāḥ | matkathāśravaṇāsaktā vahanti satataṃ hi mām || 10.146 ||
tatraiva dhvajāropaṇamāhātmye śrīviṣṇudūtoktau yatīnāṃ viṣṇubhaktānāṃ paricaryāparāyaṇāḥ | īkṣitā api gacchanti pāpino'pi parāṃ gatim || 10.147 ||
tatraiva śrībhagavattoṣaprakārapraśnottare ripavastaṃ na hiṃsanti na bādhante grahāśca tam | rākṣasāśca na cekṣante naraṃ viṣṇuparāyaṇam || 10.148 || bhaktirdṛḍhā bhavedyasya devadeve janārdane | śreyāṃsi tasya sidhyanti bhaktimanto'dhikāstataḥ || 10.149 ||
tatraivāgre adyāpi ca muniśreṣṭha brahmādyā api devatāḥ | prabhāvaṃ na vijānanti viṣṇubhaktiratātmanām || 10.150 ||
kiṃ ca dharmārthakāmamokṣākhyāḥ puruṣārthā dvijottamāḥ | haribhaktiparāṇāṃ vai sampadyante na saṃśayaḥ || 10.151 ||
tatraiva lubdhakopākhyānasyādau ye viṣṇuniratāḥ śāntā lokānugrahatatparāḥ | sarvabhūtadayāyuktā viṣṇurūpāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 10.152 || viṣṇubhaktivihīnā ye caṇḍālāḥ parikīrtitāḥ | caṇḍālā api vai śreṣṭhā haribhaktiparāyaṇāḥ || 10.153 ||
tatraiva yajñadhvajopākhyānasyādau śrīsūtavākyam haribhaktirasāsvādamuditā ye narottamāḥ | namaskaromyahaṃ tebhyo yatsaṅgānmuktibhāgnaraḥ || 10.154 || haribhaktiparā ye tu harināmaparāyaṇāḥ | durvṛttā vā suvṛttā vā tebhyo nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 10.155 || aho bhāgyamaho bhāgyaṃ viṣṇubhaktiratātmanām | yasmānmuktiḥ karasthaiva yogināmapi durlabhā || 10.156 ||
tatraiva kaliprasaṅge ghore kaliyuge prāpte sarvadharmavivarjite | vāsudevaparā martyāḥ kṛtārthā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10.157 || astyantardurlabhā proktā haribhaktiḥ kalau yuge | haribhaktiratānāṃ vai pāpabandho na jāyate || 10.158 || vedavādaratāḥ sarve tathā tīrthaniṣeviṇaḥ | haribhaktirataiḥ sārdhaṃ kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 10.159 ||
ataevoktaṃ devaistatraiva bhāratavarṣaprasaṅge harikīrtanaśīlo vā tadbhaktānāṃ priyo'pi vā | śuśrūṣurvāpi mahatāṃ sa vandyo'smābhiruttamaḥ || 10.160 ||
pādme śrībhagavadbrahmasaṃvāde darśanadhyānasaṃsparśairmartyakūrmavihaṅgamāḥ | puṣṇanti svānyapatyāni tathāhamapi padmaja || 10.161 || muhūrtenāpi saṃhartuṃ śaktau yadyapi dānavāt | madbhaktānāṃ vinodārthaṃ karomi vividhāḥ kriyāḥ || 10.162 ||
tatraiva māghamāhātmye devadūtavikuṇḍalasaṃvāde na vayaṃ yamaṃ yamalokaṃ na na dūtān ghoradarśanāt | paśyanti vaiṣṇavā nūnaṃ satyaṃ satyaṃ mayoditam || 10.163 || śvapākamiva nekṣeta loke vipramavaiṣṇavam | vaiṣṇavo varṇabāhyo'pi punāti bhuvanatrayam || 10.164 || na śūdrā bhagavadbhaktāste tu bhāvanā matāḥ | sarvavarṇeṣu te śūdrā ye na bhaktā janārdana || 10.165 || viṣṇubhaktasya ye dāsā vaiṣṇavānnabhujaśca ye | te'pi kratubhujāṃ gvaiśya gatiṃ yānti nirākulāḥ || 10.166 ||
tatraiva vaiśākhamāhātmye pañcapuruṣāṇāmuktau bhavyāni bhūtāni janārdanasya paropakārāya caranti viśvam || 10.167 ||
tathā santaḥ pratiṣṭhā dīnānāṃ daivādudbhūtapāpmanām | ārtānāmārtihantāro darśanādeva sādhavaḥ || 10.168 ||
tatraivottarakhaṇḍe śivapārvatīsaṃvāde na karmabandhanaṃ janma vaiṣṇavānāṃ ca vidyate | viṣṇoranucaratvaṃ hi mokṣayāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ || 10.169 || na dāsyamamareśasya bandhanaṃ parikīrtitam | sarvabandhananirmuktā haridāsā nirāmayāḥ || 10.170 ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe janmāṣṭamīvratamāhātmye śrīcitraguptoktau darśanasparśanālāpasahavāsādibhiḥ kṣaṇāt | bhaktāḥ punanti kṛṣṇasya sākṣādapi ca pukkaśam || 10.171 || tyaktasarvakulācāro mahāpātakavānapi | viṣṇorbhaktaṃ samāśritya naro nārhati yātanām || 10.172 ||
vāśiṣṭhe yasmin deśe marau tajjño nāsti sajjanapādapaḥ | saphalaḥ śītalacchāyo na tatra divasaṃ vaset || 10.173 ||
sadā santo'bhigantavyā yadyapyupadiśanti na | yā hi svairakathāsteṣāmupadeśā bhavanti te || 10.174 || gāruḍe satrayājisahasrebhyaḥ sarvavedāntapāragaḥ | sarvavedāntavitkoṭyā viṣṇubhakto viśiṣyate || 10.175 || vaiṣṇavānāṃ sahasrebhya ekāntyeko viśiṣyate | ekāntinastu puruṣā gacchanti paramaṃ padam || 10.176 ||
śrībhagavadgītāsu api cetsudurācāro bhajate māmananyabhāk | sādhureva sa mantavyaḥ samyagvyavasito hi saḥ || 10.177 || kṣipraṃ bhavati dharmātmā śaśvacchāntiṃ nigacchati | kaunteya pratijānīhi na me bhaktaḥ praṇaśyati || 10.178 || māṃ hi pārtha vyapāśritya ye'pi syuḥ pāpayonayaḥ | striyo vaiśyāstathā śūdrāste'pi yānti parāṃ gatim | kiṃ punarbrāhmaṇāḥ puṇyā bhaktā rājarṣayastathā || 10.179 ||
kiṃ ca tatraiva yogināmapi sarveṣāṃ madgatenāntarātmanā | śraddhāvān bhajate yo māṃ sa me yuktatamo mataḥ || 10.180 ||
śrībhāgavatasya prathamaskandhe śrīparīkṣitoktau yeṣāṃ saṃsmaraṇātpuṃsāṃ sadyaḥ śuddhyanti vai gṛhāḥ | kiṃ punardarśanasparśa pādaśaucāsanādibhiḥ || 10.181 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīvidurasya śrutasya puṃsāṃ suciraśramasya nanvañjasā sūribhirīḍito'rthaḥ | tattadguṇānuśravaṇaṃ mukunda pādāravindaṃ hṛdayeṣu yeṣām || 10.182 ||
devahūtiṃ prati kapiladevasya na karhicinmatparāḥ śāntarūpe naṅkṣyanti no me'nimiṣo leḍhi hetiḥ | yeṣāmahaṃ priya ātmā sutaśca sakhā guruḥ suhṛdo daivamiṣṭam || 10.183 ||
caturthe śrīdhruvasya yā nirvṛtistanubhṛtāṃ tava pādapadma dhyānādbhavajjanakathāśravaṇena vā syāt | sā brahmaṇi svamahimanyapi nātha mā bhūt kiṃ tvantakāsilulitātpatatāṃ vimānāt || 10.184 ||
śrīrudrasya svadharmaniṣṭhaḥ śatajanmabhiḥ pumān viriñcatāmeti tataḥ paraṃ hi mām | avyākṛtaṃ bhāgavato'tha vaiṣṇavaṃ padaṃ yathāhaṃ vibudhāḥ kalātyaye || 10.185 ||
pañcame śrījaḍabharatasya rahūgaṇaitattapasā na yāti na cejyayā nirvapaṇādgṛhādvā | na cchandasā naiva jalāgnisūryair vinā mahatpādarajo'bhiṣekam || 10.186 || ṣaṣṭhe śrīparīkṣitaḥ rajobhiḥ samasaṅkhyātāḥ pārthivairiha jantavaḥ | teṣāṃ ye kecanehante śreyo vai manujādayaḥ || 10.187 || prāyo mumukṣavasteṣāṃ kecanaiva dvijottama | mumukṣūṇāṃ sahasreṣu kaścinmucyeta sidhyati || 10.188 || muktānāmapi siddhānāṃ nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaḥ | sudurlabhaḥ praśāntātmā koṭiṣvapi mahāmune || 10.189 ||
śrīśivasya nārāyaṇaparāḥ sarve na kutaścana bibhyati | svargāpavarganarakeṣvapi tulyārthadarśinaḥ || 10.190 ||
saptame śrīprahlādasya naiṣāṃ matistāvadurukramāṅghriṃ spṛśatyanarthāpagamo yadarthaḥ | mahīyasāṃ pādarajo'bhiṣekaṃ niṣkiñcanānāṃ na vṛṇīta yāvat || 10.191 ||
kiṃ ca viprāddviṣaḍguṇayutādaravindanābha pādāravindavimukhātśvapacaṃ variṣṭham | manye tadarpitamanovacanehitārtha prāṇaṃ punāti sa kulaṃ na tu bhūrimānaḥ || 10.192 ||
aṣṭame śrīgajendrasya ekāntino yasya na kañcanārthaṃ vāñchanti ye vai bhagavatprapannāḥ | atyadbhutaṃ taccaritaṃ sumaṅgalaṃ gāyanta ānandasamudramagnāḥ || 10.193 ||
navame śrībhagavataḥ ahaṃ bhaktaparādhīno hyasvatantra iva dvija | sādhubhirgrastahṛdayo bhaktairbhaktajanapriyaḥ || 10.194 || nāhamātmānamāśāse madbhaktaiḥ sādhubhirvinā | śriyaṃ cātyantikīṃ brahman yeṣāṃ gatirahaṃ parā || 10.195 || ye dārāgāraputrāpta prāṇān vittamimaṃ param | hitvā māṃ śaraṇaṃ yātāḥ kathaṃ tāṃstyaktumutsahe || 10.196 || mayi nirbaddhahṛdayāḥ sādhavaḥ samadarśanāḥ | vaśe kurvanti māṃ bhaktyā satstriyaḥ satpatiṃ yathā || 10.197 || sādhavo hṛdayaṃ mahyaṃ sādhūnāṃ hṛdayaṃ tvaham | madanyatte na jānanti nāhaṃ tebhyo manāgapi || 10.198 ||
tatraiva śrīdurvāsasaḥ duṣkaraḥ ko nu sādhūnāṃ dustyajo vā mahātmanām | yaiḥ saṅgṛhīto bhagavān sātvatāmṛṣabho hariḥ || 10.199 || yannāmaśrutimātreṇa pumān bhavati nirmalaḥ | tasya tīrthapadaḥ kiṃ vā dāsānāmavaśiṣyate || 10.200 ||
daśame devastutau tathā na te mādhava tāvakāḥ kvacid bhraśyanti mārgāttvayi baddhasauhṛdāḥ | tvayābhiguptā vicaranti nirbhayā vināyakānīkapamūrdhasu prabho || 10.201 ||
śrībādarāyaṇeḥ nāyaṃ sukhāpo bhagavān dehināṃ gopikāsutaḥ | jñānināṃ cātmabhūtānāṃ yathā bhaktimatāmiha || 10.202 ||
tatraiva śrībhagavataḥ sādhūnāṃ samacittānāṃ mukundacaraṇaiṣiṇām | upekṣyaiḥ kiṃ dhanastambhairasadbhirasadāśrayaiḥ || 10.203 ||
kiṃ ca na hyammayāni tīrthāni na devā mṛcchilāmayāḥ | te punantyurukālena darśanādeva sādhavaḥ || 10.204 ||
api ca nāgnirna sūryo na ca candratārakā na bhūrjalaṃ khaṃ śvasano'tha vāṅmanaḥ | upāsitā bhedakṛto harantyaghaṃ vipaścito ghnanti muhūrtasevayā || 10.205 || yasyātmabuddhiḥ kuṇape tridhātuke svadhīḥ kalatrādiṣu bhauma ijyadhīḥ | yattīrthabuddhiḥ salile na karhicij janeṣvabhijñeṣu sa eva gokharaḥ || 10.206 ||
śrutistutau tava pari ye carantyakhilasattvaniketatayā ta uta padākramantyavigaṇayya śiro nirṛteḥ | parivayase paśūniva girā vibudhānapi tāṃs tvayi kṛtasauhṛdāḥ khalu punanti na ye vimukhāḥ || 10.207 ||
ekādaśe śrīvasudevasya bhūtānāṃ devacaritaṃ duḥkhāya ca sukhāya ca | sukhāyaiva hi sādhūnāṃ tvādṛśāmacyutātmanām || 10.208 || bhajanti ye yathā devān devā api tathaiva tān | chāyeva karmasacivāḥ sādhavo dīnavatsalāḥ || 10.209 ||
tatraiva śrībhagavataḥ na mayyekāntabhaktānāṃ guṇadoṣodbhavā guṇāḥ | sādhūnāṃ samacittānāṃ buddheḥ paramupeyuṣām || 10.210 || evametānmayā diṣṭānanutiṣṭhanti me pathaḥ | kṣemaṃ vindanti matsthānaṃ yadbrahma paramaṃ viduḥ || 10.211 || nimajjyonmajjatāṃ ghore bhavābdhau paramāyaṇam | santo brahmavidaḥ śāntā nordṛḍhevāpsu majjatām || 10.212 || annaṃ hi prāṇināṃ prāṇā ārtānāṃ śaraṇaṃ tvaham | dharmā vittaṃ nṝṇāṃ pretya santo'rvāgbibhyato'raṇam || 10.213 || santo diśanti cakṣūṃṣi bahirarkaḥ samutthitaḥ | devatā bāndhavāḥ santaḥ santa ātmāhameva ca || 10.214 ||
kiṃ ca na kiñcitsādhavo dhīrā bhaktā hyekāntino mama | vāñchantyapi mayā dattaṃ kaivalyamapunarbhavam || 10.215 ||
dvādaśe ca śrīparīkṣitaḥ nātyadbhutamahaṃ manye mahatāmacyutātmanām | ajñeṣu tāpatapteṣu bhūteṣu yadanugrahaḥ || 10.216 ||
śrīrudrasya ca mārkaṇḍeyamadhikṛtya śravaṇāddarśanādvāpi mahāpātakino'pi vaḥ | śudhyerannantyajāścāpi kimu sambhāṣaṇādibhiḥ || 10.217 ||
ataeva śrīdharmarājasya svadūtānuśāsane ṣaṣṭhaskandhe te devasiddhaparigītapavitragāthā ye sādhavaḥ samadṛśo bhagavatprapannāḥ | tānnopasīdata harergadayābhiguptān naiṣāṃ vayaṃ na ca vayaḥ prabhavāma daṇḍe || 10.218 ||
tathā śrīviṣṇupurāṇe yamaniyamavidhūtakalmaṣāṇām anudinamacyutasaktamānasānām | apagatamadamānamatsarāṇāṃ vraja bhaṭa dūratareṇa mānavānām || 10.219 ||
sakalamidamahaṃ ca vāsudevaḥ paramapumān parameśvaraḥ sa ekaḥ | iti matiracalā bhavatyanante hṛdayagate vraja tān vihāya dūrāt || 10.220 || kamalanayana vāsudeva viṣṇo dharaṇidharācyuta śaṅkhacakrapāṇe | bhava śaraṇamitīrayanti ye vai tyaja bhaṭa dūratareṇa tānapāpān || 10.221 || vasati manasi yasya so'vyayātmā | puruṣavarasya na tasya dṛṣṭipāte | tava gatiratha vā mamāsti cakra pratihatavīryabalasya so'nyalokyaḥ || 10.222 ||
nārasiṃhe, viṣṇupurāṇe ca ahamamarārcitena dhātrā yama iti lokahitāhite niyuktaḥ | hariguruvaśago'smi na svatantraḥ prabhavati saṃyamane mamāpi viṣṇuḥ || 10.223 ||
sugatimabhilaṣāmi vāsudevā vahamapi bhāgavatasthitāntarātmā | madhuvara vaśago'smi na svatantraḥ prabhavati saṃyamane mamāpi kṛṣṇaḥ || 10.224 || na hi śaśakaluṣacchaviḥ kadācit timiraparābhavatāmupaiti candraḥ | bhagavati ca harāvananyacetā bhṛśamalino'pi virājate manuṣyaḥ || 10.225 ||
pādme devadyūtivikuṇḍalasaṃvāde prāhāsmān yamunā bhrātā sadaiva hi punaḥ punaḥ | bhavadbhirvaiṣṇavāstyājyā na te syurmama gocarāḥ || 10.226 || durācāro duṣkṛto'pi sadācārarato'pi yaḥ | bhavadbhiḥ sa sadā tyājyo viṣṇuṃ ca bhajate naraḥ || 10.227 || vaiṣṇavo yadgṛhe bhuṅkte yeṣāṃ vaiṣṇavasaṅgatiḥ | te'pi vaḥ parivāryāḥ syustatsaṅgahatakilbiṣāḥ || 10.228 ||
skānde amṛtasāroddhāre ekādaśyāmabhuñjānā yuktāḥ pāpaśatairapi | bhavadbhiḥ parihartavyā hitā me yadi sarvadā || 10.229 || ye smaranti jagannāthaṃ mṛtyukāle janārdanam | pāpakoṭiśatairyuktā na te grāhyā mamājñayā || 10.230 || na brahmā na śivāgnīndrā nāhaṃ nānye divaukasaḥ | śaktā na nigrahaṃ kartuṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām || 10.231 || ato'haṃ sarvakālaṃ ca vaiṣṇavānāṃ bibhemi vai | bhavadbhiḥ parihartavyā vaiṣṇavā ye sadaiva hi || 10.232 || vaiṣṇavā viṣṇuvatpūjyā mama mānyā viśeṣataḥ | teṣāṃ kṛte'pamāne'pi vināśo jāyate dhruvam || 10.233 ||
kiṃ ca yeṣāṃ smaraṇamātreṇa pāpalakṣaśatāni ca | dahyante nātra sandeho vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām || 10.234 || yeṣāṃ pādarajenaiva prāpyate jāhnavījalam | nārmadaṃ yāmunaṃ caiva kiṃ punaḥ pādayorjalam || 10.235 || yeṣāṃ vākyajalaughena vinā gaṅgājalairapi | vinā tīrthasahasreṇa snāto bhavati mānavaḥ || 10.236 ||
kiṃ ca brahmaloke na me vāso na me vāso harālaye | nālaye lokapālānāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ parābhave || 10.237 || na devā na ca gandharvā na yakṣoragarākṣasāḥ | trātuṃ samarthā ṛṣayo vaiṣṇavānāṃ parābhave || 10.238 || karomi karmaṇā vācā manasāpi na vipriyam | vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahābhāgāḥ sudarśanabhayādapi || 10.239 || ekato dhāvate cakramekato harivāhanam | ekato viṣṇudūtāśca vaiṣṇave cārdite mayā || 10.240 ||
bṛhannāradīye caikādaśīmāhātmye ye viṣṇupūjanaratāḥ prayatāḥ kṛtajñāś caikādaśīvrataparā vijitendriyāśca | nārāyaṇācyuta hare śaraṇaṃ bhaveti śāntā vadanti satataṃ tarasā tyajadhvam || 10.241 || nārāyaṇārpitadhiyo haribhaktabhaktān svācāramārganiratān gurusevakāṃśca | satpātradānaniratān harikīrtibhaktān dūtāstyajadhvamaniśaṃ harināmasaktān || 10.242 || pāṣaṇḍasaṅgarahitān haribhaktituṣṭān satsaṅgalolupatarāṃśca tathātipuṇyān | śambhorhareśca samabuddhimatastathaiva dūtāstyajadhvamupakāraparāñnarāṇām || 10.243 || ye vīkṣitā harikathāmṛtasevakaiśca nārāyaṇasmṛtiparāyaṇamānasaiśca | viprendrapādajalasevanasamprahṛṣṭais tān pāpino'pi ca bhaṭā satataṃ tyajadhvam || 10.244 ||
