ataevoktaṃ śrīnāradena caturthaskandhaśeṣe śriyamanucaratīṃ tadarthinaśca dvipadapatīn vibudhāṃśca yatsvapūrṇaḥ | na bhajati nijabhṛtyavargatantraḥ kathamamumudvisṛjetpumān kṛtajñaḥ || 10.245 ||
ataeva prārthanam | nārāyaṇavyūhastave nāhaṃ brahmāpi bhuyāsaṃ tvadbhaktirahito hare | tvayi bhaktastu kīṭo'pi bhūyāsaṃ janmajanmasu || 10.246 ||
śrībrahmastutau ca daśamaskandhe tadastu me nātha sa bhūribhāgo bhave'tra vānyatra tu vā tiraścām | yenāhameko'pi bhavajjanānāṃ bhūtvā niṣeve tava pādapallavam || 10.247 ||
ataevoktaṃ śrīnārāyaṇavyūhastave ye tyaktalokadharmārthā viṣṇubhaktivaśaṃ gatāḥ | bhajanti paramātmānaṃ tebhyo nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 10.248 ||
evaṃ śrībhagavadbhaktamāhātmyāmṛtavāridheḥ | vicitrabhaṅgalekhārho lobhalolaṃ vināsti kaḥ || 10.249 || ataḥ śrībhagavadbhaktajanānāṃ saṅgatiḥ sadā | kāryā sarvaprayatnena dvau lokau vijigīṣubhiḥ || 10.250 || atha śrībhagavadbhaktasaṅgamāhātmyam
bhagavadbhaktapādābjapādukābhyo namo'stu me | yatsaṅgamaḥ sādhanaṃ ca sādhyaṃ cākhilamuttamam || 10.251 ||
tatra sarvapātakamocakatā
bṛhannāradīye yajñamālyupākhyānānte | haribhaktiparāṇāṃ tu saṅgināṃ saṅgamātrataḥ | mucyate sarvapāpebhyo mahāpātakavānapi || 10.252 ||
sāmānyato'narthanivartakatāarthaprāpakatā ca |
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye śrīmuniśarmāṇaṃ prati pretānāmuktau vināśayatyapayaśo buddhiṃ viśadayatyapi | pratiṣṭhāpayati prāyo nṛṇāṃ vaiṣṇavadarśanam || 10.253 ||
tatra śrīyamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde mahārathanṛpoktau yathā prapadyamānasya bhagavantaṃ vibhāvasum | śītaṃ bhayaṃ tamo'pyeti sādhūn saṃsevataḥ sadā || 10.254 ||
tatraiva pretopākhyāne pretoktau apākaroti duritaṃ śreyaḥ saṃyojayatyapi | yaśo vistārayatyāśu nṝṇāṃ vaiṣṇavasaṅgamaḥ || 10.255 ||
atha sarvatīrthādhikatā
tatraiva gaṅgādipuṇyatīrtheṣu yo naraḥ snātumicchati | yaḥ karoti satāṃ saṅgāṃ tayoḥ satsaṅgamo varaḥ || 10.256 ||
tatraiva bhagīrathanṛpoktau yaḥ snātaḥ śāntisitayā sādhusaṅgatigaṅgayā | kiṃ tasya dānaiḥ kiṃ tīrthaiḥ kiṃ tapobhiḥ kimadhvaraiḥ || 10.257 ||
atha sarveṣṭasādhakatā
yāni yāni durāpāṇi vāñchitāni mahītale | prāpyante tāni tānyeva sādhūnāmeva saṅgamāt || 10.258 ||
atha anarthasyāpyarthatvāpādakatā
vāśiṣṭhe śūnyamāpūrṇatāmeti mṛtirapyamṛtāyate | āpatsampadiva bhāti vidvajjanasamāgame || 10.259 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe saṅgo yaḥ saṃsṛterheturasatsu vihito'dhiyā | sa eva sādhuṣu kṛto niḥsaṅgatvāya kalpate || 10.260 ||
śrīkapiladevoktau prasaṅgamajaraṃ pāśamātmanaḥ kavayo viduḥ | sa eva sādhuṣu kṛto mokṣadvāramapāvṛtam || 10.261 ||
yataḥ arirmitraṃ viṣaṃ pathyamadharmo dharmatāṃ vrajet | prasanne puṇḍarīkākṣe viparīte viparyayaḥ || 10.262 ||
kiṃ ca śrībhagavadvākyaṃ mannimittaṃ kṛtaṃ pāpamapi dharmāya kalpate | māmanādṛtya dharmo'pi pāpaṃ syānmatprabhāvataḥ || 10.263 ||
caturthaskandhe śrīdhruvoktau te na smarantyatitarāṃ priyamīśa martyaṃ ye cānvadaḥ sutasuhṛdgṛhavittadārāḥ | ye tvabjanābha bhavadīyapadāravinda saugandhyalubdhahṛdayeṣu kṛtaprasaṅgāḥ || 10.264 ||
pādme tatraiva pretoktau rasāyanamayī śītā paramānandadāyinī | nānandayati kaṃ nāma vaiṣṇavāśrayacandrikā || 10.265 ||
atha mokṣadatvam | daśamaskandhe śrīmucukundastutau bhavāpavargo bhramato yadā bhavej janasya tarhyacuta satsamāgamaḥ | satsaṅgamo yarhi tadaiva sadgatau parāvareśe tvayi jāyate matiḥ || 10.266 ||
ataevoktaṃ śrīpracetobhiścaturthaskandhe yatreḍyante kathā mṛṣṭāstṛṣṇāyāḥ praśamo yataḥ | nirvairaṃ yatra bhūteṣu nodvego yatra kaścana || 10.267 || yatra nārāyaṇaḥ sākṣādbhagavānnyāsināṃ gatiḥ | saṃstūyate satkathāsu muktasaṅgaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 10.268 || teṣāṃ vicaratāṃ padbhyāṃ tīrthānāṃ pāvanecchayā | bhītasya kiṃ na roceta tāvakānāṃ samāgamaḥ || 10.269 ||
atha sarvasāratā | bṛhannāradīye śrīnāradasanatkumārasaṃvāde asārabhūte saṃsāre sārametadajātmaja | bhagavadbhaktasaṅgaśca haribhaktistitikṣutā || 10.270 ||
pādme tatraiva mahārathanṛpoktau asāgarotthaṃ pīyūṣamadravyaṃ vyasanauṣadham | harṣaścālokaparyantaḥ satāṃ kila samāgamaḥ || 10.271 ||
atha bhagavatkathāmṛtapānaikahetutā | pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye śrīnāradoktau prasaṅgena satāmātmamanaḥ śrutirasāyanāḥ | bhavanti kīrtanīyasya kathāḥ kṛṣṇasya nirmalāḥ || 10.272 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīkapiladevoktau satāṃ prasaṅgānmama vīryasaṃvido bhavanti hṛtkarṇarasāyanāḥ kathāḥ | tajjoṣaṇādāśvapavargavartmani śraddhā ratirbhaktiranukramiṣyati || 10.273 ||
caturthe śrīnāradoktau yatra bhāgavatā rājan sādhavo viśadāśayāḥ | bhagavadguṇānukathana śravaṇavyagracetasaḥ || 10.274 ||
tasminmahanmukharitā madhubhic caritrapīyūṣaśeṣasaritaḥ paritaḥ sravanti | tā ye pibantyavitṛṣo nṛpa gāḍhakarṇais tānna spṛśantyaśanatṛḍbhayaśokamohāḥ || 10.275 ||
pañcame śrībrāhmaṇarahūgaṇasaṃvāde yatrottamaślokaguṇānuvādaḥ prastūyate grāmyakathāvighātaḥ | niṣevyamāṇo'nudinaṃ mumukṣor matiṃ satīṃ yacchati vāsudeve || 10.276 ||
ekādaśe śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde śryailopākhyānānte teṣu nityaṃ mahābhāga mahābhāgeṣu matkathāḥ | sambhavanti hi tā n.ṇāṃ juṣatāṃ prapunantyagham || 10.277 || tā ye śṛṇvanti gāyanti hyanumodanti cādṛtāḥ | matparāḥ śraddadhānāśca bhaktiṃ vindanti te mayi || 10.278 ||
bṛhannāradīye tatraiva bhaktistu bhagavadbhaktasaṅgena parijāyate | satsaṅgaḥ prāpyate pumbhiḥ sukṛtaiḥ pūrvasañcitaiḥ || 10.279 ||
ekādaśe śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde athaitatparamaṃ guhyaṃ śṛṇvato yadunandana sugopyamapi vakṣyāmi tvaṃ me bhṛtyaḥ suhṛtsakhā || 10.280 || no rodhayati māṃ yogo na sāṅkhyaṃ dharma eva ca | na svādhyāyastapastyāgo neṣṭāpūrtaṃ na dakṣiṇā || 10.281 || vratāni yajñaśchandāṃsi tīrthāni niyamā yamāḥ | yathāvarundhe satsaṅgaḥ sarvasaṅgāpaho hi mām || 10.282 ||
ataevoktaṃ vidureṇa tṛtīyaskandhe yatsevayā bhagavataḥ kūṭasthasya madhudviṣaḥ | ratirāso bhavettīvraḥ pādayorvyasanārdanaḥ || 10.283 ||
prathamaskandhe śrīśaunakādīnāṃ, caturthe ca śrīpracetasāmuktau tulayāma lavenāpi na svargaṃ nāpunarbhavam | bhagavatsaṅgisaṅgasya martyānāṃ kimutāśiṣaḥ || 10.284 ||
caturthe ca śrīpracetasaḥ prati śrīśivopadeśaḥ kṣaṇārdhenāpi tulaye na svargaṃ nāpunarbhavam | bhagavatsaṅgisaṅgasya martyānāṃ kimutāśiṣaḥ || 10.285 ||
dvādaśe śrīmārkaṇḍeyopākhyāne śrīśivasya athāpi saṃvadiṣyāmo bhavānyetena sādhunā | ayaṃ hi paramo lābho nṛṇāṃ sādhusamāgamaḥ || 10.286 ||
ataeva śrīprahlādaṃ prati śrīdharaṇyoktaṃ haribhaktisudhodaye akṣṇoḥ phalaṃ tvādṛśadarśanaṃ hi tanvāḥ phalaṃ tvādṛśagātrasaṅgaḥ | jihvāphalaṃ tvādṛśakīrtanaṃ hi sudurlabhā bhāgavatā hi loke || 10.287 ||
ataeva vidureṇa tṛtīyaskandhe durāpā hyalpatapasaḥ sevā vaikuṇṭhavartmasu | yatropagīyate nityaṃ devadevo janārdanaḥ || 10.288 ||
śrīvidehenāpyekādaśaskandhe durlabho mānuṣo deho dehināṃ kṣaṇabhaṅguraḥ | tatrāpi durlabhaṃ manye vaikuṇṭhapriyadarśanam || 10.289 ||
ataeva hi prārthitaṃ śrīdhruveṇa caturthaskandhe bhaktiṃ muhuḥ pravahatāṃ tvayi me prasaṅgo bhūyādananta mahatāmamalāśayānām | yenāñjasolbaṇamuruvyasanaṃ bhavābdhiṃ neṣye bhavadguṇakathāmṛtapānamattaḥ || 10.290 ||
pracetasaḥ pratyupadeśe śrīśivena ca athānaghāṅghrestava kīrtitīrthayor antarbahiḥsnānavidhūtapāpmanām | bhūteṣvanukrośasusattvaśīlināṃ syātsaṅgamo'nugraha eṣa nastava || 10.291 ||
śrīpracetobhiśca yāvatte māyayā spṛṣṭā bhramāma iha karmabhiḥ | tāvadbhavatprasaṅgānāṃ saṅgaḥ syānno bhave bhave || 10.292 ||
śrīprahlādenāpi saptamaskandhe tasmādamūstanubhṛtāmahamāśiṣo jña āyuḥ śriyaṃ vibhavamaindriyamāviriñcyāt | necchāmi te vilulitānuruvikrameṇa kālātmanopanaya māṃ nijabhṛtyapārśvam || 10.293 || iti |
athāsatsaṅgadoṣāḥ | śrīkātyāyanavākye varaṃ hutavahajvālāpañjarāntarvyavasthitiḥ | na śauricintāvimukhajanasaṃvāsavaiśasam || 10.295 ||
pādme uttarakhaṇḍe śryumāmaheśvarasaṃvāde avaiṣṇavāstu ye viprāścāṇḍālādadhamāḥ smṛtāḥ | teṣāṃ sambhāṣaṇaṃ sparśaṃ somapānādi varjayet || 10.296 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīkapiladevahūtisaṃvāde satyaṃ śaucaṃ dayā maunaṃ buddhiḥ śrīrhrīryaśaḥ kṣamā | śamo damo bhagaśceti yatsaṅgādyāti saṅkṣayam || 10.297 || teṣvaśānteṣu mūḍheṣu khaṇḍitātmasvasādhuṣu | saṅgaṃ na kuryācchocyeṣu yoṣitkrīḍāmṛgeṣu ca || 10.298 || na tathāsya bhavenmoho bandhaścānyaprasaṅgataḥ | yoṣitsaṅgādyathā puṃso yathā tatsaṅgisaṅgataḥ || 10.299 ||
ekādaśe ca śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde saṅgaṃ na kuryādasatāṃ śiśnodaratṛpāṃ kvacit | tasyānugastamasyandhe patatyandhānugāndhavat || 10.300 ||
bhagavadbhaktihīnā ye mukhyā'santasta eva hi | teṣāṃ niṣṭhā śubhā kvāpi na syātsaccaritairapi || 10.301 ||
bṛhannāradīye prāyaścittaprakaraṇānte kiṃ vedaiḥ kimu vā śāstraiḥ kimu tīrthaniṣevaṇaiḥ | viṣṇubhaktivihīnānāṃ kiṃ tapobhiḥ kimadhvaraiḥ || 10.302 ||
śrīgāruḍe antaṃ gato'pi vedānāṃ sarvaśāstrārthavedyapi | yo na sarveśvare bhaktastaṃ vidyātpuruṣādhamam || 10.303 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe ahnyāpṛtārtakaraṇā niśi niḥśayānā nānāmanorathadhiyā kṣaṇabhagnanidrāḥ | daivāhatārtharacanā ṛṣayo'pi deva yuṣmatprasaṅgavimukhā iha saṃsaranti || 10.304 ||
ataevoktaṃ ṣaṣṭhe prāyaścittāni cīrṇāni nārāyaṇaparāṅmukham | na niṣpunanti rājendra surākumbhamivāpagāḥ || 10.305 ||
viṣṇudharmottare kutaḥ pāpakṣayasteṣāṃ kutasteṣāṃ ca maṅgalam | yeṣāṃ naiva hṛdistho'yaṃ maṅgalāyatano hariḥ || 10.306 ||
ataeva bṛhannāradīye lubdhakopākhyānārambhe haripūjāvihīnāśca vedavidveṣiṇastathā | dvijagodveṣiṇaścāpi rākṣasāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 10.307 ||
ataeva nijadūtān prati dharmarājasyānuśāsanaṃ ṣaṣṭhaskandhe tānānayadhvamasato vimukhānmukunda pādāravindamakarandarasādajasram | niṣkiñcanaiḥ paramahaṃsakulairasaṅgair juṣṭādgṛhe nirayavartmani baddhatṛṣṇān || 10.308 || jihvā na vakti bhagavadguṇanāmadheyaṃ cetaśca na smarati taccaraṇāravindam | kṛṣṇāya no namati yacchira ekadāpi tānānayadhvamasato'kṛtaviṣṇukṛtyān || 10.309 ||
skānde mārkaṇḍeyabhagīrathasaṃvāde yo hi bhāgavataṃ lokamupahāsaṃ nṛpottama | karoti tasya naśyanti arthadharmayaśaḥsutā || 10.310 || nindāṃ kurvanti ye mūḍhā vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām | patanti pitṛbhiḥ sārdhaṃ mahārauravasaṃjñite || 10.311 || hanti nindati vai dveṣṭi vaiṣṇavānnābhinandati | krudhyate yāti no harṣaṃ darśane patanāni ṣaṭ || 10.312 ||
tatraivāmṛtasāroddhāre śrīyamoktau janmaprabhṛti yatkiñcitsukṛtaṃ samupārjitam | nāśamāyāti tatsarvaṃ pīḍayedyadi vaiṣṇavān || 10.313 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye prahlādabalisaṃvāde karapatraiśca phālyante sutīvrairyamaśāsanaiḥ | nindāṃ kurvanti ye pāpā vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām || 10.314 || pūjito bhagavān viṣṇurjanmāntaraśatairapi | prasīdati na viśvātmā vaiṣṇave cāpamānite || 10.315 ||
daśamaskandhe ca nindāṃ bhagavataḥ śṛṇvaṃstatparasya janasya vā | tato nāpaiti yaḥ so'pi yātyadhaḥ sukṛtāccyutaḥ || 10.316 ||
ataevoktaṃ viṣṇudharmottare jīvitaṃ viṣṇubhaktasya varaṃ pañcadināni ca | na tu kalpasahasrāṇi bhaktihīnasya keśave || 10.317 ||
ataevoktaṃ śrībhāgavate ailopākhyānānte tato duḥsaṅgamutsṛjya satsu sajjeta buddhimān | santa evāsya chindanti manovyāsaṅgamuktibhiḥ || 10.318 ||
atha śrīvaiṣṇavasamāgamavidhiḥ
atha śrībhagavadbhaktān sallakṣaṇavibhūṣitān | gatvā tān dūrato dṛṣṭvā daṇḍavatpraṇamenmudā || 10.319 ||
tejodraviṇapañcarātre vaiṣṇavo vaiṣṇavaṃ dṛṣṭvā daṇḍavatpraṇamedbhuvi | ubhayorantarā viṣṇuḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ || 10.320 ||
tatra ca viśeṣo bṛhannāradīye sabhāyāṃ yajñaśālāyāṃ devatāyataneṣvapi | pratyekaṃ tu namaskāro hanti puṇyaṃ purākṛtam || 10.321 || puṇyakṣetre puṇyatīrthe svādhyāyasamaye tathā | pratyekaṃ tu namaskāro hanti puṇyaṃ purākṛtam || 10.322 || vaiṣṇavaṃ cāgataṃ vīkṣyābhigamyāliṅga vaiṣṇavam | vaideśikaṃ prīṇayeyurdarśayantaḥ svavaiṣṇavān || 10.323 ||
tathā coktaṃ śrībrahmaṇā tejodraviṇapañcarātre nārāyaṇāśrayaṃ bhaktaṃ deśāntarasamāgatam | prīṇayeddarśayaṃstasya bhaktyā nārāyaṇāśrayān || 10.324 || iti
tataśca vaiṣṇavaḥ prāptaḥ santarpya vacanāmṛtaiḥ | sadbandhuriva sammānyo'nyathā doṣo mahān smṛtaḥ || 10.325 ||
atha vaiṣṇavasammānananityatā
skānde śrīmārkaṇḍeyabhagīrathasaṃvāde dṛṣṭvā bhāgavataṃ daivātsammukhe yo na yāti hi | na gṛhṇāti haristasya pūjāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm || 10.326 || yo na gṛhṇāti bhūpāla vaiṣṇavaṃ gṛhamāgatam | tadgṛhaṃ pitṛbhistyaktaṃ śmaśānamiva bhīṣaṇam || 10.327 || athavābhyagataṃ dūrādyo nārcayanti vaiṣṇavam | svaśaktyā nṛpaśārdūla nānyaḥ pāparatastataḥ || 10.328 || śrāntaṃ bhāgavataṃ dṛṣṭvā kaṭhinaṃ yasya mānasam | prasīdati na duṣṭātmā śvapacādadhiko hi saḥ || 10.329 || vipraṃ bhāgavataṃ dṛṣṭvā dīnamāturamānasam | na karoti paritrāṇaṃ keśavo na prasīdati || 10.330 || dṛṣṭvā bhāgavataṃ vipraṃ namaskāreṇa nārcayet | dehinastasya pāpasya na ca vai kṣamate hariḥ || 10.331 || apūjito yadā gacchadvaiṣṇavo gṛhamedhinaḥ | śatajanmārjitaṃ bhūpa puṇyamādāya gacchati || 10.332 || anabhyarcya pitṝn devān bhuñjate harivāsare | tatpāpaṃ jāyate bhūpa vaiṣṇavānāmatikrame || 10.333 || pūrvaṃ kṛtvā tu sammānamavajñāṃ kurute tu yaḥ | vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahīpāla sānvayo yāti saṅkṣayam || 10.334 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye yamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde vaiṣṇavaṃ janamālokya nābhyutthānaṃ karoti yaḥ | praṇayādarato vipra sa naro narakātithiḥ || 10.335 ||
caturthaskandhe ca vyālālayadrumā vai teṣvariktākhilasampadaḥ | yadgṛhāstīrthapādīya pādatīrthavivarjitāḥ || 10.336 ||
atha vaiṣṇavastutiḥ
skānde dahnyo'haṃ kṛtakṛtyo'haṃ yadyūyaṃ gṛhamāgatāḥ | durlabhaṃ darśanaṃ nūnaṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ yathā hareḥ || 10.337 || merumandaratulyā vai puṇyapuñjā mayā kṛtāḥ | samprāptaṃ darśanaṃ yadvai vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām || 10.338 || daśamaskandhe śrīgargācāryaṃ prati śrīnandasya vākyam mahadvicalanaṃ nṝṇāṃ gṛhiṇāṃ dīnacetasām | niḥśreyasāya bhagavan kalpate nānyathā kvacit || 10.339 ||
caturthaskandhe aho ācaritaṃ kiṃ me maṅgalaṃ maṅgalāyanāḥ | yasya vo darśanaṃ hyāsīddurdarśānāṃ ca yogibhiḥ || 10.340 || adhanā api te dhanyāḥ sādhavo gṛhamedhinaḥ | yadgṛhā hyarhavaryāmbu tṛṇabhūmīśvarāvarāḥ || 10.341 || kaccinnaḥ kuśalaṃ nāthā indriyārthārthavedinām | vyasanāvāpa etasmin patitānāṃ svakarmabhiḥ || 10.342 || bhavatsu kuśalapraśna ātmārāmeṣu neṣyate | kuśalākuśalā yatra na santi mativṛttayaḥ || 10.343 ||
atha vaiṣṇavābhigamanamāhātmyam
skānde śrīmārkaṇḍeyabhagīrathasaṃvāde sammukhaṃ vrajamānasya vaiṣṇavānāṃ narādhipa | pade pade yajñaphalaṃ prāhuḥ paurāṇikā dvijāḥ || 10.344 ||
atha vaiṣṇavastutimāhātmyam | tatraiva pratyakṣaṃ vā parokṣaṃ vā yaḥ praśaṃsati vaiṣṇavam | brahmahā madyapaḥ steyī gurugāmī sadā nṝṇām | mucyate pātakātsadyo viṣṇurāha nṛpottama || 10.345 || kiṃ ca pratyakṣaṃ vā parokṣaṃ vā ye praśaṃsanti vaiṣṇavam | prasādādvāsudevasya te taranti bhavārṇavam || 10.346 ||
atha vaiṣṇavasammānamāhātmyam | tatraivāmṛtasāroddhāre śraddhayā dattamannaṃ ca vaiṣṇavāgniṣu jīryati | tadannaṃ meruṇā tulyaṃ bhavate ca dine dine || 10.347 || daive paitre ca yo dadyādvārimātraṃ tu vaiṣṇave | saptodadhisamaṃ bhūtvā pitṝṇāmupatiṣṭhati || 10.348 ||
viṣṇudharme kiṃ dānaiḥ kiṃ tapobhirvā yajñaiśca vividhaiḥ kṛtaiḥ | sarvaṃ sampadyate puṃsāṃ viṣṇubhaktābhipūjanāt || 10.349 || pūjayedvaiṣṇavānetān prayatnena vicakṣaṇaḥ | svaśaktyā vaiṣṇavebhyo yaddattaṃ syādakṣayaṃ bhavet || 10.350 ||
bṛhannāradīye yajñamālyupākhyāne haribhaktiratān yastu haribuddhyā prapūjayet | tasya tuṣyanti viprendra brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ || 10.351 || haripūjāparāṇāṃ ca harināmaratātmanām | śuśrūṣāniratā yānti pāpino'pi parāṃ gatim || 10.352 ||
tatraiva yajñadhvajopākhyānasyārambhe saṃsārasāgaraṃ tartuṃ ya icchenmunipuṅgava | sa bhajeddharibhaktānāṃ bhaktān vai pāpahāriṇaḥ || 10.353 ||
tadante ca yo viṣṇubhaktānniṣkāmān bhojayetśraddhayānvitaḥ | triḥsaptakulasaṃyuktāste yānti harimandiram || 10.354 || viṣṇubhaktāya yo dadyānniṣkāmāya mahātmane | pānīyaṃ vā phalaṃ vāpi sa eva bhagavatpriyaḥ || 10.355 || viṣṇubhaktiparāṇāṃ tu śuśrūṣāṃ kurvate tu ye | te yānti viṣṇubhavanaṃ yāvadābhūtasamplavam || 10.356 || devapūjāparo yasya gṛhe vasati sarvadā | tatraiva sarvadevāśca tiṣṭhanti śrīharistathā || 10.357 ||
laiṅge nārāyaṇaparo vidvān yasyānnaṃ prītamānasaḥ | aśnāti taddharerāsyaṃ gatamannaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 10.358 || sarvārcanādapi viśvātmā prīto bhavati mādhavaḥ | dṛṣṭvā bhāgavatasyānnaṃ sa bhuṅkte bhaktavatsalaḥ || 10.359 ||
brāhme śrībhagavadvākyam naivedyaṃ purato nyastaṃ dṛṣṭvaiva svīkṛtaṃ mayā | bhaktasya rasanāgreṇa rasamaśnāmi padmaja || 10.360 ||
pādmottarakhaṇḍe śrīśivomāsaṃvāde ārādhanānāṃ sarveṣāṃ viṣṇorārādhanaṃ param | tasmātparataraṃ devi tadīyānāṃ samarcanam || 10.361 || arcayitvāpi govindaṃ tadīyānnārcayetpunaḥ | na sa bhāgavato jñeyaḥ kevalaṃ dāmbhikaḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.362 || pumāṃstasmātprayatnena vaiṣṇavān pūjayetsadā | sarvaṃ tarati duḥkhaughaṃ mahābhāgavatārcanāt || 10.363 ||
ekādaśe śrībhagavadvākyam vaiṣṇave bandhusatkṛtyā || 10.364 || madbhaktapūjābhyadhikā || 10.365 ||
kiṃ ca skānde śrīmārkaṇḍeyabhagīrathasaṃvāde karmaṇā manasā vācā ye'rcayanti sadā harim | teṣāṃ vākyaṃ naraiḥ kāryaṃ te hi viṣṇusamā narāḥ || 10.366 ||
ityādṛto'nuśṛṇuyādbhaktiśāstrāṇi tatra ca | śrībhāgavatamatrāpi kṛṣṇalīlākathāṃ muhuḥ || 10.367 ||
atha vaiṣṇavaśāstramāhātmyam |
skānde śrībrahmanāradasaṃvāde vaiṣṇavāni tu śāstrāṇī ye śṛṇvanti paṭhanti ca | dhanyāste mānavā loke tesāṃ kṛṣṇaḥ prasīdati || 10.368 || vaiṣṇavāni tu śāstrāṇī ye rcayanti gṛhe narāḥ | sarvapāpavinirmuktā bhavanti suravanditāḥ || 10.369 || sarvasvenāpi viprendra kartavyaḥ śāstrasaṅgrahaḥ | vaiṣṇavaistu mahābhaktyā tuṣṭyarthaṃ cakrapāṇinaḥ || 10.370 || tiṣṭhate vaiṣṇavaṃ śāstraṃ likhitaṃ yasya mandire | tatra nārāyaṇo devaḥ svayaṃ vasati nārada || 10.371 || paurāṇaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ ślokaṃ ślokārdhamathavāpi ca | ślokapādaṃ paṭhedyastu gosahasraphalaṃ labhet || 10.372 || devatānāmṛṣīṇāṃ ca yogināmapi durlabham | viprendra vaiṣṇavaṃ śāstraṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ca kā kathā || 10.373 || tatraiva śrīkṛṣṇārjunasaṃvāde mama śāstrāṇi ye nityaṃ pūjayanti paṭhanti ca | te narāḥ kuruśārdūla mamātithyaṃ gatāḥ sadā || 10.374 || mama śākhyapravaktāraṃ mama śāstrānucintakam | cintayāmi na sandeho naraṃ taṃ cātmavatsadā || 10.375 ||
atha śrīmadbhāgavatamāhātmyam
tatraiva jīvitādadhikaṃ yeṣāṃ śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ kalau | na teṣāṃ bhavati kleśo yāmyaḥ kalpaśatairapi || 10.376 || dhārayanti gṛhe nityaṃ śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ hi ye | āsphoṭayanti valganti teṣāṃ prītāḥ pitāmahāḥ || 10.377 || yāvaddināni viprarṣe śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ gṛhe | tāvatpibanti pitaraḥ kṣīraṃ sarpirmadhūdakam || 10.378 || ye'rcayanti sadā gehe śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ narāḥ | prīṇitāstaiśca vibudhā yāvadāhūtasamplavam || 10.379 || yaccanti vaiṣṇave bhaktyā śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ hi ye | kalpakoṭisahasrāṇi viṣṇuloke vasanti te || 10.380 || ślokārdhaṃ ślokapādaṃ vā varaṃ bhāgavataṃ gṛhe | śataśo'tha sahasraiśca kimanyaiḥ śāstrasaṅgrahaiḥ || 10.381 || na yasya tiṣṭhate gehe śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ kalau | na tasya punarāvṛttiryāmyātpāśātkadācana || 10.382 || kathaṃ sa vaiṣṇavo jñeyaḥ śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ kalau | gṛhe na tiṣṭhate yasya sa vipraḥ śvapacādhamaḥ || 10.383 || yatra yatra bhavedvipra śāstraṃ bhāgavataṃ kalau | tatra tatra hariryāti tridaśaiḥ saha nārada || 10.384 || tatra sarvāṇi tīrthāni nadīnadasarāṃsi ca | yatra bhāgavataṃ śāstraṃ tiṣṭhate munisattama || 10.385 || tatra sarvāṇi tīrthāni sarve yajñāḥ sudakṣiṇāḥ | yatra bhāgavataṃ śāstraṃ pūjitaṃ tiṣṭhate gṛhe || 10.386 ||
kiṃ ca nityaṃ bhāgavataṃ yastu purāṇaṃ paṭhate naraḥ | pratyakṣaraṃ bhavettasya kapilādānajaṃ phalam || 10.387 || ślokārdhaṃ ślokapādaṃ vā nityaṃ bhāgavatodbhavam | paṭhetśṛṇoti vā bhaktyā gosahasraṃ phalaṃ labhet || 10.388 || yaḥ paṭhetprayato nityaṃ ślokaṃ bhāgavataṃ mune | aṣṭādaśapurāṇānāṃ phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 10.389 ||
tatraiva mārkaṇḍeyabhagīrathasaṃvāde yo hi bhāgavate śāstre vighnamācarate pumān | nābhinandati duṣṭātmā kulānāṃ pātayecchatam || 10.390 ||
pādme gautamāmbarīṣasaṃvāde ambarīṣa śukaproktaṃ nityaṃ bhāgavataṃ śṛṇu | paṭhasva svamukhenāpi yadīcchasi bhavakṣayam || 10.391 || ślokaṃ bhāgavataṃ vāpi ślokardhapādameva vā | likhitaṃ tiṣṭhate yasya gṛhe tasya sadā hariḥ | vasate nātra sandeho devadevo janārdanaḥ || 10.392 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye śrīmārkaṇḍeyendradyumnasaṃvāde śrīmadbhāgavataṃ śāstraṃ paṭhate kṛṣṇasannidhau | kulakoṭiśatairyuktaḥ krīḍate yogibhiḥ saha || 10.393 ||
gāruḍe artho'yaṃ brahmasūtrāṇāṃ bhāratārthavinirṇayaḥ | gāyatrībhāṣyarūpo'sau vedārthaparibṛṃhitaḥ || 10.394 || purāṇānāṃ sāmarūpaḥ sākṣādbhagavatoditaḥ | dvādaśaskandhayukto'yaṃ śatavicchedasaṃyutaḥ | grantho'ṣṭādaśasāhasraḥ śrīmadbhāgavatābhidhaḥ || 10.395 ||
tasminneva śrībhāgavate prathamaskandhe dharmaḥ projjhitakaitavo'tra paramo nirmatsarāṇāṃ satāṃ vedyaṃ vāstavamatra vastu śivadaṃ tāpatrayonmūlanam | śrīmadbhāgavate mahāmunikṛte kiṃ vā parairīśvaraḥ sadyo hṛdyavarudhyate'tra kṛtibhiḥ śuśrūṣubhistatkṣaṇāt || 10.396 ||
idaṃ bhāgavataṃ nāma purāṇaṃ brahmasammitam | uttamaślokacaritaṃ cakāra bhagavānṛṣiḥ || 10.397 || niḥśreyasāya lokasya dhanyaṃ svastyayanaṃ mahat | tadidaṃ grāhayāmāsasutamātmavatāṃ varam | sarvavedetihāsānāṃ sāraṃ sāraṃ samuddhṛtam || 10.398 ||
kiṃ ca kṛṣṇe svadhāmopagate dharmajñānādibhiḥ saha | kalau naṣṭadṛśāmeṣa purāṇārko'dhunoditaḥ || 10.399 ||
kiṃ ca anarthopaśamaṃ sākṣādbhaktiyogamadhokṣaje | lokasyājānato vidvāṃścakre sātvatasaṃhitām || 10.400 || yasyāṃ vai śrūyamāṇāyāṃ kṛṣṇe paramapūruṣe | bhaktirutpadyate puṃsaḥ śokamohabhayāpahā || 10.401 || dvitīye śrīśukoktau pariniṣṭhito'pi nairguṇya uttamaślokalīlayā | gṛhītacetā rājarṣe ākhyānaṃ yadadhītavān || 10.402 || rājante tāvadanyāni purāṇāni satāṃ gaṇe | yāvadbhāgavataṃ naiva śrūyate'mṛtasāgaram || 10.403 ||
dvādaśe ca rājante tāvadanyāni purāṇāni satāṃ gaṇe | yāvadbhāgavataṃ naiva śrūyate'mṛtasāgaraḥ || 10.404 || sarvavedāntasāraṃ hi śrībhāgavatamiṣyate | tadrasāmṛtatṛptasya nānyatra syādratiḥ kvacit || 10.405 || nimnagānāṃ yathā gaṅgā devānāmacyuto yathā | vaiṣṇavānāṃ yathā śambhuḥ purāṇānāmidaṃ tathā || 10.406 ||
śrīmadbhāgavataṃ purāṇamamalaṃ yadvaiṣṇavānāṃ priyaṃ yasmin pāramahaṃsyamekamamalaṃ jñānaṃ paraṃ gīyate | tatra jñānavirāgabhaktisahitaṃ naiṣkarmyamāviṣkṛtaṃ tacchṛṇvan supaṭhan vicāraṇaparo bhaktyā vimucyennaraḥ || 10.407 ||
ataevoktaṃ nigamakalpatarorgalitaṃ phalaṃ śukamukhādamṛtadravasaṃyutam | pibata bhāgavataṃ rasamālayaṃ muhuraho rasikā bhuvi bhāvukāḥ || 10.408 ||
kiṃ ca yaḥ svānubhāvamakhilaśrutisāramekam adhyātmadīpamatititīrṣatāṃ tamo'ndham | saṃsāriṇāṃ karuṇayāha purāṇaguhyaṃ taṃ vyāsasūnumupayāmi guruṃ munīnām || 10.409 ||
atha śrībhagavacchāstravaktṛmāhātmyam
bhagavaddharmavaktāraṃ bhagavacchāstravācakam | vaiṣṇavaṃ guruvadbhaktyā pūjayejjñānadāyakam || 10.410 ||
nāradapañcarātre ṛṣīn prati śrīśāṇḍilyoktau vaiṣṇavaṃ jñānavaktāraṃ yo vidyādviṣṇuvadgurum | pūjayedvāṅmanaḥkāyaiḥ sa śāstrajñaḥ sa vaiṣṇavaḥ || 10.411 || ślokapādasya vaktāpi yaḥ pūjyaḥ sa sadaiva hi | kiṃ punarbhagavadviṣṇoḥ svarūpaṃ vitanoti yaḥ || 10.412 ||
kiṃ ca nārāyaṇaḥ paraṃ brahma tajjñānenātha gamyate | jñānasya sādhanaṃ śāstraṃ śāstraṃ ca guruvaktṛgam || 10.413 || brahma prāptirato hetorgurvadhīnā sadaiva hi | hetunānena vai viprā gururgurutaraḥ smṛtaḥ || 10.414 || yasmāddevo jagannāthaḥ kṛtvā maryamayīṃ tanum | magnānuddharate lokān kāruṇyācchāstrapāṇinā || 10.415 || tasmādbhaktirgurau kāryā saṃsārabhayabhīruṇā | śāstrajñānena yo'jñānaṃ timiraṃ vinipātayet || 10.416 || śāstraṃ pāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ pavitraṃ bhogamokṣadam | śāntidaṃ ca mahārthaṃ ca vakti yaḥ sa jagadguruḥ || 10.417 ||
atha śrīkṛṣṇalīlākathāśravaṇamāhātmyam tatra pāpādiśodhakatvam
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde teṣāṃ kṣīṇaṃ mahatpāpaṃ varṣakoṭiśatodbhavam | viprendra nāsti sandeho ye śṛṇvanti hareḥ kathām || 10.418 ||
tatraivānyatra sarvāśramābhigamanaṃ sarvatīrthāvagāhanam | na tathā pāraṇaṃ nṝṇāṃ nārāyaṇakathā yathā || 10.419 ||
bṛhannāradīye yajñadhvajopākhyānānte aho harikathā loke pāpaghnī puṇyadāyinī | śṛṇvatāṃ vadatāṃ caiva tadbhaktānāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 10.420 ||
prathamaskandhe śṛṇvatāṃ svakathāḥ kṛṣṇaḥ puṇyaśravaṇakīrtanaḥ | hṛdyantaḥstho hyabhadrāṇi vidhunoti suhṛtsatām || 10.421 ||
ekādaśe ca devastutau śuddhirnṛṇāṃ na tu tatheḍya durāśayānāṃ vidyāśrutādhyayanadānatapaḥkriyābhiḥ | sattvātmanāmṛṣabha te yaśasi pravṛddha sacchraddhayā śravaṇasambhṛtayā yathā syāt || 10.422 ||
daśame śrībādarāyaṇiṃ prati śrīparīkṣiduktau naiṣātiduḥsahā kṣunmāṃ tyaktodamapi bādhate | pibantaṃ tvanmukhāmbhoja cyutaṃ harikathāmṛtam || 10.423 ||
skānde ca tatraiva śrīpradaṃ viṣṇucaritaṃ sarvopadravanāśanam | sarvaduḥkhopaśamanaṃ duṣṭagrahanivāraṇam || 10.424 ||
atha prakarṣeṇa sarvamaṅgalakāritvam
tatraiva śrotavyaṃ sādhucaritaṃ yaśodharmajayārthibhiḥ | pāpakṣayārthaṃ devarṣe svargārthaṃ dharmabuddhibhiḥ || 10.425 || āyuṣyāmārogyakaraṃ yaśasyaṃ puṇyavardhanam | caritaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ nityaṃ śrotavyaṃ sādhubuddhinā || 10.426 || kuṭumbavṛddhiṃ vijayaṃ śatrunāśaṃ balam | karoti viṣṇucaritaṃ sarvakālaphalapradam || 10.427 ||
prathamaskandhe dharmaḥ svanuṣṭhitaḥ puṃsāṃ viṣvaksenakathāsu yaḥ | notpādayedyadi ratiṃ śrama eva hi kevalam || 10.428 ||
tṛtīye śrīviduramaitreyasaṃvāde ekāntalābhaṃ vacaso nu puṃsāṃ suślokamaulerguṇavādamāhuḥ | śruteśca vidvadbhirupākṛtāyāṃ kathāsudhāyāmupasamprayogam || 10.429 ||
dvitīye śrīśaunakoktau āyurharati vai puṃsāmudyannastaṃ ca yannasau | tasyarte yatkṣaṇo nīta uttamaślokavārtayā || 10.430 ||
tṛtīye śrīviduroktau sā śraddadhānasya vivardhamānā viraktimanyatra karoti puṃsaḥ | hareḥ padānusmṛtinirvṛtasya samastaduḥkhāpyayamāśu dhatte || 10.431 ||
caturthe śrīpṛthucaritānte śrīmaitreyoktau chinnānyadhīradhigatātmagatirnirīhas tattatyaje ñcchinadidaṃ vayunena yena | tāvanna yogagatibhiryatirapramatto yāvadgadāgrajakathāsu ratiṃ na kuryāt || 10.432 ||
ekādaśe ca śrībhagavantaṃ pratyuddhavavākye tava vikrīḍitaṃ kṛṣṇa nṛṇāṃ paramamaṅgalam | karṇapīyūṣamāsādya tyajantyanyaspṛhāṃ janāḥ || 10.433 ||
caturthe pracetasaḥ prati śrībhagavaduktau gṛheṣvāviśatāṃ cāpi puṃsāṃ kuśalakarmaṇām | madvārtāyātayāmānāṃ na bandhāya gṛhā matāḥ || 10.434 ||
skānde tatraiva dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ yadiṣṭaṃ ca nṝṇāmiha | tatsarvaṃ labhate vatsa kathāṃ śrutvā hareḥ sadā || 10.435 ||
dvādaśe ca śrīśukoktau saṃsārasindhumatidustaramuttitīrṣor nānyaḥ plavo bhagavato puruṣottamasya | līlākathārasaniṣevanamantareṇa puṃso bhavedvividhaduḥkhadavārditasya || 10.436 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye nityaṃ kṛṣṇakathā yasya prāṇādapi garīyasī | na tasya durlabhaṃ kiñcidiha loke paratra ca || 10.437 ||
dvitīyaskandhe jñānaṃ yadāpratinivṛttaguṇormicakram ātmaprasāda uta yatra guṇeṣvasaṅgaḥ | kaivalyasammatapathastvatha bhaktiyogaḥ ko nirvṛto harikathāsu ratiṃ na kuryāt || 10.438 ||
daśamaskandhe śrutistutau duravagamātmatattvanigamāya tavāttatanoś caritamahāmṛtābdhiparivartapariśramaṇāḥ | na parilaṣanti kecidapavargamapīśvara te caraṇasarojahaṃsakulasaṅgavisṛṣṭagṛhāḥ || 10.439 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīkapiladevahūtisaṃvāde naikātmatāṃ me spṛhayanti kecin matpādasevābhiratā madīhāḥ | ye'nyonyato bhāgavatāḥ prasajya sabhājayante mama pauruṣāṇi || 10.440 ||
dvitīye śrīsūtoktau pibanti ye bhagavata ātmanaḥ satāṃ kathāmṛtaṃ śravaṇapuṭeṣu sambhṛtam punanti te viṣayavidūṣitāśayaṃ vrajanti taccaraṇasaroruhāntikam || 10.441 ||
tṛtīye kapiladevastutau pānena te deva kathāsudhāyāḥ pravṛddhabhaktyā viśadāśayā ye | vairāgyasāraṃ pratilabhya bodhaṃ yathāñjasānvīyurakuṇṭhadhiṣṇyam || 10.442 ||
skānde amṛtasāroddhāre śrīyamasya dūtānuśāsane ye śṛṇvanti kathāṃ viṣṇorye paṭhanti hareḥ kathām | kalāyutaṃ nāvalokyaṃ gatāste brahma śāśvatam || 10.443 || yasya viṣṇukathālāpairnityaṃ pramuditaṃ manaḥ | tasya na cyavate lakṣmīstatpadaṃ ca kare sthitam || 10.444 ||
dvādaśe yattūttamaḥślokaguṇānuvādaḥ saṅgīyate'bhīkṣṇamamaṅgalaghnaḥ | tameva nityaṃ śṛṇuyādabhīkṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇe'malāṃ bhaktimabhīpsamānaḥ || 10.445 ||
skānde yatra yatra mahīpāla vaiṣṇavī vartate kathā | tatra tatra hariryāti gauryathā sutavatsalā || 10.446 ||
śrīviṣṇudharme śrībhagavaduktau, skānde ca śrībhagavadarjunasaṃvāde matkathāvācakaṃ nityaṃ matkathāśravaṇe ratam | matkathāprītamanasaṃ nāhaṃ tyakṣyāmi taṃ naram || 10.447 ||
daśamaskandhe brahmastutau jñāne prayāsamudapāsya namanta eva jīvanti sanmukharitāṃ bhavadīyavārtām | sthāne sthitāḥ śrutigatāṃ tanuvāṅmanobhir ye prāyaśo ñjita jito'pyasi taistrilokyām || 10.448 ||
tṛtīye śrīsanakādistutau nātyantikaṃ vigaṇayantyapi te prasādaṃ kiṃ vānyadarpitabhayaṃ bhruva unnayaiste | ye'ṅga tvadaṅghriśaraṇā bhavataḥ kathāyāḥ kīrtanyatīrthayaśasaḥ kuśalā rasajñāḥ || 10.449 ||
caturthe śrībhagavantaṃ prati siddhānāṃ stutau ayaṃ tvatkathāmṛṣṭapīyūṣanadyāṃ manovāraṇaḥ kleśadāvāgnidagdhaḥ | tṛṣārto'vagāḍho na sasmāra dāvaṃ na niṣkrāmati brahmasampannavannaḥ || 10.450 ||
ataevoktaṃ prathamaskandhe śrīśaunakādibhiḥ vayaṃ tu na vitṛpyāma uttamaślokavikrame | yacchṛṇvatāṃ rasajñānāṃ svādu svādu pade pade || 10.451 ||
kiṃ ca ko nāma tṛpyedrasavitkathāyāṃ mahattamaikāntaparāyaṇasya | nāntaṃ guṇānāmaguṇasya jagmur yogeśvarā ye bhavapādmamukhyāḥ || 10.452 ||
tṛtīye śrīvidureṇa krīḍan vidhatte dvijagosurāṇāṃ kṣemāya karmāṇyavatārabhedaiḥ | mano na tṛpyatyapi śṛṇvatāṃ naḥ suślokamauleścaritāmṛtāni || 10.453 ||
daśamaskandhe ca śrīparīkṣitā brahman kṛṣṇakathāḥ puṇyā mādhvīrlokamalāpahāḥ | ko nu tṛpyeta śṛṇvānaḥ śrutajño nityanūtanaḥ || 10.454 ||
ato hi śrīpṛthurājena prārthitaṃ na kāmaye nātha tadapyahaṃ kvacin na yatra yuṣmaccaraṇāmbujāsavaḥ | mahattamāntarhṛdayānmukhacyuto vidhatsva karṇāyutameṣa me varaḥ || 10.455 ||
ataeva niścayoktaṃ pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye ambarīṣaṃ prati śrīnāradena nātaḥ paraṃ paramatoṣaviśeṣapoṣaṃ paśyāmi puṇyamucitaṃ ca paraspareṇa | santaḥ prasahya yadanantaguṇānananta śreyonidhīnadhikabhāvabhujo bhajanti || 10.456 ||
prathamaskandhe śrīsūtena yā yāḥ kathā bhagavataḥ kathanīyorukarmaṇaḥ | guṇakarmāśrayāḥ pumbhiḥ saṃsevyāstā bubhūṣubhiḥ || 10.457 ||
daśamaskandhaśeṣe ca śrībādarāyaṇinā itthaṃ parasya nijavartmarirakṣayātta līlātanostadanurūpaviḍambanāni | karmāṇi karmakaṣaṇāni yadūttamasya śrūyādamuṣya padayoranuvṛttimicchan || 10.458 ||
atha śrībhagavatkathātyāgādidoṣaḥ
ataḥ kṛṣṇakathāyāṃ tu satyāmanyakathāśrutim | tadaśrutiṃ ca vaimukhyaṃ tasyāṃ tṛptimapi tyajet || 10.459 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe kapiladevahūtisaṃvāde nūnaṃ daivena vihatā ye cācyutakathāsudhām | hitvā śṛṇvantyasadgāthāḥ purīṣamiva viḍbhujaḥ || 10.460 ||
tatraiva śrīvaikuṇṭhavarṇane yanna vrajantyaghabhido racanānuvādāc chṛṇvanti ye'nyaviṣayāḥ kukathā matighnīḥ | yāstu śrutā hatabhagairnṛbhirāttasārās tāṃstān kṣipantyaśaraṇeṣu tamaḥsu hanta || 10.461 ||
kiṃ ca skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde vācyamānaṃ tu ye śāstraṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ puruṣādhamāḥ | na śṛṇvanti muniśreṣṭha teṣāṃ svāmī sadā yamaḥ || 10.462 || na śṛṇvanti na hṛṣyanti vaiṣṇavīṃ prāpya ye kathām | dhama āyuryaśo dharmaḥ santānaścaiva naśyati || 10.463 || na śṛṇoti hareryastu kathāṃ pāpapraṇāśinīm | acirādeva devarṣe samūlaṃ tu vinaśyati || 10.464 ||
dvitīyaskandhe śrīśaunakoktau bile batorukramavikramān ye na śṛṇvataḥ karṇapuṭe narasya | jihvāsatī dārdurikeva sūta na copagāyatyurugāyagāthāḥ || 10.465 ||
tṛtīye śrībrahmastutau daivena te hatadhiyo bhavataḥ prasaṅgāt sarvāśubhopaśamanādvimukhendriyā ye | kurvanti kāmasukhaleśalavāya dīnā lobhābhibhūtamanaso'kuśalāni śaśvat || 10.466 ||
tāñchocyaśocyānavido'nuśoce hareḥ kathāyāṃ vimukhānaghena | kṣiṇoti devo'nimiṣastu yeṣām āyurvṛthāvādagatismṛtīnām || 10.467 ||
śrīmaitreyoktau ca ko nāma loke puruṣārthasāravit purākathānāṃ bhagavatkathāsudhām | āpīya karṇāñjalibhirbhavāpahām aho virajyeta vinā naretaram || 10.468 ||
caturthe śrīpṛthustutau yaśaḥ śivaṃ suśrava āryasaṅgame yadṛcchayā copaśṛṇoti te sakṛt | kathaṃ guṇajño viramedvinā paśuṃ śrīryatpravavre guṇasaṅgrahecchayā || 10.469 ||
daśamārambhe śrīparīkṣitpraśne nivṛttatarṣairupagīyamānād bhavauṣadhācchrotramano'bhirāmāt | ka uttamaślokaguṇānuvādāt pumān virajyeta vinā paśughnāt || 10.470 ||
ataevoktaṃ devaiḥ pañcamaskandhe na yatra vaikuṇṭhakathāsudhāpagā na sādhavo bhāgavatāstadāśrayāḥ | na yatra yajñeśamakhā mahotsavāḥ sureśaloko'pi na vai sa sevyatām || 10.471 ||
atha bhagavatkathāsaktiḥ
ato niṣevyamāṇāṃ ca sarvathā bhagavatkathām | muhustadrasikān pṛcchenmitho modavivṛddhaye || 10.471 ||
daśamaskandhe satāmayaṃ sārabhṛtāṃ nisargo yadarthavāṇīśruticetasāmapi | pratikṣaṇaṃ navyavadacyutasya yat striyā viṭānāmiva sādhu vārtā || 10.473 ||
ataeva tatraiva tulyaśrutatapaḥśīlāstulyasvīyārimadhyamāḥ | api cakruḥ pravacanamekaṃ śuśrūṣavo'pare || 10.474 || iti |
atha śrībhagavaddharmapratipādanamāhātmyam
tathā vaiṣṇavadharmāṃśca kriyamānāmapi svayam | sampṛcchettadvidaḥ sādhūnanyonyaprītivṛddhaye || 10.475 || śraddhayā bhagavaddharmān vaiṣṇavāyānupṛcchate | avaśyaṃ kathayedvidvānanyathā doṣabhāgbhavet || 10.476 ||
taduktam nākhyāti vaiṣṇavaṃ dharmaṃ viṣṇubhaktasya pṛcchataḥ | kalau bhāgavato bhūtvā puṇyaṃ yāti śatābdikam || 10.477 ||
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde vaiṣṇave vaiṣṇavaṃ dharmaṃ yo dadāti dvijottamaḥ | sasāgaramahīdāne yatphalaṃ labhate'dhikam || 10.478 ||
kiṃ ca tatraiva ajñānāya ca yo jñānaṃ dadyāddharmopadeśanam | kṛtsnāṃ vā pṛthivīṃ dadyāttena tulyaṃ hi tatsmṛtam || 10.479 ||
viṣṇudharmottare tatkathāṃ śrāvayedyastu tadbhaktānmānavottamaḥ | godānaphalamāpnoti sa narastena karmaṇā || 10.480 ||
pādme devadūtavikuṇḍalasaṃvāde jñānamajñāya yo dadyādvedaśāstrasamudbhavam | api devāstamarcanti bhavabandhavidārakam || 10.481 ||
bṛhannāradīye satsaṅgadevārcanasatkathāsu hitopadeśe nirato manuṣyaḥ | prayāti viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ yad dehāvasāne'cyutatulyatejāḥ || 10.482 ||
atha bhagavaddharmāḥ
te ca śrībhagavaddharmā bhagavadbhaktalakṣaṇaiḥ | vyañjitāḥ katicinmukhyā likhyante'tra pare'pi te || 10.483 || te tu yadyapi vikhyātāḥ śrīmadbhāgavatādiṣu | tathāpi yatnādekatra saṅgṛhyante samādhanāḥ || 10.484 ||
te coktāḥ kāśīkhaṇḍe dvārakāmāhātmye candraśarmaṇā adya prabhṛti kartavyaṃ yanmayā kṛṣṇa tacchṛṇu | ekādaśyāṃ na bhoktavyaṃ kartavyo jāgaraḥ sadā || 10.485 || mahotsavaḥ prakartavyaḥ pratyahapūjanaṃ tava | palārdhenāpi biddhaṃ tu bhoktavyaṃ vāsarāntare || 10.486 || tvatprītyāṣṭau mayā kāryā dvādaśyo vratasaṃyutā | bhaktirbhāgavatī kāryā prāṇairapi dhanairapi || 10.487 || nityaṃ nāmasahasraṃ tu paṭhanīyaṃ tava priyam | pūjā tu tulasīpatrairmayā kāryā sadaiva hi || 10.488 || tulasīkāṣṭhāsambhūtā mālā kāryā sadā mayā | nṛtyagītaṃ prakartavyaṃ samprāpte jāgare tava || 10.489 || tulasīkāṣṭhāsambhūtacandanena vilepanam | kariṣyāmi tavāgre ca guṇānāṃ tava kīrtanam || 10.490 || mathurāyāṃ prakartavyaṃ pratyabdaṃ gamanaṃ mayā | tatkathāśravaṇaṃ kāryaṃ tathā pustakavācanam || 10.491 || nityaṃ pādodakaṃ mūrdhnā mayā dhāryaṃ prayatnataḥ | naivedyabhakṣaṇaṃ cāpi kariṣyāmi yatavrataḥ || 10.492 || nirmālyaṃ śirasā dhāryaṃ tvadīyaṃ sādaraṃ mayā | tava dattvā yadiṣṭaṃ tu bhakṣaṇīyaṃ mudā mayā || 10.493 || tathā tathā prakartavyaṃ tava tuṣṭiḥ prajāyate | satyametanmayā kṛṣṇa tavāgre parikīrtanam || 10.494 ||
saptamaskandhe śrīprahlādena guruśuśrūṣayā bhaktyā sarvalabdhārpaṇena ca | śraddhayā tatkathāyāṃ ca kīrtanairguṇakarmaṇām | tatpādāmburuhadhyānāttalliṅgekṣārhaṇādibhiḥ || 10.495 || hariḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu bhagavānāsta īśvaraḥ | iti bhūtāni manasā kāmaistaiḥ sādhu mānayet || 10.496 ||
ekādaśe ca śrīkaviyogeśvareṇa ye vai bhagavatā proktā upāyā hyātmalabdhaye | añjaḥ puṃsāmaviduṣāṃ viddhi bhāgavatān hi tān || 10.497 ||
tatraiva prabuddhayogeśvareṇa sarvato manaso'saṅgamādau saṅgaṃ ca sādhuṣu | dayāṃ maitrīṃ praśrayaṃ ca bhūteṣvaddhā yathocitam || 10.498 || śaucaṃ tapastitikṣāṃ ca maunaṃ svādhyāyamārjavam | brahmacaryamahiṃsāṃ ca samatvaṃ dvandvasaṃjñayoḥ || 10.499 || sarvatrātmeśvarānvīkṣāṃ kaivalyamaniketatām | viviktacīravasanaṃ santoṣaṃ yena kenacit || 10.500 || śraddhāṃ bhāgavate śāstre'nindāmanyatra cāpi hi | manovākkarmadaṇḍaṃ ca satyaṃ śamadamāvapi || 10.501 || śravaṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ dhyānaṃ hareradbhutakarmaṇaḥ | janmakarmaguṇānāṃ ca tadarthe'khilaceṣṭitam || 10.502 || iṣṭaṃ dattaṃ tapo japtaṃ vṛttaṃ yaccātmanaḥ priyam | dārān sutān gṛhān prāṇān yatparasmai nivedanam || 10.503 || evaṃ kṛṣṇātmanātheṣu manuṣyeṣu ca sauhṛdam | paricaryāṃ cobhayatra mahatsu nṛṣu sādhuṣu || 10.504 || parasparānukathanaṃ pāvanaṃ bhagavadyaśaḥ mitho ratirmithastuṣṭirnivṛttirmitha ātmanaḥ || 10.505 ||
śrībhagavatā ca malliṅgamadbhaktajana darśanasparśanārcanam | paricaryā stutiḥ prahva guṇakarmānukīrtanam || 10.506 || matkathāśravaṇe śraddhā madanudhyānamuddhava | sarvalābhopaharaṇaṃ dāsyenātmanivedanam || 10.507 || majjanmakarmakathanaṃ mama parvānumodanam | gītatāṇḍavavāditra goṣṭhībhirmadgṛhotsavaḥ || 10.508 || yātrā balividhānaṃ ca sarvavārṣikaparvasu | vaidikī tāntrikī dīkṣā madīyavratadhāraṇam || 10.509 || mamārcāsthāpane śraddhā svataḥ saṃhatya codyamaḥ | udyānopavanākrīḍa puramandirakarmaṇi || 10.510 || sammārjanopalepābhyāṃ sekamaṇḍalavartanaiḥ | gṛhaśuśrūṣaṇaṃ mahyaṃ dāsavadyadamāyayā || 10.511 || amānitvamadambhitvaṃ kṛtasyāparikīrtanam | api dīpāvalokaṃ me nopayuñjyānniveditam || 10.512 || yadyadiṣṭatamaṃ loke yaccātipriyamātmanaḥ | tattannivedayenmahyaṃ tadānantyāya kalpate || 10.513 ||
kiṃ ca śraddhāmṛtakathāyāṃ me śaśvanmadanukīrtanam | pariniṣṭhā ca pūjāyāṃ stutibhiḥ stavanaṃ mama || 10.514 || ādaraḥ paricaryāyāṃ sarvāṅgairabhivandanam | madbhaktapūjābhyadhikā sarvabhūteṣu manmatiḥ || 10.515 || madartheṣvaṅgaceṣṭā ca vacasā madguṇeraṇam | mayyarpaṇaṃ ca manasaḥ sarvakāmavivarjanam || 10.516 || madarthe'rthaparityāgo bhogasya ca sukhasya ca | iṣṭaṃ dattaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ madarthaṃ yadvrataṃ tapaḥ || 10.517 ||
api cāgre kuryātsarvāṇi karmāṇi madarthaṃ śanakaiḥ smaran | mayyarpitamanaścitto maddharmātmamanoratiḥ || 10.518 || deśān puṇyānāśrayeta madbhaktaiḥ sādhubhiḥ śritān | devāsuramanuṣyeṣu madbhaktācaritāni ca || 10.519 || pṛthaksatreṇa vā mahyaṃ parvayātrāmahotsavān | kārayedgītanṛtyādyairmahārājavibhūtibhiḥ || 10.520 || māmeva sarvabhūteṣu bahirantarapāvṛtam | īkṣetātmani cātmānaṃ yathā khamamalāśayaḥ || 10.521 ||
atha śrībhagavaddharmamāhātmyam
uktaṃ ca saptamaskandhe śrīprahlādena evaṃ nirjitaṣaḍvargaiḥ kriyate bhaktirīśvare | vāsudeve bhagavati yayā saṃlabhyate ratiḥ || 10.522 ||
ekādaśe śrīnāradena śruto'nupaṭhito dhyāta ādṛto vānumoditaḥ sadyaḥ punāti saddharmo devaviśvadruho'pi hi || 10.523 ||
tatraiva śrīkaviyogeśvareṇa yānāsthāya naro rājanna pramādyeta karhicit | dhāvannimīlya vā netre na skhalenna patediha || 10.524 ||
śrīprabuddhayogeśvareṇa iti bhāgavatān dharmān śikṣan bhaktyā tadutthayā | nārāyaṇaparo māyāmañjastarati dustarām || 10.525 ||
śrībhagavatā ca evaṃ dharmairmanuṣyāṇāmuddhavātmanivedinām | mayi sañjāyate bhaktiḥ ko'nyo'rtho'syāvaśiṣyate || 10.526 ||
kiṃ cāgre na hyaṅgopakrame dhvaṃso maddharmasyoddhavāṇvapi mayā vyavasitaḥ samyaṅnirguṇatvādanāśiṣaḥ || 10.527 || iti
alābhe satsabhāyāstu śuśrūṣuṃ ca nijālaye | devālaye vā śāstrajñaḥ kīrtayedbhagavatkathām || 10.528 ||
atha śrībhagavallīlākathākīrtanamāhātmyam |
uktaṃ ca skānde śrībhagavatā arjunaṃ prati matkathā kurute yastu vaiṣṇavānāṃ sadāgrataḥ | iha bhogānavāpnoti tathā mokṣaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 10.529 ||
prathamaskandhe śrīnāradena idaṃ hi puṃsastapasaḥ śrutasya vā sviṣṭasya sūktasya ca buddhidattayoḥ | avicyuto'rthaḥ kavibhirnirūpito yaduttamaślokaguṇānuvarṇanam || 10.530 ||
kiṃ ca etaddhyāturacittānāṃ mātrāsparśecchayā muhuḥ | bhavasindhuplavo dṛṣṭo haricaryānuvarṇanam || 10.531 ||
ekādaśe śrīśukenāpi itthaṃ harerbhagavato rucirāvatāra vīryāṇi bālacaritāni ca śantamāni | anyatra ceha ca śrutāni gṛṇanmanuṣyo bhaktiṃ parāṃ paramahaṃsagatau labheta || 10.532 ||
ataeva śrīprahlādena nṛsiṃhastutāvuktam so'haṃ priyasya suhṛdaḥ paradevatāyā līlākathāstava nṛsiṃha viriñcagītāḥ | añjastitarmyanugṛṇan guṇavipramukto durgāṇi te padayugālayahaṃsasaṅgaḥ || 10.533 ||
gopikābhirapi gītam tava kathāmṛtaṃ taptajīvanaṃ kavibhirīḍitaṃ kalmaṣāpaham | śravaṇamaṅgalaṃ śrīmadātataṃ bhuvi grṇanti te bhuridā janāḥ || 10.534 ||
kirtane'pyatra tajjñeyaṃ māhātmyaṃ śravaṇe'sya yat | sidhyati śravaṇaṃ nūnaṃ kīrtanātsvayameva hi || 10.535 || śāstrābhyāsasya cābhāve pūrveṣāṃ lokaviśrutām | satāmādhunikānāṃ ca kathāṃ bandhuṣu kīrtayet || 10.536 ||
iti śrīgopālabhaṭṭavilikhite śrībhagavadbhaktivilāse satsaṅgamo nāma daśamo vilāsaḥ
11. ṇityakrtyasamapanaVilasa
ekādaśavilāsaḥ nityakṛtyasamāpanaḥ
śrīcaitanyaṃ prapadye taṃ mahāścaryaprabhāvakam | prasāde yasya duṣṭo'pi bhagavadbhaktimān bhavet || 11.1 || tato dināntyabhāgeṣu bāhyeṣu surasadmasu | yātrāṃ kṛtvā dvijaḥ sandhyāmupāsīta yathāvidhi || 11.2 ||
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe aurvasagarasaṃvāde dināntasandhyāṃ sūryeṇa pūrvā mṛkṣairyutāṃ budhaḥ | upatiṣṭhedyathānyāyaṃ samyagācamya pārthiva || 11.3 || sarvakālamupasthānaṃ sandhyāyāḥ pārthiveṣyate | anyatra sūtikāśaucavibhramāturabhītitaḥ || 11.4 || upatiṣṭhanti vai sandhyāṃ ye na pūrvāṃ na paścimām | vrajanti te durātmānastāmisraṃ narakaṃ nṛpa || 11.5 || iti |
tato yathāśramācāraṃ karma sāyantanaṃ kṛtī | nirvartya pūrvavatkuryādbhaktyā bhagavadarcanam || 11.6 || śrīkṛṣṇabhaktyāsaktyā tu sandhyopāsyādikaṃ yadi | patetkarma na pātityadoṣaśaṅkā kathañcana || 11.7 ||
atha śrībhagavadbhaktānāṃ karmapātityaparihāraḥ
pādme śrībhagavaduktau matkarma kurvatāṃ puṃsāṃ kriyālopo bhavedyadi | teṣāṃ karmāṇi kurvanti tisraḥ koṭyo maharṣayaḥ || 11.8 ||
ādipurāṇe ca smaranti mama nāmāni ye tyaktvā karma cākhilam | teṣāṃ karmāṇi kurvanti ṛṣayo bhagavatparāḥ || 11.9 || iti |
mṛduśraddhasya bhaktasya prauḍhatāmanupeyuṣaḥ | kiṃcitkarmādhikāritvātkarmāsyaitatprapañcitam || 11.10 || prauḍhaśraddhasya bhaktasyakarmasvanadhikārataḥ | pātityaṃ na bhavatyeva lekhanīyaṃ tadagrataḥ || 11.11 || kiṃciddhyānādibhedena trisandhyaṃ ca pṛthakpṛthak | proktaḥ pūjāvidhiḥ prājñaistattatkāmāśusiddhaye || 11.12 ||
atha trikālārcanavidhiviśeṣataḥ
śrīgautamīyatantre ārādhanavidhiṃ vakṣye prātaḥkāle viśeṣataḥ | varaṃ vṛndāvanaṃ dhyāyetpuṇyavṛkṣādisevitam || 11.13 || punnāgairnāgavṛkṣaiśca panasaiścaiva kāñcanaiḥ | bakulaiścaiva bilvaiśca vanyaiḥ kurvakairapi || 11.14 || sarvartukusumopetaiḥ puṣpāvanataśākhibhiḥ | tanmadhye pulinaṃ dhyāyedbahupuṣpakacampakam || 11.15 || dhūpadipairvitānena puṣpamālāvibhūṣitam | muktādāmapatākābhirvanyapuṣpairalaṅkṛtam || 11.16 || tanmadhye kalpavṛkṣasya cchāyāyāṃ paṅkajāsane | susthitaṃ veṇugītāḍhyaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 11.17 || vanamālāparivṛtaṃ gopikāśataveṣṭitam | devāsuraiśca siddhaiśca gandharvairapsarogaṇaiḥ || 11.18 || yakṣairvidyādharagaṇairvihagairbhuvi saṃsthitaiḥ | brahmarṣibhiḥ stūyamānaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ caiva śucismitam || 11.19 || nānāvidhaiśca gopālairmṛgapakṣivibhūṣitam | lelihyamānaṃ praṇayātpaśūnāṃ śatakoṭibhiḥ || 11.20 || indīvaranibhaṃ divyaṃ sundaraṃ tvindirālayam | sampūrṇacandravadanaṃ padmapatranibhekṣaṇam || 11.21 || padmābhapāṇipādaṃ ca padmarāgavarārcitam | śaraṇyaṃ sarvalokānāṃ gopīnāṃ prāṇavallabham || 11.22 || evaṃ dhyātvārcayennityaṃ ṣoḍaśenopacārataḥ | dugdhaṃ ca dadhikhaṇḍena sahitaṃ saṃnivedayet || 11.23 || sauvarṇapātre gopānāṃ grāsaṃ kāṃsye nivedayet | evaṃ samarcayedbhaktyā japanmantraṃ samāhitaḥ || 11.24 || madhyāhne sampravakṣyāmi pūjāṃ sarvārthasiddhidām | sauvarṇaparvate mūle dhātubhiḥ samalaṅkṛte || 11.25 || puṇyavṛkṣasamākīrṇe puṇyapakṣininādite | padmotpalādisaṅkīrṇe vāpībhiḥ samalaṅkṛte || 11.26 || tasmin satpuline ramye chāyāyāṃ paṅkajāsane | sauvarṇamaṇḍape samyagvitānādivibhūṣite || 11.27 || mālādiracite ramye maṇibhiḥ puṣpaśobhitaiḥ | suvarṇaratnasandohairantarāntaraśobhite || 11.28 || siṃhāsane samāsīnaṃ viśrāntaṃ kaṃsasūdanam | muktāmayaiḥ surucirairhārairdāmavibhūṣitam || 11.29 || dhyātvā samyagviśuddhātmā jātīpuṣpaiḥ samarcayet | mahārajatapātre tu naivedyānnaṃ nivedayet || 11.30 || dadyādgrāsaṃ sakhīnāṃ ca gopīnāṃ vijitendriyaḥ | devakīparamānandamevaṃ dhyāyetsukhāsanam || 11.31 || rātripūjāvidhiṃ vakṣye rukmiṇīvallabhasya ca | adhastātkalpavṛkṣasya sarvapuṣpaphalasya ca || 11.32 || ratnamaṇḍapamadhyasthaṃ divyapītāmbaraṃ harim | divyacandanaliptāṅgaṃ divyābharaṇabhūṣitam || 11.33 || anekadivyamālābhirmaṇḍitaṃ paṅkajekṣaṇam | ratnamaṇḍapamadhyasthaṃ sundaraṃ sundarasmitam || 11.34 || śobhayantaṃ svavapuṣā sarvalokānnijaśriyā | gopījanānāṃ hṛdayavallabhaṃ proktavarcasam || 11.35 || sugandhipuṣpairārādhyaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ sarvanāyakam | rājate tu payaḥ śuddhaṃ pakvaṃ pātre nivedayet || 11.36 || evamabhyarcya manasā japenmantraṃ samāhitaḥ | kālatrayārcane caiva sahasraṃ sāṣṭakaṃ japet | eṣa nityakramaḥ proktaḥ kṛṣṇamantrasya sūribhiḥ || 11.37 ||
tatraivādau saṃkṣiptatrikālapūjoktyanantaram manasā vā samabhyarcya triṣu sandhyāsu saṃyamī | pratyahaṃ tu japenmantramaṣṭottarasahasrakam | asāmarthye japenmantraṃ nityamaṣṭaśataṃ tathā || 11.38 || iti |
atha naktaṃ kṛtyāni
tato yathāsampradāyaṃ homaṃ niṣpādya vaiṣṇavaḥ | gītanṛtyādikaṃ bhaktyā vidhāya prārthayetprabhum || 11.39 ||
tathā coktam balīyasā padā svāmin padavīmavadhāraya | āgaccha śayanasthānaṃ priyābhiḥ saha keśava || 11.40 || iti |
evaṃ prārthya samarpyāsmai pāduke śayanālayam | ānīya devaṃ tatratyānupacārān prakalpayet || 11.41 || viśeṣato'rpayettatra ghanaṃ dugdhaṃ saśarkaram | tāmbūlaṃ ca sakarpūraṃ divyamālyānulepanam || 11.42 || itthaṃ bhaktyā samārādhyaṃ bhagavantaṃ svaśaktitaḥ | tatprītyai sarvakarmāṇi tatphalaṃ vārpayetkṛtī || 11.43 ||
athāhorātrākhilakarmārpaṇavidhiḥ
ekādaśaskandhe kāyena vācā manasendriyairvā buddhyātmanā vānusṛtasvabhāvāt | karoti yadyatsakalaṃ parasmai nārāyaṇāyeti samarpayettat || 11.44 ||
kiṃ cātra sādhu vāsādhu vā karma yadyadācaritaṃ mayā | tatsarvaṃ bhagavan viṣṇo gṛhāṇārādhanaṃ param || 11.45 ||
kiṃ ca apāṃ samīpe śayanāsane gṛhe divā ca rātrau ca yathā ca gacchatā | yadasti kiṃcitsukṛtaṃ kṛtaṃ mayā janārdanastena kṛtena tuṣyatu || 11.46 ||
ataivoktaṃ tṛtīyaskandhe brahmāṇaṃ prati śrībhagavatā pūrtena tapasā yajñairdānairyogasamādhinā | rāddhaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ puṃsāṃ matprītistattvavinmatam || 11.47 || iti |
itthamārādhayennityaṃ bhagavantaṃ yathā vidhi | nyāyārjitāptavittena samagraphalasiddhaye || 11.48 ||
atha pūjāphalasamprāptyupāyaḥ
daśamaskandhe ayaṃ svastyayanaḥ panthā dvijātergṛhamedhinaḥ | yacchraddhayāptavittena śuklenejyeta pūruṣaḥ || 11.49 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ ca nyāyājjitaiḥ sādhanaiśca dānahomārcanādikam | kuryānna cedadho yāti bhaktyā kurvannapi dvija || 11.50 || iti |
yatnātsiddhairnijaiḥ śuddhairdravyairdhanyo'rcayetprabhum | pūjādravyāṇyaśaktaśceddahyādīkṣeta vārcanam || 11.51 || athāśaktasya pūjāprāptyupāyaḥ
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ ārādhanāsamarthyaśceddadyādarcanasādhanam | yo dātuṃ naiva śaknoti kuryādarcanadarśanam || 11.52 || nistārāya tadevālaṃ bhavābdhermunisattama | naikaṃ ca yasya vidyeta so'dho yātyeva nānyathā || 11.53 ||
kiṃ ca tatraiva yastu bhaktyā prayatnena svayaṃ sampādya cākhilam | sādhanaṃ cārcayedvidvān samagraṃ labhate phalam || 11.54 || yo'rcayedvidhivadbhaktyā parānītaiśca sādhanaiḥ | pūjāphalārdhameva syānna samagraṃ phalaṃ labhet || 11.55 ||
kiṃ ca, pādme śrīkṛṣṇasatyabhāmāsaṃvādīyakārttikamāhātmye dharmoddeśena yo dravyamaparaṃ yācate naraḥ | tatpuṇyakarmajaṃ tasya dhanadastvāpnuyātphalam || 11.56 ||
atha darśanamāhātmyam
pādme śrīpulastyabhagīrathasaṃvāde pūjitaṃ pūjyamānaṃ ca ye paśyanti janārdanam | kapilāśatadānasya nityaṃ bhavati tatphalam || 11.57 ||
āgneye pūjitaṃ pūjyamānaṃ vā yo paśyedbhaktito harim | śraddhayā modayedyastu so'pi yāgaphalaṃ labhet || 11.58 || sampūjyamānaṃ vidhinā yaḥ paśyetśraddhayā harim | so'pi yāgaphalaṃ kṛtsnaṃ prāpnuyātnātra saṃśayaḥ || 11.59 || dṛṣṭvā sampūjitaṃ devaṃ nṛtyamāno'numodayet | asaṃśayamatiḥ śuddhaḥ paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 11.60 ||
atha śrībhagavanmūrtidarśananityatā
viṣṇudharmottare tāvadbhramanti saṃsāre manuṣyā mandabuddhayaḥ | yāvadrūpaṃ na paśyanti keśavasya mahātmanaḥ || 11.61 ||
pādme ca tatraiva pūjyamānaṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ ye na paśyanti vaiṣṇavāḥ | teṣāṃ dattaṃ hutaṃ japtaṃ daiteyāyopatiṣṭhati || 11.62 ||
kiṃ ca, tatraiva nārāyaṇanāradasaṃvāde pūjāvidhikathane yatra kutrāpi pratimāṃ vedadharmasamanvitām | na paśyanti janā gatvā te daṇḍyā yamakiṅkaraiḥ || 11.63 ||
atha bhagavadarthe dravyadānamāhātmyam
skānde viṣṇumuddiśya yatkiñcidviṣṇubhaktāya dīyate | dānaṃ tadvimalaṃ proktaṃ kevalaṃ mokṣasādhanam || 11.64 ||
kaurme yatkiñciddeyamīśānamuddiśya brāhmaṇāya ca | prabhave viṣṇave cātha tadanantaphalaṃ smṛtam || 11.65 ||
viṣṇudharmottare anugraheṇa mahatā pretasya patitasya ca | nārāyaṇabaliḥ kāryastenāsyānugraho bhavet || 11.66 || anādinidhano devaḥ śaṅkhacakragadādharaḥ | akṣayaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣastatra dattaṃ na naśyati || 11.67 || yathā kathañcidyaddattaṃ devadeve janārdane | avināśi tu tadviddhi pātrameko janārdanaḥ || 11.68 ||
tatraiva tṛtīyakāṇḍe sāmānyabhaktyā yaddattaṃ taddhi padbhyāṃ pratīcchati | ekāntabhāvopagamairmūrdhnā divjavarottamāḥ || 11.69 || ananto bhagavān viṣṇustasya kāmavivarjitaiḥ | yadeva dīyate kiñcittadevākṣayamucyate || 11.70 || padbhyāṃ pratīcchate devaḥ sakāmena niveditam | mūrdhnā pratīcchate dattamakāmena dvijottamāḥ || 11.71 ||
tathaivoktaṃ mokṣadharme śrīnāradena brahmā yadṛṣayaścaiva svayaṃ paśupatiśca yat | anye ca vibudhaśreṣṭhā daityadānavarākṣasāḥ || 11.72 || nāgāḥ suvarṇāḥ gandharvāḥ siddhā rājarṣayastathā | havyaṃ kravyaṃ ca satataṃ vidhiyuktaṃ prabhuñjate | kṛtsnaṃ tu tasya devasya caraṇāvupatiṣṭhati || 11.73 || yāḥ kriyāḥ saṃprayuktāstu ekāntagatabuddhibhiḥ | tāḥ sarvāḥ śirasā devaḥ pratigṛhṇāti vai svayam || 11.74 || brahmavaivarte ekāmapi naro dhenuṃ savatsāṃ vidhipūrvakam | dattvoddeśena kṛṣṇasya prāpnotyevābhivāñchitam || 11.75 ||
nārasiṃhe yo gāṃ payasvinīṃ viṣṇoḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ prayacchati | aśvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpya hariṃ vrajet || 11.76 || sarvapāpairvirahitaḥ sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitaḥ | gavāṃ sahasradānasya prāpyaṃ divyaṃ vrajet || 11.77 ||
viṣṇudharmottare gavāṃ lokamavāpnoti dhenuṃ dattvā payasvinīm | dadhikṣīraghṛtārthāya vāsudevasya cālaye | dattvā gāṃ madhuparkāya mahatphalamavāpnuyāt || 11.78 || jalāśayaṃ tathā kṛtvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 11.79 || sapuṣpaiḥ saphalairvṛkṣairyutaṃ kṛtvā jalāśayam | udyānaiḥ padminīṣaṇḍairāśramaiśca manoharaiḥ | śvedadvīpamavāpnoti punarnāvartate tataḥ || 11.80 || devāgre kārayedyastu ramyāmāpaṇavīthikām | rājā bhavati lokeṣu vijitārirmahāśayāḥ || 11.81 || nagaraṃ ca tathā kṛtvā sāmrājyamadhigacchati | śivikāṃ ye prayacchanti te prayāntyamarāvatīm || 11.82 || aśvadāḥ svargalokasthā rājante divi sūryavat | kavīndradānācchakrasya cirāllokācyuto naraḥ || 11.83 || rājā bhavati dharmātmā pṛthivyāṃ pṛthivīpatiḥ || 11.84 || viṣṇorāyatane dattvā tatkathāpustakaṃ naraḥ | brahmalokamavāpnoti bahukālasthiraṃ dvijāḥ || 11.85 || pustakāṃśca tathaivānyān yaḥ pradadyānnarastviha | sārasvatamavāpnoti lokaṃ kālaṃ tathā bahum || 11.86 || svabhṛtaṃ vācakaṃ kṛtvā devāgāre naraḥ sadā | vidyādānaphalaṃ prāpya brahmaloke mahīyate || 11.87 || viṣṇoḥ śaṅkhapradānena vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute | mānuṣyamāsādya tathā khyātaśabdaśca jāyate || 11.88 || ghaṇṭāpradānena tathā mahadyaśa upāśnute | kūṭāgāraṃ tathā dattvā nagarādhipatirbhavet || 11.89 || dattvā tu devakarmārthaṃ navāṃ vedīṃ dṛḍhāṃ śubhām | pārthivatvamavāpnoti vedī hi pṛthivī yataḥ || 11.90 || toraṇaṃ kārayedyastu devadevālaye naraḥ | lokeṣu tasya dvārāṇi bhavanti vivṛtāni vai || 11.91 || devaveśmopayogyāni śilpabhāṇḍāni yo naraḥ | dadyādvā vādyabhāṇḍāni gaṇeśatvamavāpnuyāt || 11.92 || yaḥ kumbhaṃ devakarmārthaṃ naro dadyānnavaṃ śubham | vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnoti sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 11.93 || caturaḥ kalasān dadyādyastu devagṛhe naraḥ | catuḥsamudravalayāṃ sa hi bhuṅkte vasundharām || 11.94 || dattvaikamapi viprendrāḥ kalasaṃ susamāhitaḥ | rājā bhavati dharmātmā bhūtale nātra saṃśayaḥ || 11.95 || vāridhānīṃ tathā dattvā vāruṇaṃ lokamaśnute | kamaṇḍalupradānena yajñasya phalamāpnuyāt || 11.96 || mātrāṃ tu paricaryārthaṃ nivedya haraye tathā | sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute || 11.97 || tālavṛntapradānena nirvṛtiṃ prāpnuyātparām | mālyādhāraṃ tathā dattvā dhūpādhāraṃ tathaiva ca | gandhādhāraṃ tathā pātraṃ kāmānāṃ pātratāṃ vrajet || 11.98 || saumdrajāni pātrāṇi dattvā vai taijasāni vā | pātraṃ bhavati kāmānāṃ vidyānāṃ ca dhanasya ca || 11.99 || śayanāsanadānena sthitiṃ vindati śāśvatīm | uttaracchadadānena sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt || 11.100 || naraḥ suvarṇadānena sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt | rūpyado rūpamāpnoti viśeṣādbhuvi durlabham || 11.101 || ratnadānena lokeṣu prāmāṇyamupagacchati | anaḍvāhapradānena daśadhenuphalaṃ labhet || 11.102 || ājñāvimahiṣoṣṭrāṇāṃ dānamaśvatarasya ca | sahasraguṇitaṃ dānātpūrvaproktātprakīrtitam || 11.103 || vāruṇaṃ lokamāpnoti dattvā vastuṃ dvijottamāḥ | avipradānācca tathā tamenaṃ lokamaśnute || 11.104 || uṣṭraṃ vā gardabhaṃ vāpi kharaṃ vā yaḥ prayacchati | alakāṃ sa samāsādya yakṣendraiḥ saha modate || 11.105 || āraṇyamṛgajātīnāṃ tathā dānācca pakṣiṇām | agniṣṭomamavāpnoti subhagaśca tathā bhavet || 11.106 || dāsaṃ dattvā sukhe loke neṣṭabhraṣṭo vijāyate | dāsīṃ dattvā tathā viprā nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 11.107 || gaṇikāṃ ye prayacchanti nṛtyagītaviśāradām | sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktāste prayāntyamarāvatīm || 11.108 || nṛtyaṃ dattvā tathāpnoti rudralokamasaṃśayam || 11.109 || prekṣaṇīyapradānena śakraloke mahīyate | gītaṃ dattvā tathāpnoti brahmalokamasaṃśayam || 11.110 || dundubhiṃ ye prayacchanti kīrtimanto bhavanti te || 11.111 || dattvā dhānyāni bījāni śasyāni vividhāni ca | rūpakāni ca tānyeva prāpnuyātsurapūjyatām || 11.112 || dattvā śākāni ramyāni viśokastvabhijāyate | dattvā ca vyañjanārthāya tathopakaraṇāni ca || 11.113 || puṣpavṛkṣaṃ tathā dattvā deśasyādhipatirbhavet | phalavṛkṣaṃ tathā nagarādhipatirbhavet || 11.114 || tathā sugandhasādhanānīha paṭavāsāni yo naraḥ | dadāti devadevasya so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet || 11.115 || kaṅkatasya pradānena viromastvabhijāyate | kūrcaprasādhanaṃ kṛtvā paraṃ maṅgalamaśnute || 11.116 || vismāpanīyaṃ yatkiñciddattvātyantaṃ sukhaṃ labhet || 11.117 || vastrālaṅkaraṇādīnāṃ kṛṣṇārpaṇaphalaṃ ca yat | upacāraprayoge prāktatra tatra vyalekhi tat || 11.118 || upacārāśca vividhāḥ śrīmadbhagavadarcane | śakyaśaktyādibhedena tāntrikairvaiṣṇavirmatāḥ || 11.119 ||
atha vividhopacārāḥ
āgame āsanasvāgate sārghye pādyamācamanīyakam | madhuparkācamasnānavasanābharaṇāni ca || 11.120 || sugandhasumanodhūpadīpanaivedyavandanam | prayojayedaracanājñāmupacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa || 11.121 || arghyaṃ ca pādyācamanamadhuparkācamānyapi | gandhādayo nivedyāntā upacārā daśa kramāt || 11.122 || gandhādibhirnivedyāntaiḥ pūjā pañcopacārikī | saparyāstrividhāḥ proktāstāsāmekāṃ samācaret || 11.123 ||
kvacicca āsanāvāhanaṃ caiva pādyārghyācamanīyakam | snānaṃ vāso bhūṣaṇaṃ ca gandhaḥ puṣpaṃ ca dhūpakaḥ || 11.124 || pradīpaścaiva naivedyaṃ puṣpāñjalirataḥ param | pradakṣiṇaṃ namaskāro visargaścaiva ṣoḍaśa || 11.125 || keciccāhuścatuḥṣaṣṭimupacārānmamārcane | teṣvanekaprakāreṣu prakāraiko'tra likhyate || 11.126 || sukhasuptasya kṛṣṇasya prātarādau prabodhanam | vedaghoṣaṇavīṇāvivādyairvandistavairapi || 11.127 || jayaśabdā namaskārā maṅgalārātrikaṃ tataḥ | āsanaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca pādyārghyācamanānyapi || 11.128 || tataśca madhuparkāḍhyācamanaṃ pādukārpaṇam | aṅgamārjanamabhyaṅgodvartane snapanaṃ jalaḥ || 11.129 || kṣīreṇa dadhnā haviṣā madhunā sitayā tathā | mantrapūtaiḥ punarvārbhiraṅgavāso'tha vāsasī || 11.130 || upavītaṃ puna¨ccācamanīyaṃ cānulepanam | bhūṣaṇaṃ kusumaṃ dhūpo dīpo dṛṣṭyapasāraṇam || 11.131 || naivedyaṃ mukhavāsastu tāmbūlaṃ śayanottamam | keśaprasādhanaṃ divyavastrāṇi mukuṭaṃ mahat || 11.132 || divyagandhānulepaśca kaustubhādivibhūṣaṇam | vicitradivyapuṣpāṇi maṅgalārātrikaṃ tataḥ || 11.133 || ādarśaḥ sukhayānena maṇḍapāgamanotsavaḥ | siṃhāsanopaveśaśca pādyādyaiḥ punararcanam || 11.134 || punardhūpādyarpaṇena prāgvannaivedyamuttamam | tataśca divyatāmbūlamahānīrājanaṃ punaḥ || 11.135 || cāmaravyajanacchatraṃ gītaṃ vādyaṃ ca nartanam | pradakṣiṇaṃ namaskāraḥ stutiḥ śrīcaraṇābjayoḥ || 11.136 || tayośca sthāpanaṃ mūrdhni tīrthanirmālyadhāraṇam | ucchiṣṭabhojanaṃ pādasevoddeśopaveśanam || 11.137 || naktaṃ śayyāvinirmāṇaṃ divyairvividhasādhanaiḥ | hastapradānaṃ śayanasthānāgamamahotsavaḥ || 11.138 || śayyopaveśanaṃ śrīmatpādakṣālanapūrvakam | gandhaprasūnatāmbūlārpaṇanīrājanotsavaḥ || 11.139 || śeṣaparyaṅkaśayanapādasaṃvāhanādikam | krameṇaite catuḥṣaṣṭhirupacārāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 11.140 || sadācārānusāreṇa yadyadācaryate svayam | nityakarmādikaṃ tattatśrīkṛṣṇasyāpi kārayet || 11.141 || ato'trālikhitaṃ yadyadupacārādikaṃ param | sarvaṃ tattacca jānīyāllokarītyanusārataḥ || 11.142 || uktānāṃ copacārāṇāmabhāve bhagavān sadā | bhaktenārcyo yathālabdhaistairantarbhāvitairapi || 11.143 || athālabdhasamādhānam tantre upacāroktavastūnāmupasaṅgrahaṇe vidhiḥ | dravyāṇāmapyabhāve tu puṣpākṣatayavaiḥ kriyāḥ || 11.144 || arcopacāravastūnāmabhāve samupasthite | nirmalenodakenaiva dravyasampūrṇatā bhavet || 11.145 || upacāreṣu dravyeṣu yatkiñcidduṣkaraṃ budhaḥ | tatsarvaṃ manasā buddhyā puṣpakṣepeṇa kalpayet || 11.146 || eteṣu copacāreṣu vittaśāṭhyavivarjitam | yadasampannameteṣāṃ manasā tu prakalpayet || 11.147 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ śrītulasīmāhātmye yadyannyūnaṃ bhavatyeva rāmārādhanasādhanam | tulasīdalamātreṇa yuktaṃ tatparipūryate || 11.148 ||
ekādaśaskandhe ca śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde dravyaiḥ prasiddhairmadyāgaḥ pratimādiṣvamāyinaḥ | bhaktasya ca yathālabdhairhṛdi bhāvena caiva hi || 11.149 ||
tato'nujñāṃ prabhoḥ prārthya daṇḍavattaṃ praṇamya ca | sāyaṃ bhuktvā yathānyāyaṃ sukhaṃ svapyātprabhuṃ smaret || 11.150 ||
atha śayanavidhiḥ
āgame nirguṇo niṣkalaścaiva viśvamūrtidharo'vyayaḥ | anādyante sadānante phaṇāmaṇiviśobhite | kṣīrābdhimadhye yaḥ śete sa māṃ rakṣatu mādhavaḥ || 11.151 || sabāhyābhyantaraṃ dehamāpādatalamastakam | sarvātmā sarvaśaktiśca pātu māṃ garuḍadhvajaḥ || 11.152 || iti rakṣāṃ puraskṛtya svapedviṣṇumanusmaran || 11.153 ||
kiṃ cānyatra adbhiḥ śaucavidhiṃ vidhāya caraṇau prakṣālycopaspṛśed dviḥ saṃsmṛtya jagatpatiṃ vrajapatiṃ śrīvallabhīvallabham | rādhāyāḥ suciraṃ pibantamamṛtāsārāyamāṇāṃ giraṃ vastreṇāṅghriyugaṃ pramṛjyaa śayanaṃ tvāpādya sadyaḥ svapet || 11.154 ||
kiṃ ca rāmaṃ skandaṃ hanumantaṃ vainateyaṃ vṛkodaram | śayane yaḥ smarennityaṃ duḥsvapnastasya naśyati || 11.155 ||
api ca skāndapādmayoḥ ṛtukālābhigāmī yaḥ svadāranirataśca yaḥ | sarvadā brahmacārīha vijñeyaḥ sa gṛhāśramī || 11.156 || ṛtuḥ ṣoḍaśayāminyaścatasrastāsu garhitāḥ | putradāstāsu yā yugmā ayugmāḥ kanyakāpradāḥ || 11.157 || tyaktvā candramasaṃ duṣṭaṃ maghāṃ mūlaṃ vihāya ca | śuciḥ sannirviśetpatnīṃ puṃnāmarkṣe viśeṣataḥ | śuciṃ putraṃ prasūyeta puruṣārthaprasādhanam || 11.158 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe aurvasagarasaṃvāde kṛtapādādiśaucaśca bhuktvā sāyaṃ tato gṛhī | gacchecchayyāmasphuṭitāmekadārumayīṃ nṛpa || 11.159 || nāviśālāṃ na vai bhagnāṃ nāsamāṃ malināṃ na ca | na ca jantumayīṃ śayyāmadhitiṣṭhedanāstṛtām || 11.160 || prācyāṃ diśi śiraḥ śastaṃ yāmyāyāmathavā nṛpa | sadaiva svapataḥ puṃso viparītaṃ tu rogadam || 11.161 || ṛtāvupagamaḥ śastaḥ svapatnyāmavanīpate | punnāmarkṣe śubhe kāle jyeṣṭhayugmāsu rātriṣu || 11.162 || nāsnātāṃ tu striyaṃ gacchennāturāṃ na rajasvalām | nāniṣṭāṃ na prakupitāṃ nāpraśastāṃ na gurviṇīm || 11.163 || nādakṣiṇāṃ nānyakāmāṃ nākāmāṃ nānyayoṣitam | kṣutkṣāmāmatibhuktāṃ vā svayaṃ caibhirguṇairyutaḥ || 11.164 || snātaḥ sraggandhadhṛkprīto nādhyātaḥ kṣudhito'pi vā | sakāmaḥ sānurāgaśca vyavāyaṃ puruṣo vrajet || 11.165 || caturdaśyaṣṭamī caiva amāvasyātha pūrṇimā | parvāṇyetāni rājendra ravisaṅkrāntireva ca || 11.166 || tailastrīmāṃsasambhogī parvasveteṣu vai pumān | viṇmūtrabhojanaṃ nāma prayāti narakaṃ nṛpa || 11.167 || nānyayonāvayonau vā nopayuktauṣadhastathā | devadvijagurūṇāṃ ca vyavāyī nāśramī bhavet || 11.168 || caityacatvaratīrtheṣu naiva goṣṭhe catuṣpathe | naiva śmaśānopavane salileṣu mahīpate || 11.169 || proktaparvasvaśeṣeṣu naiva bhūpāla sandhyayoḥ | gacchedvyavāyaṃ matimānna mūtroccārapīḍitaḥ || 11.170 || paradārānna gaccheta manasāpi kadācana | kimu vācāsthibandho'pi nāsti teṣu vyavāyinām || 11.171 || mṛto narakamabhyeti hīyate cātra cāyuṣaḥ | paradāraratiḥ puṃsāmubhayatrāpi bhītidā || 11.172 || iti matvā svadāreṣu ṛtumatsu budho vrajet | yathoktadoṣahīneṣu sakāmeṣvanṛtāvapi || 11.173 || iti |
teṣāṃ bhaktyupayogitvaṃ na syādyadyapi karmaṇām | tathāpi kṛta ullekho gṛhiṣvāvaśyakaṃ tataḥ || 11.174 || itthaṃ hi prātaru tthānātpratyahaṃ śayanāvadhi | śrīkṛṣṇaṃ pūjayan siddhasarvārtho'sya priyo bhavet || 11.175 ||
atha śrībhagavadarcanamāhātmyam
śrīkūrmapurāṇe na viṣṇvārādhanātpuṇyaṃ vidyate karma vaidikam | tasmādanādimadhyāntaṃ nityamārādhayeddharim || 11.176 ||
tatraiva bhṛgvādīn prati sākṣātśrībhagavaduktau ye'rcayiṣyanti māṃ bhaktyā nityaṃ kaliyuge dvijāḥ | vidhinā vedadṛṣṭena te gamiṣyanti tatpadam || 11.177 ||
viṣṇurahasye śrīviṣṇorarcanaṃ ye tu prakurvanti narā bhuvi | te yānti śāśvataṃ viṣṇorānandaṃ paramaṃ padam || 11.178 || iti |
tatraiva śrībhagavaduktau na me dhyānaratāḥ samyagyoginaḥ parituṣṭaye | tathā bhavanti devarṣe kriyāyogaratā yathā || 11.179 || kriyāyogo hi me'bhīṣṭaḥ parayogātsvanuṣṭhitāt | tuṣṭirme sambhavetpumbhirbhaktimadbhiramatsaraiḥ || 11.180 || ye'rcayanti narā nityaṃ kriyāyogaratāḥ svayam | dhyāyanti ye ca māṃ nityaṃ teṣāṃ śreṣṭhāḥ kriyā matāḥ || 11.181 || kriyāhīnasya devarṣe tathā dhyānaṃ na muktidam | na tathā māṃ vidurviprā dhyāninastattvato vinā | kriyāyogaratāḥ samyaglabhante māṃ samādhinā || 11.182 || yathā hi kāmadaṃ nṝṇāṃ mama tuṣṭikaraṃ param | bhaktiyogaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ śubham || 11.183 || saṃvatsareṇa yatpuṇyaṃ labhante dhyānino mama | prāpyante tadihaikāhātkriyāyogaparairnaraiḥ || 11.184 ||
ādipurāṇe na karmasadṛśaṃ dhyānaṃ na karmasadṛśaṃ phalam | na karmasadṛśastyāgo na karmasadṛśaṃ tapaḥ | na karmasadṛśaṃ puṇyaṃ na karmasadṛśī gatiḥ || 11.185 ||
nāradīye bhaktigrāhyo hṛṣīkeśo na dhanairdharaṇīdhara | bhaktyā saṃpūjito viṣṇuḥ pradadāti manoratham || 11.186 || tasmādviprāḥ sadā bhaktiḥ kartavyā cakrapāṇinaḥ | janenāpi jagannāthaḥ pūjitaḥ kleśahā bhavet || 11.187 ||
haribhaktisudhodaye kṛtāpi dambhahāsyārthe sevā tārayate janān | viphalā nānyakarmeva kṛpāluḥ ko nvataḥ paraḥ || 11.188 ||
brahmavaivarte sa samārādhito devo muktikṛtsyādyathā tathā | anicchayāpi hutabhuksaṃspṛṣṭo dahati dvija || 11.189 || dhanavān putravān bhogī yaśasvī bhayavarjitaḥ | medhāvī matimān prājño bhavatyārādhanāddhareḥ || 11.190 ||
skānde sanatkumāramarkaṇḍeyasaṃvāde viśiṣṭaḥ sarvadharmācca dharmo viṣṇvarcanaṃ nṝṇām | sarvayajñatapohomastīrthasnānaiśca yatphalam || 11.191 || tatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ viṣṇuṃ sampūjya cāpnuyāt | tasmātsarvaprayatnena nārāyaṇamihārcayet || 11.192 ||
tatraiva śrīśivomāsaṃvāde yaḥ pradadyaddvijendraya sarvaṃ bhumiṃ sasagaram | arcayedyaḥ sakṛdviṣṇuṃ tatphalaṃ labhate naraḥ || 11.193 || masardhamapi yo viṣṇuṃ nairantaryena pūjayet | puruṣottamaḥ sa vijñeyo viṣṇubhakto na saṃśayaḥ || 11.194 || madhyandinagate surye yo viṣṇuṃ paripūjayet | vasupūrṇamahidaturyatpunyaṃ tadavāpnuyāt || 11.195 || prātarutthaya yo viṣṇuṃ satataṃ paripūjayet | agnistomasahasrasya labhate phalamuttamam || 11.196 || yo viṣṇuṃ prayato bhūtvā sayaṃkale samarcayet | gavaṃ medhasya yajñasya phalamāpnoti durlabham || 11.197 || evaṃ sarvāsu velāsu avelāsu ca keśavam | sampūjayannaro bhaktyā sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt || 11.198 || kiṃ punaryo'rcayennityaṃ sarvadevanamaskṛtam | dhanyaḥ saḥ kṛtakṛtyaśca viṣṇulokamavāpnuyāt || 11.199 ||
kiṃ ca dīkṣāmātreṇa kṛṣṇasya narā mokṣaṃ labhanti vai | kiṃ punarye sadā bhaktyā pūjayantyacyutaṃ naraḥ || 11.200 ||
tatraiva śrībrahmanaradasaṃvāde saṃsāre'sminmahāghore janmamṛtyubhayākule | pūjanaṃ vāsudevasya tārakaṃ vādibhiḥ smrtam || 11.201 || sa nāma sukṛti loke kulaṃ tena hyalaṅkṛtam | ādhāraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ yena viṣṇuḥ prasaditaḥ || 11.202 || yajñānāṃ tapasāṃ caiva śubhānāmapi karmaṇām | tadviśiṣṭaphalaṃ nṝṇāṃ sadaivārādhanaṃ hareḥ || 11.203 || kalau kalimalākrāntā na jānanti hariṃ param | ye'rcayanti tamīśānaṃ kṛtakṛtyasta eva hi | nāsti śreyottamaṃ nṝṇāṃ viṣṇorārādhanatparam || 11.204 || yuge'smin tamase tasmātsatataṃ harimarcayet | arcite devadevese śaṅkhacakragadādhare || 11.205 || arcitaḥ sarvadevāḥ syuryataḥ sarvagato hariḥ | arcite sarvalokeśe surāsuranamaskṛte | keśave keśikaṃsaghne na yāti narakaṃ naraḥ || 11.206 || sakṛdabhyarcito yena helayāpi namaskṛtaḥ | sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yatsurairapi pūjitam || 11.207 || samastalokanāthasya devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ | sākṣādbhagavato nityaṃ pūjanaṃ janmanaḥ phalam || 11.208 ||
tatraivagre asāre khalu saṃsāre sārametannirūpitam | samastalokanāthasya śraddhayārādhanaṃ hareḥ || 11.209 ||
kiṃ ca yatra viṣṇukathā nityaṃ yatra tiṣṭhanti vaiṣṇavāḥ | kalibāhyā narāste vai ye'rcayanti sadā harim || 11.210 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe harerārādhanaṃ puṃsāṃ kiṃ kiṃ na kurute bata | putramitrakalatrārthaṃ rājyasvargāpavargadam || 11.211 || haratyaghaṃ dhvamsayati vyādhīnādhīnnirasyati | dharmaṃ vivardhayetkṣipraṃ prayacchanti manoratham || 11.212 ||
ata eva skānde dhruvaṃ prati markaṇḍeyasya vacanaṃ sakṛdabhyarcito yena devadevo janārdanaḥ | sa prāpnoti paraṃ sthānaṃ satyametanmayoditam || 11.213 ||
tathāṅgīrasaḥ yasyāntaḥ sarvamevedaṃ yasya nānto mahātmanaḥ | tamārādhaya govindaṃ sthānamāgryaṃ yadicchasi || 11.214 ||
pulastasya paraṃ brahma paraṃ dhāma yo'sau sasvatapūruṣaḥ | tamārādhya hariṃ yāti muktimapyatidurlabham || 11.215 ||
pulahasya aindramindraḥ paraṃ sthānaṃ yamārādhya jagatpatim | prāpa yajñapatiṃ viṣṇuṃ tamārādhaya suvrata || 11.216 ||
vasiṣṭhasya prāpnotyārādhite viṣṇau manasā yadyadicchati | trailokyāntargataṃ sthānaṃ kimu sarvottamottamam || 11.217 || yan yan kāmayate kāmānnārī vā puruṣo'pi vā | tan samāpnoti vipulān samārādhya janārdanam || 11.218 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyāṃ ārādhyaiva naro viṣṇuṃ manasā yadyadicchati | phalaṃ prāpnotyavihataṃ bhūri svalpamathāpi vā || 11.219 ||
idrsaṃ viṣṇupurane'pi kiṃcidadhikaṃ cedaṃ, śrīmarīceḥ anārādhitagovindairnarairsthānaṃ nṛpātmaja | na hi samprāpyate śreṣṭhaṃ tasmādārādhayācyutam || 11.220 ||
kiṃ ca tatraiva bhaumānmanorathān svargaṃ svargavandyaṃ tathāspadam | prāpnotyārādhite viṣṇau nirvāṇamapi cottamam || 11.221 ||
tathā brahmavaivarte yatpādodakamādhāya śivaḥ śirasi nṛtyati | yannābhinalinādāsīdbrahmā lokapitāmahaḥ || 11.222 || yadicchāśaktivikṣobhādbrahmaṇḍodbhavasaṅkṣayau | tamārādhaya govindaṃ sthānamagryaṃ yadīcchasi || 11.223 ||
nārasiṃhe markaṇḍeyasahasranikasaṃvāde yastu sampūjayennityaṃ narasiṃha nareśvara | sa svargamokṣabhāgī syānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 11.224 || tasmādekamanā bhūtvā yāvajjīvaṃ pratijñayā | arcanānnarasiṃhasya samprāpnotyabhivāñchitam || 11.225 ||
tatraiva śrīvyāsaśukasaṃvāde śrīmārkaṇḍeyamṛtyuñjayasaṃvādānantaraṃ narake pacyamānastu yamena paribhāṣitaḥ | kiṃ tvayā nārcito devaḥ keśavaḥ kleśanāśanaḥ || 11.226 || udakenāpyalābhe tu dravyāṇāṃ pūjitaḥ prabhuḥ | yo dadāti svakaṃ lokaṃ sa tvayā kiṃ na pūjitaḥ || 11.227 || narasiṃho hṛṣīkeśaḥ puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇaḥ | smaranānmuktido nṛṇāṃ sa tvayā kiṃ na pūjitaḥ || 11.228 ||
bṛhannāradīye'ditimāhātmye śrīsūtoktau yatra pūjāparo viṣṇostatra vighno na bādhate | rājā ca taskaraścāpi vyādhayaśca na santi hi || 11.229 || pretāḥ piśācāḥ kuṣmāṇḍā grahā bālagrahāstathā | ḍākinyo rākṣasāścaiva na bādhante'cyutarcakam || 11.230 ||
tatraiva yamabhagīrathasaṃvāde patraiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalairvārcya pūjārahitamacyutam | sa yāti viṣṇusālokyaṃ kulasaptatisaṃyutaḥ || 11.231 ||
tatraiva dhvajāropaṇamāhātmye śrīviṣṇudūtānāmuktau utkrāntikāle yannāma śrutavanto'pi vai sakṛt | labhante paramaṃ sthānaṃ kimu śuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ || 11.232 || muhūrtaṃ vā muhūrtārdhaṃ yastiṣṭheddharimandire | sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ kimu śuśrūṣaṇe rataḥ || 11.233 ||
tatraiva vibhāṇḍakamuneḥ sumatinṛpaṃ prati avaśenāpi yatkarma kṛtaṃ tu sumahatphalam | dadāti nṝṇāṃ rājendra kiṃ punaḥ samyagarcanā || 11.234 ||
prāyaścittaprakāraṇānte samparkādyadi vā mohādyastu pūjayate harim | sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sa yāti paramaṃ padam || 11.235 || sarvāntarāyā naśyanti manaḥsuddhiśca jāyate | paraṃ mokṣaṃ labheccaiva pūjyamāne janārdane || 11.236 || dharmārthakāmamokṣākhyā puruṣārthāḥ sanātanāḥ | haripūjāparāṇāṃ tu sidhyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 11.237 || sarvatīrthāni yajñāśca sāṅgā vedāśca sattamāḥ | nārāyaṇarcanasyaite kālaṃ narhanti sodasim || 11.238 ||
śrīviṣṇutoṣavidhipraśnottare satyaṃ vacmi hitaṃ vacmi sāraṃ vacmi punaḥ punaḥ | asārodagrasaṃsāre sāraṃ yadviṣṇupūjanam || 11.239 ||
upalepanamāhātmyante akāmādapi ye viṣṇoḥ sakṛtpūjāṃ prakurvate na teṣām | bhavabandhastu kadācidapi jāyate || 11.240 ||
yajñadhvajopākhyānānte tasmātśṛṇuta viprendra devo nārāyaṇo'vyayaḥ | jānato'jānato vāpi pūjakānāṃ vimuktidaḥ || 11.241 || te vandyāste prapūjyāśca namaskāryā viśeṣataḥ | ye'rcayanti mahāviṣṇuṃ prapannārtipraṇāśanam || 11.242 || ye yajanti spṛhāśūnyā hariṃ vā harameva vā | ta eva bhuvanaṃ sarvaṃ punanti vibudharṣabhāḥ || 11.241 ||
pādme śrīnārāyaṇanāradasaṃvāde pūjavidhiprasaṅge madbhakto yo madarcāṃ ca karoti vidhidṛṣṭaye | tasyāntarāyaḥ svapne'pi na bhavatyabhayo hi saḥ || 11.242 ||
tatraiva vaiśākhamāhātmye nāradāmbarīṣasaṃvāde putrān kalatrān dīrghāyū rājyaṃ svargāpavargakam | sa dadyādīpsitaṃ sarvaṃ bhaktyā sampūjito'jitaḥ || 11.245 || narake'pi ciraṃ magnāḥ pūrvajā ye kuladvaye | tatraiva yānti te svargaṃ yadārceta sūto harim || 11.246 ||
tatraiva śrīyamabrahmasaṃvāde anārādhya hariṃ bhaktyā ko lokān prāpnuyādbudhaḥ | ārādhite harau kāmāḥ sarve karatalasthitāḥ || 11.247 ||
viṣṇudharmottare śrīkṛṣṇāmṛtastotre so'pi dhanyatamo loke yo'rcayedacutyaṃ sakṛt | kiṃ punaḥ śraddhayā yuktaḥ sapuṣpaiḥ prativāsaram || 11.248 || vaiṣṇavānapi ye nityaṃ prapaśyantyarcayanti ca | te'pi viṣṇupadaṃ yānti kiṃ punarviṣṇusevakāḥ || 11.249 || sa yogī sa viśuddhātmā sa śāntaḥ sa mahāmatiḥ | sa śuddhaḥ sa ca sampūrṇaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ seveta yo naraḥ || 11.250 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyām ananyamanasaḥ śaśvadgaṇayanto'kṣamālayā | japanto rāmarāmeti sukhāmṛtanidhau manaḥ | pravilāpyāmṛtībhūya sukhaṃ tiṣṭhanti kecana || 11.251 || paricaryāparāḥ kecitprāsādādiṣu śerate | manuṣyamiva taṃ draṣṭuṃ vyavahartuṃ ca bandhuvat || 11.252 ||
kiṃ ca yathā vidhiniṣedhau tu muktaṃ naviopasarpataḥ | tathā na spṛśato rāmopāsakaṃ vidhipūrvakam || 11.253 ||
śrībhagavadgītāsu evaṃ satatayuktā ye bhaktāstvāṃ paryupāsate | ye cāpyakṣaramavyaktaṃ teṣāṃ ke yogavittamāḥ || 11.254 ||
ityarjunena pṛṣṭaḥ śrībhagavānuvāca mayyāveśya mano ye māṃ nityayuktā upāsate | śraddhayā parayopetāste me yuktatamā matāḥ || 11.255 ||
caturthaskandhe yatpādasevābhirucistapasvinām aśeṣajanmopacitaṃ malaṃ dhiyaḥ | sadyaḥ kṣiṇotyanvahamedhatī satī yathā padāṅguṣṭhaviniḥsṛtā sarit || 11.256 ||
kiṃ ca, nāradoktau yathā tarormūlaniṣecanena tṛpyanti tatskandhabhujopaśākhāḥ | prāṇopahārācca yathendriyāṇāṃ tathaiva sarvārhaṇamacyutejyā || 11.257 ||
ekādaśaskandhe ca kaviyogeśvarasya vākyaṃ manye'kutaścidbhayamacyutasya pādāmbujopāsanamatra nityam | udvignabuddherasadātmabhāvād viśvātmanā yatra nivartate bhīḥ || 11.258 ||
śrībhagavataśca evaṃ kriyāyogapathaiḥ pumān vaidikatāntrikaiḥ | arcannubhayataḥ siddhiṃ matto vindatyabhīpsitām || 11.259 ||
kiṃ ca māmeva nairapekṣyeṇa bhaktiyogena vindati | bhaktiyogaṃ sa labhata evaṃ yaḥ pūjayeta mām || 11.260 ||
gautamīyatantre śrīnāradasya tulasīdalamātreṇa jalasya culukena ca | vikrīṇīte svamātmānaṃ bhaktebhyo bhaktavatsalaḥ || 11.261 || atha pūjānityatā mahābhārate mātṛvatparirakṣantaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakam | yo nārcayati deveśaṃ taṃ vidyādbrahmaghātakam || 11.262 ||
ata evoktaṃ bṛhannāradīye pādodakamāhātmyākhyānārambhe haripūjāvidhānaṃ tu yasya veśmani no dvijaḥ | śmaśānasadṛśaṃ vidyānna kadāpi viśecca tat || 11.263 ||
ata evoktaṃ viṣṇudharmottare puṣpairvā yadi vā patraihphalairvā yadi vāmbubhiḥ | yaṣṭavyaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣastyaktvā kāryaśatāni ca || 11.264 ||
kiṃ ca nāradīye nimitteṣu ca sarveṣu tattatkālaviśeṣataḥ | pūjayeddevadeveśaṃ dravyaṃ sampādya yatnataḥ || 11.265 ||
ata evoktaṃ bhagavatā hāyagrīveṇa hāyaśīrṣapañcarātre pratiṣṭhitārcā na tyājyā yāvajjīvaṃ samarcayet | varaṃ prāṇasya vā tyāgaḥ śiraso vāpi karttanam || 11.266 || pūjayā nityatālekhi prākca naivedyabhakṣaṇe | māhātmyaṃ ca paraṃ śālagrāmacakraprasaṅgataḥ || 11.267 || pūjāṅgānāṃ ca māhātmyaṃ yadyadvilikhitaṃ purā | tatsarvamiha pūjāyāṃ paryavasyati hi svataḥ || 11.268 || pūjāmahimamattebhāhśāstrāraṇyavihāriṇaḥ | kīṭena kati saṅgrāhyāḥ prabhāvaṃ śrīharervinā || 11.269 ||
atha śrībhagavannāma sadā seveta sarvataḥ | tanmāhātmyaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ saṅkṣepenātra likhyate || 11.270 ||
atha śrībhagavannāmamāhātmyam
tatra śrībhagavannāmaviśeṣasya ca sevanam | ṛṣibhiḥ kṛpayādiṣṭaṃ tattatkāmahatātmanām || 11.271 ||
atha kāmaviśeṣeṇa śrībhagavannāmaviśeṣasevāmāhātmyam
tatra pāpakṣayārthaṃ
śrīkaurme śrīśabdapūrvaṃ jayaśabdamadhyaṃ jayadvayāduttaratastathā hi | triḥsaptakṛtvo narasiṃhanāma japtaṃ nihanyādapi viprahatyām || 11.272 ||
mahābhayanivaranartham
tatraiva śrīpūrvo narasiṃho dvirjayāduttaratastu saḥ | triḥsaptakṛtvo japato mahābhayanivāraṇaḥ || 11.273 ||
kālaviśeṣe tu maṅgalārthaṃ, viṣṇudharmottare mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde puruṣaṃ vāsudevaṃ ca tathā saṅkarṣaṇaṃ vibhum | pradyumnamaniruddhaṃ ca kramādabdeṣu kīrtayet || 11.274 || balabhadraṃ tathā kṛṣṇaṃ kīrtayedayanadvaye | madhavaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ tathā vai bhogasayinam || 11.275 || padmanābhaṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ tathā devaṃ trivikramam | kramena rajasardula vasantadisu kīrtayet || 11.276 || viṣṇuṃ ca madhuhantāraṃ tathā devaṃ trivikramam | vāmanaṃ śrīdharaṃ caiva hṛṣīkeśaṃ tathāiva ca || 11.277 || dāmodaraṃ padmanābhaṃ keśavaṃ ca yadūttamam | nārāyaṇaṃ mādhavaṃ ca govindaṃ ca tathā kramāt || 11.278 || caitrādiṣu ca māseṣu devadevamanusmaret | pradyumnamaniruddhaṃ ca pakṣayoḥ kṛṣṇaśuklayoḥ || 11.279 || sarvaḥ śarvaḥ sivaḥ sthāṇurbhūtādirnidhiravyayaḥ | adityādiṣu vareṣu kramādevamanusmaret || 11.280 || viśvaṃ viṣṇurvaṣaṭkāro bhūtabhavyabhavatprabhuḥ | bhūtabhṛdbhūtakṛdbhavo bhūtātmā bhūtabhavanaḥ || 11.281 || avyaktaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣo visvakarma sucisravaḥ | sadbhavo bhavano bharta prabhavaḥ prabhurisvaraḥ || 11.282 || aprameyo hṛṣīkeśahpadmanabho'maraprabhuḥ | agrahyaḥ sasvato dhata kṛṣṇaścaitanyanusmaret | devadevasya nāmāni kṛttikadisu yadava || 11.283 || brahmāṇaṃ śrīpatiṃ viṣṇuṃ kapilaṃ śrīdharaṃ prabhum | dāmodaraṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ govindaṃ madhusūdanam || 11.284 || bhūdharaṃ gadinaṃ devaṃ saṅkhinaṃ padminaṃ tathā | cakriṇaṃ ca mahārāja prathamādiṣu saṃsmaret || 11.285 || sarvaṃ vā sarvadā nāma devadevasya yādava || 11.286 ||
nāmāni sarvāṇi janārdanasya kālaśca sarvaḥ puruṣapravīrāḥ | tasmātsadā sarvagatasya nāma grāhyaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ varadasya rājan || 11.287 ||
vividhakāmasiddhaye ca
pulastyoktau kāmaḥ kāmapradaḥ kāntaḥ kāmapālastathā hariḥ | ānando mādhavaścaiva kāmasaṃsiddhaye japet || 11.288 || rāmaḥ paraśurāmaśca nṛsiṃho viṣṇureva ca | vikramaścaivamādīni japyānyarijigīṣubhiḥ || 11.289 || vidyāmabhyastatā nityaṃ japtavyaḥ puruṣottamaḥ | dāmodaraṃ bandhagato nityameva japennaraḥ || 11.290 || keśavaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣamaniśaṃ hi tathā japet | netrabādhāsu sarvāsu hṛṣīkeśaṃ bhayeṣu ca || 11.291 || acyutaṃ cāmṛtaṃ caiva japedauṣadhakarmāṇi | saṅgrāmābhimukho gacchan saṃsmaredaparājitam || 11.292 || cakriṇaṃ gadinaṃ caiva śārṅgiṇaṃ khaḍginaṃ tathā | kṣemārthī pravasannityaṃ dikṣu pracyādiṣu smaret || 11.293 || ajitaṃ cādhipaṃ caiva sarvaṃ sarveśvaraṃ tathā | saṃsmaretpuruṣo bhaktyā vyavahāreṣu sarvadā || 11.294 || nārāyaṇaṃ sarvakālaṃ kṣutapraskhalanādiṣu | grahanakṣatrapīḍāsu devabādhāsu sarvataḥ || 11.295 || dasyuvairinirodheṣu vyāghrasiṃhādisaṅkaṭe | andhākāre tamastīvre narasiṃhamanusmaret || 11.296 || agnidāhe samutpanne saṃsmarejjalaśāyinam | garuḍadhvajānusmaranādviṣavīryaṃ vyapohati || 11.297 || snāne devārcane home praṇipāte pradakṣine | kīrtayedbhagavannāma vāsudeveti tatparaḥ || 11.298 || sthāpane vittadhānyāderapadhyāne ca duṣṭaje | kurvīta tanmanā bhūtvā anantācyutakīrtanam || 11.299 || nārāyaṇaṃ śārṅgadharaṃ śrīdharaṃ puruṣottamam | vāmanaṃ khaḍginaṃ caiva duṣṭasvapne sadā smaret || 11.300 || mahārṇavādau paryaṅkaśāyinaṃ ca naraḥ smaret | balabhadraṃ samṛddhyarthaṃ sarvakarmāṇi saṃsmaret || 11.301 || jagatpatimapatyārthaṃ stuvān bhaktyā na sīdati | śrīśaṃ sarvābhyudayike karmanyāśu prakīrtayet || 11.302 || ariṣṭeṣu hyaśeṣeṣu viśokaṃ ca sadā japet | maruprapātāgnijalabandhanādiṣu mṛtyuṣu | svatantraparatantreṣu vāsudevaṃ japedbudhaḥ || 11.302 || sarvārthaśaktiyuktasya devadevasya cakriṇaḥ | yathābhirocate nāma tatsarvārtheṣu kīrtayet || 11.303 || sarvārthasiddhimāpnoti nāmnāmekārthatā yathā | sarvāṇyetāni nāmāni parasya brahmaṇo hareḥ || 11.304 || evaṃ viṣṇudharmottare ca mārkaṇḍeyavajrasaṃvāde kiṃ ca kūrmaṃ varāhaṃ matsyaṃ vā jalaprataraṇe smaret | bhrājiṣṇumagnijanane japennāma tvakhaṇḍitam || 11.306 || garuḍadhvajānusmaraṇād āpado mucyate naraḥ | jvarajuṣṭaśirorogaviṣavīryaṃ ca śamyati || 11.307 || balabhadraṃ tu yuddhārthī kṛśyārambhe halāyudham | uttarānāṃ vāṇijyārthī rāmamabhyudaye nṛpa || 11.308 || maṅgalyaṃ maṅgalaṃ viṣṇuṃ maṅgalyeṣu ca kīrtayet | uttiṣṭhan kīrtayedviṣṇuṃ prasvapanmadhavaṃ naraḥ | bhojane caiva govindaṃ sarvatra madhusūdanam || 11.309 ||
tatraivānyatra auṣadhe cintayedviṣṇuṃ bhojane ca janārdanam | śayane padmanābhaṃ ca maithune ca prajāpatim || 11.310 || saṅgrāme cakriṇaṃ kruddhaṃ sthānabhraṃśe trivikramam | nārāyaṇaṃ vṛṣotsarge śrīdharaṃ priyasaṅgame | jalamadhye ca vārāhaṃ pāvake jalaśāyinam || 11.311 || kānane narasiṃhaṃ ca parvate raghunandanam | duḥsvapne smara govindaṃ viśuddhau madhusūdanam | māyāsu vāmanaṃ devaṃ sarvakāryeṣu mādhavam || 11.312 ||
kiṃ ca kīrtayedvāsudevaṃ ca anukteṣvapi yādava | kāryārambhe tathā rājan yatheṣṭaṃ nāma kīrtayet || 11.313 ||
sarvāṇi nāmāni hi tasya rājan sarvārthasiddhyai tu bhavanti pumsaḥ | tasmādyatheṣṭaṃ khalu kṛṣṇanāma sarveṣu kāryeṣu japeta bhaktyā || 11.314 ||
tatrākhilapāponmūlanatvam atha sāmānyataḥ śrībhagavannāmakīrtanamāhātmyam
viṣṇudharme haribhaktisudhodaye coktaṃ nāradena aho sunirmala yūyaṃ rāgo hi harikīrtane | avidhūya tamaḥ kṛtsnaṃ nṛṇāmudeti sūryavat || 11.315 ||
garude pāpānalasya dīptasya mā kurvantu bhayaṃ narāḥ | govindanāmameghaughairnaśyate nīrabindubhiḥ || 11.316 || avaśenāpi yannāmni kīrtane sarvapātakaiḥ | pumān vimucyate sadyaḥ siṃhatrastairmṛgairiva || 11.317 || yannāmakīrtanaṃ bhaktyā vilāpanamanuttamam | maitreyāśeṣapāpānāṃ dhātūnāmiva pāvakaḥ || 11.318 ||
yasminnyastamatirna yāti narakaṃ svargo'pi yaccintane vighno yatra niveśitātmamanaso brāhmo'pi loko'lpakaḥ | muktiṃ cetasi yaḥ sthito'maladhiyāṃ puṃsāṃ dadātyavyayaḥ kiṃ citraṃ yadaghaṃ prayāti vilayaṃ tatrācyute kīrtite || 11.319 ||
viṣṇudharmottare sāyaṃ prātastathā kṛtvā devadevasya kīrtanam | sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ svargaloke mahīyate || 11.320 ||
vāmane nārāyaṇo nāma naro narāṇāṃ prasiddhacauraḥ kathitaḥ pṛthivyām | anekajanmārjitapāpasañcayam haratyaśeṣaṃ śrutamātra eva || 11.321 ||
skānde govindeti tathā proktaṃ bhaktyā vā bhaktivarjitaiḥ | dahate sarvapāpāni yugāntāgnirivotthitaḥ || 11.322 || govindanāmnā yaḥ kaścinnaro bhavati bhūtale | kīrtanādeva tasyāpi pāpaṃ yāti sahasradhā || 11.323 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe pramādādapi saṃspṛṣṭo yathānalakaṇo dahet | tathauṣṭhapuṭasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ harināma dahedagham || 11.324 ||
bṛhannāradīye lubdhakopakhyānānte narāṇāṃ viṣayāndhānāṃ mamatākulacetasām | ekameva harernāma sarvapāpavināśanam || 11.325 ||
ata eva tatraiva yamenoktam hari hari sakṛduccāritam dasyucchalena yairmanuṣyaiḥ | jananījaṭharamārgaluptā na mama paṭalipiṃ viśanti martyāḥ || 11.326 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye devaśarmopākhyānānte śrīnāradoktau hatyāyutaṃ pānasahasramugram gurvaṅganākoṭiniṣevanaṃ ca | steyānyanekāni haripriyeṇa govindanāmnā nihatāni sadyaḥ || 11.327 ||
anicchayāpi dahati spṛṣṭo hutavaho yathā | tathā dahati govindanāma vyājādapīritam || 11.328 ||
tatraiva śrīyamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde kīrtanādeva kṛṣṇasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ | duritāni vilīyante tamāṃsīva dinodaye || 11.329 || nānyatpaśyāmi jantūnāṃ vihāya karikīrtanam | sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottama || 11.330 ||
ṣaṣṭhaskandhe ayaṃ hi kṛtanirveśo janmakoṭyaṃhasāmapi | yadvyājahāra vivaśo nāma svastyayanaṃ hareḥ || 11.331 || stenaḥ surāpo mitradhrugbrahmahā gurutalpagaḥ | strīrājapitṛgohantā ye ca pātakino'pare || 11.332 || sarveṣāmapyaghavatāmidameva suniṣkṛtam | nāmavyāharaṇaṃ viṣṇoryatastadviṣayā matiḥ || 11.333 ||
na niṣkṛtairuditairbrahmavādibhis tathā viśuddhyatyaghavān vratādibhiḥ | yathā harernāmapadairudāhṛtais taduttamaślokaguṇopalambhakam || 11.334 ||
sāṅketyaṃ pārihāsyaṃ vā stobhaṃ helanameva vā | vaikuṇṭhanāmagrahaṇamaśeṣāghaharaṃ viduḥ || 11.335 || patitaḥ skhalito bhagnaḥ sandaṣṭastapta āhataḥ | harirityavaśenāha pumānnārhati yātanāḥ || 11.336 ||
ajñānādathavā jñānāduttamaślokanāma yat | saṅkīrtitamaghaṃ puṃso dahededho yathānalaḥ || 11.337 ||
brahmahā pitṛhā goghno mātṛhācāryahāghavān | śvādaḥ pulkasako vāpi śuddhyeran yasya kīrtanāt || 11.338 ||
laghubhāgavate vartamānaṃ tu yatpāpaṃ yadbhūtaṃ yadbhaviṣyati | tatsarvaṃ nirdahatyāśu govindānalakīrtanāt || 11.339 || sadā drohaparo yastu sajjanānāṃ mahītale | jāyate pāvano dhanyo harernāmānukīrtanāt || 11.340 ||
kaurme vasanti yāni koṭistu pāvanāni mahītale | na tāni tattulāṃ yānti kṛṣṇanāmānukīrtane || 11.341 ||
bṛhadviṣṇupurāṇe nāmno'sya yāvatī śaktiḥ pāpanirharaṇe hareḥ | tāvatkartuṃ na śaknoti pātakaṃ pātakī janaḥ || 11.342 ||
itihāsottame svādo'pi na hi śaknoti kartuṃ pāpāni yatnataḥ | tāvantī yāvatī śaktirviṣṇornāmno'subhakṣaye || 11.343 ||
viśeṣataḥ kalau, skānde tannāsti karmajaṃ loke vāgjaṃ mānasameva vā | yanna kṣapayate pāpaṃ kalau govindakīrtanam || 11.344 ||
viṣṇudharmottare śamāyālaṃ jalaṃ vahnestamaso bhaskarodayaḥ | śāntyai kaleraghaughasya nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ hareḥ || 11.345 ||
nāmnāṃ hareḥ kīrtanataḥ prayāti saṃsārapāraṃ duritaughamuktaḥ | naraḥ sa satyaṃ kalidoṣajanma pāpaṃ nihatyāśu kimatra citram || 11.346 ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe varākacāndrāyaṇataptakṛcchrair na dehiśuddhirbhavatīha tādṛk | kalau sakṛnmādhavakīrtanena govindanāmnā bhavatīha yādṛk || 11.347 ||
kīrtanakartrkulasaṅgyādipavanatvaṃ
tatraiva mahāpātakayukto'pi kīrtayennaniśaṃ harim | śuddhāntaḥkaraṇo bhūtvā jāyate paṅktipāvanaḥ || 11.348 ||
laghubhāgavate govindeti mudā yuktaḥ kīrtayedyastvananyadhīḥ | pāvanena ca dhanyena teneyaṃ pṛthivī dhṛtā || 11.349 ||
