haribhaktisudhodaye na caivamekaṃ vaktāraṃ jihvā rakṣati vaiṣṇavī | āśrāvya bhagavatkhyātiṃ jagatkṛtsnaṃ punāti hi || 11.350 ||
daśamaskandhe yannāma gṛhṇannakhilan śrotṝnātmānameva ca | sadyaḥ punāti kiṃ bhūyastasya spṛṣṭaḥ padā hi te || 11.351 ||
ata evoktaṃ prahlādena nārasiṃhe te santaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ nirupādhikabāndhavāḥ | ye nṛsiṃha bhavannāma gāyantyuccairmudānvitāḥ || 11.352 ||
sarvavyadhinasitvaṃ
bṛhannāradīye bhagavattoṣaprasaṅge acyutānandagovindanāmoccaraṇabhīṣitaḥ | naśyanti sakalā rogāḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyaham || 11.353 ||
parāśarasaṃhitāyāṃ sāmbaṃ prati vyāsoktau na sāmba vyādhijaṃ duḥkhaṃ heyaṃ nānyauṣadhairapi | harināmauṣadhaṃ pītvā vyādhistyājyo na saṃśayaḥ || 11.354 ||
skānde ādhayo vyādhayo yasya smaraṇānnāmakīrtanāt | tadaiva vilayaṃ yānti tamanantaṃ namāmyaham || 11.355 ||
vahnipurāṇe mahāvyādhisamācchanno rājavadhopāpiditaḥ | nārāyaṇeti saṅkīrtya nirāṭaṅko bhavennaraḥ || 11.356 ||
sarvaduḥkhopaśamanatvam
brhadviṣṇupurāṇe sarvarogopaśamanaṃ sarvopadravanāśanam | śāntidaṃ sarvāriṣṭānāṃ harernāmānukīrtanam || 11.357 ||
brahmavaivarte sarvapāpapraśamanaṃ sarvopadravanāśanam | sarvaduḥkhakṣayakāraṃ harināmānukīrtanam || 11.358 || dvādaśaskandhe saṅkīrtyamāno bhagavānanantaḥ śrutanubhāvo vyasanaṃ hi puṃsām | praviśya cittaṃ vidhunotyaśeṣaṃ yathā tamo'rko'bhramivātivātaḥ || 11.359 ||
viṣṇudharmottare ārtā viṣaṇṇāḥ śithilāśca bhītā ghoreṣu ca vyādhiṣu vartamānāḥ | saṅkīrtya nārāyaṇaśabdamekaṃ vimuktaduḥkhāḥ sukhino bhavanti || 11.360 ||
kīrtanaddevadevasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ | yakṣarakṣasavetalabhūtapretavinayakaḥ || 11.361 || dakinyo vidravanti sma ye tathānye ca siṃhakaḥ | sarvānarthaharaṃ tasya nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ smṛtam || 11.362 ||
kiṃ ca nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ kṛtvā kṣuṭtṛṭpraskhalitādiṣu | viyogaṃ śīghramāpnoti sarvānarthairna saṃśayaḥ || 11.363 ||
pādme devahutistutau mohanalollasajjvālajvalallokeṣu sarvadā | yannāmāmbhodharacchāyāṃ praviṣṭo naiva dahyate || 11.364 ||
kalibadhapaharitvam
skānde kalikalakusarpasya tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrasya mā bhayam | govindanāmadāvena dagdho yāsyati bhasmatām || 11.365 ||
bṛhannāradīye kalidharmaprasaṅge harināmaparā ye ca ghore kaliyuge narāḥ | ta eva kṛtakṛtyāśca na kalirbādhate hi tān || 11.366 || hare keśava govinda vāsudeva jaganmaya | itīrayanti te nityaṃ na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ || 11.367 ||
viṣṇudharmottare ye'harniśaṃ jagaddhāturvāsudevasya kīrtanam | kurvanti tānnaravyāghra na kalirbādhate narān || 11.368 ||
nārakyuddhārakatvam
nārasiṃhe yathā yathā harernāma kīrtayanti sma nārakāḥ | tathā tathā harau bhaktimudvahanto divaṃ yayuḥ || 11.369 ||
itihāsottame narake pacyamānānāṃ narāṇāṃ pāpakarmaṇām | muktiḥ sañjāyate tasmānnāmasaṅkīrtanāddhareḥ || 11.370 || prārabdhavināśitvam
ṣaṣṭhaskandhe nātaḥ paraṃ karmanibandhakṛntanaṃ mumukṣatāṃ tīrthapadānukīrtanāt | na yatpunaḥ karmasu sajjate mano rajastamobhyāṃ kalilaṃ tato'nyathā || 11.371 ||
dvādaśe ca yannāmadheyaṃ mriyamāṇa āturaḥ patan skhalan vā vivaśo gṛṇan pumān | vimuktakarmārgala uttamāṃ gatiṃ prāpnoti yakṣyanti na taṃ kalau janāḥ || 11.372 ||
uktya karmanibandheti tathā karmārgaleti ca | avaśyabhogyatāpatteḥ prārabdhe paryavasyati || 11.373 ||
ata eva bṛhannāradīye govindeti japan jantuḥ pratyahaṃ niyatendriyaḥ | sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ suravadbhāsate naraḥ || 11.374 || sarvapārādhabhajanatvam
viṣṇuyāmale śrībhagavaduktau mama nāmāni loke'smin śraddhayā yastu kīrtayet | tasyāparādhakoṭistu kṣamāmyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 11.375 ||
sarvasampūrtikāritvam
aṣṭamaskandhe śrībhagavantaṃ prati śrīśukroktau mantratastantrataśchidraṃ deśakālārhavastutaḥ | sarvaṃ karoti niśchidramanusaṅkīrtanaṃ tava || 11.376 ||
skānde ca yasya smṛtyā ca nāmoktyā tapoyajñakriyādiṣu | nyūnaṃ sampūrṇatāmeti sadyo vande tamacyutam || 11.377 || sarvavedādhikatvam
viṣṇudharmottare śrīprahlādoktau rgvedo hi yajurvedaḥ sāmavedo'pyatharvaṇaḥ | adhītastena yenoktaṃ harirityakṣaradvayam || 11.378 ||
skānde śrīparvatyuktau mā ṛco mā yajustāta mā sāma paṭha kiṃcana | govindeti harernāma geyaṃ gāyasva nityaśaḥ || 11.379 ||
pādme ca śrīrāmāṣṭottaraśatanāmastotre viṣṇorekaikanāmāpi sarvavedādhikaṃ matam | tadṛṅnāmasahasreṇa rāmanāma samaṃ smṛtam || 11.380 ||
sarvatīrthādhikatvam
skānde kurukṣetreṇa kiṃ tasya kiṃ kasya puskareṇa vā | jihvāgre vasate yasya harirityakṣaradvayam || 11.381 || vāmane tīrthakoṭisahasrāṇi tīrthakoṭiśatāni ca | tani sarvāṇyavāpnoti viṣṇornāmānukīrtanāt || 11.382 ||
viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyām viśrutāṇi bahūnyeva tīrthāni bahudhāni ca | kotyaṃśenāpi tulyāni nāmakīrtanato hareḥ || 11.383 ||
laghubhāgavate kiṃ tata vedāgamaśāstravistarais tīrthairanekairapi kiṃ prayojanam | yadyātmano vāñchasi muktikāraṇaṃ govinda govinda iti sphutaṃ raṭa || 11.384 ||
sarvasatkarmādhikatvam
gokoṭidānaṃ grahaṇe khagasya prayāgagaṅgodakakalpavāsaḥ | yajñāyutaṃ merusuvarṇadānaṃ govindakīrterna samaṃ śatāṃśaiḥ || 11.385 ||
baudhayanasaṃhitāyām iṣṭapūrtāni karmāṇi subahūni kṛtānyapi | bhavahetūni tānyeva harernāma tu muktidam || 11.386 ||
gāruḍe śrīśaunakāmbarīṣasaṃvāde vājapeyasahasrāṇāṃ nityaṃ phalamabhīpsasi | prātarutthāya bhūpāla kuru govindakīrtanam || 11.387 || kiṃ kariṣyati sāṅkhyena kiṃ yogairnaranāyaka | muktimicchasi rājendra kuru govindakīrtanam || 11.388 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīkapiladevaṃ prati devahūtyuktau aho bata śvapaco'to garīyān yajjihvāgre vartate nāma tubhyam | tepustapaste juhuvursasnuraryā brahmānūcurnāma gṛhṇanti ye te || 11.389 ||
sarvatīrthapradatvaṃ
skānde brahmanāradasaṃvāde caturmāsyamāhātmye etatsadvargaharaṇaṃ ripunigrahaṇaṃ param | adhyātmamūlametaddhi viṣṇornāmānukīrtanam || 11.390 ||
viṣṇudharmottare hṛdi kṛtvā tathā kāmamabhīṣṭaṃ dvijapuṅgavaḥ | ekaṃ nāma japedyastu śataṃ kāmānavāpnuyāt || 11.391 ||
tatraiva śrīkṛṣṇāmṛtastotre sarvamaṅgalamaṅgalyamāyuṣyaṃ vyādhināśanam | bhuktimuktipradaṃ divyaṃ vāsudevasya kīrtanam || 11.392 ||
śrīnārāyaṇavyūhastave parihāsopahāsādyairviṣṇorgṛhṇanti nāma te | kṛtārthāste'pi manujāstebhyo'pīha namo namaḥ || 11.393 ||
vārāhe ca te dhanyāste kṛtārthāśca taireva sukṛtaṃ kṛtam | tairāptaṃ janmanaḥ prāpyaṃ ye kāle kīrtayanti mām || 11.394 ||
viśeṣataḥ kalau sakṛduccārayantyetaddurlabhaṃ cākṛtātmanām | kalau yuge harernāma te kṛtārthā na saṃśayaḥ || 11.395 ||
ekādaśaskandhe ca kaliṃ sabhājayantyāryā guṇajāḥ sārabhāginaḥ | yatra saṅkīrtanenaiva sarvaḥ svartho'bhilabhyate || 11.396 ||
skānde tatraiva tathā caivottamaṃ loke tapaḥ śrīharikīrtanam | kalau yuge viśeṣeṇa viṣṇuprītyai samācaret || 11.397 ||
sarvaśaktimattvam
yathā skānde dānavratatapastīrthayātrādīnāṃ ca yāḥ sthitāḥ | śaktayo devamahatāṃ sarvapāpaharāḥ śubhāḥ || 11.398 || rājasūyāśvamedhānāṃ jñānasyādhyātmavastunaḥ | ākṛṣya hariṇā sarvāḥ sthāpitāḥ sveṣu nāmasu || 11.399 || vāto'pyato harernāmna ugrāṇāmapi duḥsahaḥ | sarveṣāṃ pāparāśīnāṃ yathaiva tamasāṃ raviḥ || 11.400 ||
ataiva brahmāṇḍe sarvārthaśaktiyuktasya devadevasya cakriṇaḥ | yaccābhirucitaṃ nāma tatsarvārtheṣu yojayet || 11.401 ||
jagadānandakatvam
śrīmadbhagavadgītāsu sthāne hṛṣīkeśa tava prakīrtyā jagatprahṛṣyatyanurajyate ca | rakṣāṃsi bhītāni diśo dravanti sarve namasyanti ca siddhasaṃghāḥ || 11.402 ||
jagadvandyatāpādakatvam
bṛhannāradīye nārāyaṇa jagannātha vāsudeva janārdana | itīrayanti ye nityaṃ te vai sarvatra vanditāḥ || 11.403 ||
śrīsūtenoktaṃ tatraiva yajñadhvajopākhyānānte svapan bhuñjan vrajaṃstiṣṭhaṃśca vadaṃstathā | ye vadanti harernāma tebhyo nityaṃ namo namaḥ || 11.404 ||
śrīnārāyaṇavyūhastave strī śūdraḥ pukkaśo vāpi ye cānye pāpayonayaḥ | kīrtayanti hariṃ bhaktyā tebhyo'pīha namo namaḥ || 11.405 ||
agatyekagatitvam
pādme bṛhatsahasranāmakathanārambhe ananyagatayo martyā bhogino'pi parantapāḥ | jñānavairāgyarahitā brahmacaryādivarjitāḥ || 11.406 || sarvadharmojjhitā viṣṇornāmamātraikajalpakāḥ | sukhena yāṃ gatiṃ yānti na tāṃ sarve'pi dhārmikāḥ || 11.407 ||
sadā sarvatra sevyatvam
viṣṇudharme kṣatrabandhūpākhyāne na deśaniyamastasminna kālaniyamastathā | nocchiṣṭhādau niṣedho'sti śrīharernāmni lubdhaka || 11.408 ||
skānde, pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye, viṣṇudharmottare ca cakrāyudhasya nāmāni sadā sarvatra kīrtayet | nāśaucaṃ kīrtane tasya sa pavitrakaro yataḥ || 11.409 ||
punaḥ skandhe na deśakālāvasthāsu śuddhyādikamapekṣate | kintu svatantramevaitannāma kāmitakāmadam || 11.410 ||
vaiśvānarasaṃhitāyām na deśakālaniyamo na śaucāśaucanirṇayaḥ | paraṃ saṅkīrtanādeva rāma rāmeti mucyate || 11.411 ||
vaiṣṇavacintāmaṇau śrīyudhiṣṭhiraṃ prati śrīnāradavākyaṃ na deśaniyamo rājanna kālaniyamastathā | vidyate nātra sandeho viṣṇornāmānukīrtane || 11.412 || kālo'sti dāne yajñe ca sthāne kālo'sti sajjape | viṣṇusaṅkīrtane kālo nāstyatra pṛthivītale || 11.413 ||
dvitīyaskandhe etannirvidyamānānāmicchatāmakutobhayam | yogināṃ nṛpa nirṇītaṃ harernāmānukīrtanam || 11.414 ||
muktipradatvam
vārāhe nārāyaṇācyutānanta vāsudeveti yo naraḥ | satataṃ kīrtayedbhūmi yāti mallayatāṃ sa hi || 11.415 ||
gāruḍe kiṃ kariṣyati sāṅkhyena kiṃ yogairnaranāyaka | muktimicchasi rājendra kuru govindakīrtanam || 11.416 ||
yathā, padmapurāṇe uttarakhaṇḍe sakṛduccāritaṃ yena harirityakṣaradvayam | baddhaḥ parikarastena mokṣāya gamanaṃ prati || 11.417 || brahmapurāṇe apyanyacitto'śuddho vā yaḥ sadā kīrtayeddharim | so'pi doṣakṣayānmuktiṃ labheccedipatiryathā || 11.418 ||
pādme devahūtistutau sakṛduccārayedyastu nārāyaṇamatandritaḥ | śuddhāntaḥkaraṇo bhūtvā virvāṇamadhigacchati || 11.419 ||
mātsye paradārarato vāpi parāpakṛtikārakaḥ | sa śuddho muktimāpnoti harernāmānukīrtanāt || 11.420 ||
vaiśampāyanasaṃhitāyām sarvadharmabahirbhūtaḥ sarvapāparatastathā | mucyate nātra sandeho viṣṇornāmānukīrtanāt || 11.421 ||
bṛhannāradīye yathā kathañcityannāsti kīrtite vā śrute'pi vā | pāpino'pi viśuddhāḥ syuḥ śuddhā mokṣamavāpnuyuḥ || 11.422 ||
bhāratavibhāge prāṇaprayāṇapātheyaṃ saṃsāravyādhibheṣajam | duḥkhaśokaparitrāṇaṃ harirityakṣaradvayam || 11.423 ||
nāradīye navyaṃ navyaṃ nāmadheyaṃ murārer yadyaccaitadgeyapīyūṣapuṣṭam | ye gāyanti tyaktalajjāḥ saharṣaṃ jīvanmuktāḥ saṃśayo nāsti tatra || 11.424 ||
prathamaskandhe āpannaḥ saṃsṛtiṃ ghorāṃ yannāma vivaśo gṛṇan | tataḥ sadyo vimucyeta yadbibheti svayaṃ bhayam || 11.425 ||
tṛtīye brahmastutau yasyāvatāraguṇakarmaviḍambanāni nāmāni ye'suvigame vivaśā gṛṇanti | te'naikajanmaśamalaṃ sahasaiva hitvā saṃyāntyapāvṛtāmṛtaṃ tamajaṃ prapadye || 11.426 ||
ṣaṣṭhe etāvatālamaghanirharaṇāya puṃsāṃ saṅkīrtanaṃ bhagavato guṇakarmanāmnām | vikruśya putramaghavān yadajāmilo'pi nārāyaṇeti mriyamāṇa iyāya muktim || 11.427 ||
śrīvaikuṇṭhalokaprāpakatvam
uktaṃ ca laiṅge śrīnāradaṃ prati śrīśivena vrajaṃstiṣṭhan svapannaśnan śvasan vākyaprapūraṇe | nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ viṣṇorhelayā kalimardanam | kṛtvā svarūpatāṃ yāti bhaktiyuktaṃ paraṃ vrajet || 11.428 || nāradīye śrībrahmaṇā brāhmaṇaḥ śvapacīṃ bhuñjan viśeṣeṇa rajasvalām | aśnāti surayā pakvaṃ maraṇe harimuccaran || 11.429 || abhakṣyāgamyayorjātaṃ vihāyāghaughasañcayam | prayāti viṣṇusālokyaṃ vimukto bhavabandhanaiḥ || 11.430 ||
bṛhannāradīye śukraṃ prati śrībalinā jihvāgre vartate yasya harirityakṣaradvayam | viṣṇorlokamavāpnoti punarāvṛttidurlabham || 11.431 ||
pādme yatra tatra sthito vāpi kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇeti kīrtayet | sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā sa gacchetparamāṃ gatim || 11.432 ||
tatraiva vaiśākhamāhātmye ambarīṣaṃ prati nāradena tadeva puṇyaṃ paramaṃ pavitraṃ govindagehe gamanāya patram | tadeva loke sukṛtaikasatraṃ yaducyate keśavanāmamātram || 11.433 ||
brahmavaivarte evaṃ saṅgrahaṇīputrābhidhānavyājato harim | samuccāryāntakāle'gāddhāma tatparamaṃ hareḥ || 11.434 || nārāyaṇamiti vyājāduccārya kaluṣāśrayaḥ | ajāmilo'pyagāddhāma kimuta śraddhayā gṛṇan || 11.435 ||
ṣaṣṭhaskandhe mriyamāṇo harernāma gṛṇan putropacāritam | ajāmilo'pyagāddhāma kimuta śraddhayā gṛṇan || 11.436 ||
vāmane ye kīrtayanti varadaṃ varapadmanābhaṃ śaṅkhābjacakraśaracāpagadāsihastam | padmālayāvadanapaṅkajaṣaṭpadākṣaṃ nūnaṃ prayānti sadanaṃ madhughātinaste || 11.437 ||
āṅgirasapurāṇe vāsudeveti manuja uccārya bhavabhītitaḥ | tanmuktaḥ padamāpnoti viṣṇoreva na saṃśayaḥ || 11.438 ||
nandipurāṇe sarvadā sarvakāleṣu ye'pi kurvanti pātakam | nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ kṛtvā yānti viṣṇoḥ paraṃ padam || 11.439 ||
viśeṣataḥ kalau, dvādaśaskandhe kalerdoṣanidhe rājannasti hyeko mahān guṇaḥ | kīrtanādeva kṛṣṇasya muktasaṅgaḥ paraṃ vrajet || 11.440 ||
yathā gāruḍe yadicchasi paraṃ jñānaṃ jñānādyatparamaṃ padam | tadādareṇa rājendra kuru govindakīrtanam || 11.441 ||
śrībhagavatprīṇanatvam
vārāhe vāsudevasya saṅkīrtyā surāpo vyādhito'pi vā | mukto jāyeta niyataṃ mahāviṣṇuḥ prasīdati || 11.442 ||
bṛhannāradīye nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ viṣṇoḥ kṣuttṛṭpraskhalitādiṣu | karoti satataṃ viprāstasya prīto hyadhokṣajaḥ || 11.443 ||
viṣṇudharmottare nāmasaṅkīrtanaṃ viṣṇoḥ kṣuttṛṭpraskhalitādiṣu | yaḥ karoti mahābhāga tasya tuṣyati keśavaḥ || 11.444 ||
atha śrībhagavadvaśīkāritvam
viṣṇurahasye yadabhyarcya hariṃ bhaktyā kṛte kratuśatairapi | phalaṃ prāpnotyavikalaṃ kalau govindakīrtanāt || 11.445 ||
ādipurāṇe gītvā ca mama nāmāni vicarenmama sannidhau | idaṃ bravīmi te satyaṃ krīto'haṃ tasya cārjuna || 11.446 || gītvā ca mama nāmāni vicarenmama sannidhau | iti bravīmi te satyaṃ krīto'haṃ tasya cārjuna || 11.447 || iti |
evaṃ ca śrutvā ca mama nāmāni ityādi || 11.448 ||
viṣṇudharme prahlādena jitaṃ tena jitaṃ tena jitaṃ teneti niścitam | jihvāgre vartate yasya harirityakṣaradvayam || 11.449 ||
svataḥ paramapuruṣārthatvam
skānde kāśīkhaṇḍe, pādme ca vaiśākhamāhātmye idameva hi māṅgalyametadeva dhanārjanam | jīvitasya phalaṃ caitadyaddāmodarakīrtanam || 11.450 ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe madhuramadhurametanmaṅgalaṃ maṅgalānāṃ sakalanigamavallīsatphalaṃ citsvarūpam | sakṛdapi parigītaṃ śraddhayā helayā vā bhṛguvara naramātraṃ tārayetkṛṣṇanāma || 11.451 ||
viṣṇurahasye etadeva paraṃ jñānametadeva paraṃ tapaḥ | etadeva paraṃ tattvaṃ vāsudevasya kīrtanam || 11.452 ||
vaiṣṇavacintāmaṇau śrīśivomāsaṃvāde aghacchitsmaraṇaṃ viṣṇorbahvāyāsena sādhyate | oṣṭhaspandanamātreṇa kīrtanaṃ tu tato varam || 11.453 || anyatra ca yena janmaśataiḥ pūrvaṃ vāsudevaḥ samarcitaḥ | tanmukhe harināmāni sadā tiṣṭhanti bhārata || 11.454 ||
viśeṣataḥ kalau, rahasye yadabhyarcya hariṃ bhaktyā kṛte kratuśatairapi | phalaṃ prāpnotyavikalaṃ kalau govindakīrtanāt || 11.455 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe dhyāyan kṛte yajan yajñaistretāyāṃ dvāpare'rcayan | yadāpnoti tadāpnoti kalau saṅkīrtya keśavam || 11.456 ||
dvādaśaskandhe kṛte yaddhyāyato viṣṇuṃ tretāyāṃ yajato makhaiḥ | dvāpare paricaryāyāṃ kalau taddharikīrtanāt || 11.457 ||
ekādaśe kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tviṣākṛṣṇaṃ sāṅgopāṅgāstrapārṣadam | yajñaiḥ saṅkīrtanaprāyairyajanti hi sumedhasaḥ || 11.458 ||
skānde ca mahābhāgavatā nityaṃ kalau kurvanti kīrtanam || 11.459 ||
bṛhannāradīye nāradoktaṃ harernāmaiva nāmaiva nāmaiva mama jīvanam | kalau nāstyeva nāstyeva nāstyeva gatiranyathā || 11.460 ||
ataivoktam sakṛduccārayantyeva harernāma cidātmakam | phalaṃ nāsya kṣamo vaktuṃ sahasravadano vidhiḥ || 11.461 ||
pādmottarakhaṇḍe śrīrāmāṣṭottaraśatanāmastotre śrīśivena rakārādīni nāmāni śṛṇvato devi jāyate | prītirme manaso nityaṃ rāmanāmaviśaṅkayā || 11.462 ||
vaiṣṇavacintāmaṇau īśo'haṃ sarvajagatāṃ nāmnāṃ viṣṇorhi jāpakaḥ | satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyeṣa harernāma gatirnṝṇām || 11.463 ||
ādipurāṇe ca śrīkṛṣṇārjunasaṃvāde śraddhayā helayā nāma raṭanti mama jantavaḥ | teṣāṃ nāma sadā pārtha vartate hṛdaye mama || 11.464 || na nāma sadṛśaṃ jñānaṃ na nāma sadṛśaṃ vratam | na nāma sadṛśaṃ dhyānaṃ na nāma sadṛśaṃ phalam || 11.465 || na nāma sadṛśastyāgo na nāma sadṛśaḥ śamaḥ | na nāma sadṛśaṃ puṇyaṃ na nāma sadṛśī gatiḥ || 11.466 ||
kiṃ ca nāmaiva paramā muktirnāmaiva paramā gatiḥ | nāmaiva paramā śāntirnāmaiva paramā sthitiḥ || 11.467 || nāmaiva paramā bhaktirnāmaiva paramā matiḥ | nāmaiva paramā prītirnāmaiva paramā smṛtiḥ || 11.468 || nāmaiva kāraṇaṃ jantornāmaiva prabhureva ca | nāmaiva paramārādhyo nāmaiva paramo guruḥ || 11.469 ||
kiṃ ca nāmayuktān janān dṛṣṭvā snigdho bhavati yo naraḥ | sa yāti paramaṃ sthānaṃ viṣṇunā saha modate || 11.470 || tasmānnāmāni kaunteya bhajasva dṛḍhamānasaḥ | nāmayuktaḥ priyo'smākaṃ nāmayukto bhavārjuna || 11.471 ||
atha śrībhagavannāmajapasya smaraṇasya ca | śravaṇasyāpi māhātmyamīṣadbhedādvilikhyate || 11.472 ||
atha śrīmannāmajapamāhātmyam
viṣṇurahasye śrībhagavaduktau satyaṃ bravīmi manujāḥ svayamūrdvabāhur yo māṃ mukunda narasiṃha janārdaneti | jīvan japatyanudinaṃ maraṇe ṛṇīva pāṣāṇakāṣṭhasadṛśāya dadāmyabhīṣṭam || 11.473 ||
kāśīkāṇḍe agnibindustutau nārāyaṇeti narakārṇavatāraṇeti dāmodareti madhuheti caturbhujeti | viśvambhareti virajeti janārdaneti kvāstīha janma japatāṃ kva kṛtāntabhītiḥ || 11.474 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye yamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde vāsudevajapāsaktānapi pāpakṛto janān | nopasarpanti tān vipra yamadūtāśca dāruṇāḥ || 11.475 ||
bṛhadviṣṇupurāṇe kva nākapṛṣṭhagamanaṃ punarāvṛttilakṣaṇam | kva japo vāsudeveti muktibījamanuttamam || 11.476 ||
śrīmannāmasmaraṇamāhātmyam
itihāsottame svapne'pi nāmasmṛtirādipuṃsaḥ kṣayaṃ karotyāhitapāparāśeḥ | prayatnataḥ kiṃ punarādipuṃsaḥ prakīrtite nāgnirjanārdanasya || 11.477 ||
laghubhāgavate te sabhāgyā manuṣyeṣu kṛtārthā nṛpa niścitam | smaranti ye smārayanti harernāma kalau yuge || 11.478 ||
pādme devahūtistutau prayāṇe cāprayāṇe ca yannāmasmaraṇānnṝṇām | sadyo naśyati pāpaugho namastasmai cidātmane || 11.479 ||
tatraivottarakhaṇḍe yannāmasmaraṇādeva pāpināmapi satvaram | muktirbhavati jātūnāṃ brahmādīnāṃ sudurlabhā || 11.480 ||
brahmavaivarte yadanudhyānadāvāgnidagdhakarmatṛṇaḥ pumān | viśuddhaḥ paśyati vyaktamavyaktamapi keśavam || 11.481 || tadasya nāma jīvasya patitasya bhavāmbudhau | hastāvalambadānāya pravīṇo nāparo hareḥ || 11.482 ||
jāvālisaṃhitāyām harernāma paraṃ japyaṃ dhyeyaṃ geyaṃ nirantaram | kīrtanīyaṃ ca bahudhā nirvṛtīrbahudhecchatā || 11.483 ||
atha śrībhagavannāmamāhātmyam
bṛhannāradīye śrīnāradoktau yannāmaśravaṇenāpi mahāpātakino'pi ye | pāvanatvaṃ prapadyante kathaṃ stoṣyāmi khinnadhīḥ || 11.484 ||
itihāsottame śrutaṃ saṅkīrtitaṃ vāpi harerāścaryakarmaṇaḥ | dahatyenāṃsi sarvāṇi prasaṅgātkimu bhaktitaḥ || 11.485 ||
ṣaṣṭhaskandhe citraketūktau na hi bhagavannaghaṭitamidaṃ tvaddarśanānnṛṇāmakhilapāpakṣayaḥ | yannāma sakṛcchravaṇāt pukkaśo'pi vimucyate saṃsārāt || 11.486 || iti |
śrīmannāmnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ māhātmyeṣu sameṣvapi | kṛṣṇasyaivāvatāreṣu viśeṣaḥ ko'pi kasyacit || 11.487 ||
atha viśeṣataḥ śrīkṛṣṇāvatāramāhātmyam
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe śrīkṛṣṇāṣṭottaraśatanāmamāhātmye sahasranāmnāṃ puṇyānāṃ trirāvṛttyā tu yatphalam | ekāvṛttyā tu kṛṣṇasya nāmaikaṃ tatprayacchati || 11.488 || idaṃ kirīṭī saṃjapya jayī pāśupatāstrabhāk | kṛṣṇasya prāṇabhūtaḥ san kṛṣṇaṃ sārathimāptavān || 11.489 || kimidaṃ bahunā śaṃsanmānuṣānandanirbharaḥ | brahmānandamavāpyānte kṛṣṇasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 11.490 ||
vārāhe ca śrīmāthuramāhātmye tatra guhyāni nāmāni bhaviṣyanti mama priye | puṇyāni ca pavitrāṇi saṃsāracchedanāni ca || 11.491 ||
tatraiva viśeṣataḥ śrīkṛṣṇeti nāmamāhātmyam
dvārakāmāhātmye prahlādabalisaṃvāde atītāḥ puruṣāḥ sapta bhaviṣāśca caturdaśa | narastārayate sarvān kalau kṛṣṇeti kīrtanāt || 11.492 || kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇeti kṛṣṇeti svapan jāgradvrajaṃstathā | yo jalpati kalau nityaṃ kṛṣṇarūpī bhaveddhi saḥ || 11.493 ||
brahmavaivarte hanan brāhmaṇamatyantaṃ kāmato vā surāṃ piban | kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇetyahorātraṃ saṅkīrtya śucitāmiyāt || 11.494 ||
viṣṇudharme kṛṣṇeti maṅgalaṃ nāma yasya vāci pravartate | bhasmībhavanti rājendra mahāpātakakoṭayaḥ || 11.495 ||
nārasiṃhe śrībhagavaduktau kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇeti kṛṣṇeti yo māṃ smarati nityaśaḥ | jalaṃ bhittvā yathā padmaṃ narakāduddharāmyaham || 11.496 ||
gāruḍe pādme ca saṃsārasarpasaṃdaṣṭaṃ naṣṭaceṣṭaikabheṣajam | kṛṣṇeti vaiṣṇavaṃ mantraṃ śrutvā mukto bhavennaraḥ || 11.497 ||
prabhāsapurāṇe nāmnāṃ mukhyataraṃ nāma kṛṣṇākhyaṃ me parantapa | prāyaścittamaśeṣāṇāṃ pāpānāṃ mocakaṃ param || 11.498 ||
pādme yatra yatra sthito vāpi kṛṣṇakṛṣṇeti kīrtayet | sarvapāpaviśuddhātmā sa gacchetparamāṃ gatim || 11.499 ||
viṣṇudharmottare śrīkṛṣṇasahasranāmastotre ballavīkānta kiṃ taistairupāyaiḥ kṛṣṇanāma te | kintu jihvāgragaṃ jāgrannirundhe hi mahābhayam || 11.500 ||
tatraivānyatra satyaṃ bravīmi te śambho gopanīyamidaṃ mama | mṛtyusaṃjīvanīṃ nāma kṛṣṇākhyāmavadhāraya || 11.501 ||
bhāratavibhāge kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ kṛṣṇa ityantakāle jalpan janturjīvitaṃ yo jahāti | ādyaḥ śabdaḥ kalpate tasya muktyai vrīḍānamrau tiṣṭhato'nyāvṛṇasthau || 11.502 ||
anyatrāpi nāma cintāmaṇiḥ kṛṣṇaścaitanyarasavigrahaḥ | pūrṇaḥ śuddho nityamukto bhinnatvānnāmanāminoḥ || 11.503 ||
ataivoktam tebhyo namo'stu bhavavāridhijīrṇapaṅka saṃmagnamokṣaṇavicakṣaṇapādukebhyaḥ | kṛṣṇeti varṇayugalaṃ śravaṇena yeṣāṃ ānandathūrbhavati nartitaromavṛndaḥ || 11.504 ||
kiṃ ca dvitīyaskandhe tadaśmasāraṃ hṛdayaṃ batedaṃ yadgṛhyamāṇairharināmadheyaiḥ | na vikriyetātha yadā vikāro netre jalaṃ gātraruheṣu harṣaḥ || 11.505 ||
itihāsottame ca nāmni saṅkīrtite viṣṇoryasya puṃso na jāyate | saromapulakaṃ gātraṃ sa bhavetkuliśopamaḥ || 11.506 ||
kātyāyanasaṃhitāyām nāmasaṅkīrtanājjātaṃ puṇyaṃ nopacayanti ye | nānāvyādhisamāyuktāḥ śatajanmasu te narāḥ || 11.507 || sā hānistanmahacchidraṃ sa mohaḥ sa ca vibhramaḥ | yanmuhūrtaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vāpi vāsudevo na cintyate || 11.508 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye yamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde avamatya ca ye yānti bhagavatkīrtanaṃ narāḥ | te yānti narakaṃ ghoraṃ tena pāpena karmaṇā || 11.509 ||
śrutayaḥ omāsya jānanto nāma cidviviktan mahaste viṣṇo sumatiṃ bhajāmahe || 11.510 ||
oṃ tatsatoṃ padaṃ devasya namasā vyantaḥ śravasyavaśrava āpannamṛktam | nāmāni ciddadhire yajñiyanti bhadrāyānte raṇayantaḥ sandṛṣṭau || 11.511 ||
oṃ tamu stotāraḥ pūrvaṃ yathāvida ṛtasya garbhaṃ januṣā pipartana | āsya jānanto nāma cidviviktan mahaste viṣṇo sumatiṃ bhajāmahe || 11.512 || ityādyā iti |
īdṛśe nāmamāhātmye śrutismṛtiviniścite | kalpayantyarthavādaṃ ye yānti ghorayātanām || 11.513 ||
atha śrībhagavannāmārthavādakalpanādūṣaṇam
śrīkātyāyanasaṃhitāyām arthavādaṃ harernāmni sambhāvayati yo naraḥ | sa pāpiṣṭho manuṣyāṇāṃ niraye patanti sphuṭam || 11.514 ||
brahmasaṃhitāyāṃ baudhāyanaṃ prati śrībhagavaduktau yannāmakīrtanaphalaṃ vividhaṃ niśamya na śraddadhāti manute yadutārthavādam | yo mānuṣastamiha duḥkhacaye kṣipāmi saṃsāraghoravividhārtinipīḍitāṅgam || 11.515 ||
jaiminisaṃhitāyāṃ ca śrutismṛtipurāṇeṣu nāmamāhātmyavāciṣu | ye'rthavāda iti brūyurna teṣāṃ nirayakṣayaḥ || 11.516 || tasmiṃśca bhagavannāmni jagadekopakāriṇi | viśvaikasevye matimānaparādhān vivarjayet || 11.517 ||
yata uktaṃ pādme śrīnāradaṃ prati sanatkumāreṇa sarvāparādhakṛdapi mucyate harisaṃśrayāt || 11.518 || harerapyaparādhān yaḥ kuryāddvipadapāṃsavaḥ | nāmāśrayaḥ kadācitsyāttaratyeva sa nāmataḥ || 11.519 || nāmno'pi sarvasuhṛdo hyaparādhātpatatyadhaḥ || 11.520 ||
atha nāmāparādhāḥ
taṃ prati tenaivoktāḥ satāṃ nindā nāmnaḥ paramamaparādhaṃ vitanute yataḥ khyātiṃ yātaṃ kathamu sahate tadvigarhām | śivasya śrīviṣṇorya iha guṇanāmādisakalaṃ dhiyā bhinnaṃ paśyetsa khalu harināmāhitakaraḥ || 11.521 || guroravajñā śrutiśāstranindanaṃ tathārthavādo harināmni kalpanam | nāmno balādyasya hi pāpabuddhir na vidyate tasya yamairhi śuddhiḥ || 11.522 || dharmavratatyāgahutādisarva śubhakriyāsāmyamapi pramādaḥ | aśraddadhāne vimukhe'pyaśṛṇvati yaścopadeśaḥ śivanāmāparādhaḥ || 11.523 || śrute'pi nāmamāhātmye yaḥ prītirahito'dhamaḥ | ahaṃmamādiparamo nāmni so'pyaparādhakṛt || 11.524 || jāte nāmāparādhe'pi pramādena kathañcana | sadā saṅkīrtayannāma tadekaśaraṇo bhavet || 11.525 ||
aparādhabhañjanaṃ
uktaṃ ca tenaiva tatra nāmāparādhayuktānāṃ nāmānyeva harantyagham | aviśrāntaprayuktāni tānyevārthakarāṇi ca || 11.526 ||
nāmaikaṃ yasya vāci smaraṇapathagataṃ śrotramūlaṃ gataṃ vā śuddhaṃ vāśuddhavarṇaṃ vyavahitarahitaṃ tārayatyeva satyam | tacceddehadraviṇajanatālobhapāṣaṇḍamadhye nikṣiptaṃ syānna phalajanakaṃ śīghramevātra vipra || 11.527 ||
ataivoktaṃ śrīnāradena bṛhannāradīye mahmināmapi yannāmnaḥ pāraṃ gantumanīśvaraḥ | manavo'pi munīdrāśca kathaṃ kṣuṇṇadhīrbhaje || 11.528 || iti |
itthaṃ śrīkṛṣṇapādābje bhaktiḥ kāryā sadā budhaiḥ | sā ca tasya prasādena mahāpuṇyātprajāyate || 11.529 ||
atha śrīmadbhakterdurlabhatvam
skānde śrīparāśaroktau na hyapuṇyavatāṃ loke mūḍhānāṃ kuṭilātmanām | bhaktirbhavati govinde smaraṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ tathā || 11.530 || tatraiva śrībrahmoktau nimiṣaṃ nimiṣārdhaṃ vā martyānāmiha nārada | nādagdhāśeṣapāpānāṃ bhaktirbhavati keśave || 11.531 ||
yogavāśiṣṭhe janmāntarasahasreṣu tapojñānasamādhibhiḥ | narāṇāṃ kṣīṇapāpānāṃ kṛṣṇe bhaktiḥ prajāyate || 11.532 ||
ādivārāhe janmāntarasahasreṣu samārādhya vṛṣadhvajam | vaiṣṇavatvaṃ labheddhīmān sarvapāpakṣaye sati || 11.533 ||
bṛhannāradīye yajñadhvajanṛpopākhyānānte janmakoṭisahasreṣu puṇyaṃ yaiḥ samupārjitam | teṣāṃ bhaktirbhavecchuddhā devadeve janārdane || 11.534 || sulabhaṃ jāhnavīsnānaṃ tathaivātithipūjanam | sulabhāḥ sarvayajñāśca viṣṇubhaktiḥ sudurlabhā || 11.535 ||
itihāsasamuccaye śiloñchavṛttivākye gaṅgāyāṃ maraṇaṃ caiva dṛḍhā bhaktiśca keśave | brahmavidyāprabodhaśca nālpasya tapasaḥ phalam || 11.536 ||
agastyasaṃhitāyām vratopavāsaniyamairjanmakoṭyāpyaniṣṭhiteḥ | yajñaiśca vividhaiḥ samyagbhaktirbhavati keśave || 11.537 ||
viṣṇudharmottare divasaṃ divasārdhaṃ vā muhūrtaṃ caikameva vā | nāśāccāśeṣapāpasya bhaktirbhavati keśave || 11.538 || anekajanmasāhasrairnānāyonyantareṣu ca | jantoḥ kaluṣahīnasya bhaktirbhavati keśave || 11.539 ||
daśamaskandhe gopīḥ prati uddhavoktau dānavrātatapohoma japasvādhyāyasaṃyamaiḥ | śreyobhirvividhaiścānyaiḥ kṛṣṇe bhaktirhi sādhyate || 11.540 ||
śrībhagavadgītāsu yeṣāṃ tvantagataṃ pāpaṃ janānāṃ puṇyakarmaṇām | te dvandvamohanirmuktā bhajante māṃ dṛḍhavratāḥ || 11.541 ||
pañcamaskandhe parīkṣitaṃ prati śrībādarāyaṇinā rājan patirgururalaṃ bhavatāṃ yadūnāṃ daivaṃ priyaḥ kulapatiḥ kva ca kiṅkaro vaḥ | astvevamaṅga bhagavān bhajatāṃ mukundo muktiṃ dadāti karhicitsma na bhaktiyogam || 11.542 || ṣaṣṭhaskandhe vṛtropākhyānānte devānāṃ śuddhasattvānāmṛṣīṇāṃ cāmalātmanām | bhaktirmukundacaraṇe na prāyeṇopajāyate || 11.545 ||
śrīmadbhaktyai namastasyai yasyā māthāmtyamandaram | yatprabhāveṇa lolo'yaṃ kīṭo'pyuddhartumicchati || 11.546 ||
atha śrībhagavadbhaktimāhātmyaṃ
tatrādau bhaktimataḥ kathañcidāpatite'pi pāpe prāyaścittāntaranirasanatvam |
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye nāradāmbarīṣasaṃvāde yathāgniḥ susamṛddhārciḥ karotyedhāṃsi bhasmasāt | pāpāni bhagavadbhaktistathā dahati tatkṣaṇāt || 11.547 ||
ṣaṣṭhe ajāmilopākhyānārambhe kecitkevalayā bhaktyā vāsudevaparāyaṇāḥ | aghaṃ dhunvanti kārtsnyena nīhāramiva bhāskaraḥ || 11.548 ||
ekādaśe ca śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde yathāgniḥ susamṛddhārciḥ karotyedhāṃsi bhasmasāt | tathā madviṣayā bhaktiruddhavaināṃsi kṛtsnaśaḥ || 11.549 ||
ataivoktaṃ tatraiva śrīkarabhājanena svapādamūlaṃ bhajataḥ priyasya tyaktānyabhāvasya hariḥ pareśaḥ | vikarma yaccotpatitaṃ kathañcid dhunoti sarvaṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭaḥ || 11.550 ||
dvārakāmāhātmye candraśarmāṇaṃ prati śrībhagavatā madbhaktiṃ vahatāṃ puṃsāmiha loke pare'pi vā | nāśubhaṃ vidyate kiñcitkulakoṭiṃ nayedditam || 11.551 ||
viṣayabhoge'pi taddoṣanirākaratvam
ekādaśaskandhe tatraiva bādhyamāno'pi madbhakto viṣayairajitendriyaḥ | prāyaḥ pragalbhayā bhaktyā viṣayairnābhibhūyate || 11.552 ||
karmādhikāranirasanatvaṃ
tatraiva tāvatkarmāṇi kurvīta na nirvidyeta yāvatā | matkathāśravaṇādau vā śraddhā yāvanna jāyate || 11.553 ||
ataivoktaṃ prathamaskandhe tyaktvā svadharmaṃ caraṇāmbujaṃ harer bhajannapakvo'tha patettato yadi | yatra kva vābhadramabhūdamuṣya kiṃ ko vārtha āpto'bhajatāṃ svadharmataḥ || 11.554 ||
ekāntilakṣaṇe yacca likhitaṃ śaraṇāgatau | lekhyaṃ ca tattadvacanairetatsudṛḍhatāmiyāt || 11.555 ||
manaḥprasādakatvam
prathamaskandhe sa vai puṃsāṃ paro dharmo yato bhaktiradhokṣaje | ahaitukyapratihatā yayātmā suprasīdati || 11.556 || ataivoktamekādaśe dharmaḥ satyadayopeto vidyā vā tapasānvitā | madbhaktyāpetamātmānaṃ na samyakprapunāti hi || 11.557 ||
tatraiva bhaktiḥ punāti manniṣṭhā śvapākānapi sambhavāt || 11.558 ||
ṣaṣṭhe etāvāneva loke'smin puṃsāṃ dharmaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ | bhaktiyogo bhagavati tannāmagrahaṇādibhiḥ || 11.559 ||
ataivoktaṃ pādme kiṃ tasya bahubhiḥ mantraiḥ śāstraiḥ kiṃ bahuvistaraiḥ | vājapeyasahasraiḥ kiṃ bhaktiryasya janārdane || 11.560 ||
sarvaguṇādisevyatākāritvam
pañcamaskandhe prahlādoktau yasyāsti bhaktirbhagavatyakiñcanā sarvairguṇaistatra samāsate surāḥ | harāvabhaktasya kuto mahadguṇā manorathenāsati dhāvato bahiḥ || 11.561 ||
caturthe śrīdhruvaṃ prati manūtkau tvaṃ pratyagātmani tadā bhagavatyananta ānandamātra upapannasamastaśaktau | bhaktiṃ vidhāya paramāṃ śanakairavidyā granthiṃ vibhetsyasi mamāhamiti prarūḍham || 11.562 ||
śrīpṛthuṃ prati śrīsanakādibhiḥ yatpādapaṅkajapalāśavilāsabhaktyā karmāśayaṃ grathitamudgrathayanti santaḥ | tadvanna riktamatayo yatayo'pi ruddha srotogaṇāstamaraṇaṃ bhaja vāsudevam || 11.563 ||
sarvamārgādhikatvam
tṛtīye śrīkāpileye na yujyamānayā bhaktyā bhagavatyakhilātmani | sadṛśo'sti śivaḥ panthā yogināṃ brahmasiddhaye || 11.564 ||
ṣaṣṭhe ca sadhrīcīno hyayaṃ loke panthāḥ kṣemo'kutobhayaḥ | suśīlāḥ sādhavo yatra nārāyaṇaparāyaṇāḥ || 11.565 ||
ataivoktaṃ dvitīye śrībādarāyaṇinā na hyato'nyaḥ śivaḥ panthā viśataḥ saṃsṛtāviha | vāsudeve bhagavati bhaktiyogo yato bhavet || 11.566 || bhagavān brahma kārtsnyena triranvīkṣya manīṣayā | tadadhyavasyatkūṭastho ratirātmanyato bhavet || 11.567 ||
bṛhannāradīye śrīnāradoktaṃ yathā samastalokānāṃ jīvanaṃ salilaṃ smṛtam | tathā samastasiddhīnāṃ jīvanaṃ bhaktiriṣyate || 11.568 || jīvanti jantavaḥ sarve yathā mātaramāśritāḥ | tathā bhaktiṃ samāśritya sarve jīvanti dhārmikāḥ || 11.569 ||
dhvajāropaṇamāhātmye śrīviṣṇudūtoktau mahāpātakayukto vā yukto vā sarvapātakaiḥ | īpsitāṃ bhagavadbhaktyā labhate paramāṃ gatim || 11.570 ||
pādme vaiśākhamāhātmye yamabrāhmaṇasaṃvāde apatyaṃ draviṇaṃ dārā hārā harmyaṃ hayā gajāḥ | sukhāni svargamokṣau ca na dūre haribhaktitaḥ || 11.571 ||
prathamaskandhe vāsudeve bhagavati bhaktiyogaḥ prayojitaḥ | janayatyāśu vairāgyaṃ jñānaṃ ca yadahaitukam || 11.572 ||
ekādaśe ca yatkarmabhiryattapasā jñānavairāgyataśca yat | yogena dāmadharmeṇa śreyobhiritarairapi || 11.573 || sarvaṃ madbhaktiyogena madbhakto labhate'ñjasā | svargāpavargaṃ maddhāma kathañcidyadi vāñchati || 11.574 ||
ataivoktaṃ dvitīye akāmaḥ sarvakāmo vā mokṣakāma udāradhīḥ | tīvreṇa bhaktiyogena yajeta puruṣaṃ param || 11.575 ||
mokṣādhikatvam
tṛtīye kāpileye animittā bhāgavatī bhaktiḥ siddhergarīyasī | jarayatyāśu yā koṣaṃ nigīrṇamanalo yathā || 11.576 ||
pañcame śryṛṣabhadevacaritānte yasyāmeva kavaya ātmānamavirataṃ vividhavṛjinasaṃsāraparitāpopatapyamānamanusavanaṃ snāpayantastayaiva parayā nirvṛtyā hyapavargamātyantikaṃ paramapuruṣārthamapi svayamāsāditaṃ no evādriyante bhagavadīyatvenaiva parisamāptasarvārthāḥ || 11.577 ||
dvādaśe ca śrīmārkaṇḍeyamuddiśya śrīśivoktau naivecchatyāśiṣaḥ kvāpi brahmarṣirmokṣamapyuta | bhaktiṃ parāṃ bhagavati labdhavān puruṣe'vyaye || 11.578 ||
ataivoktaṃ pañcame śrībhagavantamuddiśya bādarāyaṇinā yo dustyajān kṣitisutasvajanārthadārān prārthyāṃ śriyaṃ suravaraiḥ sadayāvalokām | naicchannṛpastaducitaṃ mahatāṃ madhudviṭ sevānuraktamanasāmabhavo'pi phalguḥ || 11.579 ||
ekādaśe ca bhagavatā na pārameṣṭhyaṃ na mahendradhiṣṇyaṃ na sārvabhaumaṃ na rasādhipatyam | na yogasiddhīrapunarbhavaṃ vā mayyarpitātmecchati madvinānyat || 11.580 || ataivoktaṃ ṣaṣṭhe śrīrudreṇa vāsudeve bhagavati bhaktimudvahatāṃ nṝṇām | jñānavairāgyavīryāṇāṃ neha kaścidvyapāśrayaḥ || 11.581 ||
viṣṇupurāṇe ca śrīprahlādena dharmārthakāmaiḥ kiṃ tasya muktistasya kare sthitā | samastajagatāṃ mūle yasya bhaktiḥ sthitā tvayi || 11.582 ||
ataivoktaṃ nārasiṃhe patreṣu puṣpeṣu phaleṣu toyesv akrītalabhyeṣu sadaiva satsu | bhaktyā sulabhye puruṣe purāṇe muktau kimarthaṃ kriyate prayatnaḥ || 11.583 ||
ataivoktaṃ prathamaskandhe ātmārāmāśca munayo nirgranthā apyurukrame | kurvantyahaitukīṃ bhaktimitthambhūtaguṇo hariḥ || 11.584 ||
śrīvaikuṇṭhalokaprāpakatvam
vāmane yeṣāṃ cakragadāpāṇau bhaktiravyabhicāriṇī | te yānti niyataṃ sthānaṃ yatra yogeśvaro hariḥ || 11.585 ||
skānde munirjāpyaparo nityaṃ dṛḍhabhaktirjitendriyaḥ | svagṛhe'pi vasan yāti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam || 11.586 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīvaikuṇṭhavarṇane yacca vrajantyanimiṣāmṛṣabhānuvṛttyā dūre yamā hyupari naḥ spṛhaṇīyaśīlāḥ | bharturmithaḥ suyaśasaḥ kathanānurāga vaiklavyabāṣpakalayā pulakīkṛtāṅgāḥ || 11.587 ||
daśame ca śrībrahmastutau pureha bhūman bahavo'pi yoginas tvadarpitehā nijakarmalabdhayā | vibudhya bhaktyaiva kathopanītayā prapedire'ñjo'cyuta te gatiṃ parām || 11.588 ||
bṛhannāradīye bhagavattoṣapraśnottare sarvadevamayo viṣṇuḥ śaraṇārtipranāśanaḥ | svabhaktavatsalo devo bhaktyā tuṣyati nānyathā || 11.589 ||
saptamaskandhe śrīprahlādasya bālopadeśe nālaṃ dvijatvaṃ devatvamṛṣitvaṃ vāsurātmajāḥ | prīṇanāya mukundasya na vṛttaṃ na bahujñatā || 11.590 || na dānaṃ na tapo nejyā na śaucaṃ na vratāni ca | prīyate'malayā bhaktyā hariranyadviḍambanam || 11.591 ||
śrīnṛsiṃhastutau ca manye dhanābhijanarūpatapaḥśrutaujas tejaḥprabhāvabalapauruṣabuddhiyogāḥ | nārādhanāya hi bhavanti parasya puṃso bhaktyā tutoṣa bhagavān gajayūthapāya || 11.592 ||
anyatrāpi vyādhasyācaraṇaṃ dhruvasya ca vayo vidyā gajendrasya kā kubjāyāḥ kimu nāma rūpamadhikaṃ kiṃ tatsudāmno dhanam | vaṃśaḥ ko vidurasya yādavapaterugrasya kiṃ pauruṣaṃ bhaktyā tuṣyati kevalaṃ na ca guṇairbhaktipriyo mādhavaḥ || 11.593 ||
ataivoktaṃ śrībhagavatā patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyaṃ yo me bhaktyā prayacchati | tadahaṃ bhaktyupahṛtamaśnāmi prayatātmanaḥ || 11.594 ||
prathamaskandhe śrīhanumatoktam na janma nūnaṃ mahato na saubhagaṃ na vāṅna buddhirnākṛtistoṣahetuḥ | tairyadvisṛṣṭānapi no vanaukasaś cakāra sakhe bata lakṣmaṇāgrajaḥ || 11.595 ||
śrībhagavatsaṅgamakatvam
śrībhagavadgītāsu bhaktyā tvananyayā śakya ahamevaṃvidho'rjuna | jñātuṃ draṣṭuṃ ca tattvena praveṣṭuṃ ca paraṃtapa || 11.596 ||
ekādaśaskandhe ca śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde yathāgninā hemamalaṃ jahāti dhmātaṃ punaḥ svaṃ bhajate ca rūpam | ātmā ca karmānuśayaṃ vidhūya madbhaktiyogena bhajatyatho mām || 11.597 ||
kiṃ ca bhaktyoddhavānapāyinyā sarvalokamaheśvaram | sarvotpattyapyayaṃ brahma kāraṇaṃ mopayāti saḥ || 11.598 ||
śrībhagavadvaśīkāratvam
pādme kārttikamāhātmye śrīnāradaśaunakasaṃvāde bhuktiṃ muktiṃ harirdadyātarcito'nyatra sevinām | bhaktiṃ ca dadātyeṣa yato vaśyakarī hareḥ || 11.599 ||
tatraiva vaiśākhamāhātmye śrīnāradāmbarīṣasaṃvāde māyājāniramāyo'sau bhaktyā rājanna māyayā | sādhyate sādhupuruṣaiḥ svayaṃ jānāti tadbhavān || 11.600 ||
ekādaśaskandhe ca tatraiva na sādhayati māṃ yogo na sāṅkhyaṃ dharma uddhava | na svādhyāyastapastyāgo yathā bhaktirmamorjitā || 11.601 || dharmaḥ satyadayopeto vidyā vā tapasānvitā | madbhaktyāpetamātmānaṃ na samyakprapunāti hi || 11.602 ||
svataḥ paramapuruṣārthatā
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīkāpileye sālokyasārṣṭisāmīpya sārūpyaikatvamapyuta | dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇanti vinā matsevanaṃ janāḥ || 11.603 ||
navamaskandhe cāmbarīṣopākhyāne śrībhagavaduktau matsevayā pratītaṃ te sālokyādicatuṣṭayam | necchanti sevayā pūrṇāḥ kuto'nyatkālaviplutam || 11.604 || iti |
māhātmyaṃ yacca bhagavadbhaktānāṃ likhitaṃ purā | tadbhakterapi vijñeyaṃ teṣāṃ bhaktyaiva tattvataḥ || 11.605 || tathā pūjā tadaṅgānāṃ śrīmannāmno'parasya ca | draṣṭavyamiha māhātmyaṃ teṣāṃ bhaktyaṅgatā yataḥ || 11.606 ||
atha śrīmadbhagavadbhaktinityatā
yāvajjano bhajati no bhuvi viṣṇubhakti vārtāsudhārasaviśeṣarasaikasāram | tāvajjarāmaraṇajanmaśatābhighāta duḥkhāni tāni labhate bahudehajāni || 11.607 ||
daśame brahmastutau śreyaḥsṛtiṃ bhaktimudasya te vibho kliśyanti ye kevelabodhalabdhaye | teṣāmasau kleśala eva śiṣyate nānyadyathā sthūlatuṣāvaghātinām || 11.608 ||
ekādaśe mukhabāhūrupādebhyaḥ puruṣasyāśramaiḥ saha | catvāro jajñire varṇā guṇairviprādayaḥ pṛthak || 11.609 || ya evaṃ puruṣaṃ sākṣādātmaprabhavamīśvaram | na bhajantyavajānanti sthānādbhraṣṭāḥ patantyadhaḥ || 11.610 ||
ataivoktaṃ śrībhagavatā na māṃ duṣkṛtino mūḍhāḥ prapadyante narādhamāḥ | māyayāpahṛtajñānā āsuraṃ bhāvamāśritāḥ || 11.611 ||
nityatvaṃ yadyadaṅgānāṃ bhaktervilikhitaṃ purā | tena tenaiva nityatvamasyāṃ saṃsādhitaṃ param || 11.612 || lakṣaṇāni ca tadbhakteḥ śrīmadbhāgavatādiṣu | khyātāni śravaṇādīni likhyante'thāpi kānicit || 11.613 ||
atha śrīmadbhaktilakṣaṇāni tatra sāmānyalakṣaṇam
tṛtīyaskandhe śrīkāpileye devānāṃ guṇaliṅgānāmānuśravikakarmaṇām | sattva evaikamanaso vṛttiḥ svābhāvikī tu yā | animittā bhāgavatī bhaktiḥ siddhergarīyasī || 11.614 ||
atha viśeṣasādhanabhaktilakṣaṇāni
gautamīyatantre devatāyāṃ ca mantre ca tathā mantraprade gurau | bhaktiraṣṭavidhā yasya tasya kṛṣṇaḥ prasīdati || 11.615 || tadbhaktajanavātsalyaṃ pūjāyāṃ cānumodanam | sumanā arcayennityaṃ tadarthe dambhavarjanam || 11.616 || tatkathāśravaṇe rāgastadarthe cāṅgavikriyā | tadanusmaraṇaṃ nityaṃ yastannāmopajīvati || 11.617 || bhaktiraṣṭavidhā hyeṣā yasminmlecche'pi vartate | sa muniḥ satyavādī ca kīrtimān sa bhavennaraḥ || 11.618 || bhaktiraṣṭavidhā hyeṣā yasminmlecche'pi vartate | sa muniḥ satyavādī ca kīrtimān sa bhavennaraḥ || 11.619 ||
saptamaskandhe prahlādoktau śravaṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ viṣṇoḥ smaraṇaṃ pādasevanam | arcanaṃ vandanaṃ dāsyaṃ sakhyamātmanivedanam || 11.620 || iti puṃsārpitā viṣṇau bhaktiścennavalakṣaṇā | kriyeta bhagavatyaddhā tanmanye'dhītamuttamam || 11.621 ||
tatraiva śrīnāradayudhiṣṭhirasaṃvāde śravaṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ cāsya smaraṇaṃ mahatāṃ gateḥ | sevejyāvanatirdāsyaṃ sakhyamātmasamarpaṇam || 11.622 ||
pādme kārttikamāhātmye śrīyamadhūmraketusaṃvāde śravaṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ pūjā sarvakarmārpaṇaṃ smṛtiḥ | paricaryā namaskāraḥ prema svātmārpaṇaṃ harau || 11.622 ||
tatraivottarakhaṇḍe śrīśivapārvatīsaṃvāde ādyaṃ tu vaiṣṇavaṃ proktaṃ śaṅkhacakrāṅkanaṃ hareḥ | dhāraṇaṃ cordhvarpuṇḍrāṇāntanmantrāṇāṃ parigrahaḥ || 11.623 || arcanaṃ ca japo dhyānaṃ tannāmasmaraṇaṃ tathā | kīrtanaṃ śravaṇaṃ caiva vandanaṃ pādasevanam || 11.624 || tatpādodakasevā ca tanniveditabhojanam | tadīyānāṃ ca sevāṃ ca dvādaśīvrataniṣṭhitam || 11.625 || tulasīropaṇaṃ viṣṇordevadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ | bhaktiḥ ṣoḍaśadhā proktā bhavabandhavimuktaye || 11.626 || iti |
kiṃ ca darśanaṃ bhagavanmūrteḥ sparśanaṃ kṣetrasevanam | āghrāṇaṃ dhūpaśeṣādernirmālyasya ca dhāraṇam || 11.627 || nṛtyaṃ bhagavadagre ca tathā vīṇādivādanam | kṛṣṇalīlādyabhinayaḥ śrībhāgavatasevanam || 11.628 || padmākṣamālāvidhṛtirekādaśyādijāgaraḥ | prāsādaracanādyanyajjñeyaṃ śāstrānusārataḥ || 11.629 || likhitā bhagavaddharmā bhaktānāṃ lakṣaṇāni ca | tāni jñeyāni sarvāṇi bhaktervai lakṣaṇāṃ na hi || 11.630 || teṣu jñeyāni gauṇāni mukhyāni ca vivekibhiḥ | bahiraṅgāntaraṅgāṇi premasiddhau ca tāni yat || 11.631 || bhedāstu vividhā bhakterbhaktabhāvādibhedataḥ | muktāphalādigranthebhyo jñeyāstallikhanairalam || 11.632 || premabhaktau ca siddhāyāṃ sarve'rthāḥ sevakāḥ svayam | bhagavāṃścātivaśyaḥ syāllikhyate'syāḥ sulakṣaṇam || 11.633 ||
atha premabhaktilakṣaṇam
nāradapañcarātre ananyamamatā viṣṇau mamatā premasamplutā | bhaktirityucyate bhīṣmaprahlādoddhavanāradaiḥ || 11.634 || iti |
atha śrīmadbhakterdurlabhatvam
premabhakteśca māhātmyaṃ bhaktermāhātmyataḥ param | siddhameva yato bhakteḥ phalaṃ premaiva niścitam || 11.635 || cihnāni premasampatterbāhyānyabhyantarāṇi ca | kiyantyullikhatā tasyā mahimaiva vilikhyate || 11.636 ||
saptamaskandhe śrīprahlādasya prahlādasya bālānuśāsane niśamya karmāṇi guṇānatulyān vīryāṇi līlātanubhiḥ kṛtāni | yadātiharṣotpulakāśrugadgadaṃ protkaṇṭha udgāyati rauti nṛtyati || 11.637 ||
yadā grahagrasta iva kvaciddhasaty ākrandate dhyāyati vandate janam | muhuḥ śvasan vakti hare jagatpate nārāyaṇetyātmamatirgatatrapaḥ || 11.638 ||
tadā pumānmuktasamastabandhanas tadbhāvabhāvānukṛtāśayākṛtiḥ | nirdagdhabījānuśayo mahīyasā bhaktiprayogeṇa sametyadhokṣajam || 11.639 ||
ekādaśe ca śrīkaviyogeśvarottare śṛṇvan subhadrāṇi rathāṅgapāṇer janmāni karmāṇi ca yāni loke | gītāni nāmāni tadarthakāni gāyan vilajjo vicaredasaṅgaḥ || 11.640 ||
evaṃvrataḥ svapriyanāmakīrtyā jātānurāgo drutacitta uccaiḥ | hasatyatho roditi rauti gāyaty unmādavannṛtyati lokabāhyaḥ || 11.641 ||
tatraiva śrīprabuddhayogeśvarottare smarantaḥ smārayantaśca mitho'ghaughaharaṃ harim | bhaktyā sañjātayā bhaktyā bibhratyutpulakāṃ tanum || 11.642 ||
kvacidrudantyacyutacintayā kvacid dhasanti nandanti vadantyalaukikāḥ | nṛtyanti gāyantyanuśīlayantyajaṃ bhavanti tūṣṇīṃ parametya nirvṛtāḥ || 11.643 || śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde ca kathaṃ vinā romaharṣaṃ dravatā cetasā vinā vinānandāśrukalayā śudhyedbhaktyā vināśayaḥ || 11.644 ||
vāggadgadā dravate yasya cittaṃ rudatyabhīkṣṇaṃ hasati kvacicca | vilajja udgāyati nṛtyate ca madbhaktiyukto bhuvanaṃ punāti || 11.645 ||
yathoktabhaktyaśaktau tu bhagavaccaraṇāmbujam | śaraṇāgatabhāvena kṛtsnabhītighnamāśrayet || 11.646 ||
atha śaraṇāpattiḥ
śrīmadbhagavadgītāsu sarvadharmān parityajya māmekaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja | ahaṃ tvā sarvapāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ || 11.647 ||
ekādaśaskandhe ca śrībhagavaduddhavasaṃvāde tasmāttvamuddhavotsṛjya codanāṃ praticodanām | pravṛttiṃ ca nivṛttiṃ ca śrotavyaṃ śrutameva ca || 11.648 || māmekameva śaraṇamātmānaṃ sarvadehinām | yāhi sarvātmabhāvena mayā syā hyakutobhayaḥ || 11.649 ||
tannityatā ca
brahmavaivarte prāpyāpi durlabhataraṃ mānuṣyaṃ vibudhepsitam | yairāśrito na govindastairātmā vañcitaściram || 11.650 || aśīticaturaścaiva lakṣāṃstān jīvajātiṣu | bhramadbhiḥ puruṣaiḥ prāpya mānuṣyaṃ janmaparyayāt || 11.651 || tadapyaphalatāṃ jātaṃ teṣāmātmābhimāninām | varākānāmanāśritya govindacaraṇadvayam || 11.652 ||
atha śaraṇāpattimāhātmyam
uktaṃ ca rāmāyaṇe śrīraghunāthena vibhīṣaṇagamanaprasaṅge sakṛdeva prapanno yastavāsmīti ca yācate | abhayaṃ sarvadā tasmai dadāmyetadvrataṃ hareḥ || 11.653 ||
nārasiṃhe vaikuṇṭhanāthena tvāṃ prapanno'smi śaraṇaṃ devadevaṃ janārdanam | iti yaḥ śaraṇaṃ prāptastaṃ kleśāduddharāmyaham || 11.654 ||
nāmāparādhaprasaṅge pādme śrīnāradaṃ prati śrīsanatkumāreṇa sarvācāravivarjitāḥ śaṭhadhiyo vrātyā jagadvañcakā dambhāhaṅkṛtipānapaiśunaparāḥ pāpāntyajā niṣṭhurā | ye cānye dhanadāraputraniratāḥ sarvādhamāste'pi hi śrīgovindapadāravindaśaraṇā muktā bhavanti dvija || 11.655 ||
brahmavaivarte na hi nārāyaṇaṃ nāma narāḥ saṃśritya śaunaka | prāpnuvantyaśubhaa satyamidamuktaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 11.656 ||
bṛhannāradīye kaliprasaṅge paramārthamaśeṣasya jagatāmādikāraṇam | śaraṇyaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāto govindaḥ nāvasīdati || 11.657 ||
śāntiparvaṇi rājadharme bhīṣmayudhiṣṭhirasaṃvāde sthitaḥ priyahite jiṣṇoḥ sa eva puruṣarṣabha | rājaṃstava ca durdharṣo vaikuṇṭhaḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 11.658 || ya enaṃ saṃśrayantīha bhaktyā nārāyaṇaṃ harim | te tarantīha durgāṇi na me'trāsti vicāraṇā || 11.659 ||
tṛtīyaskandhe viduramaitreyasaṃvāde śārīrā mānasā divyā vaiyāse ye ca mānuṣāḥ | bhautikāśca kathaṃ kleśā bādhante harisaṃśrayam || 11.660 ||
vāmane śrīprahlādabalisaṃvāde ye saṃśritā harimanantamanādimadhyaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ suraguruṃ śubhadaṃ vareṇyam | śuddhaṃ khagendragamanaṃ kamalālayeśaṃ te dharmarājakaraṇaṃ na viśanti dhīrāḥ || 11.661 ||
ye śaṅkhacakrābjakaraṃ saśārṅgiṇaṃ khagendraketuṃ varadaṃ śriyaḥ patim | samāśrayante bhavabhītināśaṃ teṣāṃ bhayaṃ nāsti vimuktibhājām || 11.662 ||
bṛhannāradīye prāyaścittaprakaraṇānte saṃsāre'sminmahāghore mohanidrāsamākule | ye hariṃ śaraṇaṃ yānti te kṛtārthā na saṃśayaḥ || 11.663 ||
brahmapurāṇe karmaṇā manasā vācā ye'cyutaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ | na samartho yamasteṣāṃ te muktiphalabhāginaḥ || 11.664 ||
daśamaskandhe samāśritā ye padapallavaplavaṃ mahatpadaṃ puṇyayaśo murāreḥ | bhavāmbudhirvatsapadaṃ paraṃ padaṃ padaṃ padaṃ yadvipadāṃ na teṣām || 11.665 ||
prathame yatpādasaṃśrayāḥ sūta munayaḥ praśamāyanāḥ | sadyaḥ punantyupaspṛṣṭāḥ svardhunyāpo'nusevayā || 11.666 ||
dvitīye śrīśukoktau kirātahūṇāndhrapulindapulkaśā ābhīraśumbhā yavanāḥ khasādayaḥ | ye'nye ca pāpā yadapāśrayāśrayāḥ śudhyanti tasmai prabhaviṣṇave namaḥ || 11.667 ||
tṛtīye maitreyoktau kiṃ durāpādanaṃ teṣāṃ puṃsāmuddāmacetasām | yairāśritastīrthapadaścaraṇo vyasanātyayaḥ || 11.668 ||
daśame nāgapatnīstutau na nākapṛṣṭhaṃ na ca pārameṣṭhyaṃ na sārvabhaumaṃ na rasādhipatyam | na yogasiddhīrapunarbhavaṃ vā samañjasa tvā virahayya kāṅkṣe || 11.669 ||
ekādaśe ca śrīkarabhājanayogeśvarottare devarṣibhūtāptanṝṇāṃ pitṝṇāṃ nāyaṃ kiṅkaro nāyamṛṇī ca rājan | sarvātmanā yaḥ śaraṇaṃ śaraṇyaṃ gato mukundaṃ parihṛtya kartam || 11.670 ||
ataivoktaṃ śrībhagavantaṃ prati uddhavena tāpatrayeṇābhihatasya ghore santapyamānasya bhavādhvanīśa | paśyāmi nānyaccharaṇaṃ tavāṅghri dvandvātapatrādamṛtābhivarṣāt || 11.671 ||
itthaṃ ca bodhyaṃ vidvadbhiḥ śaraṇāpattilakṣaṇam | vācā hṛdā ca tanvāpi kṛṣṇaikāśrayaṇaṃ hi yat || 11.672 ||
atha śaraṇāpattilakṣaṇam
skandhe govindaṃ paramānandaṃ mukundaṃ madhusūdanam | tyaktvānyaṃ vai na jānāmi na bhajāmi smarāmi na || 11.673 || na namāmi na ca staumi na paśyāmi svacakṣuṣā | na spṛhāmi na gāyāmi na vā yāmi hariṃ vinā || 11.674 || iti |
kecidāhuśca śaraṇāgatatvaṃ ṣaṭprakārakam | prāyaḥ sakhyaprakāre tatparyavasyedvicārataḥ || 11.675 ||
taccoktaṃ vaiṣṇavatantre ānukūlyasya saṅkalpaḥ prātikūlyavivarjanam | rakṣiṣyatīti viśvāso goptṛtve varaṇaṃ tathā | ātmanikṣepakārpaṇye ṣaḍvidhā śaraṇāgatiḥ || 11.676 || iti |
tavāsmīti vadan vācā tathaiva manasā vidan | tatsthānamāśritastanvā modate śaraṇāgataḥ || 11.677 ||
ataivoktaṃ daśame śrībhagavantaṃ prati akrūreṇa kaḥ paṇḍitastvadaparaṃ śaraṇaṃ samīyād bhaktapriyādṛtagiraḥ suhṛdaḥ kṛtajñāt | sarvān dadāti suhṛdo bhajato'bhikāmān ātmanamapyupacayāpacayau na yasya || 11.678 ||
tṛtīye śryuddhavena aho bakī yaṃ stanakālakūṭaṃ jighāṃsayāpāyayadapyasādhvī | lebhe gatiṃ dhātryucitāṃ tato'nyaṃ kaṃ vā dayāluṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajema || 11.679 || iti |
athācārā bahuvidhāḥ śiṣṭācārānusārataḥ | śrīvaiṣṇavānāṃ kartavyā likhyante'tra samāsataḥ || 11.680 ||
athācārāḥ
śrīviṣṇupurāṇe aurvasagarasaṃvāde gṛhasthācārakathanārambhe devagobrāhmaṇān siddhān vṛddhācāryāṃstathārcayet | dvikālaṃ ca nametsandhyāmagnīnupacarettathā || 11.681 || sadānupahate vastre praśastāṃ ca tathauṣadhīḥ | gāruḍāni ca ratnāni vibhṛyātprayato naraḥ || 11.682 || prasnigdhāmalakeśaśca sugandhaścāruveśadhṛk | kiṃcitparasvaṃ na haretnālpamapyapriyaṃ vadet || 11.683 || priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyānnānyadoṣānudīrayet nānyastriyaṃ tathā vairaṃ rocayetpuruṣarṣabha || 11.684 || na duṣṭaṃ yānamārohetkulacchāyāṃ na saṃśrayet || 11.685 || vidviṣṭapatitonmattabahuvairādikīṭakaiḥ | bandhakī bandhakībhartuḥ kṣudrānṛtakathaiḥ saha || 11.686 || tathātivyayaśīlaiśca parivādarataiḥ śaṭhaiḥ | budho maitrīṃ na kurvīta naikaḥ panthānamāśrayet || 11.687 || nāvagāhejjalaughasya vegamagre nareśvara | pradīptaṃ veśma na viśennārohecchikharaṃ taroḥ || 11.688 || na kuryāddantasaṅgharṣaṃ kuṣṇīyācca na nāsikām | nāsaṃvṛtamukho jṛmbhecchvāsakāsau visarjayet || 11.689 || noccairhasetsaśabdaṃ ca na muñcetpavanaṃ budhaḥ | nakhānna khādayecchindyānna tṛṇaṃ na mahīṃ likhet || 11.690 || na śmaśru bhakṣayelloṣṭaṃ na mūdnīyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 11.691 || jyotīṃṣyamedhyaśastāni nābhivīkṣeta ca prabho || 11.692 || na huṅkuryācchavaṃ gandhaṃ śavagandho hi somajaḥ || 11.693 || catuṣpathaṃ ciatyataruṃ śmaśānopavanāni ca | duṣṭastrīsannikarṣaṃ ca varjayenniśi sarvadā | pūjyadevadvijajyotiśchāyāṃ nātikramedbudhaḥ || 11.694 || naikaḥ śūnyāṭavīṃ gacchettathā śūnyagṛhe vaset || 11.695 || keśāsthikaṇṭakāmedhyabalibhasmatuṣāṃstathā | snānārdradharaṇīṃ caiva dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 11.696 || nānārthānāśrayetkāṃścinna jihmaṃ rocayedbudhaḥ || 11.697 || upasarpenna vai vyālaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhenna cotthitaḥ | yatheṣṭabhojakāṃścaiva tathā devaparāṅmukhān | varṇāśramakriyātītān dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 11.698 || atīva jāgarasvapnau tadvatsthānāsane budhaḥ | na seveta tathā śayyāṃ vyāyāmaṃ ca nareśvara || 11.699 || daṃṣṭriṇaḥ śṛṅgiṇaścaiva prājño dūreṇa varjayet || 11.700 || avaśyāyaṃ ca rājendra puro vātātapau tathā | na snāyānna svapennagno na caivopaspṛśedbudhaḥ || 11.701 || muktakacchaśca nācāmeddevādyarcāṃ ca varjayet | naikavastraḥ pravartetaa dvijavācanike jape || 11.702 || kiṃ ca na ca nirdhūnayetkeśānnācāmeccaiva cotthitaḥ | pādena nākrametpādaṃ na pūjyābhimukhaṃ nayet || 11.703 || apasavyaṃ na gacchecca devāgāracatuṣpathān | maṅgalyapūjyāṃśca tathā viparītānna dakṣiṇām || 11.704 || somārkāgnyambuvāyūnāṃ pūjyānāṃ ca na sammukham | kuryānniṣṭhīvaviṇmūtrasamutsargaṃ ca paṇḍitaḥ || 11.705 || tiṣṭhanna mūtrayettadvatpathiṣvapi na mūtrayet | śleṣmaviṇmūtraraktāni sarvadaiva na laṅghayet || 11.706 || śleṣmaṣṭīvanakotsargo nānnakāle praśasyate | balimaṅgalajapyādau na home na mahājane || 11.707 || yoṣito nāvamanyeta na cāsāṃ viśvasedbudhaḥ | na caiverṣyurbhavettāsu nādhikuryātkadācana || 11.708 || maṅgalyapuṣparatnājyapūjyānanabhivādya ca | na niṣkrāmedgṛhātprājñaḥ sadācāraparo naraḥ || 11.709 || akālagarjitādau tu parvasvaśaucakādiṣu | anadhyāyaṃ budhaḥ kuryāduparāgādike tathā || 11.710 || varṣātapādike chatrī daṇḍī rātryaṭavīṣu ca | śarīratrāṇakāmo vai sopānatkaḥ sadā vrajet || 11.711 || nordhvaṃ na tiryagdūraṃ vā nirīkṣan paryaṭedbudhaḥ | yugamātraṃ mahīpṛṣṭhaṃ naro gacchedvilokayan || 11.712 || kiṃ ca priyamuktaṃ hitaṃ naitaditi matvā na tadvadet | śreyastadrahitaṃ vācyaṃ yadyapyatyantavipriyam || 11.713 || prāṇināmupakārāya yadeveha paratra ca | karmaṇā manasā vācā tadeva matimān bhajet || 11.714 ||
bṛhannāradīye sadācāraprasaṅge asāvahamiti brūyāddvijo vai hyabhivādane | śrāddhaṃ vrataṃ tathā dānaṃ devatābhyarcanaṃ tathā | yajñaṃ ca tarpaṇaṃ caiva kurvantaṃ nābhivādayet || 11.715 || tathā snānaṃ prakurvantaṃ dhāvantamaśuciṃ tathā | bhuñjānaṃ ca abhyaktaśirasaṃ tathā || 11.716 || bhikṣānnadhāriṇaṃ caiva ramantaṃ jalamadhyagam | kṛte'bhivādane yastu na kuryātprativādanam | nābhivādyaḥ sa vijñeyo yathā śūdrastathaiva saḥ || 11.717 ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇemadrālasālarkasaṃvāde mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇemadrālasālarkasaṃvāde asatpralāpamanṛtaṃ vkpāruṣyaṃ ca varjayet | asacchāstramasadvādamasatsevāṃ ca putraka || 11.718 || keśaprasādhanādarśadarśanaṃ dantadhāvanam | pūrvāhna eva kāryāṇi devatānāṃ ca tarpaṇam || 11.719 || udakyā darśanaṃ sparśaṃ varjeta sambhāṣaṇaṃ tathā || 11.720 || na cābhīkṣṇaṃ śiraḥsnānaṃ kuryānniṣkāraṇaṃ naraḥ | śiraḥsnātaśca tailena nāṅgaṃ kiṃcidapi spṛśet || 11.721 || panthā deyo brāhmaṇānāṃ rājo duḥkhāturasya ca | vidyādhikasya gurviṇyā bhārārtasya mahīyasaḥ || 11.722 || mūkāndhavadhirāṇāṃ ca mattasyonmattakasya ca | puṃścalyāḥ kṛtavairasya bālasya patitasya ca || 11.723 || upānadvastramālyāni dhṛtānyanyairna dhārayet | upavītamalaṅkāraṃ kavalaṃ caiva varjayet || 11.724 || na kṣiptabāhujaṅghaśca prājñastiṣṭhetkadācana | na cāpi vikṣipetpādau vāsasā na ca dhūnayet || 11.725 || mūrkhonmattavyasanino virūpānmāyinastathā | nyūnāṅgānadhamāṃścaiva nopahāsenna dūṣayet || 11.726 || parasya daṇḍaṃ nodyacchetśikṣārthaṃ putraśiṣyayoḥ | nānulepanamādadyādasnātaḥ snātakī kvacit || 11.727 || na cāpi raktavāsāḥ syāccitravāsadharo'pi vā | kṣurakarmaṇi cānte ca strīsambhoge ca putraka | snāyīta celavān prājñaḥ kaṭabhūmimupetya ca || 11.728 || yugapajjalamagniṃ ca bibhṛyānna vicakṣaṇaḥ | nācakṣīta dhayantīṃ gāṃ jalaṃ nāñjalinā pibet || 11.729 || śaucakāleṣu sarveṣu guruṣvalpeṣu vā punaḥ | na vilambeta śaucārthaṃ na mukhenānalaṃ dhamet || 11.730 || vipurṣo makṣikādyāśca hastasaṅgādadoṣiṇaḥ | ajāśvau muhato medhyau na gorvatsasya cānanam || 11.731 || mātuḥ prasnavane medhyaṃ śakuniḥ phalapātane | udakyāśaucinagnāṃśca sūtikāntyācasāyinaḥ | spṛṣṭvā snāyīta śaucārthaṃ tathaiva mṛtahāriṇaḥ || 11.732 || nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātaḥ śudhyati mānavaḥ | ācamyaiva tu nisnehaṃ gāmālabhyārkamīkṣya vā || 11.733 || na cālapejjanaṃ dviṣṭaṃ vīrahīnāṃ tathā striyam | devatātithisacchāstrayajñasiddhādinindakaiḥ || 11.734 || kṛtvā tu sparśatālāpaṃ śudhyedarkāvalokanāt | avalokya tathodakyāmantyajān patitaṃ śaṭham | vidharmisūtikāṣaṇḍavivastrānyāvasāyinaḥ || 11.735 || mṛtaniryātakāścaiva paradāraratāśca ye | etadeva hi kartavyaṃ prājñaiḥ śodhanamātmanaḥ || 11.736 ||
kiṃ ca yaccāpi kurvato nātmā jugupsāmeti putraka | tatkartavyamaśaṅkena yanna gopyaṃ mahājane || 11.737 ||
bhaviṣyottare śrīkṛṣṇayudhiṣṭhirasaṃvāde upāsate na ye pūrvāṃ dvijāḥ sandhyāṃ na paścimām | sarvāmstān dhārmiko rājā śūdrakarmaṇi yojayet || 11.738 || dūrādāvasathānmūtraṃ dūrātpādāvasecanam | ucchiṣṭotsarjanaṃ bhūpa sadā kāryā hitaiṣiṇā || 11.739 || ucchiṣṭau na spṛśecchīrṣaṃ sarve prāṇāstadāśrayāḥ | keśagrāhān prahārāṃśca śirasyetāni varjayet | na pāṇibhyāmubhābhyāṃ tu kaṇḍūyājjātu vai śiraḥ || 11.740 ||
kiṃ ca suvāsinīrgurviṇīśca vṛddhaṃ bālāturau tathā | bhojayetsaṃskṛtānneva prathamaṃ caramaṃ gṛhī || 11.741 || aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte vṛddhe govāhanādike | yo bhuṅkte pāṇḍavaśreṣṭha prekṣatāmapradāya ca || 11.742 || varjayeddadhiśaktuṃ ca rātrau dhānāśca vāsare || 11.743 ||
kiṃ ca srajaśca nāvakarṣeta na bahirdhārayeta ca | gṛhe pārāvatā dhanyāḥ śukāśca sahasārikāḥ || 11.744 ||
kaurme vyāsagītāyām tṛṇaṃ vā yadi vā śākaṃ mūlaṃ vā jalameva vā | parasyāpaharan janturnarakaṃ pratipadyate || 11.745 || na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyānna śūdrātpatitādapi | nānyasmādyācakatvaṃ ca ninditādvarjayedbudhaḥ || 11.746 || nityaṃ yācanako na syātpunastatraiva yācayet | prāṇānapaharatyeṣa yācakastasya durmatiḥ || 11.747 || na devadravyahārī syādviśeṣeṇa dvijottamāḥ | brahmasvaṃ ca nāpaharedāpadyapi kadācana || 11.748 || na viṣaṃ viṣamityāhurbrahmasvaṃ viṣamucyate | devasvaṃ vāpi yatnena sadā pariharettataḥ || 11.749 || na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret | vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvan strīśūdradambhanam | pretyeha cedṛśo vipro garhyeta brahmavādibhiḥ || 11.750 || devadrohādgurudrohaḥ koṭikoṭiguṇādhikaḥ | jñānāpavāo nāstikyaṃ tasmādkoṭiguṇādhikam || 11.751 ||
kiṃ ca himavadvindhyayormadhye pūrvapaścimayoḥ śubham | muktvā samudrayordeśaṃ nānyatra nivaseddvijaḥ || 11.752 || kṛṣṇo vā yatra carati mṛgo nityaṃ svabhāvataḥ | puṇyāśca viśrutā nadyastatra vā nivaseddvijaḥ | ardhakrośānnadīkulaṃ varjayitvā dvijottamāḥ || 11.753 ||
kiṃ ca agninā bhasmanā caiva salilena viśeṣataḥ | dvāreṇa stambhamārgeṇa padbhiḥ paṅktirvibhidyate | parakṣetre carantīṃ gāṃ na cācakṣīta kasyacit || 11.754 || naṣuryapariveśaṃ vā nendracāpaṃ na cāgnikam | parasmai kathayedvidvān śaśinaṃ vā kathaṃcana | tithiṃ pakṣasya na brūyānnakṣatrāṇi vinirdiśet || 11.755 || na devaguruviprāṇāṃ dīyamānaṃ tu vārayet | nindayedyo gurūn devān vedaṃ vā sopabṛṃhitam | kalpakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ raurave pacyate naraḥ || 11.756 || tūṣṇīmāsīta nindāyāṃ na brūyātkiṃciduttaram | karṇau pidhāya gantavyaṃ na cainamavalokayet || 11.757 || varjayedrahasyaṃ ca pareṣāṃ gūhayedbudhaḥ | vivādaṃ svajanaiḥ sārdhaṃ na kuryādvai kadācana || 11.758 || na pāpaṃ pāpinaṃ brūyādapāpaṃ vā dvijottamāḥ || 11.759 || nekṣetodyantamādityaṃ śaśinaṃ vā nimittataḥ || 11.760 || āstaṃ yāntaṃ na vāristhaṃ nopasṛṣṭaṃ na madhyagam | tirohitaṃ vāsasā vā na darśāntaragāminam || 11.761 || nagnāṃ striyaṃ pumāṃsaṃ vā purīṣaṃ mūtrameva vā | patitavyaṅgacāṇḍālānucchiṣṭānucchiṣṭānnāvalokayet || 11.762 || na muktabandhanāṃ gāṃ vā nonmattaṃ mattameva vā | spṛśenna bhojane patnīṃ naināmīkṣeta mehatīm || 11.763 || kṣubantīṃ jṛmbhamāṇāṃ vā nāsanasthāṃ yathā sukham | nodake cātmano rūpaṃ nakulaṃ śvabhrameva vā || 11.764 || na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyātkṛṣaraṃ pāyasaṃ dadhi | nocchiṣṭaṃ vā ghṛtamadhu na ca kṛṣṇājinaṃ haviḥ || 11.765 || na kuryātkasyacitpīḍāṃ sutaṃ śiṣyaṃ ca tāḍayet | nātmānamavamanyeta dainyaṃ yatnena varjayet | na ca śiṣyānnaṃ satkuryānnātmānaṃ śaṃsayedbudhaḥ || 11.766 || na nadīṃ ca nadīṃ brūyātparvateṣu na parvatam | āvasettena naivāpi yastyajetsahavāsinam || 11.767 || śiro'bhyaṅgāvaśiṣṭena talenāṅgaṃ na lepayet | romāṇi ca rahasyāni svāni khāni na ca spṛśet || 11.768 || na pāṇipādavāṅnetracāpalāni samāśrayet | saṃveśayenna hi | nākṣaiḥ krīḍenna dhāveta strībhirvādaṃ ca cācaret || 11.771 || na dantairnakhalomāni chindyātsuptaṃ na bodhayet | na bālātapamāsevetpretadhūmaṃ vivarjayet || 11.772 || naikaḥ supyātśūnyagṛhe svayaṃ nopānahau haret | nākāraṇādvā niṣṭhīvenna bāhubhyāṃ nadīṃ taret || 11.773 || na pādakṣālanaṃ kuryātpādenaiva kadācana | nāgnau pratāpayetpādau na kāṃsye dhārayedbudhaḥ || 11.774 || nābhipratārayeddevān brāhmaṇān gāmathāpi vā | na spṛśetpāninocchiṣṭo viprā gobrāhmaṇānalān | na caivānnaṃ padā vāpi na devapratimāṃ spṛśet || 11.775 || nottaredanupaspṛśya sravantīṃ no vyatikramet | caityaṃ bṛhmaṃ naiva chindyānnāpsu ṣṭhīvanamutsṛjet | na cāgniṃ laṅghayeddhīmānnopadadhyādadhaḥ kvacit || 11.776 || na cainaṃ pādataḥ kuryāttilabaddhaṃ niśi tyajet | na kūpamavaroheta nācakṣītāśuciḥ kvacit || 11.777 || agnau na prakṣipedagniṃ nādbhiḥ praśamayettathā | suhṛnmaraṇamārtiṃ vā na svayaṃ śrāvayetparān | apaṇyamatha paṇyaṃ vā vikrayaṃ na prayojayet || 11.778 || puṇyasthānodakasthāne sīmāntaṃ vā kṛṣenna tu | bhindyātpūrvasamayaṃ satryopetaṃ kadācana || 11.779 || parasparaṃ paśūn vyālān pakṣiṇo na ca yodhayet | kārayitvā svakarmāṇi kārūn vidvānna vañcayet || 11.780 || bahirgandhaṃ ca kudvārapraveśaṃ ca vivarjayet | naikaścaretsabhāṃ vipraḥ samavāyaṃ ca varjayet || 11.781 || na vījayedvā vastreṇa na devāyatane svapet | nāgnigobrāhmaṇādīnāmantareṇa vrajetkvacit || 11.782 || nākrāmetkāmataśchāyāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ gavāmapi | svāntu nākrāmayecchāyaṃ patitādyairna rogibhiḥ || 11.783 || nāśnīyātpayasā takraṃ na bījānyupabījayet | vivatsāyāśca goḥ kṣīramauṣṭraṃ vā nirdaśasya ca | āvikaṃ sandhinīkṣīramapeyaṃ manurabravīt || 11.784 || hantakāramathāgryaṃ vā bhikṣāṃ vā śaktito dvijaḥ | dadyādatithaye nityaṃ budhyeta parameśvaram || 11.785 || bhikṣāmāhurgrāsamātramagryaṃ mārkaṇḍeye bhojanaṃ hantakāraṃ vā agryaṃ bhikṣāmathāpi vā | adattvā tu na bhoktavyaṃ yathāvibhavamātmanaḥ || 11.787 ||
kāśīkhaṇḍe naivotkaṭāsane'śnīyānnāgnau vastvaśuci kṣipet | śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā paraśrāddhe yo'śnīyājjñānavarjitaḥ | dātuḥ śrāddhaphalaṃ nāsti bhoktā kilbiṣabhugbhavet || 11.788 || notpāṭayellomanakhaṃ daśanena kadācana | karajaiḥ karajacchedaṃ kareṇaiva vivarjayet || 11.789 || apadvāre na gantavyaṃ svaveśmaparaveśmanoḥ | utkocadyutadautyārthadravyaṃ dūrātparityajet || 11.790 || niṣṭhīvanaṃ ca śleṣmāṇaṃ gṛhāddūre vinikṣipet | uddhṛtya pañca mṛtpiṇḍān snāyātparajalāśaye | anuddhṛtya ca tatkarturenasaḥ syātturīyabhāk || 11.791 ||
brāhme yastu pāṇitale bhuṅkte yastu phutkārasaṃyutam | prasṛtāṅgulibhiryastu tasya gomāṃsavacca tat || 11.792 ||
atrismṛtau nyūnādhikastanī yā gauryāthavābhakṣyacāriṇī | tayordugdhaṃ na hotavyaṃ na pātavyaṃ kadācana || 11.793 || ajā gāvo mahiṣyaśca yāmedhyamapi bhakṣayet | havye kavye ca taddugdhaṃ gomayaṃ ca vivarjayet || 11.794 || aṅgulyā dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ tathā | mṛttikāprāśanaṃ caiva tulyaṃ gomāṃsabhakṣaṇaiḥ || 11.795 ||
atrāpavādo manusmṛtau sāmudraṃ saindhavaṃ caiva lavaṇe paramādbhute | pratyakṣe api te grāhye niṣedhastvanyagocaraḥ || 11.796 || atrismṛtau divā kapitthacchāyā ca niśāyāṃ dadhibhojanam | kārpāsaṃ dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca śakrādapi haretśriyam || 11.797 ||
viṣṇusmṛtau ca kapilāyāḥ payaḥ pītvā śūdrastu narakaṃ vrajet | homaśeṣaṃ pibedvipro vipraḥ syādanyathā paśuḥ || 11.798 ||
parihartuṃ punarlekhaṃ tattatśāstroktamanyathā | yadatra likhitaṃ kiñcittatkṣantavyaṃ mahātmabhiḥ || 11.799 || ācārāścedṛśāḥ santi pare'pi bahulāḥ satām | te lokaśāstrato jñeyā apekṣyā yadi vaiṣṇavaiḥ || 11.800 || nityatvameṣāṃ māhātmyamapyatra likhitātpurā | sadācārasya nityatvānmāhātmyācca susidhyati || 11.801 ||
